|| śrīḥ ||

|| śrīmate rāmānujāya namaḥ | śriyai namaḥ | śrīdharāya namaḥ ||

' yato vā imāni '

sarvaśakti samastaheya pratyanīka-

'sarvajñaṃ'

ityādijagatkāraṇavādivākyapratipādyaṃ

"kalyāṇaguṇakatānaṃ brahma jijñāsyam - ityuktam | idānīṃ jagatkāraṇavādivākyānām ānumānikapradhānādipratipādanānarhatā ucyate 'īkṣatenaśabdam' ityādinā ||

idamāmnāyate chāndogye –

sadeva somyedamagra āsīdekamevādvitīyam' tadaikṣata bahu syāṃ prajāyeyeti, tattejo'sṛjata'

ityādi ||

tatra 'sandehaḥ' – kiṃ sacchandavācyaṃ jagatkāraṇaṃ, paroktam ānumānikaṃ pradhānam ? uta 'uktalakṣaṇaṃ brahma ? - iti ||

yato vā imānīti, kāraṇatvānuvādena tasya lakṣaṇatvavidhānāt, kāraṇasya cidacidvai- lakṣaṇyanirṇayasya kāraṇatvaprāpakavākyanirūpaṇasāpekṣatvāt, ammin adhikaraṇe kāraṇavākyāni nirūpyante iti ca jñāpanārtha lakṣaṇavākyopādānam | ityādi iti ādiśabdena kāraṇatva- prāpakavākyāni gṛhyante | ata eva hi anuvāda -purovādasādhāraṇyena jagatkāraṇavādi ityuktam | jijñāsyam – prāmāṇikatayā parīkṣyam | pradhānādi iti ādiśabdena kṣetrajñamātram, cidviśeṣāḥ, acidviśeṣāśca gṛhyante | ityādineti | pādaśeṣeṇa, adhyāyaśeṣeṇeti vā arthaḥ || pratijñā-dṛṣṭāntopetaṃ saṅkalpapratipādakaṃ sāmānyaśabdavat kāraṇavākyaṃ viṣayaḥ | tatra sadvidyāvākyameva, asya adhikaraṇasya sākṣādviṣaya: | sadvidyādvāreṇa īdṛśakāraṇavākyānāṃ viṣayatvaṃ na tu sākṣādeva; viṣayavākyopādānasamaye vākyāntarāṇām anupādānāt ||

idamiti viṣayamudāharati |

tatra sandeha iti | sandigdhe hi ! nyāyapravṛttiḥ | kāraṇasya 'idaṃ' śabdavācya kārya- sālakṣaṇyāvaśyambhāvekṣitṛtvābhyāṃ saṃśayaḥ | sacchandavācyam iti, dharmivācipadasya sādhāraṇatayā

1 sarvajña satyasaṅkalpaṃ tha, da, 4 uktalakṣaṇameva - du.

kiṃ prāptam ?

pradhānam - iti ||

kutaḥ 1

'sadeva somyedamagra āsīdekameva '
iti 'idaṃ' śabdavācyasya cetanabhogyabhūtasya satvarajastamomayasya viyadādi' nānā rūpa' - vikārāvasthasya vastunaḥ kāraṇāvasthāṃ vadati | kāraṇabhūta dravyasya avasthā ntarāpattireva hi ! kāryatā | ataḥ 'yadravyaṃ yatsvabhāvaṃ ca kāryāvastham, tatsvabhāvaṃ tadeva dravyaṃ kāraṇāvastham ; 'saccādimayaṃ ca kāryam' iti, guṇasāmyāvasthaṃ 'pradhānameva hi kāraṇam ; tadeva upasaṃhṛtasakala viśeṣaṃ sanmātramiti,
'sadeva somyedamagra āsīdekamevādvitīyam'
pradhānamitīti | saṃśayakoḍīkṛte śirodvaye 'pradhānam' iti śiraḥ yuktimat ityarthaḥ (śru pra ) ||
'idaṃśabdavācyasya' ityādīni viśeāgāni kāraṇasya pradhānatvasiddhayaupayikā ne tadvilakṣaṇapuruṣaviśeṣatvavirodhīni ca | parāgarthe hi 'idaṃ' śabdasvārasyam ; na tu pratyabhūpe cetane ; iti bhāvaḥ | cetanabhogyabhūtasya iti jagataḥ ciciddātmakatayā cetanasyā'pi 'idaṃ' śabdavācyāntarbhāvavyudāsāya | cidaṃśasya kāryatva kāraṇatvāyogāt na tasya kāryāntarbhāvaḥ iti, bhoktatayā avasthānameva tasya | bhogyasyaiva tu kāryatvam iti bhāvaḥ | sacceti | nirguṇaḥ kathaṃ jagataḥ sattvādikāryasukha-duḥkha-mohānvayahetutvadarśanāt santvādimayatvam | viyadādīti | ekatvāvasthaṃ kāraṇam, guṇamayasya kāraṇam ? – iti cā'bhiprāyaḥ | bahutvāvasthaṃ kāryamiti bhidāityarthaḥ | ekarūpasya puruṣasya nānārūpakāryabhāvaḥ anupapannaśca iti bhāvaḥ | guṇasāmyāvasthamiti | viṣamāvasthasya sarvasya kāryatvadarśanāt, syāpi tathātve, kāraṇāntarāpekṣayā anavasthāprasaṅgācca pradhānasya guṇasāmyāvasthatvasiddhiḥ iti bhāvaḥ | ekameva iti nāma-rūpavibhāgavyudāsaḥ, advitīyam iti guṇavaiṣamyavyudāsaḥ ' sadeva' iti ityamiprāyeṇa upasaṃhṛtasakalaviśeṣam ityuktam | sanmātramitīti |

ityabhidhīyate | tata eva ca, kārya-kāraṇayoḥ ananyatvam |

|

na bhavati tat' aśabdam - ānumānikam ; pradhānamityarthaḥ | na tat jagatkāraṇa- vādivākyapratipādyam |

sa imā~lokānasṛjata'
saīkṣāṃcakre '

'iti' | tadetat-

'na vilakṣaṇatvāt '

ityādiṣu pratipādayiṣyate | atra, 'sṛṣṭivākyāni na pradhānapratipādanayogyāni ' ityucyate | vastuvirodhastu' tatraiva parihariṣyate ||

|

yattktam – pratijñā · dṛṣṭāntayogāt 'anumānarūpameva ' idaṃ vākyam - iti, tadasat ; hetvanupādānāt |
'yenā'śrutaṃ śrutam '

iti, ekavijñānena sarvavijñāne pratipipādayiṣite, sarvātmanā tadasaṃbhava 'manvānasya' tatsaṃbhava mātrapradarśanāya hi, dṛṣṭāntopādānam | īkṣatyādiśravaṇādeva hi, anumānagandhā- bhāvaḥ avagataḥ || 5 ||
uktā kāravirahe kāraṇatvāyogāt | atretyādi | 'na vilakṣaṇatvāt ' ityādisūtrai | eṣāṃ sūtrāṇāṃ paunaruktyāśaṅkāparihāraḥ | vastuvirodhaḥ - arthasāmarthyavirodhaḥ | atra vākya tarkeṇa arthaniṣkarṣaḥ ; tatra tu ābhimānikavyāyābhāsanibandhanabāhya tarka vimarśa- tannirāsamukhena arthaniṣkarṣa:, iti bhitrā ityarthaḥ | evaṃ satvādiguṇamayasya 'idaṃ' śabdavācyasya kāraṇatvam, taduttambhakam ekavijñānena sarvavijñānapratijñānaṃ ca parihṛtaṃ bhavati ||
hetvanupādānāditi | vaktaḥ anumānavivakṣā asti cet, tatra pradhānāvayavo hetuḥ avaśyopādeyaḥ | tadabhāvāt na tadvivakṣā iti bhāvaḥ | yenā'śrutaṃ ... dṛṣṭāntopādānamiti : pratijñā dṛṣṭāntābhyāṃ hetvākṣepo'pi ayuktaḥ ; ananyathāsiddhaṃ hi ākṣepakam | sarvapramāṇa- sādhāraṇatvāt pratijñā tāvat anyathāsiddheti | siddhaṃ kṛtvā dṛṣṭāntasya hetvākṣepakatvāyoga ucyate | dṛṣṭāntopanyāsaḥ, na kevalaṃ vyāpti - pradarśanāyaiva; api tu asambhavanyāvṛttipradarśa nāyā'pi saṃbhavatīti, anyathāsiddhatvāt so'pi nā'kṣepakaḥ ityarthaḥ | anumānagandhābhāva iti | pratijñā-dṛṣṭāntayoḥ sādhāraṇyena, anumānatvaṃ śaṅkitam | īkṣaṇādyasādhāraṇahetusadbhāvāt, - śaṅkā'pi nirastā ityabhiprāya: || 5 ||

atha syāt – 'na cetanagataṃ mukhyam' īkṣaṇam ihocyate, api tu pradhānagataṃ gauṇamīkṣaṇam ;

' tatteja aikṣata'
tā āpa aikṣanta'

iti gauṇekṣaṇa sāhacaryāt | bhavati ca acetaneṣvapi cetanadharmopacāraḥ ; yathā—

vṛṣṭipratīkṣāḥ śālayaḥ

''varṣeṇa bīja' pratisañjaharṣa '
'ataḥ gauṇamīkṣaṇam' iti imāmāśaṅkām anubhāṣya pariharati-
gauṇaścennātmaśabdāt || 1-1-6 ||
atheti uttarasūtramavatārayitum īkṣagasya gauṇatvaṃ śaṅkate | cetanagatam — cetanatvānu- guṇam | iheti | prakaraṇam ālocanīyam iti bhāva: | pradhānagatam — acetanatvānuguṇam | dhānaśabdaḥ prathamekṣaṇasya tejaḥ prabhṛti kāryācidbhutatvāyogāt | prāyadarśanāya vākyadvayamupāttam | 'agryaprāya' nyāyo'pi bādhakābhāve jīvatīti bhāvaḥ | 'seyaṃ devatā ' iti devatāśabdo'pi anenaiva nyāyena, 'imāstisro devatā:' itivat, acidviṣayatvaucityāt na gauṇatvabādhakaḥ iti bhāva: |

iti |

pradhānakarmaprāye vacanam, pradhānakarmatām upohūlayati | yathā agrayaprāye likhitaṃ dṛṣṭvā, ' bhavedayamagryaḥ' iti

yaduktam – gauṇekṣaṇasāhacaryāt, sato'pi īkṣaṇavyapadeśaḥ, sarganiyatapūrvāvasthā- bhiprāyo gauṇaḥ - iti,

hantā'hamimāstisro devatā anena jīvenātmanā'nupraviśya nāmarūpe vyākaravāṇi '
iti paramātmānupraveśādeva tejaḥprabhṛtīnāṃ vastutvaṃ tattannāmabhāktvaṃ ceti,
' tatteja aikṣata'

tā āpa aikṣanta '
ityapi, mukhya eva īkṣaṇavyapadeśaḥ | ataḥ sāhacaryādapi,
' tadaikṣata '

ityatra 'gauṇatvāśaṅkā' dūrotmāritā – iti sūtrābhiprāyaḥ || 6 ||
itaśca na pradhānaṃ sacchabdapratipādyam-

atha tadeva nāmtītyāha —ata

gauṇekṣaṇa sāhacaryam abhyupagamya dūṣaṇam uktam |

tejo'vannānāmapīti api-

ityādinā | ataḥ - cidacidātmakaprāñcaṃ prati atmavān|

yadāśrayatayā prathamam īkṣaṇam

na hi kiñcidapi atadātmakam' ityabhiprāyaḥ |

śabdasya,

mukhyaṃ sa eva iti evakārābhiprāyaḥ | tejaḥprabhṛtayo'pīti apiśabdasya, sacchandavat iti bhāvaḥ | paramātmana eveti | viśeṣyatayā prādhānyena pratipādyaḥ paramātmaiva ; tejaḥ prabhṛtayastu dvāramātram iti bhāvaḥ | vastutvam — tadrūpavastutvam | tejaso vastutvaṃ tejastvam,

apāṃ vastutvam — atram, pṛthivyāḥ vastutvaṃ pṛthivītvam devamanuṣyādīnāṃ vastutvaṃ_devatva - manuṣyatvādikam tattadavasthā bhāktvam ityarthaḥ | nāmabhāktvam — nāmavācyatvam | itiśabdaḥ heturaḥ | sāhacaryādapi iti apiśabdaḥ gauṇatvaśaṅkāhetusamuccayaparaḥ | dūrotsāriteti | gauṇekṣaṇasāhacarye satyapi ātmaśabdāt prathamekṣaṇam agauṇam, ātmaśabdādeva gauṇekṣaṇa sāhacaryam asiddhaṃ ca ityabhiprāyaḥ |
asmin adhikaraṇe vyutpādyam pratijñā - dṛṣṭāntayoḥ hetvanākṣepakatvaṃ, mukhyā mukhyārthānugrāha- keṣu mukhyānugrāhakasya anuroddhavyatvaṃ ca - iti sūtradvaye spaṣṭam ||6||
itaśceti avāntarasādhyaviṣayatvaśaṅkā vyudasyate | kāraṇatvaupayikekṣaṇadharmanirūpaṇena paramātmatvaṃ sataḥ samarthitam | atha ' kāraṇaṃ tu dhyeyaḥ' iti kāraṇavastvasādhāraṇa mokṣa- pradatvadharmaṇaḥ paramātmatvaṃ sādhyate iti saṅgatiḥ |

tanniṣṭhasya mokṣopedaśāt || 1-1-7||

mumukṣoḥ śvetaketoḥ,

' tat tvamasi'
iti sadātmakatvānusandhānam upadiśya, tanniṣṭasya
' tasya tāvadeva ciraṃ yāvanna vimokṣye, atha 'sampatsye' iti 'śarīrapātamātrāntarāyaḥ brahmasampattilakṣaṇo mokṣaḥ' ityupadiśati | yadi ca pradhānam acetanaṃ kāraṇamupadiśyeta; tadā tadātmakatvānusandhānasya mokṣasādhanatvopadeśaḥ nopapadyate |
'yathākraturasmi~lloke puruṣo bhavati, tathetaḥ pretya bhavati'
sūtre tanniṣṭhasya iti padaṃ 'tasya tāvat' iti śrutau 'tasya' iti padasya vyākhyānam |

mumukṣoḥ iti padena arvācīnaphala kadṛṣṭividhisambhavāt cetane kṛtsnajagadupādānatayotkṛṣṭa- pradhānadṛṣṭividhirayam iti śaṅkā nirastā | sadātmakatveti | sūtre tacchabdaḥ, 'sadātmakatvopa- deśapara ' tattvamasī' tivākyagatatacchandaḥ prakṛtasadviṣayaḥ iti jñāpanaparaḥ, ityabhiprāyaḥ | anusandhānaśabdena niṣṭhā śabdārthaḥ uktaḥ | antarāyaśabdena vilambo lakṣyate | śarīrapātāvadhi vilambaḥ ityarthaḥ | brahmasampattilakṣaṇa iti | 'atha sampatsye' iti sampatteḥ, 'satā somya tadā sampanna bhavati' iti vākyāntarapratipannaṃ brahmava karmeti bhāvaḥ | mokṣa iti | sautrasya 'mokṣa' śabdasya śarīrapātapūrvikā brahmasampattirevārthaḥ, na tu śarīrapātamātram ityabhiprāyaḥ | yathākraturityādi | tīrthakṣetra sevādivat śāstrabalāt acidupāsanasya mokṣopayogitvaṃ upāsanānurūpaphalabhāktvaṃ yathākraturiti śrutyarthaḥ |

kiṃ na syāt iti śaṅkāyāmidam |
tataśca yopāsanāt heyaprahāṇānupapattiḥ |

iti, tanniṣṭhasya acetanasampattireva syāt | na ca mātāpitṛsahasrebhyo'pi vatsalataraṃ' śāstram, evaṃvidhatāpatrayābhihatihetubhūtām acitsampattim upadiśati |
pradhānakāraṇa vādino'pi hi pradhānaniṣṭhasya mokṣaṃ nā'bhyupagacchanti || 7 ||

itaśca na pradhānam ;

heyatvāvacanācca || 1-1-8||

yadi pradhānameva kāraṇaṃ sacchabdābhihitaṃ bhavet tadā mumukṣoḥ śvetaketoḥ
acetanasampattiḥ—acitprāptiḥ, saṃsāritvam | karmakṛtācitsambandho hi saṃsāraḥ | sampattiśabdasya prātyarthatvam ' tāpatrayābhihati hetubhūtām acitsampattim ' ityanantarabhā- vyeṇa vyaktam | mātāpitriti | mātāpitarau ekasmin janmani aihika poṣaṇamātraparau | śāstraṃ tu anekeṣu janmasu aihikāmuṣmika sarvavidharakṣaṇaparam iti bhāvaḥ | ' śāstraṃ hi vatsalataraṃ mātā pitṛsahasrata:' iti purāṇavacanaṃ smāritam | na ca .... upadiśati iti | śāstrasya prayojanaparyavasānaṃ na syāditi bhāvaḥ | evaṃ śruti siddhanyāya virodha-prayojanaparyavasānābhāvābhyāṃ mokṣopayogitvaṃ nirastam |

'vyaktāvyaktajña vijñānāt ' iti vadatā'pi sāṅkhyena pradhānasya vyādheriva parijñānamātraṃ vivakṣitam ; na tu upāsanam iti bhāvaḥ || 7 || itaśca neti ! 'tattvamasi' iti na sadātmakatvānusandhānopadeśa: ; kintu 'sthūlo'ham

nā'bhyupagacchantīti |

tadātmakatvam, mokṣavirodhitvāt 'heyatvenaiva' upadeśyaṃ syāt |

na ca tat kriyate,

pratyuta 'upādeyatvenaiva',
'tattvamasi'
'tasya tāvadeva ciram'
ityupadiśyate || 8 ||

itaśca, na pradhānam -

pratijñāvirodhāt || 1-1-9||

pradhānakāraṇatve, pratijñāvirodhaśca bhavati | vākyopakrame hi eka vijñānena sarvavijñānaṃ pratijñātam | tacca kārya-kāraṇayoḥ ananyatvena kāraṇabhūtasadvijñānāt
ityādidehātmabhramānuvādaḥ | ataḥ sacchabdavācyā cetanasya mumukṣuṇā jñātavyatvaṃ hānārtham iti śaṅkayā anantarasūtrasaṅgatiḥ ityabhiprāyaḥ |

pratyuteti | sthūlo'ham' ityādyanusandhānasyeva sadātmakatvānusandhānasya na heyatvaṃ pratipādyate ; api tu upādeyatvamevocyate | tacca pradhānaparatve anupapannam ; pradhāna- prastāveṣu 'jahātyenām' iti, tasya heyatvenaiva pratipādanāt ityarthaḥ | prati na ānukūlyābhāvamātram kintu prātikūlyaṃ cā'sti ityāśayaḥ pratijñāvirodhāditi / upakramavirodhe sati, uttaravākyagatahetūnāṃ daurbalyāt,
pradhānameva pratipādyamiti śaṅkāṃ pariharati |

paraṃ

|| 8 ||

pradhāneti | tvanmatānuguṇatvena yaduktam, tadeva tvanmatapratikūlamiti sūtrakārahṛdayam iti bhāvaḥ | pratijñātamiti | pratijñāvākye sarvaśabdābhāve'pi ' kasminnu bhagavo vijñāte, sarvamidaṃ vijñātaṃ bhavati' ' ātmani khalvare dṛṣṭe śrute mate vijñāte sarvamidaṃ vijñātam '

'tatkāryabhūtacetanācetana' prapañcasya jñātatayaiva, upapādanīyam | tattu pradhānakāraṇatve cetana- vargasya pradhānakāryatvābhāvāt, pradhānavijñānena cetanavargavijñānāsiddheḥ, virudhyate || 9 ||

itaśca na pradhānam -

svāpyayāt ||1-1-10||
'tadeva' sacchabdavācyaṃ prakṛtya āha -
'svapnāntaṃ me somya vijānīhīti | yatraitatpuruṣaḥ svapiti nāma,
iti samānaprakaraṇavākyaikārthyāt, 'sarvāḥ prajāḥ

iti vakṣyamāṇasvārasyācca 'sarvavijñānaṃ tijñātam' ityuktam | sarvavijñānaṃ 'sarvaṃ mṛṇmaya' mitivat na kāryabhūta sarvācidvi- jñānameva ; sanmūlāḥ somyemāḥ sarvāḥ prajāḥ ' iti sarvaśabdasamānādhikaraṇaprajāśabdasvārasyā- vagatānāṃ cetanānāmapi sanmūlatayā jñātavyasarvakāryāntarbhāvāt iti bhāva: | kārya-kāraṇayoriti | na hi cidacitoḥ iti bhāvaḥ | tatkāryetyādi | mṛtpiṇḍa - tatkāryādidṛṣṭāntalāt 'sanmūlā : iti vākye prajāśabdasvārasyācca cetanasyā'pi kāryatayaiva jñātṛtvaṃ vaktavyamiti bhāvaḥ devadattajñānena grāmavijñānavat nātra sarvavijñānam aupacārikam ; aupacārikatve sarvavijñānasya lokadṛṣṭatvena ‘kathaṃ nu bhagavaḥ ?' iti praśnāyogāt, ananyabhūtamṛt-tatkāryādi- dṛṣṭāntamukhena mukhyatayaiva sarvavijñānopapādanadarśanācca iti bhāvaḥ || 9 ||
itaśceti | ekavijñānena sarvavijñānapratijñānaṃ kārya kāraṇabhāvanibandhanam | suṣuptau paramātmani cetanasya pralayaśca tannibandhanaḥ iti, kārya-kāraṇabhāvanibandhanatvasāmyāt saṅgataṃ suṣuptau layaṃ hetutvenā''ha ityabhiprāya: |

2.

tadeveti | jagatkāraṇavastuviṣaya eva, atrā'pi sacchabdaḥ ityabhiprāyaḥ | ataḥ sacchabdasya jīvopasthāpakasvaśabdasamānaviṣayatvanibandhanārthāntaratvaśaṅkāvyudāsaḥ | yatreti |

kāraṇe layavākyācca svātmano layakāraṇam ||
brahmaiveti pradhānaṃ tu sacchabdena na kathyate ||

satā somya tadā sampanno bhavati | svamapīto bhavati |
tasmādenaṃ svapitītyācakṣate, svaṃ hyapīto bhavati'
iti, suṣuptaṃ jīvaṃ satā sampannam ' svamanītaḥ - svasmin pralīnaḥ' iti vyapadiśati | pralayaśca svakāraṇe layaḥ | na ca acetanaṃ pradhānaṃ, cetanasya jīvasya kāraṇaṃ bhavitumarhati | [svamapīto bhavati - ātmānameva, jīvaḥ apīto bhavatītyarthaḥ | cidvastuśarīrakaṃ tadātma- bhūtaṃ brahmaiva "jīvaśabdena' abhidhīyate iti, nāma-rūpavyākaraṇaśrutyā uktam | tat jīva- śabda / bhidheyaṃ brahma suṣupti kāle'pi pralayakāla iva nāma rūpa pariṣvaṅgābhāvāt kevala- sacchandābhidheyamiti,
'satā somya tadā sampanno bhavati, svamapīto bhavati' ityucyate | tathā samānaprakaraṇe, nāma-rūpapariṣvaṅgābhāvena prājñenaiva pariṣvaṅgāt,
'prājñenātmanā saṃpariṣvakto na bāhyaṃ kiñcana veda, nāntaram' ityucyate | amokṣāt jīvasya nāma-rūpapariṣvaṅgādeva hi svavyatirikta viṣaya- jñānodayaḥ suṣupti' kāle hi nāma-rūpe vihāya satā saṃpariṣvaktaḥ, punarapi "jāgaradaśāyāṃ " nāma-rūpe pariṣvajya tattannāmarūpo bhavatīti śrutyantare spaṣṭamabhidhīyate—

atha – heyasambandha eva pāramārthikaḥ ", kalyāṇaika' svabhāvatā' tu mithyābhūtā ! hantaivaṃ tāpatrayābhihatacetanojjijīvayipayā pravṛttaṃ śāstram, 'tāpatrayābhihatireva " tasya" pāramārthikī, kalyāṇaikasvabhāvastu bhrāntiparikalpitaḥ' iti bodhayat, samyagujjīvayati !
atha – etaddoṣaparijihīrṣayā brahmaṇaḥ nirviśeṣacinmātrasvarūpātiriktajīvatva- duḥkhitvādikaṃ, satyasaṅkalpatva-kalyāṇa guṇākaratva- jagatkāraṇatvādyapi mithyābhūtamiti kalpanīyam - iti cet,

aho bhavatāṃ vākyāparyālocana kuśalatā |

eka vijñānena sarvavijñānapratijñānam', sarvasya mithyātve sarvasya jñātavyasya abhāvāt na 'setsyati' | yathā eka vijñānaṃ paramārthaviṣayam, tathaiva sarvavijñānamapi yadi paramārthaviṣayaṃ tadantargataṃ ca tadā tajjñānena sarvavijñānamiti śakyate vaktum | nahiṃ paramārtha śuktikā jñānena tadāśrayam 'aparamārtharajataṃ jñātaṃ bhavati ||

'sanmūlā : somyemāḥ sarvāḥ prajāḥ sadāyatanāḥ satpratiṣṭhā:'
'aitadātmyamidaṃ sarvam'

ityādinā, asya jagataḥ sadātmakatā, sṛṣṭeḥ pūrvakāle nāmarūpa 'vibhāgaprahāṇam', jagadutpattau sacchabdavācyasya brahmaṇaḥ svavyatiriktanimittāntarānapekṣatvam, 'sṛṣṭikāle ahameva anantasthiratrasarūpeṇa bahu syām' iti ananyasādhāraṇasaṅkalpa viśeṣaḥ, yathā saṅkalpam anantavicitrataccānāṃ vilakṣaṇakrama viśeṣaviśiṣṭā sṛṣṭiḥ, samasteṣu acetaneṣu vastuṣu svātmakajīvānupraveśenaiva ananta ' nāmarūpa vyākaraṇam, 'svavyatiriktasya samastasya ' svamūlatvam, svāyatanatvam, svapravartyatvam, svenaiva jīvanam "svapratiṣṭhatvam' ityādyana- ntaviśeṣāḥ śāstraikasamadhigamyāḥ pratipāditāḥ |
tatsambandhitayā prakaraṇāntareṣvapi apahatapāpmatyādi nirastanikhiladoṣatā sarvajñatā- sarveśvaratva-patyakāmatva-satyasaṅkalpatya-sarvānandakaraṇaniratiśayā' nandayogādayaḥ ' sakaletara- pramāṇāviṣayāḥ sahasraśaḥ pratipāditāḥ |
evam ananyagocarānantaviśeṣa viśiṣṭa prakṛtatrahmaparāmarśitacchabdasya, nirviśeṣavastu- mātropadeśaparatvam, asaṅgatatvena unmatta 'pralapitāyeta |
svapravartyatvamiti | sadāyatanatvaphalitaṃ svapravatyatvaṃ, svenaiva jīvanaṃ ca | praśāsanena hi dhāraṇam, dhṛtyā ca tajjīvanam | śāstraiketi niṣedhāya guṇānuvādaḥ iti śaṅkāyā: vyudā- sāya, na hi vidheḥ anuvāditvam iti bhāvaḥ |
tatsambandhitayeti, śrutyantarāparyālocanāṃ pradarśayati | apahatapāpmatvādirūpā nirasta - nikhiladoṣatā ityarthaḥ | sakaletarapramāṇāviṣayāḥ iti, apūrvatā lakṣaṇaṃ guṇeṣu tātparyaliṅga darśayitumuktam (śru pra ) |

prakṛtetyanena prākaraṇikavākyavirodhaḥ vivakṣitaḥ | anena yatra kkā'pi prakṛtākāra - viśiṣṭaparāmarśitayā tacchabdavyutpattivirodhaḥ uktaḥ |

'tyaṃ' padaṃ ca saṃsāritvaviśiṣṭajīvavāci | tasyā'pi nirviśeṣasvarūpopasthāpana- paratve, svārthaḥ parityaktaḥ syāt | nirviśeṣaprakāśasvarūpasya ca vastuno hi ; avidyayā tirodhānaṃ svarūpanāśaprasaṅgādibhiḥ na sambhavatīti, pūrvamevoktam | evaṃ ca sati, samānādhikaraṇa 'vṛttayoḥ ' 'tat tvam' iti dvayorapi padayoḥ, mukhyārthaparityāgena, lakṣaṇā ca samāśrayaṇīyā ||

athocyeta - samānādhikaraṇa' vṛttānām viśeṣaṇāṃśe tātparyāsambhavādeva viśeṣaṇanivṛtteḥ, vastumātraikatvapratipādanāt na lakṣaṇāprasaṅgaḥ | yathā 'nīlam utpalam' iti padadvayasya viśeṣyaikatvapratipādana 'paratvena', 'nīlatvotpalatva- rūpa' viśeṣaṇadvayaṃ na vivakṣyate | tadvivakṣāyāṃ hi nīlatvaviśiṣṭākāreṇa utpalatvaviśi- ṭākārasya ekatvapratipādanaṃ 'prasajyeta | tattu na sambhavati | na hi ; nailyaviśiṣṭā- kāreṇa tadvastu utpalapadena viśeṣyate; jāti- guṇayoḥ anyonyasamavāyaprasaṅgāt | ataḥ nīlatvotpalasopalakṣita' vastvekatvamātraṃ', sāmānādhikaraṇyena pratipādyate | tathā 'so'yaṃ devadattaḥ' iti "atītakāla - viprakṛṣṭadeśaviśiṣṭasya tenaiva rūpeṇa saṃnihitadeśa-
tvaṃpadaṃ ceti | svābhimukhaścetanaḥ 'tvaṃ' padārthaḥ | ābhimukhyaṃ ca svavākya bodhyatvam | bodhyatvaṃ ca ajñasya saṃsāriṇaḥ svataḥ prāptam | tasmāt bodhanārhatopayogyā kāraviśiṣṭaḥ tvamarthaḥ | ataḥ sa parityakto bhavati iti bhāvaḥ | evaṃ ca satīti | nirviśeṣasvarūpopasthā panaparatve mukhyārthasambandhitayā vastumātrāvabodhane abhyupagate lakṣaṇāsvīkāraḥ ityarthaḥ ||

ekārthapratipādanaparatayā

anyathā viśeṣaṇabahutvāt ekārthatvāsiddheḥ iti bhāvaḥ | prathamasūtre hi bahuśaḥ uktaḥ | na lakṣaṇeti | vācyā- tatviti, prasaktasya aniṣṭatāṃ darśayati | na hi ityādi | yugapat abhidhāne, uddeśyopādeya vibhāgahāniprasaṅgāt padadvaya prakṛtyupasthāpitaviśiṣṭakye samāna- vibhaktyā pratipādyamāne, viśeṣyāṃśasyeva viśeṣaṇabhūnajātiguṇāṃśayorapi aikyaprasaṅgāñca krameṇās- bhidhānapakṣaḥ abhyupagantavyaḥ - iti hṛdi nihito'bhiprāya: | jāti · guṇayoriti | nīlaguṇaviśiṣṭa utpalatvānvaye, viśeṣya iva viśeṣaṇe'pi utpalatvaṃ syāt, evaṃ viparivata ityarthaḥ | nīlatvopalakṣiteti | nīlatvādeḥ bodhaḥ tyajyate iti bhāva: |

tātparyāsambhavādeveti | evakārasya pūrvamapyuktā devetyarthaḥ |
bahirbhūtapratipādanāt iti bhāvaḥ |

* padayoḥ dvayorapi -tha, dha lakṣaṇā - na • parave - tha, dha 7 nīlasvotpaladhvasvarūpa - i, vā

1 pravṛttayoḥ – i,
• viśeṣyaikya-vā, • prasate - i, vā

pravṛttānām – i, nīlotpalasvarūpa - tha

vartamānakāla - viśiṣṭatā pratipādanānupapatteḥ ubhayadeśa - kālopalakṣitasvarūpamātraikyaṃ, sāmānādhikaraṇyena pratipādyate |
yadyapi, 'nīlam' ityādyekapadaśravaṇe pratīyamānaṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ sāmānādhikaraṇyavelāyāṃ virodhāt na pratipādyate ; tathāpi, vācye'rthe pradhānāṃśasya pratipādanāt na lakṣaṇā ; api tu viśeṣaṇāṃśasya avivakṣāmātram | sarvatra sāmānādhikaraṇyasya 'eṣa eva svabhāva iti, na kaściddoṣaḥ - iti ||

tadidamasāram ; sarveṣveva vākyeṣu padānāṃ vyutpatti siddhārtha saṃsargaviśeṣamātraṃ pratyāyyam | tatra samānādhikaraṇa vṛttānāmapi nīlādipadānāṃ nailyādiviśiṣṭa evārthaḥ vyutpatti- siddha:, padāntarārthasaṃsṛṣṭaḥ abhidhīyate | yathā 'nīlam utpalam ānaya' ityukta nīlimā diviśiṣṭameva ānīyate; yathā ca 'vindhyāṭavyāṃ madamudito mātaṅgagaṇaḥ tiṣṭhati'

iti, 'padadvayāgata viśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭa evārthaḥ pratīyate, evaṃ vedāntavākyeṣvāpa 'samānādhi-

karaṇa' nirdeśeṣu tattadviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭameva brahma pratipattavyam ||

na ca viśeṣaṇavivakṣāyām itara viśiṣṭākāraṃ vastu anyena viśeṣṭavyam ; api tu 'sarvaiḥ viśeṣaṇaiḥ kharūpameva viśeṣyam | tathā hi-
'bhinnapravṛttinimittānāṃ śabdānām ekasminnarthe vṛttiḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyam' iti | anvayena, nivṛtyā vā pādāntarapratipādyādākārādākārāntarayuktatayā tasyaiva vastunaḥ padāntara' pratipādyatvaṃ sāmānādhikaraṇyakāryam | yathā 'devadattaḥ śyāmo yuvā lohitākṣaḥ |

adīnaḥ akṛpaṇaḥ anavadyaḥ' iti | yatra tu ekasmin vastuni samanvayāyogyaṃ viśeṣaṇadvayaṃ samānādhikara gapadanirdiṣṭam, tatrā'pi, anyatarat padam amukhyavṛttamāśrīyate ; na dvayam | yathā 'gauḥ 'vāhīkaḥ" iti | nīlotpala diṣu tu viśeṣaṇadvayānvayā virodhāt ekameva ubhayaviśiṣṭaṃ pratipādyate ||

syādetadevam ; yadi viśeṣaṇadvayaprati sambandhitvamātrameva, aikyaṃ nirundhyāt | na caitadasti; ekasmin dharmiṇi upasaṃhartumayogyadharmadvayaviśiṣṭatvameva hi ! ekatvaṃ. niruṇaddhi | ayogyatā ca pramāṇāntarasiddhā 'ghaṭatva- paṭatvayoḥ ' | 'nīlamutpalam' ityādiṣu tu daṇḍitva - kuṇḍalitvavat rūpavatva- rasavatva - gandhavattvādivacca, virodhaḥ nopalabhyate | na kevalam avirodha eva pravṛttinimittabhedena "ekārthaniṣṭatvarūpaṃ sāmānādhikaraṇyam upapādayatyeva dharmadvaya viśiṣṭatām' | anyathā svarūpamātraikye, anekapadapravṛttau nimittābhāvāt sāmānādhikaraṇyameva na syāt |

viśeṣaṇānāṃ svasambandhānādareṇa vastusvarūpopa' lakṣaṇaparatve' sati, ekenaiva vastu upalakṣitamiti, upalakṣaṇāntaram anarthakameva | upalakṣaṇāntaropalakṣyākāra bhedābhyupagame, " tenāsskāreṇa' saviśeṣatvaprasaṅgaḥ ||
pramāṇāntareti | na punaḥ viśeṣaṇatvaprayuktam ayogyatvam iti bhāvaḥ | nīlamutpala- miti | na kevalaṃ bhinnāśrayatvamātraṃ virodhaḥ ; api tu tanniyamaḥ | na ca nīlatvotpalatvayo tādṛśavirodhaḥ; kvacit sahapratītatvāt iti bhāvaḥ | daṇḍitveti | daṇḍādikaṃ yutasiddham rūpādikam ayutasiddham | yunasiddhatvāyuta- siddhatvā divibhāgavat, viśeṣaṇatvamapi virodhāprayojakam iti bhāvaḥ | na kevalamiti | aparyāyayoḥ padayoḥ ekārthavṛttau nimittasāpekṣatvāt tena vinā tatra vṛtyayogācca, atyantopa- yogāt, viśeṣaṇasya vivakṣitatvam ityarthaḥ | anyathā - viśeṣaṇatyāge |

anādaraḥ - tātparyābhāva: | yathā upalakṣaṇabhūtāyāṃ śākhāya na tātparyam ; api tu candra eva tātparyam, tadvat ityarthaḥ | upalakṣaṇāntareti | asmābhistu janmādibhiḥ upalakṣaṇaiḥ upalakṣyāḥ tadanuguṇajñānaśaktayādyā kārabhedāḥ svīkṛtāḥ iti, na nairarthakyam iti bhāvaḥ | tenākāreṇeti | antato gatvā'pi avarjanīyaṃ saviśeṣatvam | evaṃ ca sati upalakṣyākārāntarābhyupagamena tasya upalakṣaṇatvāṅgīkārādapi upalakṣaṇatayā abhimatānāmeva viśeṣaṇatvāṅgīkāro yuktaḥ iti bhāvaḥ |

'so'yaṃ devadattaḥ' ityatrāspi, 'lakṣaṇāgandho na vidyate ; virodhābhāvāt | deśāntarasambandhitayā atītasya saṃnihitadeśasambandhitayā vartamānatvāvirodhāt | ata " eva hi !' 'so'yam' iti pratyabhijñayā", kāladvayasambandhino vastunaḥ 'aikyamupapādyate ' vastunaḥ sthiratvavādibhiḥ | anyathā pratītivirodhe sati sarveṣāṃ kṣaṇikatvameva syāt | deśadvayasambandhavirodhastu kālabhedena parihriyate ||

yataḥ samānādhikaraṇapadānām anekaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭaikārthavācitvam, ata eva, 'aruṇayaikahāyanyā piṅgākṣyā 'somaṃ krīṇati'

'iti'āruṇyādi viśiṣṭeka' hāyanyā, ' ' krayaḥ sādhyatayā vidhīyate | taduktam-
'arthaikatve dravya - guṇayoraikakarmyānniyamaḥ syāt iti ||

virodhābhāvāt — mukhyārthabhūtaviśeṣaṇadvayasambandhe virodhābhāvāt | deśāntareti | vastuviśeṣaṇatayā nirddiṣṭāvapi deśāntarasambandha sannihitadeśasambandhau atītavartamānakālopādhitayā vivakṣitau | idaṃ ca kālopādhyantarāṇāmapi upalakṣaṇam | atīta- vartamānaśabdau atīta vartamāna- kālasambandhiparau | deśāntarasambandhitayā'tītasya - deśāntarasambandhādyupādhyavacchinnātītakāla sambandhinaḥ | saṃnihita ....rodhāditi | saṃnihita deśa sambandhādyupādhimadvartamānakālasambandhā- virodhāt ityarthaḥ | atītatvaṃ ca anyāpekṣayā vivakṣitam ; na tu ubhayakālānugata vastvapekṣayā ata eva - kāladvayasambandhāvirodhādeva | upapādyate iti | 'saugatān prati' iti śeṣaḥ | saugatamatanirāsakasya kāladvayasambandhavirodhābhyupagamaḥ viruddha ityarthaḥ | anyathā - kāladvaya- sambandhavirodhena | pratītivirodhe- vastusthiratva viṣayapratīti- virodhe | sarveṣāmiti | ekasya deśadvaya kāladrayānvayavirodhe, deśakālāntarasādhyaphalasādhanavidhānam ātmanityatvopadeśaśca apramāṇaṃ syāt iti abhiprāyaḥ | deśadvayeti | paricchinnasya vastunaḥ svānavacchinna deśa sambandhaḥ yaugapadya eva virudhyate, na tu kramabhāvitve ityarthaḥ |
yataḥ . . . ata eva iti, laukika prayogasiddhavatkāroktya pūrvoktasvaparapakṣasādhanadūṣaṇāni aruṇādhikaraṇavivakṣitānīti bhāvaḥ āviṣkriyate |

evaṃ pūrvapakṣī manyate yadyapi 'aruṇayā' iti padam, 'ākṛteriva ' guṇasyā'pi dravya prakāraka svabhāvatvāt dravyaparyantameva aruṇimānam abhidadhāti ; tathāpi ekahāyanyanvaya niyamaḥ, aruṇimno na sambhavati ; 'ekahāyanyā krīṇāti, taca aruṇayā ' iti arthadvayavidhānāsambhavāt | tataśca 'aruṇayā' iti vākyaṃ bhitvā, prakaraṇavihitasarva- dravyaparyantameva aruṇimānam, aviśeṣeṇa abhidadhāti | 'aruṇayā' iti strīliṅganirdeśaḥ prakaraṇavihita' sarvaliṅgaka' dravyāṇāṃ pradarśanārthaḥ | tasmāt ekahāyanyanvayaniyamaḥ,

tatra,

aruṇimno na syāt - iti |

atrā'bhidhīyate

'arthaikatve dravyaguṇayoraikakarmyānniyamaḥ syāt' | 'aruṇayā ekahāyanyā' iti, āruṇyaviśiṣṭadravyaikahāyanīdravyavācipadayoḥ, sāmānādhi- karaṇyena arthaikatve siddhe sati ekahāyanīdravyāruṇyaguṇayoḥ 'aruṇayā' iti
tatra evamiti | asmaduktāveva pūrvapakṣasiddhāntau sūtrakārā (jaiminiḥ ) bhipretau iti bhāvaḥ | yadyapīti | ākṛteriva guṇasyā'pi dravyaprakāra taikasvabhāvatvāt, tadvāciśabdaḥ yadyapi dravyamātraparyantaḥ ; tathā'pi ekahāyanītvāśrayadravyaviśeṣaparyantatvaṃ na siddhyatītyarthaḥ | arthadvayeti | 'ekahāyanyā krīṇāti' iti vākyeta, ekahāyanyāḥ krayasya ca sambandhaḥ, pratipannaḥ | 'tacca krayaṇam aruṇayā kartavyam' iti vākyeta, ekahāyanī sādhyatva viśiṣṭarūpeṇa krayaṇam aruṇadravyasādhyam iti pratipannam | evaṃ ca ' ekahāyanyā krayaḥ sādhya:, tathā ca aruṇayā bhavitavyam' ityuktaṃ bhavati | tataśca ekahāyanyāḥ kayasambandhaḥ aruṇimasambandhaśceti arthadvayasya ekavākyena vidhānāsambhavāt ityarthaḥ | prakaraṇam - avāntaraprakaraṇam, mahāprakaraṇaṃ vā | sarvadravyāṇi - vāso yajamānādīni | aviśeṣeṇa prakaraṇānvayāviśeṣeṇa |
atrābhidhīyata iti | asmin pūrvapakṣe prāpte, sūtrakāraḥ svasiddhāntamāha ityarthaḥ | aruṇayā ityādinā, sūtramya akṣarārthamāha | āruṇyeti | kayoḥ arthaikatve ? ityākāṅkṣāyāṃ, śabdasya arthapratisambandhitvāt, padaviśeṣayoḥ saṃnihitaviṣayavākyasthatvācca āruṇya..... padayoḥ iti adhyāhṛtam | siddhe iti | āruṇyaviśiṣṭam, ekahāyanītvaviśiṣṭaṃ ca dravyam | 2 prakāratayaika-dha, 3 sarvaliṅga –tha, dha,

'padenaivāviśeṣaṇa 'viśeṣyabhāvena sambandhitayā abhihitayoḥ, krayākhyaika' karmānviyā- virodhāt ', aruṇimnaḥ krayasādhanabhūtaika' hāyanyanvaya' niyamaḥ syāt |

.

yadi 'ekahāyanyāḥ * krayasambandhavat, aruṇimasambandho'pi vākyāvaseyaḥ " syāt, tadā vākyasya arthadvaya vidhānaṃ syāt | na caitadasti ; ' ' aruṇayā' iti padenaiva, ' aruṇimaviśiṣṭadravyam abhihitam | ekahāyanīpada sāmānādhikaraṇyena tasya ekahāyanītvamātram vagamyate ; na guṇasambandhaḥ | viśiṣṭadravyaikyameva hi ! sāmānādhikaraṇyasyā'rthaḥ |

'bhinnapravṛttinimittānāṃ śabdānām "ekasminnarthe' "vṛttiḥ' sāmānādhikaraṇyam'

iti hi ! " sāmānādhikaraṇyalakṣaṇam" |

tadvācipadayoḥ. sāmānādhikaraṇyena tattadviśeṣaṇadvārā ekaviśeṣyavṛttitvāt arthaikatve siddhe ityarthaḥ | avirodhāditi | vākyabhedaprasaṅgarūpavirodhābhāvāt ityarthaḥ | aruṇimnaḥ ityādi | sarvadravya sādhāraṇaprakaraṇānapekṣatvāt vākyasthatayaiva niyamasiddhiḥ ityarthaḥ |

atra guṇadravyayoḥ viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvaḥ prātipadikāvaseyaḥ, viśeṣyaikyaṃ sāmānādhi- karaṇyāvaseyam, krayānvaya: vākyāvaseya iti vākyasya krayānvayabodhanameva kṛtyam | ekasmin vākye arthadvayavidhānaprasaṅgābhāvān vākyabhedābhāveta, āruṇyaṃ, na prākaraṇikasarva- dravyānvayi ; api tu ekahāyanī mātrānvayī iti mūtrārtha : | tasya- aruṇima- viśiṣṭadravyasya | ekahāyanītvamātramiti | ekaha | ya nītvāśrayatvam viśeṣyaikyam ityarthaḥ | anena ākṛtyadhikaraṇasya aruṇādhikaraṇasya ca pratipādyabhedaśca ukto bhavati | prātipadikasya dharmigryantatvam ākṛtyadhikaraṇārthaḥ | aneka- prātipadikasthasamānavibhakteḥ viśeṣyaikyaparatvapratipādakam aruṇādhikaraṇam iti | viśiṣṭadravyaikya- miti | viśiṣṭaikyam ityukte viśeṣaṇāṃśasyaikyaṃ mā bhūt iti, tathoktam | dravyaśabdena viśeṣyāṃśo vivakṣitaḥ | bhinnati, sāmānādhikaraṇyalakṣaṇagranthamāha | na hi viśeṣaṇa- viśeṣyavācinoḥ padayoḥ, viśeṣaṇa- viśeṣyabhāvasambandhe vṛttiḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyam — ityuktam ityarthaḥ |

tasmāt

ata 'eva hi' 'raktaḥ paṭo bhavati' ityādiṣu, aikārthyāt ekavākyatvam | 'paṭasya bhavanakriyāsabandhe hi vākyavyāpāraḥ | rāga sambandhastu, 'rakta' padenaiva abhihitaḥ | 'rāgamambandhi dravyaṃ paṭaḥ' ityetāvanmātraṃ ' sāmāna dhikaraṇyā va seyam |'

evam – ekena guṇena dvābhyāṃ bahubhirvā, tena tena padena samastena vyastena vā, "viśiṣṭam ' upasthāpya, sāmānādhikaraṇyena, 'sarvaviśeṣaṇa' viśiṣṭo'rthaḥ ' ' ekaḥ" iti ' jñāpayitvā tasya kriyāsambadhābhidhānam, aviruddham, devadattaḥ śyāmo yuvā lohitākṣaḥ daṇḍī kuṇḍalī tiṣṭhati' 'śuklena vāsasā yavanikāṃ sampādayet' "nīlam utpalam ānaya' 'nīlotpalamānaya'" ' gāmānaya śuklāṃ śobhanākṣīm'
'agnaye pathikṛte puroḍāśamaṣṭākapāle nirvapet

'iti | evam

'aruṇayaikahāyanyā piṅgādhyā somaṃ krīṇāti'

iti |

ata eva – eva prātipadikena guṇānvayaḥ samānavibhaktyā parasparānvayaḥ, vākyeta krayānvayaḥ iti kṛtyatrayasyāpi yathoktaprakāratvādeva | rakta iti | tatra hi 'paṭo bhavati' ityetāvatā nirākāṅgatve'pi vākyasya, prayuktaṃ 'rakta padaṃ kīdṛśaḥ paṭaḥ ? ityutthāpitā kā ṅkṣatvāt, ekavākyatayā anvitam iti bhāvaḥ | vyastena veti | kārakavibhaktyantena prathamāntena vā iti ca atrā'bhipretam | devadatta iti prathamāntabahuviśeṣaṇānvaye udāharaṇam !

ayaṃ bhāvaḥ 'raktaḥ paṭaḥ' iti padadvayam acidvācakam ; 'aruṇayā' iti padaṃ cetanavācakama iti vaiṣamyaṃ, yathā na prayojakam ; tathā prathamāntatva kārakavibhaktyantatva vaiṣamyamapi prayojakam iti | daṇḍī kuṇḍalīti antaraṅgabahiraṅgaviśeṣaṇe'pi evam ityabhiprāyeṇoktam | śuklena vāsasā iti, kārakavibhaktyantaikapadānvayodāharaṇam | nīlamutpalamiti, karmakārakamudāharati | nīlotpalamiti, samastodāharaṇam | gām iti kārakavibhaktyantāne kapadānvayodāharaṇam | agnaye iti, saṃpradānakāraka viṣayaṃ vaidikaṃ ca udāharaṇam | puroḍaśam aṣṭākapālam iti, karmakārakamapi siddham | evam aruṇayeti | yathā – ekavacanādīnām ekatvādisaṅkhyānvaya-

etaduktaṃ bhavati - yathā, 'kāṣṭhaiḥ sthāsyām odanaṃ pacet" iti, anekakāra- kaviśiṣṭā ekā kriya, yugapat pratīyate tathā, samānādhikaraṇapadasaṅghātābhihitam ekaikaṃ kārakam, tattatkārakapratipattivelāyāmeva anekaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭaṃ yugapatpratipannaṃ . kriyāyām anvetīti na kaścit virodhaḥ, 'khādiraiḥ śuṣkaiḥ kāṣṭhaiḥ samaparimāṇe " bhāṇḍe pāyasaṃ śālyodanaṃ samarthaḥ pācakaḥ pacet" ityādiṣu - iti ||

tat, nopapadyate ; lokavedayoḥ dravyavācipadasamānādhikaraṇasya guṇavācinaḥ, kvacidapi kevalaguṇābhidhānādarśanāt |

upāttadravyakavākyasthaṃ guṇapadaṃ kevalaguṇābhidhāyi 'ityapi', asaṅgatam ; śuklaḥ" ityādiṣu, upātta' dravyake'pi guṇaviśiṣṭasyaiva abhidhānāt | 'paṭasya śuklaḥ' ityatra śauklyaviśiṣṭa paṭa | pratipattiḥ ', ' asamāna' vibhakti nirdeśakṛtā ; na punaḥ upāttadravyakatvakṛtā | tatraiva 'paṭasya śuklo bhāgaḥ' ityādiṣu samānavibhakti nirdeśe "śauklya' viśiṣṭadravyaṃ pratīyate |

"paṭa:

.6

yatpunaḥ – krayasya 'ekahāyanyavaruddhatayā', aruṇimnaḥ krayānvayaḥ " na sambhavati
tadapi, virodhiguṇarahitadravyavācipadasamānādhikaraṇa' guṇapadasya' tadāśraya 10 guṇābhidhānena kriyāpadānvayāvirodhāt asaṅgatam |
tat nopapadyata iti | 'śvetamālabheta' ityādiṣu guṇavācipadānāṃ guṇiparyantatvaṃ mīmāṃsakaiḥ ākṛtyadhikaraṇanyāyāt abhyupetam | guṇamātraparyantatve prayojakam upāttadravyakavākyasthatvam | tadapavādakāt sāmānādhikaraṇyāt, guṇiparyantābhidhānaṃ ca abhyupetam |
tatra guṇamātraparyantatve prayojakam uktam abhyupagamyate | prakṛte guṇamātraparyantatvam | lokavedayoriti | guṇiparyantatvāpavādakam upāttadravyakavākyasthaguṇavācipadatvaṃ yat, tadapavāda- rūpasya sāmānādhikaraṇyasya 'aruṇayā' ityādivākye vidyamānatvopapādanāt, kevalaguṇābhidhā- yitvam ayuktam ityarthaḥ |

iti,

asamānavibhaktinirdeśaḥ – asamānāyāḥ vibhakteḥ nirdeśaḥ | | na samānavibhakti- nirdeśābhāvaḥ ; 'śveto yāti' ityādiṣvapi guṇamātraparyantatvaprasaṅgāt |

abhyupagacchato'pi samānādhikaraṇapadānāṃ yathāvasthita viśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭaikya pratipādana- "paratvāśrayaṇe', 'tvaṃ' padapratipannasakaladoṣabhāgitvaṃ "parasya" prasajyeta - iti cet, naitadevam ; 'tvaṃ 'padenāspi jīvāntaryāmigaḥ parasyaiva abhidhānāt ||

etaduktaṃ bhavati - sacchabdā 'bhihitaṃ nirasta 'nikhiladoṣa gandhaṃ satyasaṅkalpatva miśrā " nava dhikātiśayāsaṅkhye kalyāṇaguṇagaṇaṃ" samasta kāraṇabhūtaṃ paraṃ brahma,' 'bahu syām' iti saṅkalpya, tejobannapramukhaṃ kṛtsnaṃ jagat sṛṣṭvā, tasmin devādivicitra' saṃsthāna' saṃsthite jagati cetanaṃ jīvavarga svakarmānuguṇeṣu śarīreṣu ātmatayā praveśya, svayaṃ ca 'svecchayaiva' jīvānta rātmatayā 'anupraviśya', evambhūteṣu svaparyanteṣu devādyākāreṣu saṅghāteṣu nāmarūpe vyākarot | evaṃrūpasaṅghātasyaiva vastutvaṃ śabdavācyatvaṃ ca akarot ityarthaḥ |
etaduktamiti | tvaṃśabdasya jīvadvārā paramātmābhidhāyitve tacchabdasya vā paramātma- dvārā jīvavācitvaṃ kiṃ na syāt, iti śaṅkānirāsārthaścāyamārambha: | sacchabdābhihita- miti | kāraṇavastuvācisacchabdaḥ kāraṇatvānuguṇānvitavastuparaḥ iti bhāva: | bahu syām iti grāhakagrahaṇena tejaḥ prabhṛtisarvanāmarūpeṣu sāmānyena paramātmīyatayā avagateṣu tatra anupraveśa- pūrva kanāmarūpavyākaraṇaśrutyā dibhiḥ sadvārakaṃ ( nāma rūpabhāktvam) adhyavasīyate ityanusandheyam | upabṛṃhaṇeṣu aviśeṣokteṣu bhagavadguṇeṣu, snigdhavarṇatvādīnāṃ satyasandhatvādīnāṃ ca guṇānāṃ divya- vigrahadvārakatvam advārakatvaṃca dṛśyate ; tathā ihā'pi īśvaraśarīrabhūtacidacihnavārakaḥ advārakaśca guṇānvayaḥ yathāśruti svīkārya : iti bhāva: | bahu syām iti | bahunāmarūpa- bhāk bhaveyamityarthaḥ | pramukhaśabdena tattvāntarasṛṣṭiḥ vivakṣitā | devādītī, vakṣyamāṇa- cidacidaśavibhāgārthaṃ jagadākārakathanam ; na tu jīvānupraveśapūrvāvasthākathanam | cetanamiti | cetanatvaṃ śarīritve prayojakamityarthaḥ | jīvavargaśabdena, 'vijñānam' 'jīvena' iti ca eka- vacanam jātau ; ‘cetanānām' ityādibhiḥ aikārthyāt iti vyākhyātaṃ bhavati | svakarmānu- guṇeṣu iti, 'anena jīvena' ityatra, 'anena' iti nirdeśasya abhiprāyavivaraṇam jīvaviśiṣṭa- paramātmaviśeṣaṇasya ‘idaṃ'śabdasya jīvadvārakavaicitryaparatvāt jīvavaicitryasya ca karmavaicitryā- dhīnatvāt | svecchayaiveti | pāratantryaṃ svātantryaṃ ca anupraveśa hetuvaiṣamyam ityarthaḥ | evambhūteṣu dvāra-dvāribhāvena vyavasthiteṣu cidacidātmakeṣu | nāmarūpe vyākaroditi | tattaddevatādyavasthāḥ tattadvāciśabdāṃśca akarot ityarthaḥ | evaṃrūpasaṅghātasyeti | cidacidīśvarā- tmakasya saṅghātasyetyarthaḥ |

gandhasatyasaṅkalpatva -tha, gandhaṃ satyasaṅkalpa — tha. dha kalyāṇaguṇam-tha, niratiśaya kalyāṇaguṇagaṇam-a, navadhikātiśayakalyaṇaguṇa-dha navadhikā tiśayā saṃkhyeyānantakalyāṇaguṇagaṇaṃ-va

nirdeśena annamayādīnāṃ śarīratvasya arthasiddhatvācca | evaṃ 'yaḥ pūrvasya' iti uttaratrā'nuvādasya purovāda nirvāhaḥ kṛtaḥ |
śrutyantarasiddhaḥ, paramakāraṇabhūtaḥ iti padadvayam, 'yaḥ' iti prasiddhavannirdeśaḥ śrutyantara- siddhim etatkaraṇasiddhiṃ ca jñāpayatītyabhiprāyeṇa |

evaṃ ca ' svavyatiriktaṃ cetanācetana vastujātaṃ svaśarīramiti, sa eva nirupādhikaḥ śārīra ātmā | ata eva 'idaṃ paraṃ brahma adhikṛtya pravṛttaṃ śāstraṃ śārīrakam' iti abhi- yuktairabhidhīyate | ataḥ vijñānamayāt jīvāt anya eva paramātmā ānandamayaḥ | ||13||

siddhānte phalaphalibhāvaḥ - yadā pāpmaśabdaḥ karmamātraparaḥ, apahṛtaśabdaḥ atyantā- bhāvaparaḥ, janmaniṣedhaśca karmamūlajanmaviṣayaḥ, vigrahaśca prākṛtavilakṣaṇaḥ ; tadānīṃ vigrahasya aicchatayā, tasya jīvāt parasmin sambhavācca, akṣyādityāntarvartī puruṣaḥ paraḥ iti, tadaikārthyāt ānandavallīpratipādyasya paramātmatvam avicālyamiti tadaikakaṇṭhayāt, sarvāṇi kāraṇavākyāni paramātmaparāṇi iti |

na hīti | śāstrakapramāṇake vastuni lokadṛṣṭavyāptimūlastarkaḥ na karmavaśyatvasādhakaḥ ityabhiprāyaḥ | satyasaṅkalpasyeti, anyathāsiddhimāha | |
triguṇātmakaśabdaḥ heyatvasūcakaḥ | 'satvaṃ laghu prakāśaka miṣṭamupaṣṭambhakaṃ calaṃ ca rajaḥ | guru varaṇakameva tamaḥ' ityukteḥ | svasvarūpānabhijñasyeti, karmācaraṇahetuḥ ||
etaduktamityārambhaḥ, svatassarvajñasya sarvaśakteḥ jñānārthaṃ pravṛtyarthaṃ ca karaṇānapekṣatvāt, na dehāpekṣā | rūpādimattve ca prākṛtatvaṃ syāt | nityatve ca anityabahvavatārāsambhavaḥ | tiryagādirūpaparigrahe 'vācikaiḥ pakṣimṛgatām' ityādiprakāreṇa tanmūlakarmasambandhaḥ syāt, iti śaṅkāparihārārtham | parasyaiveti | yathā— parasya brahmaṇaḥ dharmisvarūpaṃ, svābhāvikā:

tadvadeva 'svābhimatānurūpaika' rūpā cintya divyādbhutanityaniravadyaniratiśayaujjvalyasaundarya- saugandhyasaukumārya lāvaṇya yauvanā' dyanantaguṇagaṇanidhi' divyarūpamapi svābhāvikam asti |
tadeva upāsakānugraheṇa tattatpratipanyanurūpasaṃsthānaṃ karoti', apārakāruṇyasauśīlya- vātsalyau' dāryaiśvarya jalanidhiḥ nirastanikhila heyagandhaḥ apahatapāpmā paramātmā paraṃ brahma puruṣottamo nārāyaṇaḥ - iti ||

'yato vā imāni bhūtāni jāyante' 'sadeva somyedamagra āsīt '
'ātmā vā idameka evā'gra āsīt ' 'eko ha vai nārāyaṇa āsīnna brahmā neśānaḥ' ityādiṣu, nikhilajagadekakāraṇatayā avagatasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ, 'satyaṃ jñānamanantaṃ brahma'

'vijñānamānandaṃ brahma' ityādiṣu evambhūtaṃ svarūpamiti avagamyate | 'nirguṇaḥ'
'nirañjanam'

" apahatapāpmā vijaro vimṛtyurviśoko vijighatso'pipāsaḥ ' satyakāmaḥ satyasaṅkalpaḥ'

'na tasya kārya karaṇaṃ ca vidyate na tatsamaścābhyadhikaśca dṛśyate | parā'sya śaktirvividhaiva śrūyate svābhāvikī jñānabalakriyā ca ||' 'tamīśvarāṇāṃ paramaṃ maheśvaraṃ taṃ devatānāṃ paramaṃ ca daivatam'
'sa kāraṇaṃ karaṇādhipādhipo na cāsya kaśvijanitā na cā'dhipaḥ' 'sarvāṇi rūpāṇi vicitya dhīro nāmāni kṛtvā'bhivadan yadāste

'sarve nimeṣā jajñire vidyutaḥ puruṣādadhi'
ityādiṣu parasya brahmaṇaḥ prākṛta heyaguṇān prākṛta heya dehasambandham, tanmūlakarmavaśyatā- 'sambandha' ca pratiṣidhya, kalyāṇa' guṇān kalyāṇarūpaṃ ca vadanti |
tadidaṃ svābhāvikameva rūpam, upāsakānugraheṇa tatpratipacyanu' guṇākāraṃ deva- manuṣyādi saṃsthānaṃ karoti svecchayaiva paramakāruṇiko bhagavān | tadidamāha śrutiḥ- 'ajāyamāno bahudhā vijāyate '

iti | smṛtiśca –

'ajo'pi sannavyayātmā bhūtānāmīśvaro'pi san |
prakṛtiṃ svāmadhiṣṭhāya sambhavāmyātmamāyayā ||' 'paritrāṇāya sādhūnāṃ vināśāya ca duṣkṛtām |'
vidyutaḥ - vidyudvarṇāt | 'ādityavarṇam' 'rukmavarṇam' iti vākyasarūpārtha- svīkārasya ucitatvāt | ādityavarṇo vigrahaśca na prākṛtaḥ 'tamasaḥ parastāt' iti śruteḥ | atisukṣmā prakṛtirhi ! tamaḥ | ataḥ tatkāryavailakṣaṇyasiddhiḥ | anyathā svarūpasya tamaso'nyatvakathanaṃ syāt tadānīṃ 'parastāt' iti astātipratyayasya, ādityavarṇatokteśca vaiyarthyaṃ syāt | ataḥ vigrahaviśiṣṭasyaiva tamaḥsparśābhāvoktiriti vigrahavailakṣaṇyasiddhiḥ | prākṛta heyetyādi | anena - kvacit sato hi kvacinniṣedhaḥ | ataḥ niṣedhyaheyaguṇānāṃ prakṛti tatsaṃśliṣṭa jīveṣu vidyamānatvaṃ prākṛtetyādiśabdena darśitam | vigrahaviśeṣavidhānadarśanāt, vigrahasāmānyaniṣedhaḥ tadvyatiriktaviṣaya: ; 'na hiṃsyāt' itivat, 'nirguṇam' itivacca | viśeṣa. niṣedhadarśanāt, deha sāmānyaniṣedhaḥ viśeṣe paryavasyati 'paśunā yajeta' iti paśuśabdavat : 'nirguṇa' śabdavacca iti tātparyam |
paramakāruṇika iti | devādisājātyaṃ na karmanibandhanam ; upāsakānugrahārthatvāt ityarthaḥ |
ajāyamāna iti, karmahetuka sukhādyarthaprākṛtadeha saṅgatirūpajanmaniṣedhaḥ | bahudhā vijāyate iti ca icchā hetu ka jagadrakṣaṇārthāprākṛta deha saṅgatirūpajanmavidhiḥ | asyāḥ śruteḥ avatāraparatvam abhyupagantavyam ; tathā satyeva 'ajo'pi san' ityādya pabṛṃhaṇaikārthyaṃ sidhyet |

iti | sādhavo hi upāsakāḥ | tatparitrāṇameva uddeśyam | 'ānuṣaṅgikastu' duṣkṛtāṃ vināśa: ; saṅkalpamātreṇā'pi tadupapatteḥ | 'prakṛtiṃ svāmiti | prakṛtiḥ – svabhāvaḥ | ' svameva svabhāvamāsthāya na saṃsāriṇāṃ svabhāvam ityarthaḥ |

ātmamāyayeti |

3.

"sva saṅkalparūpeṇa' jñānena ityarthaḥ |

"māyā vayunaṃ jñānam'
iti, jñānaparyāyamapi 'māyā' śabda naighaṇṭukāḥ adhīyate | āha ca bhagavān parāśaraḥ- 'samastāḥ śaktayaścaitā nṛpa yatra pratiṣṭhitāḥ |
'tadviśvarūpa'vairūpyaṃ rūpamanyaddharermahat ||' 'samastaśaktirūpāṇi tat karoti janeśvara |
devatirthamanuṣyākhyā ceṣṭāvanti svalīlayā ||' 'jagatāmupakārāya na sā karmanimittajā |' 'iti' | mahābhārate ' ca avatāra " rūpasyā'pi aprākṛtatvamucyate- 'na bhūtasaṅghasaṃsthāno deho'sya paramātmanaḥ' iti | ataḥ parasyaiva brahmaṇaḥ evaṃrūpavaccāt, ayamapi tasyaiva dharmaḥ | tyamaṇḍalākṣyadhikaraṇaḥ, ādityādijīvavyatiriktaḥ paramātmaiva ||21||

ataḥ ādi-

'bhedavyapadeśāccā'nyaḥ ||1-1-22||

ādityādijīvebhyo bhedaḥ vyapadiśyate asya paramātmanaḥ ;
'ya āditye tiṣṭhana, ādityādantaraḥ, yamādityo na veda,
yasyāssdityaḥ śarīram, ya ādityamantaro yamayati' 'ya ātmani tiṣṭhan, ātmano'ntaraḥ, yamātmā na veda, yasyā''tmā śarīram, ya ātmānamantaro yamayati'

iti |

"yo'kṣaramantare sañcaran, yasyākṣaraṃ śarīram, yamakṣaraṃ na veda,
yo mṛtyumantare sañcaran, yasya mṛtyuḥ śarīram yaṃ mṛtyurna veda,

eṣa sarvabhūtāntarātmā'pahatapāpmā divyo deva eko nārāyaṇaḥ' iti ca asya' apahatapāpmanaḥ paramātmanaḥ sarvān jīvān śarīratvena vyapadiśya, * teṣāma' ntarātmatvena ' enaṃ ' vyapadiśati | ataḥ sarvebhyaḥ 'hiraṇyagarbhādijīvebhyaḥ " anya eva paramātmā iti siddham ||22||

|| iti antaradhikaraṇam ||7||

ākāśādhikaraṇam ||1-1-8||

'yato vā 'imāni bhūtāni jāyante "

iti, ‘jagatkāraṇaṃ brahma' ’ityavagamyate |

kiṃ tat' jagatkāraṇam ? ityapekṣāyām

'sadeva somyedamagra āsīt'
' tattejo'sṛjata'
'ātmā vā idameka evāgra āsīt '
'sa imān lokānasṛjata'
'tasmādvā etasmādātmana ākāśaḥ sambhūtaḥ'
iti, sādhāraṇaiḥ śabdaiḥ jagatkāraṇe nirdiṣṭe, īkṣaṇaviśeṣānanda viśeṣarūpaviśeṣārthasvabhāvāt pradhāna kṣetrajñādivyatirikta brahma - ityuktam |
idānīm - ākāśādiviśeṣaśabdaiḥ "nirdiśya' jagatkāraṇatva- jagadaiśvaryādivāde'pi ākāśādiśabdābhidheyatayā prasiddhacidacidvastunaḥ arthāntaram uktalakṣaṇameva brahma - iti pratipādyate ; 'ākāśastalliṅgāt' ityādinā pādaśeṣeṇa ||

ākāśastaliṅgāt || 1-1-23||

idamāmnāyate chāndogye-

'asya lokasya kā gatiriti | ākāśa iti hovāca |

sarvāṇi ha vā imāni bhūtānyākāśādeva samutpadyante, ākāśa pratyastaṃ yanti, ākāśo 'hyevaibhyo' jyāyān, ākāśaḥ parāyaṇam'

iti ||

kiṃ prāptam ? prasiddhākāśaḥ - iti |

|

kutaḥ ?

śabdaikasamadhigamye vastuni, ya evārthaḥ vyutpattisiddhaḥ śabdena pratīyate sa eva grahītavyaḥ | ataḥ prasiddhākāśa' eva' ' carācara' bhūtajātasya kṛtsnasya kāraṇam | ataḥ tasmādatiriktaṃ brahma ||

nanu - īkṣāpūrvaka sṛṣṭyādibhiḥ, acetanāt jīvācca vyatiriktaṃ brahma - ityuktam | satyamuktam ; 'ayukta' tu tat | tathā hi
'yato vā imāni bhūtāni jāyante.... tadbrahma' ityukte ; kutaḥ imāni bhūtāni jāyante ? ityādi viśeṣāpekṣāyām",
'sarvāṇi ha vā imāni bhūtānyākāśādeva samutpadyante' ityādinā viśeṣapratīteḥ, 'jagajjanmādikāraṇam ākāśa eva' iti niścite sati, 'sadeva somyedamagra āsīt '
ityādiṣvapi, sadādiśabdāḥ sādhāraṇākārāḥ, tameva viśeṣam ākāśamabhidadhati |

" ātmā vā idameka evāgra āsīt
ityādiṣu 'ātmaśabdo'pi tatraiva vartate | tasyā'pi hi cetanaikāntatvaṃ na sambhavati ; yathā 'mṛdātmako ghaṭaḥ' iti | 'āpnotīti ātmā' ' iti vyutpacyā ", sutarām ākāśe'pi 'ātma' śabdo vartate ||

ataḥ evam ' ākāśa evaṃ kāraṇaṃ brahma' iti niścite sati, īkṣaṇādayaḥ

nanviti | arthavirodhāt vyutpattisiddhārthaḥ tyajyate iti | ityādi iti ādiśabdena, 'kiṃ prayanti : kena jīvanti ?' ityapekṣā vivakṣitā | viśeṣapratīteriti etadvākyaviśeṣeṇa vastuviśeṣapratītiḥ hetutayā uktā | niścite satīti | 'sadvidyādipratipanna kāraṇānuvādilakṣaṇavākyoktaṃ brahma, ākāśa eva' iti niścite ityarthaḥ | sādhāraṇākārāḥ—sādhāraṇarūpāḥ | ' chāgo vā mantravarṇāt ' iti nyāya : abhipretaḥ | tatraiva – ākāśe eva | ākāśaśabdāpekṣayā ātmaśabdo'pi sādhāraṇaḥ ityarthaḥ | apnotīti | ātmaśabdasya avayavaśaktyā'pi ākāśaparatvam | vyavasthitarūḍhiśakteḥ 'ākāśa' śabdasya rūḍhibhaṅgādapi, nānārthasādhāraṇarūḍhiśakteḥ 'ātma' śabdasya rūḍhibhaṅgaḥ ucita iti bhāva: |
īkṣaṇādaya iti | śrutiprābalyāt liṅga tadanuguṇaṃ neyam ityarthaḥ | yadi hi

viśeṣākāṅkṣāyām — dha, na. 3 niścīyate - vā

śrī bhāṣyam 1-1-8]

tadanuguṇāḥ gauṇāḥ varṇanīyāḥ | yadi hi sādhāraṇa śabdaireva sadādibhiḥ kāraṇam abhyadhāyiṣyata ; īkṣaṇādyarthānurodhena cetanaviśeṣa eva kāraṇamiti niraceṣyata' | 'ākāśa' śabdena tu viśeṣa eva niścitaḥ iti, na arthasvābhāvyāt nirṇetavyamasti ||

nanu -

'ātmana ākāśaḥ sambhūtaḥ'
iti, ākāśasyā'pi kāryatvaṃ pratīyate !

satyam ; sarveṣāmeva ākāśavāyvādīnāṃ sūkṣmāvasthā sthūlāvasthā ceti, avasthā- dvayamasti | tatra ākāśasya sūkṣmāvasthā kāraṇam | "sthūlāvasthā tu' kāryam | 'ātmana ākāśaḥ sambhūtaḥ' iti, "svasmādeva" sūkṣmarūpāt svayaṃ sthūlarūpaḥ sambhūtaḥ' ityarthaḥ | 'sarvāṇi ha vā imāni bhūtānyākāśādeva samutpadyante' iti, sarvasya jagataḥ śrākāśādeva prabhavāpyayādiśravaṇāt, " tadeva hi ' ' kāraṇaṃ brahma' iti niścitam |
ityādinā, sadādiśabdebhyaḥ ākāśādipadaśruteḥ prābalyamupapādayati | sādhāraṇaśabdai reveti na tu viśeṣaśabdāntare sati iti bhāvaḥ | ākāśaśabdena tviti | 'rūḍhirhi yogamapaharati ' iti nyāyāt, vilamvitapratipannāt yaugikādapi, śīghradhīviṣaye rūḍhyarthe eva ākāśaśabdasya parigrāhye, sadādiśabdānām īkṣaṇādiliṅga pararāmarśasāpekṣatvena vilambitapravṛttitayā atiśīghrapravṛttitvāt prabalatarākāśapadaśrutyā apekṣite viśeṣe labdhe, artha svabhāvanirṇetavya viśeṣābhāvāt, īkṣaṇādayaḥ tadanurodhena varṇanīyāḥ | ataḥ na cetana viśeṣa - kāraṇatvasiddhiḥ ityarthaḥ ||
nanviti | kāryatayā avagatasya paramakāraṇatvānupapattiḥ iti śaṅkāśayaḥ |
satyamiti | kāryatve, abhimati:; nasya kāraṇatvabhañjakattre, anaṅgīkāraḥ | vāyvādyu- pādānam, nidarśanatayā | sūkṣmāvasthāśabdena tanmātrāvasthā kathyate | 'ātmana ākāśa : ' iti vākye 'ātma' śabda : svavācī ityarthaḥ |
'prabhavāpyayādi' iti ādi śabdena, avakāśapradānena sthitihetutvamucyate | parāyaṇam -iti hi atroktam |

yata evaṃ prasiddhākāśāt anatiriktaṃ brahma, ata eva ca- 'yadeṣa ākāśa ānando na syāt '
'ākāśo ha vai nāma-rūpayornirvahitā'
ityevamādi nirdeśo'pi 'upapannatara: ' | ataḥ prasiddhākāśāt anatiriktaṃ brahma - iti ||
evaṃ prāpte, brūmaḥ - 'ākāśastaliṅgāt' | 'ākāśa' śabdābhidheya : ' prasiddhākāśāt acetanāt arthāntarabhūtaḥ, yathoktalakṣaṇaḥ paramātmaiva |

kuta: ?

talliṅgāt | nikhila jagadekakāraṇatvaṃ sarvasmāt jyāyastvaṃ, parāyaṇatvam ityādīni paramātmaliṅgāni upalabhyante | nikhila 'kāraṇatvaṃ hi, acidvastunaḥ prasiddhākāśaśabdābhidheyasya nopapadyate ; cetanatrastunaḥ tatkāryatvāsambhavāt | parāyaṇatvaṃ ca - cetanānāṃ paramaprāpyatvam | tacca acetanasya yasya sakalapuruṣārthavirodhinaḥ na sambhavati | sarvasmāt jyāyastvaṃ ca ' nirupādhikam' – sarvaiḥ kalyāṇaguṇaiḥ sarvebhyo niratiśayotkarṣaḥ | tadapi acitaḥ nopapadyate ||
evamādīti ādiśabdena, 'kaṃ brahma khaṃ brahma' 'prāṇaṃ cāsmai tadākāśaṃ cocuḥ' 'parame vyoman pratiṣṭhitā' iti vākyānyabhipretāni ||
evamiti | pūrvādhikaraṇe iva, atra 'taddharmopadeśāt' iti noktam ; api tu 'talliṅgāt' iti vākyāntaraprasiddhasyārthasya anūditasya hetutayā vivakṣitatvāt | liṅga- sambandhitvena pramāṇāntaropalabdhaṃ hi liṅgaṃ bhavati | sarvāṇi iti 'sarva' śabdasya asaṅkocamabhi- pretya nikhiletyuktam | bhūtaśabdasya vāyvādicatuṣṭayamātraparatvavyudāsāya jagacchandaḥ | sarvabhūta kāraṇatvoktyā, cidacidātmaka samasta prapañca kā raṇatvamuktaṃ bhavatītyabhiprāyaḥ | tatra nimittā- ntarāśravaṇāt vākyāntaraikārthyācca nimittopādānādirūpa sarvavidhakāraṇatvābhiprāyeṇa ekakāraṇa- tvamuktam | nikhileti, anena ākaśasya ekakāraṇatvānupapattiśca abhipretā | na hi ākāśaḥ nimittakāraṇaṃ bhavitumarhati | acetanasyeti | acetanatvena svarūpānantarbhūtatvāt heyatvamityarthaḥ | sakaleti | sakalānāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ sākṣātpuruṣārthaḥ mokṣaḥ ; bandhahetutayā tasya- virodhinaḥ ityarthaḥ ||

yaduktam – jagatkāraṇaviśeṣākāṅkṣāyām, ākāśaśabdena viśeṣasamarpaṇāt, anyat sarva ' tadanurūpameva varṇanīyam - iti,

tadayuktam ;

'sarvāṇi havā imāni bhūtānyā kāśādeva samutpadyante'
iti prasiddhavannirdeśāt | prasiddhavanirdeśo hi pramāṇāntaraprāptiṃ mapekṣate | pramāṇāntarāṇi ca 'sadeva somyedamagra āsīt '
ityevamādīnyeva vākyāni | tāni ca yathoditaprakāreṇaiva brahma pratipādayantīti, tatpratipāditaṃ brahma, 'ākāśa' śabdena prasiddhavat nirdiśyate | sambhavati ca parasya brahmaṇaḥ, prakāśakatvāt ' ākāśaśabdābhidheyatvam ; 'ākāśate, 'ākāśayati' ca' iti ||
kiṃ ca-anena ākāśaśabdena 'viśeṣa' samarpaṇakṣameṇā'pi cetanāṃśa prati
asambhāvita kāraṇabhāvam acetana viśeṣam abhidadhānena,
'tadaikṣata, bahu syāṃ prajāyeya'
'so'kāmayata, bahu syāṃ prajāyeya'

ityādivākyaśeṣāvadhāritasarvajñya - 'satyasaṅkalpatvādi' viśiṣṭa pūrvārthapratipādana samarthavākyā- rthānyathākaraṇaṃ na pramāṇapadavī madhirohati |

evam apūrvā nantaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭāpūrvārthapratipādanasamarthanekavākyagatisāmānyaṃ ca, ekena 'anuvāda sarūpeṇa' anyathākartuṃ na śakyate ||

' sa īkṣata lokānnu sṛjā iti'
'so'kāmayata | bahu syāṃ prajāyeya' ityāditattadvākyaviśeṣā eva sthirīkurvanti |
evaṃ vākyaśeṣāvadhāritānanya sādhāraṇānekā pūrvārthaviśiṣṭaṃ nikhilajagadekakāraṇaṃ 'sadeva 'somyaṃ" ityādivākyasiddhaṃ brahmaiva, 'ākāśa' śabdena prasiddhavat,
'sarvāṇi ha vā imāni bhūtāni
ityādivākyena nirdiśyate || 23 ||

prāṇādhikaraṇam ||1-1-9||

prāṇamevā'bhisaṃviśanti, prāṇamabhyujjihate| saiṣā devatā prastāvamanvāyattā | tāṃ cedavidvān prāstoṣyaḥ, mūrdhā te vyapatibhyat'

iti ||

atra prāṇaśabdo'pi, ākāśaśabdavat prasiddhaprāṇavyatirikte parasminneva brahmaṇi vartate ; tadasādhāraṇanikhila jagatpraveśa- niṣkramaṇādiliṅgāt prasiddhavat nirdiṣṭāt ||
adhikāśaṅkā 'tu' – ' kṛtsnasya bhūtaṃ jātasya prāṇādhīnasthiti-pravṛttyādidarśanāt, prasiddha eva prāṇaḥ jagatkāraṇatayā nirdeśamarhati – iti |
parihārastu – śilā- kāṣṭhādiṣu, cetanasvarūpe ca tadabhāvāt",
'sarvāṇi ha vā imāni bhūtāni prāṇamevā'bhisaṃviśanti, prāṇamabhyujjihate'
iti, nopapadyate - iti | ataḥ 'prāṇayati sarvāṇi bhūtāni ' ' iti kṛtvā paraṃ brahmaiva 'prāṇa' śabdenāsbhidhīyate ||

ataḥ - prasiddhākāśaprāṇādeḥ anyadeva, nikhilajagadekakāraṇam, apahatapāpmatva- sārvajñya satyasaṅkalpatvādyanantakalyāṇaguṇagaṇaṃ paraṃ brahmava, ākāśa - 'prāṇādi' śabdābhi- yam - iti siddham ||24||

|| iti prāṇādhikaraṇam ||9||
mūrdhā te vyapatiṣyat iti | ātmasattā na labhyetetyarthaḥ |
ākāśaśabdavat ityatra, rūḍhiprābalya - tatparihārādikaṃ pūrvoktaṃ sarvamanusandheyam ||
kṛtsnasyeti | atra avirodhaśaṅkayā pratyavasthānaṃ, virodhasphuṭīkaraṇaṃ ca anusandheyam | | sthiti - pravṛttyādi iti ādiśabdena nivṛttirucyate, pravṛttihetubhūtajñānacikīrṣe ca | sthiti:- pravṛttihetutvena utpattikāraṇatvam arthasiddhamiti bhāvaḥ | jagatkāraṇatayeti | saṃsṛṣṭaṃ prati kāraṇatvaṃ sambhavatīti bhāvaḥ || 24 ||

(70 || || iti prāṇādhikaraṇam

jyotiradhikaraṇam ||1-1-10 ||

atuḥ paraṃ— 'jagatkāraṇatva' vyāptena yena kenāspi' niratiśayotkṛṣṭaguṇena juṣṭaṃ, jyotirindrādiśabdaiḥ " arthāntara' prasiddherapi abhidhīyamānaṃ, 'paraṃ brahmava'-' ityabhidhīyate' 'jyotiścaraṇābhidhānāt ' ityādinā ||
jyotiścaraṇābhidhānāt ||1-1-25||

imāmnāyate chāndogye-

'atha yadataḥ paro divo jyotirdīpyate viśvataḥ pṛṣṭheṣu sarvataḥ 'pṛṣṭheṣvanuttameṣūttameṣu' lokeṣu, idaṃ vā tadyadidamasminnantaḥpuruṣe jyotiḥ'

iti ||

tatra saṃśayaḥ " - kim ayaṃ jyotiśśabdena nirdiṣṭaḥ niratiśayadīptiyukto'rthaḥ prasiddham ādityādijyotireva kāraṇa bhūtaṃ brahma' ? uta " samastacidacidvastu jāta- visajātīyaḥ, paramakāraṇabhūtaḥ, amitabhāḥ, sarvajñaḥ satyasaṅkalpaḥ, puruṣottamaḥ 1 – iti ||

pṛṣṭheṣu—uparitaneṣu | 'tripādūrdhva:' 'tamasopariṣṭāt ' ityādiśrutibhyaḥ | vyaṣṭi - samaṣṭivivakṣayā viśvataḥ sarvataḥ ityuktam | anuttamaḥ – svasmādanyaḥ uttamo nā'stīti anuttamaḥ | viśvataḥ kṛtsnabhūmipradeśāt | sarvataḥ kṛtsnāntarikṣapradeśāt | pṛṣṭheṣu bahirbhūteṣu iti yojanāsambhavāt pūrvapakṣotthānam | ādityo hi ūrdhvalokeṣvapi raśmidvārā dīpyate ||

|

kiṃ yuktam ? prasiddhameva jyotiḥ iti |

kuta: ?

prasiddhavanirdeśe'pi, ākāśa' prāṇādivat' svavākyopāttaparamātmavyāptaliṅgaviśe- pādarśanāt parama puruṣapratyabhijñānāsaṃbhavāt, 'kaukṣeyajyotiṣā' aikyopadeśācca, prasiddha- meva jyotiḥ, kāraṇatvavyāpta niratiśayadīpti yogāt ' 'jagatkāraṇaṃ' brahma - iti ||

" evaṃ prāpte, pracakṣmahe —– jyotiśvaraṇābhidhānāt | dyusambandhitayā nirdiṣṭaṃ niratiśayadīptiyuktaṃ jyotiḥ paramapuruṣa eva |

kuta: ?

' pādo'sya 'sarvā' bhūtāni | tripādasyā'mṛtaṃ divi' iti, asyaiva sambandhinaḥ caraṇatvena sarvabhūtābhidhānāt |

etaduktaṃ bhavati yadyapi,

'atha yadataḥ paro divo jyotiḥ "
ityasmin vākye, parama'puruṣāsādhāraṇa 'liṅga nopalabhyate ; tathāpi, pūrvavākye dyusambandhi- tathā parama puruṣasya nirdeśāt idamapi sambandhijyotiḥ sa eveti pratyabhijñāyate iti | kaukṣeyajyotiṣā " aikyopadeśaca – phalāya tadātmakatvānusandhānavidhiḥ - iti na kaścidoṣaḥ | kaukṣeyajyotipaśca tadātmakatva bhagavatā svayamevoktam- 'ahaṃ vaiśvānaro bhūtvā prāṇināṃ dehamāśritaḥ'

iti ||25||

sarvā bhūtāni iti, kāryajagadantargatācitsaṃsṛṣṭacetanāḥ ucyante | divi iti, śabdastu, samastavyaṣṭi-samaṣṭitantvabahirbhūtāprākṛtasthānaviśeṣaparaḥ ; 'viśvataḥ pṛṣṭheṣu, sarvataḥ pṛṣṭheṣu' iti śravaṇāt | viśvaśabdavivaraṇaṃ sarvaśabda: iti gatyabhāvaliṅgam | 'tathākṣarāt saṃbhavatīha- viśvam' iti jagadantarvartivyaṣṭijāte viśvaśabdaprayogāt 'viśva' śabda: vyaṣṭiparaḥ | sarvaśabdaḥ pariśeṣāt samaṣṭitattvaparaḥ | ata eva gadye'pi gaditaṃ bhāṣyakāra :– 'samastaṃ kāryakāraṇajāta- matītya vartamāne' iti | tripāttvañca—aprākṛtaiḥ bhogya-bhogopakaraṇa- bhogasthānaviśeṣaiḥ .. sambhavati | evaṃ ca 'asya divi' iti vyatirekanirdeśa : amṛtatvaṃ ca mukhyaṃ bhavati |

etaduktamiti | pūrvavākye dyusambandhinaḥ caraṇābhidhāne sati kathamasya vākyasya paramātmaparatvam ? ekaviṣayatvāt iti cet, kathamekaviṣayatvam ? |
kaukṣeyeti, pūrvapakṣahetuṃ pariharati | phalāyeti | 'cakṣuṣyaḥ śruto bhavati' ityuktaphalāya ityarthaḥ | tadātmakatveti | ' tadetaddṛṣṭaṃ ca śrutaṃ cetyupāsīta ' ityatra tadetacchabdau tadātmakajyotirviṣayau; tadātmakatvena prakṛtatvāt iti bhāvaḥ | na kaściddoṣa iti | 'annādo vasudānaḥ ' 'caturvidhā bhajante mām' 'sakalaphalaprado hi viṣṇuḥ' ityādiśruti smṛtivaśāt sakalaphalapradasya paramātmanaḥ, cakṣupyatvādividyānuguṇaphala- hetutvamatra yuktam ityabhiprāyaḥ | kaukṣeyajyotiṣa iti | vedanasya yathārthaviṣayatve sambhavati, atasmin taddṛṣṭirūpatā''śrayaṇaṃ paroktam ayuktamiti bhāvaḥ || 25 ||

1 viśvā – du. 4 puruṣasādhāraṇaṃ - vā.

|| iti jyotiradhikaraṇam ||10||

indraprāṇādhikaraṇam || 1-1-11 ||

niratiśayadīptiyuktaṃ jyoti śabdābhidheyaṃ prasiddhavat nirdiṣṭam, paramapuruṣa eva- ityuktam | idānīṃ kāraṇatvavyāptāmṛtatvaprāptyupāyatayā upāsyatvena śrutaḥ indra- "prāṇādi' śabdā'bhidheyo'pi, paramapuruṣa eva ityāha-
prāṇastathā'nugamāt || 1-1-29||
kauṣītaki (kī) brāhmaṇe pratardana vidyāyām -

niratiśayeti, avāntarasaṅgatimāha | jyotiriti | ākāśa prāṇa tejasāṃ kramo- papatteḥ, 'ata eva prāṇaḥ' ityasyā'nantaraṃ, 'jyotiścaraṇābhidhānāt' ityadhikaraṇaṃ saṅgatamiti bhāvaḥ | prasiddhavaditi | anena adhikaraṇayoḥ paurvāparyaniyamaḥ uktaḥ | 'sarvāṇi ha vā ' iti, pūrvasmātpūrvādhikaraṇadvayaviṣayavākyayoḥ prasiddhavanirdeśo dṛśyate, atrāpi 'atha yadata : ' iti yacchabdena | tasmāt jyotiradhikaraṇaṃ pūrvādhikaraṇadvayena saṅgatam | evaṃ sati uttarādhikaraṇasya jyotiradhikaraṇānantarabhāvitvaṃ prāptam iti bhāvaḥ | kāraṇatvavyāpteti, avāntarapeṭikānu- gatamarthamāha | pūrvādhikaraṇe paramapuṣasya jyotiśśabdābhidheyatvamuktam, tadayuktam ; paramapuruṣā- danyasya indrādiśabdavācyasya, vedāntapratipādyatvāt - iti śaṅkayā saṅgatirityarthaḥ | tatra kāraṇa - -tvākṣepaka liṅgabhūyastvam anusandheyam (śru pra ) ||

ānandadyāmṛtaśabdādyaiḥ upasaṃhāradarśanāt ||

'pratardano ha vai daivodā sirindrasya priyaṃ dhāmopajagāma, yuddhena ca pauruṣeṇa ca'

ityārabhya,

'varaṃ vṛṇīṣva'
iti vaktāram indraṃ prati

' tvameva me varaṃ vṛṇīṣva, yaṃ tvaṃ manuṣyāya hitatamaṃ manyase' iti pratardaneokte',
' sa hovāca prāṇo'smi prajñātmā, taṃ māmāyuramṛtamityupasva' iti śrūyate ||

" tatra saṃśayaḥ kimayaṃ hitatamopāsanakarmatayā indraprāṇaśabda nirdiṣṭaḥ jīva eva ? uta tadatiriktaḥ paramātmā ? - iti ||
varaṃ vṛṇīṣvetīti | atra mudritakośeṣu, 'pratardano ha vai .... pauruṣeṇa ca | taṃ hendra uvāca | varaṃ te dadānīti | sa hovāca pratardanaḥ | tvameva me varaṃ vṛṇīṣva | yaṃ tvaṃ manuṣyāya hitatamaṃ manyase' ityānupūrvī dṛśyate | atra pūrvaṃ 'varaṃ vṛṇīṣva' iti vākyābhāve, 'varaṃ dadānīti' vākyamātrasatve, ‘varaṃ dehi' ityeva brūyāt pratardanaḥ; na tu 'tvameva me varaṃ vṛṇīṣva' iti | ataḥ tadātve likhitakośeṣu upalabdhaḥ bhāṣyādṛtaḥ 'varaṃ vṛṇīṣva' iti pāṭha evaṃ yuktaḥ iti pratibhāti ||

indraprāṇaśabdanirdiṣṭa iti | upakrame 'indrasya' iti indraśabdena 'prāṇo'smi prajñātmā' iti prāṇaśabdena ca nirdiṣṭatvāt evamuktam | prāṇaśabdasya paramātmaliṅga- pratipādakavākyasthatvena siddhānta hetudyotakatvāt sūtre 'prāṇa' śavdena dharmī nirdiṣṭaḥ ; na tu 'indra' śabdena | ' indra' śabda nirdiṣṭasya vicāraviṣayatvam ārtham - iti sūtrakārābhiprāyaḥ iti

kiṃ yuktam ? jīva eva – iti |

kuta: ?

indraśabdasya jīvaviśeṣa eva prasiddheḥ, tatsamānādhikaraṇasya prāṇaśabdasyā'pi tatraiva 'vṛtteḥ' | ayam indrābhidhāno jīvaḥ ", pratardanena-
' tvameva me varaṃ vṛṇīṣva yaṃ tvaṃ manuṣyāya hitatamaṃ manyase ' ityuktaḥ,

'mām.... upāsva'

iti, svātmopāsanaṃ hitatamam upadideśa | hitatamaśca amṛtatva prāptyupāya eva | jagatkāraṇopāsanasyaiva amṛtatva' prāptihetutā',
'tasya tāvadeva ciraṃ yāvanna vimokṣye atha sampatsye'
" ityavagatā | ataḥ prasiddhajīvabhāvaḥ indra evaṃ kāraṇaṃ brahma, ityāśaṅkāyām,
liṅgānuguṇatayā tvāṣṭrahananādivarṇanam ? iti | tadarthaṃ 'mām upāsva' iti kiṃ svopāsanamucyate ? uta svāvasthaparamātmopāsanam ? iti vicāraḥ ||
pūrvapakṣe phalaphalibhāvaḥ - 'māmupāsva' iti yadā svopāsanoktiḥ ; tadā tvāṣṭravadhādyanu guṇam ānandādeḥ varṇanīyaśvena, ānandādiguṇānām amukhyatvāt hitatamopāsana karmatayoktaḥ indraḥ iti, jagatkāraṇaṃ prasiddhajīvabhāvaḥ indraḥ iti ||

|

rājānte phalaphalibhāvaḥ – 'māmupāsva' iti yadā svāvasthaparamātmopāsanopadeśaḥ ; tadā ānandādiguṇānuguṇaṃ tvāṣṭravadhādeḥ varṇanīyatvāt, ānandādiguṇānāṃ mukhyatvāt, hitatamopāsana karmabhūtendraprāṇaśabdanirdiṣṭaḥ paramātme – iti, sarvavedāntavedyaṃ jagatkāraṇaṃ brahma, iti ||

prasiddheriti | anena jīvamātra (sāmānya) pakṣasya anudayaḥ sūcitaḥ | tatsamānādhikaṇa- syeti, prāṇapadasya kathaṃ jīvaparatvam? | anena prādhānyena prāṇaparatvaśaṅkānutthāna- mapi arthāt sūcitam | jagatkāraṇeti, mumukṣūpāsyatvasya kāraṇatvavyāptiṃ darśayati | ataḥ iti, pūrvapakṣopasaṃhāraḥ ||

abhidhīyate -- prāṇastathā'nugamāt - iti | ayam 'indraprāṇa' śabda nirdiṣṭa:

api tu jīvādarthāntarabhūtaṃ paraṃ brahma' |
' sa eṣa prāṇa eva prajñātmā ānando'jaro'mṛtaḥ

na jīvamātram ;

na vakturātmopadeśāditi cedadhyātmasambandhabhūmā hyasmin || 1-1-30||
anugamaḥ - pūrvavākyaprakṛtasya uttaravākyasambandhaḥ | svavākyānabhivyaktasya siddhānta- hetoḥ uttaravākyābhivyaktatayā anugamāt ityuktam | ākāśādhikaraṇe, jyotiradhikaraṇe ca siddhāntahetorhi svavākyapūrva vākyābhivyaktatayā 'anugamāt' ityanuktiḥ | ānando'jareti | sautra tathāpadaṃ, sādhakākāra parāmarśitvena vyācaṣṭe | prāṇaḥ - paramātmā | tathā'nugamāt- paramātmatvānuguṇyena anugamāt; iti sūtrayojanā | tathā satyeveti | indraprāṇaśabda nirdiṣṭe parasmin brahmaṇi satyeva ityarthaḥ | prāṇaḥ - paramātmā | tathā'nugamāt – paramātmatayā anugamāt— iti yojanā | anugamakriyāviśeṣaṇaṃ tathāśabdaḥ | buddhistha sādhyasādhakadharmavattayā anugamo vivakṣitaḥ ityabhiprāya: ||29||

'ānando'jaro'mṛtaḥ'
ityanena aikārthyāt, ayaṃ paraṃ brahma - iti,

tat, nopapadyate ;

'māmeva vijānīhi '

'triśīrṣāṇaṃ tvāṣṭramahanam '

" ityādibhiḥ " upasaṃhāraḥ, tadanuguṇa eva varṇanīyaḥ - iti cet,

|

'tadyathā rathasyāreṣu nemirarpitā, nābhāvarā arpitāḥ, evamevaitā bhūtamātrāḥ
prajñāmātra svarpitāḥ, prajñāmātrāḥ prāṇe'rpitāḥ'
' sa eṣa prāṇa evaṃ prajñātmā ānando'jaro'mṛtaḥ' iti 'bhūtamātrā' śabdena acetana vastujātam abhidhāya, 'prajñāmātrā' śabdena tadādhāratayā cetanavarga cāsbhidhāya, tasyāpyādhāratayā prakṛtam indra 'prāṇa' śabdābhidheyaṃ nirdiśya, tameva 'ānando'jaro'mṛtaḥ'
