athavā, 'adhyātmasambandhabhūmā hyasmin ' |

paramātmā sādhāraṇadharmasambandhaḥ – adhyātmasambandhaḥ | tasya bhūmā 'bahutvaṃ hi asmin prakaraṇe vidyate | tathā'hi ; prathamam,
tadyatheti, ādheyatayā sambandhe śrutimāha | na kevalam upasaṃhāravacanamātram ; kintu pūrvamapyastīti, bahutvābhiprāyeṇa sa eṣa prāṇa eva' ityādikamupāttam | prajñāmātrā... tayeti | na hi indriyāṇāṃ pṛthivyādibhūtādhāratvaṃ yuktam iti bhāvaḥ | cetanavargamiti | prākaraṇika- prajñātmaśabdaikavacanaṃ jātyabhiprāyam iti bhāvaḥ | tasyāpyādhāra....nirddiśyeti | 'tā vā etā: ' iti pūrvavākyokte parasparasākāṅkṣatve, dṛṣṭāntatvaṃ na ghaṭate prajñāmātrāṇām ādhārāntarakathanāt iti bhāvaḥ | upadiśatīti | evam arthavirodha dṛṣṭāntadāṣṭantikabhāvavaighaṭya- mukhyatvaprākaraṇika- prayogasādhāraṇyaiḥ anugṛhītasya śrutivākyasyārthaḥ upanyastaḥ ||

' sa eṣa prāṇa eva' ityetadvākyodāharaṇaṃ vākyāntarāṇyapi brahmaparāṇi santi iti pradarśanārtham | tena upakramasvārasyādapi samudāyasvārasyaṃ prabalam — iti phalitam | evam avayavisvārasya prābalyasya arthasiddhatvādapi kaṇṭhoktatvaṃ yuktamiti, mukhāntareṇa athaveti | pūrvayojanāyām, adhyātmaśabdasya ātmani ityarthaḥ| asyāṃ tu , ātmani vartamānapara: adhyātmaśabda: | ' upasaṃhāra -- vākyam upakramabādhitam' iti pūrvapakṣiṇoktam | tatredamuttaramuktaṃ bhavati, upakramopasaṃhārau vākyāvayavau | tayoḥ avayavabhūtayoḥ parasparavirodhe, upakramaprābalyam ; na tu avayavivirodhe |

' tvameva me varaṃ vṛṇīṣva yaṃ tvaṃ manuṣyāya hitatamaṃ manyase '

iti,

'mām.......upārava'

' iti ca', paramātmāsādharaṇa mokṣasādhanopāsanakarmatvaṃ prāṇaśabda nirdiṣṭasya indrasya pratīyate | "tathā",
'epa' eva' sādhu karma kārayati taṃ yamebhyo lokebhya unninīpati' ' epa evāsādhu karma kārayati taṃ yamadho ninīpati'

iti, 'sarvasya karmaṇaḥ kārayitṛtvaṃ ca paramātmadharmaḥ | tathā,
' tathā rathasyāreṣu nemirarpitā, nābhāvarā arpitāḥ, evamevaitā bhūtamātrāḥ prajñāmātrāsvarpitāḥ, prajñāmātrāḥ prāṇe'rpitāḥ
iti sarvādhāratvaṃ ca tasyaiva dharmaḥ | tathā,
" sa eva prāṇa eva prajñātmā''nando'jaro'mṛtaḥ'
' ityetespi', paramātmana eva dharmāḥ |
'eṣa lokādhipatiḥ eṣa sarveśaḥ "

iti ca paramātmanyeva sambhavati | tadevam, adhyātmasambandhabhūmnaḥ atra vidyamānatvāt paramātmaiva atra 'indraprāṇa' śabda nirdiṣṭaḥ || 30 ||

kathaṃ tarhi prajñātajīvabhāvasya indrasya, svātmanaḥ 'upāsyatvopadeśaḥ ' 'saṅgacchate' ?

'tatrā''ha'

'etaduktaṃ bhavati

'anena jīvenātmanā'nupraviśya nāmarūpe vyākaravāṇi' 'aitadātmyamidaṃ sarvam'
'antaḥ praviṣṭaḥ śāstā janānāṃ sarvātmā'
'ya ātmani tiṣṭhan ātmano'ntaro yamātmā na veda, yasyātmā śarīraṃ ya ātmānamantaro 'yamayati'
'eṣa sarvabhūtāntarātmā'pahatapāpmā divyo deva eko nārāyaṇa:' ityevamādinā śāstreṇa, jīvātma'śarīrakaṃ paramātmānamavagamya, jīvātmavācinām 'ahaṃ tva- mādiśabdānāmapi paramātmanyeva paryavasānaṃ jñātvā 'māmeva vijānīhi ' 'māmupāsva' iti svātmaśarīrakaṃ paramātmānameva upāsyatvena upadideśa ' iti |
vāmadevavat - yathā vāmadevaḥ parasya brahmaṇaḥ sarvāntarātmatvaṃ, sarvasya ca taccharīratvaṃ śarīravācināṃ ca śabdānāṃ śarīriṇi paryavasānaṃ paśyan, 'aham' iti svātmaśarīrakaṃ brahma nirdiśya, tatsāmānādhikaraṇyena manu- sūryādīn vyapadiśati-
'tadvaitatpaśyan ṛṣirvāmadevaḥ pratipede, ahaṃ manurabhavaṃ sūryaśca' 'ahaṃ kakṣīvā' nṛpirasmi vipraḥ "

'sarvagatvādanantarasya sa evā'hamavasthitaḥ |
mattaḥ sarvamahaṃ sarvaṃ mayi sarvaṃ sanātane ||'

ityādi vadati ||31 ||

asmin prakaraṇe jīvavācibhiḥ śabdaiḥ, acidviśeṣā bhidhāyibhiśca upāsyabhūtasya "parasya brahmaṇaḥ abhidhāne kāraṇaṃ codyapūrvaka māha -
jīvamukhyaprāṇaliṅgānneti cennopāsātraividhyādāśritatvā' diha' tadyogāt || 1-1-31||
'na vācaṃ vijijñāsīta, vaktāraṃ vidyāt' 'triśīrṣāṇa tvāmahanan '

'arunmukhām yatīn sālāvṛkebhyaḥ prāyaccham ' ityādijīvaliṅgāt,
'yāvaddhayasmin śarīre prāṇo vasati tāvadāyuḥ'
'atha khalu prāṇa eva prajñātmedaṃ śarīraṃ parigṛhyotthāpayati' iti mukhyaprāṇaliṅgācca, na adhyātmasambandha 'bhūmā' iti cet,

upāsātraividhyāt hetoḥ | upāsanā traividhyamupadeṣṭuṃ tattacchabdenā'bhidhānam | nikhilakāraṇa'bhūtasya brahmaṇaḥ svarūpeṇānusandhānam, bhoktṛvarga śarīrakatvānusandhānam, bhogya- bhogopakaraṇaśarīkatvānusandhānaṃ ceti, trividhamanusandhānam upadeṣṭum ityarthaḥ | tadidaṃ trividhaṃ brahmānusandhānaṃ 'prakaraṇāntareṣvapyāśritam' - 'satyaṃ jñānamanantaṃ brahma'

na;

'ānando brahma'
ityādiṣu svarūpānusandhānam |

'tatsṛṣṭvā | tadevā'nuprāviśat | tadanupraviśya' 'sacca tyaccā'bhavat | niruktaṃ cā'niruktaṃ ca | nilayanaṃ cā'nilayanaṃ ca | vijñānaṃ cā'vijñānaṃ ca | satyaṃ cānṛtaṃ ca satyamabhavat'
mukhyaprāṇaḥ – pañcavṛttiḥ | 'mukhya' śabda : indriyādivyāvṛttyarthaḥ | nañaḥ niṣedhyākāṃkṣāyām anativiprakṛṣṭaṃ sautraṃ padamadhyāhṛtam adhyātmeti |
upāsaneti, hetutvaṃ vivṛṇoti | upadeṣṭum ityanena, prayojanatayā hetutvaṃ spaṣṭam| nikhileti, traividhyaṃ vivṛṇoti | svarūpeṇānusandhānam advāraka guṇaviśiṣṭānusandhānam |

ityādiṣu, bhoktṛśarīratayā, bhogya-bhogopakaraṇaśarīratayā ca anusandhānam | ihā'pi prakaraṇe, 'tat trividha' manusandhānaṃ yujyata eva ityarthaḥ |

etaduktaṃ bhavati yatra hiraṇyagarbhādijīvaviśeṣāṇāṃ prakṛtyādyacetanaviśeṣāṇāṃ ca paramātmā sādhāraṇadharmayogaḥ, tadabhidhāyināṃ śabdānāṃ paramātma vāciśabdaiḥ sāmānādhi- karaṇyaṃ vā dṛśyate, tatra paramātmanaḥ tattaccidacidviśeṣāntarātmatvānusandhānaṃ pratipi - pādayipitam - iti |

ataḥ — atra indra-prāṇaśabda nirdiṣṭaḥ "jīvādarthāntarabhūtaḥ paramātmaiva' – iti

siddham || 31 ||

iti indra prāṇādhikaraṇam || 19 ||
iti śrīśārīrakamīmāṃsābhāṣye prathamasyādhyāyasya prathamaḥ pādaḥ
cidacidanupraveśāt īśvarasya tatprakāritvamiti, trividhopāsanaparaśrutyantarārthaviśadīkāra hetutvāt, ānandaballīvākyam ihodāhṛtam | anyattu abhipretam |

ihā'pīti, 'iha tadyogāt' ityetadyākhyānamidam | 'ānando'jaro'mṛtaḥ' iti, svena rūpeṇa anusandhānam | 'prāṇo'smi' iti, acidviśiṣṭānusandhānam | cidviśiṣṭānusandhāna miti, trividhānusandhānam ihopapadyate ityarthaḥ |
yatreti | ayaṃ nyāyaḥ na pratardana vidyāmātraviṣaya: ; api tu īdṛśasarva prakaraṇaviṣayaḥ iti bhāvaḥ | hiraṇyagarbhādīti | 'paramātmā vyavasthitaḥ | sa brahmā sa śivaḥ sendraḥ' iti śrutiḥ smāyate | ananyapareṇa nārāyaṇānuvākena sarvavidyāsūpāsyaḥ nārāyaṇa eveti nirṇītatvāt, indrādyupāsane'pi tadantaryāmī nārāyaṇa ebopāsyaḥ iti bhāvaḥ ; pāśu- patādhikaraṇa tathā anuvadiṣyamāṇatvāt | paramātmāsādhāraṇeti | paramātmāsādhāraṇadharma yogāt, tadvāciśabdasāmānādhikaraṇyācca viśiṣṭopāsanaparatvam avagamyate ityarthaḥ || 31||

'mām... upāsva' iti,

|| adhikaraṇārtha sūcanī ||

śrī śārīrakamīmāṃsābhāṣye prathame samanvayādhyāye
dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ

adhi. 1-6]-

'prathame pāde – adhītavedaḥ puruṣaḥ karmamīmāṃsāśravaṇādhigatakarmayāthātmyavijñānaḥ, kevalakarmaṇām alpāsthiraphalatvam avagamya, vedāntavākyeṣu ca āpātapratītānanta sthira- phalabrahmasvarūpatadupāsanasamupajātī paramapuruṣārtha lakṣaṇa mokṣāpekṣaḥ, avadhāritapariniṣpanna- vastu' bodhana' śabdaśaktiḥ, vedāntavākyānāṃ parasmin brahmaṇi niścitapramāṇabhāvaḥ, taditikartavyatā rūpaśārīrakamīmāṃsāśravaṇam ārabheta - ityuktaṃ, śāstrārambhasiddhaye ||
prathame pāde ityādi | kimarthamatra prathamapādādhikaraṇārthānukramaṇam

pādānuyāyinaḥ arthasya pratyadhikaraṇam anuyāyitvasphuṭīkaraṇāya | anuyāyitvakathanaṃ, tripādīsaṅgatyartham | tripādīmaṅgatiśca sūtrakārābhipretatvāt | pādabhedo hi arthabhedāt | tatkramaśca saṅgativiśeṣāt | tatra pramāṇaṃ saṃpradāyakramaḥ ||
atra adhikaraṇārthānukramaṇena tripādyāḥ anārambhaṇīyatvaśaṅkā ca arthāt parihṛtā bhavati ; tripādyām udāhariṣyamāṇānāṃ śrutīnāṃ prathamapādādhikaraṇoktanyā yā viṣayatvajñāpanārthatvāt anu-- kramaṇasya | ata eva prathamapāde vedāntavākyānāṃ paramātmaparatve darśite'pi tripādyā ārambhaṇīyatva siddham | prathame pāde'pi īkṣatyānandamayādhikaraṇayoḥ kāraṇasya cidacidvailakṣaṇye siddhe'pi, pūrvādhikaraṇanyāyā viṣayaśrutivākyārthanirṇayārtho hi uttarādhikaraṇārambhaḥ ; tadvadatrā'pi iti ||
catuḥsūtryāḥ śāstropoddhātarūpatvāt arthataḥ prathamapādāntargatatvam ; na tu prathamapādasya prathamādhyāyasya vā asādhāraṇatvāt ||
adhītavedaḥ - kṛtasāṅgasaśiraskādhyayanaḥ | tāvati aparyavasānaṃ karmetyādi tatrāpyaparyavasāne hetuḥ kevaletyādi | siddhe vastuni (śabdasya ) vyutpatyabhāvāśaṅkāya,

|

'ananta' vicitra sthiratra sarūpabhoktṛ-bhogya-bhogopakaraṇa-bhogasthānalakṣaṇa nikhila- jagadudaya - vibhava'laya' - mahānandaikakāraṇaṃ paraṃ brahma, 'yato vā 'imāni', ityādivākyaṃ bodhayati – iti ca pratyapādi ||

jagadekakāraṇaṃ paraṃ brahma, sakaletara pramāṇāviṣayatayā śāstreka' pramāṇakam -

" ityabhyadhāma ||

"śāstra' pramāṇakatvaṃ ca brahmaṇaḥ pravṛtti nivṛttyanvaya virahe'pi svarūpeṇaiva parama- puruṣārthabhūte parasmin brahmaṇi vedāntavākyānāṃ' samanvayāt niruhyate - ityabrūma ||

nikhilajagadekakāraṇatayā vedāntavedyaṃ 'brahma ca', īkṣaṇādyanvayāt ānumānika- pradhānādarthāntarabhūtaḥ cetanaviśeṣa eva – ityupāpīpadāma || -

sa ca svābhāvikānavādhikātiśayānanda-vipaścitva- nikhilacetanabhayābhayahetutva-
prathamasūtrasya tāla avadhāritetyādi | vedāntavākyairevā'lam, kimarthaḥ brahmavicāraḥ ? ityatrottaratayā taditikartavyatetyādi | śāsrārambhasiddhaye iti padam, uttarādhikaraṇatraye anuṣajyata iti, catuḥsūtryāḥ śāstrārambhasiddhyarthatvam uktaṃ bhavati |
anantetyādiviśeṣaṇaiḥ sautrasya asya iti padasya vivakṣitamarthamāha | ananta - vicitra śabdau bhoktu bhogyasādhāraṇau | mahānandaikakāraṇatvaṃ 'tadvijijñāsasva' iti vākyābhipretam tāpatrayāturajijñāsyasya hi lakṣaṇamuktam | ekakāraṇa miti eka śabdaprayogaḥ, 'aviruddhaviśeṣaṇabhedaḥ na viśeṣyabhedāvahaḥ' ityabhiprāyeṇa, nimitto. pādānaikatvābhiprāyeṇa vā ||
samanvayāt – paramapuruṣārthabhūtasyaiva brahmaṇaḥ pratipādakatayā, śāstrasya tenā'nvayāt |

satyasaṅkalpatva- samastacetanācetanāntarātmatvādibhiḥ baddhamuktobhayāvasthāt jīvaśabdābhi lapanīyācca 'arthāntarabhūtaḥ ' - iti ca samārtathāmahi ||

saca, aprākṛtākarmanimittasvāsādhāraṇa divyarūpaḥ - ityudairirāma ||

ākāśaprāṇādyacetanaviṃśeṣābhidhāyibhiḥ, jagatkāraṇatayā prasiddhavat nirddiśyamānaḥ, sakaletara' cetanācetana vilakṣaṇaḥ sa eva – iti samagariṣmahi ||

parataccāsādhāraṇa niratiśayadīptiyuktajyotiśśabdābhidheyaḥ, yusambandhitayā
" pratyabhijñānāt sa eva – ityā tiṣṭhāmahi ||

'paramakāraṇāsādhāraṇā 'mṛta' tvaprāpti hetubhūtaḥ paramapuruṣa eva, śāstradṛṣṭyā indrādi-

śabdaiḥ abhidhīyate - ityanūmahi ||

tadevam— atipatitasakaletarapramāṇasambhāvanābhūmiḥ, sārvajñya-satyasaṅkalpatvādya- parimitodāraguṇasāgaratayā svetarasamastavastu vilakṣaṇaḥ paraṃ brahma, puruṣottamo nārāyaṇa eva vedāntavedyaḥ – ityuktam ||

svābhāvikānavādhikātiśayaśabdau prativiśeṣaṇamanvitau ||
ākāśeti | ākāśa prāṇādhikaraṇārthayoḥ pṛthaganuktiḥ, nyāyasya anatibhinnatvāt | parameti | paramakāraṇa| sādhāraṇam - taddharmabhūtam amṛtatvam | tatprāptiḥ paramasā myāpattiḥ |
atipatiteti. 'puruṣatvāt asmadādivat' ityādikutarka parihārārtham | teṣāṃ dharmigrāhakamānabādhaḥ iti bhāvaḥ | puruṣottamaśabda:, jyotiradhikaraṇārthābhiprāyaḥ | nārāyaṇa śabdaḥ, pratardanavidyābhiprāya: | 'māmupāsva' ityasyā'pi nārāyaṇānuvā kaikyena sarvavidyopāsyatayā - uktanārāyaṇopāsanaparatvāt, cidacidvilakṣaṇakāraṇāstitvaṃ pādānugatārthaḥ ityarthaḥ ||

ataḥ paraṃ dvitīya tṛtīya - caturtheṣu pādeṣu - yadyapi vedāntavedyaṃ brahmaiva tathā'pi, kānicidvedāntavākya ni pradhāna' kṣetrajñānantarbhūta' vastuviśeṣasvarūpapratipādanaparāṇyeva ityā- śaṅkaya, tannirasanamukhena tattadvākyoditakalyāṇaguṇākaratvaṃ brahmaṇaḥ pratipādyate ||

21 sarvatra prasiddhyadhikaraṇam || 1-2 - 1 ||

33. sarvatra prasiddhopadeśāt || 1-2-1 ||

idamāmnāyate chāndogye

'atha khalu 'ṛtumaya: ' puruṣa:, yathākraturasmiloke puruṣo bhavati tataḥ pretya bhavati, sa kratuṃ kurvīta' 'manomayaḥ prāṇaśarīro bhārūpaḥ'

ityādi |

atheti | 'sarva khalu' ityāderdvitīyayojanāyām udāhariṣyamāṇatvāt prathamayojanāpekṣitam 'atha khalu' ityādikam upāttam | dvitīyasūtra 'bhārūpa:' ityasya uparitanavākyasya upādāsyamānatvāt, atra 'bhārūpa:' ityantam upāttam | ādiśabdena anyadgṛhītam | atra 'rātri' nyāyena manomayaḥ upāsyatayā sambadhyate; ārthavādikasyā'pi vidhyapekṣitasya svīkāryatvāt |
'sa ṛtuṃ kurvīta' ityādikamevopāttaṃ cet, phalāśravaṇanimittaṃ 'phalavatsannidhau aphalaṃ tadaṅgam ' iti nyāyena pūrvavihitopāsanāṅgatvaṃ mā bhūt iti, phalasambandhapradarśa- nārtham 'atha khalu' ityetadupādānam | 'śānta upāsīta' iti vihitopāsanādhikṛtasyaiva upāsanāntaravidhānam anupapannam iti śaṅkāvyāvṛttyartham 'atha khalu' iti vākyaṃ ghaṭakatvenopāttam | jīvasya bahuvidhopāsanapracuratvāt anekopāsanavidhānaṃ yuktam - iti bhāvaḥ ||

(tajjatva-tasmillīyamānatva-tadāyattajīvanatvāni hetukṛtya) tādātmyasya

sarvaṃ khalvidamityatra prasiddhasyopadeśataḥ |
sarvātmā paramātmaiva manomaya udīryate ||

śrīmāṣyam 1-2-1]

atra - 'sa ṛtuṃ kurvīta' prāṇaśarīraḥ' iti nirdiśyate - iti pratīyate ||

iti pratipāditasya upāsanasya, upāsyaḥ 'manomayaḥ

'tatra saṃśayaḥ - kiṃ manomayaccādi guṇakaḥ', kṣetrajñaḥ ? uta paramātmā ? – iti ||

kiṃ yuktam ? kṣetrajñaḥ - iti | kutaḥ ?

manaḥprāṇayoḥ kṣetrajñopakaraṇatvāt paramātmanastu, 'aprāṇo hyamanāḥ'
iti tatpratiṣedhācca | na ca-
'sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ brahma'
iti pūrvanirdiṣṭaṃ' brahma, atra upāsyatayā sambaddhuṃ śakyate ;
'śānta upa sīta'

iti upāsanopakaraṇaśāntinirvṛttyupāyabhūtatrahmātmakatvopadeśāya upāttatvāt ||

na ca -

'sa ṛtuṃ kurvīta'

iti upāsanasya ' upāsyasākāṃṅgatvāt vākyāntarasthamapi brahma sambaddhyate - iti yuktaṃ vaktum ; svavākyopāttena manomayatvādiguṇakena nira| kāṅgatvāt ;

'manomayaḥ prāṇaśarīraḥ'

iti 'ananyarthatayā " nirdiṣṭasya vibhakti' vipariṇāma mātreṇa ubhayākāṅkṣānivṛttisiddheḥ |

manaḥprāṇayoriti | spaṣṭasya manaḥ sambandhamātrasya jīvaliṅgatvābhāvāt, jīvaliṅgabhūtasya upakaraṇopakaraṇitvalakṣaṇasambandhaviśeṣasya aspaṣṭatvācca asya pādasya aspaṣṭajīva- liṅgakavākyaviṣayatvam ityabhiprāyeṇa kṣetrajñopakaraṇatvāt ityuktam | na ca ...... śakyate iti · anyārthatayā nirākāṅkṣatvāt ityarthaḥ ||

svavākyopātteneti | vicchedakābhāvāt mahāvākyaikatvaṃ vivakṣitam | tataśca, 'eṣa 'ātmā' iti svavākyasthena ananyārthena samānaliṅgena ātmaśabdena viśeṣyākāṅkṣa śamanāt, vākyāntarasthānyārthabhinnaliṅgabrahmaśabdāpakarṣaḥ na yuktaḥ -- iti bhāvaḥ | manomaya iti | "prathamāntānāṃ manomayādipadānām upāsanavācipadena (upāsīna ) anvayāyogāt, 'brahma' padam antretavyam - ityatrottaramidam | 'ṛtu' śabdena dvitīyāntapadasyāpi ananvayāt paśyantatayā vibhaktivipariṇāme kārye, saṃnihitasya sākāṅkṣasyaiva padasya vibhaktivipariṇāmo nyāyyaḥ ; na tu vākyāntarasthasya nirākāṅakṣasya ityarthaḥ | ubhayākāṅakṣā - manomayādipadasya 'kaṃtu kurvīta ' ityasya ca ākāṅakṣā | siddheḥ iti pañcamyantena, na vaktuṃ yuktam ityasyānvayaḥ |

1 pūrvavākya nirdiṣṭaṃ - ā, vā 4 pariṇāma - ī.

upāsyākāṅkṣatvāt dha 3 abhyārthatayā - i.

' evaṃ ' niścite jīvatve,
" etadbrahma'

iti upasaṃhārasya 'brahmapadamapi, jīve eva pūjārtha prayuktam, ' ityadhyavasīyate' iti |

'hṛdā, manīṣā manasā'bhiklapto ya enaṃ viduramṛtāste bhavanti' 'na cakṣuṣā gṛhyate, nāpi vācā'

'manasā tu viśuddhena'

tathā,

'prāṇasya prāṇaḥ'

'atha khalu prāṇa eva prajñātmā, idaṃ śarīraṃ parigṛhyotthāpayati' sarvāṇi ha vā imāni bhūtāni prāṇamevā'bhisaṃviśanti, prāṇamabhyujihate'

ityādiṣu ||

manomayatvam – viśuddhena manasā grāhyatvam | prāṇaśarīratvam – 'prāṇasyāpyā'- dhāratvam, niyantṛtvaṃ ca | evaṃ ca sati,
'eṣa ma ātmā'ntarhṛdaye, etadbrahma'
iti 'brahma' śabdo'pi mukhya eva bhavati |
'aprāṇo hyamanāḥ'
iti, mana āyattaṃ jñānaṃ, prāṇāyattāṃ sthitiṃ ca brahmaṇaḥ niṣedhati ||
hṛdā manīṣā iti, manassāmānyena sambandhaviśeṣaparaṃ vākyam | 'hṛt' iti bhaktiḥ, 'manīṣā' iti dhṛtiśca ucyete | 'na sandṛśe tiṣṭhati' iti pūrvārdham ekarūpaṃ paṭhitvā, 'bhaktyā ca dhṛtyā ca samāhitātmā jñānasvarūpaṃ paripaśyatīha' iti mahābhārate uktatvāt | abhiklṛptaḥ - grāhyaḥ | na cakṣuṣā iti, manoviśeṣeṇa sambandhaviśeṣavācivākyam ||
prāṇasya prāṇaḥ – ādhāraḥ | 'sarvāṇi havā' ityādivākye 'prāṇa' śabdasya yaugika- vṛttyā paramātmaparatvaṃ prāguktam | idānīm aparyavasānavṛttyā'pi paramātmaparatvena tadvākyamupāttam | vaiśvānarādhikaraṇanyāyena ubhayathā'pi paramātmaparatvaṃ yuktam iti bhāvaḥ ||
grāhyatvamiti | grāhya grāhakalakṣaṇa sambandhaviśeṣābhiprāyeṇa manomayaḥ ityuktam ityarthaḥ | evaṃ ca iti upasaṃhārasvārasyamāha | mana āyattam ityādinā, uktārthāviruddha manaḥ- prabhṛtiniṣedhavākyārthamāha | grāhya grāhakabhāvaniyantṛ-niyāmyatvādirūpasambandhaviśeṣaḥ vidheyaḥ | upakaraṇopakara ṇitvalakṣaṇasambandhaviśeṣaḥ niṣedhyaḥ ityabhiprāyaḥ ||

athavā

'sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ brahma, tajjalāniti śānta upāsīta' iti atraiva upāsanaṃ vidhīyate 'sarvātmakaṃ brahma śāntaḥ san upāsīta' iti |

'sa ṛtuṃ kurvīta'

iti tasyaiva guṇopādānārthaḥ anuvādaḥ | upādeyāca guṇāḥ manomayatvādayaḥ | ataḥ 'sarvātmakaṃ brahma manomayatvādi guṇakam' upāsīta' iti vākyārthaḥ ||

'sarva' śabdanirdiṣṭaṃ hi brahmādistambaparyantaṃ kṛtsnaṃ jagat | brahmādibhāvaśca pratyagātmanaḥ anādyavidyāmūlakarmaviśeṣopādhikaḥ vidyata eva | parasya tu brahmaṇaḥ sarvajñasya sarvaśakteḥ apahatapāpmanaḥ nirastasamastāvidyādidoṣagandhasya samasta heyākara' sarvabhāvaḥ nopapadyate ||

iti nirdiṣṭe sarvasmin jagati, brahmaśabdena tadātmatayā ' ' vidhīyamānaṃ', paraṃ brahmaiva

na pratyagātmā |

kuta: ?

prasiddhopadeśāt – tajjalān iti hetutaḥ, 'sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ brahma' iti, prasiddhavat "upadeśāt ' | 'brahmaṇo jātatvāt, brahmaṇi 'līnatvāt', brahmādhīnajīvanatvācca hetoḥ | 'brahmātmakaṃ sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ jagat' ityukte, 'yasmājjagajjanma -sthiti-layāḥ vedānteṣu prasiddhāḥ, tadeva atra brahma' iti pratīyate ||

tacca parameva brahma | tathā hi- 'yato vā imāni bhūtāni jāyante | yena jātāni jīvanti | yatprayantyabhi- saṃviśanti | tadvijijñāsasva | tadbrahma' |

" ityupakramya',

'ānando brahmeti vyajānāt | ānandāddhayeva khalvimāni bhūtāni "jāyante "
ityādinā, pūrvānuvāka' pratipāditānavadhi kātiśayānandayoginaḥ vipaścitaḥ parasmāt "brahmaṇa eva, jagadutpattisthitilayāḥ nirddiśyante | tathā,

'sa kāraṇaṃ karaṇādhipādhipo na cāsya kacijjanitā na cādhipaḥ' iti, karaṇādhipasya jīvasyā'dhipaḥ paraṃ brahmaiva kāraṇaṃ vyapadiśyate | ' evaṃ ' sarvatra parasyaiva brahmaṇaḥ kāraṇatvaṃ prasiddham | ataḥ parabrahmaṇo jātatvāt tasmin 'pralīnatvāt ' tena prāṇanāt, tadātmakatayā tādātmyam upapannam ||

"muktasyā'pi ', anavacchinnasvarūpasyā'pi jagattādātmyaṃ, jagajjanma-sthiti- pralaya --- kāraṇatvanimittaṃ na sambhavati' iti,

'jagavyāpāravarjam'

ityatra vakṣyate |

jīvakarmanimittatvāt jagajjanma-sthiti-layānāṃ, sa eva kāraṇam-ityapi, na sādhīyaḥ ; tatkarmanimittatve'pi, īśvarasyaiva jagatkāraṇatvāt ||

ataḥ paramātmaiva atra 'brahma' śabdābhidheyaḥ | imameva sūtrārtham abhiyuktāḥ
" bahumanvate | yathāssha vṛttikāraḥ -
'sarvaṃ khalviti sarvātmā brahmaśa:' (bo vṛ)

iti || 1 ||

34. vivakṣitaguṇopapatteśca || 1-2-2 ||

jagajjanma sthitipralayakāraṇatvanimittam iti bahuvrīhiḥ | kāraṇatvaprayuktam ityarthaḥ | tatkarmanimittatve'pīti | na hi karma jagataḥ kartṛrūpanimittam upādānaṃ ca | ubhayam īśvara eva | karma tu kevalaṃ vaiṣamyahetuḥ | yathā ghaṭādinirmāṇasya bhoktṛkarmanimittatve'pi, bhoktṛkarmaṇāṃ na kartṛtvam upādānatvaṃ vā | kulāla - mṛtpiṇḍayoreva hi tadubhayam| 'tajjalān' ityatra tacchabdasya cana karmavācitvam | ataḥ na jīvasya jagatkāraṇatvakṛtaṃ tādātmyamasti ityabhiprāyaḥ | imamevetyādinā dvitīyayojanoktārthasyaiva sāṃpradāyikatvam | sarvamiti | sarva khalvityādivākyena sarvātmatayoktaṃ brahma, īśaḥ - paramātmā, ityarthaḥ ||

vakṣyamāṇāśca guṇāḥ, paramātmanyeva upapadyante

'manomayaḥ prāṇaśarīro 'bhārūpa: ' satyasaṅkalpaḥ ākāśātmā 'sarvakarmā sarvakāmaḥ

sarvagandhaḥ 'sarvarasaḥ sarvamidamabhyātto'vākyanādaraḥ' iti | manomayaḥ pariśuddhena manasaikena grāhyaḥ ; vivekavimokādisādhana saptakānu- gṛhītaparamātmopāsananirmalīkṛtena hi 'manasā gṛhyate ' | anena heyapratyanīkakalyāṇaika- tānatayā, sakaletaravilakṣaṇasvarūpatā ucyate, malinamanobhiḥ malinānāmeva grāhyatvāt | " prāṇaśarīraḥ "jagati sarveṣāṃ prāṇānāṃ dhārakaḥ | prāṇaḥ yasya śarīram - ādheyaṃ vidheyaṃ śeṣabhūtaṃ ca saḥ prāṇaśarīraḥ | ādheyatva - vidheyatva - śeṣaccāni śarīraśabda pravṛtti- 'nimittāni' – ityupapādayiṣyate | bhārūpaḥ- bhāsvararūpaḥ | aprākṛtasvā sādhāraṇaniratiśaya- "kalyāṇa' divyarūpatvena niratiśayadītiyuktaḥ ityarthaḥ | hatasaṅkalpaḥ | ākāśātmā - ākāśavat sūkṣmasvacchasvarūpaḥ

| -

satyasaṅkalpaḥ – aprati- sakaletara kāraṇabhūtasya
vivakṣitānāmeva vakṣya-

vivakṣitatvam - vaktumicchāviṣayatvam | vakṣyamāṇā iti | māṇatvāt, 'vivakṣita' śabdena vakṣyamāṇatvaṃ lakṣyate ityarthaḥ | ' sarva khalu' iti prathamavākyā- pekṣayā vakṣyamāṇaṃ hi anantaravākyagataguṇajātam | apauruṣeyavākyapratipādyaguṇānāṃ puruṣecchāpūrvakatvāyogāt kathaṃ vivakṣitatvam ? iti ) manomaya iti | niṣedhavākyavirodhaparihārāya sāmānyaṃ manomayaśabdaṃ viśeṣavākyānuguṇaṃ vyācaṣṭe | manasaikeneti | 'abbhakṣaḥ ' 'vāyubhakṣaḥ' itivat avadhāraṇagarbhaḥ iti 'eka' śabdasya bhāvaḥ | tacca 'na cakṣuṣā gṛhyate, nā'pi vācā' 'manasā tu viśuddhena' iti śrutyānuguṇyāt | malineti | malina manogrāhyatvaṃ heyātvavyāpakam | ataḥ vyāpaka nivṛttyā vyāpyanivṛttiriti ubhayaliṅga- | katvasiddhiḥ iti bhāvaḥ | muktasyā'pi viśuddhamanogrāhyatvaṃ na sambhavati | muktaṃ svātantryeṇa gṛhṇāti cet, manaḥ aśuddham | brahmaparyantatvena cet, viśiṣṭasya brahmaṇa eva viśuddha- manogrāhyatvam iti | bhārūpa iti | jñānasvarūpaḥ ityarthastu atikliṣṭa: | 'rūpa' śabdo hi dharmavācī | 'bhā' śabdo hi dīptiparaḥ | dīptidharmakatvaṃ vigrahadvārakam | 'ādityavarṇaṃ -tamasaḥ parastāt ' ' rukmavarṇam' ityādiśrutyaikārthyādityabhiprāyeṇa bhāsvararūpaḥ iti | sūkṣmatvam – vyāpyatvam | svacchatvam – nirmalatvam | sakaletyādinā, ākāśa eva

'ākāśasyāspi ' ātmabhūtaḥ - iti vā, ākāśātmā | svayaṃ ca prakāśate, anyānapi prakāśayati – iti vā, ākāśātmā | sarvakarmā – kriyate iti karma | sarvaṃ jagat yasya "karma asau sarvakarmā | sarvā vā kriyā yasya, asau sarvakarmā | sarvakāmaḥ- kāmyante iti kāmāḥ - bhogya-bhogopakaraṇādayaḥ ; te pariśuddhāḥ sarvavidhāḥ tasya santi- ityarthaḥ | sarvagandhaḥ sarvarasaḥ-

'aśabdama sparśam'

ityādinā prākṛtagandharasādiniṣedhāt, aprākṛtāḥ svāsādhāraṇāḥ 'niravadyāḥ niratiśayāḥ kalyāṇāḥ svabhogyabhūtāḥ sarvavidhāḥ gandharasāḥ tasya santi – ityarthaḥ | sarvamidamabhyāttaḥ 'ukta' rasaparyantaṃ sarvamidaṃ kalyāṇa' guṇajātaṃ svīkṛtavān | 'abhyāttaḥ' iti 'bhuktā brāhmaṇāḥ' itivat kartari ktaḥ pratipattavyaḥ | avākī, vākaḥ – uktiḥ; saḥ asya

nāsti iti abākī | kutaḥ ? ' ityāha' - anādaraḥ iti | avāpta samasta kāmatvena ādartavyābhāvāt ādararahitaḥ | ata eva avākī alpākaḥ | paripūrṇaiśvaryatvāt brahmādistambaparyantaṃ nikhilaṃ jagat tṛṇīkṛtya joṣamāsīnaḥ ityarthaḥ | 'te' ete vivakṣitāḥ "guṇāḥ paramātmanyeva upapadyante || 2 ||

-35. anupapattestu na śārīraḥ || 1-2-3 ||
tamimaṃ guṇasāgaraṃ paryālocayatāṃ, khadyotakalpasya, śarīrasambandhanibandhanā- parimitaduḥkhasambandhayogyasya, 'baddhamuktāvasthasya jīvasya' prastuta guṇaleśa' sambandhagandho'pi ' nopapadyate - iti, na asmin prakaraṇe śarīraparigrahaśaṅkā jāyate ityarthaḥ || 3 ||
so'sya nāstīti avākī, iti phalitārthakathanametat | kuta ityāheti | kutaḥ ityatrāha ityarthaḥ | avāpteti | avākatvaṃ vāksāmarthyābhāvāt — iti śaṅkāvyāvṛttyartham anādaraḥ iti padam iti bhāvaḥ | ajalpākaḥ iti niṣedhyo vākaḥ, heyarūpaḥ iti darśayati |

te ete iti sūtrārtham upasaṃharati ||

yogyasyeti yogyaśabdaḥ, śarīraśabdena muktāvasthasyā'pi kroḍīkāropa- pādakaḥ | 'anupapattestu netaraḥ' iti vaktavye'pi, muktāvasthasya jīvasya pratipādyatvānupapatti- sphoraṇārthaṃ śārīraśabdanirdeśaḥ iti bhāvaḥ | baddhopādānaṃ dṛṣṭāntatayā | yathā nigrāhyasya nigrāhaka svarūpaikyānupapattiḥ, tathā anugrāhyasyā'pi anugrāhaka svarūpaikyānupapattiḥ ityabhiprāyaḥ | prastutaguṇānupapattiḥ pratiguṇamanusandheyā | paryālocayatāṃ ... śaṅkā na jāyate ityanvayaḥ ||

36. karmakartṛvyapadeśācca || 1-2-4||
'etamitaḥ pretyā'bhisambhavitā'smi
iti prāpyatayā paraṃ brahma vyapadiśyate ; prāptṛtayā ca jīvaḥ | ataḥ prāptā jīvaḥ upāsakaḥ ; prāpyaṃ paraṃ brahma upāsyam - iti, 'prāptuḥ anyadevedam' iti 'vijñāyate ||4||

37. śabdaviśeṣāt || 1-2-5||
abhisambhavitāsmi prāptāsmi | ata iti | prāpya prāptārāveva hi upāsyopāsakau | ataḥ upāsyaṃ brahma, upāsakāt jīvāt vilakṣaṇam ityarthaḥ | svarūpasyaiva prāpyatvamiti cet ! śrutisvārasyabhaṅgaḥ | atra idam iti nirdeśa:, 'brahma' śabdābhihitavastvabhiprāyaḥ ||

śabda viśeṣāditi | avasthābhedena prāpyatva prāptṛtvopapattiriti śaṅkāparihārārtham, ekasyāmeva avasthāyāṃ bhedavyapadeśaṃ darśayati anantarasūtradvayena | asmin sūtre asamānavibhaktikanirdeśo vivakṣitaḥ |

'eṣa ma ātmā'ntarhṛdaye'
iti śārīraḥ ṣaṣṭyā nirdiṣṭaḥ ; upāsyastu prathamayā |

evaṃ samānaprakaraṇe, vājināṃ ca śrutau śabdaviśeṣaḥ śrūyate jīva - parayoḥ | yathā- 'śrīharvā yavo vā śyāmāko vā śyāmākataṇḍulo vā 'evamayamantarātman
puruṣo hiraṇmayaḥ ; yathā jyotiradhūmam'
iti | atra 'antarātman' iti saptamyantena, śārīro nirdiśyate | 'puruṣo hiraṇmayaḥ iti prathamayā upāsyaḥ | ataḥ para eva upāsyaḥ ||5||

'sarvasya cā'haṃ hṛdi saṃniviṣṭo mattaḥ smṛtirjñānamapohanaṃ ca' 'yo māmevamasaṃmūḍho jānāti puruṣottamam' 'īśvaraḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ hṛdeśe'rjuna tiṣṭhati |
bhrāmayan sarvabhūtāni yantrārūḍhāni māyayā ' ||
'tameva śaraṇaṃ gaccha'
iti 'śārīram' upāsakam, paramātmānaṃ ca upāsyaṃ smṛtiḥ darśayati || 6 ||

31 arbhaka kastvāttadvyapadeśācca neti cenna, nicāyyatvādevaṃ vyomavacca || 1-2-7||
sarvasyeti, 'eṣa ma ātmāntarhṛdaye' iti hṛdayaśabdapratyabhijñāpanārtham | yo mām iti tadanantaravākyam upāsyatvajñāpanārtham | īśvara iti hṛtsthasya īśvaratva kaṇṭhoktiḥ | 'tameva śaraṇaṃ gaccha' ityupādānaṃ ca, hṛdayasthasya bhajanapradarśanārtham | sarvasya ityatra sarvaśabdo jīvaviṣayaḥ ; parādhīnasmṛtyādyāśrayabhūtavastuviṣayatvāt | puruṣottama śabdavācyasya jīvavailakṣaṇyaṃ talaivoktam | sarvabhūtaśabdo'pi jīvaviṣaya: ; bhrāntyāśrayatvāt dehākhyayantrārūḍhatvācca | atra caśabdaḥ avāntaraśaṅkāparihārahetudvaya samuccayārthaḥ, anvācaye vā || 6 ||

'alpāyatanatvam' – arbhakaukastvam | tadvyapadeśaḥ – alpatvavyapadeśaḥ |

'eṣa ma ātmā'ntarhṛdaye' iti aṇīyasi hṛdayāyatane sthitatvāt,
'aṇīyān vrīhervā yavādvā'
ityādinā aṇīyastvasya svarūpeṇa vyapadeśācca, na ayaṃ paramātmā; api tu jīva eva | 'sarvagataṃ susūkṣmaṃ tadavyayaṃ yadbhūtayoniṃ paripaśyanti dhīrāḥ' ityādibhiḥ paramātmanaḥ aparicchinnatvāvagamāt jīvasya ca ārāgramātratvavyapadeśāt- iti cet,

naitadevam; paramātmaiva hi 'aṇīyān' ityevaṃ nicāyyatvena vyapadiśyate | evaṃ nicāyyatvena evaṃ draṣṭavyatvena - evam upāsyatvena, iti yāvat | na punaḥ aṇīyastvameva asya svarūpam - iti | vyomavacca ayaṃ "vyapadiśyate | svābhāvikaṃ mahattvaṃ ca, atraiva vyapadiśyate'
'jyāyān pṛthivyā jyāyānantarikṣāt jyāyān divo, jyāyānebhyo lokebhyaḥ'
iti | ataḥ 'upāsanārthameva ' alpatvavyapadeśaḥ | tathā hi

aṇī .... sthitatvāt – aṇīyastvākṣepakārthavyapadeśāt | aṇī.... vyapadeśācca- ṇīyastvasya vyapadeśāt |

'sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ brahma tajjalāniti śānta upasīta'
iti, 'sarvotpattipralayakāraṇatvena sarvasyātmatayā'nupraveśakṛta 'jīvayitṛtvena ca' sarvātmakaṃ brahma upāsīta' iti upāsanaṃ vidhāya,
'atha khalu kratumayaḥ puruṣaḥ | yathākraturasmiṃlloke puruṣo bhavati,
tathetaḥ pretya bhavati'
iti, yathopāsanaṃ prāpyasiddhimabhidhāya,
' sa kratuṃ kurvīta'
iti, guṇavidhānārtham upāsanam anūdya,
'manomayaḥ prāṇaśarīro bhārūpaḥ satyasaṅkalpaḥ ākāśātmā sarvakarmā sarvakāmaḥ sarvagandhaḥ sarvarasaḥ sarvamidamabhyātto'vākyanādaraḥ'
iti, jagadaiśvaryaviśiṣṭasya svarūpaguṇāṃca upādeyān pratipādya,
' epa ma ātmā'ntarhṛdaye aṇīyān vrīhervā yavādvā sarpapādvā śyāmākādvā śyāmākataṇḍulādvā'
iti upāsakasya hṛdaye, aṇīyastvena 'tadātmatayā' upāsyasya paramapuruṣasya upāsanārtham avasthānamuktvā,
' epa ma ātmā'ntarhṛdaye jyāyān pṛthivyā jyāyānantarikṣāt jyāyān divaḥ jyāyānebhyo lokebhyaḥ
sarvātmakamiti | sarvasya brahmātmakatvaṃ śāstrādavagamya, rāgadveṣādirahitaḥ san,
vakṣyamāṇaguṇakaṃ brahma upāsīta ityartha: | 'sarvātmakaṃ brahma' ityanena, upakramasya aṇīyastvaviro- dhitvaṃ darśitam | atha khalu ityādi, prakaraṇāvicchedadyotanāya | anūdyeti | yathā 'agnihotraṃ juhoti' iti vihita homa : 'dadhnā juhoti' ityatra dadhnaḥ homasādhanatva vidhānārtha 'juhoti' padena anūdyate, tathā – iti bhāvaḥ | jagadaiśvaryeti, 'tajjalān' ityasya anuvādaḥ | svarūpaguṇān - advārakaguṇān | na hi te prakṛti - puruṣadvārakā: |

'sarvakarmā sarvakāmaḥ sarvagandhaḥ sarvarasaḥ sarvamidamyātto'vākyanādaraḥ'

iti, antarhṛdaye avasthitasya 'upāsyamānasya' prāpyākāraṃ nirdiśya,
'eṣa ma ātmāta hṛdaye, etadbrahma'

iti, 'evambhūtaṃ paraṃ brahma, paramakāruṇyena, asmadujjijīva viṣayā, asmahṛdaye saṃnihitam ' itīdamanusandhānaṃ vidhāya,
'etamitaḥ pretyā'bhisambhavitā'smi'
iti, yathopāsanaṃ prāptiniścayānusandhānaṃ ca vidhāya,
' iti yasya syādaddhā na vicikitsā'sti'
iti, evaṃvidhaprāpya prāptiniścayopetasya upāsakasya, prāptau na saṃśayo'sti - ityupasaṃhṛtam | ataḥ upāsanārtham arbhakaukastvam aṇīyastvaṃ ca ||7||
40. sambhogaprāptiriti cenna vaiśeṣyāt || 1-2-8 || jīvasyeva, parasyā'pi brahmaṇaḥ śarīrāntarvartitvam abhyupagataṃ cet ! tadvadeva śarīrasambandhaprayuktasukhaduḥkhopabhogaprāptiḥ - iti cet,
evaṃbhūtamiti, 'nicāyyatvāt' ityasyārthamāha |
dviruktiḥ ; upāsyatayā prāpyatayā ca | hṛdi sthite:

sarvakarmatvādiguṇānāṃ

dviruktiḥ ; hṛdayasthitijñāpanārthe

saulabhyānusandhānārthaṃ ca| evam atra pūrvatra uttaratra ca vibhutvābhidhānāt, arbhako kamtva- rūpopādhikathanācca madhyagatam alpatvam aupādhikam - ityayamarthaḥ ukto bhavati ||

śarīrāntarvartitvam - hṛdayavartitvam |

'tanna'; hetuvaiśeṣyāt | na hi śarīrāntarvartitvameva sukha duḥkhopabhogahetuḥ' ; api tu - puṇya-pāparūpakarmaparavaśatvam | taca apahatapāpmanaḥ paramātmanaḥ na sambhavati | tathā ca śrutiḥ-

'tayoranyaḥ pippalaṃ svādvacyanaśnannanyo abhicākaśīti'

iti ||

|| iti sarvatraprasiddhyadhikaraṇam || 1 ||

13. attradhikaraṇam ||1-2-2 ||

atra odanopasecanamūcitaḥ attā, kiṃ 'jīva eva ' 1 uta paramātmā ? – iti

sandihyate ||

kiṃ yuktam ? jīvaḥ - iti | kuta: ?

bhoktṛtvasya karmanimittatvāt jīvasyaiva tatsambhavāt |
saṃśayākāraḥ— attā jīvaḥ, uta paramātmā ? - iti vicāraḥ | tadartham idam attatvaṃ kiṃ karmaphalabhoktṛtvarūpam ? uta jagadupasaṃhartṛtvarūpam ? kimatra pūrvaprastutabrahmaprakaraṇaṃ vicchinnam ? na ? - iti 'ṛtaṃ pibantau ' iti, kiṃ buddhijīvau nirddiśyete ? uta jīvaparau ! 'pibantau' iti nirdeśa: kiṃ chatrivat lakṣaṇayā, śabdaśaktyā vā prayojyaprayojakaviṣayatayā neyaḥ ? uta na :- iti | 'taṃ durdaśam ' ' yā prāṇena' iti jīvaparayoḥ darśanaṃ, kiṃ lakṣaṇam āpādayati, uta na, iti | upakrame 'yeyaṃ prete' iti praśnaḥ, kiṃ jīvaviṣayaḥ ? utaparaviṣaya: : iti |

pūrvapakṣe upakrame 'yeyaṃ prete' iti praśnaḥ yadā jīvaviṣaya: ; tadānīm upakramānu- rodhena, 'taṃ durdarśam' ityādinā ṛtapāna nirdeśasya prayojya prayojaka viṣayatāpādanāyogāt, 'ṛtaṃ pivantau' iti vākyasya buddhijīvaparatvena, brahmaprakaraṇaṃ vicchinnamiti, iha pratipannam attṛtvaṃ karmaphalabhoktatvarūpamiti, 'attā jīvaḥ' iti phalitam |

siddhānte—yadā yeyamiti praśnaḥ paraviṣaya: ; tadānīm upakramānuguṇyena 'taṃ durdarśa'- mityādinā ṛtapānanirdeśasya prayojya prayojaka viṣayatāpādanopapatteḥ, 'ṛtam ityādivākyasya jīva-paramātmaparatvena brahmaprakaraṇavicchedābhāvāt iha pratipannam attatvaṃ jagadupasaṃhartṛtvarūpameveti 'attā paramātmā' iti phalitam ||
bhoktṛtvasyeti | odanopasecanasāmarthyāvagatasyeti bhāvaḥ | jīvasyaiveti | 'sarva- bhūtāntarātmā'pahatapāpmā' 'na karmaṇā vardhate no kanīyān ' 'anaśnannanyaḥ' iti parasya bhoktṛtvaṃ niṣiddhamiti bhāvaḥ | tatsambhavāt — karmaphalabhoktṛtvasambhavāt | brahmakṣatraśabdavācyānāṃ dehināṃ strī-puṃsātmakatvāt tattadrūpajīvānāṃ parasparaṃ bhoktṛbhogyabhāvaḥ sambhavati | jīvānāṃ nityatvādeva mṛtyuvaśyatvābhāvāt mṛtyuḥ jīvasya vidheyaḥ ityabhiprāyeṇa mṛtyūpasecanavādo'pi sambhavatīti bhāvaḥ ||

atrocyate-attā carācaragrahaṇāt |

attā paramātmaiva |

kuta: ?

carācaragrahaṇāt – 'carācarasya kṛtsnasya' 'attatvaṃ' hi tasyaiva sambhavati | na ca - idaṃ karmanimittaṃ bhoktṛtvam api tu jagajjanmasthiti laya hetubhūtasya parasya brahmaṇo viṣṇoḥ saṃhartṛtvam ;
'so'dhvanaḥ pāramāpnoti tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam'

iti atraiva darśanāt | tathā ca,
'mṛtyuryasyopasecanam '

iti 'vacanāt',

'brahma ca kṣatraṃ ca '

iti, kṛtsnaṃ carācaraṃ jagat iha adanīyaudanatvena gṛhyate | upasecanaṃ hi nāma svayam adyamānaṃ sat, 'anyasyāpyadanahetuḥ | ataḥ upasecanatvena mṛtyorapi adyamānatvāt, " tadupasicyamānasya' kṛtsnasya brahmakṣatrapūrvakasya jagataḥ carācarasya adanam, atra vivakṣitamiti 'gamyate' | īdṛśaṃ ca adanam upasaṃhāra eva | tasmāt ' īdṛśa' jagadupa- saṃhāritvarūpaṃ bhoktṛtvaṃ paramātmana eva || 9 ||
na ceti | kṛtsnacarācaraviṣayam atṛttvaṃ jīvasya anupapannam iti bhāvaḥ | attṛttvaṃ viṣṇukartṛkaṃ carācarasaṃhartṛtvamityatra prakaraṇe paramātmavācipadaśravaṇaṃ prathamo hetu:, tathā ce tyādinā dvitīyo hetuḥ |
upasecanaṃ hīti | mṛtyuḥ upasecanam, 'abādhakaḥ' ityukte, viśeṣākāropasthāpanaṃ na syāt ; abādhakatvasya loṣṭatṛṇādisādhāraṇatvāt | svayam adyamānatve sati anyasyādana hetutvaṃ tu viśeṣākāra iti, tenaiva aupacārika prayogo yuktaḥ iti bhāvaḥ | atra vyutpādito nyāyaḥ- sādhāraṇākāraṃ vihāya avāntarākāreṇa gauṇārtho varṇanīyaḥ - iti | ata iti | kṛtsnajagatsaṃhāre mṛtyvapekṣā'bhāve'pi atra mṛtyoradyamānatvaṃ vyaṣṭisaṃhārasya taddvārakatvaṃ ca abhipretam | evaṃ mṛtyūpasecanatvasāmarthyāt brahmakṣatraśabdau kṛtsnacarācara pradarśanārthī iti bhāvaḥ |

kṛtsnasya–ī ± bhoktṛtvaṃda, attṛtvaṃ bhoktṛtvaṃ-tha hetubhūtasya - da darśanāt - bha • anyasyādana hetuḥ vā tataḥ upasicyamānasya - - tha

42. prakaraṇācca || 1-2-10||
prakaraṇaṃ ca idaṃ parasyaiva brahmaṇa: ;
'mahāntaṃ vibhumātmānaṃ matvā dhīro na śocati' 'nā'yamātmā pravacanena labhyo na medhayā na bahunā śrutena |
yamevaiṣa vṛṇute tena labhyastasyaiṣa ātmā vivṛṇute tanūṃ svām ||' " iti hi prakṛtam |

'ka itthā veda yatra saḥ'

ityapi hi tatprasādāhate tasya duravabodhatvameva ' pūrvaprastuta ' pratyabhijñāyate || 10 ||

'tatrocyate'

43. guhāṃ praviṣṭāvātmānau hi tadarśanāt || 1-2-11||

ṛtaṃ pibantau iti prakaraṇavicchedaṃ darśayati | liṅgāddhi prakaraṇaṃ durbalam | ṛtapānaliṅgabalāt prakaraṇavicchedo'tra pratipādyate | karmaphalabhoktureveti | vibhum ' ityādivākyānāṃ vyavahitatvāt vicchedo yuktaḥ ; na tu 'yasya brahma ca' ityādivākyena avyavahitasya 'ṛtaṃ pibantau' iti vākyasya iti bhāvaḥ | sadvitīyasyeti dvayoḥ sama- prādhānyaśaṅkāparihārārtham | karmaphalabhoga eveti | sarva saṃhartṛtvamucyate itivat, ṛtapānasya na hi nirvāhāntaraṃ suvacam iti bhāvaḥ | yathākathañcit – upakaraṇatvena | karaṇe vatṛtvopacārāt 'ṛtaṃ pibantau' ityuktam iti bhāvaḥ ||

'mahāntaṃ

sūtre, guha praviṣṭāviti 'yo veda nihitaṃ guhāyām' paramātmano guhā praveśaprasiddhiprācuryāta, buddhi prāṇayoḥ anyatarasya tādṛśaprasiddhyabhāvācca | sūtre 'hi' śabdaḥ, sautrārthasya paramasādhyopapādakatvasūcakaḥ iti bhāvaḥ |

na prāṇa jīva, buddhi- jīvau vā 'guhāṃ praviṣṭau ṛtaṃ pivantau' ityucyete; api tu jīva - paramātmānau hi tathā vyapadiśyete |

kutaḥ ?

taddarśanāt — asmin prakaraṇe jīva- parayoreva guhāpraveśavyapadeśo dṛśyate | paramātmanastāvat-
'taṃ durdarśa gūḍhamanupraviṣṭaṃ guhāhitaṃ gahvareṣṭhaṃ purāṇam |
adhyātmayogādhigamena devaṃ matvā dhīro harṣa - śokau jahāti ||' iti | jīvasyā'pi -

' yā prāṇena sambhavatyaditirdevatāmayī |
guhāṃ praviśya tiṣṭhantī yā bhūtebhirvyajāyata ||'
iti | karmaphalāni atti-iti, aditiḥ jīva ucyate | prāṇena saṃbhavati - prāṇena saha vartate | devatāmayī - indriyādhīnabhogā | guhāṃ praviśya tiṣṭhantī - hṛdayapuṇḍarīko- daravartinī | 'bhūtebhirvyajāyata - pṛthivyādibhibhūtaiḥ sahitā devādirūpeṇa vividhā jāyate |
evaṃ ca sati 'ṛtaṃ pibantau' iti vyapadeśaḥ, 'chattriṇo gacchanti' itivat pratipattavyaḥ | yadvā, prayojya - prayojakarūpeṇa pāne kartṛtvaṃ jīva- parayoḥ upapadyate || 11 ||
iti ityasya guhāpraveśavyapadeśo dṛśyate ityanenā'nvayaḥ | karmaphalānīti | 'aditi’ śabdasya devamātari, bhūmau ca vedaprasiddheḥ, tadvyāvṛttyarthamidam | rūḍhārthasya guhāpraveśāsambhavāt, yogaparigrahaḥ iti bhāvaḥ | 'karmaphala' śabdena ṛtaśabdārtho'pi darśito bhavati |
yadveti | 'chatriṇo gacchanti' iti nyāyena nirvāhe, lākṣaṇikatvaṃ syāditi, mukhya kalpa- nirvāha: | tatprayojako hetuśca ityuktaprakriyayā, paramātmanaḥ prayojaka- kartṛtāyāḥ kartṛtvaviśeṣatvāt, tadvācitve 'ṛtaṃ pibantau' iti nirdeśa : svarasaḥ iti bhāvaḥ | idaṃ ca nirdeśasvārasyam ubhayoścetanatve eva ghaṭate, 'ubhau gṛhaṃ praviṣṭau' iti nirdeśo hi ! na daṇḍasadvitīyapuruṣaviṣaya: ; api tu puruṣadvayaviṣayaḥ ||

atra śikṣitaṃ nyāyadvayam - 1) gatyabhāve yat amukhyam, tatra dvayoḥ pratipannayoḥ, prakaraṇānuguṇasya grāhyatvam | 2) sajātīyayoreva dvivacananirdeśārhatvaṃ ceti ||11||

44. viśeṣaṇācca || 1-2-12||

asmin prakaraṇe jīva-paramātmānāveva upāsyatvopāsakatvaprāpyatva prāptṛtvaviśiṣṭau sarvatra pratipādyete | tathā hi-
'brahmajña devamīḍyaṃ viditvā nicāyyemāṃ śāntimatyantameti' iti | brahmajajñaḥ - jīva; brahmaṇo jātatvāt 'jñatvācca' | taṃ devamīḍyaṃ viditvā- jīvātmānamupāsakaṃ brahmātmakatvena avagamya, ityarthaḥ | tathā,

'yaḥ seturījānānāmakṣaraṃ brahma yat param |
abhayaṃ titīrṣatāṃ pāraṃ nāciketaṃ śakemahi ||'
iti upāsyaḥ paramātmā ucyate | nāciketam - nāciketasya karmaṇaḥ prāpyam, ityarthaḥ | 'ātmānaṃ rathinaṃ viddhi śarīraṃ rathameva ca'

ityādinā, upāsako jīva ucyate | tathā,
'vijñānasārathiryastu manaḥ pragrahavān naraḥ |
so'dhvanaḥ pāramāpnoti tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam ||'
iti prāpya prāptārāvabhidhīyete jīva- paramātmānau | ihāpi 'chāyātapau' iti, ajñatva- sarvajñatvābhyāṃ tāveva viśiṣya vyapadiśyete |
upāsyatvetyādi | tattadvākyoditaviśeṣaṇāntarāṇi ca prāpyatvaprāptṛtvopāyatvopāsa- katvāntabhūtāni ityabhiprāyaḥ | brahmajajñamiti upāsakaṃ vadati | śakemahi iti | upāsiṃtu prāptum iti śeṣaḥ |

upāsaka iti | śarīrādyupāsanaparikarasahito jīva ucyate ityarthaḥ | ihāpi 'ṛtaṃ pibantau' iti vākye'pi | 'ajñatvasarvajñatvābhyām' ityanena 'jñājñau dvau' iti

atha syāt -

'yeyaṃ prete vicikitsā manuṣye astītyeke nāyamastīti caike' iti, jīvasvarūpayāthātmyapraśnopakrama (tvā) t, sarvamidaṃ prakaraṇaṃ jīvapara miti pratīyate - iti ||

naitadevam ; na hi jīvasya dehātiriktasya astitva nāstitva'śaṅkayā ayaṃ' praśnaḥ; tathā sati, pūrvavaradvayavaraṇānupapatteḥ |
tathā hi- pituḥ sarvavedasadakṣiṇaṛtusamāptivelāyāṃ dīyamānadakṣiṇāvaiguṇyena ṛtuvaiguṇyaṃ manyamānena kumāreṇa naciketasā āstikāgresareṇa, svātmadānenā'pi pituḥ ṛtusādguṇyamicchatā,

'kasmai māṃ dāsyasi ?'

iti, asakṛt pitaraṃ pṛṣṭavatā, svanirbandharuṣṭa' pitṛvacanāt mṛtyusadanaṃ praviṣṭena, svasadanāt propuṣi yame, tadadarśanāt tatra tisro rātrīḥ upopuṣā, stropavāsa bhītatatpratividhānapravṛtta- mṛtyu' pratte varatraye, āstikyā tirekāt prathamena vareṇa, svātmānaṃ prati pituḥ prasādo vṛtaḥ | etacca sarva dehātiriktamātmānam ajānataḥ nopapadyate |
vākyaṃ smāritam | udāhṛtavākyaikārthyāt 'chāyātapa' śabdayorapi ayamevā'rthaḥ | viśeṣaṇācca iti sūtre chāyā''tapatvarūpaviśeṣaṇamapi vivakṣitamiti bhāvaḥ ||
atha syāditi | asmin prakaraṇe jīva- paramātmanoḥ viśeṣyatvam ayuktam ; upakramasya jīvaparatvāt ityāśayaḥ |

'dvitīyena ca' vareṇa, uttīrṇadehātmānubhāvya phalasādhanabhūtā agnividyā vṛtā | tadapi dehātiriktātmānabhijñasya na sambhavati |

ataḥ tṛtīyena vareṇa yadidaṃ triyate

'yeyaṃ prete vicikitsā manuṣye astītyeke nāyamastīti caike | etadvidyāmanuśiṣṭastvayā'haṃ varāṇāmeṣa varastṛtīyaḥ || '
iti, atra paramapuṣārtharūpa brahmaprāptilakṣaṇamokṣayāthātmya vijñānāya, tadupāyabhūtaparamātmo- pāsanaparāvarātmatattvajijñāsayā ayaṃ praśnaḥ kriyate | evaṃ ca 'yeyaṃ prete' iti, na śarīra- viyogamātrābhiprāyam; api tu sarvabandhavinirmokṣābhiprāyam | yathā-

'na pretya saṃjñāssti'

iti | ayamarthaḥ –mokṣādhikṛte manuṣye prete - sarvabandhavinirmukte, tatsvarūpaviṣayā vādi- vipratipattinimittā asti - nāstyātmikā yeyaṃ vicitsā, tadapanodanāya, tatsvarūpa- yāthātmyaṃ tvayā anuśiṣṭaḥ ahaṃ vidyām – jānīyām, iti ||

tathā hi bahudhā vipratipadyante-

1. kecit - vittimātrasya ātmanaḥ svarūpocchittilakṣaṇaṃ mokṣamācakṣate | pitṛprasādavaraṇasya, agnividyāvaraṇasya ca pratyeka nirapekṣa hetutvasūcanārtham, etacca sarvaṃ, tadapi iti dviruktam |
atreti | yadidaṃ triyate, yat varaṇaṃ kriyate tatra varaṇe ityarthaḥ | mokṣa .... kriyate iti | mokṣajñānāya mokṣānubandhijijñāsayā ayaṃ praśnaḥ ityarthaḥ | ' yeyam' ityādipraśnavākye mokṣa- svarūpapraśnaḥ kaṇṭhoktaḥ | prativacanaprakāreṇa upāsanādipraśnaśca arthasiddhaḥ ityavagamyate | evaṃ vibhāgajñāpanārthaṃ vijñānāya, jijñāsayā iti pṛthagukti: | mokṣaprāptipraśna prāpya prāpaka- prāpta praśno'pi antargataḥ iti bhāvaḥ darśito mavati | asti - nāstyātmikā – 'asti nā'sti' ityevamādirūpā | jānīyāmiti 'vidyām' iti padasya dvitīyāntakārakapadatvaśaṅkā- nivartanam ||

1. kecit - bauddha-mādhyamikā : |

2. anye - vittimātrasyaiva sataḥ avidyā'stamayam |
3. apare – pāṣāṇakalpasyātmanaḥ jñānādyaśeṣavaiśeṣikaguṇocchedalakṣaṇaṃ kaivalya- rūpam |

4. apare tu – apahatapāpmānaṃ paramātmānam abhyupagacchantaḥ, tasyaiva upādhi- saṃsarganimittajīvabhāvasya, upādhyapagamena tadbhāva lakṣaṇaṃ mokṣamātiṣṭhante' |

5. trayyanta niṣṇātāstu – nikhilajagadekakāraṇasya, aśeṣa heya pratyanīkānānta- jñānānandaikasvarūpasya svābhāvikānavādhikātiśayāsaṅkhyeyakalyāṇaguṇākarasya, sakaletara- vilakṣaṇasya sarvātmabhūtasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ, śarīratayā prakārabhūtasya, anukūlāparicchinna- "jñānasvarūpasya', paramātmānubhavaikarasasya, jīvasya, anādikarmarūpā vidyātirohitasvarūpasya, avidyocchedapūrvakasvābhāvika paramātmānubhavameva mokṣam - ācakṣate |
tatra 'mokṣa svarūpaṃ, tatsādhanaṃ ca tvatprasādāt vidyām' iti naciketasā pṛṣṭo mṛtyuḥ, tasyārthasya duravabodhatvapradarśanena, vividhabhogavitaraṇapralobhanena ca enaṃ parīkṣya, yogyatām abhijñāya, parāvarātmatattvavijñānam, paramātmopāsanam, tatpadaprāptilakṣaṇaṃ mokṣaṃ ca,
2. anye – śāṅkarāḥ |
3. apare – tārkikāḥ | ( pāṣāṇakalpasyeti | ātmanaḥ svarūpataḥ jñānatvā- bhāvāt pāṣāṇakalpateti bhāvaḥ |] vaiśeṣikaguṇāḥ – viśeṣaguṇāḥ, buddhisukhaduḥkhecchādveṣa- prayatnadharmādharmabhāvatāḥ |

'taṃ durdarśa gūḍhamanupraviṣṭam'

ityāramya,

'so'dhvanaḥ pāramāpnoti tādvaṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam'
ityantena upadiśya, tadapekṣitāṃśca viśeṣān upadideśa – iti sarvaṃ samañjasam | ataḥ 'paramātmaiva attā' iti siddham || 12 ||

14. antarādhikaraṇam || 1-2-3 ||
45. antara upapatteḥ || 1-2-13 ||

'idamāmananti ' cchandogāḥ-

'ya epokṣiṇi puruṣo dṛśyate | eṣa ātmeti hovāca etadamṛta- ' metadbhaya' metadbrahma'

iti ||

4) atha paramātmā ? - iti ||

kiṃ yuktam ?

1) pratibimbātmā - iti |

|

kutaḥ ?

prasiddhavanirdeśāt, 'dṛśyate' iti aparokṣā bhidhānācca |

2) jīvātmā vā tasyā'pi hi cakṣupi viśeṣeṇa saṃnidhānāt, prasiddhiḥ upapadyate | unmīlita hi cakṣuḥ udvīkṣya, jīvātmanaḥ śarīre sthiti gatī niścinvanti | 3) 'raśmibhireṣo'smin pratiṣṭhitaḥ'
uta na ?–iti | kiṃ madhyasthāgnividyayā brahmaprakaraṇaṃ vicchinnam ? uta na ? kim agnividyā brahmavidyāṅgam ? uta na ? kim agnividyāphalaṃ brahmavidyāṅgatvānuguṇam ? uta na ? – iti vicāraḥ |

atra pūrvapace phalitam - yadā agnividyā phalaṃ brahmavidyāṅgatvānanuguṇam ; tadā agnividyāyāḥ brahmavidyāṅgatvābhāvena, brahmaprakaraṇa vicchedena, pūrvaprakṛtatrahmaṇaḥ iha parāmarśābhāvena amṛtatvādī- nāma mukhyatayā prativimbādiṣvanyatamaḥ - iti ||
siddhānte phalitam - yadā agnividyāphalaṃ brahmavidyānuguṇam ; tadā aṅgatvenā'gnividyāyāḥ brahmavidyāprakaraṇa vicchedakatvābhāvena prakṛtabrahmaṇaḥ iha parāmarśasambhavena, amṛtatvādīnāṃ ca svataḥ- prāpta mukhyatvasiddheḥ paramātmā iti phalitam ||
pratibimba-jīva- ādityānāṃ prasiddhaya: ; aparokṣaprasiddhiḥ, yauktikaprasiddhiḥ, śruti- prasiddhiśceti yathākramamanusandheyāḥ |

prasiddhavanirdeśaḥ 'ya, eṣa:' iti padābhyām |

sannidhānāt — saṃnidhānāvagamāt | jīvasya dehasaṃnidheḥ cakṣuṣi avagamāt ityarthaḥ | unmīlitaṃ ... niścinvanti iti | yathā ‘assin karmaṇi, asya sāmarthyaṃ dṛṣṭam' ityukte, 'tatkarma, tatsāmarthya sūcakam' ityuktaṃ bhavati, evam 'akṣaṇi dṛśyate' ityukte, 'dehe jīvāvasthānaṃ cakṣuradhīna niścayam' ityuktaṃ bhavati ityarthaḥ | anyat upakramānuguṇaṃ neyam | tasmāt amṛtatva- saṃyadvāmatvādyuktiḥ stutirūpā | ' eṣa ātmeti' iti 'iti' karaṇaṃ ca arthāt tātparyadyotakam |

iti śruti' prasiddhā, cakṣuḥpratiṣṭhaḥ | devatāviśeṣo vā | eṣveva prasiddhavanirdeśopapatteḥ, eṣāmanyatamaḥ-

eti prāpte, pracakṣmahe – antara upapatteḥ |

akṣyantaraḥ paramātmā |

kuta: ?

'eṣa ātmeti hovāca | etadamṛta' metadbhaya' metadbrahmati' 'etaṃ 'saṃyadvāma' ityācakṣate ; etaṃ hi sarvāṇi vāmānyabhisaṃyanti' 'eṣa u eva vāmanīḥ ; eṣa hi sarvāṇi vāmāni nayati' 'eṣa u eva bhāmanīḥ : epa hi sarveṣu lokeṣu bhāti ityeṣāṃ guṇānāṃ paramātmanyeva upapatteḥ || 13 ||

46 sthānādivyapadeśācca ||1-2 - 14|| 'cakṣupi sthiti' - niyamanādayaḥ paramātmana eva,
'yaścakṣupi tiṣṭhan'
'ityevamādau vyapadiśyante | ataśca,

devatāviśeṣaḥ – ādityaḥ | eṣveveti | na tu paramātmani ityarthaḥ ||
rāddhānte eṣāṃ guṇānāmiti | nirupādhikāḥ amṛtatvādayaḥ, na jīvādiṣu sambhavanti ityarthaḥ | upakramasthamekaṃ liṅgam uparitanānekaguṇavirodhe durbalam | ata eva ca na amṛtatvādyuktiḥ stutiḥ iti bhāvaḥ | ' iti hovāca' ityādiṣu 'iti' karaṇam arthaparameva dṛṣṭam iti cānusandheyam | upapatteḥ akṣyantaraḥ paramātmā ityanvaya: ||13||

yaścakṣupīti śrutyā ca sthānādivyapadeśataḥ |
niyantṛtvāmṛtatvādiguṇebhyaśca paraḥ pumān ||

" ya eṣo'kṣiṇi puruṣaḥ'

iti, sa eva pratīyate | ataḥ prasiddhavanirdeśaśca paramātmani upapadyate | tata eva, 'dṛśyate' iti sākṣātkāravyapadeśo'pi, yogibhiḥ dṛśyamānatvāt upapadyate || 14||
47. sukhaviśiṣṭābhidhānādeva ca || 1-2 - 15|| itaśca - akṣyādhāraḥ, puruṣottamaḥ ;
'kaṃ brahma khaṃ brahma'

iti prakṛtasya sukhaviśiṣṭasya brahmaṇaḥ upāsanasthāna vidhānārtha, saṃyadvāmatvādiguṇa- vidhānārthaṃ ca,

' ya eṣo'kṣiṇi puruṣaḥ'
ityabhidhānāt | " evakāraḥ nairapekṣyaṃ hetoḥ dyotayati ||
sthitīti | ' anavasthite:' iti vyatirekasya vakṣyamāṇatvāt, 'cakṣuṣi tiṣṭhan' iti śrutyanurodhena, 'sthāna' śabda: sthitiparatayā vyākhyātaḥ ; na pradeśaparatayā sautrasya ādiśabdasya vyākhyānaṃ niyamaneti | atra 'ādi' śabdena dhārakatvādirabhipretaḥ | tata eva śrutiprasiddhereva | śāstrapratipannaṃ hi tatvaṃ yogibhirupāsyaṃ dṛśyaṃ ca ; 'yattadadreśyam' ityuktvā, ' paripaśyanti dhīrā:' ityukteḥ || 14||

sukhaviśiṣṭam - sukhatvaviśiṣṭam ; vyakayo: itivat | ' tadguṇasāratvāt iti nyāyena, dharmabhūtasūkha viśiṣṭam iti vā |
upāsana.... bhidhānāditi | 'ke brahma' ityuktasya brahmaṇaḥ, 'etadamṛtametadbhayametadbrahma' iti, 'brahma' śabdena pratyabhijñānāt ityarthaḥ | nairapekṣyamiti | pūrvasūtroktaṃ hetudvayaṃ, vyati- rekoktisāpekṣam ; 'anavasthiterasaṃbhavāt ' iti hi vakṣyate | ayaṃ tu hetu:, vyatirekoktinirapekṣaḥ iti bhāva: |

kaṃ brahmeti sukhāḍhyasya dhyānārthamiha cākṣaṇi |
abhidhānāt sthiteratra hyakṣyantasthaḥ paraḥ pumān ||

nanu – agnividyāvyavadhānāt 'kaṃ brahma' iti prakṛtaṃ brahma, na iha saṃnidhatte |

tathā hi ; agnayaḥ,

'prāṇo brahmakaṃ brahma khaṃ brahma'
iti brahmavidyām upadiśya,

'atha hainaṃ gārhapatyo'nuśaśāsa' ityārabhya, agnīnāmupāsanam upadidiśuḥ |
na ca - agnividyā brahmavidyāṅgam - iti 'śakyaṃ vaktum' ;
brahmavidyā'phalānantargata' tadvirodhi sarvāyuḥprāpti-santatya vicchedādiphalaśravaṇāt ||

'vyādhibhiḥ pratipūrṇo'smi'

'iti, brahmaprāpti 'vyatiriktanānāvidhakā mopahatipūrvaka garbha janma-jarā-maraṇādi bhavabhayopa- taptāya' upakosalāya,
'eṣā somya te asmadvidyā ātma vidyā ca'
iti samuccityopadeśāt, mokṣaikaphalātmavidyāṅgatvam agnividyāyāḥ pratīyate | evaṃ ca aṅgatve avagate sati, 'phalānukīrtanam arthavādaḥ 'iti gamyate ' ||

na ca – atra mokṣavirodhiphalaṃ kiñcit śrūyate ; 'ahapate pāpakṛtyām | lokibhavati | sarvamāyureti | jyok jīvati | nā'syāvarapuruṣāḥ kṣīyante | upa vayaṃ taṃ bhuñjāmo'smiṃśca loke'muṣmiśca' ityamīṣāṃ phalānāṃ mokṣādhi' kṛtasya anuguṇatvāt | apahate pāpakṛtyām - brahma- prāptivirodhi pāpaṃ karma ' apahanti' | lokī bhavati tadvirodhini pāpe niraste, brahma- lokaṃ prāpnoti | sarvamāyureti brahmopāsana' samāpteḥ ", yāvat āyuḥ apekṣitam, tatsarvam eti | jyok jīvati - "vyādhyādibhiḥ" anupahataḥ, yāvadrahmaprāpti jīvati | na asya avarapuruṣāḥ kṣīyante - asya śiṣya - praśiṣyādayaḥ, 'putra-pautrādayo'pi brahmavida eva bhavanti |

'nāssyābrahmavit kule bhavati'
iti ca śrutyantare brahmavidyāphatvena śrūyate | upa vayaṃ taṃ bhuñjāmoḍa smiṃśca' loke'muṣmiṃśca, vayam - agnayaḥ, tam enam upabhuñjāma: - yāvadbrahmaprāptivighnebhyaḥ paripālayāma iti ||

ataḥ agnividyāyāḥ brahmavidyāṅgatvena tatsannidhānāvirodhāt, sukhaviśiṣṭaṃ prakṛta- ' upāsana sthānavidhānārthaṃ ; guṇavidhānārthaṃ 'cocyate ||

meva brahma,

nanu -

'ācāryastu te gatiṃ vaktā'
iti gatimātra pariśeṣaṇāt ācāryeṇa gatireva upadeśyā, iti gamyate | tatkathaṃ sthāna- guṇavidhyarthatā ucyate ?

tadabhidhīyate-

'ācaryastu te gatiṃ vaktā'

ityasya, ayamabhiprāyaḥ – brahmavidyām anupadiśya propupi gurau tadalābhāt anāśvāṃsam upakosalam ujjīvayituṃ, svaparicaraṇaprītāḥ gārhapatyādayaḥ guroragnayaḥ, tasmai brahma- svarūpamātraṃ, tadaṅgabhūtāṃ ca agnividyāmupadiśya,
nā'syeti, kule brahmavidutpatteḥ brahmavidyānuguṇaphalatvaṃ darśayati | bhukhāma iti | 'bhuja pālanābhyavahārayoḥ ' iti dhāto: 'bhujo'navane' iti hi ātmanepadam | ataḥ parasmaipaditvān ayaṃ dhātuḥ, avanārtha ityarthaḥ | paripālayāma iti | śrutau 'upa'śabdārthaḥ, 'pari'ṇā upasargeṇa sūcitaḥ | vighnanivāraṇaparyantaḥ kiñcitkāraḥ brahmavidyāṃ prati agrividyāyāḥ aṅgatvanirvāhakaḥ ityabhiprāya: ||
nanviti | sthāna- guṇayoḥ vidheyatve sati hi tadarthaṃ punarbahmaṇo'bhidhānam | tattu nā'sti ; pūrvaṃ tayoḥ ācāryeṇa vakṣyamāṇatvākathanāt | ataḥ sthānādiparavākyaṃ na paramātmaviṣayamiti; anyaparamidaṃ vākyam ityarthaḥ |

"ācāryādvaiva' vidyā viditā sādhiṣṭhaṃ 'prāpat"
iti śrutyartha' mālocya', 'sādhutamatvaprātyartham ācārya eva asya saṃyadvāmatvādiguṇakaṃ brahma, tadupāsanasthānam, arcirādikāṃ ca gatim upadiśatu '

iti matvā,

'ācāryastu te gatiṃ vaktā'
ityavocan | 'gati' grahaṇam upadeśya vidyāśeṣapradarśanārtham | ata eva ' ācārtho'pi, 'ahaṃ tu te tadvakṣyāmi, yathā puṣkarapalāśa āpo na lipyante
evam evaṃvidi pāpaṃ karma na śliṣyate'

ityupakramya, saṃyadvāmatvādikalyāṇaguṇaviśiṣṭaṃ brahma, akṣisthānopāsyam, 'arcirādikāṃ ca' gatim upadideśa | ataḥ,

'kaṃ brahma khaṃ brahma'

iti, sukhaviśiṣṭasya prakṛtasyaiva brahmaṇaḥ, ' atrā'bhidhānāt ", ayam akṣyādhāraḥ paramātmā || 15||

nanu ca-

'kaṃ brahmakhaṃ brahma'

iti paraṃ brahmābhihitam iti kathamavagamyate ? yasya iha akṣyādhāratayā abhidhānaṃ brūpe yāvatā -
sādhutamatva śabdena sādhiṣṭhaśabda: vyākhyātaḥ | 'sādhiṣṭham' iti bhāvapradhāno nirdeśaḥ ityarthaḥ | gatigrahaṇamiti | 'upadeśyavidyāśeṣam ācāryo baktā' iti, ‘ācāryastu ne gatiṃ vaktā' iti badatāmabhiprāyaḥ ityarthaḥ | saṃyadvāmatvādīti | saṃyanti - saṅgatāni, vāmāni - bananīyāni, prārthanī- yāni yasmin, saḥ —saṃyadvāmaḥ | sarvakalyāṇaguṇāśrayatvaṃ satyasaṅkalpatvaṃ vā saṃyadvāmatvam | bāmanītvam – svāśriteṣu śobhanaprāpakatvam | bhāmanītvam sarvalokavyāptadīptimadvigrahayuktatvam | ataḥ — upakosalaviṣādaparihārasya apekṣitatvāt, gurumukhācchravaṇena sādhutamatvāya apuṣkalamupadiṣṭatvācca ‘gati’grahaṇasya pradarśanārthatvāt ityarthaḥ || 15 ||

48. ata eva ca sa brahma || 1-2-16||
yatastatra, 'yadeva kaṃ, tadeva kham' iti 'sukhaviśiṣṭasya ākāśasyā ''bhidhānam, ata eva 'kha' śabdābhidheyaḥ saḥ - ākāśaḥ paraṃ brahma' | etaduktaṃ bhavati - agnibhiḥ,

'prāṇo brahmakaṃ brahmakhaṃ brahma'
ityukte, upakomala uvāca -

' vijānāmyayaṃ yat prāṇo brahma, kaṃ ca tu khaṃ ca na vijānāmi'

iti ||

śrī mādhyam 1.2.2]

anyonyavyavacchedaka ve— 'a ricchiyānandeka' svarūpatvaṃ brahmaṇaḥ syāt - iti, anyatara prakāra nirdidhaḥ rayipayā,
'kaṃ ca tu khaṃ ca na vijānāmi'

ityuktavān |

upakosalasya imam āśayaṃ jānantaḥ agnayaḥ,

'yadvāva kaṃ tadeva khaṃ, yadeva deva kam)
ityucire | brahmaṇaḥ sukharūpatvameva aparicchinnam irthaḥ | ataḥ - prāṇaśarīra tathā | prāṇaviśiṣṭaṃ yat brahma, tadeva apariṣṭisukharūpaṃ ca - iti nigamitam ;
'prāṇaṃ ca hasmai tadākāśaṃ cacuḥ'

khaṃ

iti | ataḥ,

'ka brahmakha brahma'

ityatra, aparicchinna sukhaṃ brahmapratipāditamiti paraṃ brahmaiva tatra prakṛtam | tadeva ca atra akṣyādhāratayā 'abhidhīyate iti, akṣyādhāraḥ paramātmā || 16 ||
upakosalasyetyādi | 'yadvāva kaṃ tadeva khaṃ, yaṃdeva khaṃ tadeva kam' ityatra na dṛṣṭividhitvaṃ śaṅkanīyam ; tathā sati 'kaṃ ca tu khaṃ ca na vijānāmi' ityasya uttaratvāyogāt | na hi arthāntaradṛṣṭivivyajñānasya, dṛṣṭividhyantaram uttaraṃ bhavati — ityabhiprāyaḥ |

evaṃ ketu vijñānāmi' iti ajñānavacanāt mumukṣorupadiṣṭatvāt arthāntarājānaṃ prati, dṛṣṭavidhyantavya ucaratvāyogācca 'yadvāva kam' ityādivākyasya dṛṣṭividhiparatvānupapatteḥ, anyonyavyavacchedapatim | 'asmadvidyā cāss vidyā ca' iti agnīnāṃ vacane pūrvaprakṛta- vidyāyāḥ ātmavidyāgamācca brahmaṇo dṛṣṭiviśeṣaṇatvaśaṅkā nirastā | praṇaṃ ceti | upasaṃhārasya upakramānuguṇyasya nyāyyatvā | 'prāṇaṃ ca' ityupasaṃhāravākyam uktārthaparam ityarthaḥ | 'prāṇaṃ ca' iti, 'prāṇo brahma' ityuktārthanimana| 'tadākāśe ca' iti 'yadvāva kam' ityādivākyārthanigamanam | 'tadākāśam' ityatra prakṛtaparāmarśinā tacchabdena sukharūpatvamabhipretam | tadeva ceti | anyathā vākyabhedaśca prasajet ; sambhavaḥnekavākyatve' vākyabhedo'nupapannaḥ iti bhāvaḥ | itīti | 'kaṃ brahma' iti prakṛtasyaiva akṣyādhāratvapratipādanāt akṣipuruṣa: paramātmā - ityarthaḥ || 16 || 3 vidhīyate - dha

49. śrutopaniṣatkagatyabhidhānācca || 1-2-17 ||
śrutopaniṣatkasya – adhigata para napuruṣayāthātmyasya, anusandheyatayā śrutyantaraprati - pādyamānā arcirādikā gatiḥ yā; tām apunarāvṛttilakṣaṇaparama puruṣaprāptikarīm, upakosalāya 'akṣipurupaṃ śrutavate,
"te'rciṣamevā'bhi saṃbhavanti acipo'haḥ ahnaḥ āpūryamāṇapakṣam

ityārabhya,

'candramaso vidyutam tatpuruṣo'mānavaḥ'
' sa enān brahma gamayati | eṣa devapatho brahmapathaḥ | etena 'pratipadyamānā ' imaṃ mānavamāvate nāssvartante'
ityante' nopadiśati' | ato'pi, ayam akṣipuruṣaḥ paramātmā || 17 ||
śrutopaniṣatkasya iti, vyākhyeyaṃ paraṃ samastamapi vyākhyānārthaṃ vyasya nirdiṣṭam | adhigatetyādi, upanipatyu pratīkopāsanānā arcirādigatirahitānāmapi vidyamānatvāt, tadvyāvṛttyartham | paramapuruṣaśabdena, 'taṃ tvaupaniṣadaṃ puruṣam' iti śrutiḥ smārilā | śrutyantarapratipādyamāneti | chāndogya eva pradeśāntare — ' tadya itthaṃ viduḥ' ityādiḥ, vājasaneyake—'ya evametadviduḥ ityādiḥ, kauṣītakīnāṃ paryaṅkavidyāyāṃ - 'so'gnilokam ' ityādiḥ ; tābhiḥ pratipādyata ityarthaḥ | gaticintanasya brahmavidyāṅgatvajñāpakaśrutyantarajñāpa- nārthaṃ śrutopaniṣatkatvavyapade ityabhiprāyaḥ | apunarāvṛttīti | akṣipuruṣasya abrahmatve, | | tatkratunayavirodhaḥ ityani yaḥ | 'arciṣameva ' ityataḥ pūrvam 'atha yadu caivā'smin....yadu cana' iti vākyam | asminniti ekavacanaṃ jātyabhiprāyam; 'te'rciṣam' iti bahuvacanā- nurodhāt | pañcānā gatiḥ ihānūditā apunarāvṛttividhānārtham ||

ācāryeṇa upadeśa cāpakāt (akṣyantaḥ puruṣaḥ, paraṃ brahmaiva ; na jīvaḥ) ||]

śrutopaniṣadāṃ yā ca gatiruktā'rcirādikā |
saivā'tropāsakasyoktetyato'pi sa paraḥ pumān ||

50. anavasthiterasambhavācca netaraḥ || 1-2-18||
pratibimbādīnām akṣaṇi niyamenā'navasthānāt, amṛtatvādīnāṃ ca nirupādhi- kānāṃ teṣvasambhavāt na paramātmana itara : chāyādiḥ akṣipuruṣo bhavitumarhati |

prativimbasya tāvat - puruṣāntarasannidhānāyattatvāt, na niyamenāvasthānasambhavaḥ | jīvasyā'piṃ—- sarvendriyavyāpārānuguṇatvāya sarvendriya kandabhūte sthānaviśeṣe vṛttiriti, cakṣuṣi na avasthānam |

devatāyāśca-

'raśmibhireṣo'smin pratiṣṭhitaḥ'
iti raśmidvāreṇā'vasthānavacanāt deśāntarāvasthitasyā'pi indriyādhiṣṭhānopapatteḥ ;

na cakṣuṣyavasthānam | sarveṣāmeva eṣāṃ nirupādhikāmṛtatvādayaḥ na sambhavantyeva | tasmāt akṣipuruṣaḥ paramātmā || 18 ||

śrīmāṇyam 1-2-4]

15 antaryāmyadhikaraṇam ||1-2-4||

'sthānādivyapadeśācca'

ityatra,

'yacakṣuṣi tiṣṭhan '

ityādinā pratipādyamānaṃ cakṣupi sthiti-niyamanādikaṃ paramātmana eva – iti siddhaṃ kṛtvā akṣipuruṣasya paramātmatvaṃ sādhitam || idānīṃ tadeva samarthayate -
51 antaryāmyādhadaivādhilokādiṣu taddharmavyapadeśāt || 1.2.19||

16. adṛśyatvādiguṇakādhikaraṇam || 1-2-5 ||

54. adṛśyatvādiguṇako dharmokteḥ || 1-2-22||

ātharvaṇikāḥ adhīyate-

'atha parā yayā tadakṣaramadhigamyate' 'yattadadreśyamagrāhyamagotramavarṇamacakṣuśśrotraṃ tadapāṇipādam | nityaṃ vibhuṃ sarvagataṃ susūkṣmaṃ tadavyayaṃ yadbhūtayoniṃ paripaśyanti dhīrā:' (muṇḍa.u. 1-1-6 ⟩ iti | tathā uttaratra -

'akṣarātparataḥ paraḥ'

iti ||

tatra sandihyate—kimiha adṛśyatvādiguṇakamakṣaram, 'akṣarātparataḥ paratha' ; prakṛti - puruṣau ? atha ubhayatra paramātmaiva ? – iti |
athetyādi | adṛśyatvādiguṇakaśabdena akṣaram, pulliṅganirdeśena akṣarātparataḥ paraśca dvayamapi viṣayīkriyate ityabhiprāyeṇa vākyadvayamapi viṣayatayā upāttavān | adreśyam- jñānendriyāviṣayaḥ | agrāhyam - pāṇyādikārya hānopādānādyaviṣayaḥ (śru pra ) ||
kimiheti | adṛśyatvādiguṇakā kṣarasya prakṛtitvam, akṣarātparataḥ parasya jīvatvaṃ ca śaṅkayate ; na tu ubhayorubhayātmakatvam | ubhayatra vākyadvaye'pi |

'kiṃ tāvat prāptam' ?

prakṛti - puruṣau – iti |

kuta: ?

asya akṣarasya,

'adṛṣṭo draṣṭā'
ityādāviva na draṣṭṛtvādiḥ cetana dharmaviśeṣaḥ ", iha śrūyate |
'akṣarātparataḥ paraḥ'

iti ca, sarvasmādvikārātparabhūtāt akṣarādasmātparaḥ 'kṣetrajña' samaṣṭipuruṣaḥ pratipādyate |
saṃśayākāraḥ — kim adṛśyatyadiguṇakam akṣarātparataḥ paraśca pradhānapuruṣau ? uta paramātmā? iti vicāraḥ | tadarthaṃ pṛthivyādisambandhitayā dṛṣṭaṃ dṛśyatvādikaṃ niṣidhyamānaṃ, kim — pṛthivyādi- sajātīyam | acetanamevāśrayamupasthāpayati ? kim – ūrṇanābhidṛṣṭāntopādānam, acetanā- śrayopasthāpakatvam upabṛṃhayati ? uta na ? - iti | kim – 'akṣarāt sambhavatīha viśvam' iti 'viśva' śabdaḥ, cidacidātmakaprapañcamupasthāpayati ? uta acetanam ? - iti !
— acetanopasthāpakatve sati, urṇanābhidṛṣṭāntasya ca acidā- śrayopasthāpakatopabṛṃhakatayā niṣidhyamānaṃ dṛśyatvādikam acidupasthāpakamiti, adṛśyatvādiguṇa kaṃ, pradhānam ; tataḥ paraśca jīvaḥ iti syāt |

- yadā 'viśva' śabda : civacidātmaka jagadupasthāpakaḥ ; tadā ūrṇanābhidṛṣṭāntasya ca acetanopasthāpakatvānupabṛṃhakatvāt niṣidhyamānaṃ dṛśyatvādikamapi na acetanopasthāpakamiti, adṛśyatvādiguṇakaḥ paramātmā paramātmanaśca parāntarāsambhavāt, akṣarāt parataḥ paraśca paramātmā iti |
asya akṣarasyeti | antaryāmividyāyāmapi na kevalamadṛṣṭatvaṃ paramātmatva sādhakam ; kintu draṣṭṛtvādisacivam | iha tu tatsācivyābhāvāt, adṛśyatvaṃ na acivyāvartakam ityarthaḥ | adṛṣṭo draṣṭā ityādinā, saṅgatiśca abhipretā | sarvasmādvikārātparabhūtāt akṣarāt iti | samānavibhaktinirdeśasvārasyāt, 'parata: akṣarāt' iti sāmānādhikaraṇyam iti bhāvaḥ (śru pra ) ||

etaduktaṃ bhavati – rūpādimatsthūlarūpācetanaṣpṛthivyādibhūtāśrayaṃ dṛśyatvādika pratiṣidhyamānaṃ, pṛthivyā disajātīya sūkṣmarūpācetanameva upasthāpayati | tacca pradhānameva | tasmātparatvaṃ ca samaṣṭipuruṣasyaiva prasiddham | tadadhiṣṭhitaṃ ca pradhānaṃ mahadādiviśeṣaparyantaṃ vikārajātaṃ prasūte - iti, tatra 'dṛṣṭāntāḥ ' ' upanyasyante'

'yathorṇanābhiḥ sṛjate gṛhṇate ca yathā pṛthivyāmoṣadhayaḥ sambhavanti |
yathā sataḥ puruṣāt keśalomāni tathā'kṣarāt sambhavatīha

viśvam ||'

-

iti | ataḥ asmin prakaraṇe pradhānapuruṣāveva pratipādyete - iti ||

evaṃ prāpte, brūmaḥ - adṛśyatvādiguṇako dharmokteḥ | adṛśyatvādiguṇakaḥ, akṣara (tparataḥ paraśca paramapuruṣa eva |

kuta: ?
taddharmokteḥ |

'ya: sarvajñaḥ sarvavit'

rūpādi.... upasthāpayatīti | brāhmaṇādipratyāsanne sajātīye kṣatriyādau vartante ; na tu śvapacādau paśvādau vā | 'abālakṛśa' śabdādayaḥ pratyāsannāvasthāntarāśraye tasminneva dharmiṇi vartante ; na tu dhayantare | saṃnikṛṣṭabodhanasya nyāyyatvāt | evam adṛśyatvādiguṇānāṃ dharmiviśeṣākāṅkṣāyāṃ niṣidhyamāna- dṛśyatvasya acidādhāratvena acetane pratipanne, tasyaiva sūkṣmāvasthasya adṛśyatvādau ca sambhavati, vijātīyadharmyantarakalpane gauravaprasaṅgāt, tatparatvāśrayaṇam ayuktamiti bhāvaḥ | tacceti | na dṛśyatvādivyāpyam acetanatvam ; manaādau vyabhicārāt iti bhāvaḥ | yathā pṛthivyāmiti, cetanānādhiṣṭhitatve'pi dṛṣṭāntaḥ | yathā sata iti cetanasaṃnidhimātrāpekṣatve dṛṣṭāntaḥ | ata iti, pūrvapakṣamupasaṃharati ||
tadanyārthakanajupaśliṣṭāḥ abrāhmaṇādayaḥ śabdā hi

paramapuruṣaḥ iti, 'parātparaṃ puruṣam' iti vākyaṃ smārayati |

ityādinā, sarvajñatvādikāḥ tasyaiva dharmā ucyante | tathā hi-- 'yayā tadakṣaramadhigamyate' ityādinā, adṛśyatvādiguṇakam 'akṣaramabhidhāya', ''akṣarāt sambhavatīha viśvam' iti, tasmāt viśvasaṃbhavaṃ cā'bhidhāya,
'yaḥ sarvajñaḥ' sarvavit yasya jñānamayaṃ tapaḥ |
tasmādetadbrahma nāma rūpamaṃtra ca jāyate ||' iti, bhūtayoneḥ akṣarasya sarvajñatvādiḥ pratipādyate | paścāt,
'akṣarātparataḥ paraḥ'

iti ca, prakṛtam adṛśyatvādiguṇakaṃ bhūtayonyakṣaraṃ sarvajñameva, paratvena vyapadiśyate | ataḥ, 'akṣarātparataḥ paraḥ' iti, 'akṣara' śabda: pañcamyantaḥ prakṛtam adṛśyatvādi- guṇakam akṣaraṃ, na abhidhatte; tasya sarvajñasya viśvayoneḥ, sarvasmātparatvena, tasmādanyasya paratvāsambhavāt | ataḥ atra 'akṣara' śabdaḥ, bhūtasūkṣmam acetanaṃ brūte || 22 ||

iti vakṣyamāṇatvāt | cīyate – upacīyate |

'bahu syām' *

iti saṅkalparūpeṇa jñānena, brahma sṛṣṭayunmukhaṃ bhavati - ityarthaḥ | tato'nnamabhijāyate adyate iti annam | viśvasya bhoktṛvargasya bhogyabhūtaṃ bhūtasūkṣmam avyākṛtaṃ, parasmādbrahmaṇo jāyate - ityarthaḥ | prāṇa- manaḥprabhṛti ca svargāpavargarūpaphalasādhanabhūtakarma- paryantaṃ 'sarva vikārajātaṃ', tasmādeva jāyate |

'yaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarvavit'

ityādinā, sṛṣṭayupakaraṇabhūtaṃ 'sārvajñya' - satyasaṅkalpatvādikamuktam | sarvajñāt satya- saṅkalpāt parasmādbrahmaṇaḥ akṣarāt etat - kāryākāraṃ brahma, nāma-rūpa- vibhaktaṃ bhoktṛ- bhogya' rūpaṃ ca jāyate |

' tadetatsatyam'
iti, parasya brahmaṇaḥ nirupādhikasatyatvamucyate-
lokāḥ – svargādrayaḥ | karmasu cāmṛtam - karmasu āyattam amṛtam | amṛtatvasādhanabhūtaṃ karmetyarthaḥ iti bhāva: |
viśvastha bhoktṛvargasya ityanena etadvākyastha 'satya' śabdaḥ vyākhyāto bhavati | 'satya' śabdavācyasya bhoktṛvargasyetyarthaḥ |
yaḥ sarvajñaḥ iti | yathā - 'dadhnā juhoti' iti vākye 'juhoti' padena, 'agnihotraṃ juhoti' iti vihitaṃ homamanūdya, dadhnaḥ tatsādhanatvaṃ vidhīyate; tathā kāraṇānuvādena sarvajñatvādi- guṇavidhānam ityarthaḥ | bhoktṛ-bhogyarūpaṃ ca jāyate ityanena, 'anna' śabda : vyākhyāto bhavati | 'adyate'tti ca bhūtāni' iti hi 'anna' śabdanirvacanam |---

'mantreṣu karmāṇi kavayo 'yānyapaśyan',
tāni tretāyāṃ bahudhā santatāni |
'tānyācarata' niyataṃ satyakāmāḥ'

iti, sārvajñya- satyasaṅkalpatvādi kalyāṇaguṇākaram akṣaraṃ puruṣaṃ svatassatyaṃ nityaṃ kāmayamānāḥ, tatprāptaye phalāntarebhyo viraktāḥ, ṛgyajussāmātharvasu kavibhirdṛṣṭāni, varṇāśramocitāni tretāgniṣu bahudhā santatāni karmāṇyācarata - iti |

3.

'eṣa vaḥ panthāḥ'

ityārabhya,

'eṣa vaḥ puṇyaḥ sukṛto brahmaloka:'
ityantena, karmānuṣṭhānaprakāram, śruti - smṛticoditeṣu karmasu 'ekatara 'karmavaidhurye'pi, " itareṣāma' nuṣṭhitānāmapi niṣphalatvam, ayathānuṣṭhitasya ca ananuṣṭhitasamatvamabhidhāya, 'plavā hyete acchā yajñarūpā aṣṭādaśottamavaraṃ yeṣu karma |
etacchreyo ye'bhinandanti mūḍhā jarāmṛtyū te punarevā'piyanti||'

'ityādinā', phalābhisandhipūrvakatvena jñānavidhuratayā ca 'avaraṃ karma' ācaratāṃ, punarāvṛttimuktvā,
'tapaśśraddhe ye hyupavasanti'
ityādinā punarapi 'phalābhisandhirahitaṃ jñāninā anuṣṭhitaṃ karma, brahmaprāptaye bhavati' iti praśasya",

'parīkṣya lokān'

ityādinā, kevalakarmaphaleṣu 'viraktasya', yathoditakarmānugṛhītaṃ brahmaprāpyupāyabhūtaṃ jñānaṃ jijñāsamānasya ca ācāryopasadanaṃ vidhāya

' tadetatsatyaṃ, yathā sudīptāt' -

ityādinā,

'so'vidyāgranthiṃ vikiratīha somya'
ityantena, pūrvoktasya, akṣarasya bhṛtayoneḥ parasya brahmaṇaḥ paramapuruṣasya, anuktaiḥ svarūpa- guṇaiḥ saha sarvabhūtāntarātmatayā 'viśva' śarīratvena viśvarūpatvam, tasmādviśvasṛṣṭiṃ ca vispaṣṭamabhidhāya,

'āviḥ saṃnihitam '

ityādinā, tasyaivā'kṣarasya : avyākṛtātparato'pi puruṣāt parabhūtasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ, paramavyomni pratiṣṭhitasya anavadhikā tiśayānandasvarūpasya, 'hṛdaya' guhāyām upāsana- prakāram upāsanasya ca parabhakti rūpatvam, upāsīnasya avidyā vimoka' pūrvakaṃ 'brahmasamaṃ brahmānubhavaphalaṃ ca upadiśya upasaṃhṛtam |

ataḥ evaṃ viśeṣaṇāt bhedavyapadeśācca, na asmin prakaraṇe pradhānapuruṣau pratipādyete ||

56. rūpopanyāsācca || 1-2-24||
'agnirmūrdhā cakṣuṣī candrasūryau diśaḥ śrotre vāgvivṛtāśca vedāḥ |
vāyuḥ prāṇo hṛdayaṃ viśvamasya padbhyāṃ pṛthivī hyeṣa sarvabhūtāntarātmā' ||

itīdṛśaṃ rūpaṃ, sarvabhūtāntarātmanaḥ paramātmana evaṃ sambhavati ; ataśca paramātmā ||24||

17. vaiśvānarādhikaraṇam || 1-2-6||

57. vaiśvānaraḥ sādhāraṇaśabdaviśeṣāt || 1-2-25||

idamāmananti chandogāḥ-

'ātmānamevemaṃ vaiśvānaraṃ saṃpratyadhyepi, tameva no brūhi' iti prakramya,

'yastvetamevaṃ prādeśamātramabhivimānamātmānaṃ vaiśvānaramupāste'

iti,

tatra sandehaḥ – kimayaṃ vaiśvānara ātmā, paramātmeti śakyanirṇayaḥ 1 uta na ? -

iti |

vaiśvānararādhikaraṇam

1. jāṭharāgnau tāvat -

'ayamagnirvaiśvānare .... yenedamannaṃ pacyate yadidamadyate | tasyaiva ghoṣo bhavati ; 'yāvadetatkarṇāvapidhāya śṛṇoti | sa yadotkramiṣyan bhavati, nainaṃ ghoṣaṃ śṛṇoti'

iti |

2. mahābhūtatṛtīye ca -

'viśvasmā agniṃ bhuvanāya devā vaiśvānaraṃ ketumahāmakṛṇvan'

iti |

3. devatāyāṃ ca-

'vaiśvānarasya sumatau syāma rājā hikaṃ bhuvanānāmabhiśrīḥ'

iti |

4. paramātmani ca —

‘tadātmanneva hṛdayye'gnau vaiśvānare prāsyat'

iti,

' sa eṣa vaiśvānaro viśvarūpaḥ prāṇo'gnirudayate'
iti ca | vākyopakramādiṣu upalabhyamānānyapi liṅgāni, sarvānuguṇatayā netuṃ śakyāni – iti ||

evaṃ prāpte, abhidhīyate—vaiśvānaraḥ sādhāraṇaśabda viśeṣāt | vaiśvānaraḥ para eva ātmā' |

kutaḥ ?

sādhāraṇaśabda viśeṣāt | viśeṣyata iti viśeṣaḥ | sādhāraṇasya ' vaiśvānara' - śabdasya, paramātmāsādhāraṇaiḥ dharmaiḥ viśeṣyamāṇatvāt ityarthaḥ |
tathā hi ; aupamanyavādayaḥ pañcame' maharṣayaḥ sametya, 'ko na atmā ? kiṃ brahma ?'

iti vicārya,

'uddālako ha vai bhagavanto'yamāruṇiḥ saṃpratīmamātmānaṃ
vaiśvānaramadhyeti, taṃ hantā'bhyā' gacchāma"
iti, uddālakasya vaiśvānarātmavijñāna' mavagamya', tamabhyājagmuḥ | sa ca uddālakaḥ, etān vaiśvānarātmajijñāsūna abhilakṣya, ātmanaśca tatra akṛtsnaveditvaṃ matvā, tān hovāca-
'aśvapatirvai bhagavanto'yaṃ kekayaḥ saṃpratīmamātmānaṃ
vaiśvānaramadhyeti taṃ hantā'bhyāgacchāma'
viśeṣyate ityādi | nedaṃ viśeṣaśabdasya pratyayārthavyākhyānam ; api tu 'sādhāraṇa- śabda viśeṣāt' iti samāsāntargataṣaṣṭhī vyākhyānam | karmaṇi ṣaṣṭhī; na tu kartari ityarthaḥ | 'viśeṣaḥ iti kṛdantaṃ padaṃ karmārthaṣaṣṭhīnimittam' ityabhiprāyeṇa viśeṣapadopādānam | yadvā- 'viśeṣyate iti viśeṣa:' iti ghañaḥ karmārthatāmāha | 'sādhāraṇaśabda eva viśeṣa:' iti karma- dhārayaḥ | dharmaiḥ iti, 'dharma' śabda: liṅgaparaḥ | viśeṣyamāṇatvāt iti phalitārtha uktaḥ ;

na ābhidhānikaḥ | 'kona ātmā ? jñātavyatvābhiprāyam |

kiṃ brahma ?' iti 'kiṃ'śabdadvayam, ātmatva-brahmatvayoḥ dvayorapi 'ātma- brahma' śabdavācyadharmyekatvānekatvajijñāsayā vā 'kiṃ' śabda
dvayaprayogaḥ | ātmanaśceti, viṣayavākye 'na me stenaḥ' ityādeḥ prayojanamāha |

| | iti | te ca uddālakaṣaṣṭāḥ, tamaśvapatim abhyājagmuḥ | sa ca tān maharṣīn yathārhaṃ pṛthagabhyarcya,

'na me stenaḥ'

ityādinā,

'yakṣyamāṇo ha vai bhagavanto'hamasmi'
ityantena, ātmano vratasthatayā pratigrahayogyatāṃ jñāpayanneva brahmavidbhirapi pratiṣiddha- pariharaṇīyatāṃ vihitakarmakartavyatāṃ ca prajñāpya,
'yāvadekaikasmā ṛtvije dhanaṃ dāsyāmi, tāvadbhagavadbhayo dāsyāmi,
vasantu 'bhavantaḥ "

ityavocat | te ca mumukṣavaḥ, "vaiśvānaramātmānaṃ' jijñāsamānāḥ, 'tamevā''tmānamasmākaṃ brūhi' ityavocan | tadevam,

|

' ko na ātmā ? kiṃ brahma ?'
iti, jīvātmanāmātmabhūtaṃ brahma jijñāsamānaiḥ, tajjñam anvicchadbhiḥ, vaiśvānarātmajña- sakāśamāgamya, pṛcchayamāno vaiśvānarātmā', paramātmā - iti vijñāyate |

“ātma'-brahma'śabdābhyāmupakramya, paścāt sarvatra 'ātma-vaiśvānara' śabdābhyāṃ vyavahārācca, 'brahma' śabdasthāne nirdiśyamānaḥ 'vaiśvānara' śabdaḥ, 'brahmavā'bhidhatte' iti vijñāyate | kiṃ ca-

'sa sarveṣu lokeṣu sarveṣu bhūteṣu sarveṣvātmasvannamatti ' 'tadyatheṣīka (kā) tūlamanau protaṃ pradūyetaivaṃ hāsya sarve pāpmānaḥ pradūyante'

' iti ca vakṣyamāṇaṃ vaiśvānarātmavijñānaphalam, vaiśvānarātmānaṃ 'paraṃ brahma' iti jñāpayati ||25||

itaśca vaiśvānaraḥ paramātmā -

'iti' śabdaḥ prakāravacanaḥ | itthambhūtaṃ rūpaṃ pratyabhijñāyamānaṃ, vaiśvānarasya paramātmatve anumānaṃ syāt | śruti - smṛtiṣu hi paramapuruṣasya itthaṃ rūpaṃ prasiddham | yathā ātharvaṇe
'agnimūrdhā cakṣuṣī candrasūryau diśaḥ śrotre vāgvivṛtāśca vedāḥ |
vāyuḥ prāṇo hṛdayaṃ viśvamasya padbhayāṃ pṛthivī hyeṣa sarvabhūtāntarātma |'

iti | aniriha dyulokaḥ ;
'asau vai loko'gniḥ'
iti śruteḥ | smaranti ca munayaḥ-

'dyāṃ mūrdhānaṃ yasya viprā vadanti khaṃ vai 'nābhi' candrasūryau ca netre | diśaḥ śrotre viddhi pādau kṣitiṃ ca so'cintyātmā sarvabhūtapraṇetā || '

iti :

'syāsgnirāsyaṃ dyauḥ khaṃ nābhicaraṇau kṣitiḥ |
sūryacakṣurdiśaḥ śrotraṃ tasmai lokātmane namaḥ ||'

iti ca ||

iha ca dyaprabhṛtayaḥ, vaiśvānarasya mūrdhādyavayavatvenocyante | aupamanyavaprabhṛtibhirmaharṣibhiḥ,

| tathā hi-taiḥ

itiśabda iti | 'anumāna 'śabdena hetutvasiddheḥ itiśabdavaiyarthyam ityatrottaramidam | paramātmaparatvena sādhinatvāt, abhibhūrdhā' iti vākyamudāhṛtam | vāgvivṛtāḥ vāgindriyavyāpārāḥ | vāyuḥ prāṇaḥ - mahāvāyureva dehadhārakaḥ prāṇaḥ | hṛdayaṃ viśvamasya- jagat sarvam amya dehāntarvartihṛdayākhyamāṃsa viśeṣaḥ | padbhyāṃ pṛthivī - pādāveva pṛthivī | sarvabhūtāntarātmā - sarvabhūtānāṃ taccharīratvāt sarveṣāmantarātmā |

agniriheti | dhauḥ mūrdhatvena śrūyate |

'agniḥ mūrdhā' iti |

yasyā'gnirāsyam ityatra, 'agni'śabdaḥ prasiddhāgniparaḥ; 'dyaurmūrdhā' iti pṛthagukteḥ |

'ātmānamevemaṃ vaiśvānaraṃ sampratyadhyeṣi, tameva no brūhi'
iti pṛṣṭaḥ kekayaḥ, tebhyaḥ vaiśvānarātmānam upadidikṣuḥ, viśeṣapraśnānyathānupapattyā, 'vaiśvānarātmani etaiḥ kiñcit jñātam, kiñcidajñātam " iti vijñāya, jñātājñātāṃśa- bubhutsayā, tān ekaikaṃ papraccha | tatra,

'aupamanyava kaṃ tvamātmānamupāsse'

iti pṛṣṭe,

'divameva bhagavo rājan '

iti tena cokte, divi tasya pūrṇavaiśvānarātmabuddhiṃ nivartayan, 'vaiśvānarasya dyaurmūrdhā' " iti ca' upadiśan, tasyāḥ vaiśvānarāṃśabhūtāyāḥ divaḥ, 'sutejā:' iti guṇanāmadheya prācikhyapat |
evaṃ satyayajñādibhiḥ ādityavāyvākāśāppṛthivīnām ekaikena ekaikam upāsya- mānatayā kathitānāṃ, 'viśvarūpaḥ, pṛthagvartmā, bahulaḥ, rayiḥ, pratiṣṭhā' iti ekaikaguṇa- nāmadheyāni, vaiśvānarātmanaḥ cakṣuḥ- prāṇa-sandeha - vasti-pādāvayavatvaṃ ca upadiṣṭam | sandehaḥ – madhyakāya ucyate | ataḥ evambhūtadyumūrdhatvādiviśiṣṭaṃ paramapuruṣasyeva rūpamiti, "vaiśvānaraḥ" paramapuruṣa eva || 26 ||

punarapi "anirṇayamevāśaṅkaya' pariharati-
59. śabdādibhyo'ntaḥ pratiṣṭhānācca nota' cenna tathādṛṣṭayupadeśādasambhavāt puruṣamapi cainamadhīyate || 1-2-27||
dyaprabhṛtīnāmanusandheyam ityartha: | 'smaryamāṇa' śabdaśca pratyabhijñāyamānopasthāpakaḥ ; tarkapāde 'anusmṛteśca iti sūtre 'anusmṛti' śabdasya pratyabhijñāparatvena sūtrakāraiḥ prayuktatvāt | ||26|

yaduktam - vaiśvānaraḥ paramātmeti niścīyate iti,
tanna ; śabdādibhyaḥ, antaḥ pratiṣṭhānācca 'jāṭharasyā'pyagneḥ ' iha pratīyamānatvāt | vājināṃ vaiśvānaravidyāprakaraṇe-

śabdastāvat,

' sa eṣo'gnirvaiśvānaraḥ'
iti vaiśvānarasamānādhikaraṇatayā 'agniḥ' iti śrūyate | asmin prakaraṇe ca - 'hṛdayaṃ gārhapatyaḥ, mano'nvāhāryapacanaḥ, āsyamāhavanīyaḥ' iti vaiśvānarasya hṛdayādi sthasya', agnitrayakalpanaṃ kriyate |
'tadyadbhaktaṃ prathamamāgacchet taddhomīyam | sa yāṃ prathamāmāhutiṃ juhuyāt, tāṃ juhuyāt prāṇāya svāhā'
ityādinā prāṇāhutyādhāratvaṃ ca vaiśvānarasya avagamyate | tathā vaiśvānarasya asmin puruṣe antaḥpratiṣṭhānaṃ, vājasaneyinaḥ samāmananti —
'sayo " haitameva ' magniṃ vaiśvānaraṃ puruṣavidhaṃ puruṣe'taḥ pratiṣṭhitaṃ veda'
iti | ataḥ 'agni'śabdasāmānādhikaraṇyāt, agnitretāparikalpanāt prāṇāhutyādhāra- bhāvāt, 'antaḥpratiṣṭhānācca vaiśvānarasya jāṭharatvamapi pratīyata iti, na ekāntataḥ paramātmatvam - iti cet,
yaduktamiti, neti padaṃ vyācaṣṭe | śrutyantarasyahetvobhararuyoḥ dūrasthatvāt, tau kaṇṭhoktau |
etatprakaraṇasthabhū agnitrayakalpanaṃ prāṇāhutyādhāratvaṃ ca 'ādi' śabdopāttam ; saṃnikṛṣṭatvāt | etadabhiprāyeṇa 'vājināṃ prakaraṇe, vāja mananti' ityuktam |
asmin prakaraṇe ca iti caśabdena vājināmapyastīti sūcitam | agnitrayeti, homādhāratvamātraṃ phalitam | tadyadbhaktamiti, homādhāratva viśeṣaḥ | antaḥ pratiṣṭhānaṃ 'agni' śabdanirdeśasahitaṃ vivakṣitam | antaḥ pratiṣṭhatvamātraṃ hi parasyā'pi sambhavati | 'sayo dvaitamevamagniṃ vaiśvānaraṃ puruṣavidhaṃ puruṣe'ntaḥ pratiṣṭhitaṃ veda' iti hi śrūyate |
śabdādibhyaḥ ityādisūtrakhaṇḍaṃ jāṭharaśaṅkāmātraparam, na tu devatābhūtaparam ; antaḥpratiṣṭhānopanyāsāt, hetvantarāṇāṃ ca tatsāhacaryāt |

hainameva – da

tanna ; tathā dṛṣṭayupadeśāt – 'pūrvoktasya trailokyaśarīrasya' 'parasya brahmaṇo vaiśvānarasya jāṭharāgniśarīratayā, "tadviśiṣṭasya upāsanopadeśāt' | 'agni' śabdādibhirhi na kevala jāṭharaḥ pratipādyate ; api tu jāṭharāgniviśiṣṭaḥ paramātmā | kathamidamava- gamyate 1 – iti cet,
asambhavāt—–jāṭharasya kevalasya trailokyaśarīratvāsambhavāt | trailokyaśarīratayā pratipannavaiśvānarasamānādhikaraṇaḥ, jāṭharaviṣayatayā pratīyamānaḥ 'agni' śabdaḥ, jāṭharaśarīra- tayā tadviśiṣṭaṃ paramātmānameva abhidadhātītyarthaḥ | yathoktaṃ bhagavatā - 'ahaṃ vaiśvānaro bhūtvā prāṇināṃ dehamāśritaḥ |
prāṇāpānasamāyuktaḥ pacāmyannaṃ caturvidham ||'
iti | jāṭharānalaśarīro bhūtvā - ityarthaḥ | ataḥ tadviśiṣṭasyopāsanam atropadiśyate : kiñca – puruṣamapi ca enamadhīyate vājasaneyinaḥ-

tannati, śaṅkita jāṭharaviṣayatvaṃ pariharati | jāṭharāgniśarīratayā iti, 'yasyāgniḥ śarīram' iti śrutyabhiprāyeṇa | upāsanaśabdena sautradṛṣṭiśabdavyākhyānam ; 'ātmā vā are draṣṭavyaḥ' 'tasmin dṛṣṭe parāvare' iti śrautaprayogāt | 'vaiśvānaramupāste' iti ca atra śrūyate | paramārthaviṣayatve sambhavati, aparamārthaviṣayatvāśrayaṇāyogāt, na 'mano brahma' ityādi- dṛṣṭitulyatvaśaṅkā | jāṭhare hi ghumūrdhatvādikam atyanta bādhitam | 'jāṭharāgniśarīratayā' ityanena, paramārthaviṣayasambhavaśca darśitaḥ | utkṛṣṭe paramātmani, apakṛṣṭāgnidṛṣṭyayogācca na parasmin agnidṛṣṭiḥ | paramātmaliṅgānāṃ jāṭharāgniliṅgānāṃ ca darśanāt viśiṣṭo- pāsanaparatvam abhyupagantavyam | yathā pratardanavidyāyām iti vākyārthaḥ pratipādyate | jñāpyate | kathamityādi | kevalāgnau prasiddhasya kathaṃ tadviśiṣṭaparatvam ityarthaḥ |
yathoktamiti, jāṭharaviṣayatayā prasiddhasya śabdasya, tadviśiṣṭa paramapuruṣe pravṛtti smṛtyā draḍhayati |
puruṣamapi ceti | api ca ityatra vibhaktaḥ caśabdaḥ hetusamuccayārthaḥ | na kevalam agnitvaṃ puruṣe'ntaḥ pratiṣṭhitatvaṃ ca; kintu 'puruṣa' śabdavācyatayā'pyadhīyate

1 pūrvoktatrailokyaśarīrasya – na, • tadviśiṣṭopāsanasya upadeśāt-na

paramātmā'pi pratīyata iti na ekāntataḥ paramātmatvam-tha

eṣo'gnirvaiśvānaraḥ

' sa eṣo'śvānaraḥ yat puruṣaḥ' na hi jāṭharasya kevalasya puruṣatvam

paramātmana eva hi nirupādhika

iti |

puruṣatvam ! yathā-

'sahasraśīrṣā puruṣaḥ' 'puruṣa evedaṃ sarvam'

ityādau ||| 27 ||

60. ata eva na devatā bhūtaṃ ca || 1-2-28 ||
uktebhya eva hetubhyaḥ, devatāyāśca, tṛtīyasya mahābhūtasyā'pi na vaiśvānarasva-

prasaṅgaḥ || 28 ||

ityabhiprāyaḥ | puruṣatvam - puruṣaśabdavācyatvam | nirupādhikamiti | jīvasya 'puruṣa' śabdavācyatvaṃ, 'dvāvimau puruṣau loke' 'pradhānapuruṣāvetau' ityādiṣu pradhānādipadāntarasamabhivyā- hārākṣāratvādyupādhinā āśrīyate ; na tu svataḥ | ataḥ 'puruṣa' śabdavācyatvaṃ paramātmana eva nirupādhikam | ataḥ jāṭharasya puruṣatvaṃ dūrato nirastam iti bhāvaḥ | yathetyādinā, 'sa eṣo'gnirvaiśvānaraḥ yat puruṣaḥ' iti prasiddhavannirdeśāt, tasya ca prāpakapramāṇasāpekṣatvāt, paramātmana eva nirupādhika puruṣatvavivakṣāyāṃ pramāṇataḥ prasiddhiṃ darśayati || 27 ||

ata eveti | jāṭharaprasiddhiśaṅkā pūrvasūtre parihṛtā | tadvyatiriktārthadvayaśaṅkā atra parihriyate | devatā bhūtatṛtīyayoḥ, jāṭharavat paricchinnatvābhāvāt trailokyaśarīratvopapattiḥ | devatāyāḥ, adhiṣṭhātṛtayāsya trailokyaśarīratvam | mahābhūtatṛtīya ca trivṛtkṛtasya trailokyarūpeṇa pariṇatatvāt, samuditasya dyāvāpṛthivyādayaḥ mūrdhādyavayavāḥ syuḥ | ataḥ trailokyaśarīratvakalpa- nopapattiriti asambhavākṣepeṇa adhikāśaṅkā |
uktebhya iti | 'ātma- brahma puruṣa' śabdanirdeśa || 28 |

.

61. sākṣādapyavirodhaṃ jaiminiḥ || 1-2-29||

'agni' śabdo'pi, agranayanā' dinā yenaiva guṇena yogātā jvalane vartate, tasyaiva guṇasya nirupādhikasya kāṣṭhāgatasya paramātmani sambhavāt, asmin prakaraṇe paramātmā sādhāraṇadharmaviśeṣitaḥ, paramātmānameva abhidhatte— iti || 29 ||

iti niścīyate; evam

'yastvetamevaṃ prādeśamātramabhivimānam'
iti, aparicchinnasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ prabhṛti pṛthivyantapradeśa sambandhinyā mātrayā paricchinnatvaṃ kathamupapadyate ? tatrā''ha -
62. abhivyakterityāśmarathyaḥ || 1-2-30 ||

upāsakābhivyaktyarthaṃ prādeśamātratvaṃ paramātmanaḥ iti, āśmarathya- ācāryo manyate | 'dyaurmūrdhā, ādityaścakṣuḥ, vāyuḥ prāṇaḥ, ākāśo madhyakāyaḥ, āpo vastiḥ,
vivakṣitam agranayanādinā iti ādiśabdena, agratvena prāpyatvaṃ vivakṣitam | kāṣṭhāgatasyeti | 'agni' śabdāvayavārthasya brahmaṇi puṣkalatvāt, 'brahma' śabdarūḍhiḥ na tyājyā | 'brahma'śabdāvaya- vārthapauṣkalyasya agnau asambhavāt, 'agni' śabdarūḍhireva tyājyā iti bhāvaḥ | paramātmā.... viśeṣita iti | upakramādyānuguṇyācca 'brahma'śabdaḥ prabala: ; 'agni' śabdastu tadanuguṇārtho neyaḥ iti bhāvaḥ ||29||
abhivyakteriti | paricchinnatvaṃ mahābhūtatṛtīya- devatayoreva sambhavatīti śaṅkāparihārārtha midaṃ sūtram | abhivyaktayartham – buddhisaukaryāya | paricchinnatve hi buddhisaukaryaṃ syāt |

mūrdhatvādimātrābhiḥ paricchinnatvamatra tu |
upāsakābhivyakterityā śmarathya munermatam ||
1 viśeṣito'pi - a, i, 2 dinyāyenaiva - tha 3 pṛthivyantāvayavasya prādeśa - vā.

pṛthivī pādau' iti, dyuprabhṛti 'pradeśa' sambandhinyā mātrayā paricchinnatvaṃ, ' kṛtsnamabhi- vyāptavataḥ vigatamānasya hi, abhivyaktereva hetorbhavati || 30 ||

mūrdhaprabhṛtyavayavaviśeṣaiḥ puruṣavidhatvaṃ parasya brahmaṇaḥ kimartham ? – iti cet,

tatrā''ha -

63. anusmṛtervādariḥ || 1-2-31|| tathopāsanārtham iti bādarirācāryo manyate |
'yastvetamevam.... 'abhivimānamātmānaṃ vaiśvānaramupāste, sa sarveṣu lokeṣu, sarveṣu bhūteṣu sarveṣvātmasvannamatti '
iti, brahmaprāptaye hi ! upāsanamupadiśyate | etamevamiti — uktaprakāreṇa puruṣākāram ityarthaḥ | sarveṣu lokeṣu sarveṣu bhūteṣu sarveṣvātmasu vartamānaṃ yadannaṃ - bhogyam, tadatti sarvatra vartamānaṃ, svata evānavādhikātiśayānandaṃ brahmānubhavati |

yatta - sarvaiḥ karmavaśyairātmabhiḥ, pratyekam ananyasādhāraṇam annaṃ bhujyate- tat, mumukṣubhistyājyatvāt iha na gṛhyate || 31 ||
dhuprabhṛtipradeśetyādinā pradeśamātra śabdo vyākhyātaḥ | abhivimānam ityatra abhiśabdaṃ vyācaṣṭe, kṛtsnamabhivyāptavataḥ iti | vigatamānasya iti, vimānaśabdaṃ vyācaṣṭe || 30 ||
mūrceti | prākṛtamūrghādidravyāvayavatvam aprākṛtarūpasya paramātmanaḥ na saṃbhavati ; api tu devatāyā eva ityabhiprāyaḥ |
anusmṛtiḥ – upāsanam | upāsanārthaṃ puruṣavidhatvam ityarthaḥ | ||31||

lokaprabhṛtīnāṃ tu mūrdhatvādiprakalpanam |
tathaivopāsanaṃ vaktum iti bādarirabravīt ||

yadi paramātmā vaiśvānaraḥ, kathaṃ tarhi uraḥprabhṛtīnāṃ 'vedyāditvopadeśaḥ ? yāvatā jāṭharāgniparigrahe eva etadupapadyate, ityatrāha -

64. sampatteriti jaiministathā hi darśayati || 1-2-32 ||
asya paramātmana eva vaiśvānarasya dyuprabhṛtipṛthivyantaśarīrasya, samārādhanabhūtāyāḥ, upāsakaiḥ aharahaḥ kriyamāṇāyāḥ prāṇāhuteḥ, agnihotratvasaṃpādanāya, ayam uraḥprabhṛtīnāṃ veditvādyupadeśaḥ - iti, jaiminirācāryo manyate |
tathā hi- paramātmopāsanocitameva phalaṃ prāṇāhutyā agnihotrasampattiṃ ca darśayati, 'iyaṃ śruti:-
'saya idamavidvānagnihotraṃ juhoti, yathā aṅgāranāpohya bhasmani juhuyāt, tāhaka tat syāt '
yadīti | paramātmanaḥ prabhṛtipradeśasambandhinyā mātra yā paricchinnatve puruṣavidhatve ca prayojanamukhena bhūtṛtīya- devatānyudāsaḥ kṛtaḥ | atha jāṭharaśaṅkāvyudāsakatvaṃ - yāvateti | pratyutetyarthaḥ |
sampattiḥ– sampādanam ; atasmin tatkalpanam |
paramātmo .... darśayati iti | agnihotrasampatteḥ paramātmasamārādhanārthatvam ' tasya sarveṣu lokeṣu' ityādyuktaphalaviśeṣa eva jñāpayatīti bhāvaḥ | idamavidvān – uraḥprabhṛtīnāmagni- hotropakaraṇatāmajānan yaḥ | agnihotram - prāṇāhutim | juhoti - anutiṣṭhati, ityarthaḥ |

agnihotratvasampatteḥ prāṇāhutyā stu jaiminiḥ |
uroveditvā diklṛptimabravīdāha hi śrutiḥ ||

'atha ya etadevaṃ' vidvānagnihotra juhoti, tasya sarveṣu lokeṣu sarveṣu bhūteṣu sarveṣvātmasu hutaṃ bhavati'
'tadyathe pīka tūlamagna protaṃ pradūyeta evaṃ hāsya sarve pāpmānaḥ pradūyante'

iti ||32||

'sarveṣu lokeṣu'

ityādinā brahmaprāptiṃ ca phalamupadiśya, asyaivopāsanasya 'aṅgabhūtaṃ' prāṇāgnihotraṃ, 'tasya ha vā etasya'

ityādinā 'upadiśati | yaḥ pūrvamupāsyatayā upadiṣṭo vaitrā naraḥ, tasya avayava- bhūtān 'agnyādityādīn ' sutejo - viśvarūpādināmadheyān upāsakaśarīre mūrvādi- pādānteṣu 'sampādayati' |

mūrdheva sutejā: – upāsakasya mūdhaiva, paramātmamūrdhabhūtā dyauḥ ityarthaḥ | cakṣu- viśvarūpaḥ – āditya ityarthaḥ | prāṇaḥ pṛthagmī - vāyurityarthaḥ | sandeho bahula:- upāsakasya madhyakāya eva paramātmamadhya kāya"bhūtaḥ ākāśaḥ ityarthaḥ | vastireva rayiḥ asya vastireva tadavayavabhūtāḥ āpaḥ ityarthaḥ | pṛthivyeva pādau - asya pādāveva tatpādabhūtā pṛthivī ityarthaḥ |

evam upāsakaḥ svaśarīre paramātmānaṃ trailokyaśarīraṃ vaiśvānaraṃ saṃnihita- manusandhāya, svakīyāni uro - loma - hṛdaya - mana- āsyāni, prāṇāhutyādhārasya paramātmano vaśvānarasya vedibarhigarhipatyānvāhāryapacanāhavanīyān agnihotropakaraṇabhūtān parikalpya prāṇāhuteca agnihotratvaṃ parikalpya evaṃvidhena prāṇāgnihotreṇa, paramātmānaṃ vaiśvānaramārādhayet — iti,
'ura eva vedilamāni barhirhṛdayaṃ gārhapatyaḥ' ityādinā upadiśyate |

ataḥ - paramātmā puruṣottama eva vaiśvānaraḥ - iti siddham || 33 ||
