18. dyubhvādyadhikaraṇam || 1-3-1 ||
66. dyubhvādyāyatanaṃ svaśabdāt || 1- 3-1 ||

ātharvaṇikā adhīyate-

' yasmin dyauḥ pṛthivī cāntarikṣamotaṃ manaḥ ssaha prāṇaiśca sarvaiḥ | tamevaikaṃ jānathā''tmānamanyā vāco vimuñcatha, amṛtasyaiṣa setuḥ || '

tatra saṃśayaḥ - kimayaṃ

pṛthivyādīnāmāyatanatvena śrayamāṇaḥ ; jīvaḥ 1 uta

paramātmā ? - iti ||

ki yuktam ?

jīvaḥ - iti |

kuta: ?

'arā iva rathanābhau saṃhatā yatra nāḍyaḥ |
sa eṣo'ntaścarate bahudhā jāyamānaḥ'

iti parasmin śloke, 'pūrvavākya ' prastutaṃ yu- pṛthivyādyāyatanaṃ, ' 'yatra' iti, punarapi saptamyantena parāmṛśya, tasya nāḍyādhāratvamuktvā, punarapi,
'sa epo'ntaścarate bahudhā jāyamānaḥ'
iti, tasya bahudhā jāyamānatvaṃ ca ucyate | nāḍīsambandhaḥ, devādirūpeṇa bahudhā jāyamānatvaṃ ca, jīvasyaiva dharmaḥ | asminnapi loke,
tatreti | kiṃ dya-pṛthivyādyāyatanaṃ, jīvaḥ uta paramātmā : - iti vicāraḥ | tadartham — 'yasmin dyauḥ' ityādivākyajātaṃ kim adṛśyatvādigugaka prakaraṇāt bhinnam ? uta abhinnam ? tadartha • manaḥ prāṇādisambandhaḥ, kim upakaraṇopakaraṇitvalakṣaṇa: ? uta ādhārādheyabhāvalakṣaṇa: ? tadarthaṃ dyu- pṛthivyādyāyatanatvāmṛta setutvādidharmāḥ, kiṃ karmaphalāttuḥ jīvasya sambhavanti ? uta anaśnata: : iti |

yadā karmaphalātuḥ sambhavanti tadā mana ādisambandhasya karaṇa- tadvattārūpatayā, tasya prakaraṇavicchedakatvena, śubhvādyāyatanaṃ, jīvaḥ syāt |
yadā amṛtasetutvādayaḥ anaśnato dharmāḥ; tadā mana ādisambandhasya ādhārā- dheyabhāvarūpatvena paraprakaraṇāvicchedāt, dyu- pṛthivyādyāyatanaṃ paramātmā - iti phalitaṃ syāt |
nāḍīsambandha iti | nāḍīnām indriyasambandhitvena jīvasya jñānaprasara hetutvāt, 'tāmiḥ pratyavasṛpya purītati śete' 'tayordhvamāyannamṛtatvameti viṣvaṅkanyā utkramaṇe bhavanti ' ityādiśrutibhiḥ jīvasya nāḍīsaṃyogaprasiddheśca, tatsambandhaḥ jīvaliṅgam iti bhāvaḥ | devādirūpeṇa iti, bahudhāśabdavivaraṇam | antarādityavidyāyām akarmakṛtasvecchā gṛhīta divya vigrahayoga :

'otaṃ manassaha prāṇaiśca sava:'

iti, prāṇapañcakasya manasaśca āśrayatvamucyamānaṃ, jīvadharma eva | evaṃ jīvatve 'niścite sati', 'yu-pṛthivyādyāyatanatvādikaṃ yathākathañcit 'saṅgamayitavyam' – iti ||

evaṃ prāpte, pracakṣma he bhvādyāyatanaṃ svaśabdāt | dyu- pṛthivyādīnāmāyatanaṃ,

paraṃ brahma | kutaḥ ?

svaśabdāt - parabrahmāsādhāraṇaśabdāt |
'amṛtasyaiṣa setu : ' iti, parasya brahmaṇaḥ asādhāraṇa śabdaḥ | 'tamevaṃ vidvānamṛta iha bhavati,
nā'nyaḥ panthā ayanāya vidyate'

'ātma' śabdaśca nirupādhikaḥ, 'parasmin brahmaṇi' mukhyavṛttaḥ | āmoti iti hi' ātmā | svetarasamastasya niyantṛtvena vyāptiḥ, tasyaiva sambhavati |

ataḥ so'pi

tasyaiva śabdaḥ |

'yassarvajñaḥ sarvavit'
ityādayaśca uparitanāḥ parasyaiva brahmaṇaḥ śabdāḥ |
nāḍyādhāratvaṃ tasyā'pi sambhavati ;
'santataṃ sirābhistu lamba tyā kośasannibham '

ityārabhya,

'tasyāḥ śikhāyā madhye paramātmā vyavasthitaḥ'

iti śravaṇāt ||

'bahudhā jāyamānaḥ'
ityapi, parasmin brahmaṇi saṅgacchate ;
'ajāyamāno bahudhā vijāyate, tasya dhīrāḥ parijānanti yonim'

- iti, devādīnāṃ samāśrayaṇīyatvāya tattajjātīyarūpa - saṃsthāna - 'guṇa-karma' samanvitaḥ, svakīyaṃ svabhāvam ajahadeva, svecchayā bahudhā 'vijāyate ' "paraḥ puruṣaḥ " – ityabhidhānāt | smṛtirapi -
ātmaśabdaśca iti, 'antaḥ praviṣṭaḥ śāstā janānāṃ sarvātmā' iti śrutyanusāreṇa 'ātma' śabda: na vyāptṛmātravācī; kintu vyāptṛviśeṣavācī; yogarūḍhatvāt | na hi gaganādiṣu 'ātma' śabdaprayogaḥ | ataḥ niyantṛrūpavyāptṛviśeṣavācī ityarthaḥ | 'amṛtasyaiṣa setuḥ 'ātmānam ' 'yaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarvavit' ityādayaḥ svaśabdā ityarthaḥ |
nāḍyādhāratvamiti | nāḍyādhāratvaṃ hṛdayadvārā paramātmanaḥ sambhavatītyarthaḥ | paroktahetu nirvāhamāha ||
ajāyamāna iti | janmano hetuviśeṣaḥ phalaviśeṣaśceti bhāvaḥ | smṛti- rapīti, tadvaiśadyāyāha | [devādīnāmiti, bhagavadavatāra prayojanam |
tattajjātīyeti, samāśrayaṇasaulabhyopapādanam | svakīyamiti, āśrayaṇaśālināmabhimatasiddhiḥ | svecchayā–

'ajo'pi sannavyayātmā bhūtānāmīśvaro'pi san |
prakṛtiṃ svāmadhiṣṭhāya sambhavāmyātmamāyayā ||'

iti |

manaḥprabhṛtijīvopakaraṇādhāratvaṃ ca sarvādhārasya parasyaiva upapadyate || 1 ||

itaśca paramapuruṣaḥ-

67. muktopasṛpyavyapadeśācca || 1-3-2 ||
ayaṃ dyu- pṛthivyādyāyatanabhūtaḥ puruṣaḥ, saṃsārabandhānmuktairapi prāpyatayā vyapadiśyate-

'yadā paśyaḥ paśyate rukmavarṇa kartāramīśaṃ puruṣaṃ brahmayonim |
tadā vidvāna puṇyapāpe vidhūya nirañjanaḥ paramaṃ sāmyamupaiti ||' 'yathā nadyaḥ syandamānāḥ samudre astaṃ gacchanti nāma rūpe vihāya |
tathā vidvān nāma-rūpādvimuktaḥ parātparaṃ puruṣamupati divyam ||' iti | saṃsārabandhanādvimuktā eva hi ! vidhūtapuṇya-pāpāḥ, nirañjanāḥ nāma-rūpābhyāṃ 'vinirmuktāva' | puṇya-pāpanibandhanācitsaṃsargaprayuktanāma-rūpabhāktvameva hi ! saṃsāraḥ | svasaṅkalpena | anena akarmavaśyatā | vijāyate – āvirbhavati | svāṃ prakṛtim – svakīyaṃ svabhāvam, adhiṣṭhāya āśritya | avatāropādānabhūtaṃ divyaṃ vigrahaṃ svīkṛtyeti yāvat | ātmamāyayā - ātmīyena jñānena, ātmasaṅkalpenetyarthaḥ | sambhavāmi - avatarāmi

śrutivākyadvayopādānam, nāmarūpaprahāṇamātraṃ pralayadaśāyāmapyastīti, tadya vṛttyarthaṃ nāma rūpaprāṇasya vidhūtapuṇya pāpatvapūrvakatvaṃ darśayitum | upāttaśrutivākye 'banvānmuktaḥ' iti śabdasyāśrutatvāt mukti pratipādanaṃ darśayati saṃsāra- bandhanāditi | vākyadvayoktaviśeṣaṇāni āha vidhūteti |

ataḥ vidhūtapuṇya-pāpaiḥ nirañjanaiḥ - prakṛtisaṃsargarahitaiḥ pareṇa brahmaṇā paramaṃ sāmyamāpannaiḥ', prāpyatayā nirdiṣṭaḥ dyu- pṛthivyādyāyatanabhūtaḥ puruṣaḥ paraṃ brahma ||2||

'parabrahmā sādhāraṇa' śabdādibhiḥ, 'parameva brahma' iti 'prasādhya', "pratyagātmā'- sādhāraṇaśabdābhāvācca ayaṃ para eva' ityāha-
68. nānumānamatacchabdātprāṇabhṛcca || 1-3-3 ||
yathā asmin prakaraṇe pratipādaka' śabdābhāvāt pradhānaṃ na pratipādyam; evaṃ prāṇabhṛdapi ityarthaḥ | anumīyata iti anumānam ; paroktaṃ pradhānamucyate | anumānapramitatvāt ānumānam iti vā | atacchabdāt - tadvāciśabdābhāvāt ityarthaḥ |

prakṛti saṃsargarahitaiḥ iti padam, nirañjana śabdavyākhyānam | paramaṃ sāmyamiti | yena guṇena sāmyaṃ, tasya aparicchinnatvam – paramasāmyam, sāmyam ||2|

na tu sarvathā

parabrahmeti | pūrvamanvayaḥ uktaḥ ; atha taddārthyāya vyatireka ucyate ityarthaḥ |
yatheti | muktopasṛpyatve abhihite, 'pradhāna' śaṅkānudayena tasya vyudasanīyatvābhāvāt, tadupanyāsaḥ dṛṣṭāntatayā iti bhāvaḥ | ' anumīyata iti' iti pradhānopasthāpakaṃ vyākhyeyaṃ padaṃ vyācaṣṭe | 'kṛtyalyuṭo bahulam' iti bahulagrahaṇāt, 'karmaṇi lyuṭ' iti bhāvaḥ | ānumānam iti rūpam 'śeṣe' iti 'aṇ' pratyaye, ādivṛddhau ca satyāṃ bhavati | tadvācīti, 'atacchabdāt ' tadvyatiriktavāciśabdāt, iti pratītiśaṅkā nivṛttyartham |

'arthābhāve ' yadavyayam" iti avyayībhāvaḥ || 3||

itaśca ayaṃ na pratyagātmā-
69. 'bhedavyapadeśāt || 1–3–4||
'samāne vṛkṣe puruṣo nimagno'nīśayā śocati muhyamānaḥ |

juṣṭaṃ yadā paśyatyanyamīśamasya mahimānamiti vītaśokaḥ ||' ityādibhiḥ, jīvādvilakṣaṇatvena ayaṃ vyapadiśyate | anīśayā - bhogyabhūtayā prakṛtyā,

'arthābhāve' iti na sūtropādānam ; api tu arthopādānam ; sūtrasya drāghīyastvāt |
itaśceti | prāṇabhṛtpratiṣedhakasūtrānantaryāt tadanantara sūtrasyā'pi prāṇabhṛtpratikṣepaḥ sādhyaḥ ityarthaḥ | ayaṃśabdaḥ dyubhvādyāyatanātmaviṣayaḥ | bhedavyapadeśaśabdena 'samāne vṛkṣe' iti mantre 'anya' śabdārthaḥ vivakṣitaḥ |

.

anīśayā iti, strīliṅgasāmarthyāt prakṛteḥ viśeṣyatvamuktam | svasmāt iti, 'anya' śabdasāmarthyalabdhaṃ pratiyoginaṃ nirdiśati | sarvasya iti, 'īśa' śabda sāmarthya prāptam īśitatryaṃ mānāntarānurodhenāha | prīyamāṇam iti, 'juṣṭa' śabdaṃ vyācaṣṭe | ' ādikarmaṇi ktaḥ ' iti bhāvaḥ | mahimānaṃ ceti | samuccetavyasāmarthya prāptaḥ caśabdaḥ iti bhāvaḥ | niyamanarūpamiti | 'iti' śabda : buddhisthaprakāraparaḥ | īśaśabda- śravaṇāt, niyamanarūpaprakāra: buddhisthaḥ iti bhāvaḥ | tadā iti, yattadornityasambandhāt |

mudyamānaḥ śocati jīva: ' ; ayaṃ yadā svasmādanyaṃ sarvasyeśaṃ, prīyamāṇam, asya - - īśvarasya, mahimānaṃ ca - nikhilajaganniyamanarūpaṃ paśyati tadā vītaśoko bhavati ||4||

70. prakaraṇāt || 1-3-5||
prakaraṇaṃ cedaṃ parasya brahmaṇaḥ - iti,

71. sthityadanābhyāṃ ca || 1-3-6||
'dvā suparṇā sayujā sakhāyā samānaṃ vṛkṣaṃ pariṣasvajāte |

tayoranya: pippalaṃ svādvacya naśnannanyo'bhicākaśīti || ' iti 'ekasya' karmaphalādanam, 'anyasya ca' karmaphalamanaśśrata eva dīpyamānatayā śarīrāntaḥ sthitimātraṃ pratipādyate | tatra karmaphalamanaśnan dīpyamāna eva sarvajñaḥ, 'amṛtasetuḥ ", sarvātmā dyasvādyāyatanaṃ bhavitumarhati ; na punaḥ karmaphalamadana, śocan pratyagātmā | ataḥ bhvādyāyatanaṃ paramātmā - iti siddham || 6 || || iti bhvādyadhikaraṇam ||1||

sthityadanābhyāṃ ca iti, anuktaṃ pradhānabhūtaṃ hetumāha | ' sthiti' śabdasya pūrvanipātaḥ, 'alpāctaram' iti, 'abhyarhitaṃ pūrva prayoktavyam' iti vā | sthite: abhyarhitatvam, paramātma pratyabhi - jñāpanena prakaraṇāvicchedahetutvāt | bhāṣye śrutikrameṇa vyākhyātam ; na punaḥ sūtrakrameṇa |

sarvajña iti | 'sarvajña : ' 'amṛta setu:' ityādiviśeṣaṇāni prākaraṇikatayoktāni | ubhayoḥ prastutatve'pi sārvajñyādisamānāśrayatvāt svayaṃ yogyatvāt, manaḥ prāṇasambandhādeḥ paramātmani amukhyatvābhāvācca dyubhvādyāyatanatvaṃ paramātmadharmaḥ ityarthaḥ | ' anaśnan' 'adan' iti sautrahetvanuvādaḥ | śocan iti 'bhedavyapadeśāt' iti sautra hetoḥ ihānyo'bhipretaḥ | ' śocati mujhamāna:' iti hi tadviṣayavākyam | tadapi pratyagātmaparatvapratikṣepadvārā paramātmaparatva- sādhakam ; vyatirekasya anvayaśeṣatvāt iti bhāva: ||6||

19. bhūmādhikaraṇam || 1-3-2 ||
72. bhūmā samprasādādadhyupadeśāt || 1-3-7 ||

'idamāmananti cchandogāḥ '

'yatra nā'nyatpaśyati, nā'nyacchraṇoti, nā'nyadvijānāti, sa bhūmā | atha yatrā'nyatpaśyati, anyacchṛṇoti anyadvijānāti tadalpam ||'

iti ||

'atra' ayaṃ 'bhūma' śabda: bhāvapratyayānto vyutpāyate | tathā hi - pṛthvādiṣu 'bahu' śabda: payyate | tataḥ,
bhūmeti | paramātmaprakaraṇāvicchedena paramātmaparatvaṃ sādhitam ; tarhi jīvaprakaraṇā- vicchedena jīvaparatvam - iti śaṅkotthānāt saṅgatiḥ | iyaṃ pramāṇadvārikā | pūrvam, 'otaṃ manaḥ saha prāṇaiśca sarvaiḥ' iti prāṇasambandhinaḥ paramātmatvamuktam | atra prāṇasambandhina eva hiṃsāyogyatvaśravaṇāt jīvatvaṃ pratīyate iti arthadvārā ca saṅgatiḥ ||
atrā'yaṃ bhūmeti, viśeṣaviṣaya saṃśayasambhavapradarśanāya 'bhūma’śabdavyutpattiprakāramanusaran vicāraviṣayasaṃśayasiddhiṃ darśayati | tathā hīti, bhāvapratyayāntatvamupapādayati | paṭhyate iti,

jīvādūrdhvaṃ copadeśāt bhūmā'tra paramaḥ pumān |
sanatkumāraguruṇā nāradāya viśeṣataḥ ||

pṛthvādibhya imanijvā'
iti 'imanic' pratyaye kṛte,
'bahorlopo bhū ca bahoḥ'

iti prakṛti-pratyayayoḥ vikāre, "bhūmā' iti bhavati | bhūmā – bahutvamityarthaḥ | 'atra ca', ayaṃ 'bahu' śabda: vaipulyavācī, na saṅkhyāvācī ;
'yatrā'nyatpaśyati .tadalpam'
iti alpapratiyogitvaśravaṇāt | "alpa' śabda nirdiṣṭadharmi pratiyogipratipādanaparatvādeva, 'dharmiparaśca' niścīyate ; na dharmamātraparaḥ | tadevaṃ, bhūmeti vipula ityarthaḥ | 'vaipulyaviśeṣyaśca' iha, ātmā ityavagataḥ;

'tarati śokamātmavit'
iti prakramya bhūmavijñānam upadiśya, 'ātmaivedaṃ sarvam'
iti tasyaiva upasaṃhārāt ||

atra saṃśayyate - kimayaṃ bhūmaguṇaviśiṣṭaḥ pratyagātmā ? uta paramātmā ? - iti || ākṛtigaṇatvavyāvṛttyartham | vikāre iti | 'bahu' śabdātparasya pratyayasya āderaco lopa:, 'ādeḥ parasya' iti sūtravaśāt | 'bahu' śabdasya 'bhū'śabdādeśaśca, ityevaṃ prakṛti - pratyayayorvikāraḥ |
atra cāyāmiti | 'bahu' śabdasya 'bahavaḥ puruṣāḥ' ityādau saṅkhyāyām, 'alpaṃ vā bahu vā' ityādī vaipulye ca prayogo dṛśyate | tasmāt saṅkhyāvyāvṛttyartham | tadevamiti | 'bahu' śabdasya saṅkhyā - vipulatvayoḥ prayogadarśanāt 'alpa'śabdasya saṅkhyāyāṃ prayogādarśanācca ananyathā siddhā lpaśabdānuguṇārtha : 'bahu' śabda iti | prakramya .... upasaṃhārāditi | upakramopasaṃhārayoḥ 'ātma' śabdadarśanāt ityarthaḥ ||
atra saṃśayyate iti | - 1. kiṃ bhūmā, pratyagātmā ? uta paramātmā ? 2. prāṇaśabdanirdiṣṭe pratyagātmani praśna prativacanasamādhyā 'bhūma' śabda : tatra paryavasitaḥ uta atiriktaviṣayaḥ ? 3. kim eṣa tu vā ativadati, yaḥ satyenāti

kiṃ yuktam ? pratyagātmā - iti |

kuta: ?

'śrutaṃ hyeva me bhagavaddyaśebhyaḥ, tarati śokamātmavit' iti ātmajijñāsayā upaseduṣe nāradāya, nāmādiprāṇaparyanteṣu upāsyatayā upadiṣṭeṣu, 'asti bhagavo nāmno bhūyaḥ'
'asti bhagavo vāco bhūyaḥ'

ityādayaḥ praśnāḥ,

'vāgvāva nāmno bhūyasī'
'mano vāva vāco bhūyaḥ'

ityādīni ca prativacanāni, prāṇāt prācīneṣu dṛśyante ; prāṇe tu na paśyāmaḥ | ataḥ prāṇaparyanta evāyam ātmopadeśaḥ – iti pratīyate | tena iha 'prāṇa' śabda nirdiṣṭaḥ, prāṇasahacārī pratyagātmaiva ; na vāyuviśeṣamātram |

vadati' iti prāṇātivādinaḥ aṅgatayā satyavacanaṃ vidhatte ? uta 'satya' śabdavācya paropāsa kamya ativāditvaṃ vadati ? 4. kim asmin vākye prāṇātivādinaḥ pratyabhijñā asti ? na ? 5. 'tu' śabda: kiṃ pratijñāvirodhī ? na ? - iti |
pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ yadā 'tu'śabdaḥ pratyabhijñāvirodhī na bhavati tadā prāṇātivādinaḥ pratyamijñāsambhavāt, 'eṣa tu vā' iti vākyaṃ tadaṅgabhūta satyavacana vidhiparamiti, prāṇātiriktavastva- bhāvāt, 'bhūma' śabda: prāṇe paryavasita iti bhūmā pratyagātmā iti |

rāddhāntaḥ—yadā 'tu'śabdaḥ pratyabhijñāvirodhī bhavati tadā prāṇātivādinaḥ pratyabhi- jñānābhāvena, 'satyenātivadati' iti vākyasya satyopāsakā tivadanaparatvena prāṇātiriktavastu- sadbhāvena, praśna- prativacanasamāptāvapi 'bhūma' śabdasya prāṇaparyavasitatvabhāvāt, bhūmā paramātmā- iti |

'prāṇo ha pitā, prāṇo ha mātā' ityādayaśca prāṇasya cetanatāmavagamayanti |
'pitṛhā... mātṛhā'

ityādinā, saprāṇeṣu pitṛprabhṛtiṣu upamardakāriṇi hiṃsakatvanimittopakrośavacanāt, teṣveva vigataprāṇeṣu atyantopamardakāriṇyapi upakrośābhāvavacanācca hiṃsāyogyaḥ cetana eva prāṇaśabda nirdiṣṭaḥ | aprāṇeṣu sthāvareṣvapi cetaneṣu upamardabhāvābhāvayoḥ hiṃsā - tadabhāvadarśanāt, ayaṃ hiṃsāyogyatayā nirdiṣṭaḥ prāṇaḥ, 'pratyagātmaiva' iti niścīyate | ata eva ca, ara- nābhidṛṣṭāntādyupanyāsena 'prāṇaśabda nirdiṣṭaḥ paraḥ' iti na śramitavyam parasya hiṃsāprasaṅgābhāvāt jīvāditarasya tadbhogya- 'bhogopakaraṇa- bhūtasya' kṛtsnasya acidvastunaḥ jīvāyattasthititvena pratyagātmanyeva ara- nābhidṛṣṭānto- papatteśca |

ayameva ca prāṇaśabdanirdiṣṭo bhūmā; 'asti bhagavaḥ prāṇādbhayaḥ' iti praśnasya, 'ado vāva prāṇādbhūyaḥ' iti prativacanasya ca abhāvāt, "bhūmasaṃśabdanāt prāk 'prāṇa prakaraṇasya' avicchedāt ||

kiṃ ca - prāṇavedinaḥ ativāditvamuktvā, tameva

'eṣa tu vā ativadati'

iti pratyabhijñāpya,

'yassatyenātivadati'

iti, tasya 'satyavadanaṃ' prāṇopāsanāṅgatayā' upadiśya', upādeyasya satyavadanasya 'śeṣitayā ' pūrvanirdiṣṭaprāṇayāthātmya vijñānam,
'yadā vai vijānātyatha satyaṃ vadati
iti upadiśya, 'tatsiddhayarthaṃ ca ' 'manana' -śraddhā-niṣṭhā prayatnān upadiśya, tadārambhāya ca, prāpyabhūtaprāṇaśabda nirdiṣṭapratyagātmasvarūpasya sukharūpatājñānam upadiśya, tasya ca sukhasya vipulatā,
'bhūmā tveva vijijñāsitavyaḥ'
ityupadiśyate | tadevaṃ pratyagātmana eva avidyāviyuktaṃ rūpaṃ vipulasukhamityupa- diṣṭamiti ;

' tarati śokamātmavit'
ityupakramāvirodhaśca | ataḥ bhūmaguṇaviśiṣṭaḥ pratyagātmā |

yata evaṃ bhūmaguṇaviśiṣṭaḥ

pratyagātma | ", " śrata eva ' ahamarthe pratyagātmani,

"ata

'ahamevā'dhastāt ahamupariṣṭāt'

bhūmaguṇaviśiṣṭaḥ na pratyagātmā ; api tu paramātmā | kutaḥ 1

saṃprasādādadhyupadeśāt | samprasādaḥ - pratyagātmā; 'eṣa samprasādo'smāccharīrāt samutthāya, paraṃ jyotirūpasampadya, svena rūpeṇā'bhiniṣpadyate

iti upaniṣatprasiddheḥ | samprasādāt — pratyagātmanaḥ, adhikatayā 'bhūmaviśiṣṭasya' satyaśabdābhidheyasya upadeśāt ityarthaḥ | satyaśabdābhidheyaṃ ca paraṃ brahma |
etaduktaṃ bhavati - yathā nāmādiṣu prāṇaparyanteṣu pūrvapūrvādhikatayā uttarottarā- bhidhānāt pūrvebhyaḥ uttareṣām arthāntaratvam evaṃ prāṇaśabda 'nirdiṣṭāt pratyagātmanaḥ adhikatayā nirdiṣṭaḥ " satyaśabdābhidheyaḥ, tasmāt arthāntarabhūta eva ; satyaśabdanirdiṣṭa eva bhūmā iti, 'satyākhyaṃ paraṃ brahmaiva bhūmā' ityupadiśyate iti |

tadāha vṛttikāraḥ—

'bhūmā tveveti bhūmā 'brahma'; nāmādiparamparayā ātmana ūrdhvamasyopadeśāt '

iti |

prāṇaśabda nirdiṣṭāt adhikatayā satyasya upadeśaḥ kathamavagamyate ? – iti cet,
sūtre samprasādanirdeśa: śuddhasvarūpābhiprāyeṇa kṛtaḥ ||
satyeti | 'satya' śabdātpaścāt prakaraṇavicchedābhāvena 'satya' śabdavācya eva bhūma- viśiṣṭaḥ iti bhāvaḥ |

etaduktamiti | pūrvanirdiṣṭā dūrdhvamabhidhānaṃ hi pūrvanirdiṣṭapratiyogitayā uttarottareṣā- madhikatayā arthāntaratve hetuḥ | yatheti | hetoḥ sapakṣe vyāptiḥ | evaṃśabdena heturabhipretaḥ |
bhūmā tveva .... śāt iti | pūrvanirdiṣṭādadhikatayā uttarottaranirdeśa prakaraṇatvāt, 'prāṇa'- śabdavācyāt jīvādūrdhvamuktaḥ 'satya' śabdavācyaḥ upāsyo bhūmā, tato'dhikaṃ paraṃ brahma iti vṛttikāragranthasyārthaḥ | ātmana ūrdhvam ityanena 'prāṇa' śabda nirdiṣṭasya jīvātmatvaṃ

śrī bhāṣyam 1-3-2]

'sa vā eṣa evaṃ paśyannavaṃ manvāna evaṃ vijānan ativādī bhavati'

iti, prāṇavidaḥ ativāditvamuktvā,

'eva tu vā ativadati, yassatyenātivadati' iti, satyaveditvena ativādinaṃ 'tu' śabdena pūrvasmāt ativādinaḥ vyāvartayati ||

'ata eva -

'eṣa tu vā ativadati'

ityatra prāṇātivādinaḥ na pratyabhijñā | ataḥ asya ativāditvanimittaṃ satyam, pūrvātivāditvanimittāt prāṇāt adhikam iti vijñāyate |
nanu ca - prāṇavedina' eva satyavadanam aṅgatvena upadiṣṭam | ataḥ prāṇa- prakaraṇāvicchedaḥ - ityuktam |

naitadyuktam ; "tu' śabdena hi ativādyeva anyaḥ pratīyate ; na tu tasyaiva ativādinaḥ satyavadanāṅgaviśiṣṭatāmātram ||
vṛttikārābhimatam ; na tu pañcavṛttiprāṇaparatvam ityavagatam | ataḥ 'prāṇa' śabdavācyasya jīvatvamāśrityaiva pūrvapakṣa siddhāntavarṇanaṃ vṛttikārābhimatamiti jñāpanam, vṛttikāragranthopādānaphalam | vyāvartayatīti | adhikatayā vyāvartayatītyarthaḥ | upāsakādhikyāt tannimittopāsyādhikyaṃ vaktuṃ tuśabdasvārasyena upāsakavailakṣaṇyamuktam | tuśabdo hi asaṅkucitavṛttiḥ dharma-dharmiṇoḥ aviśeṣeṇa bhedakaḥ ||
ata eveti | prakṛta- prakariṣyamāṇasādhāraṇaḥ etacchabda: ; nā'yamekāntataḥ prakṛtamabhi- niviśate | pāścātyayadvṛttapratinirdeśarūpatvācca nā'yaṃ prakṛtaparaḥ | tuśabdasvārasyaṃ ca prakaraṇa- vicchedakam | ataḥ pūrvātivādinaḥ pratyabhijñā nāstīti ativādyantaraṃ pratipannamityarthaḥ ||
agnihotryantarāpratīteriti | ativādisthānīyaḥ agnihotrī ; upāsanasthānīyamagni- hotram | dravya-devatā sthānīyam upāsyam | atra agnihotryantarāpratītiḥ, agnihotrāntarā pratīteḥ | sā ca dravya-devatāviśeṣāpratīteḥ | sā ca 'satyavacana' śabdasya dadhyādidravya devatā viśeṣopa-

' epa tu vā agnihotrī, yassatyaṃ vadati'

ityādiṣu agnihotryantarāpratīteḥ pratītasyaiva agnihotriṇaḥ satyavadanāṅgavidhānam iti kliṣṭā gatiḥ āśrīyate | atra tu ativādyantaratvanimittaṃ satyaśabdābhidheyaṃ paraṃ brahma

pratīyate | satyaśabdava,
'satyaṃ jñānamanantaṃ brahma'
ityādiṣu parasmin brahmaṇi prayuktaḥ ||

ataḥ tanniṣṭasya ativādinaḥ pūrvasmāt adhikatvaṃ sambhavati' iti, vākyasvarasa- siddham anyatvaṃ na bādhitavyam | ativāditvaṃ hi vastvantarāt puruṣārthatayā ati- krāntastropāsyavastuvāditvam | nāmādyāśāparyantopāsyavastvatikrāntasvopaḥ syaprāṇaśabda- nirdiṣṭapratyagātmavāditvāt prāṇavidaḥ, ativāditvam | tasyā'pi sātiśayapuruṣārthatvāt, niratiśaya puruṣārthatayā upāsya parabrahmavādina eva sākṣādativāditvamiti,
'eṣa tu vā ativadati, yassatyenātivadati'

ityuktam ||

sthāpanāsāmarthyāt | evam agnihotryantarasamarpaka śabdābhāvāt tatra 'tu' śabdasvārasyaṃ bhagnam ityarthaḥ | atra tviti | atra tu ativādyantaratvanimittam upāsanāntaraṃ taddhetubhūtamupāsyāntaraṃ ca pratīyate | iha tatsamarpaṇa samarthasya 'satya' śabdasya sadbhāvāt, 'tu' śabdasvārasyaṃ na bhañjanīyam | ataḥ ativādyantaraṃ siddhamityarthaḥ ||
ativāditvaṃ hīti | na hi prāṇātivādinyeva 'ativādi' śabdoṃ rūḍhaḥ ; 'klṛptāva - yavaśaktyaiva labdhe svārthāvabodhane | naṣṭāvayavanāmatvaṃ pratyācaṣṭe hi sūtrakṛt' iti darśitāt, 'prokṣaṇīṣvarthasaṃyogāt' iti sūtritāt prokṣaṇīnayāt, avayavaśaktyā svārthabodhe sambhavati, rūḍhi kalpanā'yogāt | ataḥ na prāṇātivādinyeva 'ativādi 'śabdasvārasyam ityarthaḥ | tasyāpīti | asya śabdapravṛttinimittasya paramātmopāsake evaṃ puṣkalatvāt, tasminneva 'ativādi' śabdo mukhya iti, 'yaḥ satyenātivadati ' iti vadata ācāryasyābhiprāya: | tuśabdātivādiśabdayorubhayorapi asmatpakṣa eva svārasyamiti bhāvaḥ |

'satyena ' iti itthambhūtalakṣaṇe tṛtīyā | satyena – pareṇa brahmaṇā upāsyena upalakṣitaḥ yaḥ 'ativadati ityarthaḥ | ata eva evaṃ śiṣyaḥ prārthayate -

'soshaṃ bhagavaḥ satyenātivadāni'

iti | ācāryaśca,

'satyaṃ tveva vijijñāsitavyam'

ityāha ||

'ātmataḥ prāṇaḥ'
iti ca prāṇaśabda nirdiṣṭasya 'ātmana' utpattirucyate | ataḥ, ' tarati śokamātmavit'
iti prakrāntaḥ ātmā, prāṇaśabda nirdiṣṭādanyaḥ - iti gamyate ||

" yattaktam " - ' asti bhagavaḥ prāṇādbhūyaḥ' iti praśnasya, 'ado vāca prāṇādbhūyaḥ' iti prativacanasya ca adarśanāt prakrāntaḥ ātmopadeśaḥ, prāṇopadeśaparyavasāno gamyate iti,

tadayuktam ; na hi praśnaprativacanābhyāmeva arthāntaratvaṃ gamyate ; pramāṇāntareṇā'pi tatsambhavāt | uktaṃ ca pramāṇāntaram ||

tṛtīyeti | satyavadanapakṣe eva tṛtīyā'nupapattiḥ upasarga (ati) vaiyarthyaṃ ca iti bhāvaḥ | ata eva – 'satya' śabdasya upāsyāntaraparatvādeva | 'so'haṃ bhagavaḥ satyenātivadāni' iti śiṣyaḥ prārthayate ; na tu prāṇenātivadānīti ityarthaḥ | ācāryaśceti | 'satyaṃ tveva vijijñā- sitavyam' ityāha ; na tu prāṇa eveti ityarthaḥ (śru pra ||
ātmata iti sūtrābhipretena hetvantareṇā'pi adhikatayā upāsyāntaratvamupapādayan samprasādādadhyupadeśaṃ vivṛṇoti |
uktaṃ ceti | praśna prativacane hi liṅgam | tadabhāve'pi 'tu' śabdaśrutiḥ, ātmanaḥ prāṇotpattiśca liṅgamiti pramāṇāntaramuktam ityarthaḥ ||

'asti bhagavaḥ prāṇādbhūyaḥ' ityapṛcchataḥ ayamabhiprāyaḥ – nāmādiṣu āśāparyanteṣu acetaneṣu puruṣārthabhūyastayā pūrvapūrvamati' krānteṣvapi ' 'uttarottareṣu' upadiṣṭeṣu, tattadvedinaḥ ācāryeṇa ativāditvaṃ noktam | prāṇaśabdanirdiṣṭa pratyagātmayāthātmyavedinastu puruṣārtha bhūyastvātiśayaṃ manvānena,
'sa vā eṣa evaṃ paśyanevaṃ manvāna evaṃ vijānannativādī bhavati' iti, atikrāntavastuvāditvamuktam | ataḥ atraiva ātmopadeśaḥ samāptaḥ iti matvā, śiṣyaḥ bhūyo na papraccha |

ācāryastu idamapi sātiśayaṃ matvā, niratiśayapuruṣārthabhūtaṃ 'satya' śabdābhidheyaṃ

paraṃ brahma,

'eṣa tu vā ativadati, yassatyenātivadati'
iti svayameva upacikṣepa | śiṣyo'pi paramapuruṣārtharūpe parasmin brahmaṇi upakṣipte, tatsvarūpa- tadupāsanayāthātmya bubhutsayā,
'soshaṃ bhagavaḥ satyenātivadāni'

iti prārthayāmāsa ||

tataḥ brahmasākṣātkāranimittātivāditvasiddhaye, brahmasākṣātkāropāyabhūtaṃ brahmopā- sanam,
puruṣārtha.... manvāneneti | ātmopadeśasya tatparyantatvaṃ manvānenetyarthaḥ || upacikṣapeti | bhagavaṅgītāyāmapi 'tasmādyogī bhavā'rjuna' iti jīvātmayogaṃ parisamāpya 'yogināmapi sarveṣāṃ maganenāntarātmanā | śraddhāvān bhajate yo māṃ sa me yuktatamo mataḥ || ' iti paramātmopāsanaṃ bhagavāneva hi upakṣiptavān tadvat iti bhāvaḥ ||

brahmasākṣātkāranimittetyanena, 'yadā vai vijānāti atha satyaṃ vadati' ityasya vākyasya arthaḥ ukto bhavati | vijānātiśabdaḥ, sākṣātkāraparaḥ na tu śāstrajanyajñānaparaḥ; 'yadā vai manute, atha vijānāti' iti jānātyarthasya mananasādhyatvāvagamāt | satyaṃ vadati – satya -

'satyaṃ tveva vijijñāsitavyam' ityupadiśya, tadupāyabhūtaṃ brahmamananam,
'matistveva vijijñāsitavyā'
ityupadiśya, śravaṇapratiṣṭhārthatvāt mananasya, mananopadeśena śravaṇam' arthasiddhaṃ matvā, śravaṇopāyabhūtāṃ brahmaṇi śraddhām,
'śraddhā tveva vijijñāsitavyā'
ityupadiśya, tadupāyabhūtāṃ ca tanniṣṭām
'niṣṭhā tveva vijijñāsitavyā' ityupadiśya, tadupāyabhūtāṃ ca tadudyoga' prayatna rūpāṃ kṛtimapi, 'kṛtistveva vijijñāsitavyā'
ityupadiśya, śravaṇādyupakramarūpakṛtisiddhaye, prāpyabhūtasya satyaśabdābhihitasya brahmaṇaḥ sukharūpatā jñātavyā iti,
mativadati, ityarthaḥ ; ativādasya prakṛtatvāt | yadā brahma sākṣātkaroti; tadā ativadati | tasmāt ativāditvanimitta-tatsākṣātkārāya tadupāyabhūtaṃ satyopāsanaṃ kuryāt ityarthaḥ | śravaṇo ... śraddhāmiti | śravaṇa-manana nididhyāsana-prāptiviṣayo hi brahma | tadviṣayaśraddhā ca tat śrotuṃ | mantuṃ draṣṭuṃ prāptuṃ ca bhavati | tatra 'śravaṇopāyabhūtāṃ śraddhām' ityanena śrotuṃ śraddhā vivakṣitā | śraddhā - tvarā | tanniṣṭhā - tasmin brahmaṇi niṣṭhā | ' brahmaiva śrotavyaṃ nā'nyat' iti vyava - sāya: - niṣṭhā | śrotavyatvaniścaye hi śrotuṃ tvarā bhavati | tadudyogeti | udyogaśabda- vācyaprayatna rūpāṃ kṛtim ityarthaḥ | sā ca śrotavyāntareṣu heyatvānusandhānena manaso niyamanarūpā | anyasmin upādeyabuddhyā pravaṇamanaskasya hi 'brahmaiva śrotavyaṃ, nā'nyat' ityadhyavasāyo na bhavati | śravaṇādīti | jijñāsitasya puruṣārthatve, tasya niratiśayatve ca jñāte sati manasaḥ sātiśayapuruṣārthāntaravaimukhyakaraṇaṃ sambhavati ityabhiprāyaḥ | sukharūpateti | niratiśaya- puruṣārthatvāvagamaśca śāstrādeva āpātapratītidaśāyāṃ bhavati ; yādṛcchika sajjana saṃvādādinā ca siddhayati | 'yadā vai śraddhadhāti...' mananādīnāṃ śraddhādyānantaryaśravaṇāt bhāṣye teṣāmupāyopeyabhāvaḥ uktaḥ | 'yadā vai sukhaṃ labhate' iti śrutau 'lābha' śabdo buddhivācī ; labhateḥ prātyarthatvāt ityabhiprāyaḥ ; na hi śravaṇādeḥ pūrvameva niratiśaya sukhaprāptirupapadyate | vijijñāsitavyamiti | satyavyatiriktaviṣayāṇi 'jijñāsitavya' padāni sampādyatvavācīni ||

'sukhaṃ tveva vijijñāsitavyam'

ityupadiśya, niratiśayavipulameva sukhaṃ paramapuruṣārtharūpaṃ bhavati iti, tasyaiva brahmaṇaḥ sukharūpasya niratiśaya' vipulatā' jñātavyā iti,

tveva vijijñāsitavyaḥ'
ityupadiśya, niratiśayavipulasukharūpasya brahmaṇaḥ lakṣaṇamidamucyate

bhūmā

'yatra nā'nyatpaśyati, nā'nyacchuṇoti, nā'nyadvijānāti, sa bhūmā '

iti ||

ayamarthaḥ – anavadhikātiśayasukharūpe brahmaṇi anubhūyamāne, tato'nyatkimapi na paśyati 'anubhavitā' ; brahmasvarūpa - tadvibhūtyantargatatvācca kṛtsnasya vastujātasya | ataḥ aiśvaryāparaparyāya vibhūtiguṇaviśiṣṭaṃ, niratiśayasukharūpaṃ brahma anubhavan tavyatiriktasya vastunaḥ abhāvādeva kimapyanyat na paśyati | anubhāvyasya sarvasya sukharūpatvādeva duḥkhaṃ ca na paśyati | tadeva hi sukham ; yadanubhūyamānaṃ puruṣānukūlaṃ bhavati ||

ayamartha iti 'yatra nānyaditi vākye anyadarśanaśravaṇādiniṣedhāt, dṛśya mithyātvaśaṅkā syāditi tadvyudāsāya | anubhūyamāne iti, arthasvabhāvādadhyāhāraḥ kṛtaḥ ; na hi brahmasadbhāvāt duḥkharūpavastvantarādarśana bhavati | tato'nyat - sukharūpāt brahmaṇo'nyat | na paśyati---duḥkharūpaṃ tadvyatiriktaṃ ca kiṃcinna paśyati ityarthaḥ | tatra duḥkhadarśanābhāvahetuḥ anavadhikā tiśaya sukharūpatvam | kṛtsnasyeti | svarūpāntargatāḥ jñānādiguṇāḥ, vibhūtyantargataṃ jagadakhilam ityabhiprāyaḥ | abhipreta hetusamuccayārthaḥ caśabdaḥ | ataḥ – brahmaṇaḥ sukharūpatāyāḥ, kṛtsnavibhūteḥ tadantarbhāvasya ca prāmāṇikatvāt | vibhūtiviṣayo guṇaḥ vibhūtiguṇaḥ | sa eva aiśvaryāparaparyāyaṃ niyamanam ; tadviśiṣṭaṃ brahma | na hi īśitavyajñānaṃ vinā aiśvarya viśiṣṭānubhavasambhavaḥ ; nirūpakeṇa vinā nirūpyajñānāyogāt | tadvyatiriktasyeti | viśiṣṭabrahmaṇaḥ sakāśāt ) anyadarśana niṣidhyate ; na tu (kevalam ) anyadarśanaṃ ityarthaḥ | sarvasyeti | savibhūtikasyetyarthaḥ | pūrvābhipratādeva hetoḥ iti, ekkārābhiyaḥ | anukūlamiti | anukūlatvaṃ nāma svata iṣṭatvam | tacca aniṣṭa nivṛttirūpatayā, iṣṭasādhanatayā ca vinā'pi iṣṭatvam ||

nanu ca - idameva jagat, brahmaṇo'nyatayā anubhūyamānaṃ, duḥkharūpaṃ parimita- 'sukharūpaṃ ca ' bhavat, kathamiva "brahmavibhūtitvena tadātmakatayā'nubhūyamānaṃ, sukharūpameva bhavet ?

ucyate - karmavaśyānāṃ kṣetrajñānāṃ 'brahmaṇo'nyatvena' anubhūyamānaṃ kṛtsnaṃ jagat, tattatkarmānurūpaṃ duḥkhaṃ ca parimitasukhaṃ ca bhavati | ataḥ 'brahmaṇo'nyatayā ', parimiti - sukhatvena, "duḥkhatvena ca ' jagadanubhavasya karmanimittatvāt karmarūpāvidyā" vimuktasya', tadeva jagat vibhūtiguṇaviśiṣṭatrahmānubhavāntargataṃ sukhameva bhavati yathā – pittopahatena pīyamānaṃ payaḥ pittatāratamyena alpasukhaṃ, viparītaṃ ca bhavati tadeva payaḥ pittānu pahatasya sukhāyaiva bhavati | 'yathaiva' rājaputrasya piturlīlopakaraṇam atathātvenānu- 'sandhīyamānaṃ priyatvamanupagataṃ, 'tathātvānusandhāne' priyatamaṃ bhavati, tathā - " nirati- śayā "nanda "svarūpasya " brahmaṇaḥ anavadhikātiśayāsaṅkhyeyakalyāṇa " guṇākarasya " līlo- pakaraṇaṃ, tadātmakaṃ ca anusandhīyamānaṃ jagat niratiśayaprītaye bhavatyeva ||

|

ataḥ jagadaiśvaryaviśiṣṭam anavādhikātiśayasukharūpaṃ brahma anubhavan tato'nya ṭika- mapi na paśyati, duḥkhaṃ ca na paśyati | etadeva upapādayati vākyaśeṣaḥ-
nanu ceti | na hi ekam ekadaiva sukha-duḥkhātmaka viruddhasvabhāvāliṅgitamiti yujyate | na ca jagataḥ śakyaṃ duḥkharūpatvamapotum | ataḥ sukharūpatvameva tyaktavyamityabhiprāyaḥ | ucyata iti | svato'paricchinnasukharūpasyā'pi (brahmaṇa:) upādhitaḥ anyathārūpasambhavāt avirodhaḥ | karma, tattirodhānakṛtam abrahmātmakatvajñānaṃ ca upādhi : (iti bhāvaḥ) | yathetyādi svato madhurasyā'pi doṣasambandhādananukūlatve, anukūla buddhya viṣayasya kenacitsamvandhitvajñānāt anukūlatve ca dṛṣṭāntadvayam ||
etat – aupādhikaduḥkharupatāyā:, tadvigame anavasthānam | ratyādiśabdānāṃ prītiparatve, vyadhikaraṇabahuvrīhiḥ [ātmani

'sa vā eṣa evaṃ paśyanevaṃ manvāna evaṃ vijānannātmaratirātmakrīḍaḥ ātmamithuna ātmānandassa svarāḍbhavati, tasya sarveṣu lokeṣu kāmacāro bhavati, atha ye'nyathā'to viduranyarājānaste kṣayya-

lokā bhavanti, teṣāṃ sarveṣu lokeṣvakāmacāro bhavati' iti | svarāṭ – akarmavaśyaḥ | anyarājānaḥ- karmavaśyāḥ | tathā-

'na paśyo mṛtyuṃ paśyati na rogaṃ nota duḥkhatām |
sarvaṃ ha paśyaḥ paśyati sarvamāmoti sarvaśaḥ ||'

'iti ca ' ||

73. dharmopapatteśca || 1-3-8||

'sve mahimni'

74. akṣaramambarāntavṛteḥ || 1-3-9 ||

vājasaneyinaḥ gārgipraśre samāmananti-
'sa hovāca etadvai tadakṣaraṃ gārgi brāhmaṇā abhivadanti, asthūlamanaṇvahrasvama dīrgha ma lohitamasnehamacchāyam'

ityādi ||

tatra saṃśayaḥ -- kim etadakṣaraṃ pradhānam ? jīvo vā ? uta paramātmā ? - iti ||

kiṃ yuktam ? 'pradhānam' – iti |

akṣaramiti | pūrvādhikaraṇe sve mahimni pratiṣṭhitatvamuktam | tattu paramātmavyatiriktasya

śrūyate iti śaṅkayā saṅgatiḥ ||

و

paramātmā vā : - iti saṃśayaḥ |

saṃśaya iti | akṣaram - pradhānaṃ vā ? jīvo vā ?
tadartham —laukikapramāṇāviṣaye śabdapratītiśaitrayamasti na

iti | ' kasminnu khalvākāśa

otaśca protaśca' ityatra 'ākāśa' śabda nirdiṣṭaḥ - kiṃ bhūtākāśaḥ ? uta avyākṛtākāśaḥ ? - iti | dhāraṇaṃ ca kiṃ sattāmātreṇa ? uta praśāsanena ? – iti ca |
1. yadā pratītiśaiprayamasti, 'ākāśa' śabdavācyaśca bhūtākāśaḥ, dhāraṇaṃ ca sattayā syāt ; tadā taddhāraṇasya pradhāne'sambhavāt taddhārakam akṣaraṃ pradhānam |

akṣaraṃ tu paraṃ brahma vyākṛtaṣṭateriha |
otatvena ca sarvasya protatvenā'kṣare śruteḥ ||

1 pradhānameva - i.

kutaḥ ?

'akṣarātparataḥ paraḥ'

ityādiṣu 'akṣara' śabdasya pradhāne prayogadarśanāt, asthūlatvādīnāṃ 'ca tatra '

samanvayāt ||

'yayā tadakṣaramadhigamyate '
ityādiṣu parasminnapi 'akṣara' śabdo dṛśyate - iti cet, na; pramāṇāntaraprasiddha - śrutiprasiddhayoḥ
pratītaparigrahe 'virodhābhāvāt | kiṃ ca'

pramāṇāntaraprasiddhasya prathamapratīteḥ

'yadūrdhvaṃ gārgi divo yadavaka pṛthivyāḥ' ityārabhya, sarvasya kālatritayavartinaḥ' kāraṇabhūtākāśādhāratve pratipādite, 'kasminnu khalvākāśa otaśca protaśca'
iti, ākāśasyāpi kāraṇaṃ tadādhārabhūtaṃ kim ? – iti pṛṣṭe, pratyucyamānam akṣaraṃ, sarvavikārakāraṇatayā tadādhārabhūtaṃ pramāṇāntaraprasiddhaṃ pradhānam - iti pratīyate | ataḥ akṣaraṃ pradhānam ||

iti prāpte, ucyate-akṣaramambarāntadhṛteḥ | akṣaram,

paraṃ brahma |

2. yadā, 'ākāśa' śabdavācyaḥ avyākṛtākāśaḥ, dhāraṇaṃ ca sattāmātreṇa; tadā jīve'pi tatsambhavāt, taddhārakam akṣaraṃ, jīvaḥ - |

rāddhānte – yadā śabdapratītiśaiprayasya laukikapramāṇagocaratvam aprayojakam, 'ākāśa' śabdavācyaśca avyākṛtā kāśaḥ, dhāraṇaṃ ca praśāsanena tadā tasya jīveṣvasambhavāt, tadādhāra-

bhūtamakṣaram paramātmā - (śru pra ) |

na pramāṇāntareti | anumānasya pratyakṣamātrāpekṣā; śrutestu vyutpantyādau ubhayāpekṣā'sti | ataḥ ānumānikādakṣarāt, śrutipratipannasya vilamba iti, laukikālaukikayoḥ laukikasya prasiddhiprāthamyāt prābalyam | śrutiliṅgādiṣvapi hi śīghradhahetutvāt prābalyamiti bhāvaḥ | virodhābhāvāditi | 'akṣara' śabdasya pradhānaparatvamityarthaḥ ||
ambarānteti | 'anta' śabda: avadhivacana: | kāryasya ambarasya, deśataḥ kālata-
1 tana-tha, ca tatraivavā " virodhābhāvāt - ca, ṭha.

ambarāntadhṛteḥ ; ambarasya – ākāśasya, antaḥ - 'pārabhūtam' avyākṛtam, ambarāntaḥ | tasya dhṛteḥ - tadā dhāratayā asya akṣarasya upadeśāt iti yāvat ||

ayamarthaḥ -

'kasminnu khalvākāśa otaśca protaśca'

ityatra 'ākāśa' śabdanirdiṣṭaṃ, na vāyumadambaram api tu tatpārabhūtam avyākṛtam | ataḥ tasya avyākṛtasyā'pi ādhāratvena ucyamānam akṣaraṃ, na avyākṛtaṃ bhavitumarhati - iti ||

nanu 'ākāśa' śabda nirdiṣṭaḥ, na vāyumān - iti kathamavagamyate ? ucyate-
'yadūrdhvaṃ gārgi divo yadarvāk pṛthivyā yadantarā dyāvā - pṛthivī ime yadbhūtaṃ ca bhavacca' bhaviṣya cetyācakṣate ākāśa

eva tadotaṃ ca protaśca'

yattu śrutiprasiddhāt pramāṇāntaraprasiddhaṃ prathamaṃ pratīyate - iti,

'akṣara' śabdasya avayavaśaktyā svārthapratipādane pramāṇāntarānapekṣaṇāt | sambandhagrahaṇadaśāyām arthasvarūpaṃ yena pramāṇena avagamyate, na tat pratipādanadaśāyā- mapekṣaṇīyam ||9||

tanna ;

evaṃ tarhi—'akṣara' śabdanirdiṣṭaḥ jīvo'stu tasya, bhūtasūkṣmaparyantasya kṛtsnasya acidvastunaḥ 'ādhāratvopapatteḥ', asthūlatvādyucyamānaviśeṣaṇopapatteśca |

'avyaktamakṣare līyate'
'yasyāsvyakta śarīraṃ.... yasyākṣaraṃ śarīram'

'kṣaraḥ sarvāṇi bhūtāni kūṭastho'kṣara ucyate ityādiṣu, pratyagātmanyapi akṣaraśabdaprayogadarśanāt – ityatra uttaram ;
75. sā ca praśāsanāt || 1-3-10 ||
sā ca – ambarāntadhṛtiḥ, asya akṣarasya praśāsanādeva bhavati ; ityupadiśyate- 'etasya vā akṣarasya praśāsane gārgi ! sūryācandramasau vidhṛtau tiṣṭhataḥ | etasya vā akṣarasya praśāsane gārgi ! dyāvāpṛthivyau vidhṛte tiṣṭhataḥ | etasya vā akṣarasya praśāsane gārgi ! nimeṣā muhūtī ahorātrāṇyardha-
māsā māsā ṛtavaḥ saṃvatsarā iti vidhṛtāstiṣṭhanti'
akṣaraśabdasyeti | avayavaśaktireva arthapratipattikāraṇamiti, na pramāṇāntaramapekṣitam | na hi 'daṇḍi 'śabdaḥ svārthapratipādane daṇḍadevadattagrāhipratyakṣāpekṣaḥ ||
avyaktamiti | 'avyaktamakṣare' iti avyaktasya pṛthagupādānāt 'akṣara' śabdaḥ jīvaparaḥ ; na pradhānaparaḥ, iti jñāpanāya avyaktamupāttam |

(bhagī 15 - 16)

praśāsane svakṣarasya vidhṛtāviti śaṃsanāt |
śāsanāt sā vidhṛtirityajño jīvo na kāraṇam ||

2 ādhā topapatteḥ tha.

ityādinā | praśāsanam - prakṛṣṭaṃ śāsanam | na ca – 'īdṛśaṃ svaśāsanādhīna 'sarvavastuvidharaṇaṃ, baddha - muktobhayāvasthasyā'pi pratyagātmanaḥ sambhavati | ataḥ puruṣottama eva praśāsita akṣaram ||10||
76. anyabhāvavyāvṛtteśca || 1-3 - 11 ||

anyabhāvaḥ - anyatvam, pradhānādibhāvaḥ | asya akṣarasya paramapuruṣādanyatvaṃ 'vākyaśeṣe' vyāvartyate-
" tadvā etadakṣaraṃ gārgi ! adṛṣṭaṃ draṣṭṛ, aśrutaṃ śrotu, amataṃ mantṛ, avijñātaṃ vijñātṛ, nā'nyadato'sti draṣṭṛ, nā'nyadato'sti śrotṛ, nā'nyadato'sti mantu, nā'nyadato'sti vijñātṛ, etasminnu khalvakṣare gārgi ! ākāśa otaśca protaśca'

evaṃ vā anyabhāvavyāvṛttiḥ | anyasya sadbhāvavyāvṛttiḥ - anyabhāvavyāvṛttiḥ | 'yathā' etadakṣaram anyairadṛṣṭaṃ sat, anyeṣāṃ draṣṭṛ ca sat svavyatiriktasya samastasya ādhārabhūtam evam "anyena' adṛṣṭam etasya draṣṭṛ ca sat, etasya ādhārabhūtam anyannāsti' iti vadan,

'nā'nyadato'sti draṣṭṛ '

ityādivākyaśeṣaḥ, anyasya sadbhāvaṃ vyāvartayan asya akṣarasya pradhānabhāvaṃ pratyagātma- bhāvaṃ ca pratipedhati ||

kiṃ ca-

'etasya vā akṣarasya praśāsane gārgi ! dadato manuṣyāḥ praśaṃsanti,
yajamānaṃ devāḥ, dava pitaro'nvāyattāḥ'
iti, śrautaṃ smārta ca yāga- dāna - homādikaṃ sarva karma yasya ājñayā pravartate ; tadakṣaraṃ parabrahmabhūtaḥ puruṣottama eva – iti vijñāyate |

api ca-

'yo vā etadakṣaraṃ gārgi ! aviditvā'smi~lloke juhoti, yajate, tapastapyate, bahūni varṣasahasrāṇi antavadevāsya tadbhavati | yo vā etadakṣaraṃ gārgi ! aviditvā'smāllokātpreti ; sa kṛpaṇaḥ | "atha yaṃ etadakṣaraṃ gārgi ! viditvā'smāllokātpreti sa brāhmaṇa: ' iti, yadajñānāt saṃsāraprāptiḥ, yajjñānācca amṛtatvaprāptiḥ, tadakṣaraṃ paraṃ brahmaiva - iti siddham ||11||

|| iti akṣarādhikaraṇam ||3||

kiṃ ca iti caśabdārthamāha | 'ca'śabdaḥ pūrvatrat na uktasamuccayaparaḥ ; anuktasamuccayaparaḥ ityabhiprāyaḥ | anvāyattāḥ - anuvaśāḥ | idaṃ padaṃ, deva. manuṣya- pitṛsādhāraṇam |
dvitīyāntapadānāṃ praśaṃsantītyanenā'nvayaḥ |
'yadaprabodhāt kārpaṇyaṃ brāhmaṇyaṃ yatprabodhataḥ |
tadakṣaraṃ praboddhavyaṃ yathoktasvaravartmanā ||' iti ||] || 11||

api tu

11. īkṣatikarmādhikaraṇam || 1-3-4 ||

paramātmaiva na samaṣṭipuruṣa: ]

77. īkṣatikarma vyapadeśāt saḥ || 1-3 - 12||

ātharvaṇikāḥ satyakāmapraśne adhīyate-

‘yaḥ punaretaṃ trimātreṇomityane (tyete) naivākṣareṇa 'paraṃ puruṣa 'mabhidhyāyīta, sa tejasi sūrye sampannaḥ ; yathā pādodarastvacā vinirmucyate, evaṃ 'ha vai' sa pāpmanā vinirmuktaḥ, sa sāmabhirunnīyate brahmalokam sa etasmājjīvadhanāt parātparaṃ puriśayaṃ puruṣamīkṣate'

iti ||

atra saḥ ityuktiḥ, asmin pāde sarvādhikaraṇeṣu sādhyapratijñānuvṛtyarthā | yathā prathama - pāde 'bhedavyapadeśāccā'nyaḥ' iti 'anya' śabdoktiḥ, yathā ca dvitīye, 'ata eva ca sa brahma' iti 'brahma' śabdoktiḥ, yathā caturthe ' vadatīti cenna prājñaḥ' iti 'prājña' śabdoktiśca ; tadvat | pūrvam 'adṛṣṭaṃ draṣṭṛ' iti paramātmanaḥ draṣṭṛtvamuktam ; tarhi īkṣatikarmatvenoktaḥ na paramātmā - iti śaṅkayā saṅgatiḥ |
sāmabhiḥ - ' etat sāmagāyannāste' itivat sāmagānasahitaiḥ |

'

atra 'dhyāyatīkṣati' śabdau ekaviṣayau; dhyānaphalatvāt īkṣaṇasya | 'yathākraturasmi~lloke puruṣaḥ'
iti nyāyena, dhyānaviṣayasyaiva prāpyatvāt,
" paraṃ puruṣam "
iti, ubhayatra karmabhūtasyārthasya pratyabhijñānācca ||

tatra saṃśayyate -- " kimiha',

" paraṃ puruṣam "

iti nirdiṣṭaḥ, jīvasamaṣṭirūpaḥ aṇḍādhipatiścaturmukhaḥ 1 uta sarveśvaraḥ puruṣottamaḥ ? iti ||

kiṃ yuktam 1 samaṣṭikṣetrajñaḥ - iti |

kuta: ?

'sa yo ha vai tadbhagavan ! manuṣyeṣu prāyaṇāntamoṅkāramabhidhyāyīta,
katamaṃ vā sa tena lokaṃ jayati'
iti prakramya, ekamātraṃ praṇavamupāsīnasya, manuṣyalokaprāptim abhidhāya, dvimātramupāsīnasya antarikṣalokaprāptim abhidhāya, trimātramupāsīnasya prāpyatayā abhidhīyamānaḥ ' brahmalokaḥ, antarikṣāt paraḥ jīvasamaṣṭirūpasya caturmukhasya lokaḥ - iti vijñāyate | tadgatena ca īkṣyamāṇaḥ tallokādhipatiḥ caturmukha eva |
'etasmājjīvaghanāt parātparam '

iti ca, dehendriyādibhyaḥ parāt dehendriyādibhiḥ saha ghanībhūtāt 'jīva' vyaṣṭipuruṣāt brahmalokavāsinaḥ samaṣṭipuruṣasya caturmukhasya paratvena, upapadyate| ataḥ atra nirdiśya- mānaḥ paraḥ puruṣaḥ ', "samaṣṭipuruṣaścaturmukha eva | " evaṃ caturmukhatve niścite', ajara- tvādayaḥ yathākathañcit 'netavyāḥ " ||

iti prāpte, pracakṣmahe — 'īkṣati' karma vyapadeśāt saḥ | īkṣatikarma, saḥ - paramātmā | kuta: ?
vyapadeśāt ; vyapadiśyate hi īkṣatikarma, paramātmatvena | karmaviṣayatayā udāhṛte śloke-

tathā hi, īkṣati-

caturmukhasya loka iti | bhūmyantarikṣakramaprāptaḥ caturmukhalokaḥ ityarthaḥ | etasmāditi | 'jīvaghanāt parāt param' iti nirdeśaḥ, jīvadhana - paraśabdayoḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyena upapadyate ityarthaḥ | samaṣṭīti | brahmaṇaḥ samaṣṭipuruṣatvaṃ devatiryagbhāvādyapekṣayā | netavyā iti | cirakālasthāyitvābhiprāyeṇa vā, caturmukhasyaiva pariśuddhāvastha svarūpaparatvena vā nirvāha: iti bhāvaḥ | ata eva, upakrame 'paraṃ cāparaṃ ca' iti nirdeśaśca kṛtanirvāhaḥ ||

'tamoṅkāreṇaivāya (ta) nenānveti vidvān, yattacchāntamajaramamṛtamabhayaṃ paraṃ ca '

iti | 'paraṃ, śāntam, ajaram, 'abhayam, amṛtam' iti hi' paramātmana eva etadrūpam ;
'etada' mṛtamabhaya' metadbrahma'

ityevamādiśrutibhyaḥ |

'etasmājjīvaghanātparātparam '
iti ca paramātmana eva vyapadeśaḥ, na 'caturmukhasya

tasyā'pi ' ' jīvadhana' śabda -

gṛhītatvāt | yasya hi karmanimittaṃ dehitvaṃ, saḥ 'jīvaghanaḥ' ityucyate, caturmukhasyā'pi

tat śrūyate-

'yo brahmāṇaṃ vidadhāti pūrvam'

ityādau ||

yatpunaruktam — antarikṣa lokasyopari nirdiśyamānaḥ brahmalokaḥ caturmukhalokaḥ iti pratīyate, ataḥ tatrasthaḥ caturmukhaḥ - iti |

tadayuktam ;

'yattacchānta 'majaramamṛtamabhayam "
ityādinā īkṣatikarmaṇaḥ paramātmatve niścite sati, 'īkṣituḥ sthānatayā' nirdiṣṭaḥ brahmalokaḥ, na kṣayiṣṇuḥ caturmukhaloko bhavitumarhati ||

etadrūpam – napuṃsakāntaśabdanirdiṣṭaṃ svarūpam | caturmukhaparatve hi pulliṅgāntatā syāt | etadamṛtamiti | anena 'saḥ' iti sautrapadasya prayojanaṃ darśitaṃ bhavati | adhikārādeva sādhyapratijñāsiddheḥ, tasya prayojanaṃ vaktavyam | śāntamabhayamiti vyapadeśasya, kathaṃ paramātma- sādhakatvam ? ityapekṣāyāṃ śrutyantareṣu paramātmanaḥ tādṛktvena prasiddheḥ iti vaktavyam | ataḥ tathātvena śrutyantaraprasiddhidyotanaṃ saḥ iti padasya prayojanamiti | caturmukhasyā'pi iti | saśarīratvādeva hi caturmukhatvam iti bhāvaḥ | ādau iti 'ādi’padena ‘yo vai vedāṃśca prahiṇoti tasmai' iti pādo'pi vivakṣitaḥ | tena ....sṛjyatvāt, upadeśasāpekṣajñāna- prasaratayā saṅkucitajñānatvena karmavaśyatvācca caturmukhasya jīvaghanaśabda gṛhītatva siddhiḥ ||

1 amṛtamabhayamiti - da 2 bhayametadamṛta - tha caturmukhasyā'pi - da

kiṃ ca-

'yathā pādodarastvacā vinirmucyate, evaṃ ha vai sa pāpmanā vinirmuktaḥ
sa sāmabhirunnīyate brahmalokam '
iti sarvapāpavinirmuktasya prāpyatayā ucyamānam, na caturmukhasthānam | ata 'eva ca' udāharaṇaśloke imameva brahmalokamadhikṛtya śrūyate-

'yattatkavayo vedayante'
iti | kavayaḥ – sūrayaḥ | sūribhiḥ dṛśyamānaṃ ca vaiṣṇavaṃ padameva,
'tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padaṃ sadā paśyanti sūrayaḥ' ityevamādibhyaḥ | na ca antavikṣātparaḥ caturmukhalokaḥ ; madhye svargalokādīnāṃ 'bahūnāṃ sadbhāvāt | ataḥ "
'etadvai satyakāma paraṃ cāparaṃ ca brahma yadoṅkāraḥ tasmādvidvāne-
tenai 'vāyanenaika' taramanveti'

iti prativacane, yat aparaṃ kārya 'brahma nirdiṣṭaṃ', tat aihikāmuṣmikatvena dvidhā vibhajya, ekamātraṃ praṇavamupāsīnānām aihikaṃ manuṣyalokāvāptirūpaṃ phalam abhidhāya, 'dvimātra " mu- pāsīnānām āmuṣmikam antarikṣaśabdopalakṣitaṃ phalaṃ ca abhidhāya, trimātreṇa parabrahma-
kavayaḥ –krāntadarśinaḥ | akarmavaśyatvāta asaṅkucitajñānāḥ | sūrayaśca jñānādhikāḥ prasiddhāḥ | ataḥ arthavaśāt sūripratyabhijñānam ityarthaḥ | evamādibhya iti ādiśabdena, 'tadviprāso vipanyavaḥ' 'yatrarṣayaḥ prathamajā ye purāṇā : ' 'yatra pūrve sādhyāḥ santi devā:' ityādi - vākyāni vivakṣitāni | ajaratvādibhiḥ niścitaparamātmabhāvasya sthānatvāt, sarvakarmavinirmuktasya prāpyasthānatvāt, sūribhirdṛśyatvapratyabhijñānācca na caturmukhalokaḥ ityarthaḥ | ataḥ udāhṛta- bahuvākyasvārasyānuguṇyāt | brahma yadoṅkāraḥ iti sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ vācyavācakabhāvanibandhanam ; anantaram etena, ekataram iti padābhyām oṅkārasya brahmaṇaśca nirdeśāt | ayanena upāyabhūtena | ekataram - kāryakāraṇabrahmaṇoranyatarat | vibhajyeti | upari pṛthakphalatvena kīrtanāt vibhāgo'bhipretaḥ ityarthaḥ || 12 ||

vācinā praṇavena 'paraṃ puruṣaṃ' dhyāyatāṃ, parameva brahma prāpyatayā upadiśati - iti sarva samañjasam | ataḥ īkṣatikarma, paramātmā || 12 ||

22. daharādhikaraṇam ||1-3-5 ||

78. dahara uttarebhyaḥ || 1-3-13 ||

'idama | mananti chandogā : 1.

'atha yadidamasmin brahmapure daharaṃ puṇḍarīkaṃ veśma daharo'sminnantara ākāśasta- sminyadantastadanveṣṭavyaṃ tadvāva vijijñāsitavyam'

iti ||

tatra sandehaḥ – kimasau hṛdayapuṇḍarīka madhyavartī daharākāśaḥ ", mahābhūtaviśeṣaḥ ? uta pratyagātmā ? atha paramātmā ? - iti |
daha iti | pūrvādhikaraṇe 'parātparaṃ puriśayaṃ puruṣam' ityuktasya paramātmatvaṃ sādhitam | atra puriśayasya 'ākāśa' śabdavācyasya aparamātmatvaṃ nirasyate saṅgatiḥ | para- smin anveṣṭavyāntarānupapatteḥ, pakṣāntaradvaye'pi upamānopameyabhāvādyanupapatteśca saṃśayaḥ |
sandeha iti | kiṃ daharākāśaḥ, bhūtākāśaḥ ? uta jīvaḥ ? atha paramātmā ? - iti | tadartham anveṣṭavyāntaranirdeśaḥ, kiṃ daharākāśasya paramātmatvavirodhī ? uta na ? 'tasmin yadantaḥ' iti nirdeśaḥ, kiṃ daharākāśasya guṇajātaviṣayaḥ ? uta arthāntaraparaḥ ? 'eṣa ātmā' iti vākyoktam apahatapāpmatvādikam kiṃ prajāpativākyoktajīvadharma: ? uta na ? – iti | jīvadharmatve, apahatapāpmatvādiśrutisvārasyamasti ? usa na ? – iti vicāraḥ |

kiṃ tāvadyuktam ? mahābhūtaviśeṣaḥ - iti |

kuta: ?

ākāśaśabdasya bhūtākāśe, brahmaṇi ca prasiddhatve'pi, bhūtākāśe prasiddhiprakarṣāt,
'tasmin yadantastadanveṣṭavyam'
iti anveṣṭavyāntarasya ādhāratayā pratīteśca ||

ityevaṃ prāpte, abhidhīyate – dahara uttarebhyaḥ | daharākāśaḥ paraṃ brahma | kuta: ?
uttarebhyo vākyagatebhyo hetubhyaḥ ||

'eṣa ātmā'pahatapāpmā vijaro vimṛtyurviśoko vijighatso'pipāsaḥ satyakāmassatyasaṅkalpaḥ'

iti nirupādhikātmatvam, apahatapāpmatvādikaṃ, satyakāmatvaṃ, satyasaṅkalpatvaṃ ceti daharākāśe śrūyamāṇāḥ guṇāḥ daharākāśa paraṃ brahma, iti jñāpayanti | 'atha ya ihātmānamanuvidya vrajantyetāṃśca satyān kāmān teṣāṃ sarveṣu lokeṣu kāmacāro bhavati'

' ityādinā',

'yaṃ kāmaṃ kāmayate, so'sya maṅkalpādeva 'samuttiṣṭhati' | 'tena sampanno mahīyate'
" ityanena', daharākāśavedinaḥ satyasaṅkalpatvaprāptiśca ucyamānā, daharākāśe 'paraṃ brahma' ityavagamayati ||

'yāvānvā ayamākāśastāva | nepo'ntarhṛdaya ākāśaḥ' iti upamānopameyabhāvaśca, daharākāśasya bhūtākāśatve, nopapadyate | hṛdayāvaccheda nibandhanaḥ upamānopameyabhāvaḥ - iti cet,
tathā sati, hṛdayāvacchinnasya dyāvāpṛthivyādisarvāśrayatvaṃ nopapadyate ||

nanu ca - daharākāśasya paramātmatve'pi, bāhyākāśopameyatvaṃ na sambhavati ; 'jyāyān pṛthiyā jyāyānantarikṣāt '

i

ityādau sarvasmājjāyastvaśravaṇāt |

nirupādhiketi, jīvasyā'pi vyāvartanakṣamamātmatvam ityabhiprāyeṇa | nirupādhikatvaṃ-

apahatapāpmatvāderapi nirupādhikatvamabhipretam |

devādikatipaya śarīra viśeṣānavacchinnatvam |

'apahatapāpmatvādikam ' iti 'ādi' śabdena satyakāmatvādigrahaṇe śakye'pi guṇānāṃ vidhi-
niṣedharūpeṇa dvaividhyajñāpanāya satyakāmatvādeḥ pṛthagupādānam ||

upamāneti | ekasyaiva upamānopameyabhāvo na saṅghaṭate ityarthaḥ ||

nanu ceti | nyūnaparimāṇasyeva adhikaparimāṇasyā'pi na madhyamaparimāṇena sādharmyaṃ yuktamiti codyāśayaḥ ||

naivam ; daharākāśasya hṛdayapuṇḍarīkamadhyavartitva' prāptālpatva' nivṛttiparatvāt asya vākyasya | yathā—adhikajave'pi savitari 'iṣuvadgacchati savitā' iti vacanaṃ gatimāndyanivṛttiparam ||

|

atha syāt ;

'eṣa ātmā'pahatapāpmā'
' ityādinā, daharākāśo' na nirdiśyate,
'daharo'sminnantara ākāśastasminyadantastadanveṣṭavyam' iti daharākāśāntarvartinaḥ tato'nyasya anveṣṭavyatvena prakṛtatvāt, iha
'eṣa ātmā'pahatapāpmā'
iti, tasyaiva anveṣṭavyasya nirdeṣṭuṃ yuktatvāt |
syādetadevam ; yadi śrutireva daharākāśaṃ tadantarvartinaṃ ca na vyabhāṅkṣayat ; tathā hi-

vyabhāṅgīttu sā |

"atha yadidamasmin brahmapure daharaṃ puṇḍarīkaṃ veśma daharo'sminnantara ākāśaḥ tasmin yadantastadanveṣṭavyam'
iti brahmapuraśabdena, upāsyatayā sannihitaparabrahmaṇaḥ puratvena upāsakaśarīraṃ nirdiśya, tanmadhyavarti ca tadavayavabhūtaṃ puṇḍarīkākāram alpaparimāṇaṃ hṛdayaṃ parasya brahmaṇo veśmatayā abhidhāya, sarvajñaṃ sarvaśaktim āśritavātsalyaikajaladhim upāsakānugrahāya veśmani sannihitaṃ, sūkṣmatayā dhyeyaṃ daharākāśaśabdena nirdiśya, tadantarvarti ca apahatapāpmatvādi
naivamiti, anyūnaparimāṇatvamātraṃ sādharmyamiti pariharati ||
upāsyatayeti, brahmapuraśabdārthamāha | na kevalaṃ brahmaṇaḥ sarvavyāpi - tvāt śarīreṇa sādhāraṇaṃ sambandhamātram ; api tu upāsanasthānabhūtahṛdayādhāratvāt sambandhaviśeṣaśca vidyate | brahmaśabdaśca parasmin mukhyaḥ | ataḥ brahmapuraṃ śarīramityarthaḥ | alpaparimāṇa- mityanena, 'daharaṃ puṇḍarīkam' ityatra 'dahara' śabda: vyākhyāto bhavati ||

svabhāvato nirastanikhila heyatva- satyakāmatvādisvābhāvikānavadhi' kātiśaya 'kalyāṇaguṇa-

"jātaṃ ca dhyeyam",
" tadanveṣṭavyam”

ityupadiśyate ||

atra ' tadanveṣṭavyam' iti tacchabdena, daharākāśaṃ tadantarvarti' guṇajātaṃ ca parā- mṛśya, "tadubhayama' nveṣṭavyam ityupadiśyate |
'yadidamasmin brahmapure daharaṃ puṇḍarīkaṃ veśma'
ityanūdya, tasmin "dahara' puṇḍarīkaveśmani yaḥ daharākāśaḥ, yacca tadantarvartiguṇajātaṃ tadubhayam anveṣṭavyam iti vidhīyate ityarthaḥ ||

daharākāśaśabdanirdiṣṭasya parabrahmatvam,
' tasmin yadantaḥ'

iti nirdiṣṭasya ca tadguṇatvam, tacchabdena ubhayaṃ parāmṛśya, 'ubhayasyā'pi ' anveṣṭa- vyatayā vidhānaṃ ca kathamavagamyate - iti cet,

yadidamiti, tacchabdasya ubhayaparāmarśitvamāha | anūdyeti | ayamarthaḥ – 'yadidaṃ daharaṃ puṇḍarīkaṃ veśma, tasmin daharākāśa:' iti daharākāśasya puṇḍarīkaveśmādhāratvasya uktatvāt, punarapi 'tasmin yadantaḥ' iti ākāśasya puṇḍarīkaveśmādhāratvakathanavaiyarthyaṃ syāditi | 'man yadantaḥ' iti guṇaviṣayatve tu na vaiyarthyaṃ ; pūrvamanuktatvāt | ataḥ apekṣitavidheḥ anapekṣitavidhānaṃ durbalam | tasmin dahareti | śrutivākye 'antaḥ śabdaḥ antarvartiparaḥ | asmin vartamāno daharākāśaḥ, tadantarvarti ca yat, tadubhayamanveṣṭavyamiti vākyasyārthaḥ ityarthaḥ| ‘tyadādīni sarvairnityam' iti puṃliṅga - napuṃsakaliṅgayoḥ ekaśeṣaḥ | ‘napuṃsakamanapuṃsakenaikacaccāsyānyatarasyām ' iti napuṃsakāntatvamekavacanā- ntatvaṃ ca | tasmāt ākāśa-tadantargatānveṣṭavyaguṇaparaṃ puṃ- napuṃsakāntayacchandadvayārthaviṣayaṃ 'tat' iti padam ityabhiprāyaḥ | evaṃ, 'tadanveṣṭavyam' iti tatpadenaiva ātmano'pyuktatvāt, anveṣṭavyatayā prādhānyāvagatiḥ, anveṣṭavyasya ātmatvaprasiddhiśca upapanne iti codyaparihārasiddhiḥ |
daharākāśeti | vivakṣitārthasya prāmāṇikatve siddhe hi kliṣṭanirvāhāśrayaḥ upapadyate

tat, avahitamanāḥ śṛṇu -

'yāvānvā ayamākāśastāvāne po'ntarhṛdaya ākāśaḥ' iti, daharākāśasya atimahattām abhidhāya,
'ubhe asmin dyāvāpṛthivī antareva samāhite ubhāvagniśca
vāyuśca sūryācandramamā vidyunnakṣatrāṇi'
iti prakṛtameva daharākāśam ‘asmin' iti nirdiśya tasya sarvajagadādhāratvam abhidhāya, " yaccāsyehā'sti' yacca nā'sti sarvaṃ tadasmin samāhitam ' iti, punarapi 'asmin' iti, tameva daharākāśaṃ parāmṛśya, tasmin asya upāsakasya
tasmāt daharākāśasya parabrahmatve tadantarvartinaḥ guṇajānattre tayoranveṣṭavyatve ca pramāṇaṃ vaktavyamityarthaḥ |

taditi, prativakti tadanveṣṭavyam' 'yāvānvā ayam' iti vākyayormadhye, kiṃ tadatra vidyate' iti vākyaṃ śrūyate | paramātmano'nnarvartinaḥ kasyacit. asambhavāt 'kiṃ tat' iti vākyam ākṣepaparam ityāhuḥ pare | praśnaparamidaṃ vākyamiti ucitam | bubhutsākhyāpanamātraṃ praśnaḥ | anupapasyudghāṭana arthākṣepapūrvakaṃ parihārabubhutsākhyāpanaṃ - codyam | abubhutsita, parihāreṇa vaktrā pratiṣedhaḥ pratikṣepaḥ | 'kutastu khalu somya' itivat | na pratikṣepaparamidam ; parihārasya vakṣyamāṇatvāt | na kevalaḥ praśnaḥ ; anupapattervidyamānatvāt | sarvāntarasya paramātmano hi, antarabhūtaṃ tattvāntagm asambhāvitam | anupapattyudghāṭanamukhenākṣepasya kaṇṭottyabhāvāt, na kevalaṃ codyam ityapyucitam | ataḥ vinayaṃ prakāśayatā śiṣyeṇa anupapattimabhipretya, tāmasphuṭaṃ bruvatā, vubhutsākhyāpanaṃ kriyata iti praśnavākyatulyaṃ phalataḥ codyaparamidaṃ vākyam | tacchabdanirdiṣṭayo: ākāśa nadannarvartinoḥ annayerti viśeṣānavagamāt uktānupapattigarbhaḥ tadantarvartya - śaviṣayaḥ praśnaḥ– kiṃ tadatreti | sa brūyāt – ācāryo brūyādityarthaḥ | prākaraṇikānām idame - tacchabdānām ākāśaparāmarśaparatvārtha, tasya avyavahitatvajñāpanārtha, śabdataḥ arthataśca ākāśasya prādhānyajñāpanārthaṃ ca ' yāvān vā ayam' ityādi pradarśyate | idaṃ vākya daharākāśasya alpatvanivṛttiparam ityabhiprāyeṇa atimahattām ityuktam | prakṛtameveti | antarvarti anveṣṭavyam ; ‘ākāśa’śabdavyavahitatvāt na 'idaṃ'śabdaviṣayaḥ iti bhāvaḥ | tameveti, daharākāśasya anugatyavicchedaṃ darśayati | asyeti vyākhyeyam | upāsakasyeti, vyākhyānam |

| |

'etatsatyaṃ brahmapuram'

iti tameva daharākāśa, 'satyabhūtaṃ brahmākhyaṃ puraṃ nikhilajagadāvāsabhūtam iti upapādya, 'asmin kāmāḥ samāhitāḥ'
iti daharākāśam ' asmin' iti nirdiśya, kāmyabhūtāṃśca guṇān 'kāmāḥ' iti nirdiśya, teṣāṃ daharākāśāntarvartitvamuktvā tadeva daharākāśasya kāmyabhūtakalyāṇaguṇa- viśiṣṭatvaṃ tasya ātmatvaṃ ca,

'eṣa ātmā'pahatapāpmā'

ityādinā,

śrīmādhyam 1-3-5]

'satyasaṅkalpaḥ'
ityantena sphuṭīkṛtya,

'yathā hyeveha prajā anvāviśanti'

ityārabhya,

'teṣāṃ sarveṣu lokeṣvakāmacāro bhavati'
ityantena tadidaṃ guṇāṣṭakaṃ tadviśiṣṭaṃ daharā' kāśaśabda nirdiṣṭamātmānaṃ ca aviduṣām etadvyatirikta bhogyasiddhaye ca karma kurvatām, antavatphalāvāptim asatyasaṅkalpatvaṃ ca abhidhāya,
'atha ya ihātmānamanuvidya 'vrajantyetāṃśca' satyān kāmāṃsteṣāṃ
sarveṣu lokeṣu kāmacāro bhavati'
ityādinā, dahārākāśaśabda nirdiṣṭamātmānam, tadantarvartinaśca kāmyabhūtān apahṛta - pāpmatvādikān guṇān vijānatām udāraguṇasāgarasya tasya paramapuruṣasya prasādādeva sarvakāmāptiḥ satyasaṅkalpatā ca ucyate ||

tadetat, vākyakāro'pi spaṣṭayati-
' tasmin yadantariti 'kāma' vyapadeśaḥ ityādinā | ataḥ etebhyo hetubhyaḥ, daharākāśaḥ parameva brahma || 13||

'itaśca daharākāśaḥ paraṃ brahma'

79. gatiśabdābhyāṃ tathā hi dṛṣṭaṃ liṅgaṃ ca || 1-3-14||
'tadyathā hiraṇya'nidhiṃ' 'nihita 'makṣetrajñā 'uparyupari' sañcaranto na vindeyuḥ evamevemāssarvāḥ prajāḥ aharahargacchantya evaṃ brahmalokaṃ na vidantyanṛtena hi pratyūḍhāḥ'

iti, 'etam' iti prakṛtaṃ daharākāśaṃ nirdiśya tatra aharahaḥ sarveṣāṃ kṣetrajñānāṃ gamanam, " gantavyasya tasya daharākāśasya gamayataḥ ||

" brahmaloka' śabda 'nirdeśaśca daharākāśasya parabrahmatāṃ

kathamanayoḥ asya parabrahmatvasādhakatvam ? ' ityata āha ' tathā hi dṛṣṭam' iti | tadetaditi, antarvartinaḥ kāmatvaṃ sampradāyato draḍhayati ||13||
'aharahargacchantya iti, suṣuptau gamanaṃ vivakṣitam | tacca pṛthakpratipattivyavahārānarha - tayā sthitiḥ | nirgamanam - pṛthakpratipacyarhatā pattiḥ ||
kathamiti | śrutiṣu paramātmadharmatvena ambayadarśanādvā, jīvādipratipādakavākyeṇyadarśanena jīvādi asambhāvitatvādra', gati-śabdayoḥ paramātmatvasādhakatvam ityarthaḥ | liṅga ca'

tasmin gati - brahmalokaśabdābhyāṃ daharaḥ paraḥ |
sampadyeti brahmaloka ityanya śrutiliṅgataḥ ||

parasmin brahmaṇi sarveṣāṃ kṣetrajñānām aharahaḥ suṣuptikāle gamanam anyatra abhidhīyamānaṃ

dṛṣṭam –

'evameva khalu somyemāḥ sarvāḥ prajāḥ sati sampadya na viduḥ,
sati 'sampatsyāmaha' iti'

iti,

'sata āgamya na viduḥ sata āgacchāmaha iti' iti ca | tathā brahmalokaśabdaśca parasmina brahmaṇi dṛṣṭaḥ-

'eṣa brahmalokaḥ samrāḍiti hovāca'

iti ||

mā bhūdanyatra, brahmaṇi gamanadarśanam | etadeva tu daharākāśe sarveṣāṃ kṣetrajñānāṃ pralaya- kāla iva, nirastanikhila duḥkhānāṃ suṣuptikāle avasthānaṃ śrūyamāṇam asya parabrahmatve paryāptaṃ liṅgam | tathā brahmalokaśabdaśca samānādhikaraṇavṛcyā asmin daharākāśe prayujyamānaḥ asya brahmatve prayogāntara nirapekṣaṃ paryāptaṃ liṅgam, ityāha 'liṅgañca' iti | 'niṣādasthapati' nyāyācca paṣṭhīsamāsāt samānādhikaraṇasamāso nyāyyaḥ || iti sūtrakhaṇḍaṃ vyācaṣṭe mā bhūditi | etadeva tu iti, gateḥ śrutyantare'nvaya darśana nairapekṣyeṇa sādhakatvamāha | śrūyamāṇamiti | nidhidṛṣṭāntasacivamiti bhāvaḥ | nidhidṛṣṭāntaśca paramapuruṣārthatva-- jñāpakaḥ | ataḥ dṛṣṭāntānugṛhītam aharahargamanam ekaṃ paryāptaṃ liṅgam ityarthaḥ | niṣādasthapatīti | 'etayā niṣādasthapatiṃ yājayet' ityatra karmadhārayaparigrahe - niṣādajātīyasya sthapateḥ agni- vidyayorasambhavena, svato niradhikāratvāt paṣṭhīsamāsaparigrahe ca traivarṇikāntabhūtasthapatikaṭā- kṣeṇāpi 'niṣādasya pati' śabdanirvāhasambhavāt ṣaṣṭhīsamāsaparigraheṇa 'apaśūdrādhikaraṇa' nyāyānurodho yuktaḥ iti prāpte, 'sthapatirniṣādaḥ syāt śabdasāmarthyāt ityabhihitam | ayamarthaḥ ṣaṣṭhīsamāsapakṣe niṣādasambandhaviśiṣṭo'bhidheyaḥ | karmadhārayapakṣe, niṣādatvaviśiṣṭaḥ | tatra ṣaṣṭhyāḥ aśravaṇāt, niṣādaprātipadikena vibhaktyartho lakṣaṇīyaḥ | na cedaṃ yuktam ; śrautārthaparigrahe virodhābhāvāt | na ca agni- vidyārāhityaṃ doṣaḥ, dravyavat etadupayogyami-vidyayorapi yathākathañcidārjanasya vidhyanumatisambhavāt | tasmāt karmadhāraya eva nyāyyaḥ iti | etannyāyānugṛhīto 'brahmaloka 'śabdaḥ śrutyantare'nvaya nirapekṣaḥ paramātmatva- sādhakaḥ ityarthaḥ ||

athavā

'aharahargacchantyaḥ'

iti, na suṣuptiviṣayaṃ gamanamucyate, apitu, antarātmatvena sarvadā vartamānasya daharā- kāśasya paramapuruṣārthabhūtasya uparyupari aharahargacchantyaḥ sarvasmin kāle vartamānāḥ, tamajānatyaḥ, taṃ na vindanti na labhante | yathā - hiraṇyanidhiṃ nihitaṃ, tatsthāna- majānānāḥ, tadupari sarvadā vartamānā api na labhante, tadvat – ityarthaḥ ||

'seyam evamantarātmatvena ' sthitasya' daharākāśasyopari tanniyamitānāṃ sarvāsāṃ prajānāmajānatīnāṃ sarvadā gatiḥ, asya daharākāśasya parabrahmatāṃ gamayati | tathā hi-- anyatra parasya brahmaṇaḥ antarātmatayā avasthitasya, svaniyāmyābhiḥ svasmin vartamānābhiḥ prajābhiḥ avedanaṃ dṛṣṭam | yathā, antaryāmitrāhmaṇe-

'ya ātmani tiṣṭhan ātmano'ntaro yamātmā na veda,
yasyāss mā śarīraṃ ya ātmānanantaro yamayati'

iti,

'adṛṣṭo draṣṭā aśrutaśrotā'

iti ca |

mā. bhūdanyatra darśanam : svayameva tu iyaṃ nidhidṛṣṭāntāvagataparamapuruṣārthabhāvasya asya hṛdayasthanya upari tadādhāratayā aharahaḥ, sarvadā sarvāsāṃ prajānām ajānatīnāṃ gatiḥ, asya parabrahmatve paryāptaṃ liṅgam ||14||
athavetyādi, 'gati' śabdamātrasya yojanāntaram | pūrvayojanāyāṃ dṛṣṭānte eva upariśabdānvayaḥ | atra tu dāṣṭantike'pi ||
daharākāśasyopāre iti, dhamkyābhiprāyeṇoktam | antarātmatayā sthitatvena hi ādhāraḥ ; na tu upāsakānugrāhakatayā sthitatvena | upari tanniyamitānāmiti | asmin prakaraṇe 'satya'śabdanirvacanāvagataṃ hi cetanācetanayorniyantṛtvaṃ parasya 'ya ātmānamantaro yamayati' ityādiśrutyantaraprasiddhaṃ cetyabhipretam | asya parabrahmatve paryāptaṃ liṅgam iti, 'liṅgam’: ityasyaivārthaḥ ||14||
1 evameva anta-tha 2 avasthitasya vā. 3 parabrahmabhāve - i. da.

itaśca daharākāśaḥ paraṃ brahma

80. dhṛteśra mahimno'syāsminnupalabdheḥ || 1-3-15||

'atha ya ātmā'
iti, prakṛtaṃ daharākāśaṃ nirdiśya,
'sa seturvidhṛtireṣāṃ lokānāmasambhedāya'
iti, asmin jagadvidharaṇaṃ śrayamāṇaṃ, deharākāśasya parabrahmatāṃ gamayati | jagadvidharaṇaṃ hi ! parasya brahmaṇo mahimā

'eṣa sarveśvara eṣa bhūtādhipatireṣa bhūtapāla eṣa seturvidharaṇaḥ,
eṣāṃ lokānāmasambhedāya'

iti,

'etasya vā akṣarasya praśāsane gārgi ! sūryācandramasau vidhṛtau tiṣṭhataḥ'
ityādibhyaḥ | sa cāyaṃ tasya parasya brahmaṇo 'dhṛtyākhyo mahimā' asmin " daharākāśe " upalabhyate | ataḥ dahara kāśaḥ paraṃ brahma ||15||

dhṛteriti | asya – paramātmanaḥ | mahimā - dhṛtiḥ | tasya, asmin – daharākāśe, upalabdheḥ | dhṛtiḥ—vidhṛtiḥ | paraṃ brahmeti | bhūtākāśādanyatve tātparyam ; bhūtākāśatvaṃ pratikṣiptam' iti hi vakṣyate | sa eva vastugatyā paramātmā paraṃ brahma ityuktam ||

saḥ uktalakṣaṇaḥ ātmā, setuḥ — seturiva | vidhṛtiḥ – vidhārakaḥ | asambhedaḥ - asaṅkaraḥ | sinotibadhnāti, svasmin cidacidvamtajātam asaṅkīrṇam iti seturucyate | yadyayaṃ paramātmā svaśāsanena jagat na dhārayet, sarvadharmāṇāṃ sāṅkaryameva syāt | pṛthivyāḥ gandhavattvam , jalasya śaityam, tejasa auṣṭyam, ityādayo hi dharmāḥ paramātmājñayā vyavasthitā bhavanti ||15||

asambhedāya lokānāṃ mahimno vidhṛteriha |
parasya tasya cāsmiṃstu śravaṇāddaraḥ paraḥ ||

81. prasiddheśva || 1-3-16||

82. itaraparāmarśātsa iti cennāsambhavāt || 1-3-17||

yaduktam - vākyaśeṣavaśāt daharākāśaḥ paraṃ brahma - iti, tadayuktam ; vākyaśeṣe parasmāditarasya jīvasyaiva sākṣātparāmarśat, 'atha ya eṣa saṃprasādo'smāccharīrātsamutthāya paraṃ jyotirūpasampadya, svena rūpeṇābhiniṣpadyate epa ātmeti hovāca etadamṛtamabhayametadrahma'

iti | yadyapi,

'daharo'sminantara ākāśaḥ "

iti hṛdayapuṇḍarīkamadhyavartitayā upadiṣṭasya ākāśasya, upamānopameyabhāvādyasambhavāt, bhūtākāśatvaṃ na sambhavati tathāpi vākyaśepavaśāt pratyagātmatvaṃ yuktamāśrayitum | ākāśaśabdo'pi, prakāśādiyogāt jīva eva vartiṣyate iti cet,

parasmāt iti, itaraśabdasya pratisambandhisāpekṣatvādāha | jīvasyaiveti | daharākāśasya paramātmatvaṃ vadato'pi asya vākyasya jīvaparatvaṃ saṃpratipannamiti, evakārābhiprāyaḥ | sākṣāditi | na jīvasambandhiliṅgadvārā parāmarśa: ; kintu tadvācakaśabdena parāmarśaḥ | tasmāt ityarthaḥ | ‘atha ya eṣa:' iti etacchando hi prakṛtaparāmarśī | ataḥ pūrvavākyeṣvapi jīva eva pratipādyata iti parāmarśaśabdābhiprāyaḥ | jīva parāmarśa sahakāriṇī tadantarvartya veṣṭa- vyāntarapratītiśca jīvatva heturanusandheyaḥ | yadyapīti | upamānopameyabhāvādyasvārasyaṃ vākyaśeṣa- vaśāt soḍhavyam iti bhāvaḥ ||

jīvasya ca parāmarśātsaṃprasāda iti śruteḥ |
vākyaśeṣe sa iti cenna jīvo'nupapattitaḥ ||

'atrottaram' – nāsambhavāt iti | na ayaṃ jīvaḥ ; na hi apahatapāpmatvādayo guṇāḥ jīve sambhavanti ||17||
83. uttarāccedāvirbhūtasvarūpastu || 1-3-18 ||

uttarāt- 'prajāpati' vākyāt, jīvasyaiva apahatapāpmatvādiguṇayogo niścīyate-

" iti cet,

etaduktaṃ bhavati – prajāpativākyaṃ jīvaparameva | tathā hi-

'ya ātmā'pahatapāpmā vijaro vimṛtyurviśoko vijighatso'pipāsaḥ satyakāmaḥ satyasaṅkalpaḥ so'nveṣṭavyaḥ sa vijijñāsitavyaḥ sa sarvāṃśva lokānāprati sarvāṃca kāmān ' ; yastamātmāna' manuvidya' vijānāti '

iti prajāpativacanam aitihyarūpeṇa upaśrutya, anveṣṭavyātmasvarūpajijñāsayā prajāpati - na hīti | parāmarśānyathāsiddheḥ vakṣyamāṇatvāt, apahatapāpmatvādiśrutisvārasyam anapoditam iti bhāvaḥ ||17||
uttara diti | utaravākyānusāreṇa daharavākye'pi jīvasyaiva iti niścīyate ityarthaḥ |
jīva parameveti | 'sa utama: puruṣa:' ityanena paramātmaparatvaṃ na śaṅkayam ; tadukti: prāsaṅgikī iti bhāvaḥ | maghavate iti | yadyapi, virocano'pi prajāpatimupāsadat ; tathā'pi, chāyā puruṣādhigamamātratṛtiyā apakrāntaḥ atastadanuktiḥ | indra eva arthatattvabubhutsayā

mupaseduṣe maghavate, prajāpatiḥ jāgarita- svama- suṣuptyavasthaṃ jīvātmānaṃ 'saśarīraṃ krameṇa śuśrūṣuyogyatā parīcikṣipayā' upadiśya, 'tatra tatra' bhogyamapaśyate, pariśuddhātmasvarūpo- padeśayogyāya tasmai maghavate,
maghavan martya vā idaṃ śarīramāttaṃ mṛtyunā, tadasyāmṛtasyāśarīrasyātmano'dhi- ṣṭhānam
iti śarīrasya 'adhiṣṭhānatām', ātmanaśca adhiṣṭhātṛtām 'aśarīrasya ca tasya amṛtatvasvarūpatāṃ ca uktvā,
' na ha vai saśarīrasya sataḥ priyāpriyayorapahatirasti |
aśarīraṃ vāva santaṃ na priyāpriye spṛśataḥ || '
iti, karmārabdhaśarīrayoginaḥ tadanuguṇasukha-duḥkhabhāgitva 'rūpānartha, tadvimokṣe ca tadabhāvam abhidhāya,
' evamevaiṣa samprasādo'smāccharīrātsamutthāya paraṃ jyotirūpasampadya,
svena rūpeṇābhiniṣpadyate '
iti, jīvātmanaḥ svarūpameva śarīra viyuktam upadideśa |
sthita iti, sacchiṣyatvāt tannirdeśaḥ | jāgaritetyādinā, khaṇḍapañcakasyārtha saṅkṣepeṇāha | śuśrūṣuyogyatāparīcikṣipayā iti, anatisphuṭaṃ gahanamupadiśataḥ prajāpatestātparyamuktam | maghavan ! martyaṃ vā idam ityādinā, niṣkṛṣṭātmamvarūpaṃ sphuṭataramupādiśat | evamevaiṣa iti, muktatyavasthopadeśaṃ darśayati | evameva_bāyvabhrastanayitnvādivadeva; eṣa samprasādaḥ – ayaṃ jīvo'pi | asmāt śarīrāt- svādhiṣṭhānatayā sthitāt dehāt avadhibhūtāt | samutthāya - mārgaviśeṣeṇa gatvā | paraṃ jyoti- rupasampadya–paraṃ brahma prāpya | svena rūpeṇa – saṃsāryavasthāprahāṇena paramātmatulyarūpeṇa | abhiniṣpadyate – āvirbhavati | evamarthe sati hi dṛṣṭānta - dāntika vākyasāmañjasyaṃ bhavati |

sa uttamaḥ puruṣaḥ, sa tatra paryeti, 'jakṣatkrīḍan' ramamāṇaḥ strībhirvā
yānairvā jñātibhirvā nopajanaṃ smarannidaṃ śarīram ' iti prāpyasya parasya jyotiṣaḥ puruṣottamatvam, nivṛttatirodhānasya paraṃ jyotirūpasampannasya pratyagātmanaḥ brahmaloke yatheṣṭabhogāvāptim, priyāpriyāviyukta' karmanimittaśarīrādya- puruṣārthānanusandhānaṃ ca abhidhāya,
' sa yathā prayogya ācaraṇe' 'yukta evamasmin śarīre prāṇo yuktaḥ '

iti yathoktasvarūpasyaiva "saṃsāradaśāyāṃ karmatantraṃ śarīra' yogaṃ yugyaśakaṭa yogadṛṣṭāntena abhidhāya,

'atha yatraitadākāśamanuviṣaṇṇaṃ 'cakṣuḥ saḥ cākṣuṣaḥ puruṣaḥ, darśanāya cakṣuḥ | atha yo vededaṃ jighrāṇīti, sa ātmā; gandhāya ghrāṇam | atha yo vededamabhivyāharāṇīti, sa ātmā ; abhivyāhārāya vāk | atha yo vededaṃ śruṇavānīti, sa ātmā; śravaṇāya śrotram' 'atha yo vededaṃ manvānīti, sa ātmā; 'mano'ssya 'divya' cakṣuḥ

tata eva

' sa vā eva etena divyena cakṣuṣā manasaitān kāmān paśyan ramate,

84. anyārthaśca parāmarśaḥ || 1.3.19 ||

mantreti | mantrāśca arthavādāśca ityarthaḥ | anyaparatvāditi | anuṣṭheyārthaprakāśana-- stutyoḥ anyaparatvāt ityarthaḥ | karmavidhayaśceti | mā saitsīt anyapareṇa mantrārthavādena devatāḥ vigrahādi | kiṃ tu karmavidhibhistveva setsyati ; karmaṇaḥ dravya-devatā nirūpyatvāt | kṣaṇadhvaṃsina karmaṇaḥ kālāntarabhāviphalasādhanatvanirvāhāya, devataiśvaryasya apekṣitatvāt | śrutahānena aśrutāpūrva- kalpanāyogācca, ityataḥ | ayamabhiprāyaḥ yadyapi devatāvatsu yāgahomādikarmasu devataiścaryāṅgīkāreṇā'pi phalapradatvanirvāhasambhavaḥ ; tathā'pi dānatapaḥprabhṛtiṣu devatānāmabhāvāt, kalpanīyameva apūrvam | tathā ca samāna nyāyatayā yāga - homādāvapi apūrvameva kalpyatām ; kim ardhavaiśasena ?–iti |

ata eveti | yata eva na vigrahavattvaṃ devatānām, ata eva arthitvamapi na sambhavati ; duḥkhasya tajjihāsāyāśca dehasambandhamūlatvāt, arthitvasya duḥkhajihāsārūpatvācca, ityarthaḥ || paṭutaraśabdaḥ manuṣyadehādapi paṭutvātiśayaparaḥ ||
sakalopaniṣatsu iti | tattvopadeśapradhānamantra brāhmaṇarūpavedabhāgāḥ upaniṣadaḥ | tāśca dvividhāḥ—tattvaparāḥ, upāsanaparāścati | ubhayīṣvapi tāsu, devatānāṃ vigrahādimattvam avagamyate ityarthaḥ | sadevetyādi | nāmarūpavyākaraṇasya kaṇṭhoktyā nāmarūpāṇāṃ cāturvidhyasya pramāṇasiddhatvāt, nāmarūpavyākaraṇaśrutyaiva devatā vigrahavattvaṃ sāmānyena siddham ityartha: | 'indro varuṇaḥ somo rudraḥ

evaṃ sarvatra sṛṣṭivākyeṣu, deva- tiryaṅ - manuṣya- sthāvarātmanā caturvidhā sṛṣṭiḥ āmnāyate | devādibhedaśca tattatkarmānuguṇatrahmalokaprabhṛti caturdaśalokasthaphalabhogayogya- dehendriyādi 'yogāyattaḥ'; ātmanāṃ khato devāditvābhāvāt ||

tathā-

"tobhaye' 'devāsurā' anububudhire, te hocuḥ indro ha vai devānāmabhi' - pravavāja, virocano'surāṇāṃ tau 'hāsaṃvidā nāveva' samitpāṇī,

'vajrahastaḥ purandaraḥ'
'tenendro vajramudayacchat'
ityādibhiḥ pratīyamānaṃ vigrahādimacaṃ, pramāṇāntarāviruddhaṃ tatprameyameva ||
na ca anuṣṭheyārthaprakāśana - stutiparatvābhyāṃ pratīyamānārthāntarānivakṣā śakyate vaktum ; stutyādyupa' yogitvāt tena vinā stutyādyanupapatteśva guṇakathanena hi parjanyo yamo mṛtyurīśānaḥ ' 'nārāyaṇāt brahmā jāyate' ityādīni viśeṣoktimanti ca vākyānya- bhipretāni |
devādīti | upāsanadaśāyāṃ paramakāraṇabhūtasya upāsyasya lakṣaṇatvena prāpyadaśāyāṃ prāpyaviśeṣaṇatvena, vigrahādimattvaṃ siddham ityarthaḥ ||

karmavidhīti | tatra prathamasūtre, siddhavastuni tātparya sambhavasya phalatvena, siddhiruktā |
atra, stāvakatva-prakāśakatva hetutayā, 'phalamata upapatte: ' ityatra phala - sādhanatvena, iti tridhā vibhāga | pramāṇāntarā viruddhamiti, 'ādityo yūpaḥ ityā divyāvṛttyartham ||
na cetyādinā, anyaparatvamanūdya nirasyati | stutyādi iti, ādinā anuṣṭheyārtha-

1 bhogāyattaḥ - vā hasaṃvidānāveva-bha.

mantrāśca karmasu viniyuktāḥ, tatra tatra kiñcitkaratvāya 'anuṣṭheyamartha' prakāśayantaḥ, devatādigata' vigrahādi ' guṇaviśeṣam abhidadhata eva 'taṃtra' kiñcitkurvanti; anyathā indrādismṛtyanupapatteḥ | na ca nirviśeṣā 'devatā' dhiyamadhirohati | tatra pramāṇāntarā- prāptān guṇān svayameva bodhayitvā taiḥ karma prarocayanti, guṇaviśiṣṭaṃ vā prakāśayanti | prāptāṃśca 'anūdya taiḥ' prarocana - " prakāśane " kurvanti | viruddhatve tu tadvācibhiśśabdaiḥ, aviruddhān guṇān lakṣayitvā kurvanti ||

karmavidheca, "devatāyā" aiśvaryam apekṣitameva | kāminaḥ kartavyatayā karma prakāśanaṃ mantrakṛtya gṛhyate | 'mantrārthavādeṣu' iti uddeśe, anuṣṭheyārthaprakāśana- stutipatvābhyām ityanuddeśe ca avagataḥ kramaḥ, atra parityaktaḥ ; tatra kāraṇaṃ – 'arhan bibharṣi sāyakāni dhanvetyāha | stautyevainametat' iti brāhmaṇenaiva, 'arhan' ityādimantrasya stutiparatvamavagamyate, iti stuteḥ vyāpakatvam | tena vinā – pratīyamānārthena vinā | stutitvameva prarocakatvameva | hīyeteti | śeṣabhūtasya śeṣiṇaṃ prati kiñcitkāro'pekṣitaḥ | sa na siddhyatīti yāvat | na ca . . . . jāyata iti | anena, 'na asatā stutirupapadyate' iti dramiḍabhāṣyaṃ smāryate
devatādigateti | dravya-devate hi karmanirūpike | 'etadaivatyam idaṃ karma' iti prati- pattau devatāviśiṣṭatvena hi karmapratipattiḥ | devatā ca ' īdṛśī' ityeva pratipattavyā | tasmāt anuṣṭheyārthaprakāśakatvasiddhyarthaṃ, mantrāṇāṃ vigrahādimantre prāmāṇyamastyevetyarthaḥ | bodhayitvā - vidhāya | prarocayanti, | arthavādāḥ | prakāśayanti, mantrāḥ | lakṣayitvā - lakṣaṇayā bodhayitvā | kurvanti, prarocana - prakāśane ||
karmavidheveti | 'phalamata upapatte:' ityadhikaraṇārtha: buddhi- saukaryārtham atra saṅgamayya kathyate ; na ca etadadhikaraṇāsādhāraṇārthatayā |

vidhīyamānaṃ, svayaṃ kṣaṇapradhvaṃsi, kālāntarabhāvinaḥ phalasya svargāde: ' sādhakam apekṣate | mantrārthavādayośca
'vāyurvai kṣepiṣṭhā devatā vāyumeva svena bhāgadheyenopadhāvati sa evainaṃ bhūtiṃ gamayati '

yadanena haviṣā''śāste tadaśyāttadṛddhyāt tadasmai devā 'rādhantām ' '

tadanuguṇaṃ ca aiśvarya

ityādiṣu devatāyāḥ karmaṇā ārādhitāyāḥ phaladāyitvaṃ pratīyamānam apekṣitatvena vākyārthe samanvīyate | devapūjābhidhāyino ' yaji' dhātośca 'yāgākhyaṃ karma svārādhyadevatāpradhānaṃ pratīyate ||

tadevaṃ, kṛtsnavākyaparyāloca'nayā', vākyādeva vidhyapekṣitaṃ sarvam avagatamiti, na apūrvādikaṃ vyutpattisamayānavagataṃ karmavidhiṣu 'abhidheyatayā', kalpyatayā 'vā' āśrayitavyam |
tathā saṅkīrṇatrāhmaṇamantrārthavādamūleṣu dharmaśāstretihāsapurāṇeṣu, brahmādīnāṃ devāsura-

'prabhṛtīnāṃ ca ' dehendriyādayaḥ, svabhāvabhedāḥ, sthānāni, bhogāḥ, kṛtyāni ca ityevamādayaḥ suvyaktāḥ pratipādyante | ataḥ vigrahādimaccāt devādīnāmapi adhikāraḥ astyeva ||25|| 91. virodhaḥ karmaṇīti cennānekapratipatterdarśanāt || 1-3-26 ||

devādīnāṃ vigrahādimaccābhyupagame, karmaṇi virodhaḥ prasajyate | bahuṣu yāgeṣu yugapat, ekasyendrasya vigrahabacce,

'agnimagna āvaha'
'indra āgaccha hariva āgaccha'
ityādinā 'āhūtasya tasya', sannidhānānupapatteḥ | darśayati ca agnyādīnāṃ tatra tatra āgamanam -
vibhāgavatām asmadādibhiranadhītāsu adhītāsu ca śākhāsu tatrā'pi anyataraprakaraṇeṣu prasaṅgādanupraviṣṭānāṃ, bhagavatā vyāsena vyasteṣu avyasteṣu ca vedabhāgeṣu viprakīrṇānāṃ vākyānām, arthāḥ itihāsādiṣu saṅkalayya prakāśyante iti bhāva: | 'saṅkīrṇa' śabdena, nityānumeya- śrutimūlatva-utsannaśākhā mūlatvayoḥ vyudāsaḥ ; tadubhayamapi hi anupapannam | devādīnāmapi iti | devatānāṃ hi, vidyā— karmaṇī na kvacit pratiṣiddhe | prāktanajñānāpramoṣāt sāmarthya ca sthitam || 25 ||
virodha iti | vigrahābhyupagame, tadvat ārthavādike āhvānāgamane ca abhyupeye | tadā yugapat anekakarmasu āhnāne virodha: ; śarīrādhīnatvādāgamanasya ityarthaḥ ||

'kasya 'vāha' devā yajñamā gacchanti, kasya vā na, bahūnāṃ yajamānānāṃ
yo vai devatāḥ pūrvaḥ parigṛhṇāti sa enāśśvobhūte yajate ' iti | ataḥ – vigrahādimattve karmaṇi virodhaḥ prasajyate - iti cet,

'tanna'; anekapratipatterdarśanāt | dṛśyate hi saubhariprabhṛtīnāṃ śaktimatāṃ yugapat anekaśarīrapratipattiḥ || 26||
92. śabda iti cennātaḥ prabhavāt pratyakṣānumānābhyām ||1-3-27||

virodha iti vartate | mā bhūt karmaṇi virodhaḥ; aneka śarīrapratipatteḥ | śabde tu vaidike, virodhaḥ prasajyate ; anityārthasaṃyogāt | vigrahavatve hi sāvayavatvena indrāderarthasya, anityatvamanivāryam | 'tataḥ, devadattādi śabdavat indrādyarthajanmanaḥ prāk, 'vināśāt ' ūrdhvaṃ ca, indrādiśabdānāṃ vaidikānām, arthaśūnyatvam anityatvaṃ vā vedasya syāt - iti cet,
dṛśyate hīti | ' ātmano vai śarīrāṇi bahūni manujeśvara | yogī kuryāt balaṃ prāpya taiśca sarvāṃ mahīṃ caret || ' iti bhāratavacanādi draṣṭavyam | yogyānupalambhastu, mantrauṣadhādibhiḥ -manuṣyāṇāmiva śaktiviśeṣaiḥ devānāmapi sambhavatīti na virodhaḥ iti bhāvaḥ || 26 ||
vaidike iti, śabdaśabdaṃ viśinaṣṭi |
arthaśūnyatvam anityatvaṃ vā iti | devadattādivināśe devadattādiśabdasyeva,

'tanna'; ataḥ prabhavāt | asmāt indrādiśabdādeva, 'punaḥpunaḥ indrādyarthasya prabhavāt ||

etaduktaṃ bhavati na hi devadattādiśabdavat indrādiśabdāḥ vaidikāḥ, vyakti- viśeṣa mātre saṅketa' pūrvakāḥ pravṛttāḥ ; api tu svabhāvata eva gavādiśabdavat ākṛti - viśeṣavācitvena | tataśca ekasyām indravyaktau vinaṣṭāyām, ata eva vaidikāt indraśabdāt manasi ' vipari'vartamānāt avagata tadvācyabhūtendrādyarthākāraḥ dhātā, tadākārameva aparam indraṃ sṛjati ; yathā kulālaḥ, ghaṭaśabdāt manasi 'vipari' vartamānāt, tadākārameva ghaṭam - iti ||

kathamidam avagamyate ?

pratyakṣānumānābhyām | śruti smṛtibhyām ityarthaḥ | śrutistāvat, 'vedena rūpe vyākarot | satāsatī prajāpatiḥ' iti | tathā,

anarthakatvam i anityatvam anuccāryamāṇatvam | nityatve, arthaśūnyatvam | vigrahādima- dindrādyarthasya yāvacchandabhāvitvābhāvāt | arthavattve anityatvaṃ vā ; śabdasya yāvadartha - bhāvitvāt, arthānāṃ ca anityatvāt ityarthaḥ | ayamabhiprāya : – ' indra āgaccha hariva āgaccha ' ityādikaṃ mantrajātaṃ vaidikakarmasu viniyujyate | tatra pūrvameva indravyaktau vinaṣṭāyām 'indra' śabdavācyavyakteḥ pāścātyayajñakarmaṇi āgamanābhāvāt, 'indra āgaccha ' ityādiśabdānām, uccāryamāṇanām arthaśūnyatvam anuccāryamāṇatvaṃ vā syāt – iti |

dhātā tadākārameva iti padaiḥ, 'sūryācandramasau dhātā yathāpūrvamakalpayat' iti śrutiḥ, yathaṣvṛtuliṅgāni nānārūpāṇi paryaye | dṛśyante tāni tānyeva tathā bhāvā yugādiṣu || ' iti smṛtiśca smāryate | dhīsthasya śabdasya tattadākṛtismārakatayā hetutvam | manasi viparivartamānatvam — ālocyamānatvam |
pratyakṣagrāhyo vedaḥ, pratyakṣa śabdavācyaḥ | svānurūpavedānumāpikā smṛtiḥ, anumāna- śabdavācyā ityarthaḥ | satāsatī - cidacitī rūpe | vyākarot – vividhamakarot,

'sa bhūriti vyāharat | sa bhūmimasṛjata | sa bhuva iti vyāharat | so'ntarikṣamasṛjata'
ityādi | vācakaśabdapūrvakaṃ tattadarthasaṃsthānaṃ smaran, tattatsaṃsthānaviśiṣṭaṃ taṃ tamartha sṛṣṭavān, ityarthaḥ | smṛtirapi,

'anādinidhanā hyeṣā vāgutsṛṣṭā svayambhuvā |
ādau vedamayī divyā yatassarvāḥ prasūtayaḥ ||'

iti |

'sarveṣāṃ tu sa nāmāni karmāṇi ca pṛthakpṛthak |
vedaśabdebhya evādau pṛthaksaṃsthāca nirmame ||'
iti | saṃsthāḥ – saṃsthānāni rūpāṇi iti yāvat | tathā, 'nāma rūpaṃ ca bhūtānāṃ kṛtyānāṃ ca prapañcanam |
vedaśabdebhya evādau devādīnāṃ cakāra saḥ ||' ataḥ devādīnāṃ vigrahavatve'pi vaidikaśabdānām ānarthakyaṃ, vedasya ādimavaṃ ca na prasajyate || 27 ||

iti |

93. ata eva ca nityatvam ||1-3-28 ||

ityarthaḥ |
||27||

sūryācandramasau dhātā yathāpūrvamiti śruteḥ |
dhātā veda hi saṃsmṛtya sṛjatītyasya nityatā |

yata eva indra- vasiṣṭhādiśabdānāṃ devarṣivācināṃ tattadākāravācitvam, tattacchabdena "tattadartha 'smṛtipūrvikā ca tattadartha sṛṣṭiḥ, tata eva ;

'mantrakṛto vṛṇīte'
'nama ṛṣibhyo mantrakṛdbhyaḥ
"ayaṃ so'gniriti viśvāmitrasya sūktaṃ bhavati' ityādibhiḥ; vasiṣṭhādīnāṃ mantrakṛtva- 'kāṇḍakṛttva - kapitvādau pratīyamāne'pi, vedasya nityatvam upapadyate ||

ebhireva,

'mantrakṛto vṛṇīte'

ityādibhiḥ vedaśabdaiḥ, tattatkāṇḍasūktamantrakṛtām ṛṣīṇām, ākṛtiśaktyā dikaṃ parāmṛśya, ' tattadākārān' tattacchakti'yuktāṃśca sṛṣṭvā, prajāpatiḥ tāneva tattanmantrādi- karaṇe" niyuṅkte | te'pi prajāpatinā āhitaśaktayaḥ, tattadanuguṇaṃ tapastaptvā,

yata eveti | arthavādānāṃ pratīyamānārthe tātparyamasti iti cet, 'mantrakṛtaḥ' ityādi- vākyeṣvapi, mantrakṛtye tātparyasambhavāt anityatvamiti śaṅkā | iyamapi indrādiśabdānāmā- kṛtivā citvāt, tattacchabdena tattadarthammṛtipūrvakatattadarthasṛṣṭyā parihṛtetyarthaḥ | mantrakṛtvaṃ nāma anadhītyaiva, mantrāṇām askhalitakramasvaravarṇatayā darśanam | tacca tattanmantrasmṛtipūrvakaṃ prajāpatinā yathāpūrvaṃ sṛpraiḥ, tattannāmarūpabhāgbhiḥ, pravāhaparamparayā āgataiḥ, vasiṣṭhādibhiḥ kriyata iti, veda- nityatva-mantrakṛttvāvirodhaḥ ityartha: |

niyuṅakte- 'mantradarśanānuguṇaṃ tapaścarata' iti niyuṅkte ityarthaḥ | askhalitān paśyanti | pūrva kalpā dhauta vedamantratva pratyabhijñāpūrvakaṃ paśyanti ; na tu idamprathamatayā ityarthaḥ | śrutirapi ca ṛṣijñānapūrvakrameva mantrāṇām, anuṣṭhānaṃ darśayati | uktaṃ ca bhagavatā vyāsenaiva ; 'yugānte'- ntarhitān vedān setihāsān maharṣayaḥ | lebhire tapasā pūrvam anujñātāḥ svayambhuvā ||' iti |

'nitya' siddhān pūrvapūrvavasiṣṭhādi dṛṣṭān' tāneva mantrādīn adhītyaiva, svarato varṇataśca askhalitān paśyanti | ataśca vedānāṃ nityatvam eṣāṃ ca mantrakṛccam upapadyate ||28||

atha syāt, naimittikapralayādiṣu indrādyutpattau vedaśabdebhyaḥ pūrvapūrvendrādi ' smaraṇena prajāpatinā devādi sṛṣṭiḥ upapadyatāṃ nāma | prākṛtapralaye tu sraṣṭuḥ 'prajāpateḥ ' bhūtādyahaṅkārapariṇāmaśabdasya ca "vinaṣṭatvāt kathaṃ prajāpateḥ śabdapūrvikā sṛṣṭiḥ upapadyate ? kathantarāṃ vinaṣṭasya vedasya nityatvam ?
ataḥ - vedanityatvavādinā, devādīnāṃ vigrahavattvābhyupagame'pi, lokavyavahārasya pravāhānāditā āśrayaṇīyā - iti ||

atra uttaraṃ paṭhati

94 samānanāmarūpatvāccāvṛttāvapyavirodho daśanāt smṛteśca || 1-3-29 ||
atra 'itihāsa' śabdena vedaśabdāntargatānāṃ purāvṛttānāṃ vivakṣitatvāt 'setihāsān' ityanena, vedānām arthajñānena saha labdhatvamuktaṃ bhavati ||
atha syāt . . . . itīti | mahāpralaye tattadarthasmṛtipūrvakasraṣṭṛsampradāyapravartakamya prajāpateḥ, tatpravartyasya śabdasya ca abhāvāt na śabdanityatvamiti prapañca pravāhānāditva māśrayaṇīyam iti codyaparihārārtham idaṃ sūtramityarthaḥ ||

iti saṅkalpya, bhogya - bhoktṛjātaṃ svasmin śaktimātrāvaśeṣaṃ pralīnaṃ vibhajya, mahadādi- "brahmāṇḍaṃ hiraṇyagarbhaparyantaṃ yathāpūrva sṛṣṭvā, vedāṃca pūrvānupūrvīviśeṣa saṃsthitān āviṣkṛtya hiraṇyagarbhāya upadiśya, pūrva 'deva' devādyākārajagatsarge 'taṃ niyujya', svayamapi tadanta- rātmatayā avasthe | ataḥ yathoktaṃ sarvamupapannam ||

etadeva ca vedasya apauruṣeyatvaṃ nityatvaṃ ca - yat pūrvapūrvoccāraṇakrama janita saṃskāreṇa tameva kramaviśeṣaṃ smṛtvā tenaiva krameṇa uccāryatvam | tat asmāsu sarveśvare'pi samānam | iyāṃstu viśeṣaḥ - saṃskārānapekṣameva svayameva anusandhatte puruṣottamaḥ ||
evakāreṇa sūtrasthaḥ caśabdaḥ, avadhāraṇārthaḥ iti gamyate | bhagavān iti, jñānaśaktyā divaicitryam | bhagavatpuruṣottamaśabdābhyām, 'sarvavyāpī ca bhagavān' 'mahān prabhurvai puruṣa' ityādiśvetāśvataravākyaṃ smāritam | vidyamānasyaiva bhogya - bhoktṛvargasya bhogyatvādyanuguṇa- śaktirasti | na tu tatkāryaṃ bhoktṛtvādikamiti bhāva: | [tam — hiraṇyagarbham | tadantarātma- tayā—tasyāntaḥpraviśya niyantṛtayā | svayamapīti, caturmukhādereva svatantrasraṣṭṛtvabhramanirā- sātha | anena caturmukha-paramātmanoḥ kartṛtvavacasām, aika kaṇṭyasiddhiḥ | ' tavāntarātmā mama ca' iti caturmukhavacanam, 'sṛṣṭiṃ tataḥ kariṣyāmi tvāmāviśya prajāpate' iti ca bhagavadvacanam | 'tadanu praviśya' iti ca sāmānyaviṣayā śrutiḥ | sarvamupapannamiti samānanāmarūpavatva-tatkṛta- vedanityatva- tatsārthakatvāni upapannāni ityarthaḥ ||

saṃskārānapekṣam – adhyāpakoccāraṇasvoccāraṇa janitasaṃskārānapekṣam | svayameva anu sandhatte – iti | vedākhyaṃ śabdarāśiṃ pūrvapūrvakramaviśiṣṭameva uccārayeyam ; na tu kramāntaraṃ

kuta idaṃ yathoktamavagamyate ? - iti cet,
tatrā''ha – darśanāt smṛteśca | darśanaṃ tāvat-

'yo brahmāṇaṃ vidadhāti pūrva, yo vai vedāṃca prahiṇoti tasmai' iti | smṛtirapi mānavī-

'āsīdidaṃ tamobhūtam'

ityārabhya,

'so'bhidhyāya śarīrātsvātsisṛkṣurvividhāḥ prajāḥ |
apa eva sasarjādau tāsu vīryamapāsṛjat ' || 'tadaṇḍamabhavadvaimaṃ sahasrāṃśusamaprabham |
tasmin jajñe svayaṃ brahmā sarvalokapitāmahaḥ ||'

iti | tathā paurāṇikī

'tatra suptasya devasya nābhau padmamajāyata |
tasmin padme mahābhāga vedavedāṅgapāragaḥ ||
brahmotpannassa tenoktaḥ prajāssṛja mahāmate ||'

tathā-

'paro nārāyaṇo devastasmājjātaścaturmukhaḥ'

ataḥ devādīnāmapi arthitvasāmarthyayogāt, 'brahmavidyāyāmadhikāraḥ' asti

iti siddham ||29||

25. pramitādhikaraṇagarbhe madhyadhikaraṇam ||1-3-8 || adhikaraṇārtha : - vasvādipadaṃ prāptānāmapi madhuvidyādiṣu adhikāraḥ ]
95. madhvādiṣvasambhavādanādhikāraṃ jāmīnaḥ || 1-3-30||

brahmavidyāyāṃ devādīnāmapi adhikāro'stīti, uktam ||

kiṃ prāptam ?

nāstyadhikāraḥ, teṣu madhvādiṣu — iti jaiminiḥ manyate | kutaḥ ?
asambhavāt | na hi ! ādityavaskhādibhiḥ upāsyāḥ ādityavasvādayaḥ, 'anye' sambhavanti | na ca 'vasvādīnāṃ satāṃ, vasvāditvaṃ prāpyaṃ bhavati' ; prāptatvāt | madhuvidyāyām ṛgvedādipratipādya karma niṣpādyasya', raśmidvāreṇa prāptasya rasasya āśrayatayā labdhamadhuvyapadeśasya ādityasya " aṃśānāṃ " " vakhādibhiḥ " bhujyamānānām upāsyatvaṃ, vasvāditvaṃ ca prāpyaṃ śrūyate-
'asau vā ādityo devamadhu'

ityupakramya,

'tadyatprathamamamṛtaṃ tadvasava upajīvanti'

ityuktvā,

- yadā vasutvamātraṃ phalam ; tadā vasvādidevatāmātrasya upāsyatvena, tasyā devatāyāḥ tadvidyopasaṃhārasāmarthyābhāvāt yacchabdasya tadvyatiriktaviṣayatvāt, tatrānadhikāraḥ— iti |

'sa ya etadevamamṛtaṃ veda, vasūnāmevaiko bhūtvā, abhinaiva mukhenaitadevāmṛtaṃ dṛṣṭvā tṛpyati'

ityādinā || 30 ||

97. bhāvaṃ tu bādarāyaṇo'sti hi || 1-3-32||

āditya vasvādīnāmapi teṣu adhikārabhāvaṃ bhagavān bādarāyaṇo manyate | asti hi āditya va svādīnāmapi svāvasthabrahmopāsanena' 'vasvāditya' prāptipūrvakatrahma- presa sambhavaḥ | idānīṃ vasvādīnāmapi satāṃ', 'kalpāntare'pi vasvāditvaprāptiśca apekṣitā bhavati ||

|

atra hi kārya-kāraṇobhayāvasthabrahmopāsanaṃ vidhīyate ;
'asau vā ādityo devamadhu'

ityārabhya,

'atha tata ūrdhva udetya'
ityataḥ prāka, āditya-svādikārya' viśeṣāvasthaṃ 'brahma, upāsyamupadiśyate |
svāvasthabrahmopāsanaśabdena karma-kartṛvirodhaḥ parihṛtaḥ ||
atra hīti | upāsanasya brahmaparyantatve, phalasya brahmaprāptiparyantatve ca pratipādye sati hi, karmakartṛvirodhābhāvaḥ prāptaphalatvābhāvaśca | tat katham ? ityatra |
asau vā ityādi | ādityā dināma tadūpaviśiṣṭajīvaśarīrakaṃ brahma kāryāvastham ; tadupāsanaṃ vihitam | atha tata ityādi | idānīmādityā dināmarūpabhājo'pi sṛṣṭeḥ pūrvam ādityādināma- rūparahita jīvamātrasya antaryāmi brahma kāraṇāvastham tadupāsanaṃ vidhīyate ||

janmāntare vasutvasya prāptyarthaṃ svatanorbhavet |
parasyopāsanaṃ teṣāṃ bādarāyaṇa āha hi ||

'atha 'tata' ūrdhva udetya'
ityādinā, ādityāntarātmatayā avasthitaṃ, kāraṇāvasthameva brahma 'upāsyamupadiśyate | tadevaṃ kārya kāraṇobhayāvasthaṃ brahma upāsīnaḥ, kalpāntare vastrāditvaṃ prāpya, tadante kāraṇaṃ paraṃ brahmaiva āmoti ||

' na ha vā asmā udeti, na nimrocati sakṛddivā haivāsmai bhavati,
ya etāmevaṃ brahmopaniṣadaṃ veda'
iti kṛtsnāyāḥ madhuvidyāyāḥ brahmopaniṣatyaśravaṇāt brahmaprāptiparyantavastrāditvaphalasya śravaṇācca', vasvādibhogyabhūtādityāṃśasya vidhīyamānam upāsanaṃ, tadavasthasyaiva brahmaṇaḥ ityavagamyate | ataḥ evaṃvidhamupāsanam, ādityavasvādīnāmapi sambhavati | evañca brahmaṇa eva upāsyatvāt,
'taṃ devā jyotiṣāṃ jyotiḥ'
ityapi, upapadyate | tadāha vṛttikāra:-
'asti hi madhvādiṣu sambhavo brahmaṇa eva sarvatra nicāyyatvāt '

iti || 32 ||
