26. śramitādhikaraṇagarbhe apaśūdrādhikaraṇam ||1-3-9||
98. śugasya tadanādaraśravaṇāt tadādravaṇāt sūcyate hi || 1-3-33||

brahmavidyāyāṃ śūdrasyā'pi adhikāraḥ asti ? na vā ? - iti vicāryate ||

kiṃ yuktam ?

asti iti |

kutaḥ ?

arthitva-sāmarthyaprayuktatvāt adhikārasya ; śūdrasyā'pi tatsambhavāt ||

yadyapi agnividyāsādhyeṣu karmasu, anagnividyatvāt śūdrasya anadhikāraḥ; tathā'pi manovṛttimātratvāt brahmopāsanasya, tatra adhikāraḥ astyeva | śāstrīyakriyāpekṣatve'pi upāsanasya, tattadvarṇāśramocitakriyāyā eva apekṣitatvāt śūdrasyā'pi svavarṇocitapūrva- varṇaśuśrūṣaiva kriyā bhaviṣyati |
'tasmācchūdro yajñe'navaklataḥ '
ityapi, agnividyāsādhyayajñādikamanadhikāra eva nyāya siddhaḥ, anūdyate ||

nanu – anadhītavedasya aśrutavedāntasya brahma svarūpa tadupāsana' prakārānabhijñasya kathaṃ brahmopāsanaṃ sambhavati 1
ucyate—anadhītavedasya aśrutavedāntavākyasyā'pi itihāsa - purāṇaśravaṇenā'pi brahmasvarūpa-tadupāsanajñānaṃ sambhavati | asti ca śūdrasyā'pi itihāsa purāṇaśravaṇānujñā ;
tayoḥ svātantryeṇa brahmāvagatihetutvāt, brahmaviṣayasmṛtisantatiḥ anadhītavedasyā'pi sambhavatīti, sāmarthyalābhāt śūdrasyā'pyadhikāraḥ sambhavati iti |
rāddhāntaḥ—yadā 'śūdra'śabdo yaugika: ; tadā 'śūdra !' iti āmantraṇasya itihāsa- purāṇayoḥ brahmāvabodhanairapekṣyā navagamakatayā svātantryeṇa brahmāvabodhakatvāt vedādeva brahmāvagati- riti, anadhītavedasya tadasambhavena, sāmarthyābhāvāt na brahmopāsanādhikāraḥ iti |

vidyā vedavidyā | agniḥ - āhitaḥ |
nyāyasiddhaḥ anūdyate iti | na hi asāvarthaḥ śrutye kasamadhigamyaḥ ; yena yāvadvacanatā syāt, parisaṅkhyā vā | kintu nyāyasiddhaḥ | nyāyaśca agnividyāsādhyeṣu anagnividyasya adhi- kārānupapattirūpaḥ karmopāsanasādhāraṇaḥ | naitāvatā brahmavidyānadhikāraḥ; agnividyaḥsādhyakarma- viṣayatvāt nyāyasya | yajñādikarmaṇāmeva agnividyā sādhyatvāt, brahmavidyāyāḥ tannirapekṣatvācca | karmakāṇḍāpaśūdrādhikaraṇanyāya siddhaḥ ityarthaḥ ||

'śrāvayeccaturo varṇān kṛtvā brāhmaṇamagrataḥ ' ityādau | dṛśyante ca itihāsa - purāṇeṣu vidurādayo brahmaniṣṭhāḥ |
tathā upaniṣatsvapi saṃvargavidyāyāṃ śūdrasyā'pi brahmavidyādhikāraḥ pratīyate | "śuśrūṣu hi' jānaśrutim ācāyoṃ raikvaḥ, 'śūdra' ityāmantrya tasmai brahmavidyām upadiśati,
'ājahāremāḥ śūdrānenaiva mukhenālāpayiṣyathāḥ'
ityādinā | ataḥ śūdrasyā'pi adhikāraḥ sambhavati ||

iti prāpte, ucyate na śūdra' syādhikāraḥ 'sambhavati' ; sāmarthyābhāvāt | na hi brahmasvarūpa - tadupāsanaprakāram ajānataḥ, tadaṅgabhūtavedānuvacanayajñādiṣu anadhi- kṛtasya, upāsanopasaṃhāra' sāmarthya sambhavaḥ' | asamarthasya ca arthitvasadbhāve'pi adhikāro na sambhavati | asāmarthyaṃ ca vedādhyayanābhāvāt ||

yathaiva hi, traivarṇikaviṣayādhyayana vidhi siddhasvādhyāyasampādyajñānalābhena, karmavidhayaḥ jñāna- tadupāyādīna aparān na svīkurvanti ; tathā brahmopāsanavidhayo'pi | ataḥ adhyayana- vidhisiddhasvādhyāyādhigatajñānasyaiva brahmopāsanopāyatvāt śūdrasya brahmopāsanasāmarthyā-

na svātantryeṇa | śūdrasya itihāsa - purāṇaśravaṇānujñānaṃ pāpa' kṣayādiphalārtham ' ; na upā- sanārtham | vidurādayastu bhavāntarādhigatajñānāpramopāt jñānavantaḥ ; prārabdhakarmavaśācca | īdṛśajanmayogina iti, teṣāṃ brahmaniṣṭhatvam ||

" yattu " - saṃvargavidyāyāṃ śuśrūṣoḥ 'śūdra !' iti sambodhanam, śūdrasya adhikāraṃ " sūcayati – iti',

tannetyā''ha - śugasya tadanādaraśravaṇāttadā dravaṇāt sūcyate hi | |

śocati iti hi śūdraḥ |
'śucerdaśca'

iti ' ra ' pratyaye, dhātośca dīrghe, cakārasya ca dakāre, 'śūdra' iti bhavati | ataḥ śocita- tvameva asya śūdraśabdaprayogena sūcyate ; na jātiyogaḥ |

na upāsanārthamiti | upabṛṃhaṇīyavedajñānānadhikārāt, upabṛṃhaṇamātreṇa upāsanopa- yogijñānapauṣkalyaṃ na sambhavati | na hi vyākhyeyānadhikāriṇaḥ, vyākhyānamātreṇa puṣkalajñāna- sambhavaḥ iti, dṛṣṭasāmarthyavaikalyam | 'itihāsa .... bṛṃhayet' iti vedārtha - viśadīkāra hetutayaiva tadavagamavidhānāta, aṅgatayā vihitasya svatantratayopādāne, tajjanyajñānasya pustakapāṭhādilabdhavedārthajñānasyeva śāstravirodhāt adṛṣṭasāmayaivaikalyaṃ ca | ataḥ tada virodhasya nyāyyatvāt śrāvaṇa-vidhyavaiyarthyācca ||

śūdra iti | śocitṛtvaṃ, 'śūdra' śabdārthaḥ | śokakāraṇam, anādaravākyaśravaṇam ; tatkāryam, ādravaṇam | 'ra' pratyaye iti | 'ra' pratyaye iti | 'rak' pratyayaḥ prakaraṇasiddhaḥ cakārākṛṣṭaḥ | 'amitamyohīṃrghaśca' iti sūtre 'dīrghaśca' ityaṃśo'nuvartate iti bhāvaḥ | śuca eva hi upapādyatvaṃ pratīyate ; prathamāntanirdeśāt | śravaṇādravaṇaśabdau hi pañcamyantau | tasmāt śoka eva 'śūdra' śabdapravṛttinimittam - |

' kṣayārtham - da. śūdraśabdena - tha.

sūcyate - da.

jānaśrutiḥ kila pautrāyaṇaḥ, bahudravyapradaḥ bahvannapradaśca babhūva | tasya dhārmikā- gresarasya dharmeṇa prītayoḥ, kayościnmahātmanoḥ asya brahmajijñāsāmutpipādayiṣatoḥ, haṃsarūpeṇa niśāyām asya avidūre gacchatoḥ, anyataraḥ itaramuvāca -

"bho bhoyi' bhallākṣa ! bhallākṣa ! jānaśruteḥ pautrāyaṇasya samaṃ
divā jyotirātataṃ tanmā prasāṅgī staccā' mā pradhākṣīt '

iti |

evaṃ jānaśrutipraśaṃsārūpaṃ vākyam upaśrutya, paro haṃsaḥ pratyuvāca-
'kambara enametatsantaṃ sayugvānamiva raikkamāttha'
iti | kaṃ santamenaṃ jānaśrutiṃ, sayugvānaṃ raikkaṃ brahmajñamiva guṇaśreṣṭham etadāttha ? sa brahmajña raiṃka eva loke guṇavattaraḥ | mahatā dharmeṇa saṃyuktasyāpi asya jānaśruteḥ abrahmajñasya ko guṇaḥ ? yadguṇajanitaṃ tejaḥ, raikvateja iva māṃ dahet ityarthaḥ ||

evamuktena pareṇa 'ko'sau raikvaḥ ?' iti pṛṣṭaḥ, 'loke yat kiścit sādhvanuṣṭhitaṃ karma, yacca sarvacetanagataṃ vijñānam, tadubhayaṃ yadīyajñānakarmāntarbhūtam sa raikvaḥ'

ityāss ||

viditvā āgate, svayamapi raikva 'mupasadya', gavāṃ pachataṃ niṣkam aśvatarīrathaṃ ca, raikvāya upahṛtya, rakka prārthayāmāsa
'anu ma etāṃ bhagavo devatāṃ śādhi, yāṃ devatā' mupāsse" iti | tvadupāsyāṃ parāṃ devatāṃ 'mama' anuśādhi ityarthaḥ ||

sa ca raikkaḥ, svayogamahima vidita lokatrayaḥ – jānaśruteḥ brahmajñānavidhuratā nimittā- nādara garbhahaṃsavākyaśravaṇena śokā' viṣṭatāṃ tadanantarameva brahmajijñāsayā udyogaṃ ca viditvā asya brahmavidyāyogyatām abhijñāya, 'cirakālasevāṃ vinā 'dravyapradānena ' śuśrūṣamāṇasya asya, yāvacchaktipradānena brahmavidyā pratiṣṭhitā bhavati' iti matvā tamanugṛhṇan, "tasya' śokāviṣṭatām upadeśayogyatākhyāpikāṃ śūdraśabdenāmantraṇena jñāpayan idamāha --
'aha hāre tvā śūdra ! tatraiva saha gobhirastu'
iti | saha gobhiḥ ayaṃ rathaḥ tatraiva astu na etāvatā mahyaṃ dattena, brahmajijñāsayā śokāviṣṭasya tava, brahmavidyā pratiṣṭhitā bhavati ityarthaḥ ||

ataḥ śūdraśabdena 'vidyopadeśa yogyatākhyāpanārtha, śoka eva asya sūcitaḥ " ;

na caturthavarṇatvam || 33 |

tadevam, 'upakramagatā 'khyāyikāyāṃ kṣatriyatvapratītiruktā | 'upasaṃhāra'gatā- khyāyikāyāmapi kṣatriyatvam asya pratīyate ityāha-
100. uttaratra caitrarathena liṅgāt || 1-3-35 ||

asya jānaśruteḥ upadiśyamānāyām asyāmeva saṃvargavidyāyām, uttaratra kīrtya - mānena, abhipratārināmnā caitrarathena kṣatriyega, asya kṣatriyatvaṃ gamyate |

katham ?

'atha ha śaunakaṃ ca kāpeyamabhipratāriṇaṃ ca

kākṣa seniṃ pariviṣyamāgau', brahmacārī vibhikṣe'

ityādinā,

'brahmacārina nedamupāsmahe'

ityantena, kāpeyābhipratāriṇoḥ bhikṣamāṇasya brahmacāriṇaśca saṃvargavidyāsambandhitvaṃ pratīyate | teṣu ca abhipratārī kṣatriyaḥ ; itarau brāhmaṇau| ataḥ asyāṃ vidyāyāṃ brāhmaṇasya, taditareṣu' ca 'kṣatriyasyaiva' anvayo dṛśyate ; na śūdrasya | ataḥ asyāṃ vidyāyāmanvitāt
kathamiti | caitrarathaḥ kṣatriyaścet, jānaśrutirapi kṣatriyaḥ iti na hi niyamaḥ ityarthaḥ | pariviṣya māṇau – sūdena bhojyamānauṃ | brahmacāriṇo bhikṣaṇaṃ kāpeyābhipratāriṇoḥ traivarṇika- tvaliṅgamiti arthasiddham | ekatra brāhmaṇa kṣatriyajātidvayasambandhitayā avagatāyām, anyatrā'pi brāhmaṇasambandhitayā nirṇītāyām, tatsambandhipuruṣāntaraviṣayasandehe sati, agrayaprāyanyāyāt

raikvāḍrāhmaṇādanyasya jānaśruterapi kṣatriyatvameva yuktam ; na caturthavarṇatvam ||

nanu – asmin prakaraṇe, abhipratāriṇaḥ caitrarathatvaṃ kṣatriyatvaṃ ca na śrutam ; tatkatham asya abhipratāriṇaḥ caitrarathatvam ? kathaṃ vā kṣatriyatvam ?

tatrā''ha - liṅgāt – iti |

'atha ha śaunakaṃ ca kāpeyamabhipratāriṇaṃ ca kākṣasenim' iti abhipratāriṇaḥ kāpeyasāhacaryālliṅgāt, asya abhipratāriṇaḥ kāpeyasambandhaḥ pratīyate | anyatra ca,
'etena vai caitrarathaṃ kāpeyā ayājayan'
iti kāpeyasambandhinaḥ 'caitrarathatvaṃ śrūyate | tathā caitrarathasya' kṣatriyatvam, 'tasmāccaitraratho nāmaikaḥ kṣatrapatirajāyata' iti | ataḥ abhipratāriṇaḥ caitrarathatvaṃ kṣatriyatvaṃ ca gamyate || 35 ||
tadevaṃ, nyāyavirodhini śūdrasyādhikāre, liṅgaṃ nopalabhyate - ityuktam | idānīṃ 'nyāyasiddhaḥ śūdrasya' anadhikāraḥ, śrutismṛtibhiḥ anugṛhyate, ityāha- 101 saṃskāraparāmarśāttadabhāvābhilāpācca || 1-3-36||
tasya puruṣasya brāhmaṇakṣatriyayoranyataratvam, pariśeṣeṇa kṣatriyatvaṃ ca niścīyate ityarthaḥ |
kāpeyasāhacaryāt — kāpeyasahapāṭhāt | kāpeyasahapāṭhavalena kāpeyasambandhaḥ siddhaḥ | tena tatpurohitakasya caitrarathatvaṃ siddham | tena kṣatriyatvamavagatam ityarthaḥ || 35 ||
tadevamiti | 'śūdra'śabdasya rūḍhisiddhārthavirodhi liṅga, yaugikārthānuguṇaliṅgaṃ ceti, ubhayavidhaṃ liṅgamapi śūdānadhikārajñāpakam ityarthaḥ |
idānīmiti | ayam anadhikāraḥ śrutismṛtibhiḥ anugṛhyate ityāhetyarthaḥ |

brahmavidyāprakaraṇe saṃskāra parimarśanāt | saṃskārābhāvavacanānna śūdrasyādhikāritā ||
1 caitrarathasya – du. 2 nyāyasiddha śūdrasya - da.

3 śrutibhiḥ - du.

brahmavidyopadeśapradeśeṣu upanayanasaṃskāraḥ parāmṛśyate-
'upa tvā neṣye'
'taṃ hopaninye' ( )

102. tadabhāvanirdhāraṇe ca pravṛtteḥ || 1-3-37||
'naitadabrāhmaṇo vivaktumarhati, samidhaṃ somyāhara' iti śuśrūṣoḥ jābālasya śūdratvābhāvanirdhāraṇe satyeva vidyopadeśa' pravṛtteśva; na śūdrasyā- dhikāraḥ || 37 ||

na śūdre pātakaṃ kiñcit – sandhyāvandanādyabhāvanimittaṃ pāpaṃ nāstītyarthaḥ | ekajāti- riti | na tu dvijātirityarthaḥ || 36 ||
śūdratvābhāveti | tacchannasya pūrvasūtraprakṛtasaṃskāravāci- tāyām etatsūtraviṣayavākyānuguṇyā bhāvāt tacchabdaḥ prakṛtaparāmarśīti bhāvaḥ || 37 ||

jābālasya tu śuśrūṣoḥ śūdrasvābhāvaniścaye |
upadeśapravṛtteśca na śūdrasyādhikāritā ||

103. śravaṇādhyayanārthapratiṣedhāt || 1-3-38 ||

śūdrasya vedaśravaṇa-tadadhyayana - tadarthānuṣṭhānāni pratiṣidhyante
'padyuha vā etacchamaśānaṃ yacchūdraḥ, tasmācchūdrasamīpe nādhyetavyam'

'tasmācchūdro bahupagurayajñīyaḥ '

iti | bahupaśuḥ – paśusadṛśaḥ ityarthaḥ | anupaśṛṇvataḥ adhyayana - tadarthajñāna-tadarthānuṣṭhānāni na sambhavanti | ataḥ tānyapi pratiṣiddhānyeva || 38 ||

104. smṛteśca || 1-3-39||
arthaśabdena tadanuṣṭhānaṃ vivakṣitam |

padyu – jaṅgamam | samīpoccāraṇapratiṣedhādeva śravaṇādhyayanapratiṣedhaḥ arthasiddhaḥ | tasmācchūdraḥ iti, arthānuṣṭhānapratiṣedhaṃ darśayati | paśusadṛśa ityartha iti | īṣadasamāptapaśutvaḥ iti vaktavye, sadṛśaśabdragrahaṇam, 'īṣadasamāpteḥ sādṛśyaparyavasānajñāpanārtham ' | 'vibhāṣā supo bahuc purasta tu' iti pāṇinīyasūtreṇa īṣadasamāsyarthe 'bahuc' pratyayaḥ ; tasya purastāt prayogaśca vihitaḥ | adhyayaneti | 'śūdro nādhīyīta' iti vākyamapi anusandheyam | anena yajñe viśeṣaniṣedhāt anyatra vaidike karmaṇi śūdrasyādhikāraśaṅkā ca nirastā bhavati || 38 ||

śrāmnāyaśravaṇādhyāyayāgādipratiṣedhataḥ |
upāsanepyadhīkāro na śūdrasyeti niścitam ||
jñāpakāt (na śūdravarṇīya: vedokta vidyādhikārī ) | ]

na cāsyopadiśeddharmamityādismṛtitastathā |
upāsane nā'dhikāraśśūdrasyeti ca gamyate ||

smaryate ca śravaṇādiniṣedhaḥ ;

'atha hāsya vedamupaśṛṇvataḥ trapujatubhyāṃ śrotra' prati pūraṇam', udāharaṇe jihvācchedaḥ, dhāraṇe śarīrabhedaḥ'

iti,

'na cātyopadiśeddharbha na cāsya vratamādiśet' iti ca | ataḥ śūdrasya anadhikāraḥ - iti siddham || 39 ||

ye tu - nirviśeṣacinmātraṃ brahmaiva paramārthaḥ anyatsarva mithyābhūtam | bandhatha apāramārthikaḥ | sa ca vākyajanya vastuyāthātmyajñānamātranivartyaḥ ; tannivṛttireva mokṣaḥ- 'iti' vadanti,
taiḥ, brahmajñāne 'śūdrādeḥ' anadhikāro vaktuṃ na śakyate ; anupanītasya anadhīta- vedasya aśrutavedāntavākyasyāpi, yasmātkasmādapi ' 'nirviśeṣacinmātraṃ brahmaiva paramārthaḥ, anyatsarvaṃ tasmin 'parikalpitaṃ mithyābhūtam" iti vākyāt vastuyāthātmyajñānotpatteḥ ; tāvataiva bandhanivṛtteśca |

na ca tattvamasyādivākyenaiva jñānotpattiḥ kāryā na vākyāntareṇa - iti niyantuṃ śakyam ; jñānasya apuruṣatantratvāt, satyāṃ sāmagracām anicchato'pi jñānotpatteḥ|

atha hetyādi, adhyayanādau prāyaścittasmaraṇam upadeśaniṣedhasmaraṇaṃ ca | anadhikāra- jñāpakatvāt ubhayamapi 'smṛti' śabdavivakṣitamiti bhāvaḥ ||
ata ityādi | ' rathakāro'mmīnādadhīta' ityādiṣu 'rūḍhiryogamapaharati' iti nyāyena rūḍhārtha: svīkṛtaḥ | tadapavādaḥ, arthavirodhaḥ | anukūla liṅgasadbhāve, virodhiliṅgābhāve ca rūḍhe prābalyam | virodhiliṅgasadbhāvaḥ tadapavādaḥ ityuktaṃ bhavati | ' rathakāro'mīnādadhīta' ityatrāpi, 'saudhanvanā ṛbhava:' iti anukūla liṅgamupalabhyate iti ||39|| anupanītasyeti | nahi 'nāḍyaṃ sarpa:, rajjureṣā' iti vākyāt bhramanivṛtteḥ, upanayanā- disaṃskārāpekṣā iti bhāvaḥ |
satyāṃ sāmagryām iti, apuruṣatantratve hetumāha |

na ca vedavākyādeva vastuyāthātmyajñāne sati, bandhanivṛttirbhavati - iti 'śakyaṃ vaktum ' ; yena kenāpi vastuyāthātmyajñāne sati, 'bhrānti' nivṛtteḥ | pauruṣeyādapi, 'nirviśeṣacinmātraṃ brahma paramārthaḥ '; anyatsarvaṃ mithyābhūtam' iti vākyāt jñānotpatteḥ ; tāvataiva bhramanivṛtteśca | yathā – pauruṣeyādapi āptavākyāt, śuktikārajatādibhrānti:, brāhmaṇasya śūdrāderapi nivartate ; tadvadeva śūdrasyāpi vedavitsampradāyāgatavākyāt vastu- yāthātmyajñānena, jagadramanivṛttirapi bhaviṣyati ||

'na' cāsyopadiśeddharmam'

ityādinā 'vedavidaḥ śūdrādibhyo na vadanti' iti ca na śakyaṃ vaktum ; tacca- masyādivākyāvagatabrahmātmabhāvānāṃ vedaśirasi vartamānatayā, dagdhākhilādhikāratvena, niṣedhaśāstra kiṅkaratvābhāvāt | atikrāntaniṣedhairvā kaiścit uktāt vākyāt śūdrādeḥ jñānamutpadyata eva ||

nididhyāsanena dvaitavāsanāyāṃ nirastāyāmeva, tatvamasyādivākyaṃ nivartakajñāna- mutpādayati- iti cet; pauruṣeyamapi vākya, śūdrādeḥ tathaiveti, na kaścidviśeṣaḥ |
'nididhyāsana hi nāma, brahmātmabhāvābhidhāyi vākyaṃ yadarthapratipādanayogyaṃ, tadarthabhāvanā | saiva viparītavāsanāṃ nivartayati' iti, dṛṣṭārthatvaṃ nididhyāsanavidheṣe | vedānuvaca' nādīnyapi', vividipotpattāveva upayujyanta iti, śūdrasyā'pi vividiṣāyāṃ jātāyāṃ, pauruṣeyavākyāt nididhyāsanādibhiḥ viparītavāsanāyāṃ nirastāyām, jñāna- mutpatsyate ; tenaiva apāramārthiko bandhaḥ nivartiṣyate ||

anenaiva nyāyena brāhmaṇādīnāmapi brahmavedana siddheḥ ; upaniṣacca tapasvinī dattajalāñjaliḥ syāt ||

na ca vācyam – naisargika' loka 'vyavahāre bhrāmyataḥ asya, kenacit 'ayaṃ laukika- vyavahāraḥ bhramaḥ ; paramārthastu evam" iti samarpite' satyeva pratyakṣānumānavṛttabubhutsā jāyata iti, tatsamarpikā śrutirapi āstheyā - iti
yataḥ, bhavabhayabhītānāṃ sāṃkhyādaya eva pratyakṣānumānābhyāṃ vastunirūpaṇaṃ kurvantaḥ, pratyakṣānumānavṛttabubhutsāṃ janayanti | bubhutsāyāṃ ca jātāyāṃ, pratyakṣānumānābhyāmeva viviktasvabhāvābhyām, 'nityaśuddhasvaprakāśādvitīya kūṭasthacaitanyameva sat; anyatsarvaṃ tasmin abhyastam' iti suvivevam |
evambhūte svaprakāśe vastuni śrutisamadhigamyaṃ viśeṣāntaraṃ ca na abhyupagamyate | adhyasta tadrūpa' nivartinī' hi ! śrutirapi tvanmate |
na ca – 'sataḥ " ātmanaḥ, ānandarūpatājñānāya upaniṣat āstheyā; cidrūpatāyā eva sakale 'tarātadrūpavyāvṛttāyāḥ ānanda rūpatvāt |
anenaiveti | 'ahi śāstramarthavat' | ataḥ prāptārthatvāt bhedajñānajanakatvācca, brāhmaṇāderapi śrutiḥ anādaraṇīyā ityarthaḥ | tapasvinī - śocanīyā | deśa-kāla-vratādyaneka- niyamavatī iti bhāvaḥ |
naisargikaśabdaḥ anāditvaparaḥ | prasañjakatvena śrutirapekṣitā iti śaṅkāgranthārthaḥ |

sāṅakhya-hairaṇyagarbha-naiyāyika-vaiśeṣika- bauddhārhatādibhireva

sāṅkhyādaya eveti |

yasya tu mokṣasādhanatayā vedāntavākyairvihitaṃ jñānam, upāsanarūpam ; tacca parabrahmabhūtaparama puruṣaprīṇanam, tacca śāstraikasamadhigamyam | 'upāsanaśāstra ca' upanayanādi- 'saṃskṛtā' dhītasvādhyāya janitaṃ jñānaṃ viveka vimokādi 'sādhanā'nugṛhītameva svopāyatayā svīkaroti; evaṃrūpo pāsanaprītaḥ puruṣottamaḥ', upāsakaṃ svābhāvikātmayāthātmya- jñānadānena karmajanitājñānaṃ nāśayan bandhāt 'mocayati' – iti pakṣaḥ, tasya yathoktayā nītyā' śūdrāderanadhikāraḥ upapadyate || 39 ||

|| iti apaśūdrādhikaraṇam || 9 ||

2. kṣatriyatva 3. uttaratra 4. saṃskāra 5. tadabhāva 6. śravaṇā 7. smṛteśca ]
|| iti apaśūdrādhikaraṇam || 9 ||

pramitādhikaraṇa śeṣaḥ

tadevaṃ prasaktānuprasaktā 'dhikārakathāṃ parisamāpya, prakṛtasya aṅguṣṭhapramitasya, bhūtabhavyeśitṛtvāvagata parabrahmabhāvottambhanaṃ hetvantaramāha -

'bhayādasya nistapati bhayāttapati sūryaḥ |
bhayādindraśca vāyuśca mṛtyurdhāvati pañcamaḥ ||'
iti kṛtsnasya jagataḥ, agni-sūryādīnāṃ ca asmin aṅguṣṭhamātre puruṣe 'prāṇa' śabda- nirdiṣṭe sthitānāṃ sarveṣāṃ tato niḥsṛtānāṃ tasmāt sañjāta' mahābhaya' nimittam, ejanam - kampanaṃ śrūyate | tacchāsanā tivṛttau 'kiṃ' bhaviṣyati ? iti mahato bhayāt vajrādiva udyatāt, kṛtsnaṃ jagat kampate ityarthaḥ ||

itaśca, aṅguṣṭhapramitaḥ puruṣottamaḥ

106. jyotirdarśanāt || 1-3-41 ||

'tayoreva' aṅguṣṭha' pramitaviṣayayoḥ" vākyayormadhye, parabrahmāsādhāraṇaṃ sarvatejasāṃ
chādakaṃ, sarvatejasāṃ kāraṇabhūtam anugrāhakaṃ ca aṅguṣṭhapramitasya jyotiḥ dṛśyate
'na tatra sūryo bhāti na candratārakaṃ nemā vidyuto bhānti kuto'yamagniḥ |

tameva bhāntamanubhāti sarve tasya bhāsā sarvamidaṃ vibhāti |' iti | ayameva ślokaḥ ātharvaṇe paraṃ brahmādhikṛtya śrūyate | parajyotiṣṭuṃ ca sarvatra parasya brahmaṇaḥ śrayate | yathā
'paraṃ jyotirūpasampadya svena rūpeṇābhiniṣpadyate ' 'taṃ devā jyotiṣāṃ jyotirāyupāsate'mṛtam' 'atha yadataḥ paro divo jyotirdīpyate' ityādiṣu | ataḥ aṅguṣṭhu pramitaḥ paraṃ brahma ||| 41||

|| iti pramitā dhikaraṇaśeṣaḥ ||

anubhānaṃ_paścādbhānam | tena kārya kāraṇabhāvaḥ siddhaḥ | paurvāparyaniyamo hi kārya-kāraṇabhāva: iti bhāvaḥ | anugrāhakamiti | 'yasyādityo bhāmupayujya bhāti' ityādiśrutiśca anugrāhakatve pramāṇam | jyotiḥ – dīptiḥ; 'jyotiṣmadbhrājamānaṃ mahasvat' iti prayogāt | ityādiṣviti | evaṃ (pūrva) śabdataḥ pratyabhijñānaṃ hetutayā uktam | arthataḥ śrutyantarapratyabhijñānaṃ ca asya mantrasya paramātmatvasādhakatvopapādakam ityabhiprāyaḥ | ādiśabdena ' tacchubhraṃ jyotiṣāṃ jyotiḥ' 'nārāyaṇaparo jyotiḥ' ityādi abhipretam ||41||

kāraṇabhūtamiti | anumānaṃ paścādbhānam |

27. arthāntaratvādivyapadeśādhikaraṇam || 1-3-10 ||
(adhikaraṇārtha ḥ— nāma-rūpanirvoḍhā ākāśaḥ, paramātmā ]
107. ākāśo'rthāntaratvādivyapadeśāt || 1-3-42 ||

chāndogye śrūyate -

'ākāśo ha vai nāma rūpayornirvahitā, te yadantarā |
tadbrahma tadamṛtaṃ sa ātmā'

iti ||

tatra saṃśayaḥ - kim ayam 'ākāśa' śabda nirdiṣṭaḥ, muktātmā ? uta paramātmā ? -

iti |

śāditi | pūrva daharākāśasya parabrahmatve sādhyamāne, 'alpaśruteḥ ' iti sūtreṇa alpaśrutiḥ parihṛtā | alpaśrutiprasaṅgena aṅguṣṭhapramitasya paratvaṃ sādhitam | tatprasaṅgena adhikaraṇatrayamuktam | atha pūrvaprakṛnadaha ra vidyāyāḥ buddhisthatvena tadanantara- bhūtasya 'ākāśo ha vai' ityādiprakaraṇasya jīvaparatvam āśaṅkaya parihriyate iti saṅgatiḥ ||
saṃśaya iti | kimākāśaśabdanirdiṣṭaḥ, muktaḥ ? uta paramātmā ? iti saṃśayaḥ | tadartha- 'dhūtvā śarīram' iti, muktasyānantaraprakṛtatvam, 'ākāśa' śabdasya muktaparatāmavagamayati ? na ? iti | kiṃ muktasya nāma rūpanirvodṛtvaṃ sambhavati ? na ? iti | kimatra nāma-rūpanirvodṛtvaṃ, nāma rūpabhāktayā ? uta nāma-rūpavyā kartṛtayā ? iti | kiṃ 'te yadantarā' iti nirdeśaḥ, katṛtayā nirvodṛtvamupapādayati ? na ? iti |

ākāśo

nāmarūpavinirvoḍhā hyākāśaḥ parameśvaraḥ |
baddhamuktobhayāvasthādanyatvenāsya kīrtanāt ||

kiṃ yuktam ? muktātmā - iti |

kutaḥ ?

'aśva iva romāṇi vidhūya pāpaṃ candra iva rāhormukhāt pramucya |
dhūtvā śarīramakṛtaṃ kṛtātmā brahmalokamabhisambhavāni ||' iti muktasya anantaraprakṛtatvāt | ' te yadantarā' iti ca nāma-rūpa 'vinirmuktasya' tasya abhidhānāt | ' nāma-rūpayornirvahitā' iti ca sa eva pūrvāvasthayā upalilakṣayiṣitaḥ | sa eva hi devādirūpāṇi nāmāni ca pūrvam avi (bhaḥ) bhṛta | tasyaiva nāma-rūpavinirmuktā' sāmpratikī avasthā, ' tadbrahma tadamṛtam' ityucyate | 'ākāśa' śabdaśca tasminnapi asaṅkucita prakāśayogāt upapadyate ||

'nanu' - daharavākyaśeṣatvāt asya sa eva daharākāśo'yam iti pratīyate | 'tasya ca', paramātmatvaṃ nirṇītam ;
"maivam ' ; prajāpativākyavyavadhānāt | prajāpativākye ca, pratyagātmanaḥ muktyava-

sthāntaṃ rūpamabhihitam | anantaraṃ ca ' vidhūya pāpam' iti sa eva muktāvasyaḥ prastutaḥ | ataḥ atra ākāśaḥ, muktātmā ||

iti prāpte, ucyate - ākāśo'thāntaratvādi 'vyapadeśāt - iti' |

ākāśaḥ paraṃ brahma' |

kuta: ?

"arthāntaratvādi vyapadeśāt | 'arthāntaratva' vyapadeśastāvat, 'ākāśo ha vai nāma-rūpayornirvahitā'
nāma-rūpayornivatvaṃ – baddha - muktobhayāvasthāt pratyagātmanaḥ arthāntaratvam, ākāśasya upapādayati | baddhāvasthaḥ svayaṃ 'karmavaśāt' nāma-rūpe bhajamānaḥ, na nāma-rūpe nirvoḍhuṃ śaknuyāt | muktāvasthasya jagadvyāpārāsambhavāt, "na nitarāṃ nāma-rūpa-

iti |

nirvodṛtvam |

īśvarasya tu 'sakala' jagannirmāṇadhurandharasya nāma-rūpayornivodṛtvaṃ śrutyaiva pratipannam | 'anena jīvenātmanā'nupraviśya nāma-rūpe vyākaravāṇi ' 'yaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarvavit yasya jñānamayaṃ tapaḥ |
tasmādetad brahma nāma rūpamannaṃ ca jāyate ||'
'sarvāṇi rūpāṇi 'vicitya dhīro nāmāni kṛtvābhivadan yadāste'
ityādiṣu | ataḥ nirvāhyanāmarūpāt pratyagātmanaḥ, nāma-rūpayornirvoḍhā ayamākāśaḥ,
vyapadeśastāvaditi | pradaśyate iti śeṣaḥ | arthāntaratvaśabdaḥ tadupa- pādakadharmaṃ lakṣayati | arthāntaratvamākāśasyopapādayati iti hi uktam | bhedakadharmavyapadeśāt ityarthaḥ | nāma-rūpayorityādi | nāma-rūpanirvotvaṃ ca spaṣṭabhedakadharmaḥ | avayavaśaktyā 'nirvodṛ' śabdasya vahanamātra paratvādapi 'rūḍhiyoṃga mapaharati' iti nyāyāt kartṛparatvaṃ yuktamiti

arthāntarabhūtaḥ parameva brahma | tadevopapādayati, 'te yadantarā' iti | 'yasmāt ' ayamākāśaḥ, nāma-rūpe antarā - tābhyāmaspṛṣṭaḥ arthāntarabhūtaḥ; tasmāt, tayornirvoḍhā apahatapāpmatvāt, satyasaṅkalpatvācca nirvahitā ityarthaḥ | ' ādi śabdena brahmatvātmatvā- mṛtavāna gṛhyate |
nirupādhikavṛhatvādayo hi paramātmana eva sambhavanti | tena atrākāśaḥ parameva brahma |
yat punaruktam- 'dhūtvā śarīram' iti, muktaḥ 'anantara' prakṛtaḥ - iti, tanna : 'brahmalokamabhisambhavāni' iti parasyaiva brahmaṇaḥ, anantaraprakṛtatvāt | ; 'yadyapi, abhisambhavituḥ muktasya abhisambhāvyatayā 'paraṃ brahma' nirdiṣṭam ; tathāpi, abhisambhavituḥ muktasya nāma-rūpanirvoḍhatvāsambhavāt, abhisambhāvyaṃ parameva brahma tatra pratyetavyam ||

kiśca - 'ākāśa' śabdena prakṛtasya 'daharākāśasya atra' pratyabhijñānāt, prajāpati- vākyasyāpi upāsakasvarūpakathanārthatvāt upāsya eva daharākāśaḥ prāpyatayā upasaṃhiyate- iti yuktam | 'ākāśa' śabdaca, pratyagātmani na kvacit dṛṣṭacaraḥ | ataḥ atra ākāśaḥ, paraṃ brahma || 42 ||
tadevopapādayati te yadantareti | karmakṛtanāmarūpabhāktatvābhāvāt tayoḥ svecchayā nirvoḍhā paramātmā | na hi ! karmaparādhīnaceṣṭitasya baddhasya, jagadvyāpārānuguṇasaṅkalpādirahitasya muktasya ca nāmarūpanirvodṛtvaṃ sambhavati ityarthaḥ | nirvahitā iti, śrutisthapadānukāraḥ | nirupādhikaśabdaḥ anavacchinnaparaḥ | upādhirhi ! avacchedakaḥ | jīvasya jñānato bṛhattvam | tacca paramātmaprasādāyattam | bṛhattvādayaḥ iti | dharmāḥ iti śeṣaḥ | brahmaloka: brahmaiva lokaḥ |
yadyapi .... pratyetavyamiti | prathamānirdiṣṭasya abhisambhavitu: prādhānyena prākaraṇikatva- svārasye'pi, liṅgabalāt prākaraṇikatvasvārasyabhaṅgo yuktaḥ ityabhiprāya: |

kiñcākāśeti | avāntaraprakaraṇena pūrvavākyasya jīvasvarūpaparatvamabhyupetya parihṛtam | vastutastu brahmalokaśrutyanugṛhīta mahāprakaraṇabalāt pūrvavākyamapi na jīvaparam | kintu taya paramaprāpyabhūta parabrahmaparatvameva ityāśayaḥ | prajāpativākyasyeti | prajāpativākyasya vyavadhāya- katvaśaṅkāvyāvṛtyartham | ākāśetyādinā, hetvantaramāha || 42 ||

anantara:- 4 yadyapi hi – da.

atha syāt –pratyagātmanaḥ arthāntara 'bhūtam ātmāntarameva ' nā'sti; aikyopadeśāt, dvaitapratiṣedhācca | śuddhāvastha eva hi ' ! pratyagātmā 'paramātmā' 'paraṃ brahma' 'parameśvaraḥ ' " iti ca vyapadiśyate | ataḥ prakṛtāt muktātmanaḥ abhisambhavituḥ, na arthāntaram abhisambhāvyo brahmalokaḥ | ataḥ nāma-rūpayoḥ nirvahitā ākāśo'pi sa eva bhavitumarhati — iti |

ataḥ uttaraṃ paṭhati'_

108. suṣuptyutkrāntyorbhedena || 1-3-43 ||

'vyapadeśāt' iti 'vartate'| suṣuptyutkrāntyoḥ pratyagātmano'rthāntaratvena paramātmanaḥ vyapadeśāt, pratyagātmano'rthāntarabhūtaḥ 'paramātmā astyeva | tathā hi vājasaneyake—
nāstīti | dṛḍhapramāṇasiddhaṃ na bhavatītyarthaḥ | aikyopadeśāditi | muktaprāpyatayoktasya, 'brahmaloka 'śabdanirvācyatayā bṛhadāraṇyake pratyabhijñāyamānasya, 'ekadhaivānudraṣṭavyam' 'sa vā eṣa mahānaja ātmā; yo'yaṃ vijñānamayaḥ' iti, jīvena aikyapratipādanāt ityarthaḥ | dvaitaprati- pedhācceti | 'neha nānā'sti kiṃcana' iti bhedaniṣedhācca ityarthaḥ | 'ca' śabdaḥ samuccaye | aikyopadeśa-bhedaniṣedhayoḥ pratyekaṃ bhedavirodhitvāt | brahmaloka iti | tatra hi 'eṣa brahmaloka : samrāḍiti hovāca' iti prakaraṇāvasāne tasya paramātmana: 'brahmaloka 'śabdanirdeśo'vagamyate | ' nāma.... hitā' iti yathāśruti anuvādaḥ | ata eva hi ! 'nāma-rūpayornirvodratvam' iti svagranthe nirdiṣṭam | āpātataḥ arthāntaratve pratīte'pi na tat siddhāntīkartavyam iti śaṅkāgranthasyārthaḥ |
vyapadeśāditi vartata iti | yathā 'atha śabdānuśāsanam' ityatra, keṣāṃ śabdānām ? ityukte, samastasyāpi 'anuśāsana' padasya vibhāgena anuṣaṅgaḥ ; tathā ihāpi iti bhāvaḥ | sūtre suṣuptyutkrāntyoḥ iti saptamī | arthāntarabhūta iti | asya sūtrasya arthāntaratvavyapadeśa-

prājñena sampariṣvaktaḥ suṣuptau kīrtyate pṛthak | bhedena caivamutkrāntau jīvādanyaḥ paraḥ pumān || bhūtaparamātmāntarameva - i. eva - da.

3 iti - vā.

'katama ātmeti | yo'yaṃ vijñānamayaḥ prāṇeṣu'
iti prakṛtasya pratyagātmanaḥ, suṣuptyavasthāyām 'akiñcijjñasya', sarvajñena paramātmanā pariṣvaṅgaḥ āmnāyate
'prājñenāssmanā sampariṣvakto na bāhyaṃ kiṃcana veda, nā''ntaram' iti | tathā utkrāntāvapi

'prājñenā''tmanā'nvārūḍha utsarjana yāti'

iti |

na ca svapataḥ utkrāmato 'vā akiñcijjñasya' tadānīmeva 'svenaiva sarvajñena' satā pariṣvaṅgānvāroha sambhavataḥ | na ca kṣetrajñāntareṇa ; tasyā'pi sarvajñatvāsambhavāt || 43 ||

itaśca – pratyagātmano'rthāntarabhūtaḥ paramātmā - ityāha,

109. patyādiśabdebhyaḥ || 1-3-44 ||

ayaṃ pariṣvañjakaḥ paramātmā, uttaratra patyādiśabdaiḥ vyapadiśyate- "sarvasyā'dhipatiḥ'
rūpahetvasiddhiśaṅkāparihāraparatvāt, arthasiddhaṃ sādhyanirdeśaṃ darśayati | avāntarārthaprati- jñātvāt sūtre kaṇṭhottyabhāvaḥ | pradhānārthapratijñānasya hi ! prāyeṇa kaṇṭhottirupalabhyate | vijñāna ... iti prakṛtasyeti, dehasya prājñapariṣvaktatvaśaṅkāvyāvṛttyartham | utsarjanniti | 'śarīram' iti śeṣaḥ || 43 ||
itaśceti, muktāvasthenā'manā anvāroha: ityatrāha |

sarvasyādhipatītyādiśabdaiśva vyapadeśataḥ |
prājñaśca paramātmāyaṃ jīvādanyaḥ suniścitaḥ | |

28. ānumānikādhikaraṇam ||1-4-1 ||
110. ānumānikamapyekeśāmiti cenna śarīrarūpakavinyasta- gṛhīterdarśayati ca || 1-4-1 ||

uktaṃ paramapuruṣārthalakṣaṇa mokṣasādhanatayā jijñāsyaṃ jagajanmādikāraṇaṃ brahma,

ānumānikamiti | 'prathamapādaḥ, cidacidvilakṣaṇa kāraṇatvāyogavyavacchedaparaḥ | anyayogavyavaccheda parāyāṃ tripādyām aspaṣṭa spaṣṭajīvā diliṅgakavākyaviṣayoṃ dvitīya tṛtīyau | spaṣṭatarajīvādipratipādakavākyaviṣayaḥ caturthaḥ' iti pādasaṅgatiḥ, dvitīyapādārambhe kathitā | sūtrakārastu, anyayogavyavacchedasya tripādīkṛtyatvaṃ caturthapādārambhe kaṇṭhoktam ; 'ānumānika- mapyekeṣām' iti hi uktam |
ataḥ bhāṣyakāraḥ caturthapādārambhe tripādyarthasphuṭīkaraṇamukhena saṅgatiṃ vivakṣan prathama- pādārthānubhāṣaṇapūrvakaṃ dvitīya - tṛtīyayorarthaṃ vṛttaṃ darśayati – uktamiti |

.

acidvastunaḥ pradhānādeḥ cetanācca baddhamuktobhayāvasthāt vilakṣaṇaṃ, nirastasamasta heyagandhaṃ sarvajñaṃ sarvaśakti satyasaṅkalpaṃ samasta kalyāṇaguṇātmakaṃ sarvāntarātmabhūtaṃ niraṅkuśaiśvaryam iti ||
idānīṃ -kāpilatantrasiddhābrahmātmakapradhāna - puruṣādipratipādanamukhena, pradhānakāraṇa- tvapratipādanaccha| yānusārīṇyapi kānicidvākyāni kāsucit śākhāsu santi - ityāśaṅkaya; brahmaikakāraṇatvasthemne, tat nirākriyate ||

kaṭavallī āmnāyate—

'indriyebhyaḥ parārthā arthabhyaśca paraṃ manaḥ |
manasastu parā buddhirbuddherātmā mahān paraḥ ||'
'mahataḥ paramavyaktamavyaktāt puruṣaḥ paraḥ |
puruṣānna paraṃ kiṃcit sā kāṣṭhā sā parā gatiḥ ||'

iti |

tatra sandeha: - kiṃ kāpilatantrasiddham abrahmātmakaṃ pradhānam, iha 'avyakta'- śabdenocyate ? uta na ? – iti ||

pradhānādeḥ iti 'ādi' śabdena prāṇākāśādigrahaṇam | vilakṣaṇam ityantena prathamapādārtha uktaḥ | nirastetyādipadaiḥ dvitīya tṛtīyayorapyarthaṃ darśayati | vailakṣaṇyaṃ ca ubhayaliṅgatayā | samastaśabdena cidvatam acigataṃ ca heyaṃ vivakṣitam | gandha- śabdena muktavyāvṛttiḥ | sarvajña sarvaśakti iti dvitīyapādārthaḥ sūcitaḥ | samasta .... ṇātmakam iti padaṃ dharmisvarūpaparam | niraṅkuśaiśvaryam iti tṛtīyapādārthasūcanam |
pradhānakāraṇatveti | na kevalaṃ liṅgapratipādakāni ; kintu liṅgināmeva pradhānādīnāṃ jagatkāraṇatvapratipādanaśaṅkāspadānīti | ataḥ, jīvādiviṣayatāyāḥ spaṣṭataratvam iti bhāvaḥ | brahmaketi 'eka' śabdena tripādyāḥ anyayogavyavacchedaparatvaṃ sūcitam | sthene ityanena, caturthapādārthaḥ sūcitaḥ | pūrvapādadvayapratipannasya anyayogavyavacchedasya sthairyam, asmin pāde pradhānādikāraṇatvapratipādanavyudasane kṛte hi bhavati ||
kaṭheti | tantrasiddhatastvakramapratyabhijñābhāsajanakāni vākyāni viṣayaḥ | tantrasiddhakrama- tadviparyayapratītibhyāṃ saṃśayaḥ |
tatra sandeha iti | kaṭhavallyām abrahmātmakapradhānakāraṇatvapratipattiḥ asti ? na vā ?

kiṃ yuktam ? pradhānam – iti |

kuta: ?

'mahataḥ paramavyaktamavyaktāt puruṣaḥ paraḥ' iti 'tantrasiddha prakriyā' pratyabhijñānena, tasyaiva pratīteḥ ;
'puruṣānna paraṃ kiṃcit sā kāṣṭā sā parā gatiḥ'
iti pañcaviṃśakapuruṣātiriktatacca niṣedhācca | ataḥ 'avyaktaṃ kāraṇam' iti prāptam | tadidamuktam- 'ānumānikamadhye kepa miti cet' – iti |
ekeṣāṃ śākhināṃ śākhāsu, ānumānikaṃ pradhānamapi kāraṇamāmnāyate - iti cet,

"atra' uttaram ; na – iti | na 'avyakta' śabdena abrahmātmakaṃ pradhānam iha abhidhīyate |

kuta: ?

iti prathamavicāro'bhipretaḥ | tadartham – 'indriyebhyaḥ parāḥ' ityatra 'avyakta' śabdaḥ, kim abrahmātmakapradhānapara: ? uta na ? iti vicāraḥ | tattvagaṇana krama pratyabhijñānamasti ? na? iti | śabdābhyāṃ mahajjīvatantre nirdiśyete ? uta jīva- parau ? iti | | kimiha indriyādi- śabdaiḥ pūrvavākyastharūpakarūpitendriyādipratipattiḥ asti ? uta na ? iti | kim atra paratvaṃ kāraṇabhāvena ? uta vaśīkāryatā ? iti |
pūrva : pakṣa:- yadā kāraṇabhāvena ; tadā rūpitendriyādipratipattyabhāvena, śabdayoḥ mahajjīvatattva samarpakatayā tāntrika prakriyā pratyabhijñānāt, abrahmātmakaṃ pradhānamucyata iti, tasyaiva kāraṇatvaṃ pratīyate iti |

tadartham kim asmin mantre, tantrasiddha-

tadartham ---ayaktātpūrvāparanirdiṣṭa 'mahatpuruṣa '-

'mahat - puruṣa -
'avyakta' śabdena,

siddhānte tu – yadā vaśīkāryatayā paratvaṃ vivakṣitam ; tadā pūrvarūpitendriyādipratipantyā 'mahat- puruṣa' śabdayoḥ jīva paramātmaparatvena, tantrasiddha prakriyāpratyabhijñānābhāvāt, 'avyakta'- śabdaḥ na abrahmātmakapradhānapara iti, na tasyā'tra kāraṇatvapratītiḥ - iti phalaphalibhāvaḥ ||

śarīrarūpaka vinyastagṛhīteḥ – śarīrākhyarūpaka vinyastasya 'avyakta' śabdena gṛhīteḥ, ātma śarīra buddhi mana- indriya-viṣayeṣu rathi - ' rathādibhāvena rūpitepu', ratharūpaṇena 'vinyastasya śarīrasya', atra 'avyakta' śabdena grahaṇāt ityarthaḥ |

etaduktaṃ bhavati – pūrva hi !
'ātmānaṃ rathinaṃ viddhi śarīraṃ rathameva ca | buddhiṃ tusārathiṃ viddhi manaḥ pragrahameva ca ||
indriyāṇi hayānāhurviṣayāsteṣu gocarān ||'

ityādinā,

'so'dhvanaḥ pāramāmoti tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam'
ityantena, saṃsārādhvanaḥ pāraṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ padaṃ prepsantam upāsakaṃ rathitvena, 'taccharīrādīni' ca ratha- rathāṅgatvena rūpayitvā ' yasyaite rathādayaḥ vaśe tiṣṭhanti sa eva adhvanaḥ 'pāraṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ padam āmoti' ityuktvā, teṣu rathādirūpitaśarīrādiṣu, yāni yebhyo ' vaśīkārya - tāyāṃ pradhānāni tānyucyante --
'indriyebhyaḥ parāḥ "" ityādinā ||

tatra hayatvena rūpitebhyaḥ indriyebhyaḥ, gocaratvena rūpitāḥ viṣayāḥ, vaśī' kāryatve' parāḥ ; vaśyendriyasyāpi viṣaya saṃnidhau indriyāṇāṃ durnigrahatvāt | tebhyo'pi paraṃ, 'pragraha' rūpitaṃ manaḥ ; manasi viṣayapravaṇe, viṣayāsaṃnidhānasyā'pi akiścitkaratvāt |
tasmādapi, sārathitvarūpitā buddhi: parā; adhyavasāyābhāve, manaso'pi akiñcitkaratvāt | tasyā api, rathitvarūpitaḥ ātmā, kartutvena prādhānyāt paraḥ | sarvasya cāsya' ātmecchāyattatvāt, ātmaiva 'mahān' iti ca 'viśeṣyate ' | tasmādapi, ratharūpitaṃ śarīraṃ param ; tadāyattatvāt jīvātmanaḥ sakalapuruṣārthasādhanapravṛttīnām |
tasmādapi paraḥ sarvāntarātmabhūtaḥ antaryāmī, adhvanaḥ pārabhūtaḥ paramapuruṣaḥ ; yathoktasya ātmaparyantasya samastasya tatsaṅkalpāyattapravṛttitvāt | ' sa khalu ! antaryāmitayā upāsanasyāpi nirvartakaḥ |

'parāttu tacchruteḥ'

iti hi ! 'jīvātmanaḥ kartṛtvaṃ paramapuruṣa yattam' iti vakṣyate | vaśīkāryopāsana'- nirvṛcyupāya kāṣṭhābhūtaḥ paramaprāpyaśca sa eva | tadidamucyate-
'puruṣāna paraṃ kiñcit sā kāṣṭhā sā parā gatiḥ'
iti | tathā ca antaryāmitrāhmaṇe_
'ya ātmani tiṣṭhan ' ityādibhiḥ, 'sarvaṃ sākṣātkurvan sarva niyamayati' ityuktvā,
'nānyo'to'sti draṣṭā'
'iti' niyantrantaraṃ niṣidhyate | bhagavadgītāsu ca, 'adhiṣṭhānaṃ tathā kartā karaṇaṃ ca pṛthagvidham |
'vividhā ca pṛthaka ceṣṭā daivaṃ caivātra pañcamam ||'

iti | daivamatra puruṣottama eva ;

'sarvasya cāhaṃ hṛdi sanniviṣṭo mattaḥ smṛtijñanimapohanaṃ ca' iti vacanāt | tasya ca vaśīkaraṇa, taccharaṇāgatireva | yathāsss-

'īśvaraḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ hṛdeśe'rjuna tiṣṭhati |
bhrāmayan sarvabhūtāni yantrārūḍhāni māyayā ||

tameva śaraṇaṃ gaccha ' !

||'

'avyakta' śabdenocyate iti niścīyate | ataḥ kāpi tantra' siddhasya' pradhānasya prasaṅga " eva nāsti ||

na ca atra " tattantra siddhaprakriyāpratyabhijñā; 'indriyebhyaḥ parā hyarthā:' iti indriyebhyaḥ arthānāṃ śabdādīnāṃ paratvakīrtanāt | na hiṃ ! śabdādayaḥ, indriyāṇāṃ kāraṇabhūtāḥ tadarśane |
'arthabhyaśca paraṃ manaḥ' ityapi na tattantra saṅgatam ; akāraṇatvādeva | tathā 'buddherātmā mahān paraḥ' ityapi asaṅgatam ; 'buddhi' śabdena 'mahattattvasya abhidhānābhyupa- gamāt | na hi ! mahato mahān paraḥ sambhavati | mahataḥ 'ātma' śabdena viśeṣaṇaṃ ca na saṅgacchate | ataḥ, rūpakavinyastānāmeva grahaṇam |

darśayati ca tadeva-

'eṣa sarveṣu bhūteṣu gūḍho'tmā na prakāśate |

dṛśyate tvagracyā buddhyā sūkṣmayā sūkṣmadarśibhiḥ ||' ' yacchedvāmanasī prājñaḥ tadyacchejjñāna ātmani |
jñānamātmani mahati niyacchet tadyacchecchānta ātmani || ' iti | ajitabāhyābhyantarakaraṇaiḥ, asya paramapuruṣasya durdarśatvamabhidhāya, hayādirūpitānāṃ indriyādīnāṃ vaśīkāraprakāro'yamucyate-
yacchedvāṅmanasī - vācaṃ manasi niyacchet vākpūrvakāṇi karmendriyāṇi upāsananivata katvamastu ; kathaṃ sarvasmātparasya anīśvareṇa saṃsāriṇā vaśīkartuṃ śakyatvam ? śakyaścet, tatprakāro'pi kvacit śrūyeta na ca śrūyate ; 'yacchedvāṅmanasī prājñaḥ' ityādau anupalabdheḥ ityatra |
akāraṇatvādeveti | 'indriyebhyaḥ parā hyarthā:' ityatra tantraprakriyā pratyabhijñānavyudā- saheta revetyarthaḥ | mahata ityādi | 'ātma'śabdaḥ svataḥ cetanaikāntaḥ ; agatyā kvacit tasya atatparatvāśrayaṇam | avayavaśaktyā svakāryavyāptiparatve'pi mahattattvādapi pradhāne eva avayavārtha- prakarṣāt, mahata ḥ 'ātma' śabda viśeṣyatvamayuktam ityarthaḥ | rūpakavinyastānāmeva grahaṇam ityetadbhayaṃ etaduktaṃ bhavati iti pūrveṇānvitam | 'iti' śabdāprayogaḥ ativiprakarṣāt | darśayati ceti | sūtrakhaṇḍopādānam | tadeva vaśāt paratvameva | | vaśīkāryatveṃ |

1 prasiddhasya – vā. mahattatvābhyupagamātvasya – i, vā

jñānendriyāṇi ca manasi niyacchet ityarthaḥ | 'vāk' śabde dvitīyāyāḥ,

'supāṃ su-luk'
iti luka | 'manasī' iti saptamyāḥ chāndaso dīrghaḥ |
'tadyacchet jñāna ātmani' | tat – manaḥ, buddhau niyacchet | 'jñāna' śabdenātra, pūrvoktā buddhiḥ abhidhīyate | 'jñāne' 'ātmani' iti, vyadhikaraṇe saptamyau | ātmani vartamāne jñāne niyacchet ityarthaḥ |

|

|

'jñānamātmani mahati niyacchet | buddhiṃ kartari mahati ātmani, niyacchet |
' tadyacchet śānta ātmani | taṃ kartāraṃ parasmin brahmaṇi sarvāntaryāmiṇi, niyacchet | vyatyayena 'tat' iti napuṃsakaliṅgatā | evambhūtena rathinā vaiṣṇavaṃ padaṃ gantavyam ityarthaḥ || 1 ||
'avyakta' śabdena kathaṃ vyaktasya śarīrasyā'bhidhānam ? tatrāha -
yacchediti | yacchet vāk manasī iti padavibhāgaḥ ] vākpūrvakāṇīti | vākśabdaḥ sarvendriyopalakṣaṇārthaḥ | manasī iti nirdeśa:, 'īdūtau ca saptamyarthe' iti prakṛtibhāvasmaraṇanibandhanaḥ |

jñānaśabdeneti ahaṅkāraparatvamasvarasam, buddhiparatvaṃ tu svarasam ityabhi.-- prāyaḥ | pūrvoktā buddhiḥ - anātmātmaviṣaya heyopādeyatādhyavasāyaḥ | jñānasāmānyavāciśabdasya jñānaviśeṣavācitvaṃ mukhyamiti bhāvaḥ | vyadhikaraṇe saptamyau iti | 'ātma' śabdasya svataḥ cetanaikāntatvāt, anantaravākyasthātmaśabdasya asya ca ekarūpārthatve svārasyācca, sāmānādhikaraṇya- mātrasvārasyam upekṣyam iti bhāvaḥ | saptamyarthasya sphuṭīkaraṇāya vartamānapadam adhyāhṛtam |
buddhimiti | vāco manasi niyamanam – mano'nanuguṇapravṛttivai mukhya pādanam | manaso buddhau niyamanam — adhyavasāyānuguṇapravṛttitāpādanam | buddhi: - artheṣu heyatādhyavasāyarūpā | tasyāḥ buddheḥ ātmani niyamanaṃ nāma 'sa eva upādeyatayā sākṣātkāryaḥ' ityetadarthaviṣayatvāpādanam| śānte- – svataḥ ūrmipaṭka pratibhaṭe | śānte ātmani mahataḥ ātmanaḥ jīvasya niyamanaṃ nāma taccheṣatāpratipattiḥ | saiva parasyā'pi vaśīkaraṇaṃ bhavati | vyatyayeneti | 'suptiṅapagraha- liṅganarāṇāṃ kālahalacsvara kartṛyaṅāṃ ca | vyatyayamicchati śāstrakṛdeṣāṃ so'pi ca sidhyati bāhulakena ||' iti śābdikairuktaliṅgavyatyayena taditi napuṃsakaliṅgatetyarthaḥ ||1||

avyakta .... tatrāheti | pāriśeṣyāt 'śarīrāvyakta' śabdayoḥ aikāyai avaśyaṃ bhāvini sati, upakramādhikaraṇanyāyena upakramastha 'śarīra' śabdānuguṇam, 'avyakta' śabde neye, tatra 'avyakta śabdasya vṛttiḥ katham ? ityapekṣāyāmāha ityabhiprāya: ||

111. sūkṣmaṃ tu tadarhatvāt || 1-4-2 ||

bhūtasūkṣmam avyākṛtaṃ hi ! avasthāviśeṣamāpannaṃ śarīraṃ bhavati | tat avyākṛtam, iha śarīrāvastham 'avyakta' śabdenocyate | tadarhatvāt – tasya avyākṛtasya acidvastuna eva 'vikārā'pannasya, rathavat puruṣārthasādhanapravṛttyarhatvāt ||

112. tadadhīnatvādarthavat || 1-4-3 ||

113 jñeyatvāvacanācca ||1-4-4||
yadi, tantrasiddham iha avyaktamavivakṣiṣyat ! tadā asya jñeyatvam 'avakṣyat ' ;

115 trayāṇāmeva caivamupanyāsaḥ praśnaśca || 1- 4-6 || asmin prakaraṇe hi ! upāyopeyopetaṇāṃ trayāṇāmeva ca evamupanyāsaḥ' jñeyatvenopanyāsaḥ, tadviṣayaśca praśnaḥ dṛśyate ; na anyasya avyaktādeḥ |

tathā hi- naciketāḥ mumukṣussan, mṛtyupratte varatraye, prathamena vareṇa, ātmanaḥ puruṣārthayogyatāpādinīm ātmani pituḥ sumanaskatāṃ pratilabhya ; dvitīyena vareṇa, 'mokṣopāyabhūtāṃ nāciketāgnividyāṃ va-
'satvamani svayamadhyeṣi mṛtyo prabrūhi taṃ śraddhadhānāya mahyam |
svargalokā amṛtatvaṃ bhajante eta dvitīyena vṛṇe vareṇa ||' iti | 'svarga' śabdenātra paramapuruṣārthalakṣaṇo mokṣaḥ abhidhīyate ; 'amṛtatvaṃ bhajante ' prakṛtatvaṃ darśitam | mahacchabdena ātmanirdeśaḥ, indriyādiparikaravatvalakṣaṇaprādhānyavivakṣāti- śayāt || 5 ||
trayāṇāmiti | prathamaḥ 'ca'śabdaḥ hetusamuccaye | dvitīyaḥ 'ca'śabdaḥ praśnopanyāsayoḥ samuccaye | praśnasya paścādukti: ; praśnavākyaṃ hi ! muktamātraviṣayamiti, kathamatra trayāṇāmupanyāsaḥ ? iti śaṅkāyāṃ prativacanaprakāreṇa praśnārthasya vijñeyatvāt, praśnavākye trayamapi pṛṣṭaṃ bhavati ityabhiprāyeṇa | jñeyatveneti | 'jñeyatvāvacanācca' iti pūrvasūtrasthajñeyatvam ' evaṃ 'śabdena parāmṛśyate ityarthaḥ |

mokṣopāyabhūtāmiti | nāciketāgnividyāyāḥ mokṣopāyatvam aṅgidvārā bhavati | tacca tasyāḥ paramātmātmaka svātmānusandhānagarbhatvāt | mokṣaḥ abhidhīyate iti | mokṣaśabdaḥ, mokṣasthānapara: ; 'loka' śabdasāmānādhikaraṇyāt, bhāṣye tatrasthasya iti vakṣyamāṇatvācca | paramapuruṣārthalakṣaṇaśabdaḥ, 'svarga' śabdasya mokṣavā citvopapādakaḥ | svargaśabdasya

iti, tatrasthasya 'janana' - maraṇābhāvaśravaṇāt, uttaratra kṣayiphalakarmanindādarśanācca ; “triṇāciketa ṃstribhiretya sandhi trikarmakṛttarati janmamṛtyū '

iti ca prativacanāt |

tṛtīyena vareṇa, mokṣasvarūpapraśnadvāreṇa upeyasvarūpam, upetṛsvarūpam, upāya- 'bhūta' karmānugṛhītopāsana svarūpaṃ ca pṛṣṭam -
'yeyaṃ prete vicikitsā manuṣye astītyeke nāyamastīti caike | etadvidyāmanuśiṣṭastvayā'haṃ varāṇāmeṣa varastṛtīyaḥ || ' iti | evaṃ mokṣe pṛṣṭe, tadupadeśayogyatā parīkṣya, upadideśa-
'taṃ durdarśa gūḍhamanupraviṣṭaṃ guhāhita gahvareṣṭhaṃ purāṇam |
adhyātmayogādhigamena devaṃ matvā dhīro 'harṣaśokau jahāti ||' iti | tadevaṃ sāmānyenopadiṣṭe, naciketāḥ prītaḥ san, 'devaṃ matvā' iti upāsyatayā nirdiṣṭasya prāpyabhūtasya devasya, 'adhyātmayogādhigamena' iti veditavyatayā nirdiṣṭasya prāptuḥ pratyagātmanaśca, 'matvā dhīro harṣa - śokau jahāti' iti nirdiṣṭasya 'brahmopāsanasya * ca, svarūpa 'viśodha' nāya punaḥ papraccha -
prakṛṣṭasukhopalakṣaṇatvāt, mokṣasya niravadhikānandarūpatvācca 'svarga' śabdavācyatvam iti bhāvaḥ | tatrasthasyeti | svargalokā iti hi bahuvrīhiḥ | niratiśaya sukharūpaparamavyomasthāḥ iti bhāvaḥ | kṣayiphalakarmanindeti | ' śvo'bhāvā martyasya .... jarayanti teja:' ityādinetyarthaḥ | triṇāciketaḥ – 'ayaṃ vāva yaḥ pavate' ityādyanuvākatrayādhyāyī | trikarmakṛt – yajanādhyayanadānakṛt, pākayajña - haviryajña-somayajñakṛdvā | tribhiḥ – agnibhiḥ tribhiḥ hetubhiḥ | sandhim — paramātmopāsanena sambandham, prāpya, janma-mṛtyū taratītyarthaḥ ; 'karoti tadyena punarna jāyate' ityanenaikārthyāt |

prete—parityaktacarmaśarīre ityarthaḥ ; 'na pretya saṃjñā'sti' iti prayogāt | durdarśam - draṣṭumaśakyam | gūḍham guṇatraya tirohitatvāt (iti hetugarbham ) | anupraviṣṭam – śarīrānu- praviṣṭam | tatrāpi guhāhitam - hṛdayaguhāgatam | gahvareṣṭham - tadantargate jīvāntaḥ sthitamityarthaḥ | jahātīti, upadideśa ityanvayaḥ ; 'yeyaṃ prete' jīvapraśnamātraparatve, paramātmaviṣayatayā prativacanavaighaṭyaṃ syāt ityabhiprāyaḥ | veditavyatayeti | 'adhyātmayoga' śabdaḥ prāptṛsvarūpaviṣayayogaparaḥ ityabhiprāyaḥ |

4) jñeyatvāvacanācca

yathā atharvaṇikāḥ adhīyate-
'vikārajananīmajñāmaṣṭarūpāmajāṃ dhuvām dhyāyate'dhyāsitā tena tanyate preryate punaḥ || sūyate puruṣārthaṃ ca tenaivādhiṣṭhitā jagat | gaura nādyantavatī sā janitrī bhūtabhāvinī | sitā'sitā ca raktā ca sarvakāmadughā vibhoḥ | pibantyenāmaviṣamāmavijñātāḥ kumārakāḥ || ekastu pivate devaḥ svacchando'tra vaśānugām |
'dhyāna' kriyābhyāṃ bhagavān bhuṅkte'sau prasabhaṃ vibhuḥ ||
sarvasādhāraṇīṃ dogdhrīṃ pīḍyamānāṃ tu yajvabhiḥ | 'caturviṃśatisaṅkhyātamavyaktaṃ vyaktamucyate'
iti | atra prakṛtyādīnāṃ svarūpamabhihitam | yadātmakāśca ete prakṛtyādayaḥ saḥ parama puruṣo'pi,

aṣṭarūpatvam, 'bhūmirāpaḥ' ityādyuktaprakāreṇa | ājam — utpattirahitām | dhruvām - nāśa- rahitām | dhyāyate— paramātmanā saṅkalparūpeṇa jñānena jñāyate | adhyāsitā adhiṣṭhitā | tanyate - vistāryate, sthūlāvasthaṃ kriyate ; samaṣṭibhūtarūpeṇa kriyate | preryate vyaṣṭisṛṣṭyarthaṃ preryate | puruṣārtha – puruṣāṇāmarthanīyaṃ bhogya-bhogopakaraṇa - bhogasthānarūpaṃ jagat, sūyate | janitrī bhūta- bhāvinī iti, samaṣṭi vyaṣṭisṛṣṭirucyate | sitā'sitā ca raktā ca gauṇyā vṛtyā santvarajastamomayī | vibhoḥ – īśvarasya | sarvakāmadughā - līlārasadogdhrī | aviṣamām- bhokta karmānuguṇapariṇāmitvāt apakṣapātinīm | avijñātāḥ – dehebhyo vivicya aparijñātāḥ | ekastu iti | karmavaśyabahubhoktṛvyāvṛtyartham 'ekaḥ, svacchanda:' ityuktam | dhyānakriyābhyām - | saṅkalpasargādivyāpārābhyām aikṣata, asṛjata' iti dvayamapi hi śrūyate | pīḍyamānāṃ tu yajvabhiriti | vatsā hi ! mātaraṃ kṣīrakṣaraṇārtha pīḍayitvā, bhuñjate ; tadvat karmavaśyaiḥ ātmabhiḥ bhoktatryaphalānurūpaṃ pariṇamyamānām ityarthaḥ | prakṛtyādīnām – prakṛtipuruṣamahadā-

'taṃ paḍviṃśaka mityāhuḥ saptaviṃśamathāpare |
puruṣaṃ nirguṇa sājhakhyamatharvaśiraso viduḥ ||'

iti pratipādyate ||

apare cātharvaṇikāḥ-

'aṣṭau prakṛtayaḥ ṣoḍaśa vikārā: '

ityabhidhīyate |

śvetāśvatarāśca, evaṃ prakṛtipuruṣeśvarasvarūpamāmananti —

'saṃyuktametat kṣaramakṣaraṃ ca vyaktāvyaktaṃ bharate viśvamīśaḥ |

30. saṅkhyopasaṅgrahādhikaraṇam ||1-4-3 ||
120. na saṅkhyopasaṅgrahādapi nānābhāvādatirekācca ||1-4-11||

vājasaneyinaḥ samāmananti-

' yasmin pañca pañcajanā ākāśaśca pratiṣṭhitaḥ |
tamevamanya ātmānaṃ vidvān brahmāmṛto'mṛtam ||'

iti ||

kim ayaṃ mantraḥ, kāpilatantra siddhataccapratipādanaparaḥ ? uta na ? – iti sandite ||

na saṅkhyeti | 'ajā' śabdasya svatantraprakṛtyupasthāpakatve viśeṣābhāvāt vākyānta- rānurodhena prakṛteḥ brahmātmakatvaṃ sādhitam | tadatrā'nupapannam ; paramātmaparatve saṅkhyādhikyāt, paramātmanā vinā'pi saṅkhyā pūraṇena, 'abrahmātmikā prakṛtiḥ' iti viśeṣaniścayasambhavāt iti śaṅkayā saṅgatiḥ |
vājeti | pañcaviṃśatisaṅkhyānvaya-tattva | dhikyādipratītijanakamidaṃ vākyam | evaṃ- jātīyakavākyāni santi cet, tānyapi viṣayaḥ ||
sandihyate iti | pañcaviṃśatisaṅkhyānvaya-tattvādhikyapratītibhyāṃ saṃśayaḥ | yasmin pañca pañcajanā:' iti mantraḥ, kiṃ kāpila tantrasiddha tatva para ? una na ? iti | kiṃ 'pañca

kiṃ yuktam 1

tantrasiddha tattvapratipādanaparaḥ - iti |

kuta: ?

'pañca' śabdaviśeṣitāt 'pañcajana' śabdāt pañcaviṃśatitattvapratīteḥ |
etaduktaṃ bhavati – 'pañcajanā :' iti samāsaḥ samāhāraviṣayaḥ ; 'pañcānāṃ janānāṃ samūhāḥ pañcajanāḥ ; pañcapūlyaḥ itivat | 'pañcajanā:' iti liṅgavyatyayaḥ chāndasaḥ | te ca samūhāḥ kati ? ityapekṣāyāṃ pañcajana' śabda viśeṣaṇena prathamena 'paśca' śabdena, 'samūhāḥ pañca' iti 'pratīyante' ; yatha 'pañca pañcapūlyaḥ' iti | ataḥ 'pañca pañcajanāḥ ' iti pañcaviṃśatipadārthāvagatau, te katame 1 ityapekṣāyāṃ, mokṣādhikārāt mumukṣubhiḥ jñāta- vyatayā smṛtiprasiddhāḥ prakṛtyādaya eva jñāyante |

pañcajana'śabdena pañcaviṃśatitaccapratītirasti ? na ? iti | kim ākāśasya pṛthanirdeśaḥ, avāntarasaṅkhyānvayanimittavirodhī ? uta na ? iti | kiṃ vākyaśeṣeṇa 'pañcajana' śabdasya prāṇādiviṣayatvaniścayaḥ śakyaḥ ? uta na ? iti ca |
pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ - yadā na virodhī, yadā ca niścayo na śakya: ; tadā, avāntarasaṅkhyānvaya- nimittasambhavena, samāsasya samāhāraviṣayatayā, 'pañca pañcajana' śabdena pañca pañcakānyucyanta iti, pañcaviṃśatitattvapratīteḥ, ayaṃ mantraḥ tantrasiddhatattvapratipādanaparaḥ iti |

rāddhāntaḥ—yadā ākāśasya pṛthaṅanirdeśaḥ avāntarasaṅkhyānvayanimittavirodhī, vākya- śeṣeṇa ca prāṇādiviṣayatvaniścayaḥ śakya: ; tadā avāntarasaṅkhyānvayanimittābhāvena, samāsasya saṃjñāviṣayatayā, 'pañca pañcajana' śabdena pañcapadārthā ucyanta iti, pañcaviṃśatitatvā- pratīteḥ, ayaṃ mantraḥ na tantrasiddha tattvapratipādanaparaḥ iti |
pañcaśabdeti | asya 'pañcajana' śabdasya saṅkhyāpratītisvārasyānurodhe sambhavati, tatpari-- tyāgāyogāt saṅkhyā pūrvasamāsatvaṃ yuktamityabhiprāyaḥ |
chāndasa iti | tribhuvanādiśabdavat strīliṅgasmṛteḥ apavādābhāvāt chāndasatvamiti bhāvaḥ | bhūtatvajñānendriyatvādikam avāntarasaṅkhyā niveśanimittamastītyabhipretam | taddhi sphuṭī bhaviṣyati ||

'mūlaprakṛtira vikṛtirmahadādyāḥ prakṛtivikṛtayaḥ sapta |

poḍaśakastu vikāro na prakṛtiḥ na vikṛtiḥ puruṣaḥ || ' iti hi ! kāpilānāṃ prasiddhiḥ | ataḥ tantrasiddhatacca pratipādanaparaḥ,

'na saṅkhyopasaṅgrahādapi, iti 'api' śabdasya, 'pañca pañcajanā:' ityatra pañca- viṃśatitacca' pratipattireva na sambhavati - ityabhiprāyaḥ |

katham ?

pañcabhirārabdhasamūhapañcakāsambhavāt | na hi ! tantrasiddha tattveṣu pañcasu pañcasu, anugataṃ tatsaṅkhyāniveśanimittaṃ jātyādi asti |
na ca vācyam – pañca karmendriyāṇi pañca jñānendriyāṇi pañca mahābhūtāni, pañcatanmātrāṇi, avaśiṣṭāni pañca' iti avāntarasaṅkhyāniveśanimittam astyeva- iti; ākāśasya pṛthaṅginirdeśena, pañcabhirārabdhamahābhūtasamūhāsiddheḥ ||

ataḥ 'pañcañjanāḥ' ityayaṃ samāsaḥ na samāhāraviṣayaḥ ; 'ayaṃ tu' 'dik- saṅkhye saṃjñāyām '

iti saṃjñāviṣayaḥ " | anyathā 'pañcajanāḥ' iti liṅgavyatyayaśca | pañcañjanāḥ nāma kecit santi ; te ca pañcasaṅkhyayā viśeṣyante ; 'pañca pañcajanāḥ' iti | 'sapta saptarṣayaḥ' itivat || 11 ||
antarbhāvāt, saptaviṃśatvam; kālasya svatantra vastutvābhiprāyeṇetyarthaḥ | śrutiprasiddheti | sāṅkhya prasiddhādapi śrutiprasiddhaṃ grāhyam; antaraṅgatvāt iti bhāvaḥ |
pañcabhirityādi | anena pañcasvanugatopādhyasambhavāt ityuktaṃ bhavati | ekopādhi- gṛhītatvaṃ hi samūhatvam |

na ca .... -samūhāsiddheriti | ākāśasya pṛthakkaraṇāt ghrāṇapañcameṣu, vāyvādiṣu, manaḥ pañcameṣu, rasanādiṣu, guṇatraya mahadahaṅkāreṣu ca anugatopādhyabhāvādityarthaḥ |
anyatheti | liṅgavyatyayaśca kliṣṭaḥ ityarthaḥ | sapteti |
iti śrīmadvaiṣṇavapurāṇaprayogo'bhipretaḥ | ayamatrābhisandhiḥ

'sapta saptarṣayo'malā : ' saṃjñipada pratipannasaṅkhyāyāḥ vyutpatti-

samaye taṭasthatvenāvagatāyāḥ tātparyāviṣayatvāt punarvākyārthānvayitvajñāpanāya, 'pañca' śabdena saṅkhyāyāḥ viśeṣaṇatvajñāpanaṃ yuktam | padadvayasya sāmarthyaṃ cedameva ; yat vigrahavākye arthābhi- dhānaśaktiḥ | ataḥ samāsa upapannaḥ | ataḥ arthasya avivakṣitatvāt punastayā saṅkhyayā viśeṣaṇa saphalamiti || 11 ||

121. prāṇādayo vākyaśeṣāt || 1-4 -12 ||

'prāṇasya prāṇamuta cakṣuṣa' cakṣuḥ śrotrasya śrotramannasyānna '
manaso 'ye mano' viduḥ'

iti vākyaśeṣāt brahmāśrayāḥ prāṇādaya eva pañca pañcajanāḥ iti vijñāyante || 12 ||
atha syāt kāṇvānāṃ mādhyandinānāṃ ca,
' yasmin pañca pañcajanāḥ'

ityayaṃ mantraḥ samānaḥ |
'prāṇasya prāṇam'

ke punaste iti | saṅkhyā pūrvasamāsatvamayuktam ; asmin vākye sakhyeyaviśeṣāprati- patteḥ iti ||
prāṇādaya iti | prāṇādiṣu .... indriyatva rūpai ko pā ghisiddhayupapādanena vākyaśeṣārthanirṇayāya sūtrapraṇayanaṃ prayojanavat | sandehavyudāsakaścātra agryaprāyanyāya : abhipretaḥ | prāṇānnaśabdau dvau | cakṣuḥ śrotramanaḥ- śabdāḥ trayaḥ | ataḥ tadanuguṇatayā prāṇānnaśabdayorapi indriyaparatvāt prāṇādayaḥ padārthāḥ indriyāṇīti ta eva pañcajanāḥ ityarthaḥ || 12||

ityādivākyaśeṣe, kāṇvānām annasya pāṭho na vidyate | teṣāṃ 'pañca pañcajanāḥ prāṇā- dayaḥ, iti na śakyaṃ' vaktum - iti, atrottaram -
122. jyotiṣaikeṣāmasatyanne || 1-4 -13 ||
ekeṣām - kāṇvānāṃ pāṭhe, asati anne jyotiṣā, pañcajanāḥ indriyāṇi iti jñāyante | teṣāṃ vākyaśeṣaḥ pradarśanārthaḥ |
etaduktaṃ bhavati yasmin 'pañca pañcajanā:' ityasmāt pūrvasmin mantre,

'taṃ devā jyotiṣāṃ 'jyotiḥ āyupāsate'mṛtam”
iti, jyotiṣāṃ jyotiṭvena 'brahmaṇyabhidhīyamāne, brahmādhīna' svakāryāṇi kānicit jyotīṃṣi pratipannāni | tāni ca viṣayāṇāṃ prakāśakāni indriyāṇi iti, ' yasmin pañca pañca- janā:' iti anirdhāritaviśeṣanirdeśena avagamyante – iti |

na śakyaṃ vaktamiti | kāṇvānām annapāṭhavaikalyena, prāṇādiṣu pañcasaṅkhyā niveśā- nupapatteḥ, pañcajanatvamayuktamiti bhāvaḥ ||
jyotiṣā - ṣaṣṭhayantajyotiśśabdena, ityarthaḥ | sūtre'pi jyotiśśabdaḥ śabdasvarūpārthaḥ | śrutivākyasvārasya siddhayaupayikatvāt sūtrastha 'jyoti: ' śabdāsvārasyaṃ soḍhavyamityarthaḥ |
brahmā... kāryāṇi ityanena, 'prāṇasya prāṇaḥ' ityāderartha uktaḥ | 'pavitrāṇāṃ pavitram ' itivat iti bhāvaḥ | jyotīṃṣi ityanena prakāśakatvamabhipretam | tataśca karmendriyādi- vyāvṛttisiddhiḥ ; teṣāṃ prakāśakatvābhāvāt | kāniciditi | ādityādijyotīṃṣi ? na vā ? iti saṃśaye viśeṣanirdhāraṇākāṅkṣā tiṣṭhatīti bhāvaḥ | anirdhāriteti saṅkhyeya viśeṣo - pasthāpakavākyāntarasākāṅkṣatvaṃ dyotitam | tataśca tatra śrutapañcasaṅkhyāviśeṣitatvāt pañcasaṅkhyā- -kajyotirantaraprasiddhyabhāvācca, pariśeṣeṇa indriyatvāvagamaḥ |

keṣāṃ cidannasyetyāderasadbhāve'pi śākhinām |
jyotiṣāmiti śabdena bodhyante hīndriyāṇi tu ||

'prāṇasya' iti prāṇaśabdena sparśanendriyaṃ gṛhyate ; vāyusambandhitvāt 'sparśa- nendriyasya | mukhyaprāṇasya 'jyotiḥ ' śabdena pradarśanāyogāt | 'cakṣuṣaḥ' iti, cakṣu- rindriyam | 'śrotrasya' iti śrotrendriyam | 'annasya' iti ghrāṇa-rasanayoḥ tantreṇopā-

dānam | 'anna' śabdoditapṛthivī sambandhitvāt anenetyannam' iti, rasanendriyamapi gṛhyate | 'manasaḥ' iti manaḥ | ghrāṇa- rasanayoḥ 'tantreṇopādānamiti, pañcatvamapi aviruddham | prakāśakāni manaḥparyantāni indriyāṇi, 'pañcajana' śabdanirdiṣṭāni | tadavirodhāya ghrāṇa- rasanayoḥ tantreṇopādānam | tadevam,
' yasmin pañca pañcajanāḥ ākāśaśca pratiṣṭhitaḥ'
iti 'pañcajana' śabda nirdiṣṭāni indriyāṇi, 'ākāśa' śabda pradarśitāni mahābhūtāni ca brahmaṇi pratiṣṭhitānīti, sarvatattvānāṃ brahmāśrayatvapratipādanāt na tantra siddhapaścaviṃśati- tattvaprasaṅgaḥ |

ghrāṇendriyamanena' gṛhyate | 'adyate

ataḥ sarvatra vedānte saṅkhyopasaṅgrahe tadabhāve vā, na kāpilatantrasiddha tattva - pratīti' riti ' ' siddham' || 13 ||

31 kāraṇatvādhikaraṇam | 1-4-4 || ( sū - 14,15)
123. kāraṇatvena cākāśādiṣu yathāvyapadiṣṭokteḥ || 1-4 -14 || punaḥ pradhānakāraṇavādī pratyavatiṣṭhate - na vedānteṣu ekasmāt sṛṣṭiramnāyate iti, jagataḥ brahmaikakāraṇatvaṃ na yujyate vaktum' | tathā hi-

evaṃ saṅkhyābhyupagamāt tadanabhyupagamācca dvidhā parihṛtam | saṅkhyopasaṅgrahe—–saṅkhyā - sambhave | tadabhāve sakhyāyā asambhave || 13 ||

|| iti saṅkhyopasaṅgrahā dhikaraṇam || 3 ||

kāṇaratveneti | 'yasmin' iti sarvatattvānāṃ brahmāśrayatvapratītyā, svātantryeṇa pradhānasya kāraṇatvaṃ nirastam | atha – svātantryeṇa kāraṇatvaṃ pratīyate ; brahmāśrayatvāpratīteḥ iti śaṅkayā saṅgatiḥ |

punarityādi | avarjanīyā cidbodhavyākulārthaśabdo palakṣitakāraṇavākya samudāyo viṣayaḥ | vyākulatvādeva saṃśayaḥ—kiṃ jagatkāraṇa viṣaya vedāntavākyapratipādyam, avyaktam ? uta brahma ? iti | kim 'asadvā idamagra āsīt ' 'tadbhedaṃ tatryākṛtamāsīt' ityatra 'asadavyākṛta' śabda,

'sadeva somyedamagra āsīt '
iti, satpūrvikā sṛṣṭirānnāyate |
'asadvā idamagra āsīt '
iti, asatpūrvikā ca | anyatra,

'asadevedamagra āsīt | tat sadāsīt | tat samabhavat' iti ca | ataḥ vedānteṣu sraṣṭuḥ avyavasthiteḥ, jagataḥ brahmaikakāraṇatvaṃ na niścetuṃ śakyam ; pratyuta pradhānakāraṇatvameva niśretuṃ śakyate |

'taddhedaṃ tarhyavyākṛtamāsīt'
iti, avyākṛte pradhāne jagataḥ pralayamabhidhāya,
'tannāmarūpābhyāmeva vyākriyata'
iti, avyākṛtādeva jagataḥ sṛṣṭiśva, abhidhīyate | avyākṛtaṃ hi ! avyaktam ; nāma- rūpābhyāṃ na vyākriyate na vyajyate ityarthaḥ | avyaktaṃ – pradhānameva | asya ca svarūpanityatvena pariṇāmāśrayatvena ca jagatkāraṇa 'vādivākyagatau' sadasacchandau, brahmaṇīva asmin na virotsyete ||
avyākṛtanāmarūpāvasthabrahmaparau ? uta avyaktaparau ? iti | tadarthaṃ kiṃ tadapyeṣa śloko bhavati' iti sākṣitvena nirdeśa : asacchabdasya prakṛtabrahmaparatāmavagamayatiḥ na ? iti | sa eṣa iha praviṣṭa:' iti praśāsituḥ anukarṣaśca, 'avyākṛta' śabdasya brahmaparatāmavagamayati ? na ? iti saṃśayaḥ arthasiddhaḥ |
– yadā, ' sa eṣa:' ityanukarṣaḥ, na avyākṛtaśarīrabrahmaparatām avagamayati, yadā 'ca 'tadapyeṣaḥ' iti nirdeśaḥ, prakṛtabrahmaparatām asacchabdasya nāvagamayati ; tadānīm asada- vyākṛtaśabdayoḥ, avyākṛtanāmarūpāvasthabrahmaparatvābhāvāt kāraṇavākyapratipādyam avyaktamiti prāpnoti |
yadā 'tadapyeṣaḥ' iti nirdeśa:, asacchabdasya brahmaparatāmavagamayati, yadā ca' sa eṣa:' ityanukarṣaḥ, 'avyākṛta' śabdasya avyākṛtaśarīrakabrahmaparatāmavagamayati ; tadānīm, 'asadavyākṛta' śabdayoḥ, avyākṛtanāma-rūpāvasthabrahmaparatvāt jagatkāraṇavākyapratipādyaṃ brahma___ iti siddhyati |
ekasmāt — ekarūpāt kāraṇāt | sraṣṭuriti | brahmakāraṇavāde sṛṣṭikāraṇasya avyavasthiterityarthaḥ | brahmaṇīva iti, vaidharmyadṛṣṭāntaḥ |

evam avyākṛṃtakāraṇatve niścite sati, īkṣaṇādayaḥ kāraṇagatāḥ, 'sṛṣṭayau - nmukhyābhiprāyeṇa yojayitavyāḥ | ' brahmātma' śabdāvapi bṛhacca vyāpitvābhyāṃ, pradhāne eva vartate | ataḥ smṛtinyāya prasiddhaṃ pradhānameva jagatkāraṇaṃ vedāntavākyaiḥ pratipādyate :

124 samākarṣāt || 1-4-15||

itiṃ, 'ādi' śabdena, muṇḍakopaniṣadādi vivakṣitam | satyaṃ jñānamiti | kāraṇatvapratipādanāt prāgeva, kāraṇatvadharmiṇaḥ prathamata eva cetanatvapratipādanaṃ darśitam | prakṛtaṃ vipaśvideveti | 'sambhūta:' iti sṛṣṭipratipādanātpūrvameva prayuktaḥ svataścetanaikāntaḥ 'ātma'śabdaḥ prabalataraḥ ; tataḥ pūrvameva tasya kāraṇasya vipaścittvapratipādanāt 'ātmaśabdasya kliṣṭanirvāhaśaṅkā dūrotsāritā' ityabhiprāyaḥ | tatheti | ' tattejo'sṛjata' iti kāraṇatvapratipādanāt pūrvameva, 'tadaikṣata' iti īkṣitṛtvapratipādanāt īkṣaṇānuguṇaṃ kāraṇatvaṃ neyam ; na tu kāraṇatvānuguṇamīkṣitṛtvaṃ neyam ityarthaḥ | draṣṭavyamiti | 'eka evāgra āsīt | sa īkṣata' ityādiṣu sṛṣṭayukteḥ pūrvameva paramacetana-tadasādhāraṇekṣaṇādikamavagantavyamityarthaḥ | upakramasthabahuśrutivirodhāt avyākṛtaśruti- mātradaurbalyamarthasiddham || 14 ||

samākṛṣya bravītīti brahmavā'tra hi kāraṇam ||

'ya eteṣāṃ kartā, yasya vaitat karma' iti pṛthanirdeśasya ca, ayamabhiprāyaḥ— ye tvayā brahmatvena nirdiṣṭāḥ puruṣāḥ, teṣāṃ yaḥ kartā - te yatkāryabhūtāḥ, kiṃ viśiṣyābhi- dhīyate ? kṛtsnaṃ jagat yasya kāryam - utkṛṣṭāḥ, apakṛṣṭāḥ, cetanā acetanātha' sarve padārthāḥ yatkāryatve tulyāḥ saḥ paramakāraṇabhūtaḥ puruṣottamo veditavyaḥ - iti ||

'etasmādātmanaḥ prāṇāḥ yathāyatanaṃ vipratiṣṭhante ; prāṇebhyo devāḥ,

iti, jīvādarthāntara' bhūtaparamātma parameva ' ||

'suṣuptasya' hi jīvasya yatroṣitasya, jāgaritasvamadaśa | sambandhivicitrasukha- duḥkhānubhavakāluṣya viraheṇa 'samprasannasya' 'svasthatā 'pattiḥ punarapi asya yasmādbhogāya niṣkramaṇam ; so'yaṃ paramātmā | tathā hi,
'satā somya ! tadā sampanno bhavati'
'prājñenātmanā sampariṣvakto na bāhya kiścana veda, nā'ntaram' iti suṣutyādhāratayā prasiddhaḥ, jīvādarthāntarabhūtaḥ prājñaḥ paramātmā | ataḥ - praśnaprati- vacanābhyāṃ jīvasaṅkīrtanam jīvādarthāntarabhūtaparamātmapratipādanārtham - iti niścīyate ||
yaduktam - praśna vyākhyāne jīvapare ; suṣuptisthānaṃ ca nāḍya eva | karaṇagrāmaśca, 'prāṇa' śabda nirdiṣṭe jīve eva ekadhā bhavati - iti |
tadayuktam ; nāḍīnāṃ svamasthānatvāt | ukta ' rītyā brahmaṇa eva suṣupti- sthānatvāt, 'prāṇa' śabdanirddiṣṭe brahmaṇyeva, jīvasya tadupakaraṇabhūtavāgādikaraṇagrāmasya ca, ekatāpattivibhāga' vacanācca ||
prājñeneti, suṣuptiviṣayaṃ śrutyantaraṃ sadvidya vākyasya paramātmaparatvavaiśadyāya | ata iti | anena nirvāha vaiṣamya pradarśanena atideśasūtrasya adhikāśaṅkā parihārau darśitau bhavataḥ | pratardana- vidyāyām, upakrame jīvaliṅgāvagamaḥ ; mahāvākyasvārasyaṃ tu paramātmaparatve | iha tu — upakramopa- -saṃhāramadhyeṣu jīvaliṅgāvagamāt ata eva paramātmanaḥ cidviśiṣṭatā paratvābhāvena, upāsā- traividhyāpratipatteḥ, prātardananyāyānavakāśaḥ iti adhikāśaṅkā | trividhopāsanābhāve'pi jīvāti- riktabrahmabodhanarūpaprayojanāntarasambhavāt astyavakāśaḥ iti adhikaḥ parihāra:, iti hi ! avagamyate ||

nāḍīnāmityādi | 'tāsu tadā bhavati' ityatra 'tadā' śabda : svapnāvasthāparaḥ | 'yadā 'suptaḥ...... ekadhā bhavati' ityanantaravākye 'atha 'śabdena 'tadā' śabdanirdiṣṭāvasthāyāḥ, suṣupteśca avasthāntaratvāvagamāt ; uttaravākye svapnapratiṣedhena, pūrvavākyasya svapnaprasañjakatayā, tadviṣayatvā- cagamācceti bhāvaḥ | uktarītyeti | udāhṛtaśrutyantare kāya lakṣaṇayā ityarthaḥ ||

'api caivameke' | vājasaneyinaḥ, asminneva bālākyajātaśatrusaṃvāde, supuptāt vijñānamayāt bhedena tadāśrayabhūtaṃ paramātmānamāmananti
'ya eṣa vijñānamayaḥ puruṣaḥ, kaipa tadā'bhūt ? kuta etadāgāt ? " yatraiṣa etatsupto'bhūt ya eṣa vijñānamayaḥ puruṣaḥ, tadaiteṣāṃ prāṇānāṃ vijñānena vijñānamādāya, ya eṣo'ntarhṛdaya ākāśaḥ, tasmiñcchete'

iti' | 'ākāśa' śabdaśca paramātmani prasiddhaḥ ;
'daharo'sminnantara ākāśaḥ

iti | ataḥ atra jīvasaṅkīrtanaṃ, tasmādarthāntarabhūtasya prājñasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ pratibodha- | - nārtham – ityavagamyate ||

tasmāt - asmin vākye puruṣādarthāntarabhūtasya nikhila jagatkāraṇasya parasyaiva brahmaṇaḥ veditavyatayā abhidhānāt na 'tantrasiddhasya puruṣasya tadadhiṣṭhitasya vā pradhānasya kāraṇatvaṃ kvacidapi vedānte pratīyate - iti siddham || 18 ||

|| iti jagadbācitvādhikaraṇam || 5 ||

vaiṣa tadābhūt ? – suptaḥ ? ityarthaḥ | prāṇānāṃ mādāya - 'prāṇa' śabda- vācyendriyajanyaṃ jñānaṃ manasā sahādāya, ityarthaḥ | uparatavyāpāraṃ manaḥ | anena indriyāṇāmapi vyāpāroparatiḥ phalitā; teṣāṃ manassāpekṣatvāt | ata iti | paramātmaguṇānvayasya, 'paramātma' śabdasāmānādhikaraṇyasya vā śravaṇe sati, tattadviśiṣṭopāsa- nārthatvanirṇayaḥ | ato'tra paramātmadharmānvayaśravaṇābhāvāt, vaiyadhikaraṇyanirdeśācca anyathā siddhatvāt na upāsanārthatvaṃ jīvaliṅgasya ; api tu bhedajñāpanārthatvamityarthaḥ | pradhānasyeti | iha pradhānopādānaṃ, jīvasya tena vinā kāraṇatvānupapattyabhiprāyakṛtam | yadvā anenādhikaraṇadvayena īkṣatyānandamayādhikaraṇākṣepa dvayasya parihṛtatvajñāpanārtham ||

|| iti jagadvā citvādhikaraṇam || 5 ||
2 tantrasiddhasya - tha. 3 sthitam - ā, i, ṅa, ña, u.

33. vākyānvayādhikaraṇam ||1-4-6 ||

ityārabhya,

'na vā are sarvasya kāmāya sarva priyaṃ bhavati, ātmanastu kāmāya sarva priya bhavati, ātmā vā are draṣṭavyaḥ śrotavyo mantavyo nididhyāsitavyaḥ, maitreyi ! ātmani khalvare dṛṣṭe śrute mate vijñāte, idaṃ sarva viditam'

iti ||

tatra saṃśayaḥ – kim asmin vākye, draṣṭavyatayā upadiśyamānaḥ, tantra siddhaḥ puruṣa eva ? athavā sarvajñaḥ satyasaṅkalpaḥ sarveśvaraḥ 1 –iti ||

kiṃ yuktam ? puruṣaḥ- iti |

kuta: ?

ādimadhyāvasāneṣu puruṣasyaiva pratīteḥ | upakrame tāvat, pati - jāyā- putra-vitta- paśvādipriyatvāyogāt, jīvātmaiva pratīyate | madhye'pi,
'vijñānaghana evaitebhyo bhūtebhyaḥ samutthāya tānyevānuvinaśyati ; na pretya saṃjñāssti'
sarvajñaḥ satyasaṅkalpaḥ iti, siddhāntānuguṇaviśeṣaṇam | jīvakarmaviśeṣābhijñaḥ tatphaladānaśaktaḥ iti bhāvaḥ | kiṃ jagatkāraṇavā divedāntavedyaṃ puruṣādhiṣṭhitaṃ pradhānam ? uta brahma ? iti vicāraḥ | tadarthaṃ vṛhadāraṇyake maitreyībrāhmaṇe draṣṭavyatayoktaḥ ātmā, kiṃ pañcaviṃśakaḥ ? uta paramātmā ? iti | kim upakrame patijāyāputrādipriyasambandhaḥ 'ātma'- śabdasya pañcaviṃśapuruṣa paratāmavagamayati | uta na ? iti | kiṃ vākyāvayavānāṃ sarveṣāṃ paramātmani anvayāsāmañjasyam ? uta pañcaviṃśa: ? iti |
yadā paramātmani anvayāsāmañjasyam ; tadā patijāyādipriyasambandhena 'ātma' śabdasya puruṣaparatvāvagamāt draṣṭavyatayoktaḥ pañcaviṃśaka iti, tadadhiṣṭhitaṃ pradhānaṃ vedānta- vaidyamiti syāt |

iti, utpatti-vināśa yogāt', sa eva avagamyate | tathā ante ca,
'vijñātāramare kena vijānīyāt ?'

iti, sa eva jñātā kṣetrajña eva pratīyate ; na īśvaraḥ | ataḥ tantrasiddhapuruṣapratipādana- param idaṃ vākyam - iti niścīyate ||

nanu -

"amṛtatvasya tu nāssśā'sti vittena
ityupakramāt amṛtatvaprābhyupāyopadeśaparam idaṃ vākyam - ityavagamyate | tat kathaṃ puruṣapratipādanaparatvam asya vākyasya ?
' taducyate ' - ata eva hi ! atra puruṣapratipādanam | tantre hi ! aciddharmādhyāsa- viyukta puruṣa 'svarūpa' yāthātmya vijñānameva, amṛtatvahetutvenocyate | ataḥ jīvātmanaḥ prakṛtiviyuktaṃ svarūpam, iha amṛtatvāya,
'ātmā vā are draṣṭavyaḥ'

ityupadiśyate ||

sarveṣāmātmanāṃ prakṛtiviyuktaṃ svarūpam ekarūpamiti, prakṛtiviyuktasvātmayāthātmya- 'vijñānena', sarva evātmāno viditā bhavantīti, ātmavijñānena sarvavijñānam upapannam ||

yadā pañcaviṃśe vākyāvayavānāṃ samanvayāsāmañjasyam ; tadā pati - jāyādi- priyasambandhāt 'ātma' śabdasya pañcaviṃśaparatvānavagamāt maitreyībrāhmaṇe draṣṭavyatayoktaḥ pañcaviṃśati- rikta iti, kāraṇavākyānāṃ paramātmaparatvaṃ syāt - iti ||
tathānte ceti | ādimadhyagatajīva hetvanugṛhītaḥ 'vijñātṛ ' śabdaḥ tatparaḥ iti bhāvaḥ || nanviti | pati- jāyā dipriya sambandhapratipādakavākyāt pūrvameva, amṛtatvaprābhyupāya- paratvam avagamyate ityarthaḥ |
ata eva – amṛtatvaprātyupāyopadeśaparatvā deva, ityarthaḥ ||
sarveṣāmiti | 'aikyopadeśaphalabhūtam ekavijñānena sarvavijñānaṃ, jīvapratipādanaparatve

devādisthāvarānteṣu sarveṣu bhūteṣu, ātmasvarūpasya 'jñānaikaprakāratvāt', 'idaṃ sarva yadayamātmā'
iti 'aikātmyopadeśaḥ ; devādyākārāṇām anātmā' kāratvāt",
'sarva taṃ parādāt'
ityādinā anyatvaniṣedhaśca |
'yatra hi dvaitamiva bhavati'

" iti ca nānātvaniṣedhena, 'ekasvarūpe hyātmani' devādiprakṛtipariṇāmabhedena nānātvaṃ, mithyā' - ityucyate ||
'tasya ha vā etasya (asya ? ) mahato bhūtasya niśvasitametat yaddaśvedaḥ '

ityādyapi, prakṛteradhiṣṭhātṛtvena puruṣanimittatvāt 'jagadutpatteḥ ", upapadyate ||

evam asmin vākye puruṣapare niścite sati tadaikārthyāt sarve vedāntāḥ tantrasiddhaṃ puruṣameva abhidadhatīti, tadadhiṣṭhitā prakṛtireva jagadupādānam ; na īśvara:- iti ||
kathaṃ ghaṭate ? bahavo hi jīvā : ; na ca mithaḥ kāryakāraṇabhūtāśca' ityāśaṅkaya, prakāraikyā t upapadyate ( ityuttarātmakamidam ) ||
devādisthāvarānteṣu.........anyatvaniṣedhaśceti | devādyākārasya anātmākāratvāt, tasya tadananyatvamuktam | tasmāt ātmanaḥ anyatvadarśanaṃ nāma, niṣkṛṣṭa svarūpavyatirikta- devādyākāradarśana miti, tasya anarthahetutvamucyate ityarthaḥ | yatra hīti | anātmākārāṇa devatvādīnāṃ bahutvāt, ekākāre ātmani, devatvādīni na santīti niṣidhyate ityarthaḥ || puruṣanimittatvāditi | jīvādhiṣṭhitapradhānāt ākāśaguṇaśabdotpattiriti, ṛgvedādi- kāraṇatvamupapadyate ityarthaḥ ||

evaṃ prāpte, pracakṣmahe—vākyānvayāt - iti | sarveśvara eva asmin vākye pratīyate |

'kutaḥ ' ?

evameva hi ! vākpāvayavānām anyonyanvayaḥ samañjaso bhavati ||

" amṛtatvasya tu nā''śā'sti vittena'

iti yājñavalkyenābhihite,

'yenā'haṃ nā'mṛtā syāṃ kimahaṃ tena kuryām ; yadeva bhagavān 'veda' tadeva me brūhi'
iti, amṛtatvānupāyatayā vittādyanādareṇa, amṛtatvaprātyupāyameva prārthayamānāyai maitreyyai, tadupāyatayā draṣṭavyatvenopadiṣṭaḥ ayamātmā paramātmaiva ;
" tameva viditvā'timṛtyumeti'

' tamevaṃ vidvānamṛta iha bhavati nānyaḥ panthāḥ ' ityādibhiḥ, amṛtatvasya paramapuruṣavedana kopāyatayā pratipādanāt ||
paramapuruṣavibhūtibhūtasya prāpturātmanaḥ svarūpa' yāthātmyam", apavarga 'sādhana'- parama puruṣavedanopayogitayā avagantavyam ; na svata eva upāyatvena | ataḥ, atra paramātmaiva, amṛtatvopāyatayā 'draṣṭavyaḥ' ityādinā upadiśyate | tathā,
' tasya ha vā etasya (asya 1) mahato bhūtasya niḥśvasitametat yadṛgveda : '
ityādinā kṛtsnasya jagataḥ kāraṇatvamucyamānaṃ, paramapuruṣādanyasya karmaparavaśasya muktasya nirvyāpārasya ca puruṣamātrasya na sambhavati | tathā,
samañjaso bhavati – svārasiko bhavati ityarthaḥ | asvarasānvayo hi ! parairucyate | śruteḥ svārasyaṃ nāma sajātīyaśrāyaikārthyam ityabhiprāyaḥ ||
paramapuruṣeti | pradhānabhūtopāyaparavākyaikārthyaṃ svārasikamityarthaḥ | paramapuruṣādanyasya
na sambhavati iti | baddhasya sarvajñatvādyayogāt, muktasya jagadvyāpāraśūnyatvācca kṛtsnajagatkāraṇatvaṃ na sambhavatītyarthaḥ | puruṣamātrasya – jīvamātrasya | mātracā alpaśaktitva-

amṛtasya - tha. yāthāramyavijñānam bha, i, gha.

5 tameva – na. SB II-49

'ātmano vā are darśanena'
ityādinā, ekavijñānena sarvavijñānam abhidhīyamānaṃ, sarvātmabhūte paramātmanyeva avakalpate ||

'yattvetat' – ekarūpatvāt ātmanām, ekātmavijñānena sarvātmavijñānamucyate-

iti,

tadayuktam ; acetanaprapañcajñānābhāvena, sarvavijñānābhāvāt | pratijñopapādanāya ca, 'idaṃ brahma idaṃ kṣatram

ityupakramya,

'idaṃ sarvaṃ yadayamātmā'

iti, pratyakṣādisiddhaṃ cidacinmiśraṃ prapañcam, 'idam' iti nirdiśya, 'etat ayamātmā' iti, aikātmyopadeśaśca paramātmana evopapadyate | na hi ! 'idaṃ' śabdavācyaṃ cida- cinmiśraṃ jagat puruṣeṇā'citsaṃsṛṣṭena tadviyuktena svarūpeṇa vā' avasthitena ca, aikyamupagacchati ||

ata eva,

'sarva taṃ parādāt yo'nyatrātmanaḥ sarve veda' iti, vyatiriktatvena sarvavedana nindā ca |
mabhipretam | ekavijñāneneti | kāraṇatvaphalabhūtam ekavijñānena sarvavijñānapratijñānamapi rāddhāntopapādakamityarthaḥ ||
pratijñopapādanāyeti, aikātmyopadeśānupapattimāha | etat ayamiti | śrutisthedaṃ- śabdasya sthāne 'etat śabdaḥ bhāṣye, svavākyatvajñāpanāya prayuktaḥ | idam ayamātmā iti, kāryasya kāraṇatādātmyopadeśaḥ | nahītyādi | kāryasya kāraṇena hi ! svarūpaikyasambhavaḥ | sūkṣma-sthūlacidaciccharīrakaḥ paramātmeti, tasyaiva hi ! kāryeṇa jagatā aikyaṃ sambhavati ; na tu akāraṇabhūtasya baddhasya vā muktasya vā jīvasya ityarthaḥ |

ata eva sarvasya paramātmātmakatvādeva | vyati nindā iti atadātmakatvānu- sandhānaniṣedhaḥ | na tu devādidṛṣṭiniṣedhaḥ ; bhedaniṣedhasya prāguktahetunibandhanatvāt ityarthaḥ |

tathā prathame ca maitreyībrāhmaṇe,

'mahadbhūtamanantamapāram'

iti śrutāḥ mahattvādayo guṇāḥ, paramātmana eva sambhavanti | ataḥ sa eva atra pratipādyate ||

yattūktam - 'pati- jāyā - putra - vitta paśvādi' priyānvayinaḥ jīvātmanaḥ, upakrame tu anveṣṭavyatayā pratipādanāt tadviṣayameva idaṃ vākyam - iti,

tadayuktam ; 'ātmanastu kāmāya' iti, 'ātma' śabdena ' jīvātma' saṃśabdane, tasya 'ātmā vā are draṣṭavyaḥ' ityanena ananvayaprasaṅgāt,

'ātmā vā are draṣṭavyaḥ'
iti ātmano draṣṭavyatvopayogitayā,
'ātmanastu kāmāya'

ityupadiṣṭamiti pratīyate | ātmanastu kāmāya - ātmanaḥ kāmasampattaye | kāmyate iti kāmaḥ | ātmanaḥ 'iṣṭasampattaye', iti yāvat ||

yasmāt, ātmana iṣṭasampattaye patyādayaḥ priyā bhavanti, tasmāt patyādipriyaṃ parityajya, tadviyuktam ātmasvarūpam anveṣṭavyam - iti, asaṅgataṃ bhavati | pratyuta, 'na patyādi śeṣatayā ' patyādīnāṃ priyatvam api tu, ātmanaḥ 'śeṣatayā' patyādīnāṃ priyatvam' ityukte, svaśeṣatayā ta eva upādeyāḥ syuḥ ||

'ātmanastu kāmāya sarva priyaṃ bhavati'
ityasya pareṇa ananvaye, vākyabhedaḥ 'prasajyate' | abhyupagamyamāne'pi vākyabhede, # pūrvavākyasya na kiñcit prayojanaṃ dṛśyate ||

3.

ataḥ, patyādisarva priyaṃ parityajya ātmana eva anveṣṭavyatvaṃ yathā pratīyate ; tathā vākyārtho varṇanīyaḥ | so'yamucyate-
'amṛtatvasya tu nāssśā'sti vittena'
iti vittādīnāṃ nitya nirdoṣaniratiśayānandarūpāmṛtatva prāpyanupā' yatā 'muktvā, 'vitta- 'putra- pati- 'jāyādīnāṃ sātiśayaduḥkhamiśrakādācitkapriyatvam anubhūyamānam, na patyādi - svarūpaprayuktam ; api tu niratiśayānandasvabhāva paramātmaprayuktam ; ataḥ, ya eva svayaṃ niratiśayānandaḥ san anyeṣāmapi priyatvaleśā 'spadatva mā pādayati, sa paramātmaiva draṣṭavyaḥ' ityupadiśyate ||

pratyuteti | na kevalaṃ svābhimatārthāsaṅgatiḥ, anabhimatārthāsaṅgatiścetyarthaḥ | upādeyāḥ syuriti | sakaletaratyāgapūrvakam ātmano draṣṭavyatvasya virodha eva syāt ityarthaḥ ||

pareṇa - 'ātmā vā' iti pareṇa vākyena | vākyabhedaḥ - mahāvākyabhedaḥ | 'sambhavatyekavākyatve vākyabhedastu neṣyate' iti nyāyāt, ekavākyatvena nirvāhe suśake'pi vākyabhedaḥ anupapannaḥ ityarthaḥ ||
ata iti | evaṃ parapakṣānupapatteḥ, pariśeṣāt arthāntaraparatvaṃ siddhamityarthaḥ |

svayaṃ......... māpādayatīti | anyeṣāmānando yadadhīnaḥ tasyā''nandaḥ, anavadhikāti- śayaḥ, iti arthasiddham | yathā anyeṣāṃ vidyāṃ dhanaṃ vā dadataḥ puruṣasya, jñānādhikatvaṃ dhanādhikatvaṃ ca arthasiddham | sa eko brahmaṇa ānanda:' 'yato vācaḥ ' 'rasaṃ vā'yaṃ' ityādi- śratyantareṣu tat spaṣṭamabhihitaṃ ca ityabhiprāyaḥ | upadiśyate ityantena śrutestātparyārtha uktaḥ ||

1 śeṣitayā - da. 2 prasajyeta ṭha

tadayamartha:-

'na vā are patyuḥ kāmāya patiḥ priyo bhavati'
na hi ! 'pati- jāyā' - putra- vittādayaḥ, 'matprayojanāya ahamasya priyaḥ syām' iti svasaṅkalpāt priyā bhavanti ; api tu, ātmanaḥ kāmāya - paramātmanaḥ svārādhaka- priyapratilambhanarūpeṣṭanirvṛttaye, ityarthaḥ | paramātmā hi ! karmabhirārādhitaḥ, tatta- tkarmānuguṇaṃ pratiniyata deśakālasvarūpa parimāṇam, ārādhakānāṃ ' tattadvastugataṃ priya' tva- māpādayati ;

' epa hyevānandayāti'

iti śruteḥ | na tu tattadvastu svarūpeṇa priyamapriyaṃ vā | yathoktam-

'tadeva prītaye' bhūtvā punarduḥkhāya jāyate |
tadeva kopāya yataḥ prasādāya ca jāyate ||
tasmāduḥkhātmakaṃ nāsti na ca kiṃcit sukhātmakam ||'

iti ||

'ātmanastu kāmāya' ityasya jīvātmaparatve'pi, 'ātmā vā are draṣṭavyaḥ iti tu, paramātmaviṣayameva | ' tatra' ayamarthaḥ-

34. prakṛtyadhikaraṇam || 1-4-7 ||

:~

yadyapi - īkṣaṇādiguṇayogāt, sarvajñam īśvaraṃ jagatkāraṇatvena vedāntāḥ prati- pādayanti tathā'pi 'vedāntaireva, jagadupādānatayā pradhānameva pratipādyate' iti ; pratīyate | na hi ! vedāntāḥ, sarvajñasya apariṇa minaḥ adhiṣṭhātuḥ īśvarasya, adhiṣṭheyena acetanena pariṇāminā pradhānena vinā 'jagataḥ kāraṇatvam' avagamayanti ||

tathā hi ; apariṇāminam enam, prakṛtiṃ ca etadadhiṣṭhitāṃ pariṇāminīm adhīyate-

'gauranādyantavatī sā janitrī bhūtabhāvinī'

yadā noparodhakā bhavanti ; tadā pratijñādṛṣṭāntayoḥ svarasatayā neyatvāt upādānatvamapi vedāntāḥ pratipādayantīti, upādānamapi brahmaiva – iti rāddhānte phala-phalibhāvaḥ ||
īkṣaṇādi iti 'ādi' śabda : ānandādiparaḥ | vedāntaireva brahmaikakāraṇatvaparatayā tvadabhimataireva iti bhāvaḥ | na hīti | īśvarasya cetanatvāt apariṇāmitvena adhiṣṭhātṛtvena ca' pradhānasya acetanatayā pariṇāmitvena adhiṣṭheyatvena ca vedāntapratipāditatvaṃ pratijñātārthe hetu: ityarthaḥ | niṣkalam ityādivākyam, īśvarasya apariṇāmitvaviṣayam | vikārajananīm iti, prakṛteḥ vikāritvādhiṣṭheyatva viṣayam | janitrī - samaṣṭisṛṣṭikarī | bhūtabhāvinī- vyaṣṭisṛṣṭikarī | tatheti | pradhānapratipādake vākye tadviśeṣaṇatayā īśvarasya adhiṣṭhātṛtvaṃ darśitam | īśvarapratipādake vākye tasya pradhānādhiṣṭhātṛtayā upādānabhūtāttasmādbheda darśayati | 'maheśvara 'śabdena, 'māthi' śabda nirdiṣṭasya nimittakāraṇasya, māyāṃ prati prerakatvaṃ vivakṣitamiti bhāvaḥ |

'asmānmāyī sṛjate viśvametat ........ māyāṃ tu prakṛtiṃ vidyāt māyinaṃ tu maheśvaram'

iti | smṛtirapi -

'mayā'dhyakṣeṇa prakṛtiḥ sūyate sacarācaram'

evaṃ prāpte, abhidhīyate – prakṛtiśca pratijña ( - dṛṣṭāntānuparodhāt - iti |

136. pariṇāmāt || 1-4-27||

pariṇāmasvābhāvyāt | na atropadiśyamānasya pariṇāmasya parasmin brahmaṇi doṣāva 'hatvaṃ svabhāvaḥ' ; pratyuta ! niraṅkuśaiśvaryāvahatvameva ityabhiprāyaḥ | evameva hi ! pariṇāma upadiśyate |
aśeṣaheya pratyanīkakalyāṇaikatānaṃ, svetarasamastavastuvilakṣaṇaṃ, sarvajña, satya- saṅkalpam, avāptasamasta kāmam, anavadhikā tiśayānandaṃ, svalīlopakaraṇabhūtasamasta- cidacidvastujātaśarīratayā tadātmabhūtaṃ, paraṃ brahma; khaśarīrabhūte prapañce tanmātrāhaṅkārādi- kāraṇaparamparayā tamaśśabdavācyā tisūkṣmācidvastvekaśeṣe sati tamasi ca svaśarīra-

pariṇāmāditi | pūrvapakṣe hi ! nimittopādānabhedaśrutiḥ nirdoṣaśrutyanyathānupapattiśca | hetutayā upanyaste | rāddhānte tu, 'pratijñā dṛṣṭāntānuparodhāt ' 'abhidhyopadeśācca' iti siddhāntasādhakatayā śrutyarthāpattidvayaṃ darśitam | 'sākṣāccobhayānnānāt' ityādisūtradvayena, 'yoniśca hi gīyate' ityanena ca, siddhāntasādhakaśrutayo darśitāḥ | ' pariṇāmāt' iti sūtrantu pūrva pakṣyupanyastaśruti śrutyarthāpattiparihāraparam ityarthaḥ | pariṇāmeti | 'pariṇāmāt' ityukte virodhahetutvamevāsya pratīyate ; na tu virodhaparihārahetutvam ityāśaṅkaya | na........ ityabhiprāya iti | 'pariṇāma' śabda: pariṇāmaviśeṣapara: | ' gāmānaya' ityādiṣu - sāmānyaśabdaprayogaḥ vivakṣitaviśeṣābhiprāyeṇa dṛṣṭa: | 'śabdādeva pramita: ' 'dharmokte : ' ityādiṣu viśeṣābhiprāyeṇa sāmānyanirdeśaḥ sautra eva dṛśyate | iha pratipannāt śabdāt itivat iha pratipannāt pariṇāmāt, ityarthaḥ |

'tayā'pi pṛthaṅ' nirdeśānarhātisūkṣmadaśāpacyā svasmin ekatā' māpanne sati tathābhūta- tamaḥ śarīraṃ brahma; 'pūrvavat vibhakta - nāmarūpa' cidacinmiśraprapañcaśarīraṃ syām' iti saṅkalpya, adhyayakrameṇa jagaccharīratayā ātmānaṃ pariṇama 'yatīti, sarveṣu vedānteṣu pariṇāmopadeśaḥ ||
tathaiva vṛhadāraṇyake, kṛtsnasya jagataḥ brahmaśarīratvaṃ brahmaṇaḥ 'tadātmatvaṃ' ca "āmnāyate".
'yaḥ pṛthivyāṃ tiṣṭhan pṛthivyā antaraḥ, yaṃ pṛthivī na veda, yasya pṛthivī śarīraṃ, yaḥ pṛthivīmantaro yamayati, eṣa ta ātmā'ntayobhyamṛtaḥ'

'yasyā''tmā śarīram' iti viśeṣaḥ | loka - ' veda-yajñānāṃ paramātmaśarīratvamadhikam |
subālopanipadi ca pṛthivyādīnāṃ tattvānāṃ paramātmaśarīratva' mabhidhāya, * vājasaneyake, anuktānāmapi taccānāṃ śarīratvaṃ brahmaṇaḥ ātmatvaṃ ca śrūyate |

'yasya buddhiḥ śarīram' (subāu 7) yasyā'haṅkāraḥ śarīram' ( 'yasya cittaṃ śarīram' 'yasyā'vyaktaṃ śarīram' (

'yo mṛtyumantare sañcaran, yasya mṛtyuḥ śarīraṃ yaṃ mṛtyurnaveda, eṣa sarvabhūtānta-
rātmā'pahatapāpmā divyo deva eko nārāyaṇaḥ'
iti | atra 'mṛtyu' śabdena, 'parama' sūkṣmam acidvastu 'tamaḥ śabdavācyam abhidhīyate ; 'avyaktamakṣare līyate, akṣaraṃ tamasi līyate' :
iti, tasyāmevopaniṣadi kramapratyabhijñānāt | sarveṣāmātmanāṃ jñānāvaraṇānarthamūlatvena, tadeva hi ! tamaḥ, mṛtyuśabdavyapadeśyam |

subālopaniṣadyeva', brahmaśarīratayā tadātmakānāṃ taccānāṃ brahmaṇyeva pralayaḥ

āmnāyate

'pṛthivyapsu pralīyate, āpastejasi līyante, tejo vāyau līyate, vāyu- rākāśe līyate, ākāśamindriyeṣu, indriyāṇi tanmātreṣu, tanmātrāṇi bhūtādau līyante | bhūtādirmahati līyate, mahānavyakte līyate, avyaktamakṣare līyate, akṣaraṃ tamasi līyate, tamaḥ pare deva ekī bhavati'
iti | avibhāgāpattidaśāyāmapi, cidacidvastu atisūkṣmaṃ sakarmasaṃskāraṃ tiṣṭhatīti, uttaratra vakṣyate—

'mṛtyu' śabdasya devatāviśeṣe saṃsāre ca prayuktatvāt tayośca atra anvayānarhatvādāha | sarveṣāmiti | jñānāvaraṇarūpatvāt 'tama:' śabdavācyam | tadeva jñānāvaraṇarūpānartha- mūlatvāt 'mṛtyu' śabdavācyamityarthaḥ |
'ākāśamindriyeṣu, indriyāṇi tanmātreṣu' ityatra 'laya' śabdāprayogaḥ, layasthānatvā- bhāvāt | tatra sāhityamātraṃ vivakṣitam | 'tanmātreṣu' iti bahuvacane na tātparyam ; 'aditiḥ pāśān' itivat | śabdatanmātre ityarthaḥ | avibhāgāpattīti | viṣamasṛṣṭyanupapattyā cetanabahutva sthitivat, tatkarmānuguṇadeha sṛṣṭyanuguṇā cidvastusthitirapi svarūpa- pariṇāmānupapatticalasiddhā, ityarthaḥ |

iti ||

evaṃ svasmādvibhāgavyapadeśānarhatayā paramātmanā ekībhūtātyantasūkṣma cidacidvastu- śarīrāt ekasmādeva advitīyāt niratiśayānandāt 'sarvajñāt ' satyasaṅkalpāt brahmaṇaḥ, nāma-rūpavibhāgārhasthūla cidacidvastuśarīratayā bahubhavana saṅkalpapūrvakaḥ, jagadākāreṇa pariṇāmaḥ śrūyate

'satyaṃ jñānamanantaṃ brahma'
'tasmādvā etasmādvijñānamayāt, anyo'ntara ātmā''nandamaya:' 'epa hyevā''nandayāti'
'so'kāmayata | bahu syāṃ prajāyeyeti | sa tapo'tapyata |
sa tapastaptvā | idaṃ sarvamasṛjata | yadidaṃ kiṃca | tat sṛṣṭvā | tadevā'nuprāviśat | tadanupraviśya'
'sacca tyaccā'bhavat| niruktaṃ cā'niruktaṃ ca | nilayanaṃ cā'nilayanaṃ ca | vijñānaṃ cāsvijñānaṃ ca | satyaṃ cā'nṛtaṃ ca satyamabhavat'

iti ||

atra 'tapaḥ śabdena, prācīna jagadā kāraparyālocanarūpaṃ jñānamabhidhīyate : 'yasya jñānamayaṃ tapaḥ'
" ityādiśruteḥ | prāk sṛṣṭaṃ jagatsaṃsthānam ālocya idānīmapi tatsaṃsthānaṃ jagat asṛjat ityarthaḥ | tathaiva hi ! brahma, sarveṣu kalpeṣu ekarūpameva jagat sṛjati |

vibhāgavyapadeśānarheti | ānandavallyām anuktamapi avibhaktanāmarūpatvaṃ śrutyantarasthena ekatvāvadhāraṇā dinā yathā avagamyate ; evaṃ cidaciccharīrakatvamapi kāraṇāvasthāyāmiha vivakṣita - mityabhyupagantavyamiti bhāvaḥ | ālocyeti | aucityāt jagadālocanamarthaḥ ityarthaḥ | 'tapa ālocane' ityasmāddhātoḥ 'tapaḥ' śabdaniṣpattiriti bhāvaḥ | tatsaṃsthānamiti | yathāpūrvaṃ sṛṣṭyarthamityabhi- prāyaḥ | tathaiva hīti | yathāpūrvasṛṣṭipratipādanaṃ hi tadālocane liṅgaṃ bhavati |

'sūryācandramasau dhātā yathāpūrvamakalpayat |
divaṃ ca pṛthivīṃ cā'ntarikṣamatho suvaḥ ||' 'yathartuṣvṛtuliṅgāni nānārūpāṇi paryaye |
dṛśyante tāni tānyeva tathā bhāvā yuga| diṣu ||' iti śruti smṛtibhyaḥ ||

tadayamarthaḥ - svayam aparicchinna' jñānānanda svabhāva:, atyantasūkṣmatayā 'asa- kalpa' svalīlopakaraṇacidacidvastuśarīratayā tanmayaḥ paramātmā, vicitrānantakrīḍana- kopāditsayā svaśarīrabhūta prakṛtipuruṣa samaṣṭiparamparayā mahābhūtaparyantam ātmānaṃ 'tattaccharīrakaṃ pariṇamayya, tanmayaḥ punaḥ sat-tyacchabdavācya' vicitracida' cinmiśra- devādisthāvarāntajagadrūpo'bhavat " - iti |
' tadevānuprāviśat | tadanu praviśya' iti, ' kāraṇāvasthāyām ātmatayā'vasthitaḥ paramātmaiva, kāryarūpeṇa vikriya' māṇadravyasyāpi ātmatayā avasthāya tattadabhavat", ityucyate |

evaṃ paramātma- cidacitsaṅghātarupajagadā 'kāra' pariṇāme, paramātmaśarīrabhūta cidaṃśa- gatāḥ sarve eva, apuruṣārthāḥ | tathābhūtācidaṃśagatāśca sarve, vikārāḥ | paramātmani kāryatvam – tattadavasthayoḥ tayoḥ " niyantṛtvenā''tmatvam | paramātmā tu tayoḥ khaśarīra- bhūtayoḥ niyantṛtayā ātmabhūtaḥ, tadgatāpuruṣārthaiḥ vikāraiśca na spṛśyate | aparicchinna- jñānānandamayaḥ sarvadā ekarūpa eva, jagatparivartana līlayā avatiṣṭhate | tadetadāha- 'satyaṃ cā'nṛtaṃ ca satyamabhavat' – iti | vicitracidacidrūpeṇa vikriyamāṇamapi brahma, satyamevā'bhavat - nirasta nikhiladoṣagandham, aparicchinnajñānā' nandam ekarūpa 'mevāḍa- bhavat ityarthaḥ |

'sarvāṇi cidacidvastūni, sūkṣmadaśāpannāni sthūladaśāpannāni ca parasya brahmaṇaḥ lopakaraṇāni | sṛṣṭyādayaśca līlā ' iti bhagavadvaipāyana- parāśarādibhiruktam ; 'avyaktādi' viśeṣāntaṃ pariṇāmadhi ' saṃyutam |
krīḍā hareridaṃ sarvaṃ kṣaramityupadhāryatām ||' 'krīḍato bālakasyeva ceṣṭāṃ tasya niśāmaya'
'bālaḥ krīḍanakai 'riva"
ityādibhiḥ | vakṣyati ca -

tatprakāra bhūtakṣetrajñagatācaṃ, apuruṣārthāḥ' iti vivektuṃ, prakṛti-puruṣayoḥ brahma'śarīrabhṛtayoḥ', tadānīṃ tathā nirdeśānahātisūkṣmadaśāpacyā brahmaṇā ekībhūtayorapi bhedena vyapadeśaḥ
' tadātmānaṃ svayamakuruta' ityādibhiraikārthyāt | tathā ca mānavaṃ vacaḥ
'so'bhidhyāya śarīrāt svāt sisṛkṣurvividhāḥ prajāḥ |
apa eva sasarjāssaau tāsu vīryamapāsṛjat ||'
iti | ata eva brahmaṇaḥ "nirdoṣatva-nirvikāratva' śrutayaśca upapannāḥ | ataḥ, brahmaka jagato nimittam, upādānaṃ ca || 27 ||
137. yoniśca hi gīyate || 1-4-28 ||
