.

etena pādacatuṣṭayoktanyāyakalāpena, sarvavedānteṣu jagatkāraṇapratipādanaparāḥ * sarve vākyaviśeṣāḥ, cetanā cetana vilakṣaṇasarvajña sarvaśaktibrahmapratipādanaparāḥ vyākhyātāḥ | ' vyākhyātāḥ' iti padābhyāsaḥ, adhyāyaparisamāptidyotanārthaḥ || 29 ||

|| iti sarvavyākhyānādhikaraṇam || 8 ||
iti śrībhagavadrāmānuja viracite śārīrakamīmāṃsābhāṣye prathamasyā'dhyāyasya caturthaḥ pādaḥ ||

|| iti prathamo'dhyāyaḥ ||
|| śrīmate rāmānujāya namaḥ ||

|| śrīrastu ||

anudāhṛtāḥ kāraṇavādivākyaviśeṣāḥ kim uktalakṣaṇacidacidvilakṣaṇabrahmaparāḥ ? uta na ? iti vicāraḥ | tadarthaṃ te kim uktanyāyānām aviṣayā: ? uta viṣayāḥ ? iti | viṣayatvaṃ sambhavati ? na ? iti | yadā na sambhavati tadā tadaviṣayatvāt uktalakṣaṇabrahma- paratvābhāvaḥ | yadā sambhavati tadā uktanyāyaviṣayatvāt uktalakṣaṇabrahmaparāḥ iti phalaphalibhāvaḥ |

pādacatuṣṭayokteti eteneti padasya, prakṛtyadhikaraṇanyāyamātraparāmarśitvavyudāsāya | vākyaviśeṣā iti pūrvamanudāhṛtāḥ aniścitārthāḥ ityarthaḥ | sarvajñatvasarvaśaktitve hi ! nimittopādānatopayoginī | vyākhyātā iti | tasmāt ayogānyayogavyavacchedena nārāyaṇasya kāraṇatvaṃ pādacatuṣṭayena pratipāditamiti siddham | padābhyāsa iti | 'vyākhyātāvyākhyātāḥ ' ityukte, 'vyākhyāta' śabdasya 'avyākhyāta' śabdasya ca samāsena aikapadye sati, sarvavākya- viśeṣāṇāṃ vyākhyātatvamuktaṃ na syāditi tadvyudāsārtham asamastatayā pṛthakpadatvaṃ jñāpaya abhyāsasya prayojanamāha || 29 ||

prājñaiḥ pūrvairgrathitā lā ghyāvyākhyāḥ samālokya | ladhvī ṭippanyeṣā suyojitā'dhyetṛlābhāryam || | oṃ namaḥ paramaṛṣibhyaḥ |
|| iti sarvavyākhyānādhikaraṇam || 8 ||

uttaraśāntipāṭhaḥ

oṃ vāṅ me manasi pratiṣṭhitā mano me vāci pratiṣṭhitam āvirāvīrma edhi | vedasya ma āṇī sthaḥ, śrutaṃ me mā prahāsī, anenādhītenāhorātrān vadiṣyāmi, satyaṃ vadiṣyāmi, tanmāmavatu, tadvaktāramavatu, avatu mām, baktāram | oṃ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ |

sandadhāmi | ṛtaṃ avatu vaktāramavatu

śaṃ no bhavatvaryamā | śaṃ na indro bṛhaspatiḥ | namaste vāyo | tvameva pratyakṣaṃ brahmāsi | tvāmeva
pratyakṣaṃ brahmāvādiṣam | ṛtamavādiṣam | satyamavādiṣam | tanmāmavīt | tadvaktāramāvīt | āvīnmām | āvīdvaktāram | oṃ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ ||
oṃ saha nāvavatu | saha nau bhunaktu | saha vīryaṃ karavāvahai | tejasvi nāvadhītamastu. mā vidviṣāvahai | oṃ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ ||
oṃ pūrṇamadaḥ pūrṇamidaṃ pūrṇāt pūrṇamudacyate | pūrṇasya pūrṇamādāya pūrṇamevāvaśiṣyate | oṃ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ ||
oṃ āpyāyantu mamāṅgāni vākprāṇaścakṣuḥ śrotramatho balamindriyāṇi ca sarvāṇi sarva brahmaupaniṣadam māhaṃ brahma nirākuryām, mā mā brahma nirākarot | anirākaraṇamastu anirākaraṇaṃ me'stu | tadātmanirate ya upaniṣatsu dharmāḥ te mayi santu te mayi santu | oṃ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ ||
oṃ bhadraṃ karṇebhiḥ śṛṇuyāma devāḥ | bhadraṃ paśyemākṣabhiryajatrāḥ | sthirairaṅgaistuṣṭu vāṃsastanūbhiḥ | vyaśema devahitaṃ yadāyuḥ | svasti na indro vṛddhaśravāḥ | svasti naḥ pūṣā viśvavedāḥ | svasti nastārkṣyo ariṣṭanemiḥ | svasti no bṛhaspatirdadhātu | oṃ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ ||
oṃ namo brahmaṇe dhāraṇaṃ me astvanirākaraṇaṃ dhārayitā bhūyāsaṃ karṇayoḥ śrutaṃ mācyovaṃ mamāmuṣya om | oṃ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ ||

oṃ śaṃ no mitraḥ śaṃ varuṇaḥ | śaṃ no viṣṇururukramaḥ | namo brahmaṇe |

iti śrī bhagavadrā mā nu ja viracite

anubandhaḥ - 1

śrīḥ

śrīmate rāmānujāya namaḥ | śriyai namaḥ | śrīdharāya namaḥ |
1) śrī senevarāryaviracitaḥ śārīrakanyāyakalāpasaṅgrahaḥ
īkṣatyadhikaraṇam

* gauṇī tu gauṇekṣaṇa sāhacaryādīkṣā yatastañjagatāṃ nidānam | pradhānamityetadapā cakāra mukhyaṃ yato vīkṣaṇamātmaśabdāt || 12 ||

gauṇe.... caryāt – 'teja aikṣata, āpa aikṣanta' ityuktā cetanagatatvāhamukhyekṣaṇa saha- pāṭhāt jñāpakāt īkṣā - ' tadaikṣata' ityuktekṣaṇa kriyā'pi yataḥ yasmāddhetoḥ, gauṇī tu - sṛṣṭyābhimukhyarūpā amukhyaiva grāhyā, tat tasmāt jagatāṃ nidānam – 'sadeva somyedamagra āsīt' iti jagatkāraṇatayā 'sat' padena bodhitaṃ vastu, pradhānam – sāṅkhyoktaṃ prakṛtitattvameva bhavitumarhati ityetat — evaṃrūpaṃ sāṅkhyānāmāśaṅkanam, apācakāra – sūtrakāro dūrīkṛtavān, yataḥ—yasmāddhetoḥ, vīkṣaṇam— 'tadaikṣata' iti pratipannamīkṣaṇam, ātmaśabdāt – 'aitadātmyam' iti ātmarūpacetanasamabhivyāhārāt jñāpakāt mukhyam - saṅkalpātmakaṃ cetanāsādhāraṇam anupacaritameva grāhyam || 12 ||

2) śrī vātsya varada guruviracitaḥ tattvasāraḥ ||
chāndogye kecidāhurdiśatirutatamādeśamaprākṣya itya- trāssṅpūrvastūpadeśaṃ prakaṭayati sato na praśāsti ghañantaḥ | no kartaryasti karmaṇyagaṇi ghañiha, sat kartṛ śāstau na karma
syāt syāt karmopadeśe tadidamuditamādeśavācopadeśyam || 16 || kecit–advaitinaḥ, chāndogye – chāndogyopaniṣatsambandhini, uta..... maprākṣya ityatra —- ‘uta tamādeśamaprākṣyaḥ ?' ityasmin vākye, āṅpūrvaḥ _ 'āṅ' ityupasargapūrvakaḥ, ghañantaḥ—

* śārīrakanyāyakalāpasaṃgraha - tattvasāra-adhikaraṇasārāvalīnāṃ pratipadārthavivṛtiḥ, ādita: etad- granthasampādakaiḥ N.S. rāmabhadrācāryaiḥ saṅgrathitetitteyam |

'ghañ' pratyayāntaḥ, diśatiḥ – 'diś' dhātuḥ, ('ādeśa' śabda: iti yāvat ) sataḥ — sacchanda- vācyabrahmaviṣayakam, upadeśam — ajñātajñāpanaṃ, prakaṭayati — pratipādayati, sataḥ praśāstim- satpadārthakartṛkaṃ niyamanam, na prakaṭayati naiva bodhayati, (kuta iti cet !) ghañ kartari no asti — ghañpratyayaḥ kartṛrūpārthabodhane na anuśāsana siddha:, (kintu ) karmaṇikarmarūpārthabodhane eva, agaṇi— anuśāsanasiddhaḥ 'akartari ca kārake saṃjñāyām' iti, iha - asmin prakaraṇe, sat—sacchabdavācyaṃ vastu, śāstau – śāsanarūpārthasvīkāre, kartṛ syāt - 'śās' dhātvarthakriyāṃ prati kartṛkārakameva bhavet karma na syāt karmakārakaṃ naiva bhavet, (kartṛbhinnaṃ kārakaṃ na bhavet iti yāvat) tat — tasmāddhetoḥ, idam - sat, upadeśe – diśdhātoḥ upadeśārthakatve, karma syāt — karmaiva bhavet, (ityataḥ) ādeśavācā - ādeśapadena, idam- satpadabodhyam, upadeśam – upadeśakarmatvenaiva uditam — śrutyabhiprāyaviṣayaḥ, iti āhuḥ evaṃ varṇayanti || 16 ||

atra brūmaḥ praśāstiṃ vadati diśira sāvāṅmukho nopadeśa

śāstau so'tiprasiddho na hi para iha cā'pekṣitārtha prasaṅgaḥ | yuktossādhāraṇoktyā ghañagaṇi karaṇe'pyatra vaivakṣikatvaṃ
śābdoktaṃ kārakāṇāṃ nanu karaṇatayā kartari syādvivakṣā || 17 ||

atra—asmin ākṣepe pravṛtte sati brūmaḥ — evaṃ samādhānaṃ varṇayāmaḥ, asau āṅmukhaḥ diśi:- asmin vākye prayuktaḥ 'ādeśa' śabdaḥ, praśāsti vadati - ājñāpanarūpamevā'rtham— niyamanam śaktyā bodhayati, upadeśam – ajñātajñāpanam, na-naiva bodhayati, (kuta iti cet ) saḥ—āṅpūrvaḥ ‘diś’dhātuḥ, śāstau praśāsane eva, atiprasiddha:: bahujana prayogaviṣayaḥ, pare—anantarokte upadeśarūpe'rthe, na hi naiva prasiddhaḥ khalu iha ca - praśāsanārthatve ca, apekṣitārthaprasaṅgaḥ – nimittakāraṇatvaupayikasya ājñārūpārthasya prastāva:, asādhāraṇoktyā- paramātmāsādhāraṇadharmasya kathanena yuktaḥ - yogyatvena saṅgato bhavati | atra – 'ghañ' pratyayānu- śāsanaprakaraṇe, 'ādeśa' śabde vā, karaṇe'pi ghañakaraṇārtha ko'pi ghañpratyayaḥ, agaṇi- upadiṣṭaḥ, (bahulagrahaṇāt 'upadeśa' ityatra karaṇe ghañ iti bhāvyāt) kārakāṇām- karmādīnām, vaivakṣikatvam - prayoktrabhiprāyanibandhanatvam, śabdoktaṃ nanu -- vaiyākaraṇaiḥ 'vivakṣāta kārakāṇi bhavanti' iti aṅgīkṛtameva khalu, (ataḥ ) kartari praśāsanasya kartṛkāraka bhūte'pi brahmaṇi, karaṇatayā vivakṣā—karaṇa kārakatva tātparya viṣayatvam syāt- yujyata eva || 17 ||

".

3) śrīvedāntācāryaviracitā adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ ātmanyevaṃ parasminnakṛta iti 'bhite' viśvahetutvalakṣye
śāstrakasthāpanīye nirupadhiparamapremayogye prasakte | īdṛktvaṃ syādyathā prakṛtipuruṣayornā'numānādyayogyau
duḥkhāspṛṣṭau ca tāvityatha parakathanaṃ dodhavīti krameṇa || 54 ||
evam - - adhikaraṇacatuṣṭayātmikayā upodghātapeṭikayā, 'akṛta:' iti mite- 'siddha svarūpa : iti śāstrajanyapramāgocare, viśvahetutvalakṣye—jagatkāraṇatvarūpeṇa lakṣaṇena pratipattavye, śāstraka- sthāpanīye --- śāstratarā nirṇeya svayāthātmye, nirupadhiparamapremayogye – abādhitānavadhika| tiśayā- nandavati, parasmin ātmani sarvādhike sarvātmani paramapuruṣe, prasakte – jijñāsyatvopayogidharma- vattayā siddhe sati, atha– dvitīyapeṭikāyām, krameṇa - yathākramaṃ pañcamaṣaṣṭhayoḥ adhikaraṇayo:, īdṛktvam—ādyapeṭikāsiddhaṃ jagatkāraṇatvādikam, yathārham - yathāyogyam, prakṛti - puruṣayoḥ syāt—sāṃkhyasammatayoḥ pradhāna puruṣaśabdavācyayoḥ tattvayoreva abādhena yujyate, tau- pradhāna- puruṣau ca, anumānādyayogyau — anumānādipramāṇāgocarauṃ, duḥkhāspṛṣṭau ca – duḥkhasambandhānaha ca, na-na bhavitumarhataḥ, (kintu) anumānādigocarau, duḥkhasaṃspṛṣṭāveva ca bhavataḥ, iti parakathanam - īdṛśaṃ pareṣāṃ pūrvapakṣavacanam, dodhavīti – viśodhya pariharati || 54 || gauṇekṣā sāhacaryāt sati bahubhavanaprekṣaṇaṃ naiva mukhyaṃ dṛṣṭāntādyairihā''bhātyanumitiracitastādṛśājanma yuktam |

sacchabdastena mūlaprakṛtirmāvikṛtiṃ vyāharedityayuktaṃ
śrutyā'nyeṣāṃ nirodhāt tvadabhimatatiraskāriliṅgādimiśca || 55 ||

sati — sacchabdabodhye vastuni, bahubhavanaprekṣaṇam – bahubhavanaviṣayakaṃ 'bahu syām iti saṅkalparūpaṃ jñānam, mukhyaṃ naiva — anupacaritaṃ naiva bhavitumarham, kintu gauṇameva ; gauṇekṣāsāhacaryāt – tejojalā digatasya sṛṣṭyaunmukhyātmakasya gauṇekṣaṇasya 'tatteja aikṣata' ityevamādinā sahapāṭhāt iha - sadvidyāprakaraṇe, dṛṣṭāntādyaiḥ – 'yathā somyaikena mṛtpiṇḍena' ityādibhiḥ dṛṣṭāntayuktyādibhiḥ, anumitiḥ ābhāti - anumitiḥ prakṛteti bhāsate, (ataḥ) tādṛśāt acitaḥ - ānumānikādeva acetanāt pradhānāt janma - sthūlasya. acetanasya jagataḥ utpattiriti yuktam — upapannaṃ bhavati | tena – uktena hetunā, sacchabda:- 'sadeva' iti prakramoktaḥ 'sat' ityayaṃ śabdaḥ, avikṛtim – svayaṃ vikārānātmikām,

-I

mūlaprakṛtim — ata eva mūlaprakṛtiśabdavācyāmeva, vyāharet — pratipādayet na cetanam, iti ayuktam — evaṃ cintanaṃ saṅgataṃ na bhavati ; śrutyā- 'aitadātmyam' iti ātmapadaghaṭitayā pratyakṣayā upaniṣadvācā, (tathā) tvadabhimatatiraskāriliṅgādibhiśva - yuṣmadabhyupagatārthanirāsa- tātparya : īkṣaṇādibhirjñāpakaiśca anyeṣāṃ nirodhāt – prakṛtyādīnāṃ tajjñāpakaliṅgādīnāṃ vā upasthiteḥ pratibandhāt || 55 ||

jñāte sarva viditamiti bhavatyaikyasiddhayai pratijñā
mṛttatkāryādayaśca traya iha kathitāstasya dṛṣṭāntabhedāḥ | tenā'vyaktānumānaṃ kathitamiti vṛthotkaṇṭhitaṃ hetvanukteḥ
sārūpyādeśva hetorupari parihṛteratra sambhāvanokteḥ || 56 ||

ekatra – ekasmin vastuni, jñāte hi - vijñāte sati hi sarva viditam – jagat sarvaṃ jñātaṃ bhavati, iti pratijñā - evaṃrūpā anumānāṅgatayā jñātā siṣādhayiṣitārthanirdeśarūpā pratijñā upaniṣadi śrutā, (sā ca) aikyasiddhayai bhavati kārya-kāraṇayoḥ abhedapratipattaye, prayojikā bhavati, iha – pratijñātārthopapādane kārye, tasya kārya-kāraṇayorabhedasya ( siṣādhayiṣitasya) mṛt-tatkāryādayaḥ trayaḥ mṛttikā, tajjanyo ghaṭa - śarāvādi:, lohamaṇiḥ, tatkārya lohamayam, (tathā) nakhanikṛntanaṃ, tatkārya kāṣrṇāyasam iti trayo'rthāḥ dṛṣṭāntabhedāḥ – bhinnabhinnāḥ dṛṣṭāntaviśeṣatayā kathitāḥ pratipāditāḥ tena pratijñā - dṛṣṭāntayoḥ kathanena, avyaktānu- mānam – sāṅkhyoktasya pradhānasya anumānameva kathitamiti – uktaprāyam ityāśaṅkanam, vṛthotkaṇṭhitam - niṣphalaṃ kautūhalamātram, (kuta: ?) hetvanukteḥ – anumānapradhānāṅgasya hetu- vākyasya abhāvāt, sārūpyādeśca hetoḥ – prapañcapradhānayoḥ sukha-duḥkha- mohātmakatvena samāna- rūpatvātmakasya liṅgasya tu, upari parihṛteḥ – uttaratra sāṅkhyādhikaraṇe nirākaraṇāt, atra—asmin adhikaraṇe, sambhāvanokteḥ– asambhāvitatvaśaṅkāmātraparihārāt, na pauna- ruktyamiti || 56 ||

ādeśātmasvaśabdaira nitaraśaraṇaistvantadai kyopadeśāt
jīvena svena sāhaṅkaraṇamanahamo'cigaṇasya praveśāt | ekajñānena sarva viditamiti girā sarvatādātmyavācā
śākhā vidyāntarāderapi bahubhavitā viśvavidviśvamūrtiḥ || 57 ||
anitaraśaraṇaiḥ – pratipādyāntaravattayā nirvoḍhumaśakyaiḥ, (ananyathāsiddhairiti yāvat ) ādeśātmasvaśabdaiḥ—'tamādeśam ' 'sa ātmā' 'svamapītaḥ' iti vākyeṣu śrūyamāṇaiḥ etaiḥ

śabdaiḥ (jñāpakībhūtaiḥ), tvaṃ- tadaikyopadeśāt - tvaṃśabdārthasya tacchandārthasya ca 'tattvamasi' iti abhedopadeśāt jñāpakāt, svena – svātmakena, jīvena - jīvātmanā saha, sāhaṅkaraṇam- ahaṅkārapūrvakam – svakartṛtvadhīpūrvakam, anahamaḥ– ahamarthavyatiriktasya, acidgaṇasya – acetana- jātasya, praveśāt–antaranupraveśāt, ekajñānena sarva viditamiti girā - ' yasminnu khalu vijñāte sarvamidaṃ vijñātaṃ bhavati' iti pratijñārūpayā vācā, sarvatādātmyavācā - 'aitadātmyamidaṃ sarvam' ityevaṃrūpayā brahmaṇaḥ sārvātmyapratipādikayā śrutigirā, śākhāvidyāntarāderapi taittirīyākhyavedaśākhāntarāt, tathā daharādividyāntarāt evaṃ smṛtipurāṇāderapi jñāpakāt bahubhavitā - 'bahu syām' iti saṅkalpapūrvaka kartṛtvavān viśvavit – sarvajñaḥ, viśvamūrti:- sarvaśarīrakaḥ paramātmaiva, ‘sat 'padabodhyaḥ ; na sāṅkhyābhimataṃ pradhānam || 57 ||
4) śrīmahācāryaviracitaḥ adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ
evaṃ catussūcyā vedāntavākyānāṃ brahmaṇi prāmāṇyasamarthanarūpaṃ parīkṣaṇīyasthāpanaṃ, tatparikarasthāpanaṃ ca kṛtam ; atha pramāṇaviśeṣaparīkṣā kriyate |
īkṣatyadhikaraṇe - 'sadeva sīmyedamagra āsīt ityatra sacchanda- vācyasya, 'idaṃ' śabdavācyam acetanaṃ prati kāraṇatvena, kāraṇasya ca kāryasājātyāvaśyaṃ bhāvāt, acetanaṃ pradhānameva sacchandapratipādyaṃ kāraṇam-ityāśaṅkaya,
īkṣatiśravaṇāt cidaciccharīrakasya parasya sarvasālakṣaṇyasambhavācca, 'sacchanda-- vācyaḥ, paramātmā' – ityuktam ||

5) śrīmadbhiḥ lakṣmīpuraṃ śrīnivāsācāryaiḥ viracitā nayasaṅgatimālikā vedāntasārasādvidyā vakti prakṛtihetutām |
ityākṣepapratikṣepaḥ syādīkṣatinaye'dhunā || 31 ||
evaṃ bahvayaḥ saṅgatayaḥ proktā mukhavibhedataḥ | tatra mukhyā sagṛhītā śiṣyavikṣepaśāntaye || 32 ||
|| iti īkṣatyadhikaraṇam || 5 ||

ānandamayādhikaraṇam || 1-1-6 ||
1) śārīrakanyāya kalāpasaṅgrahaḥ

vikāravācī mayaḍityamuṣmāt kṣetrajñamānandamayaṃ viśaṅkaya | asambhavāt, prāṇamaye ca hāneḥ, prācuryavācīti nirācakāra || 13 ||
mayaṭ — ānandamayaśabde 'mayaṭ ' pratyayaḥ, vikāravācī 'tasya vikāraḥ' iti vihitavikārārthakaḥ, ityamuṣmāt - evaṃrūpāt hetoḥ, ānandamayam - ānandavallīpaṭhitānandaśabdābhidheyam, kṣetrajñaṃ viśaṅkaya – jīvātmaivetyākṣipya, asambhavāt - jīvātmani ānandamayatvabādhāt prāṇamaye ca ' prāṇamaya' śabde eva, hāneḥ mayaṭo vikārārthatvasya tyāgāt prācuryaca | cīti - 'ānandamaya' śabdaghaṭaka ḥ mayaṭ,

'tatprakṛtavacane mayaṭū' iti sūtreṇānuśiṣṭaḥ san prācuryarūpārthabodhaka eveti, nirācakāra- ānandamayasya jīvasvarūpatvaśaṅkāṃ nirastavān bādarāyaṇaḥ || 13 ||

vikārarūpārthabodhakamayaṭpratyayasyā'pi śravaṇamasti tat — tadebhyo hetubhyaḥ, puccham —'brahma pucchaṃ pratiṣṭhā' iti paṭhitaṃ pucchameva brahma bhavet – lokenā'viditaṃ niravayavaṃ nirvikāraṃ, svataḥ śuddhaṃ ca sat 'brahma' padavācyaṃ bhavitumarhati | ānandamayastu na brahma-ānandamaya- śabdābhihitastu arthaḥ brahma na bhavitumarhaḥ | sa ca - ānandamayastu, annamayavat — annamaya- -śabdābhihitaḥ artha ivaṃ, bahiḥ kośaḥ parasmin brahmaṇi avataraṇaṃ prati upāyabhūtaḥ bāhyasampuṭa eva, kathyate śrutyā pratipādyate || 18 ||

pulliṅgāntapradhānaprakṛta paravaco rūpaṇaṃ cāpyananyaiḥ
ślokāḥ pucchaṃ na cāsshuḥ svarasamapi ca naḥ sūtramanyo'ntaro na | ṣaṣṭhyādivāṃśabhāvānniravadhikatayā rūpaṇādānurūpyāt
paryāyatvādananyātmakavacanatayā pucchahetuḥ parāsta: || 19 ||
pulliṅgāntapradhāna prakṛtaparavacaḥ – pulliṅgāntam, prādhānyena prastutārthaparaṃ ca yat “so'kāmayata' iti vākyasthaṃ 'saḥ' iti padam, (tathā) ananyaiḥ api – ānandamayāt svasmāt anatiriktairapi śiraḥ - pakṣa- pucchādibhiḥ avayavaiḥ, rūpaṇaṃ ca-ānandamayasyaiva nirūpaṇamapi, (evam) ślokāśca - annamayādayaḥ etāvadantāḥ ślokā api pucchaṃ na ca āhuḥ -pucchaṃ naiva vadanti khalu ! (etat avacanam ), naḥ – ānandamayasyaiva brahmatvaṃ vadatā- masmākam, svarasam— anupacaritārthapratipādakam, (tathā) sūtramapi ca – ānandamayādhikaraṇa- sūtramapi, (evam) anyo'ntaraḥ na - pūrvapūrva paryāye iva ānandamayaparyāye 'anyo'ntara ātmā' ityasyābhāva:, ete'rthāḥ ānandamayasyaiva paramātmatvabodhakāḥ | ṣaṣṭhayādiśca – 'tasyaiṣa : ' ityatra 'tasya' iti ṣaṣṭhī, śloke ānandamayānuktiḥ, jñātatvam brahmaṇo niravayavatā, śārīrātmaśravaṇam, ānandamayasya śodhanīyatoktiḥ, vikārārthako mayaṭ ityevaṃrūpaḥ, pucchahetuḥ - pucchasyaiva brahmatva- sthāpakatayā pūrvapakṣibhiḥ pratipāditaḥ arthastu aṃśabhāvāt – aṃśāṃśibhāvasya bodhanāt, niravadhikatayā_ajñātena niratiśayānandarūpatvena mayaṭā prācuryabodhanāt rūpaṇāt- niravayave'pi buddhisaukaryārthaṃ śiraḥ pucchādyavayavasya rūpaṇamātratva svīkārāt, ānurūpyāt- paramātmani tadanurūpasya prasannatāpādanātmakasya śodhanasya abhyupagamāt paryāyatvāt - brahma- ānandamayaśabdayoḥ samānārthopasthāpakatvāt, ananyātmakavacanatayā – (ata eva ) anyātma- katvāvedakavacanarūpatvāsambhavāt parāstaḥ- ābhāsatām ( hetvābhāsatām ) āpāditaḥ || 19 ||

3) adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ
mukhyekṣā yadyabhīṣṭā bhavatu taducite sā punarjīvatatve sadvidyāyāṃ hi śabdaistribhirupari satastasya jīvaikyamuktam | ityūhādujjihānaṃ praśamayitumatha prastuto viśvakartā

jīvasyā'pyantarātmā 'nirupadhika 'mahānandathuḥ sthāpyate'tra || 58 ||

īkṣā—'tadaikṣata' ityatra pratipāditā svasya bahubhavanopayoginī īkṣaṇakriyā, mukhyā anupacaritā, cetanāsādhāraṇī saṅkalpātmikā, abhīṣṭā yadi -vivakṣitā bhavati cet, sā- vilakṣaṇā īkṣaṇakriyā, taducite - tādṛśekṣaṇasambandhārhe, jīvatacce punaḥ - puruṣapadavācye jīve vidyamānaiva bhavatu –aṅgīkriyatāṃ nāma | hi yataḥ sadvidyāyām – sadvidyāntaḥ pātibhiḥ, tribhiḥ śabdaiḥ – 'tat tvam, asi' ityetaiḥ tribhiḥ padaiḥ, upari-uparyupari navakṛtvaḥ, tasya sataḥ – 'sadeva somye 'ti prastutasya 'sat 'padābhidheyasya, jīvaikyam – jīvabhūta' tvaṃ' padārthā- bhinnatvam uktam– abhyāsena pratipāditam iti – anayā rītyā, ūhāt — aucitya - cintanarūpāṃ yuktimāśritya ujjihānam - cidaciduttīrṇasya brahmaṇaḥ jagatkāraṇatvaniṣedhe udyuktam pūrvapakṣiṇam, praśamayitum - akṣubdhatayā abhyupagateśvaraṃ kartum, atha — paścāttane, atra – ānandamayādhikaraṇe, prastutaḥ - pūrva jagatkāraṇatvena lakṣitaḥ, nirupadhika mahānandathuḥ - svābhāvikaparamānandamahodadhiḥ, viśvakartā—jagatsraṣṭā, jīvasyā'pi - kevalaṃ saṅkalpasambandhāsya cetanasyāpi, antarātmā - antaḥsthaḥ san dhārakaḥ niyantā śeṣī ca puruṣottamaḥ, sthāpyate— nyāyavyutpādanapūrvakaṃ nirṇīyate || 58 ||

dṛṣṭaḥ pūrva vikāre mayaḍiti carame'pyevamastvityayuktaṃ
madhye tadbhaṅgadṛṣṭeḥ pracuramiha vadet pratyayo'nyasya bādhāt ānandaprācurī ca prakṛtaparasukha | lpatva labdhāvadhitvāt
duḥkhālpatvānapekṣā paraduritabhida: śāsitustadvirodhāt || 59 ||
pūrvam—prāthamike ‘annarasamaya’śabde, vikāre maya - vikārārthabodhakatvena anuśiṣṭaḥ mayaṭ pratyaya eva, dṛṣṭa iti - jñāta iti hetunā jñāpakabhūtena, carame'pi ante śrūyamāṇe 'ānandamaya' śabde'pi, evamastu iti — vikārārthaka eva bhavatviti āśaṅkanam, ayuktam- abādhitaṃ na bhavati | madhye – ' prāṇamayādi śabde, tadbhaṅgadṛṣṭeḥ mayaṭi vikārārthakatā viccheda-

darśanāt, (tasmāt ) iha — ānandamayaśabde, pratyayaḥ - mayaṭpratyayaḥ, anyasya bādhāt- prācuryetarasyā'rthasya ānandamaye ayogāt, pracuram – svaprakṛtyarthaprācuryavantamevārtham, vadet- bodhayet | ānandaprācurī capracurānandavattvaṃ ca duḥkhālpatvānapekṣā - svātmānaṃ prati avadhitayā svasamānādhikaraṇaduḥ khanyūnatāvalambi na bhavati ; prakṛtaparasukhālpatvalabdhāvadhi- - tvāt — prastutāḥ ye manuṣyādyāḥ prajāpatiparyantāḥ parabrahmaṇo'nye, tadgatena sukhanyūnatvena svasya avadhilābhāt , paraduritabhidaḥ – anyadīyānāṃ duḥkhamūlānāṃ pāpānāṃ nirmūlayituḥ, śāsituḥ - -sarvaniyantuḥ puruṣottamasya, tadvirodhāt - alpaduḥkhavattāyāḥ bādhāt || 59 ||

ātmā tasyaiṣa evetyuditamanitarātmatvamasyaiva vaktuṃ
śārīroktiśca tasminnakhilatanutayā syādasaṅkocavṛttiḥ | śodhyatvaṃ tattadarthānuguṇamiti vibhostatprasādyatvamātraṃ
prāpye'smin prāptirūpā paravida upasaṅkrāntirānanda sindhau || 60 |

asya - paramātmanaḥ, anitarātmatvam - niyantrantararāhityam, vaktumeva — pratipādayitu- meva, (na tu cidacitoriva ātmāntaraṃ kathayitum ), tasyaiṣa eva ātmā iti - (ānandamaya- prakaraṇe'pi) ‘tasyaiṣa eva śārīra ātmā' iti, uditam — śrutau āmnātam | tasmin — (aśarīratvenā'pi śrutigamye) paramātmani, śārīroktiśva - 'śārīra ātmā' iti śarīrasambandhitā- varṇanamapi, akhilatanutayā - tasya 'sarvātmā' iti śrutipratipannena sarvaśarīrakatvena, asaṅkoca- vṛttiḥ–anupacāreṇa mukhyameva, śodhyatvam —–śodhanātvam | tattadarthānuguṇamiti - dvastusvarūpānubandhi ; na tu sarvatra ekavidhameveti hetoḥ, vibhoḥ tat - sarvaniyantuḥ sarvapāvanasya ānandamayasya parabrahmaṇaḥ sambandhi śodhanā hetvam, prasādyatvamātram - tadgatanigrahasaṅkalpadūrīkaraṇena kevalaṃ prasannatāpādanarūpameva ; na tu cidacitoriva mālinyadūrīkaraṇātmakam | prādhye – mukta - prāpyatayā śrutipratipanne ānanda sindhau – niratiśaya mahānandajaladhau, asmin — paramātmani, paravidaḥ paramātmopāsakasya, upasaṅkrāntiḥ - 'ānandamayamātmānamupaṅkrāmati' iti śrutyukta- - mupasaṅkramaṇaṃ tu, prāptirūpā - pūrva mananubhūta paramātmakarma kā nubhavātmakameveti, (sarva susthameveti || 60 || nirdehe'sminniraṃśe na hi bhavati śiraḥ- pakṣa- pucchādi phlaptiḥ tasmāt 'pucchaṃ pratiṣṭhe' tyuditamiha parabrahma 'bhātīti cenna | soḍhā pucchatva' klaptiryadi kathamitarannānumanyeta kalpyaṃ brahmaṇyātmapratiṣṭhāvacanamanitarādhāratā khyāpanāya || 61 ||

nirdehe — 'aśarīram' ityādiśrutyā śarīrarahitatvena śrutisiddhe, niraṃśe – 'niṣkalam " ityādiśrutyā (niraṃśatvena) avayavarāhityena śrutigamye ca asmin – parasmin brahmaṇi, śiraḥ-pakṣa-pucchādiklaptiḥ– mūrdha - pāśca vālādyavayavānāṃ kalpanā, hi yataḥ, na bhavati — naiva saṅgatā bhavati| tasmāt — tenaiva hetunā, iha — ānandamayaprakaraṇe, pucchaṃ pratiṣṭhetyuditam - 'brahma pucchaṃ pratiṣṭhā' iti śrutyupāttaṃ vastveva, parabrahma parabrahmaśabdavācyamiti bhāti- śrutyarthacintakānāṃ sphurati, iti cet — evamāśaṅkanam, nana yauktikam, pucchatvaklaptiḥ— niravayave'pi vastuni puccharūpatvakalpanam, yadi soḍhā - yuktihīnamapi marṣitaṃ yadi, itarat - pucchātiriktaṃ śiraḥ - pakṣādikam katham – kaṃ vā hetumavalambya kalpyam – kalpanīyatvena, na - kaṃvā anumanyeta — aṅgīkṛtaṃ na bhavet, (aṅgīkṛtameva syāt aviśeṣāt ) | brahmaṇi-ānanda- mayaśabdokte paramātmani, ātmapratiṣṭhāvacanam – svaṃ prati pratiṣṭhātvakathanam, anitarādhāratā- khyāpanāya – sve mahimna pratiṣṭhitatvadṛḍhīkaraṇāyaiva || 61 ||

dehitvataḥ – 'hiraṇyaśmaśruḥ, hiraṇyakeśaḥ, āpraṇakhāt' ityādyuktanakhaśmaśru keśādyava- yavonnītāt saśarīratvāt jñāpakāt maṇḍalamadhyavartī – 'ya eṣo'ntarāditye' iti āditya- maṇḍalāntarvartitayā śrutipratipannaḥ puruṣaḥ, puṇyottara:- atiśayitasukṛtaviśeṣaśālī, kaścit cetana eva – pratyagātmaiva (jīva ) kaścana ; na tu paramātmā, ityevaṃ śaṅkām – etādṛśīm ākṣepabuddhim, lokeśatādyaiḥ – loka kāmeśatvāpahatapāpmatvādirūpaiḥ, paramātmadharmaiḥ paramātmetarā - vṛttibhiḥ prakaraṇapaṭhitaiḥ dharme, utsārayate sma - bhagavān bādarāyaṇo dūrīcakāra || 14 ||

.

kaṃ pibatīti—jalaṃ pibatīti vyutpattyā, nālam – kamalanālam, kapirbhavati - ' kapi' śabdavācyaṃ bhavitumarhati | tasmin kapiśabdabodhye kamalanāle, yasya – yasya vastunaḥ, āsaḥ āsanam – 'āsa' padabodhyaḥ sthitiviśeṣaḥ svābhāvikaḥ, tat - nālasthaṃ vastu, manīṣiṇaḥ brahmanandyādayaḥ śrutyarthajñāḥ kapyāsam– 'kapyāsa ' padavācyam uśanti - abhiprayanti | idam – uktayā rītyā 'kapyāsa 'śabdapratipāditam, ujjvalam – śobhā tiśayo- petam, udāram— atiśayena sphītam, sarojam - puṇḍarīkama, īśvarasya – bhagavato nārāyaṇasya, cakṣurnidarśanam – nayanayoḥ upamānatayā, udīritam – 'tasya yathā kapyāsaṃ puṇḍarīkameva makṣiṇī' iti śrutyā bodhitam || 21 ||

kamudakamiha pyāso yasyāsanaṃ yadi vodbhavaḥ
kavibhiruditaṃ kapyāsaṃ tadbhīrajalodayāt | śiśiramadhuraṃ śrīmaddāmodarākṣinidarśanaṃ
bhavati nalinaṃ luptākārastvapiḥ pihitādivat || 22 ||

kam – 'kapyāsa’śabdaghaṭaka 'ka' śabdavācyo'rthaḥ, udakam – jalameva | iha – jale,

āsanam — sthitiḥ yasya — yādṛśasya arthasya sambhavati, yadi vā - athavā, iha — jale, pyāsaḥ – ' kapyāsa' śabdaghaṭaka 'pyāsa'- śabdārthabhūtaḥ, udbhavaḥ – utpattirūpaḥ artho vā yasya – yādṛśārthasya sambhavati, - jale tat- avasthitaṃ, jale utpannaṃ vā ambhojarūpaṃ vastu, kavibhiḥ - kāntadarśibhiḥ ṛṣibhiḥ, kapyāsam—'kapyāsaṃ puṇḍarīkamevamakṣiṇī' iti śrutistha' kapyāsa 'śabdabodhyamiti, uditam - uktam | gabhīrajalodayāt – agādhe ambhasi utpantyā sthityā ca śiśiramadhuram- nikhilatāpanivāraṇakṣamātyantaśītalatva sakalendriyākarṣaṇakṣamā tiśayitaramaṇīyatvābhyāṃ pūrṇam, śrīmat — atiśayitakāntisampannam nalinam – (komalasukumārataram ) ambhojam, dāmoda- rākṣinidarśana bhavati — śrīyaśodākṛta vātsalyabandha vaśīkṛtasya, bhagavataḥ puruṣottamasya akṣṇoḥ upamānaṃ bhavitumarhati | api ḥ— 'pyāsa' śabdaghaṭaka ḥ 'api' ityayam upasargaḥ, luptākārastu - akārāśravaṇavāneva | pihitādivat yathā apihitādiśabde, 'vaṣṭi bhāgurirallopam' ityakāra- lopena pihitādirūpasiddhiḥ ; tadvat || 22 ||

pyāsaḥ – 'kapyāsa 'śabdaghaṭaka 'pyāsa' śabdārthabhūtam

kapyāsaṃ bhānubimbaṃ haribhajanapadaṃ hṛtsarojaṃ ca yadvat tadvaccopāsakākṣidvayamiti tu na sattatra yatpuṇḍarīkam | gauṇaṃ tasyā'kṣiṇī ityubhayapada nirākāṅkṣantānādareṇā-
. pyadhyāhāraḥ samānādhikṛtapadagatityatirūrdhvaṃ taduktiḥ || 23 ||

kapyāsam – 'kapi’śabdavācyasya sūryasya adhiṣṭhānabhūtam, bhānubimbam - sūryamaṇḍalam, (tathā) hṛtsarojaṃ ca dehinaḥ hṛdayakamalamapi, yadvat – yathā, haribhajanapadam - bhagavadupāsana- sthānamiti śāstrasiddham, tadvat tathaiva upāsakākṣidvayaṃ ca - upāsakacetanasambandhinetra- dvayamapi, haribhajanapadam - paramātmopāsanasthānam, (iti 'tasya yathā kapyāsaṃ puṇḍarīkamevama kṣiṇī' iti śrutyarthaḥ) iti tu na sat - evamarthavarṇanaṃ tu atyantamasamīcīnam yat - yasmāt kāraṇāt, tatra – hṛdayakośe, puṇḍarīkam - puṇḍarīkapadam gauṇam – puṇḍarīkasāmyanibandhana- miti hetoḥ amukhyam, guṇasāmyādāgatam, 'tasyā'kṣiṇī' ityubhayapada (yoḥ ) - śrutisthayoḥ 'tasya' 'akṣiṇī' ityanayoḥ dvayoḥ padayoḥ avayavāvayavibhāvena mitho'nvitārtha kayoḥ vidyamānāyāḥ, nirākāṅkṣatāyāḥ – itarapadanairapekṣyeṇa arthabodhakatāyāḥ, anādareṇa – anādararūpadoṣeṇa saha, (padayoranyatrā''kāṅkṣā kalpanāt ) adhyāhāro'pi - ' upāsanasthānam' ityasya, (tathā) 'upāsa- kasya' ityasya ca aśrutasya adhyāharaṇarūpo'pi doṣa: samasti (tathā) samānādhikṛtapada- viśeṣyatvaviśeṣaṇatvāharthavācinoḥ samānavibhaktayantayoḥ 'kapyāsa-puṇḍarīka'- padayoḥ yā gatiḥ svārasikī pravṛtti ( bhinnapravṛttinimittakatvaikaviśeṣyānvayitvarūpā ) tasyāḥ parityāgo'pi doṣa:, (tathā) ūrdhvam – kapyāsavākyādanantaram, taduktiḥ — uttaratra akṣi- vidyāyāṃ satyāmeva, tataḥ pūrvameva antarādityavidyāyāṃ bhagavadupāsanasthānatayā upāsakasyākṣṇoḥ 'evamakṣiṇī' ityaneneoktiḥ doṣa: ; paunaruktyāt iti doṣāḥ || 23 ||

kacijjalpati markaṭasya jaghanaṃ kapyāsamakṣṇorayaṃ
dṛṣṭāntaḥ kathamiṣyate bhagavataḥ satyeva gatyantare | sāmānādhikaraṇyamatra padayoḥ prāptaṃ kutastyajyate ?

padmaṃ cedviśinaṣṭi tatsadṛśi tacchabdastu gauṇa stava' || 24 ||

markaṭasya vānarasya, jaghanam - pṛṣṭhabhāgam, kapyāsam — śrutistha‘kapyāsa’śabdabodhya- tvena kaścit jalpati – gūḍhasya śāstrakāvyādidṛṣṭasya ahīnasya aupamyasya anālocakaḥ vyākhyāti, (yatkiñcideva vadati ; na tu sarvātmanā yogyamevā'rtham) | gatyantare — prakārāntare upāyāntare vā, satyeva – vidyamāne eva, bhagavataḥ sarvātiśāyiśrīpūrṇasya nārāyaṇasya, akṣṇoḥ – nayanayoḥ ayam - adhastana pradeśasambandhī jaghanarūpo'rthaḥ heyatayā prasiddhaḥ, dṛṣṭāntaḥ–upamānatāmāpanna iti, kathamiṣyate ? - hīnopamānatvāt abhyupagantuṃ kathaṃ śakyate ?

atra asmin vākye, padayoḥ kapyāsa - puṇḍarīkapadayoḥ, prāptam – samānavibhaktikatayā svabhāva- siddham abādhitaṃ ca, sāmānādhikaraṇyam — aneka viśeṣaṇa viśiṣṭa kārthabodhakatvam, kutaḥ tyajyate ?–kena hetunā upekṣyate ? tacchabdaḥ - sa śabdaḥ kapyāsaśabdaḥ, padmam – puṇḍarīka- rūpamartham, viśinaṣṭi cet — kapipṛṣṭha nirūpitasādṛśya viśiṣṭatayā bodhayati cet ! tadā tava - tava pakṣe, tatsadṛśi —tadaupamyavatyarthe, tacchabdaḥ —tadvācakaḥ kapyāsaśabdaḥ, gauṇaḥ - amukhyavṛtta eva prasajyate || 24 ||

daravikasitaṃ kaścit kapyāsamāha gatistviyaṃ
bhavatu bhagavaccakṣurdṛṣṭāntapaṅkajasaṅgatā | tadapi ca na sattasminnarthe yato na tu kutracit
tadavayavaśaḥ saṃhatyā vā padaṃ vyudapadyata || 25 ||

kaścit anyaḥ kaścana kapyāsaśratyarthavarṇanapravṛttaḥ. daravikasitam – īṣadvikāsaṃ sampannam, kapyāsamāha — ' kapyāsa ' śabdārtha iti varṇayati, iyaṃ tu gatiḥ– pūrvāpekṣayā vilakṣaṇā iyamarthavarṇanarītiḥ param, bhagavaccakṣurdṛṣṭāntapaṅkajasaṅgatā bhavatu — yadyapi bhagavannayanopamāna (svena parigṛhīte)bhūte padme anvayayogyataivā'sti (padmasya īṣadvikā sitvāt ) tadapi - tathāpi, tacca tathā arthavarṇanamapi, na sat - naiva samīcīnaṃ bhavati, yataḥ -- yasmāt kāraṇāt, tat padam - kapyāsapadam, kutracit – laukika - vaidikādinighaṇṭuṣu pada- tadarthavyutpādanaparatayā śiṣṭapari- gṛhīteṣu, tasmin arthe – īṣadvikasitarūpe arthe, avayavaśo vā yaugikavyutpattyā vā saṃhatyā vā—samudāyavyutpattyā vā, (rūḍhyā vā) na tu vyudapadyata naiva vyutpattimalabhata || 25 || ṣaṭsvartheṣu samīriteṣu caturāḥ kapyāsavācastrayaḥ
gambhīrāmbha iti prakṛtya bhagavadrāmānujāṅgīkṛtāḥ |
tatsvīkāra bahiṣkṛtāstaditare heyāstrayastadvidā

ślokaiḥ ṣaḍbhiramībhiritthamuditaḥ so'yaṃ vibhāgo mayā || 26 ||
samīriteṣu – spaṣṭabhukteṣu ṣaṭsu kapyāsavācaḥ artheṣu – ṣaṭprakāreṣu kapyāsaśabdārtheṣu madhye, trayaḥ – 'kapistvādityaḥ ' 'nālaṃ kapiḥ ' 'kamudakam ' iti ślokatrayoktāḥ triprakārāḥ arthāḥ, 'gambhīrāmbha' iti prakṛtya – ( śrīmati vedārthasaṅgrahe) 'gambhīrāmbhassamudbhūte tyā dinā prakramya, bhagavadrāmānujāṅgīkṛtāḥ – śrīmadbhiḥ rāmānujāyaiḥ abhyupagatāḥ santaḥ, caturāḥ abhihitasyaiaupamyasya yogyāḥ puṣṭidāḥ santi | taditare - uktātiriktāḥ 'kapyāsaṃ bhānu' 'kaścijjalpati' 'daravikasitam' iti ślokatrayapratipāditāḥ, trayaḥ - triprakārāḥ arthāḥ,

tatsvīkāra bahiṣkṛtāḥ — bhāṣyakārāṇām abhyupagamāt dūrīkṛtāḥ, 'taiḥ asvīkṛtāḥ | heyāḥ- (ata eva ) varjyā:, tadvidā mayā bhagavadāmānujābhiprāyavedinā (vātsyavaradāryeṇa ) mayā, ayaṃ saḥ vibhāgaḥ–tatsvīkṛtāsvīkṛnārthānāṃ vivicya kṛto'yaṃ pradarśanavyāpāraḥ, amībhiḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ ślokaiḥ pūrvoktaiḥ ṣaṭsaṃkhyākaiḥ ślokaḥ, itthamuditaḥ anena prakāreṇa prakaṭitaḥ || 26 ||

3) adhikaraṇamārāvaliḥ bhūyiṣṭhānantapuṇyopacayabalasamudbuddhapūrvoktabhūmnāṃ
śakrādinyāyataḥ syāt triguṇatanubhṛtāmīśvarāṇāṃ pravāhaḥ | tannā'karmotthadivyākṛtijanimahimā śāsitā sarvapuṃsāṃ
nityaśrīrtraghnabimbe śruta iti sa ya ityukta evaiṣa ekaḥ || 62 ||

bhūyiṣṭhānām – atyantamatiśayitānām, anantānām – gaṇayitumaśakyānām, puṇyā- nām sukṛtaviśeṣāṇa m, upacayabalena -- ādhikyagatena śaktiviśeṣeṇa, samudbuddhapūrvokta- bhūmnām — phalajananayogya sraṣṭṛtvādimāhātmyavatām, triguṇatanubhṛtām — sattvarajastamomaya viśiṣṭa- dehadhāriṇām, īśvarāṇām – nikhilajagadīśitṛtvayogyānāṃ puruṣaviśeṣāṇāmeva pravāhaḥ— nirantarānuvartamānaikajātīya paramparā, śakrādinyāyataḥ – pratimanvantaraṃ samānaiśvaryavatpuruṣaviśeṣasya bhinnabhinnasyā'pi trilokādhipatitvena anuvṛttirūpaḥ purāṇādisiddho yo nyāyaḥ tadanusāreṇa, syāt – siddhayedeva; (sārvakālika eka eveśvaro bhavediti tu na nirbandha : ;)

tat na –uktamāśaṅkanaṃ na yuktimat akarmotthādivyākṛtijanimahimā karmānadhīnā- prākṛtatejomayamaṅgalavigraha| virbhāvaiśvaryaviśeṣavān, sarvapuṃsāṃ śāsitā – nikhilānāṃ cetanānāṃ svābhāvika niyantṛtvavān nityaśrīḥ – trikālānapāyilakṣmyāśrayaḥ (parama: puruṣa: ) braghna- bimbe – ādityamaṇḍale vidyamānatayā śruta iti – 'ya eṣo'ntarāditye' iti śrutyā adhigata iti hetoḥ, sa ya ityuktaḥ - taittirīye'pi sa ya eṣo'ntarāditye ' iti pratipāditaḥ | (ataḥ) eṣaḥ -- eṣo'ntariti śrutaḥ nikhilaniyantā paramapuruṣaḥ, eka eva sarvadeśasarvakāla sarvāvasthāsu abhinna eva ; ( na tu īśvaraparamparā iti) || 62 ||

sarvebhyaḥ kalmaṣebhyo hyudita iti vadatyantarāditya vidya|
tasmāccheṣābhyanujñānayata iha 'vibhoḥ' puṇyayogo'stu, maivam | āmnātoṃ'nanyaśāsyaḥ svavaśaparaphalaḥ sādhunā naiva bhūyān
syāt puṇye lakṣmayogādapi na sukṛtamityādinā pāpmaśabdaḥ ||63||

1 vibhau -bha, ā. S B II-56

antarāditya vidyā- 'ya eṣo'ntarāditye hiraṇmayaḥ puruṣaḥ' iti śrutyādirūpā upaniṣat sarvebhyaḥ kalmaṣebhyo hyudita iti – (paramātmānam ) aspṛṣṭasamasta pāpaḥ iti khala, vadati – 'sa eka sarvebhyaḥ pāpmabhya uditaḥ' iti vākyena pratipādayati | tasmāt — kevalaṃ pāpasambandhasyaiva niṣiddhatvāt, śeṣābhyanujñānayataḥ – 'viśeṣaniṣedhe śeṣābhyanujñānam' iti tāntrikā bhyupagatāt nyāyāt, iha—asyāṃ vidyāyām, vibhoḥ - paramātmanaḥ, puṇyayogaḥ - puṇyasambandhaḥ, astu —abhyupagataḥ syānnāma, (tataśca puṇyasambandhavān jīva eva antararāditya vidyā pratipādyaḥ iti) | maivam – ayaṃ pakṣaḥ nocita:, (yataḥ) ananyaśāsyaḥ - ( ayaṃ sarveśvaraḥ ) anyadīya- śāsanāviṣayaḥ svavaśaparaphalaḥ – svādhīnānyadīyeṣṭāniṣṭaphalaśca iti āmnātaḥ - śruti- smṛtyādiṣu pratipāditaḥ | (tathā) sādhunā - puṇyena karmaṇā, epaḥ- paramātmā, na bhūyān syāt - vṛddhiṃ na prāpnuyāt iti ca, āmnātaḥ - 'na karmaṇā vardhate' ityādiśrutyuktaśca, puṇye'pi – sukṛtaśabdābhidheye karmaṇyapi, lakṣmayogāt - aniṣṭaphalakatvarūpasya pāpalakṣaṇasya saṅgamāt, pāpmaśabdaḥ–pāpabodhakaḥ śabdaḥ, puṇyespi – puṇyātmake'pyarthe bodhakatayā vartata eva | (ayaṃ cārthaḥ) na sukṛtamityādinā - 'na sukṛtaṃ na duṣkṛtam, sarve pāpmāno'to nivartante iti sukṛta- duṣkṛte ubhe apyuddiśya 'pāma' śabdam prayogavatā śrutivākyena sidhyati ||63|| prakhyātaṃ śuddhasatvaṃ kimapi tadanaghaṃ dravyamavyaktato'nyat

}-

tadrūpaṃ rūpamaiśa divi kanati tathā śeṣa - śeṣāśa 'nāde:' | nityaṃ tat sūrisevyaṃ parataramajahatsvasvabhāvaḥ sa devaḥ

svabhāvamajahadeva san, sūrisevyam - nityamūribhirnirantarānubhāvyam parataram - sarvasmāt parasmāt param, nityam – sanātanaṃ sadātanaṃ ca tat svakīyaṃ śuddhasatvamayaṃ rūpam, puṃsām - anāditaḥ saṃsaratāṃ cetanānām, saṃsāraśāntyai – saṃsāravimocanāya vyūhapūrvaiḥ vibhāgaiḥ – vyūha- vibhava-arcā- hārdātmakaiḥ, svakīyasthitiviśeṣaiḥ, vipariṇamayati- vaividhyena pariṇataṃ karoti || 64 || dehatvāt saptadhātu trimalamaghabhavaṃ duḥkhakṛnnāśayuktaṃ

sāṃśatvā 'deva' hetoriti yadi, tadasaddharmi' mānena bādhāt' | bādhaḥ śāstraikavedye kvacidapi na bhavedanyathā'tiprasaṅgāt
yattu svecchāvatā reṣvabhinayati tadapyāsuropaplavārtham ||65||

( bhagavato divyaṃ rūpam ) saptadhātu – 'raktāsṛṅmāṃsamedo'sthimajjāśuklāni dhātavaḥ ' ityuktadhātusaptakapariṇatam, trimalam – trividhamalasambaddham, aghabhavam - puṇyapāpātmaka karmāyatta- svarūpasiddhimat | duḥkhakṛt – (dehināṃ ) duḥkhajanakam, dehatvāt hetoḥ – etādṛśasādhyā vinā- bhūtāt dehatvāt jñāpakadharmāt (tathā) nāśayuktaṃ ca dhvaṃsānvayi ca sāṃśatvādeḥ hetoḥ sāvayavatva - dṛśyatvādirūpāt nāśāvinābhāvino dharmājjñāpakāt iti yadi - evamucyate cet, tadasat – dehatva sāṃśatvādinā saptadhātupariṇatatva - vināśitvādeḥ sādhanamityetat hetvābhāsadūṣitam | dharmimānena bhagavadvigrahasādhakena āgametihāsādinā pramāṇena, bādhāt- virodhāt sādhyatvābhimatadharmābhāvavattāyāḥ pakṣe niścayāt iti vā | śāstrakavedye— śāstramātreṇa nirṇaye kvacidapi – kasminnapyarthe, bādhaḥ – śāstratareṇa pramāṇābhāsena abhāvapramā, na bhavet — naiva siddhā bhavati | anyathā - pramāṇābhāsenā'pi pramāṇamūrdhanyaśāstra- siddhārthabādhābhyupagame, atiprasaṅgāt – vedapratipādyātmāstitvāderapi pratyakṣādinā bādhāpatteḥ | svecchāvatāreṣu –svasaṅkalpamātrādhīneṣu vibhavāvatāreṣu rāmādirūpeṣu yattu – priyajana viyogādi- janitaṃ duḥkhādikaṃ tu, abhinayati – svakīyakriyāsu, svavākyādiṣu vā lokāya pradarśayati tadapi tādṛśaṃ pradarśanamapi, āsuropaplavārtham - asura prakṛtimatāṃ kaṃsādīnāṃ vināśanārtham | ( te hi ! bhagavadrūpeṣu mānuṣabuddhayā pravṛtaścit tannāśanaṃ sulabhamiti ||65||

sa ekaḥ' iti ānandamayasya ādityamaṇḍalāntarvartinaśca ekasvāvagamāt, ānandamayaḥ jīva eva – ityāśaṅkaya,

antarāditya vidyāyāmeva, apahatapāpmatvādīnāṃ jīveṣvasambhāvitadharmāṇāṃ śravaṇāt, paramātmano'pi karacaraṇādiyuktā prākṛtanityavigrahasadbhāvācca, ādityamaṇḍalāntarvartī paramātmeti, ānandamayo'pi tadabhinnaḥ paramātmā iti siddhāntitam ||

5) nayasaṅgatimālikā
sa yaścāyamiti śrutyā pūrvādhikṛtiśeṣataḥ | antarṇaye puṇyajīvakāraṇatvamapodyate ||34||
|| iti antaradhikaraṇam ||7||

ākāśādhikaraṇam || 1-1-8 ||
prāṇādhikaraṇaṃ (ca) 1) śārīrakanyāya kalāpa saṅgrahaḥ
ākāśa ityeva viśeṣanāmnā sa eva tu syājjagatāṃ nidānam | prāṇo'thavetyetadapācakāra bhūtābhisaṃveśamukhaiśca liṅgaiḥ || 15||
ākāśa iti - 'sarvāṇi havā.... ākāśādeva' iti śrutigatena, viśeṣanāmnā- ākāśatvarūpāsādhāraṇadharmapravṛttinimittakena śabdaviśeṣeṇa jñāpakībhūtena, jagatāṃ nidānam - sakalajagadādikāraṇaṃ vastu, sa eva tu syāt - asandehena ākāśa eva bhavitumarhati; nānyat, athavā – yadvā, prāṇaḥ – 'prāṇamevā'bhisaṃviśanti' iti śrutyuktaḥ prāṇa eva vā jagatāṃ nidānam — sakalajagatkāraṇaṃ syāt ityetat – evaṃ rūpamāśaṅkanam bhūtābhisaṃveśamukhaiśca- 'abhisaṃviśanti' iti tatraivoktaiḥ sarvabhūtalayādhāratādibhiḥ, liṅgaiḥ – jñāpakībhūtaiḥ arthaḥ apācakāra - sūtrakāro bādarāyaṇaḥ dūrīkṛtavān || 15||

2) adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ
itthaṃ vidyātrayeṇa sthiracara cidaciddehinaḥ sarvahetoḥ

avyaktājjīvavargādapi samadhikatā yadyapi syāt tathāpi | utthānadvārabhedāt kramata iha mṛdūpa' krama krūraniṣṭhān adhyāye'sminnirundhannadhikaraṇagaṇaistadguṇānudgṛṇāti || 66 ||

ittham - - uktaprakāreṇa, vidyātrayeṇa sadvidyā - ānandamaya vidyā - antarādityavidyārūpa- vidyā tritayena tribhiradhikaraṇairvicāritena sthiracaracidaciddehinaḥ — sthāvarajaṅgamātmakatacenā- cetanavargaśarīrakasya, sarvahetoḥ - sarva prati nimittopādānatvātmaka sarvavidhakāraṇatvavataḥ bhagavataḥ- parasya brahmaṇaḥ, avyaktāt – avyaktaśabdavācyapradhānāpekṣayā, jīvavargādapi –baddhamuktanityā -- tmakatrividhacetanavargāpekṣayā ca samadhikatā aunnatyarūpaṃ vailakṣaṇyam, yadyapi syāt vastutaḥ siddhayatyeva | tathāpi - tallāmasatve'pi, iha - śārīrakaśāstra, evaṃsiddhe brahmaṇi vā, utthāna-- dvārabhedāt – pūrvapakṣiṇāṃ pratyavasthāna (śaṅkā ) nidānabhūtasyārthasya bailakṣaṇyāt, kramataḥ – aspaṣṭa- spaṣṭa-chāyānusāryādiśrutivākya cintanakrameṇa pratyavasthitān, mṛdūpakrama krūraniṣṭhān - vādāhatva- dyotanāya ādau pramāṇaprameyavyavasthābhyupagantāra iva mārdavena upakramya, ante tatvālā rūpe kraurye paryavasānakāriṇaḥ prativādinaḥ, asmin adhyāye – avaśiṣṭe prathame'dhyāye, nirundhan - nivārayitukāmaḥ, adhikaraṇagaṇaiḥ śiṣṭaiḥ samuditairadhikaraṇaiḥ, tadguṇān paramātmasambandhino guṇān, udgṛṇāti — viśeṣataḥ spaṣṭīkaroti bhagavān bādarāyaṇaḥ || 66 ||

śabdaiḥ sadrahmamukhyaiḥ śrutiśirasi mitaṃ kāraṇaṃ kiścidekaṃ
saṅkalpā'bhyāsa' rūpairatadananuguṇaiścintitastadviśeṣaḥ " bhūtākāśādiśaṅkājananasamucitairnāmabhiḥ kāraṇasthaiḥ
kṣiptvā tatpādaśeṣe śrutisamudayanāsambhavoktyā bhunakti || 67 ||

sabrahmamukhyaiḥ śabdaiḥ – 'sat- brahma' prabhṛtibhiḥ jagatkāraṇavācibhiḥ śabdaiḥ; śrutiśirasi — 'sadeva somyedamagra āsīt ' ' brahma vā idamekamevātra āsīt' ityādyupaniṣadi, kiñcit–viśeṣarūpeṇānirdhāritam ekam – ekameva kāraṇam - nikhilajagannimittopādānabhūtaṃ vastu, mitam – pramitigocaratāmāpāditam ; atadananuguṇaiḥ - paramātmetara cetanācetanavyāvṛttaiḥ, saṅkalpābhyāsarūpaiḥ – jagannirmāṇasaṅkalpaniratiśayadaśā śiraskatayā'bhyasyamānānandā prākṛta divya - maṅgalavigrahaviśeṣaiḥ, tadviśeṣaḥ - pradhāna - jīvasāmānya viśiṣṭapuṇyavajjīvavilakṣaṇaḥ paramātmākhyaḥ cetanaviśeṣaḥ, cintitaḥ – pūrvataneṣvadhikaraṇeṣu vicārya nirṇītaḥ | pādaśeṣe—– ākāśa| dhi- karaṇamārabhya avaśiṣṭe pāde, bhūtākāśādiśaṅkājananasamucitaiḥ bhautika prasiddhā kā śatva- – bhautikaprāṇavāyutva-ādityādijyotiṣṭra - trilokādhipendratvaprakāraka saṃśayotpādanā haiḥ, kāraṇa sthaiḥ jagatkāraṇapratipādakavākye śrūyamāṇaiḥ, nāmabhiḥ - ākāśa - prāṇa- jyotirindrādibhiḥ rūḍhanāmadheyaiḥ, tat — pūrvādhikaraṇatrayacintitaṃ paramātmāsādhāraṇaṃ liṅgam, kṣitvā pūrvapakṣato nirasya, - 2 ścidviśeṣaḥ - bhā.

tat — paramātmāsādhāraṇaṃ liṅgameva, śrutisamudayanāsambhavoktyā – yogāsahakṛta ke valarūvyātmaka- śruteḥ kārya karatvāsambhavapratipādanena, bhunakti-parirakṣati ||67||

ākāśa-prāṇaśabdāvanitaragatiko rūḍhibhaṅgena neyau
jyotiśśabda 'stu' rūḍhyā prathayati puruṣaṃ divyatejo viśiṣṭam | prakhyātendrādiśabdastadanu niyamitastadviśiṣṭapravṛtye-
tyevaṃ syāt peṭikaiṣā dvikayugalavatī śabdavṛttikrameṇa ||68||

anitaragatikau paripaṭhitaparamātmā sādhāraṇaliṅgakavākyasthatayā gatyantararahitau, ākāśa- prāṇaśabdau – 'ākāśa | deva samutpadyante' 'prāṇamevābhisaṃviśanti' iti vākye śrutau ' ākāśa- 'prāṇa' śabdau rūḍhibhaṅgena – loka siddha samudāyaśakti parityāgena ('ā - samantāt kāśate' ityavayava- śaktimātrataḥ) neyau - paramātmavācakatayā cintanīyau | jyotiḥśabdastu -- ' atha yadataḥ paro divo jyoti:' iti vākyastha : 'jyotiḥ śabdaḥ param divyatejoviśiṣṭam aprākṛtatejo- mayavigrahaviśiṣṭam, puruṣam — paramapuruṣam, rūḍhyā – samudāyaśaktyaiva, prathayati – sphuṭaṃ prati- pādayati | tadanu tadanantarādhikaraṇīya viṣayavākyagataḥ, prakhyātendrādiśabdaḥ prasiddhendrādi- bodhanayogyatāvān śabdaḥ, tadviśiṣṭapravṛtyā indrādirūpajīva viśeṣaprakāribhūte eva paramātmani bodhakatvena pravṛttyā, niyamitaḥ - prāptaniyama:, iti uktayā rītyā, śabdavṛttikrameṇa- śabdagatāṃ, kevalayaugikīṃ (tathā) kevala viśeṣaṇā viśrāntarūḍhyātmikāṃ ca arthabodhanapravṛttimanurudhya, iyaṃ peṭikā - ākāśādhikaraṇādyadhikaraṇacatuṣṭayātmikā iyaṃ peṭikā, dvikayugalavatī_ākāśa- prāṇādhikaraṇe evaṃ jyotirindraprāṇādhikaraṇe iti adhikaraṇadvikātmakāvāntara peṭikādvayavatī, syāt —bhavet || 68 ||

atrākāśastvaśeṣa prabhava' - vilayabhūḥ sāmni dṛṣṭaḥ svanāmnā
nirdiṣṭastaittirīye'pyanitarajanitaḥ svātmanaḥ sambhavoktyā | maivaṃ, siddhānuvādo hyayamatha ca paraprāpyatādirna tasmin tatkartātmā vipaśvicchrata 'iti' vihatā svātmanastatprasūtiḥ || 69 || atra - chāndogye ādhe prapāṭhake, sāmni – sāmaprastāve, svanāmnā–rūḍhyā svabodhakena 'ākāśa:' ityevaṃrūpeṇa śabdena san, aśeṣa prabhavavilayabhūḥ - samasta bhūtajātasya sṛṣṭi saṃhārayoḥ 'sarvāṇi.... samutpadyante, ākāśaṃ pratyastaṃ yanti' ityevamānupūrvyā jñātaḥ, taittirīye'pi—

ākāśastu - ākāśa eva, nirdiṣṭaḥ śrutau paripaṭhitaḥ ādhārasthānatayā dṛṣṭaḥ-

taittirīyopaniṣadgate vākye'pi, ākāśaḥ uktaḥ ākāśarūpo'rthaḥ svātmanaḥ sambhavoktyā- svasvarūpādeva svasya āvirbhāvasya 'ātmana ākāśaḥ sambhūtaḥ' iti śrutirūpayā vācā uktatvena, anitarajanitaḥ—ananyotpādyatvena nirdiṣṭaḥ śrutipāṭhapratipannaḥ, iti (laukikā kāśa eva jagata: utpattipralayakāraṇam iti prāpte) maivam — evamāśaṅkanaṃ naiva yuktaṃ bhavati, hi yataḥ, ayam- chāndogyagataḥ 'sarvāṇi ha vā' iti śrutipāṭhaḥ, siddhānuvādaḥ – pūrva pramitasyaiva arthasya punarvacanātmakaḥ, (kāraṇatayā pūrva pramitaśca 'sadeva somyedamagre' ityādivākyagamyaḥ sacchabdārtha eva ; tasmiṃśca 'ākāśa' śabdasya kevalā rūḍhirnāsti iti) atha ca - pratyuta, anantaroktaḥ iti vā | paraprāpyatā diḥ - parāyaṇatva- sarvebhyo jyāyastvādiko dharmaḥ, tasmin — kevalayā rūḍhyā pratipādye laukikākāśe, nanaiva sambhavī, (kiṃ ca) ātmā - ātmarūpo'rtha:, vipaścit – vividhaṃ paśyaccetanatvavāneva, tatkartā ākāśasyā'pi sraṣṭatayā śruta iti 'ātmana ākāśaḥ sambhūtaḥ' iti śrutyā pratipanna iti hetoḥ, svātmanaḥ – svasvarūpādeva, tatprasūtiḥ–ākāśotpattiḥ, vihatā bādhitā, asambhāvitā || 69 ||

@

3) adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ

ākāśādhikaraṇe – 'sarvāṇi ha vā imāni bhūtānyākāśādeva samutpadyante iti 'ākāśa' śabdenābhidhānāt ākāśa eva kāraṇam – ityāśaṅkaya, 'sarvāṇi bhūtāni' iti sarvaśabdavācya cetanācetanātmakaprapañca prati ākāśasya kāraṇatvāsambhavāt, ‘ā. samantāt, kāśate' iti vyutpattyā, 'ākāśa' śabdasya paramātma- paratvopapatteḥ 'ākāśa' śabdavācyaḥ paramātmā - iti siddhāntitam ||

M

4) nayasaṅgatimālikā
viśeṣaliṅgaṃ prabalaṃ sāmānyaśrutito matam | viparīta mihākāśanaye'taḥ pratyudāhṛtiḥ ||35|| || iti ākāśādhikaraṇam || 8 ||
prāṇādhikaraṇam || 1-1-9 || 1) adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ
prāṇāyattaṃ hi deha | dikamiha viditaṃ tena tatkāraṇatvaṃ
śrutyuktaṃ rūḍhiśaktyā sudṛḍhamiti na tadvyomavadvādhanīyam |
tanna prāṇasya kāṣṭhādiṣu mahimahateḥ pūrvavaccānuvādāt
'ākāśokte rivokterbhagavati' nikhilaprāṇanasyā'pi dṛṣṭeḥ ||70||

hi-yataḥ, iha dehādikam - asmi~lloke paridṛśyamānaṃ dehendriyādikam prāṇāyattam- prāṇādhīna pravṛttimaditi viditam - sarvajanaprasiddham tena uktena hetunā, śrutyuktam - "sarvāṇi ha vā imāni bhūtāni prāṇamevā'bhisaṃviśanti prāṇamabhyujjihate' iti chāndobhyaśruti- pratipāditam, tatkāraṇatvam - prāṇasyaiva sarva jagadudayādihetutvam, rūḍhiśaktyā - 'prāṇa' - śabdagatayā mukhyaprāṇabodha'nukūlayā samudāyaśaktyā, sudṛḍhamiti asandigdhatayā niśceyamiti hetoḥ vyomavat – ākāśe iva tat sarvakāraṇatvam bādhanīyaṃ na - nirasanīyaṃ na bhavatīti ( pūrvapakṣe ), tat pūrvoktamāśaṅkanam, nana yogyaṃ bhavati | kāṣṭhādiṣu kāṣṭhādiṣu jagadantarga- teṣu jāyamāneṣu vastuṣu prāṇasya mahimahateḥ - mukhyaprāṇasambandhino mahimnaḥ svāyattapravṛttikatva- rūpasya bhagnatvāta pūrvavacca – pūrvaprasaktākāśavadeva ca anuvādāt – 'havā' ityetaddarśanena anuvādatāsiddheḥ, bhagavati ākāśokteriva – 'yadeṣa ākāśaḥ' iti bhagavati ākāśaśabda- - prayogasyeva, bhagavati prāṇasya ukteḥ – 'prāṇa' śabdasyā'pi bhagavati 'prāṇasya prāṇa:' ityādiṣu prayogāt, nikhilaprāṇanasyāpi samasta prāṇiprāṇayitṛtvarūpasya 'prāṇa'śabdapravṛttinimittasyā'pi dṛṣṭerapi'ko hyevānyāt kaḥ prāṇyāt' ityādinā bhagavati vidyamānatādarśanācca, 'prāṇa' śabdārthaḥ bhagavāneva chāndogyaśrutyuktaḥ ; na prasiddho mukhyaḥ prāṇaḥ || 70 ||

2) adhikaraṇārthasaṅagrahaḥ prāṇādhikaraṇe - kṛtsnasya bhūtajātamya prāṇāyattasthitikatvena,

prasiddhaḥ prāṇaḥ

hiyataḥ, svavākye –' idaṃ vāva tat yadidamasmin antaḥ puruṣe jyotiḥ' iti jyotiḥ pratipādake vākye, paraliṅgadharmāḥ asādhāraṇyena paramātmatvajñāpakāḥ dharmāḥ, na naiva śrutāḥ, iti hetoḥ, jyotistu – vākyapratipannaṃ jyotistu, tat — lokaprasiddham, bhūtatṛtīya- meva' pṛthivyapteja:' iti paṭhiteṣu bhūteṣu tṛtīyaṃ bhautikaṃ teja eva (iti pūrvapakṣe prāpte) ; etat - vākyapratipanne jyotiṣi bhūtatṛtīyatvam, na śaṅkayam —– śaṅkāhaṃ na bhavitumarhati hi yasmāddhetoḥ, bhūtāni sarvāṇi bhūtāni cetanavargātmakāni tasya – lokaprasiddhāḥ, na pādaḥ – na pādatāṃ pratipattumarhanti, kintu — pratyuta, parasya puṃsaḥ paramapuruṣasyaiva, pādaḥ – pādo bhavitumaīnti, -iti siddhāntaḥ || 16 ||

2) tatvasāraḥ

gāyatrīnāma sāmaśrutiśirasi paraṃjyotiradhyāyi sidhya- catuṣpadyaṃ tu bhūta-kṣiti-tanu-hṛdayaiḥ ṣaḍvidhaṃ tadvidhābhiḥ | gāna - trāṇādikābhiḥ śrutigatavacanavyaktibhedātta siddhā vākprāṇoktiḥ parārthā : svarasanigamanaśrutyadhīnaṃ tadetat ||27||

sāmaśrutiśirasi — sāmavedīyatāṇḍiśākhāntargate chāndogyopaniṣadi bhūta - kṣiti-tanu- hṛdayaiḥ bhoktṛcetanavarga-pṛthivī śarīra-hṛdayākhyaiḥ caturbhiḥ arthaiḥ sidhyaccātu- upadyam – pādacatuṣṭayavattāsampattimat, tadvidhābhiḥ – catuṣpātvasambandhiprakāraviśeṣātmakaiḥ, gāna- trāṇādikābhiḥ - bhūnagānakartṛtva-bhūtatrāṇa kartṛtva-bhūtapratiṣṭhātva-bhūtānativartana-prāṇapratiṣṭhātva-prāṇā- nativartanarūpaiḥ prakāraiḥ, ṣaḍvidham – prakāraṣaṭkavat paraṃjyotiḥ – sarvajyotiravabhāsaka prakāśavat 'paro divo jyotiḥ' iti śrutyuktaṃ paraṃ brahma, gāyatrīnāma - gāyatrīsā dṛśya kṛtagāyatrīpadavācyatvavattayā, adhyāyi adhītamasti, vāk prāṇoktiḥ – 'vāgvai gāyatrī' iti vācaḥ kīrtanam, (tathā) 'asmin hīme prāṇāḥ pratiṣṭhitā:' iti prāṇakīrtanaṃ ca parārthā — gāna - trāṇakartṛtvasya, (tathā) prāṇapratiṣṭhātvādeśca lābhātmakasya prayojanāntarasya sādhakam iti, śrutigatavacanavyaktibhedāca- bhūtādicatuṣṭayavacanabhaṅgayāḥ, (tathā) vāk-prāṇayoḥ vacanabhaṅgayāśca vailakṣaṇyādeva jñāyate tadetat-bhūtādicatuṣṭayavattādhīnena catuṣpācvena, gānādikartṛtvādyadhīnena ṣaḍvidhatvena ca gāyatrī - sādṛśyakṛtaṃ brahmaṇi gāyatrītvānusandhānamityetat svarasanigamanaśrutyadhīnam – anupacaritārtha- pratyāyakatvarūpasvārasyavatī yā 'saiṣā catuṣpadā ṣaividhā gāyatrī' ityevaṃrūpā catuṣpāttva - ghāividhyayoḥ upasaṅgṛhītrī śrutiḥ, tannibandhanam || 27 ||

3) adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ
noktiṃ vyāhanti liṅgaṃ kimapi bhavati tu khyātatattvānukūlaṃ śabdaścānanyaniṣṭhaḥ śruta iti na paro jyotirādyuktivedyaḥ | viśvotpattyuktyabhāve'pyavagatamiha talliṅgamityākṣipantaṃ

rundhe'thādhikriyābhyāṃ taducitacidacidvargavaiśiṣṭayayuktyā || 71 ||

uktim – lokasiddhasyāgnyādeḥ rūḍhyā pratipādakaṃ śrutyātmakaṃ 'jyoti' rādiśabdam, kimapi liṅgam – tadbhinnārthaviśeṣagatā sādhāraṇadharmātmakaṃ samabhivyāhṛtaśabdabodhyasāmarthyarūpaṃ yatkiñcit na vyāhanti na bādhate, (tu — pratyuta) khyātataccānukūlam - lokaprasiddhasya abhnyādestattvasya anvayayogyam, liṅgaṃ tu – kaukṣeyajyotiraikyādirūpaṃ jñāpakaṃ tu bhavati- vartata eva, ananyaniṣṭhaḥ– asādhāraṇyena paramātmamātravācakatāśūnyaḥ, śabdaśca – jyotirādyātmakaḥ śabda eva ca, śrutaḥ– spaṣṭatayā śrutipaṭhito vartate | (ataḥ ) jyotirādyukti vedyaḥ – 'jyotiḥ prabhṛtibhiḥ śabdaiḥ abhidheyaḥ arthaḥ, na paraḥ - paramātmā na bhavitumarhati | iha viśvotpattyuktatya- bhāve'pi - jyotirādipadaghaṭite asmin vākye, asmāt jagatsṛṣṭyādeḥ pratipādakasya śabdasya avidyamānatve'pi, talliṅgam – avyabhicaritatayā jagatkāraṇatvasahacaritaṃ niratiśayadīptirūpaṃ jñāpakam, avagatam — 'atha yadataḥ paro divo jyotirdīpyate' iti śrutikhaṇḍena jñātam, iti ākṣipantam — evaṃrūpayā yuktyā asyāḥ śruteḥ laukikāmipratipādanaparatayā paramātmaparatā- nirasane pravṛttaṃ pūrvapakṣiṇam, atha adhikriyābhyām – avyavadhānena uttaratra pratipādyābhyām adhikaraṇābhyām, taducitacidacidvargavaiśiṣṭyayuktyā - paramātmanyeva asādhāraṇyena anvayārha yat indrādyātmakacetana prasiddhāgnyādyātmakā cetanā pekṣayā vaiśiṣṭayaṃ viśeṣa:, atiśayaḥ, vailakṣaṇyaṃ vā, tadyogātmikayā śrutisthayā yuktyā, rundhe – nirākaroti || 71 ||

kaukṣeyajyotiṣaikyaṃ kathitamiha parajyotiṣastasya viśvo-
pādānatvaṃ ca vidyāntaraviditamataḥ kāraṇaṃ vahnirastu | maitraṃ; puṃsūktavākyoditaparapuruṣapratyabhijñatyabādhād
gāyatryuktistu sāmyādapi ca nigaditāstasya bhūtādipādāḥ || 72 ||

iha—chāndogyīye śrutivākye, parajyotiṣaḥ – 'paro divo jyotiḥ' ityuktasya parajyotiḥ śabdābhidheyasya, kaukṣeyajyotiṣā – kukṣisthena jaṭharāgninā sākam, aikyam – ananyatvam, kathitam — 'idaṃ vāva tat yadidamasmin antaḥpuruṣe jyotiḥ' iti pratipāditam | tasya -- kaukṣeyajyotirabhinnasya parasya jyotiṣaḥ, viśvopādānatvam - jagatkāraṇatvaṃ tāvat,

viditaṃ ca vaiśvānarādividyā pratipāda ke upaniṣadvākyaviśeṣe paricitameva | ataḥ—uktābhyāṃ śruti- liṅgābhyām prasiddheśca vahniḥ lokavidito'gnireva kāraṇamastu – jagadupādānamityaṅgī-

kriyatāṃ nāma ; ko doṣa: ? ( iti pūrvapakṣe prāpte), maivam – uktaprakāramaṅgīkaraṇaṃ na vicārasaham, puṃsūktavākyoditaparapuruṣasya - ' pādo'sya viśvā bhūtāni | tripādasyā'mṛtaṃ divi' iti puruṣasūktagatavākyapratipannasyaiva paramapuruṣasya yā pratyabhijñaptiḥ – 'pādo'sya sarvā bhūtāni, 'tripādasyā'mṛtaṃ divi' iti chāndogyīyaitatprakaraṇasthena vākyena jātam ' sa evāyam' iti yat pratyabhijñānam ; tasya, abādhāt — bādhitumaśakyatvāt, ( pratyabhijñāyāśca paramapuruṣatā sādhakatvāt, atra pratipādyaḥ paramātmaiva ; na laukiko vahniriti ) gāyatryuktistu – prakaraṇapratipādye pare jyotiṣi prayujyamānaḥ 'gāyatrī' śabdastu, sāmyāt – gāyatrīgatabhūta- pṛthivī śarīra- hṛdayā khya- pādacatuṣṭayavactrena gānakarmatva-trāṇakarmatva- sarvabhūtapratiṣṭhātva tadanativartanarūpavidhāṣaṭkavatvena ca gāyatrīviśeṣātmakaṛṅmantrasādṛśyāt tasya – gāyatrīsā dṛśya nibandhana 'gāyatrī' śabdavācyatvavato brahmaṇa:, bhūtādipādā api 'sarvabhūtaśabdavācyaḥ jīvātmarūpaḥ bhoktṛvarga:, tadīyapuṇya. pāpātmaka karmāyattaprāptimat pṛthivīrūpaṃ tadbhogasthānam, tādṛśa karmādhīna nirmāṇavat bhogāyatanarūpaṃ śarīram, tādṛśaśarīrāvarti ātmāvasthitegadhārabhūtaṃ hṛdayam' ityevaṃrūpāḥ, viśiṣṭasya brahmaṇaḥ viśeṣaṇatayā aṃśabhūtāḥ catvāraḥ pādāḥ api nigaditāśca prakaraṇe spaṣṭaṃ pratipāditā eva || 72||

utthānaṃ jyotirādāvadhikaraṇayuge kāraṇavyāpta liṅgā- '
dityābhāṣyā'nyaliṅgaṃ svavacasi 'vihitaṃ' neti bhāṣyaṃ kathaṃ syāt | itthaṃ; viśvādiliṅgaṃ sadiha na tu parābhīṣṭaliṅgaṃ samastī-

tyutpaśyan pūrvapakṣī vyavaharati tathā vyāhatistanna śaṅkayā || 73 || jyotirādau adhikaraṇayuge – jyotirindraprāṇākhyā dhikaraṇadvaye, utthānam— vicārāṅgapūrvapakṣotthitiḥ, kāraṇavyāpta liṅgāt — jagatkāraṇatāniyatāt jñāpakadharmāt iti ābhāṣya - evaṃ bhāṣye spaṣṭamuktatvā, (anantaram ) svavacasi – svavākye, vihitam - jñāpitam, liṅgam – avyabhicaritadharmātmakaṃ jñāpakam, na śrutau na paṭhitam iti bhāṣyam – etadarthāvedakaṃ bhāṣyam, kathaṃ syāt – avyāhatārthakaṃ kathaṃ vā yojyam ? (iti praśna); ittham - vakṣyamāṇena prakāreṇa yojyam | (tathā hi ) viśvādiliṅgam – 'viśvataḥ pṛṣṭheṣu' ityādivākyāvagatasarvāva- bhāsakatvarūpaviśvakāraṇatvākṣepakaṃ liṅgam, iha sat - svavākye eva yadyapi vartate ; tathā'pi, parābhīṣṭaliṅgam — paramapuruṣamātragatatayā saṃmatam ubhayaliṅgatvavatparamapuruṣatva pratiṣṭhāpakaṃ liṅgam,

na tu samasti naiva svavākye sambhavati, iti utpaśyan – evamutprekṣamāṇaḥ, sambhāvya cintayan pūrvapakṣī vicārāṅgapūrvapakṣotthāpakaḥ, tathā vyavaharati — kāraṇavyāptaliṅgāt pūrva- pakṣotthānam, svavākye tadasādhāraṇaliṅgābhāvaṃ ca prakaṭayati | tattasmāt vyāhatiḥ- bhāṣyavacasoreva mitho viruddhārthakatā, na śaṅkayā - naiva sambhāvanīyā || 73 ||

4) adhikaraṇārtha saṅgrahaḥ

jyotiradhikaraṇe—'atha yadataḥ paro divo jyotirdīpyate' iti vākye 'jyotiḥ 'śabdaśravaṇāt svavākye virodhiliṅgābhāvācca, prasiddhajyotireva kāraṇam –

ityāśaṅkaya,

svavākye virodhiliṅgābhāve'pi, upakramavākye sarvabhūtapādavantvarūpavirodhiliṅgaśravaṇāt, 'jyoti : ' śabdavācyaḥ paramātmā - iti siddhāntitam ||

5) nayasaṅgatimālikā
svavākyoktabrahmaliṅgaviśeṣādarśanādiha |
jyotirvicāryate tasmāt pratyudāhṛtisaṅgatiḥ || 37 || || iti jyotiradhikaraṇam || 10 ||
indraprāṇādhikaraṇam ||1-1-11||

1) śārīrakanyāyakalāpasamūha: indre tu nirdhāritajīvabhāve svopāstiśabdāt sa hi kāraṇaṃ syāt | 'etanna' sat tasya hi śāstradṛṣṭyā tathopadeśo'mṛtatā ca 'dharmaḥ || 17|| nirdhāritajīvabhāve – śrutismṛtītihāsapurāṇāgamaiḥ pramāṇatayā pratipannaiḥ nirṇītajīvatvake, indre tu – prasiddhendre eva, svopāstiśabdāt – svakarmopāsana vidhāyakasya śabdasya upaniṣadi śrutatvāt, saḥ - prasiddhendra eva, kāraṇaṃ syāddhi – sarvakāraṇatayā vedāntavedyaṃ tattvaṃ bhavitumarhati khalu, etat — evaṃvidhamāśaṅkanam, na sat naiva sādhu, hi-yataḥ, tasya – indrasya, tathopadeśaḥ—upāsyatvenopadeśaḥ, śāstradṛṣṭyā – atharvaśikhādau śāstre vāmadevādiṣu dṛṣṭāṃ rītim 'ahaṃ manurabhavaṃ sūryaśca' ityādikāmanurudhyaiva, dharmaśca – anena upāsanena sampādyeo dharmastu, amṛtatā - mokṣa eva || 17 ||

sarvajñamānandamayaṃ sarūpaṃ tejasvinaṃ prāṇanakāraṇaṃ ca | susthānamātmānamudājahāra pādena pūrveṇa jagannidānam || 18 || sarvajñam —jagatsṛṣṭi-sthiti-saṃhāropayogisarvaviṣayakā saṅkucitajñānāśrayam ānanda- mayam — niratiśayadaśā śiraskatayā'bhyasyamānānandapracuram, sarūpam — karmādhīna divya vigraham, tejasvinam - sarva prakāśana hetu bhūtānyānadhīna svaprakāśam, prāṇanakāraṇaṃ ca – sarvajagajjīvana- kāraṇam, susthānam - aprākṛta divyasthāna dedīpyamānajyotirmayasvarūpam, ātmānam - agnīndrādi- devatāntarātmabhūtaṃ ca, jagannidānam - nirasta jagatkāraṇatvāyogaṃ paramātmānam, pūrveṇa pādena- śārīrakādyapādena, udājahāra — upaniṣadyatayā spaṣṭīcakāra, bādarāyaṇaḥ || 18 ||

2) adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ
vidyā prātardanī sā vadati hitatamopāstikarmendra meva khyātaprāṇendra cihnānvitamapi tadasau viśvakarteti cenna | brahma tredhā hyupāsyaṃ bahuvidhacidacitkañcukaṃ svātmanā ca prāṇendraprakramo'pi prabalataramahāvākyavaighaṭyabhagnaḥ || 74 ||

sā – kauṣītakī brāhmaṇopaniṣadi prasiddhā, prātardanī vidyā - pratardananāmnaḥ kṣatriyasya hitopadeśāya pravṛttā upāsana viśeṣopadeṣṭrī śrutivākyatatiḥ, 'pratardano ha vai' ityādinopakrāntā, indrameva - devarāja- śacīpatyādibhiḥ śabdaiḥ purāṇeṣu prasiddhaṃ devatāviśeṣameva khyātaprāṇendra- cihnānvitamapi — lokaprasiddhau yau, dehadhārakaḥ prāṇaḥ, devarājaḥ indraśca ; tayoḥ cihna:- āyurniyāmakatva-triśīrṣatvāṣṭrahananādirūpaiḥ lāñchanaiḥ sahitatayā śrutipratipannamapi santam, hitatamopāstikarma – puruṣārthasampādakatayā 'hita' śabdavācyānāṃ karmādīnāṃ madhye, paramapuruṣārtha- rūpaniśśreyasa pradatayā atiśayena hitaṃ yadupāsanaṃ tadārādhyamiti vadati – 'māmupāsva' iti indroktaparipāṭyā bodhayati | tat — tasmāt asau – prasiddhaḥ indra eva, viśvakartā- jagatkāraṇatvena śrutisammataḥ, iti cet — evamāśaṅkanam na – yukttimanna bhavati | hi—yataḥ, brahma – niśśreyasārthamārādhyaṃ paraṃ brahma, bahuvidhacidacitkañcukam – nānārūpacetanā cetanaśarī- rakam, (tathā) svātmanā ca - advārakatayā svasvarūpeṇa ceti, tredhā — triprakāreṇa,

upāsyam- upāsanena prīṇanīyam, prāṇendraprakramo'pi – lokaviditaprāṇāsādhāraṇadharmaviśiṣṭaveṣeṇa prāṇasya, (tathā) tādṛśendrāsādhāraṇadharmavattayā indrasyā'pi ca upakramo'pi prabalataramahāvākyavaighaṭya- bhagnaḥ—upakramāpekṣayā atyantaṃ prabalaṃ yat mahāvākyam — āmūlāgraprakaraṇātmakam, tena sākaṃ virodhe sati, pratihataśaktika eva khalu || 74 ||

yalliṅgaṃ kāraṇaikasthitamiti kathitaṃ jyotiṣīndre ca tattu prakhyātānyaikaniṣṭhaṃ prathamamitamatastanmukhotthityayogaḥ | aprāpte tadvimarśe prakṛtaśithilatā neti cet ! satyametad
viṣṇū 'tpatyuktinītikramata' iha purovādamutprekṣya śaṅkā || 75 ||

jyotiṣi – jyotiradhikaraṇīyaviṣayavākyapratipādye jyotiṣi indre ca – indraprāṇāṣi - karaṇīyaviṣayavākyavedye indre ca kāraṇaikasthitamiti — jagatkāraṇatvavyāptatvena | bhimatam, yat liṅgam – jñāpakatveneṣṭaṃ yādṛśamasādhāraṇadharmasvarūpam kathitam - pūrvapakṣiṇā varṇitam | tattu — talliṅgameva, prakhyātānyaikaniṣṭham – kāraṇatayā śrutyantara siddhaparamapuruṣamātravṛttīti, prathamamitam - prāgevākāśādhikaraṇe nirṇītam | ataḥ prāktanādhikaraṇe eva vicārya nirṇītatvāt, tanmukhotthitya yogaḥ kāraṇatvavyāptena liṅgana prakhyātendrādau jagatkāraṇatāpūrvapakṣotthānā- sambhava:, tadvimaśai— jagatkāraṇatāyāḥ liṅgaviśeṣasya ca vyāpyavyāpakabhāvavicāre tannirṇaye ca, aprāpte - pūrvamasampanna sati, prakṛtaśithilatā na śārīrakaśāstra nirṇayatvābhimata sakalajagadeka- kāraṇabhūtaparabrahmasiddhibhaṅgaśaṅkā anavakāśagrastā iti cet — evaṃ kṣobheṇa adhikaraṇayugasyā- pravṛttirevetyāśaṅkane prāpte, etat satyam - adhikaraṇāpravṛttiśaṅkanaṃ kiñcidaṃśe vāstavameva ; athā'pi na sarvāṃśe, (sakarlo'śaḥ kaḥ ? iti cet ) viṣṇutpatyuktinītikramataḥ - parama puruṣasya bhagavato viṣṇoḥ yāḥ purāṇeṣūpalabhyamānā: janmapratipādikāḥ giraḥ, tāsāṃ yaḥ nayanakramaḥ 'utpattirbhagavataḥ upendrādyavatārātmikā na martyasādhāraṇī karmāyattā' ityevaṃrūpaḥ, tamanusṛtya, iha – upāsyatvenokteṣvindrādiṣvapi viṣayeṣu paripaṭhitam purovādam - prathamataḥ pravṛttamākhyāyi- kādirūpaṃ vacanam, utprekṣya - upendrādiparatayā nayanaṃ kṛtvā śrutyarthaścintanīya iti, śaṅkā- pūrva pakṣotthānamiti, upendrādeḥ upāsyatā, anyatra nirṇītatvena nā'tra nirṇeyā ; kintu indrādi- śarīrakaḥ paramātmaiva upāsyaḥ iti nirṇeyamiti ca kṛtvā adhikaraṇasāphalyamiti || 75 ||

jyotiḥ prāṇendraśabdāḥ parataraviṣayāḥ kāraṇavyāptadharmā-
dityetat sādhvamīṣāṃ bahuvihatimatī khyātamātre tu vṛttiḥ | tat kaukṣeyānalātmā kathita iha tathādhyānatastatphalāptyai mukhyaprāṇādiliṅgaṃ tadapi hitaparopāsanānmokṣaṇāya || 76 ||
tvatyuktirītibhramata-bha, tyadhyādirītibhramata - ā.

jyotiḥ prāṇendraśabdāḥ – chāndogyaśrutaḥ jyotiśśabdaḥ kauṣītakyāṃ śrutau indra- prāṇaśabdau ca kāraṇavyāptadharmāt — kāraṇaikāntaniratiśaya dīpti-hitatamopāstikarmatvādeḥ dharmasya śrutau paṭhanāt jñāpakāt parataraviṣayāḥ — paramātmamātrapratipādakāḥ ityetat — evaṃ nirvahaṇam, sādhu – ubhayavādisammatatvāt samīcīnameva | amīṣām – uktānāṃ jyotirādi- śabdānām, khyātamātre – prasiddhajyotiṣi, prasiddhendre, prasiddhe prāṇe ca vṛttistu– pratipādakatayā vartanaṃ tu, bahuvihatimatī – nānāvidhavirodhajuṣṭam, tasmāt – uktāddhetoḥ, kaukṣeyānalātmā — jāṭhara miśarīrakaḥ paramātmaiva, tathā dhyānataḥ – śarīratayā jāṭharānala- viśiṣṭatvenopāsanāt, tatphalāsyai - upasaṃhārāvagatābhirūpya kīrtyaptirūpaphalasampattaye, iha kathitaḥ atra 'idaṃ vāva tat yadidamasminnantaḥ puruṣe jyoti:' iti uktaḥ, mukhyaprāṇādi- liṅgam – prasiddhaprāṇagatam āyurdhārakatvarūpaṃ prasiddhendragataṃ triśīrṣatvā hananādirūpaṃ ca asādhāraṇa- svarūpaṃ param, tadupahitaparopāsanāt – mukhya prāṇaśarīrakatvaviśiṣṭasya, (tathā) prasiddhendraśarīra- katvaviśiṣṭasya ca paramātmanaḥ upāsanāt sādhanabhūtāt, mokṣaṇāya - mokṣaprāptiḥ sambhavatīti jñāpanāyaiva || 76 ||

kāryaṃ yat karmavaśyaṃ yadapi dṛḍhamitaṃ tannirūḍhaistu śabdai-
nirdiṣṭe brahmaṇi syāt kvacidagatihatā rūḍhi raindrīnayena | talliṅgānanyathāsiddhyadhigamanabalāt tadviśiṣṭe vivakṣā
syādīśe jyotirindrādyabhilapana' pare'hantva 'mādīrite ca || 77 || yat—–ākāśāgnyādirūpaṃ vastu kāryam – janyamiti, dṛḍhamitam — asandigdhatayā

itya dau tvamādiśabdaiśca bodhite ca sati, talliṅgānanyathāsiddhyadhigamana balāt — kārya karmavaśyā- dyarthātmakāsādhāraṇaliṅgasya anyathānupapattiniścayarūpaṃ balamāśritya tadviśiṣṭe – tattatpadarūḍhyartha bhūta- jyotirindrādyarthaviśiṣṭe paramātmani, vivakṣā - śrutyāditātparyaviṣayatā, syāt - bhavedeva || 77 || svecchātaḥ sarvahetuḥ śubhaguṇavibhavānantanissīmaharṣaḥ
śuddha karmottha 'divyā' kṛtiranupadhikākāśanādisvabhāvaḥ | saprāṇāprāṇabhedavyatibhidurajagatprāṇano divyadīptiḥ
prāṇendrādyantarātmā prabhuradhikaraṇaiḥ saptabhiḥ pratyapādi || 78 ||

&

saptabhiḥ adhikaraṇaiḥ – īkṣati - ānandamaya - antaḥ - ākāśa-prāṇa jyotiḥ - indraprāṇākhyaiḥ saptasaṃkhyākaiḥ śārīrakaśāstrīyaiḥ prathamapādagataiḥ adhikaraṇaiḥ, (kramaśaḥ ) svecchātaḥ sarvahetuḥ - svasaṅkalpamātrādhīnajagajjanmasthitilaya hetuḥ, śubha.... harṣaḥ svakīya sarvajaganmaṅgalaguṇavibhūtyātmaka- niravadhikā tiśayā paricchinnānandamahodadhiḥ, śuddhākarmo... kṛtiḥ samstvaikamaya karmāna dhīna divya- maṅgalavigrahaḥ, anu ....svabhāvaḥ - asaṅkucitasvābhāvikaprakāśamayaḥ, saprāṇā ···· prāṇanaḥ- nikhila cetanā cetana sattā hetu:, divyadīptiḥ—aprākṛtajyotirmayaḥ, prāṇe....rātmā - prāṇendrādeḥ prāṇātmakendrādeva antaḥ praviśya niyantā ca prabhuḥ sarveśvaraḥ paramapuruṣaḥ, pratyapādi- pratipāditaḥ || 78 ||

....

3) adhikaraṇārtha saṅgrahaḥ

indraprāṇādhikaraṇe' pratardano ha vai daivodāsi:' iti vākye, 'mām... upāsva' iti hita tamopāsanakarmatayā uktasya, 'indra' śabdena vyapadeśāt, upakrame eva tvāṣṭrahananarūpaliṅga- darśanācca hitatamopāsanakarmatayoktaḥ, prasiddhendra eva – ityāśaṅkaya,
bahuṣu vākyeṣu paramātmaliṅgadarśanāt, mahāvākyāpekṣayā upakramasya durbalatvācca, hita tamopāsana karmatayoktaḥ 'indra' śabdanirdiṣṭaḥ indraśarīrakaḥ paramātmaiva iti siddhāntitam || 4) nayasaṅgatimālikā pūrvoktāt pūrvapakṣaḥ syādupakramaparākramāt | indraprāṇādhikaraṇe saṅgatistvāpavādikī || 38 | evaṃ samagamannādyapāde prathamalakṣaṇe |

peṭikātritayaṃ caikādaśādhikaraṇānyapi || 39 || || indraprāṇādhikaraṇam || 11 ||
|| prathamādhyāye prathamaḥ pādaḥ samāptaḥ ||

prathamādhyāyasya dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ
1) śārīrakanyā yakalāpasamūha:
śāstre tvamuṣmin jagatāṃ nidānaṃ brahmaiva sākṣācidaciccharīram | ityevamarthaṃ viśadīkariṣyan pādānatha trīn pracakāra śeṣān || 19 ||

amuṣmin śāstre – śārīrakāravye asmin śāstre, cidaciccharīram — cetanācetana- śarīra kam, brahmaiva paraṃ brahmaiva, sākṣāt – advārakameva sat, jagatāṃ nidānam - sarva- jagatkāraṇam ; ityevamartham etādṛśamimamartham, viśadīkariṣyan – sphuṭīkartukāmaḥ, atha trīn śeṣān pādān — paścāttanān avaśiṣṭān dvitīya-tṛtīya- caturthapādān trīn, pracakāra - viramimīta (bādarāyaṇaḥ) || 19 ||
tatra dvitīyena śarīramāsīt tatpratyaśeṣaḥ cidacitprapañcaḥ |
tṛtīyatastasya tadātmabhāvaḥ caturthatastadviparītabhaṅgaḥ || 20 ||

tatra — dvitīyādipādatrayamadhye, dvitīyena ādito dvitīyena pādena, aśeṣaḥ — nikhilaḥ, cidācitprapañcaḥ – vistṛtaḥ cetanācetanavargaḥ, tatprati śarīramāsīt — parabrahma - nirūpitaśeṣatva-dhāryasva-niyāmyatvavaditi vicārya nirṇītamāste, tṛtīyataḥ - tṛtīyena pādena, tadātmabhāvaḥ – cetanā cetanā khya sarvajaganniyantṛtāvicāraḥ, caturthataḥ turīyena pādena, tadvipa rītabhaṅgaḥ _ cetanācetana śarīraka paramātmakāraṇatāvirodhinirāsaśca kṛtaḥ || 20||

12 sarvatra prasiddhyadhikaraṇam || 1-2-1 ||
svakarmataḥkāraṇatā'bhiyogāt alpatvato'lpāyatanatvataśca |
sarva samāśaṅkaya nirāsa jīvaṃ prasiddhitastadguṇatassmṛteśca || 21 ||

svakarmataḥ kāraṇatā'bhiyogāt – svīya śubhāśubhakarmanibandhana jagatsṛṣṭiprayojakatvātmaka- jagatkāraṇatvākṣepāt, alpatvataḥ – 'aṇīyān vrīheḥ' ityuktālpaparimāṇānvayāītvāt, alpāyatanatvataśca – ‘ātmāntarhṛdaye' ityuktālpasthānavāsayogyatvācca jñāpakāt jīvam- karmavaśyaṃ cetanameva, sarva samāśaṅkaya – 'sarvaṃ khalvidam' iti śrutistha sarvapadavācyatvena pūrva- pakṣayitvā prasiddhita: 'sarva khalu' iti 'khalu' śabdabodhyaprasiddhiviṣayībhūtakāraṇatvānanvayāt, tadguṇataḥ paramātmā sādhāraṇamanomayatvādiguṇaśravaṇāt jñāpakāt smṛteśca – 'sarvasya cā'haṃ hṛdi' iti hṛdayākhyālpāyatanatvasya parasminnapi sambhavācca, jīvaṃ sarvaṃ nirāsa - jīve śrutistha- sarvaśabdavācyatāṃ nirākṛtavān || 21||

2) adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ
atrāyogānyayogavyapanayananayairbrahma pādatripādī-
'bhāgā' rūḍhairmṛdapakrama kaṭhinaparaiḥ prāya ādhe prasādhyam | kṛtsnākṣepopaśāntyai prathama iha tataḥ pāda uktātripādī- kvācitkākṣepapūrvākhilakalahasamunmūlanāya praṇītā || 1 ||

atra ādye – śārīrakīyaprathamādhyāye, pāda - tripādībhāgārūḍhai : – ekapādātmakena bhāgena, pādatrayātmakena ca bhāgena labdhamvarūpaiḥ, mṛdūpakrama kaṭhinaparaiḥ – upakrame alpena, uttarottaraṃ cādhikena prayāsena sādhyaiḥ, prāyaḥ ayogānyayogavyapanayananayaiḥ– (kramaśaḥ) brahmaṇi jagatkāraṇatvāyogavyāvartanapracuraiḥ, tathā jagatkāraṇatvasya, brahmānyayogavyāvartanapracuraiśca adhikaraṇaiḥ, brahma prasādhyam - cetanācetanavilakṣaṇaṃ paraṃ brahma vedāntavedyatayā samarthanīyam | tataḥ- uktāddhetoḥ, iha prathamaḥ pādaḥ – śārīra kādyādhyāyīyaḥ ādyaścaraṇaḥ kṛtsnākṣepopaśāntyai— sarveṣāṃ vedāntavākyānāṃ viṣaye prāptasya nikhilacetanācetanavilakṣaṇabrahmā pratipādakatvā kṣepasya śamanāya uktaḥ – nibaddhaḥ, iha tripādī - ādyādhyāyīyāvaśiṣṭapādatrayaṃ tu, kācitkā.... samunmūlanāya — katipaya vedāntavākyaviśeṣaviṣayaka brahmā pratipādakatvākṣepapūrvaka kramaśaḥ pravṛttasarva- vedāntavākyākṣepanirāsāya praṇītā racitā vartate || 1 ||
aspaṣṭa spaṣṭarūpa- sphuṭatara cida cilliṅgavadvākyacintā bhāṣye dīpāvatāre'pyagaṇi nayagaṇaiḥ sampravṛttā tripādyām | atyantāspaṣṭaliṅgānvitaviṣayamuṣantyādyapādaṃ tu kecit
tatredaṃ tāratamyaṃ niyata nijabalaiḥ prācyatārtīyamānaiḥ || 2 ||

tripādyām —prathamādhyāyagatadvitīyādipādatraye, nayagaṇaiḥ – adhikaraṇasamūhaiḥ, aspaṣṭa... cintā - ( kramaśaḥ) jhaṭiti cetanācetanāsādhāraṇadharmapratipattya janakānāṃ tādṛśadharmapratyāyakānāṃ, jhaṭiti tādṛśadharmapratyayakānāṃ ca vedāntavākyānāṃ vicārapūrvikā nirṇītiḥ, sampravṛttā-- sampannati, bhāṣye - śrībhāṣyagranthe, dīpāvatāre'pi - (tathaiva ) vedāntadīpasyāvataraṇikā granthe'pi, agaṇisphuṭaṃ darśitam | kecittu apare bhāṣyavyākhyātārastu, ādyapādam – prathamaṃ caraṇam, | – atyanta spaṣṭaliṅgānvitaviṣayam – asphuṭatara cidacilliṅga kavākya vicārātmakamiti, uśanti- pratipādayanti | idaṃ tāratamyam - īdṛśaṃ vailakṣaṇyam, tatra — teṣu teṣu vedāntavākyeṣu,

niyatanijabalaiḥ suvyavasthitasvīyabalatāratamyavadbhiḥ, karmatārtīyamānaiḥ – karmakāṇḍīyatṛtīyā - dhyāyasiddhaiḥ viniyogagrāhakaiḥ śrutyādipramāṇaiḥ siddhaṃ bhavati (iti jñeyam ) || 2 || pūrvatrāsiddharūpaiḥ svamativiracito nītibhiḥ pūrvapakṣa:
siddhaiḥ sādhāraṇairapyupadhiniyamitaiḥ pratyavasthā dvitīye | spaṣṭāsādhāraṇatyairupari paramatānūktikalpairatheti
nyāyaikatriṃśadatra praticaraṇavibhaktyanvitā'nveṣaṇīyā || 3 ||

pūrvatra pāde svamativiracitonnītibhiḥ taistairvādibhiḥ svīyabuddhipari kalpitākāravadbhiḥ, asiddharūpaiḥ – pakṣāvṛttitvātmaka svarūpāsiddhimadbhirhetubhiḥ, pūrvapakṣa:-nyāya- samādheyāṃśotthānam ; dvitīye – anantarapāde, siddhaiḥ sādhāraṇairapi pakṣavṛttitva-pakṣetara vṛttitvā- tmakarūpadrayavadbhiḥ hetubhiḥ, upadhiniyamitaiḥ upādhyadhīna sādhyasāhacaryavadbhiḥ, dharmaviśeṣa- viśiṣṭaveṣeṇa sādhyāvyabhicaritatayā darśitairiti vā, pratyavasthā - pūrvapakṣotthānaprakāraḥ, upari- tṛtīyapāde, spaṣṭā sādhāraṇatvaiḥ sphuṭatarapratipanna jīvāsādhāraṇatāvadbhiḥ hetubhiḥ pratyavasthā - pūrvapakṣotthitiḥ | atha– caturthe pāde, paramatānūktikalpaiḥ parābhimatārthānuvādasadṛśaiḥ hetubhiśca pratyavasthānamiti, iti – uktābhiḥ rītibhiḥ, atra – ādime'dhyāye, praticaraṇavibhaktyanvitā- “pādavibhāgānurodhyarthavibhāgamahitā, nattatvādapratiniyata vibhājaka dharmasahitā vā, nyāyaikatriṃśat- adhikaraṇānāmekatriṃśatsaṅkhyā, anveṣaṇīyā - nipuṇamālocanīyā bhavati || 3 || viśvaṃ pāde dvitīye vapuriti kathayaścintyate vākyavargaḥ viśvādhāraḥ sa ātmetyabhilapanaparastarkaṇīyastṛtīye |

tu sāṅkhyā dipakṣoditaparipaṭhana bhrāntirunmīlanīye
tyevaṃ kecit tripādīṃ jagadurayamapi śrotṛbuddheḥ samādhiḥ || 4 ||

kecit — anye kecana bhāṣyakārāśaya cintakāḥ, dvitīye pāde – tripādyantargate ādyā- dhyāya dvitīyapāde, 'viśvam samantaṃ jagat vapuḥ' – 'parabrahmaṇaḥ śarīram' iti kathayan vākyavargaḥ evaṃ pratipādayan vedāntavākyagaṇaḥ, cintyate - vicārya nirṇīyate | tṛtīye- tṛtīye pāde, sa ātmā - 'viśvaśarīrakaḥ paramātmā', viśvādhāraḥ viśvaśarīrakatvopapādaka- viśvādhāratvavāṃśca', ityabhilapanaparaḥ vākyavargaḥ īdṛśārthapratipādanedaṃpara: vedāntavākyagaṇaḥ, tarkaṇīyaḥ – vicārya nirṇeyaḥ | turye caturthe pāde 'sāṅkhyādibhrāntiḥ ---sāṅkhya- pātañjalā dipakṣasiddhasyaivārthasya anuvadanaṃ vedānteṣvasti itīyaṃ bhrāntiḥ unmūlanīyā' - apunaḥ praroha nirasanīyā vartate', ityevam – anayā rītyā tripādīm — pratipāda vibhaktārthavat

pādatrayam, jagaduḥ — kathitavantaḥ | ayamapi — uktaḥ prakāro'pi śrotṛvuddheḥ– arthavibhāga--

śravaṇakutukināṃ buddheḥ, samādhiḥ – ākāṅkṣāśāmakaḥ prakāro bhavati || 4 ||

yasya prāṇaśśarīraṃ sa khalu hitatamopāstikarma prasaktaḥ
tasmin jīvatvaśaṅkāṃ jagadupajanake sauti śāṇḍilyavidyā | pūrvanyāyācca yuktaṃ damanamiha mahāvākyataḥ prakramasye-
tyutthāne prakramoktānuguṇamiti mahāvākyamekīkaroti || 5 ||

yasya – viśvaśarīrakasya yasya paramātmanaḥ prāṇaḥ śarīram – jagatprāṇanahetubhūtaḥ prāṇākhyo'rthaḥ ādheyatva-vidheyatva-śeṣatvavān hitatamopāstikarma saḥ atyantahitātmana upāsanena prepsitaḥ sa paramātmā, prasaktaḥ khalu– pūrvapeṭikāyām indraprāṇādhikaraṇe prastutaḥ khalu, tasmin jagadupajanake — jagatkāraṇatayā śrutipratipanne tasmin vastuni viṣaye, jīvatva- śaṅkām— 'etādṛśaguṇaviśiṣṭo'rthaḥ jīva eva bhavitumarhati' itīmaṃ bhramam, śāṇḍilyavidyā- chāndogyatṛtīyaprapāṭhakoktā 'sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ brahma' ityādivākyarūpā śrutitatiḥ, sauti — janayatī - vāsti | mahāvākyataḥ — āmūlāgraprakaraṇa sthayatkiñcidekārthatātparya kaba huvākyastomā nuguṇyena, prakramasya damanam – upakramasthavākyatātparyānyathākaraṇam, yuktamiti – nyāyasammatamityevamākā- rāt pūrvanyāyāt – indraprāṇādhikaraṇoktanyāyāt, iha utthāne—asminnadhikaraṇe pūrvapakṣa- pravṛttau satyām, mahāvākyam - eka tātparya kabahuvākyagarbham amūlāgra prakaraṇam, prakramoktānu- guṇamiti - upakramasthavākyatātparyāviruddha nāsakamiti, ekīkaroti – aikakaṇṭhyameva nyāyaṃ vyutpādayati (atra sūtrakāraḥ) || 5 ||
anvāruhyātra bhedaṃ prathamamadhikṛtirbhāṣitā kiṃ nimittaṃ ?
vidhaikatve'nuvādaḥ para iha guṇa' vidhyarthameveti' yuktam | satyaṃ brahmānumatya kvacidupaniṣadi vā'pi kalpye vivāde
cintaipodāhṛtiḥ syāt paramataracitetyarthasiddhistu boddhayā || 6 ||
atra—śāṇḍilyavidyāgataśrutivākyeṣu, bhedam — vākyabhedam, brahma- manomayaśabdayoḥ brahma-jīva-rūpārthabhedaṃ vā, anvāruhya — anabhyupagatamapi abhyupagatamiva kṛtvā, prathamam – prathamayojanāyām, adhikṛtiḥ– adhikaraṇaracanā, kimarthaṃ bhāṣitā kasmai prayojanāya śrībhāṣye pradarśitā ! vidhaikatve-'sa ṛtuṃ kurvīta' 'śānta upāsīta ' ityuktayoḥ ṛtūpāsanayoḥ eka vidyāntargatatvapakṣe, paraḥ – 'sa ṛtuṃ kurvīta' ityayaṃ vidhibhāgaḥ,

iha guṇavidhyartha eva asyāṃ vidyāyāṃ śamarūpaguṇasya upāsakagatasya upāsanāṅgatājñāpanārtha eva, iti yuktam– etadevocitam (ityāśaṅkane pravṛtte), satyam – ukte'rthe kiñcidaśaḥ aṅgīkārya eva | kvacidupaniṣadi-upaniṣadaḥ kasmiścana bhāge, brahma anumatya - brahmā'stitvamabhyupagamya, kvā'pi - upaniṣadaḥ bhāgāntare, vivāde kalpyetadviruddhe vāde śaṅkanīye, paramataracitā epā cintā- matāntare pradarśiteyaṃ vicāraprakriyā, udāhṛtirbhavet iti — dṛṣṭāntarūpā bhavitumarhatītyevaṃrūpā, arthasiddhiḥ—adhikaraṇaprayojanasampattiḥ bodhyā- jñeyā || 6 ||
sarvatvaṃ karmabhiḥ svairjanimati ghaṭate brahmaśabdo'pare ce- ' tyalpasthāno'lpamānaḥ sukhataditarabhugjīva eveti cenna | tajjatvāderanūktervividhaguṇabhidādarśanāt sarvatādeḥ
svārasyādapyaṇutvaṃ hyupadhikṛta mihopāstaye jyāyasi syāt || 7 ||

svaiḥ karmabhiḥ janimati – svakīyapuṇyāpuṇyātmaka karmādhīnotpattimati jīve, sarvatvaṃ ghaṭate—'sarvaṃ khalvidam' iti śrutyuktaṃ sarvātmakatvaṃ yujyate | (tathā) 'brahma' śabdaśca- 'idaṃ brahma' ityukta: 'brahma' śabdo'pi, apare – parasmādbrahmaṇo bhinne jīve'pi ghaṭate – nirbādhaṃ yujyata eva | alpasthānaḥ - hṛdayākhyālpapramāṇasthānavartī, alpamānaḥ aṇuparimāṇaścārthaḥ, sukha-taditarabhuk — sukhaduḥkhānubhavāhaḥ, jīva eva – jīvātmaiva na paramātmā, iti cet- evamāśaṅkanam, na–nocitam tajjatvādeḥ – 'yato vā imāni ' iti śrutiprasiddhasya brahmajatva-brahmaṇi līyamānatva-brahmādhīnasthitimattvādeḥ dharmasya, anūkteḥ — 'tajjalān' ityanena anuvādāt, vividhaguṇabhidādarśanāt - mitho vilakṣaṇānāṃ brahmāsādhāraṇānāṃ manomayatvādi- guṇaviśeṣāṇāṃ śrutipratipannatvāt sarvatādeḥ sarvātmakatvādeḥ, svārasyādapi - bahucetanagata- tvāpekṣayā ekacetanagatatve aucityādapi, ihā'ṇutvam – 'aṇīyān vrīheḥ' ityuktaṃ sūkṣmapari- māṇatvam, jyāyasi – niratiśayabṛhati paramātmani viṣaye, upāstaye—upāsanasaukaryāya, upadhikṛtaṃ syāt – hṛdayātma kāvacchedasthāna nibandhanameva bhavet ; va svabhāvakṛtamiti || 7 ||

'sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ brahma' iti vākye, 'sarvamidaṃ brahma' iti, caturmukhādisthāvarāntasarvatādā- - ramyopadeśāt, caturmukhāditādātmyasya jīveṣvapi sambhavāt 'brahma' śabdanirdiṣṭaḥ jīva eva

ityāśaṅkaya,

' tajjalān' iti, hetutayā janmādīnāṃ prasiddhavadupadeśāt, teṣāṃ ca paramātmasambandhitayā prasiddhaḥ sarvaśarīrakasya parasya sarvatādātmyopapatteśca 'brahma'śabdanirdiṣṭaḥ paramātmā iti siddhāntitam ||

4) nayasaṅgatimālikā
atha dvitīyapāde'smin trikadvika vibhāgataḥ | alpasthāna- mahāsthānasthiteśopāstipeṭike || 40 ||
mahāvākyagataṃ brahmaliṅga prabalamīritam |
tādṛkṣa liṅgataḥ sarvanaye śaṅkā vyapodyate || 41 ||
|| iti sarvatraprasiddhyadhikaraṇam ||1||
adhikaraṇam || 1-2-2 ||
1) śārīrakanyā ya kalāpasaṅgrahaḥ

attā tu jīvo na paraḥ svatantraḥ sa eva yatkarmaphalopabhogī | ityapyasat ; taddhi carācarasya mṛtyūpasekādadanaṃ tvapītiḥ || 22 ||
attā - 'yasya brahma ce 'ti atṛtayā śrutipratipannaḥ arthaḥ, jīvastu – jīva eva, paro na paramātmā na bhavitumarhati svantatraḥ tasya svatantratvāt, yat - yasmāddhetoḥ, sa eva- jīvātmaiva, karmaphalopabhogī - svārjitānāṃ puṇyāpuṇyātmakānāṃ karmaṇāṃ phalabhoktā bhavitumarhati, ityapi — evamāśaṅkanamapi, asat - asādhu ! hi-yataḥ, tat śrutyuktam, carācarasya mṛtyūpasekāt adanam - jaṅgama-sthāvarātmaka jagatkarmakaṃ mṛtyūpasecanapūrvakaṃ bhakṣaṇam, apītistu- paramātmakartṛkaḥ sarvajagata upasaṃhāra eva || 22 ||

2) adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ
attā khalbodanāderbhava' bhṛditi' kaṭhaśrutyadhīto'pyasau syāt na syānmṛtyupasiktasthiracaranikhilagrāsa' tastallayokteḥ | jīvavyāvartanaṃ ca prakaraṇaviditaṃ bhoktṛtoktidvayostu preyatva-prerakatvapratiniyatarasācchatrinītyā'thavā syāt || 8 ||

odanāde: annaprabhṛtibhojyadravyasya, attā - bhakṣakaḥ, bhavabhṛt khalu – prakṛticakre karmavaśāt bhramyamāṇo jīva eva khala, iti - anena hetunā, kaṭhaśrutyadhīto'pi - 'yasya brahma ca kṣatraṃ ca' iti kaṭhaśrutau paripaṭhito'pyattā, asau syāt — jīva eva bhavitumarhati (iti pūrvapakṣe) na syāt – śrutipaṭhito'ttā na jīvo bhavitumarhati (iti uttaram ) mṛtyū ... grāsataḥ mṛtyvātmakopasecanasahitatayā sthāvarajaṅgamātmaka samasta prapañca bhakṣaṇoktirūpeṇa jñāpakena, tallayokteḥ- jagallayātmakasya bhakṣaṇasyaiva śrutau paṭhanāt tasya ca bhavabhṛti asambhavāt | jīvavyāvartanaṃ ca- paramātmāsādhāraṇadharmakathanena jīvā bodhanamapi, prakaraṇaviditam - 'mahāntaṃ vibhum ' 'nāyamātmā" ityādirūpeṇa paramātmaprakaraṇena suspaṣṭaṃ jñāpitam | dvayoḥ - jīvapa/yorubhayorapi, bhoktṛ- toktistu - 'ṛtaṃ pibantau' iti ślokena pānopalakṣitabhogakartṛtvavarṇanātu, preryatva-prerakatvaprati- niyatarasā - prayojyakartṛtva prayojaka kartṛtvābhyāṃ jīvātmaparamātmarūpatattadarthānuguṇabhoktṛtva mātra viva- kṣāyattā, athavā – yadvā, chatrinītyā syāt – chatrighaṭitasamudāyapraviṣṭatvamātrataḥ chatripada- prayogavat, karmaphalapatṛghaṭita samudāyapraviṣṭe paramātmanyapi taduktiḥ bhavet ||

satvaṃ svādvacyanaśnan jña iti vibhajanāt paijayadhītaṃ tu sattvaṃ buddhiḥ prāṇo'thaveti tvṛtamiha 'pibatorjīva' ekastayo' śva' | maivaṃ jantau 'ca' saccaśrutiriyamucitā karmabhuṅnā'pyanaśnan
tatpraśnaprakramo'nyāśaya idamapi cā bhāṣi pūrvāparādyaiḥ ||9||

'sattvam – sattvākhyaṃ vastu, svādu atti- āsvādanapūrvakaṃ bhuṅkte, jñaḥ – jñānā- śrayībhūto'rthaḥ, anaśnan'–bhoktṛtvābhāvavān', iti vibhajanāt — evaṃrītyā paiṅgiśrutau vivicya kathanāt, paṅgayadhītaṃ sattvaṃ tu — paiṅgiśrutiparipaṭhitaṃ sattvapadābhidheyaṃ tu, buddhiḥ athavā prāṇaḥ- iti tu–buddhirūpo'rthaḥ prāṇākhyo'rtho vā, bhavitumarhati iti hetoreva, iha — kaṭhavallīgate asmin prakaraṇe, ṛtaṃ pibato: 'ṛtaṃ pibantau' iti śrutyuktayoḥ, tayoḥ ekaḥ sattva- jñaśabdārthayoranyataraḥ, jīvaḥ - jīvātmā; ( iti pūrvapakṣe ) maivam – uktā rīti : nocitā | ( yataḥ) iyaṃ saccaśrutiḥ – upadarśitā sampadātmikā śrutiḥ, jantau ca ucitā prāṇi- rūpārthabodhikā ca dṛṣṭā'sti | karmabhuk - karmaphalabhoktṛtvena śrutyādisiddho jīvaḥ, anaśnannapi na—abhoktṛtvavāṃśca na bhavitumarhati | tat – tasmāddhetoḥ, praśnakramaḥ – naciketaḥ kartṛkapraśna- prakāraḥ, anyāśayaḥ– vilakṣaṇatātparyavān | idamapi ca – vilakṣaṇaṃ praśna tātparyamapi, pūrvāparādyaiḥ- tātparyanirṇāyakaiḥ upakramopasaṃhāra dibhiḥ abhāṣi - śrībhāṣye samyak pratyapādi ||9||

3) adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ

adhikaraṇe – 'yasya brahma ca kṣatraṃ cobhe bhavata odanaḥ' ityādivākye karmanimittaka-

bhoktṛtva śravaṇāt, 'attā jīva eva – ityāśaṅkaya,

mṛtyūpasecanatvasāmarthya siddha carācarādanasya jagadupasaṃhārarūpatvāt tasya ca paramātmā-
sādhāraṇatvāt, attā paramātmā - ityuktam ||

4) nayasaṅgatimālikā

bhoktṛtvaṃ nāsti cedantaryāmigo jīva eva saḥ | attā syāditi śaṅkātaḥ saṅgatiḥ pratyudāhṛtiḥ ||42|| || iti adhikaraṇam ||2||

antarādhikaraṇam || 1-2-3||
1) śārīrakanyāyakalāpasaṅgrahaḥ
akṣyantarastveṣa iti prasiddhayā jīvo'thavā vimbamathā'pi daivam |
ityapyasat syādamṛtatvaśabdāt sukhaśruteśvāpyanavasthiteśca ||23||

14.

akṣyantarastu – chāndogyaśrutau ya eṣo'kṣiṇi' ityasyām akṣimaṇḍalāntarvartitayā pratipanno'rthaḥ, eṣa iti prasiddhayā – 'ya eṣa:' iti prasiddhi dyotakaśabdasamabhivyāhārarūpeṇa jñāpakena, jīvaḥ śarīrādhiṣṭhātā karmavaśyo jīvo bhavet | athavā bimbam - yadvā, cakṣurantaḥ- paridṛśyamānaṃ pratibimbaṃ vā bhavet | athā'pi yadvā daivam - devatāviśeṣo vā bhavet,

_

ityapi — akṣyantaratayokte jīvātmatva- pratibimbatva - devatā viśeṣatvānyatamāśaṅkanam, asat syāt – na sādhu bhavet | amṛtatvaśabdāt - 'amṛtamabhayam' iti akṣipuruṣasya amṛtatvokteḥ jñāpakāt sukhaśruteḥ - 'kaṃ kham' iti anavacchinnānandarūpatvokteḥ, anavasthiteśca - pratibimbānāṃ nairantaryeṇa cakṣurgola ke avasthānābhāvācca ||23||

ya eṣo .... śrūyamāṇaḥ - 'ya eṣo'kṣiṇi puruṣo dṛśyate' iti chāndogyaśrutipratipanno'rthaḥ, yadvṛttādeḥ — yacchabdaprayogādirūpāt prasiddhi dyotakasamabhivyāhārāt jñāpakāt jīvaḥ dehādhiṣṭhātā jīvātmā vā yadvā – athavā, akṣṇordevatā arka: netrādhidevatā bhūtaḥ ādityaḥ, athavā pakṣāntare, tatra dṛśyā – cakṣurgolake dṛśyamānā, pratikṛtiḥ - draṣṭṛpuruṣāntarapratibimbo vā, iti cet — evamāśaṅkanam, na na sādhu | ' etadrahma, etadevā'mṛtam idamabhayam, kaṃ kham' ityādyadhīteḥ uktervākyaiḥ akṣipuruṣe brahmatva - amṛtatva abhayatvānāṃ (tathā) sukha rūpatva-ākāśarūpatvayorapi ca śrutau paripaṭhanāt saṃyadvāmatvamukhyaiḥ saṃyadvāmatvaprabhṛtibhiḥ guṇaiḥ paramapuruṣāsādhāraṇaliṅgaiḥ, sthitiniyatibalāt – cakṣuṣi niyatāvasthānarūpāt dṛḍhā liṅgāt, aciradyuktiśca - akṣyantarapuruṣavedanena brahmaniṣṭhamātragamanayogyasya arcirādivartmanaḥ upadeśācca muktaprāpyaḥ paramapuruṣa eva akṣyantarvartitayopāsyaḥ puruṣaḥ, na jīvārka pratikṛtayaḥ || 10 || svātantryottasitāsu śrutiṣu na phaladasyaiva vedyatvavādaḥ kalyāṇālokanādekhi vidhivalato vedanasyā'rthavattvāt | tasmādakṣyantarasthaḥ pratikṛtipuruṣo yujyate pūrvapakṣe
seyaṃ pūrvāparāsvapyadhikṛtiṣu yathāsambhavaṃ nītiruhyā || 11 ||

svātantryottaṃsitāsu tarka vyavahārādyanadhīnatayā pravṛtti-nivṛttijanakatvarūpasvātantryā- laṅkṛtāsu, śrutipu – vedarūpāsu bākṣu, kalyāṇālokanāderiva maṅgaḻāvahasya darpaṇadarśanādeḥ karmaṇa iva, vidhibalato vedanasya vidhimātrādhīnasya upāsanasya, arthavattvāt — phalavatvāt, phaladasyaiva vedyatvavādaḥ phalapradānasamarthasyaiva, vastunaḥ upāsyatvamiti niyamo nāstyeva | tasmāt — uktāddhetoḥ, akṣyantarasthaḥ - akṣyantarvartitayā upāsyatvena vihitaḥ, pūrvapakṣe- pūrvapakṣarītyā, pratikṛtipuruṣo yujyate pratibimbātmakaḥ puruṣa evetyetat yuktaṃ bhavati | seyaṃ nītiḥ—ukteyaṃ pūrvapakṣotthāpikā nīti: ( śrutyāssvedite'rthe yuktāyuktatvacintādūrīkaraṇena pravṛttirūpā ), pūrvāparāsu adhikṛtiṣvapi prāktana paścāttanādhikaraṇeṣvapi yathāsambhavam- `tattatsthalīya viṣayādikamanurudhya, udyā – spaṣṭamanuktā'pi pūrvapakṣotthāpakatvena cintanīyā || 11|| pūrvanyāye'gnividyā purata upanatā madhyatastvatra tasmāt

tadvanna brahmavidyā'nugatiriti bhavedakṣividyā tato'nyā |
maivaṃ, vicchittiraṅgairna hi bhavati mitā cā'ṅgatā'nekādhā'syāḥ proktaṃ ca brahmavidyā'nuguṇamiha phalaṃ 'prākca' na brahmadṛṣṭiḥ ||12||

prāktu – ā.
S B II-59
