pūrvanyāye – attradhikaraṇīya viṣayavākye kaṭhavalīye, agnividyā – 'sattvamagniṃ svargyam iti praśnamārabhya, 'etamagniṃ tavaiva pravakṣyanti janāsaḥ' ityantenokā agnividyā purata upanatā - brahmavidyātaḥ pūrvameva sthitā | atra tu – antarādhikaraṇīya viṣayavākye tu chāndogyīye, madhyata upanatā — brahmavidyāyā madhye sthitā | tasmāt – uktāddhetoḥ, tadvat — kaṭhavallīya viṣaya- vākyayoriva, atra – antarā dhikaraṇīya viṣayavākyeṣu, brahmavidyā'nugatiḥ - brahmavidyāyāḥ avicche- denānuvṛttiḥ, netinaiva vartata iti, akṣividyā – 'ya eṣo'kṣiṇi puruṣo dṛśyate' ityuktā akṣividyā, tato'nyā– prāṇo brahma ke brahma khaṃ brahma' ityuktabrahmavidyā'pekṣayā bhinnā, (ityevaṃ pūrvapakṣe ), maitram —– evaṃ varṇanaṃ na samīcīnam | aṅgaiḥ – aṅgatvena śrutivihitaiḥ padārthaiḥ, vicchittiḥ — aṅgisvarūpānanuvṛttiḥ, na hi bhavati na sañjāyate ityetat pūrvatantraprasiddhameva | asyāśca – agnividyāyāśca chāndogyīyāyāḥ, aṅgatā – brahmavidyopakārakatā, bahudhā mitā- anekaprakāreṇa pramāṇasiddhā, iha proktaṃ phalaṃ ca - agnividyopadeśānte śrutam āyuḥ kīrtyādi - rūpaṃ phalaṃ ca brahmavidyā'nuguṇam - brahmavidyā niṣpādakameva, na tadvirodhi | prāk ca- ācāryopadeśāt pūrvam agnikartṛkopadeśaśca na brahmadṛṣṭiḥ – abrahmaṇi prāṇādau brahmadṛṣṭividhirūpo na bhavati, tathā adṛṣṭatvāt iti || 12||

3) adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ

antarādhikaraṇe ' ya eṣo'kṣiṇi puruṣo dṛśyate' ityādivākye cakṣuṣyavasthitasya prasiddhavat nirdeśāt, tatprasiddheśca pratyakṣayuktyāgamaiḥ pratibimbajīvādityādiṣūpapatteḥ, 'akṣipuruṣaḥ teṣvanya- tamaḥ' ityāśaṅkaya,
saṃyadvāmatvādīnāṃ paramātmanyevopapatteḥ, tasya cakṣuṣyavasthānasya 'yaścakṣuṣi tiṣṭhan' ityādiṣu prasiddhatvāt, 'akṣipuruṣaḥ paramātmā' – ityuktam ||

4) nayasaṅgatimālikā
aturdurdarśatāyāṃ syādakṣidṛśyaḥ sudarśanaḥ | pratyudāhṛtisaṅgatyā'thāntarādhikriyodayaḥ ||43|| || iti antarādhikaraṇam ||3||
15) antaryāmyadhikaraṇam ||1-2-4||
1) śārīrakanyāyakalāpasaṅgrahaḥ
draṣṭeti śabdādiha vākyaśeṣe draṣṭṛntarasya pratiṣedhataśca | kṣetrajñamantaryamayantamāhetyetanna jāgartyamṛtatvaśabdāt || 24||

iha antaryāmitrāhmaṇasambandhini, vākyaśeṣe 'adṛṣṭo draṣṭā' ityādirūpe pradhānavākyāṅga- bhūte vākye, draṣṭeti śabdāt — cakṣuradhīna darśana kartṛtvabodhakāt 'draṣṭā' iti śabdāt jīvatvajñāpa- kāt, (tathā) draṣṭṛntarasya - anyasya darśanakartuḥ, pratiṣedhataśca tatraiva vidyamānena 'nā'nyo'- to'sti draṣṭā' iti vākyakhaṇḍena niṣedhācca jñāpakāt antaryamayantam – sarvatrāntaḥ praviśya niyamanasvabhāvamartham, kṣetrajñamāha — jīva rūpamevāha, ( antaryāmibrāhmaṇarūpā śrutiḥ), ityetat - evamāśaṅkanam, na jāgarti – na pratiṣṭhitaṃ bhavati, amṛtatvaśabdāt - antaḥ praviśya niyamanaśīle vastuni paramātmāsāghāraṇāmṛtatvabodhakāt 'antaryāmyamṛta:' ityevaṃrūpāt śrutigatāt śabdāt ||24|| 2) taccasāraḥ

tamo nāma dravyaṃ 'bahala' viralaṃ mecakacala
'pratīmaḥ kenāspi kvacidapi na bādhaśca dadṛśe | ataḥ kalpyo hetuḥ pramitirapi śābdī vijayate
nirālokaṃ cakṣuḥ prathayati 'hi' darśanavāt ||28||

bahala-viralam – sāndratva- sāntarālatvobhayavat mecaka- calam - nīlarūpavattva - calanā- śrayatvobhayavat, tamo nāma dravyaṃ pratīmaḥ – 'tama:' iti sarvajanaprasiddhaṃ vastu, dravyatvena pratyakṣayāmaḥ | kenāspi yena kenā'pi puruṣeṇa, kvacidapi yasmin kasminnapi deśe, yadā kadāpi vā kāle, bādhaśva – 'tamo nīlatvābhāvavat tamo niścalam' ityevaṃ rūpa- kriyāniṣedhabuddhiśca na dadṛśe naivānubhavasiddhā | (tathā) pūrvajñāne bhramatvānumāpaka: kāraṇa- doṣo'pi nā'nubhūyate | ataḥ bādhakapratyayasya kāraṇadoṣasya ca abhāvāt guṇa-karmavattayā ( dravyatve siddhe sati ) hetuḥ - tamasaḥ utpattau tamaḥ pratyakṣotpattau ca kāraṇabhūto'rthaḥ kalpyaḥ- anumeyaḥ | pramitirapi śābdī - pramātmakaṃ jñānamapi śāstrajanyam, vijayate – pratipakṣanirāsakaṃ bhavati | hi – yataḥ, nirālokam - ālokā sahakṛtam, cakṣuḥ cakṣurnāmakamindriyameva, tat prathayati–tamorūpaṃ vastu prakāśayati, (idaṃ ca ) darśanavaśāt —ālokaṃ vinaiva rūpābhāvasya tejo'bhāvasya ca pratyakṣīkaraṇāt, (tadānuguṇyena siddhyati) ||28||

3) adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ antaryāmī sa jīvo bahuvidhakaraṇāyattadhīvṛttyanūkteḥ
nā'nyo draṣṭeti cokteriti yadi na niyantrantarasya vyudāsāt | draṣṭṛtvādyaṃ tu tattadviṣayaghaṭitadhīrūpamīśe hi mukhyaṃ
taddharmāḥ kāṇvamādhyandinapari paṭhitāstasya cā''tmā śarīram ||13||

saḥ antaryāmī – 'yaścakṣuṣi tiṣṭhan' ityādinā cakṣurādāvavasthitipūrvakaṃ tattanniyantṛtvena antaryāmibrāhmaṇapratipādyaḥ, jīvaḥ jīvātmaiva, bahuvidhakaraṇāyattadhīvṛttyanūkteḥ – 'draṣṭā śrotā ' ityādinā cakṣurādinānāvidhendriyādhīnajñānātmaka vṛttimattāyāḥ anuvādāt (tathā) nā'nyo draṣṭā iti ukteśca vākyaśeṣe 'nā'nyo'tosti draṣṭā' ityasmin kāraṇāyattadarśanavataḥ jīvātiriktasya niṣedhokteśca jñāpakāt iti yadi – evaṃ śaṅkanam na naiva sādhu niyantrantarasya vyudāsāt - 'nānyo'to'sti' iti śrutyā, 'pṛthivyādiṣu sarvatra niyantṛtayā'vasthitasya paramapuruṣasya anyo niyantā nāsti' iti jñāpanāt, tattadviṣayaghaṭitadhīrūpam - rūpa - tadāśrayaviṣayaka - jñānavattva-śabda-tadāśrayaviṣayaka jñānavattvādyātmakam, draṣṭṛtvādyaṃ tu - draṣṭā śrotā' ityādyuktaṃ draṣṭṛtva śrotṛtvādikaṃ param īśe hi ! – sarvaniyantari paramātmanyeva khalu, mukhyam - anupacaritam | taddharmāḥ – sarvaviśvaniyamanādirūpāḥ paramātmagatā : asādhāraṇāḥ dharmāḥ, kāṇva - mādhyandinapari- paṭhitāḥ–kāṇvaśākhīyaiḥ 'yo vijñāne tiṣṭhan' ityādighaṭitayā praṇālyā, mādhyandinaśākhīyaiśca 'ya ātmani tiṣṭhan' ityādighaṭitayā praṇālyā ca spaṣṭārthatayā adhītāḥ santi | tasya ca tathā antaryāmitayoktasya paramātmanaśca, ātmā - cetanavargaḥ, śarīram - ādheyatva - vidheyatva- śeṣatvavat ||13||
sthānaikyādatra śākhādvayaparipaṭhitāvātma-vijñānaśabdau
ekārthāvityakampyaṃ tadapi kathayato buddhimevetyapārtham | lokāmnāyaprasiddhayoranugamata imau cetane hyekatānau
bādhaḥ kenā'pi nā'smin bhavati ca sa ta ityādibhissāmarasyam ||14|| atra—bṛhadāraṇyakagate antaryāmibrāhmaṇe, śākhādvayaparipaṭhitau-kāṇvamādhyandinaśākhā- dhītau, ātma-vijñānaśabdau – 'ya ātmani tiṣṭhan' iti 'ātma' śabdaḥ, 'yo vijñāne tiṣṭhan ' iti ‘vijñāna’śabdaścetyubhau, sthānaikyāt — tvacaḥ anantaraṃ, retasaḥ (tvacaḥ anantaraṃ, retasaḥ pūrvaṃ ca vidyamānatārūpasya

1 ca-ā. 2 paṭhitigatā - ā.

sthānasya ekatvāt, ekārthau – abhinnārthābhidhāyaka, ityakampyam – ityetat avipratipacyā- abhyupagamanīyam | tadapi - tathā satyapi, buddhimeva kathayata iti - antaḥkaraṇameva bodhayata iti, apārtham-aprasiddhārthakavarṇanamevaitat hi yataḥ, imau - ātma-vijñānaśabdāvubhau lokāmnāya - prasiddhayoḥ–lokaprasiddheḥ vedaprasiddheśva, anugamataḥ — ekarūpeṇānuvṛttyā, cetane jīvātmani, ekatānau - mukhyavṛttyā pratipādakau, asmin - 'ātma-vijñānaśabdayorabhinnabodhyakatve, kenāpi - prabalena kenā'pi ca pramāṇena śruti - smṛtyādyātmakena, bādho na bhavati – pratibandho nā'sti | sata ityādibhiḥ sa ta ātmā antaryāmyamṛtaḥ ' 'eṣa ta ātmā antaryāmymṛtaḥ' ityatra, 'te ātmā' iti vyatirekanirdeśa paraiḥ vākyaissaha, sāmarasyaṃ ca bhavati — ekatātparyakatā ca siddhyati ||14||

4) adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ

antaryāmyadhikaraṇe—'yaḥ pṛthivyāṃ tiṣṭhan' ityādivākyoktaḥ antaryāmī, 'draṣṭā śrotā ' iti vākye karaṇāyattajñānatvāvagamāt yogaviśeṣayukto jīva eva – ityāśaṅkaya,
etenaiva vākyena sarvaniyamanasya paramātmadharmasya vyapadeśāt, draṣṭrādiśabdānāṃ rūpādi- sākṣātkāriparatayā teṣāṃ paramātmanyupapatteśca, 'antaryāmī paramātmā' – ityuktam ||
5) nayasaṅgatimālikā akṣisthapuruṣasyaiva brahmatvasyopapādikā | athā'ntaryāmyadhikṛtiḥ tenopodghātasaṅgatiḥ ||44||
|| iti antaryāmyadhikaraṇam ||4||

adṛśyatvādiguṇakā dhikaraṇam ||1-2-5||
1) śārīrakanyāyakalāpasaṅgrahaḥ
sthūlatva - dṛśyatvaniṣedhanena sūkṣmaṃ tvadṛśyatvaguṇaṃ pradhānam |
kṣetrajña evākṣaramityasat syāt pratijñayā tatparataḥ paratvāt ||25||
sthūlatva.... nena - 'susūkṣmam' iti sthūlatvasya, 'adeśyam' iti dṛśyatvasya ca vyāvṛttyā, sūkṣmaṃ tu — 'susūkṣmam' iti śrutau sūkṣmapadābhidheyaṃ vastu tu, adṛśyatvaguṇaṃ pradhānam - atīndriyaṃ mūlaprakṛtisaṃjñaṃ vastveva, akṣaram tadvākyasthākṣarapadavācyaṃ tu kṣetrajña eva-jīva eva, iti — evamaṅgīkaraṇam asat syāt asādhveva bhavet, pratijñayā – 'atha parā yayā tadakṣaramadhigamyate' iti pratijñāvākyena bodhyasyā'kṣarasya, (yadvā) eka vijñānena sarvavijñānasya

pratijñārūpeṇa jñāpakena tatparataḥ paratvāt – avyākṛtāpekṣayā utkṛṣṭāt kṣetrajñasamaṣṭipuruṣādapi

utkṛṣṭatvāt bhūtayonerakṣarasya | ataḥ bhūtayonyakṣaraṃ paraṃ brahmaiva ||25||

2) adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ
dṛśyatvāderniṣedho vikṛtimati bhavatyakṣare saṃnikarṣāt
pañcamyuktākṣaraṃ tat tadavadhikaparaḥ pañcaviṃśo'stu mā bhūt | sarvajñatvādidṛṣṭeḥ prathamasamuditaṃ tvakṣaraṃ brahma śuddhaṃ
paścāduktaṃ tu jīvādikamavadhitayā bhedatastatparokteḥ ||15||
dṛśyatvāderniṣedhaḥ - muṇḍakāntargatena 'adeśyamagrāhyam' ityādinā dṛśyatva - grāhyatvādeḥ abhāvajñāpanam, sannikarṣāt - niṣedhyadṛśyatvāśraya pṛthivyā dismṛtirūpāt sambandhāt, vikṛtimati akṣare — vikāraśabdavācyāvasthāyogye pradhāne eva bhavati - uktaṃ bhavati | (aṃtaḥ) pañcamyuktā - 'kṣaram – 'akṣarātparata:' ityatra 'akṣarāt' iti pañcamyantenākṣarapadena pratipāditam, tat- pradhānameva bhavitumarhati | tadavadhikaparaḥ – tādṛśākṣarāvadhikaparatvāśrayaḥ arthastu, pañcatriṃśaḥ astu - tattveṣu pañcaviṃśako jīva eva bhavatu (sāṅkhyoktaḥ) na tu paraṃ brahma (ityevamāśaṅkanam ), mā bhūt — na sambhavati | sarvajñatvādidṛṣṭeḥ - 'yaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarvavit ityādikayā śrutyā, bhūtayonyakṣare sarvajñatvādeḥ paramātmaikagatasya guṇasya pratipādanāt prathamasamuditam akṣaraṃ tu- 'tadakṣaram' iti ādau pratipannam akṣarapadavācyaṃ tu, śuddhaṃ brahmasvarūpataḥ svabhāvataśca vikāra- rahitaṃ paraṃ brahmaiva | paścaduktaṃ tu dvitīyavāram 'akṣarāt parataḥ' ityuktākṣarapadābhidheyaṃ tu, jīvādikam - jīvo vā bhavatu pradhānaṃ vā bhavatu | avadhitayā bhedataḥ - paratvarūpotkarṣāvadhi- tvanibandhanaṃ bhedaṃ nimittīkṛtya, tatparokteḥ tadavadhikotkarṣātmakaparatvāśrayasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ 'parataḥ paraḥ' ityanena uktatvāt || 15||

3) adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ

adṛśyatvādiguṇakādhikaraṇe - 'atha parā yayā tadakṣaramadhigamyate' iti vākye, 'adreśyam' ityādinā niṣidhyamānasya dyāvāpṛthivyādyacenanagatadṛśyatvādeḥ pṛthivyādisajātīyasūkṣmācidupa. sthāpakatvāt ; akṣaram pradhānam ; akṣarāt parataḥ paraśca, jīvaḥ ityāśaṅkaya,

sarvajñatvādīnāṃ paramātmadharmāṇāṃ pratipādanāt akṣaraḥ, paramātmā | akṣarāt avyaktāt paraḥ yaḥ pratyagātmā jīvaḥ, tasmādapi paraśca sa paramātmā | ata eva 'akṣarātparataḥ paraḥ ' iti siddhāntitam ||

4) nayasaṅgatimālikā adṛśyatvādhikaraṇe draṣṭṛtvāderanuktitaḥ |
prakṛtirvā pumānveti śaṅkātaḥ pratyudāhṛtiḥ || 45||
|| iti adṛśyatvādiguṇakādhikaraṇam ||5||

pādenetthamanenā'rthājjagatastaccharīratā |
sāmānādhikaraṇyena tadanyena ca sādhitā || 28 ||
anena pādena – dvitīyena caraṇena, ittham arthāt — uktaprakārādarthāt, jagataḥ - viśvasya taccharīratā - paramātmānaṃ prati vapuvam sāmānādhikaraṇyena—' sarva brahma' iti ekavibhaktikanirdeśena, tadanyena – 'ma ātmā' ityādivyadhikaraṇanirdeśena ca sādhitā-

nirvyūḍhaṃ vartate ||28||

2) adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ svarlokādityavātāmbarasalila mahīrūpamṛrdhādiklatyā

dhyeyo vaiśvānarātmā 'sthira bahu viśaya : ' śabda liṅgādisāmyāt | maivaṃ; brahmetyadhīterbhuvanatanutayā yogatastvagniśabdo
vaiśiṣṭyādvā kriyāṅga svavapuṣi paradhīrgārhapatyādidhīśca || 16 ||

svarlokā.... klṛptyā – suvarloka-sūrya-anila vyoma-ap pṛthivyātmakānāṃ mūrdha - netra- prāṇa- madhyakāya-mūtrasthāna-caraṇānāṃ sthānaviśeṣāṇāṃ kalpanapūrvakam, dhyeyaḥ – 'yastvetamevaṃ prādeśamātra.... 'rvaiśvānaramupāste',....ityevam upāsanīyatayā upadiṣṭaḥ, vaiśvānarātmā vaiśvānarākhyaḥ ātmā, chāndogye yaḥ asti, saḥ, sthirabahuviśaya : – durnivāra bahukoṭika saṃśayāspadaḥ | śabda liṅgādi- sāmyāt– jāṭharāgnyādiṣu caturṣvartheṣu vaiśvānaraśabdasya sāmyāt, (tathā) jāṭharāgnau prāṇāhutyā - dhāratārūpasya tejorūpe bhautikāmau devatāyāṃ ca aparicchinnatārūpasya paramātmani pāpapradāha- katārūpasya ca tattalliṅgasya sadbhāvāt ityevamāśaṅkane prāpte, maivam – etādṛśī āśaṅkā nocitā, 'brahma' ityadhīteḥ - 'kona ātmā ? kiṃ brahma ? iti brahmaśabdasthaivokte:, 'brahma' śabdasya asati bādhake paramātmamātraparatvāt, (tathā) bhuvanatanutayā - 'mūdhaiva sutejā:' ityādinokta jagaccharīra- katvena ca | agniśabdastu – 'sa eṣo'gnirvaiśvānaraḥ' iti vaiśvānare agniśabdaprayogastu, yogataḥ ayaṃ nayatīti avayavavyutpattimavalambyaiva vā, vaiśiṣṭyādvā śarīra - śarīribhāva- rūpāt agnivaiśiṣṭyādvā, svavapuṣi - upāsakasya svaśarīre, paradhīḥ paramātmāvayava kalpanātmikā dhīḥ, 'mūdhaiva sutejā:' ityādinoktā, (tathā) svavapuṣi gārhapatyādidhīśva – 'ura eva vedi...... hṛdayaṃ gārhapatyaḥ' ityādinopadiṣṭā hṛdayādau gārhapatyādikalpanātmikā matiśca, kriyāṅgam- brahmavidyāṅgabhūtaprāṇāhutyādikriyopakārakam || 16 |

anyasminnanyadṛṣṭyā na bhavati viduṣāṃ vā'pi niśśreyasāpti- stasmādvaiśvānaro'sau na para 'iti' phalaṃ ' cānna' siddhayādi maivam | brahmaiva hyanyadṛṣṭayanvitaghaṭitamiha brahmaśabdādyabādhāt
sarvāghadhvaṃsa uktaḥ phalamapi paramaṃ brahma ca vyāptamannam || 17 ||

anyasmin anyadṛṣṭyā - vastvantare vastvantaratvopāsanarūpayā buddhayā, kvā'pi— dṛṣṭayupāsane kutrā'pi niśśreyasāptiḥ - mokṣasiddhiḥ, viduṣām – dṛṣṭayupāsanopadeśaparāṇāṃ jñāni- nām, na bhavati naiva sammatā bhavitumarhati | tasmāt - dṛṣṭaṭyupāsanasya niśśreyasāsādhakatvāt, asau vaiśvānaraḥ vaiśvānara vidyāyāmupāsyatvenopadiṣṭaḥ, vaiśvānarātmā na paraḥ – paramapuruṣo na bhavitumarhati, iti — ato hetoḥ phalaṃ ca-vaiśvānarātmopāsana labhyaṃ prayojanamapi, anna- siddhayādi - phalaśrutau jñātam annalābhādikameva ; na muktiḥ, ityāśaṅkane prāpte, maivam - evamāśaṅkanaṃ na sādhu, hi— yataḥ, iha — asmin prakaraṇe, brahmaiva paraṃ brahmaiva, anyadṛṣṭayanvita- ghaṭitam – ghumūrdhatvādidṛṣṭiviṣayasva svarūpaghaṭitam, dhyeyatvenopadiṣṭam ; na anyat, brahma- śabdādyabādhāt – 'kiṃ brahma ?' 'ko na ātmā ?' iti ca prakrāntayoḥ brahmātmaśabdayoḥ abādhato'nvayena tattyāgāyogāt sarvāghadhvaṃsaḥ sarveṣāṃ pāpmanāṃ samūlanāśaḥ, uktaḥ - 'sarve pāpmānaḥ pradūyante' iti upāsanaphalatayoktaḥ | api - kiṃ ca, phalam annam – upāsanaprayoja - natvenoktamannapadābhidheyamapi vyāptam — sarvatra sambaddham, vibhu, paramaṃ brahma ca – 'ahamanna- mahamannam' iti taittirīyoktaṃ paraṃ brahmaiva || 17 ||

_

triṣvatropāsitṝṇāṃ mitahṛdayaguhākṣyantaraścintya ukto viśvāntaryāmitādervipulaparimitiścintanīyastraye'tha |
ṣaṭsu brahmātmaśabdau puruṣapadamapi kṣetra - tajjñaprapaśca-
vyāvṛtte viśvahetau prakaraṇa niyamānnāmavṛttyā namanti || 18 ||

atra tripu - ādyeṣu triṣvadhikaraṇeṣu, upāsitṛṇām mokṣārthopāsakānām, mita- hṛdayaguhākṣyantaraḥ – (paramātmā) alpaparimāṇahṛdaya guhā. akṣyantassthatvena, cintya uktaḥ --- 'antahṛdaye' 'guhā hitam ' ' eṣo'ntarakṣiṇi' iti vākyaiḥ upāsyatvena upadiṣṭaḥ, atha traye- tatpaścāttane'dhikaraṇatraye tu, viśvāntaryāmitādeḥ nikhilajagadantaryāmitvādikaṃ hetukṛtya,

vipulaparimitiḥ – tadviruddhamahāparimāṇavattayā, cintanīyaḥ - upāsyatayoktaḥ | ṣaṭsu - etatpādīyepu ṣaḍadhikaraṇeṣu, brahmātmaśabdau puruṣapadamapi 'brahma, ātmā, puruṣaḥ' itīmā ni padāni, kṣetra - tajjñaprapañca vyāvṛtte - kṣetra kṣetrajñātmakajagadvilakṣaṇe, viśvahetau - ata eva tannimitto- pādānabhūte paramātmani, prakaraṇa niyamāt — paramātmaprakaraṇāyattāt niyantraṇāt, nāmavṛttyā - rūḍhyātmikayā vṛttyā, namanti saṃjñārūpā bhavanti, || 18 ||
tajjatvādehiṃ sarvaṃ jagadabhigaditaṃ brahmabhāvena pūrva
sarvāntaryāmitā ca prabhavituruditā sarvataddehatā ca | tasmādvivaikyabādhaprabhṛtibahuvidhāpārthavambhramyamāṇa-
kṣudrakṣīboktijālaṃ nikhilamiha nayaiḥ sūtrakāro nirāsa || 19 ||

tajjatvādeḥ – tasmājjātatva- tasmi~llīyamānatva- tadadhīnaprāṇanatvāni hetūkṛtya, pūrvam – sarvatra- prasiddhyadhikaraṇe, sarva jagat – akhilaṃ viśvam, brahmabhāvena gaditam - brahmatādātmyavattayā nirṇītam, prabhavituḥ – sarvajagatkāraṇasya paramātmanaḥ sarvāntaryāmitā sarvaniyantṛtvarūpā sarvātmatā, sarvataddehatā ca – akhilataccharīrakatā ca uditā antaryāmibrāhmaṇāditaḥ, samarthitā, tasmāt — tato hetoḥ, nikhilam – samastamapi, viśvaikya .... jālam – 'viśvaṃ brahma ca ekameva ' 'brahmaiva satyam' 'viśdhaṃ bādhitam' ityevamādinānā prakāra śrutiviruddhārtha cintanakṛtabhrānti- paramparāpraviṣṭānāṃ kṣudraṃ mattapralāpavadbhāsamānaṃ ca vacanajātam, iha nayaiḥ - etatpādīyairadhikaraṇaiḥ, sūtrakāraḥ – bādarāyaṇaḥ, nirāsa - nirācakāra || 19||

svādhīnāśeṣasattā-sthiti-yatanatayā sarvabhāvena tiṣṭhan

grastāśeṣo'kṣinitya sthitira khilatanuḥ kalpitāgnyādigātraḥ | svarlokādyaṅgavaiśvānaramati viṣayo lakṣaṇasyādimasya
proktaḥ pāde dvitīye śrutinikara śiraḥ śekharaḥ śrīnivāsaḥ || 20 ||

ādimasya lakṣaṇasya – prathamasyādhyāyasya dvitīye pāde – dvitīye caraṇe, svādhīnā... yatanatayā – (bhagavataḥ ) svāyattasarvajagatsambandhisvarūpalābha- kālāntarānuvṛtti- pravṛttikatvena, bhāvena tiṣṭhan – sarvajagattādātmyena vartamānaḥ, grasta śeṣaḥ - kabalīkṛta nikhila carācaraḥ, akṣi- nitya sthitiḥ– dehinām akṣimaṇḍale sthirāvasthānavān, akhilatanuḥ - sarvaśarīrakaḥ, kalpitāgnyādigātraḥ – 'agnirmūrdhe' ti upāsanārtha kalpita| gnipadavācya dyumūrdhatva | diguṇaviśiṣṭaḥ,

bhūtaśca, śrutinikaraśiraḥśekharaḥ - sarvaśrutyantabhūṣaṇabhūtaḥ, śrīnivāsaḥ - tannāmā bhagavān nārāyaṇaḥ, ' proktaḥ - vicārya samyaṅnirdhārita: (bādarāyaṇena ) || 20 ||

3) adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ

vaiśvānarādhikaraṇe – 'ātmānamevemaṃ vaiśvānaram' ityādivākye, 'vaiśvānara' śabdasya, jāṭharabhūta tṛtīya- devatā- paramātmasu aviśeṣeṇa prayogadarśanāt, 'paramātmā' iti anirṇaya ḥ—

ityāśaṅkaya,

sādhāraṇasya 'vaiśvānara' śabdasya, mumukṣubhiḥ pṛcchyamānatvādibhiḥ paramātmaliṅgai: viśeṣya- māṇatvāt, 'paramātmā' iti śakyanirṇayaḥ iti siddhāntitam ||

4) nayasaṅgatimālikā
prāgviśvarūpopanyāsaḥ dyumūrdhatvādinā kṛtaḥ | tatklatyā cintyate vaiśvānara vidyā'pavādataḥ || 46 || itthaṃ hyadhikriyāpaṭukaṃ peṭikādvayalakṣaṇam | adhyāye prathame dvaitīyīkapāde susaṅgatam || 47 || || iti vaiśvānarādhikaraṇam || 6 ||
|| iti prathame'dhyāye dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ ||

-0-

yuktā, sphuṭam ātmaśabdāt — spa śrutāt 'tamevaikaṃ jānathātmānam' iti nirupādhikātmatva- bodhakāt 'ātma'śabdāt muktopasṛpyavyapadeśataśca - (tathā) ' tadā vidvān puṇya-pāpe.... sāmyamupaiti' iti muktānāṃ paramagatitvasya mukhyatayā pratipāditatvādapi | ( ataḥ paramātmaiva dya - pṛthivyāyatanam ; na jīvaḥ) || 29 ||

-spaṣṭaṃ

2) adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ
spaṣṭairjīvādiliṅgairyutamiha hi ! vacaḥ sādhyate brahmaniṣṭhaṃ madhye'trā'dhikriyoktistriṣu kimiti na tattatprasaṅgāttadukteḥ | kiścāsyāmardhalokāyatikanirasanaṃ prastutārthopayuktaṃ

|

paraviṣayakeṣu saptasvadhikaraṇeṣvapi tātparyabhūmiḥ paryavasitapratipādyakāṣṭhā, sarvādhāraḥ - svetara- samasta jagatsthitikāraṇam, svamahimanilayaḥ svamahima pratiṣṭhitaḥ, sa ātmā para ātmā, tatsiddhayai—ananyādhāratvasahita sarvādhāratāsampantyartham, iha – etatpādīyākṣarādhikaraṇādau, kathi- tam — pratipāditam | śāsanādyam – sarvapraśāsitṛtvādikam mithaḥsyūtam – anyonyam apekṣitārthopapādakatayā saṃśliṣṭamiti, ālocanīyam - cintanīyam | paṣṭhapramitanayamitam - etatpādīye ṣaṣṭhe pramitādhikaraṇe nirṇītam, sarveśatvaṃ ca bhūtabhavyeśitṛtvaṃ tu, paścimanyāya- rakṣyam –antimāghikaraṇena arthāntaratvā dinayena paripoṣaṇīyamiti || 2 ||

siddhaṃ prāgeva muṇḍopaniṣadi paramaṃ brahma taddharmabhedai- bhedoktetyakāṇḍe kimiti punarimāṃ piṣṭapeṣaṃ pinaṣṭi | satyaṃ kṣetrajñadharmaiḥ paṭubhirupanatā prakriyābhedaśaṅkā
prakhyāpya pratyabhijñāmapunarudayamunmūlyate śabdapūrvaiḥ || 3 ||

prāgeva - pūrvapādīye adṛśyatvādhikaraṇe eva, paramaṃ brahma - jijñāsyaṃ paraṃ tattvam, taddharmabhedaiḥ – svāsādhāraṇaiḥ sarvajñatvādidharmaviśeṣaiḥ, (tathā) bhedokteśca - jīvabheda ghaṭitaparatva- vacanācca, muṇḍopaniṣadi muṇḍakaśrutau pratipādyatayā, siddhamiti vicārya nirṇītamiti sthite, akāṇḍe – avasarāprāptāvapi, imām – buddhisthāṃ muṇḍakopaniṣadameva, kimiti – kasmāddhetoḥ, piṣṭapeṣaṃ pinaṣṭimṛditasyaiva punarmardanamiva punarvicārayatyanena pādena, iti śaṅkāyām ; satyām — vicāritatvāṃśaḥ aṅgīkriyate, paraṃ piṣṭapeṣaṇatvaṃ nā'sti, paṭubhiḥ – spaṣṭapratipannaiḥ, kṣetrajñadharmaiḥ — jīvāsādhāraṇairnāḍyādhāratvādibhirdharmaiḥ upanatā - sambhāvitā, prakriyābhedaśaṅkā- prakaraṇāntaratvasaṃśayotpattiḥ, pratyabhijñāṃ prakhyāpya – 'amṛta setutvādibhiḥ paramātmā sādhāraṇaiḥ dharmaiḥ pūrvaprakāntaḥ paramātmaivāyam, nā'nya iti' buddhiṃ sthirīkṛtya, apunarudayam - yathā punarnotpadyeta tathā śabdapūrvaiḥ - śrutiprabhṛtibhiḥ pramāṇaiḥ, unmūlyate—utpāṭyate || 3 || yasminnotaṃ manonyaiḥ saha karaṇagaṇairjāyate yaśca nānā nāḍyādhāraśca yo'ntaścarati sa karaṇī karmabhokteti cenna | viśvādhārātmabhāvādamṛtavitaraṇānmuktasṛpyatvavādāt

prāguktaprakriyaikyādanaśanasahitāt kāśanāccā'nyasiddheḥ || 4 ||
anyaiḥ karaṇagaṇaiḥ saha— itaraiḥ prāṇādibhiḥ karma- jñānendriyavṛndaiśva sākam, manaḥ—

cetaḥ, yasmin otam – yasmin vastuni samavetam yaśca nānā jāyate yaścārthaḥ devādi- bahvākāreṇa janmāśrayo bhavati, yaśca nāḍyādhāraḥ yo'rthaḥ nāḍīsaṅghātādhārabhūtaḥ, yaḥ

antaścarati—ya eva cā'rthaḥ deha madhyasañcaraṇavān, saḥ tādṛśo'rthaḥ, karaṇī karmabhoktā- karaṇagrāmavān puṇyāpuṇyaphalabhoktaiva ( jīva eva ) iti cet — evamāśaṅkanam, na sādhu na bhavati ; viśvādhārātmabhāvāt – dyāvāpṛthivyupalakṣitasakala jagadādhārasvarūpatvāt, amṛta- vitaraṇāt - amṛtaśabdavācyamokṣapradātṛtvāt, muktasṛpyatvavādāt - muktātmabhiḥ paramasāmyā- pacitaḥ prāpyatvaśruteḥ, prāguktaprakriyaikyāt – pūrvaślokoktāt prakaraṇābhedāt, anaśanasahitāt kāśanācca – karmaphalabhogābhāvasaha kṛtāt amlānadīptiyogādapi, anyasiddheḥ --jīvādanyasya puruṣaviśeṣasyaiva pratīteḥ, 'yasmin dyauḥ' ityuktaḥ paramātmaiva || 4 ||

vyāpte'ssminna kṣamataṃ yadi kimiha tato janma cāsyecchayoktaṃ nāḍīcakrasya nābhirbhavati ca sa paro hārdarūpeṇa tiṣṭhan | niṣkampavyāpino'ntaścaraṇamapi śubhairvigrahairasti loke
saubālāmnātavaḍā caraṇamidamapi syādadhiṣṭhānamātram || 5 ||

vyāpte–'antarbahiśca tatsarvaṃ vyāpya' ityuktarītyā sarvavyāpake, asmin — paramātmani, akṣam – sarvāntargatam indriyādikamapi, otaṃ yadi – nityasambaddhaṃ yadi, tataḥ – indriya- sambandhamātreṇa, iha kim – atra kiṃ chinnam ? asya ca - paramātmano'pi, icchayā janma- svasaṅkalpāyattaṃ śarīragrahaṇam, uktam- 'bahudhā jāyamānaḥ' iti pratipāditam | hārdarūpeṇa - hrārdasaṃjñakena antaryāmirūpeṇa, tiṣṭhan - hṛdayākhye dehāntaḥ pradeśe avasthitaḥ, sa paraḥ – vedānta- pratipādyaḥ paramātmā'pi, nāḍīcakrasya nāḍībṛndasya, nābhirbhavati – ādhāro bhavitumaītyeva, niṣkrampavyāpino'pi –calanaṃ vinaiva sarvaiḥ saha sambandhavato'pi paramātmanaḥ, loke—jagati, antaḥsañcaraṇamapi—upāsakānugrahopayogi hṛdayāntaḥ sañcā rākhyaṃ, karmā'pi, (tathā) śubhai- vigrahaiḥ śubhāśrayapadavācyaiḥ aprākṛtairdivyaiśśarīraiḥ sañcaraṇamapi ca asti-vibhavādirūpeṇa śāstrasammataṃ sambhavatyeva, vā— athavā saubālāmnātavat – 'yaḥ pṛthivīmantare sañcaran' iti subālopaniṣaduktena krameṇa idamapi caraṇam – sa eṣo'ntaścarate' ityuktamantaḥ sañcaraṇamapi, -' - adhiṣṭhāna mātraṃ syāt — antaryāmitvaveṣeṇa avasthānamātrameva bhavet || 5 ||

'amṛtasyaiṣa setuḥ' iti amṛtopāyatvasya paramātmāsādhāraṇasya śravaṇāt hṛdayadvārā nāḍīsambandhasya paramātmanyupapatteśca dyubhvādyāyatanaṃ paramātmā iti siddhāntitam ||

و

4) nayasaṅgatimālikā
atha dvābhyāṃ caturbhica tribhirekena ca kramāt | nyāyaistṛtīyacaraṇe peṭikānāṃ catuṣṭayam || 48 || dyupṛthivyādisambandhaḥ prāk pādānte nirūpitaḥ | jīve'stītyapavādena bhvādinayanirṇayaḥ || 49 ||
iti bhvādyadhikaraṇam || 1 ||

-0

bhūmādhikaraṇam ||1-3-2||

1) śārīrakanyāya kalāpa saṅgrahaḥ nāmādyupakramya kṛtopadeśaḥ samāpitaḥ prāṇananāni jīve |
tataḥ sa bhūmeti na śaṅkanīyaṃ satyopadeśādadhikaṃ tvamuṣmāt || 30 ||

nāmādi nāma vākprabhṛtikam, upakramya – 'nāmopāsva' ityādinā''rabhya, kṛtopa- deśaḥ – chāndogya saptamaprapāṭhake sanatkumāreṇa nāradāya kṛtaḥ upadeśaḥ, prāṇananāmni - prāṇa- śabdābhidheye, jīve - jīvātmani, samāpitaḥ - 'prāṇo hyevaitāni sarvāṇi bhavati' ityanenopa- saṃhṛtaḥ, tataḥ tasmāddhetoḥ, saḥ - upadeśānte upasaṃhṛtaḥ prāṇākhyo jīva eva, bhūmā 'bhūmā tveva vijijñāsitavyam' iti jijñāsitavyatvenoktaḥ 'bhūma' śabdārthaḥ, iti na śaṅkanīyam — evaṃ śaṅkā naiva kāryā; amuṣmāt adhikam – prāṇādapyutkṛṣṭatayā satyopadeśāt – satyapadārthasyaiva upāsyatayā upadiṣṭatvāt || 30 ||

'satya' padābhidheyaṃ paraṃ brahmatra, prāṇākhyāt – 'prāṇa' śabdavācyāt avarātmanaḥ adhikam - jīvādutkṛṣṭaṃ 'satyaṃ tveva vijijñāsitavyam' iti jijñāsyatayā upadiṣṭamāste, tubalataḥ – 'eṣa tu vā ativadati yaḥ satyenātivadati' iti vākyagata 'tu ' śabdabalāt satyavit—satyā khyārthaviṣaya kātivāditvavān puruṣaḥ, tajjñādhikaḥ - prāṇavedinaḥ puruṣāt śreṣṭhaḥ tadapekṣayā'tiriktaḥ, iti ca upadiśyate | hi yataḥ, yaḥ satyaṃ vadatīti vāk- agnihotraprakaraṇastham agnihotrapraśaṃsanaparaṃ yat 'eṣa tu vā agnihotrī yassatyaṃ vadati' iti vākyamasti tat prakṛtāgnihotryavagamāt – prakaraṇapaṭhitavākyāvagatāt agnihotraṇo'pekṣayā, agnihotryantaram — anyamagnihotriṇam, gamayitum — bodhayitum, na śaknoti na samartha bhavati ; tatsāmathryabhāvāt iti, (tatra) 'tu' śabda śriyaḥ – 'tu' śabdasvarasatāyāḥ, bhaṅgaḥ - tyāgaḥ, (agatyā sahyate) prakṛte tu tuśabdasvārasyatyāgo doṣāvaha eveti, satyātivādī, prāṇāti- vādino'nyaḥ utkṛṣṭa eveti || 29 ||

3) adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ
ātmajñānābhilāṣa|danuparataśuce nāradāya prayuktaṃ
prāṇe sānatkumāraṃ viramati vacanaṃ hiṃsanāhaḥ sa jīvaḥ | alpapratyarthibhūmā niravadhikasukho'pyeṣa eveti cenna

prāṇākhyāt 'satya' śabdoditamadhikatayopāsyamatra hyupāttam || 6 || ātmajñānābhilāṣāt-ātmavedanecchātaḥ, anuparataśuce - anivṛttaśokāya, nāradāya - nāradanāne munaye, prayuktam – pravṛttam, sānatkumāraṃ vacanam – sanatkumārakṛtopadeśātmakaṃ bhūmavidyāgataṃ vākyajātam, prāṇe viramati - prāṇarūpe arthe upadiṣṭe samāptaṃ bhavati | saḥ - prāṇapadārthaḥ, hiṃsanāhaḥ – hiṃsitumarhaḥ, jīvaḥ - saprāṇaścetana eva, alpapratyarthibhūmā - samabhivyāhṛtā lpaśabdārthapratidvandvibhūtaḥ ' bhūma' śabdārtho'pi, niravadhikasukhaḥ - aparicchinnānanda- rūpaḥ, eṣa eva – jīvarūpaḥ prāṇa eva iti cet — evaṃ śaṅkanam, na – yuktaṃ na bhavati, hi yataḥ, atra — prakaraṇe'smin satyaśabdoditam – 'satyaṃ tveva vijijñāsitavyam ' iti satyaśabdollikhitam, upāsyam – upāsanīyaṃ vastu, prāṇākhyāt- 'prāṇa' padavācyajīvāpekṣayā, adhikatayā — atiriktatayā utkṛṣṭatayā ca upāttam - upadiṣṭaṃ vartate || 6 ||

nāmādyāśāntavedye prativacanavaśāt praśnataścādhikokteḥ prāṇe viśrāntidṛṣṭyā bhavatu tadavadhiḥ prastutātmopadeśaḥ | maivaṃ, jāto hi nāmādivadiha paramādātmanaḥ prāṇa uktaḥ
svasmādityatra tatsyāditi na vighaṭanāt khārasikyā vibhakteḥ || 7 ||

nāmādyāśāntavedye 'nāmopāsva' ityārabhya 'āśāmupāsva' ityantaṃ vedyatayā vihite nāmaprabhṛtyāśāparyante arthacaturdaśa ke, praśnataḥ- 'asti bhagavo nāmno bhūyaḥ' ityādirūpāt praśnāt, prativacanavaśāt ca- 'vāgvāva nāmno bhūyasī' ityādirūpāt uttarācca siddhāyāḥ, adhikokteḥ atiśayitānyānyavastukathanasya, prāṇe viśrāntidṛṣṭayā - prāṇaparyantatāyāḥ śrutau darśanāt, prastutātmopadeśaḥ – prakrāntaḥ ātmasambandhī upadeśaḥ, tadavadhiḥ bhavatu tatparyanta eva syāt, (iti āśaṅkane prāpte ;) maivam - īdṛśamāśaṅkanaṃ na yujyate, hi – yataḥ, iha- vedyārthopadeśaprakaraṇe, nāmādivat – nāmaprabhṛtipadārthasadṛśa tayā, prāṇaḥ prāṇarūpaḥ artho'pi, paramāt ātmanaḥ parasmādātmanaḥ sakāśāt, jāta uktaḥ—'ātmataḥ prāṇa: ' iti utpattimāneva pratipāditaḥ | atra - śrutivākyagatam, tat - ātmata utpattivacanam, svasmāt iti syāt – svahetukāvirbhāvarūpaṃ bhavennām iti na evaṃ cintana- mayuktameva svāra sikyāḥ bheda ghaṭita janya- janakabhāvapratipādana svabhāvāyāḥ,

vibhakteḥ—

pañcamyāḥ, vighaṭanāt—svārthapratipādakatvābhāvaprasaṅgāt || 7 ||
prāṇadraṣṭātivādī tadanuvadanatacoditaḥ satyavādaḥ
tasmāt sarvāhamarthaḥ sakalajanayitā prāṇa eveti cenna | eṣa tvityanyatokterativadanakṛtaḥ prāktanādasya tadvat

na tvanyo'styagnihotrī svamahimanilaye hyatra dharmopapattiḥ || 8 ||
prāṇadraṣṭā - nāmādyupāsyaparamparāntimaprāṇarūpārtha sākṣātkaraṇaśīlaḥ puruṣaḥ, ativādī- svopāsyasya prāṇasya sarvotkṛṣṭatvakathanakkṛtā tivāditvavānityuktaḥ, tadanuvadanataḥ - prāṇātivādinaḥ anuvādapūrvakam, satyavādaḥ – satyārthavādarūpaḥ arthaḥ, coditaḥ - 'eṣa tu vā ativadati, yaḥ satyenāti vadati' iti vākyena aṅgatayā vihitaḥ | tasmāt – 'eṣa tu vā ativadati' ityasya prāṇopāsanāṅgatayā satyavādavidhāyakatvaniścayāt, sarvāhamarthaḥ sarvaiḥ prayujyamānasya 'ahaṃ' śabdasyābhidheyaḥ, 'ahamevedaṃ sarvam' iti uttaratrokto vā, sakalajanayitā – sarvasya jagataḥ svīyādṛṣṭadvāra kasraṣṭṛtvavān, prāṇa eva – 'prāṇa' padavācyo

jīvātmaiva, iti cet — evamāśaṅkanam, na naiva yuktam ; epa tviti 'tu' śabdaghaṭitena 'eṣa tu vā ativadati' iti vākyena asya ativadanakṛtaḥ satyātivāda kartuḥ puruṣasya, prāktanāt — prāṇātivādyapekṣayā, anyatokteḥ - bhedasya kaṇṭhokteḥ, tadvat-prāṇātivādyatiriktaḥ satyātivādīva, anyaḥ agnihotrī - pūrvāgnihotraṇo bhinnaḥ kaścana agnihotrī, na asti naiva prakaraṇapaṭhito vartate iti, pūrvāgnihotryanuvādena satyavadanasyāṅgatoktiyuktā | svamahimanilaye— sve mahimni pratiṣṭhite, atra hi - paramātmanyeva khla, dharmopapattiḥ prakaraṇopāttānāṃ svābhāvi- kāmṛtatvādīnāṃ sarveṣāṃ dharmāṇāmabādhenā'nvayaḥ sambhavati || 8 ||

nāmāssdau vāk ca tasyopari tadanu manaścātha saṅkalpanāmā
cittaṃ dhyānaṃ ca tasmā' tparamapi ca 'balaṃ syācca vijñāna pūrvam | annaṃ toyaṃ ca tejo gaganamapi tato manmathaḥ ' syāttathā''śā

"prāṇaḥ satyaḥ parātmā' sakala 'niyamitā' gamyate bhūmavākye || 9 ||

bhūmavākye bhūmavidyāsambandhini vākyasandarbhe, ādau nāma prathamataḥ vedyatayoktaṃ nāmātmakaṃ vastu, tasyopari ca nāgno'nantaraṃ ca vāk – vāgrūpo'rthaḥ, tadanu ca manaḥ- vācaḥ paścāt manorūpaṃ vedyam, atha ca saṅkalpanāmā — manaso'nantaraṃ saṅkalpākhyo'rthaḥ cittaṃ dhyānaṃ ca–saṅkalpādupari cittākhyaṃ vastu tathaiva dhyānamapi tasmāt paramapi ca – dhyānā- nantaramapi, vijñānapūrva balaṃ ca syāt – vijñānaṃ balaṃ ca vedyaṃ bhavet, annaṃ toyaṃ ca- tadanantaram annarūpaḥ jalarūpaścārthaḥ, (tathaiva) tejaḥ gaganamapi teja ākāśau, tataḥ— - - - tadanantaram, manmathaḥ syāt — manmathaparyāya sadṛśaḥ smaraṇārthakaḥ smaraḥ upadiṣṭo'sti | tathā āśā - tathaiva digātmakaḥ artho'pi vedyatvenopadiṣṭaḥ prāṇaḥ paścāt prāṇasaṃjñako jīvaḥ upadiṣṭaḥ, (tataśca) sakalaniyama (yama) yitā - sarveṣāmantaḥ praviśya niyantā, parātmā- paramātmapadavyapadeśyaḥ, satyaḥ satyarūpo'rthaḥ, gamyate vedyatvena jñāpyate || 9 ||

4) adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ

bhūmādhikaraṇe – 'tarati śokamātmavit' iti prakrāntasya ātmopadeśasya prāṇaparyava sāyitvāt, prāṇādūrdhvaṃ ca prāṇādūrdhvaṃ ca 'asti bhagavaḥ prāṇādbhūyaḥ ?' itya | dipraśna prativacanabhāvena prakaraṇasyā'vicchinnatvāt, 'prāṇa' śabda nirdiṣṭaḥ pratyagātmaiva 'yatra nānyat paśyati' iti vākye 'bhūma' śabdanirdiṣṭaḥ – ityāśaṅkaya,

bhavet jñānameva balañca - ṇā. 4 yamayitā - ā.

syāt smaraḥ -- ā. * prāṇo'tho satya ātmā - ā.

'eṣa tu vā ativadati, yaḥ satyenātivadati' iti 'prāṇa' śabdanirdiṣṭāt pratyagātmano'pi " satya' śabdavācyasya bhūmnaḥ adhikatayā nirdeśāt, bhūmā paramātmā – iti siddhāntitam || nayasaṅgatimālikā
uktāt prakaraṇasyaikyādeva siddhāntayuktitaḥ | apavādo bhūmanaye jīvenāstra nirākṛtaḥ || 50 || || iti bhūmādhikaraṇam || 2 ||
akṣarādhikaraṇam ||1-3-3 ||
śārīrakanyāya kalāpasaṅgrahaḥ

avyākṛtaṃ cetana eva ' vā'pi syādakṣaraṃ cāssspadamambarasya | "kāryāspada 'yādacidāśrayatvādityapyasat tasya dhṛteḥ praśāsteḥ || 31 ||

ambarasya āspadam – ākāśaṃ prati ādhāratayā ucyamānam, akṣaram – akṣarapada- vācyaṃ vastu, avyākṛtam-- bhūtasūkṣmaṃ pradhānam api vā – athavā, cetana eva syāt - jīvātmaiva bhavitumarhati, kāryāspadatvāt – pradhānasya vikārādhāratvāt acidāśrayatvāt — jīvasya acetanavastvādhāratvāt ityapi asat - evaṃ śaṅkanamapi na sādhu, tasya dhṛteḥ praśāsteḥ pradhānasyā'pi dhāraṇasya tatpraśānasya ca paramātmāsādhāraṇasya asmin akṣare śravaṇāt asyākṣarasya pradhānatva jīvatvayoḥ ayogāt || 31 ||

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ

prakhyātākāśapūrvasva vikṛtivahanādakṣarākhyaṃ pradhānaṃ tasyā'pyākāśatoktau dhṛtanikhilajagat kṣetritaccaṃ tu tat syāt maivaṃ draṣṭṛttrapūrvairanitaraniyataiḥ śāsanāyattadhṛtyā
kiñca draṣṭṛntarasya vyudasanamiha tattulyatadraṣṭra pohaḥ || 10 ||

prakhyātā .... vahanāt – lokaprasiddhākāśaprabhṛti svīyavikārān prati ādhāratvāt, akṣarākhyam —'etadvai tadakṣaraṃ gārgi ityatrākṣarapadābhidheyam, pradhānam- mūlaprakṛtireva bhavitumarhati tasyā'pi — pradhānasyā'pi, ākāśatoktau – 'ākāśa' padenābhi- dhānaṃ yadi sammataṃ ; tarhi, dhṛtanikhilajagat — sakalā cetanavargadhārakam, kṣetritacyaṃ tu – samaṣṭi-

jīvatattvameva, tat syāt — atratyākṣarapadābhidheyaṃ bhavitumarhati (iti pūrvapakṣe ), maitram — akṣarapadena pradhānasya vā jīvasya vā bodhanaṃ na yuktam ; kintu paramātmana eveti, anitaraniyataiḥ- tvādisāhacaryeṇa paramātmetarasmin asambhāvitaiḥ, draṣṭṛtvapūrvaiḥ – draṣṭṛtva - śrotṛtva-mantṛtvādibhirdharmaiḥ, śāsanāyattadhṛtyā apratihatasaṅkalpadhāryatvaśravaṇena niścīyate | kiñca_api ca, iha - 'nānya- dato'sti draṣṭṛ' iti vākye, draṣṭṛntarasya - anyasya darśanakartuḥ, vyudasanam - abhāvajñāpanam, tattulyatadraṣṭra pohaḥ – viśvādhāratva niyantṛtvādinā paramātmasadṛśasya tadraṣṭuranyasya abhāvabodhanameva ; na tu draṣṭṛsāmānyābhāvasya bodhanamiti || 10 ||

adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ

akṣarādhikaraṇe – 'etadvai tadakṣaraṃ gārgi' ityādivākye, 'akṣara' śabdasya pradhāne jīve ca prayogadarśanāt, 'akṣara' śabdavācyaḥ tayoranyataraḥ – ityāśaṅkaya, kālatrayavartivikārāśrayasya 'ākāśa' śabdavācyasya pradhānasyā'pyādhāratayā nirdiṣṭaḥ akṣaraḥ paramātmā — iti siddhāntam ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā

prakṛtau puruṣe vaiṣā sve mahimni pratiṣṭhitiḥ | proktā'stītyapavādena syādakṣaranayodayaḥ || 51 || || iti akṣarādhikaraṇam || 3 ||
īkṣatikarmādhikaraṇam || 1-3-5 || śārīrakanyāyakalāpa saṅgrahaḥ
bhuvo'ntarikṣādadhikopadiṣṭo yo brahmalokaḥ sa vidhātṛlokaḥ |

'tatra' trimātrapraṇavānucintyo dhātetyasat syādamṛtābhidhādyaiḥ || 32 || bhuvaḥ–manuṣyalokāpekṣayā, antarikṣāt — antarikṣalokāpekṣayā ca, adhikopadiṣṭaḥ- ūrdhvaṃ vidyamānatayā trimātraṃ praṇavamupāsīnasya prāpyatayā ca śrutyuktaḥ, brahmaloko yaḥ saḥ- śrutisthatrahmalokapadārthaḥ yo'sti saḥ, vidhātṛlokaḥ jīvasamaṣṭirūpasya caturmukhasya loka eva | tatra dhātā – satyalokasthaḥ hiraṇyagarbha eva, trimātrapraṇavānucintyaḥ – mātratrayavatā oṅkāreṇa upāsya:, iti – evaṃ śaṅkanam asat syāt - apādhu bhavet, amṛtābhidhādyaiḥ amṛtā- bhayādināmadheyaprabhṛtibhiḥ jñāpakaiḥ || 32 ||

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ lakṣmībhūto'yamekaḥ khalu puruṣa iha dhyāyaterīkṣateśca
kṣetrajñaḥ so'yamaṇḍādhipa itaraparāt svānyajīvāt paro'sau | no ced bhaumādibhogaprakaraṇa vihatirbobhavītītyayuktaṃ

tasmin śāntāmṛtatvaprabhṛtiparaguṇa khyātyanūktyorayogāt || 11 ||

iha praśnopaniṣadgate 'paraṃ puruṣamabhidhyāyīta' parātparaṃ puriśayaṃ puruṣamīkṣate' ityādirūpe vākye, dhyāyateḥ īkṣateśva - 'dhyai ' dhātubodhyasya dhyānasya, 'īkṣa' dhātubodhyasya darśanasya ca lakṣmībhūtaḥ - viṣayabhūto dhyeyo draṣṭavyaśca, ayaṃ puruṣaḥ- śrutisthapuruṣaśabdavācyaḥ arthaḥ ekaḥ khalu – abhinna eveti prasiddham ; ( dhyātavyasyaiva draṣṭavyatvāt ) | so'yam — dhyā nekṣaṇakarmabhūto'rthaḥ, aṇḍādhipaḥ – brahmāṇḍasyeśaḥ, kṣetrajñaḥ— caturmukhasaṃjñako jīva eva, asau – brahmāṇḍādhipatiḥ, itaraparāt — anyebhyo'cetanebhyaḥ utkṛṣṭāt, svānyajīvāt – svetarasmāt cetanavargāt paraḥ – utkṛṣṭa eveti parātpara eva, no cet — yadyevaṃ nāṅgīkriyeta tarhi, bhaumādibhogaprakaraṇa vihatiḥ – pṛthivī - antarikṣa- brahmalokātmakabhogaloka- prakramasya virodhaḥ, bobhavīti — aparihāryaḥ samāpatati, iti — evaṃrītyā cintanam, ayuktam- asaṅgatam, tasmin — aṇḍādhipe caturmukhe, śāntā .... tyanūktyoḥ – śāntatvāmṛtatvādīnāṃ paramātmasambandhināṃ guṇānāṃ prasiddheḥ (tathā) tadanuvādasya ca bādhāt || 11 ||

nanvatroṅkāramātrātrayaphalagaṇanārūḍhabhūmyantarikṣa-

pratyāsacyā nivāsaḥ sarasijabasaterbrahmaloko'stu maivam | pāponmuktena labhyo hyaya 'miti' kathitaḥ sūryasampattipūrvaṃ
soḍhavyo madhyalokairvyavadhiriti samastatsamādhānamārgaḥ || 12 || atra—praśnopaniṣatsambandhitrimātrapraṇavopāsanaprakaraṇe, oṅkāra.... pratyāsacyā – 'o3m” ityākārakapraṇavasambandhi upāsyamānaṃ mātrātritayaṃ yadasti, tatphalaparvapraviṣṭau yau pṛthivyanta- rikṣākhyau lokau tatsamabhivyāhāreṇa jñāpakabhūtena brahmaloka : – 'sa sāmabhirunnīyate brahmalokam ' iti śrutigata brahmalokapadābhidheyo'rthaḥ

paurāṇika prasiddhasya caturmukhasya, nivāsaḥ astu – nilayabhūto loka eva bhavatu ; na paramātmanaḥ sthānam, ityevaṃśaṅkane kṛte, maivam anena prakāreṇa śaṅkanaṃ nocitam hi yataḥ ayam-

ukta brahmalokaḥ, pāponmuktena evaṃ ha vai sa pāpmanā vinirmukta:' ityuktarītyā sarvapāpasambandhaprahāṇavatā puruṣeṇa, sūryasampattipūrvam –'sa tejasi sūrye sampanna : '

(praśna u 5 - 5 ) ityuktarītyā sūryaprāptipūrvakam labhya iti – prāptavya iti kathitaḥ tatraiva śrutau pratipāditaḥ, madhyalokaiḥ –antarikṣāduparitanaiḥ, brahmalokādadhastanaiśca lokaiḥ, vyavadhiḥ – vyavadhānam, soḍhavya iti marṣaṇīyam ityevaṃrūpaḥ, tatsamādhānamārgaḥ —antarikṣa- samabhivyāhāraḥ paramātmasthānasya na sambhavati ityataḥ, tasya kathaṃ jñāpakatvam ? ityākṣepasya samādhānaprakāraḥ, samaḥ - paramātmasthānasya prāpyatvavādinaḥ caturmukhalokasya prāpyatāvādina- cetyubhayo: samāna eva; (caturmukhalokasyā'pi antarikṣāvyavadhānasya avilakṣaṇatvāditi || 12 || (3) adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ

īkṣatikarmādhikaraṇe – 'yaḥ punaretaṃ trimātreṇomityetenaivākṣareṇa paraṃ puruṣamabhidhyāyīta' ityādivākye, 'parātparaṃ puriśayaṃ puruṣamīkṣate ' iti, īkṣati - karmatayā nirdiṣṭaḥ puruṣaḥ, caturmukhaḥ ; dvimātramupāsīnasya antarikṣalokaprāptimabhidhāya, trimātramupāsīnasya prāpyatayā uktasya brahmalokasya, antarikṣalokāt paratvena caturmukhalokatvāt -

ityāśaṅkaya,

'yattacchāntamajaramamṛtam' ityādinā, īkṣatikarmaṇaḥ paramātmatvena vyapadeśāt paramatryomno'pi antarikṣāttaratvasambhavācca paramātmā – ityuktam ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā uktādṛṣṭadraṣṭṛtā'tra na saṃśliṣyata ityataḥ | bhavedīkṣatikarmādhikaraṇe pratyudāhṛtiḥ || 52 || || iti īkṣatikarmādhikaraṇam || 4 ||
daharādhikaraṇam || 1-3-5 ||
śārīrakanyāyakalāpa saṅgrahaḥ
dahrastu khaṃ syādatha cetano vā prasiddhiyogādguṇayogataśca |
na sādhvidaṃ' khe nahi satyabhāvaḥ kāmāśca jīve na nisargasiddhāḥ || 33 || dahastu - 'daharo'sminnantara ākāśa : ' iti śrutau 'dahara' śabda- cācyo'rthastu khaṃ syāt — bhūtākāśa bhavet, prasiddhiyogāt — tatraiva 'ākāśa' śabdaprasiddheḥ

1 sāspada-- ga. sāmprataṃ - pā.

anvayāt, athavā cetanaḥ– jīvo vā bhavet, guṇayogataśca – anveṣṭavyāpahatapāpmatvādiguṇa- sambandhasya satvāt | idaṃ na sādhu - idaṃ yuktaṃ na bhavati, hi – yataḥ, khe – prasiddhe ākāśe, satyabhāvaḥ— 'etat satyaṃ brahmapuram ' ityuktaṃ satyatvam, na—naivā'sti | jīve – cetane, kāmāśca – kāmyamānāḥ apahatapāpmatvādayo guṇā api, nisargasiddhā:- nirupādhikāḥ, na—naiva santi || 33 ||

taccasāraḥ

asvārasyacatuṣṭayaṃ paramate yacchanda liṅgavyathā
tasminnityana' pekṣayā' vyavahite hṛtpuṇḍarīke'nvayaḥ | apyākāṅkṣitakāmavedanavidhikṣepaḥ svapakṣe dvayam
yacchabdasya yadaikaśeṣyamapi yā tatraikavadbhāvitā || 30||

paramate — advaitināṃ mate, asvārasyacatuṣṭayam – daharavidyāgatasya 'atha yadidamasmin brahmapure daharaṃ puṇḍarīkaṃ veśma daharo'sminnantara ākāśaḥ tasmin yadantastadanveṣṭavyam, tadvāva vijijñāsitavyam' ityasyārthavarṇane śabdasya asvābhāvikārthavarṇanakṛtaṃ doṣacatuṣṭayaṃ vartate | (kiṃ tat ? iti cet ) yacchanda liṅgavyathā - pūrvam 'antarākāśa:' iti pulliṅgena nirdiṣṭasyārthasya 'tasmin yadantaḥ' iti vākyakhaṇḍasthena napuṃsaka - yacchabdena grahaṇakṛtaḥ liṅgaviparyāsarūpo doṣa:, tasmin ityanapekṣayā - tadrītyā arthavarṇane, uktavākyakhaṇḍagata saptamyanta tacchabdopapādanavaiyarthyena sākam, vyavahite— ākāśapadārthena madhye vidyamānena prāptavyavadhāne, hṛtpuṇḍarīke - 'daharaṃ puṇḍarīkam' iti pūrvanirdiṣṭe hṛdayākhyasthānarūpe padmākāre vastuni, anvayaḥ - 'tasmin' ityasya parāmṛśyamānatayā sambandhaḥ, api tathaiva, ākāṅkṣitakāmavedanavidhikṣepaḥ 'kāma' śabdavācyānāṃ paramātma- sambandhinām apahatapāpmatvādīnāṃ guṇānām upāsyatvavidheḥ āvaśyakasyā'pi tyāgaḥ, (iti) | svapakṣe viśiṣṭādvaitināṃ pakṣe tu, dvayam – asvārasyadvitayamasti | ityanu- śāsanena 'yat' iti ekavacanāntatābhyupagamaḥ iti yoṃ'śaḥ ; saḥ iti || 30||

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ
dahaṃ hṛtpuṇḍarīke gaganamabhihitaṃ taittirīyaśrutau yat chandogaistatra gītaṃ yadapi ca daharākāśa ityetadekam | bhūtādyaṃ tatprasiddhermahimata iti na pratyanīkairanekaiḥ
śrautī ca syāt prasiddhibhagavati balinī liṅgavarṇaiḥ sanāthā || 13 ||

taittirīyaśrutau taittirīyanārāyaṇopaniṣadi hṛtpuṇḍarīke – puṇḍarīkasadṛśe hṛdayākhye dehāntarvartini sthānaviśeṣe, daham - sūkṣmam yat gaganam – gaganapadavācyaṃ yadvastu, abhihitam — 'dahaṃ vipāpmaṃ tatrā'pi dahaṃ gaganam' iti vākyenoktam, daharākāśa iti - ' daharaḥ, ākāśa:' iti padābhyām, tatra – hṛdayapuṇḍarīkavartitayā yadapi ca - yādṛśaṃ ca vastu, chandogaiḥ – sāmaśākhādhyāyibhiḥ, gītam – 'atha yadidamasmin- daharo'sminnantarākāśaḥ' iti paṭhitam etat etadubhayamapi, ekam - abhinnameva | tat – gaganā kāśapadābhyāṃ bodhitaṃ vastu, prasiddhermahimataḥ – prasiddhyādhikyāt, bhūtādyam - pañcabhūteṣvādimaṃ bhūtameva laukikākāśa eva, iti na – evaṃ śaṅkā anucitā, pratyanīkaiḥ etatpakṣapratibhaṭaiḥ, anekaiḥ liṅgavarṇaiḥ bahubhiḥ asādhāraṇaiḥ hetugaṇaiḥ, nirupādhi- kātmatvāpahatapāpmatvādibhiḥ paramātmā sādhāraṇaiḥ 'eṣa ātmāpahatapāpmā' ityādivākyoktaḥ, sanāthā - sahakṛtā, śrautī ca śrutitaḥ prāptā tu bhagavati prasiddhiḥ paramātmani ākāśa - padaprayogapracuratā, balinī syāt – ākāśapadasya bhautikākāśapratipādakatvajñānapratibandhikā- bhavati || 13 ||

bāhyākāśaśca yāvānayamapi hi tathetyetadakliṣṭarmā śe
satyātmatrahmaśabdā nabhasi na kathamapyanvayaṃ prāpnuvanti | kāmādhāraśca yo'sau samagaṇi daharākāśavācā'tra nityaḥ
tasyaiva hyeṣa ātmetyanuvadanamatastadguṇāścintyakāmāḥ || 14 ||

bāhyākāśaśca - bhautikaḥ lokaprasiddhākāśaśca yāvān - yādṛśavaipulyādiguṇayuktaḥ, ayamapi - daharākāśo'pi, tathā — tādṛśavaipulyādimān ityetat – īdṛśam, upamānopameya- bhāvakathanam, īśe – bāhyākāśādbhinne paramātmani, akliṣṭam — svarasato'nvayi | ( kiṃ ca), nabhasi – kevale ākāśe, satyātmabrahmaśabdā: - 'etat satyaṃ brahmapuram eṣa ātmā' ityuktāḥ 'satyam, ātmā, brahma' ityete śabdāḥ, kathamapi — mukhyayā

amukhyayā vā vṛyā, anvayam - yogyaṃ sambandham, na prāpnuvanti naiva labhante, yaśca - ya evārthaḥ, kāmādhāraḥ - kāmyānāṃ kalyāṇaguṇānāmāśrayatayā, samagaṇi - ' asmin kāmāḥ samāhitāḥ' iti śrutāvuktaḥ, asau nityaḥ - saḥ svarūpataḥ svabhāvataśca vikārarahitaḥ, atra daharākāśavācā_etadvākyasthena daharākāśapadena, samagaṇi—kathitaḥ, tasyaiva hi — kāmyamānaguṇāśrayasya daharākāśamyaiva khalu eṣa ātmeti – 'eṣa ātmā'pahatapāpmā vijaro vimṛtyuḥ' ityādinā uparitanena vākyena, anuvadanam —- parāmarśa:, ata:- daharākāśasya paramātmatvāt tadguṇāḥ - tadavinābhūtāḥ apahatapāpmatvādisatyasaṅkalpatvāntāḥ guṇāḥ, cintyakāmāḥ paramātmavadeva upāsyāḥ spṛhaṇīyāśca svasminnapi āvirbhavanīyatayā kāmyamānāśca vā bhavanti || 14 ||

sarveśādhāratoktyā bhavatu ca hṛdayavyoma tadvājyadhītaṃ
chāndogyastho nipādasthapatinayapadaṃ brahmalokādiśabdaḥ | āpastambaśca vaibhājanapuramavadadbrahma sarvātmabhūtaṃ
pustasya prāṇinaḥ syustadapi tadapi hi syātpuraṃ sarvavāsāt || 15 ||
vājyadhītaṃ tat — vājasaneyaśākhāntargatavṛhadāraṇyakaparipaṭhitam 'ākāśa' śabdābhidheyaṃ vastu, sarveśādhāratoktyā - ' ya eṣo'ntahṛdaya ākāśa : ...tasmin śete .... sarvasyeśānaḥ ' iti sarveśvarasya gūḍhanivāsasthānatvavarṇanena, hṛdayavyoma bhavatu — hṛdayākhya- sūkṣmāvakāśarūpaprasiddhāṃ kā śo bhavatu, tatsammanyate ; chāndogyasthaḥ brahmalokādiśabda:- chāndogyopaniṣatpaṭhitaḥ brahmaloka - brahmapurādyātmakaḥ śabdastu, niṣādasthapatinayapadam – niṣāda - sthapatinyāyasya karmadhārayasamāśrayaṇaucityaparasya āspadaḥ, ('brahmaiva lokaḥ, brahmaiva puram' iti) āpastambaśca -- tannāmako munirapi, sarvātmabhūtaṃ brahma - cidacidātmakasakala- jagaccharīrakaṃ brahma, vaibhājanaṃ puram - jīvānāṃ svasvakarmānuguṇyena pṛthakpṛthagavasthātuṃ yogyatvāt, vaibhājana’padavācyanagaramiti, avadat - ' sa vai vaibhājanaṃ puram' itya- sūtrayat | tasya–paramātmanaḥ, pūḥ– pūrṇatayā'vasthātumarhatvena 'pū: 'padavācyāḥ, prāṇinaḥ syuḥ- sarve jantavo bhaveyuḥ | tadapi - brahma prati sarveṣāṃ puratve'pi tadapi paraṃ brahmāpi, sarvavāsāt - sarvān prati ādhāratvāt puraṃ syāt hi – suvyavasthitapariṣkṛtavāsayogyaṃ bhavatyeva khalu ! || 15 ||

jīvasta ātmā guṇagaṇaghaṭanāt tatparāmarśadṛṣṭeḥ alpatvādyuktitaścetya sadanupadhikāt satyasaṅkalpatādeḥ | viśvaikādhāratāderapi sa khalu 'pare' dahataupādhikī syāt

prājāpatye tu vākye parasamadaśayā tadguṇoktirvimukte || 16 ||

||

tarhi –bhautikasyākāśasya daharākāśatve anupapanne sati, guṇagaṇaghaṭanāt - apahṛta - pāpmatvādīnāṃ guṇānām abādhitatvāt tatparāmarśadṛṣṭe :– 'eṣa ātmeti hovāca' iti jīvātmano grahaṇasya śrutau dṛṣṭatvāt eva ātmā - ' eṣa ātmā'pahatapāpmā' ityā dinoktaḥ ātmā, jīvaḥ - jīvarūpa ātmā bhavatu | alpatvādyuktitaśca - jīve'nvetumarhasya alpatvāde- dharmasya 'dahara’śabdena kathanādapi jñāpakāt ityasat - evaṃ śaṅkanamasādhu, anupadhikāt - sārvakālikāta, satyasaṅkalpatādeḥ apratihatasaṅkalpaprabhṛtikāt guṇajātāt, (tathā) viśvakā - dhāratāderapi sakalajagat prati mukhyādhāratvādapi 'asmin dyāvā - pṛthivī antareva samāhite iti śrutyuktāt sa khalu - paramātmaiva eṣa ātmā'pahatapāpmetyādyuktaḥ, pare- sarvatra vyāpte paramātmani tu, dahatā - alpaparimāṇatā, aupādhikī syāt – hṛdayāvaccheda- nibandhanaiva bhavet ; na svābhāvikī | prājāpatye vākye tu – indraṃ prati prajāpatikṛtātmasvarūpopa- deśapradarśa ke 'ya ātmā'pahatapāpmā .... iti ha prajāpatiruvāca' iti śrutyātmake vākye tu, vimukte paraṃ jyotirūpasampanne jīve, parasamadaśayā - paramātmanā sākaṃ parama- sāmyāvasthāyā, tadguṇoktiḥ - apahatapāpmatvādīnāṃ guṇānāṃ kathanam || 16 ||

daśo'pavargaprada iti gadituṃ samprasādoktiratra
prājāpatye tu vākye paraparipaṭhanaṃ prāpyaniṣkarṣaṇārtham | ākāṅkṣādyaistadevaṃ para- taditarayoranvite vākya yugme'

yuktaṃ nānyo'nyabādhaprabhṛti 'kamiha ' tat sāmarasyaṃ hi sautram || 17 || dahrākāśaḥ – upaniṣadi 'daharākāśa' padena vyapadiṣṭaḥ paramātmā, apavargapradaḥ - upāsakānāṃ niśśreyasapradātā, iti gaditum - imamartha pratipādayitum atra - dahara vidyāvākye, samprasādoktiḥ – 'eṣa samprasādo'smāccharīrāt' iti vākye samprasāda- śabdasya prayogaḥ, prājāpatye vākye - indraṃ prati brahmaṇā ute vākye 'sa uttamaḥ puruṣaḥ ityevamādau, paraparipaṭhanaṃ tu — paramātmanaḥ pratipādanaṃ tu prāpyaniṣkarṣa - ṇārtham—upeyasya tanttrasya niṣkarṣeṇa - viviktatayā bodhanāya, tat – uktāddhetoḥ, ākāṅkṣādyaiḥ—

anyonyaṃ vidyamānaiḥ ākāṅkṣā- yogyatā - āsattibhiḥ, para- taditarayoḥ parātma- jīvātmanoḥ sambandhini, vākyayugme – daharavākye prajāpativākye ca, evam uktayā rītyā, anvite - abādhena saṅgate sati, iha — uktayoḥ vākyayoḥ, anyonyabādhaprabhṛtikam - dahara vidyāvākyena prajāpativākya bodhyārthasya bādhakalpanaṃ vā, prajāpativākyena daharavidyāvākyabodhyārthabādhakalpanamapi vā, na yuktam — saṅgataṃ na bhavati | hi yataḥ, tatsāmarasyam – tayorvākyayoḥ abādhitānupa- caritārthabodhakatvapratipādanam, sautram - śārīrakasūtraḥ samyak pratipannam || 17 ||

adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ

daharādhikaraṇe – 'daharo'sminnantarākāśa: ' iti vākye, 'ākāśa'- śabdasya bhūtākāśe prasiddhi prācuryāt, anveṣṭavyāntarābhāvācca, daharākāśo bhūtākāśa eva-

-ityāśaṅkaya,

uttaravākye daharākāśasambandhitayā apahatapāpmatvādipratipādanāt teṣāmeva anveṣṭa-- vyatvācca, daharākāśaḥ paramātmā – iti siddhāntitam ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā

nirṇītaḥ prāk puriśayaḥ puruṣaśrutivaibhavāt | pratyudāhṛtisaṅgatyā daharādhikriyotthitiḥ || 53 || || iti daharādhikaraṇam || 5 ||
pramitādhikaraṇam || 1-4-6 ||
śārīrakanyāyakalāpa saṅgrahaḥ
prāṇādhipaḥ sañcaratīti vākye prasiddhito'ṅguṣṭhamito hi ! jīvaḥ | ityapyasādhveva bhavedamuṣminnīśānaśabdaśravaṇāddhi vākye || 34 ||
prāṇādhipaḥ .... vākye - ' praśaṇādhipaḥ sañcarati svakarmabhiḥ aṅguṣṭhamātro ravitulyarūpaḥ ' ityasmin vākye, prasiddhitaḥ jīvātmana eva aṅguṣṭhaparimitatayā prasiddhi - matvāt, aṅguṣṭhamitaḥ – 'aṅguṣṭhamātraḥ puruṣaḥ' iti 'aṅguṣṭhamātra' śabdena kaṭhavallayāṃ pratipannaḥ arthaḥ, jīvo hi jīvātmaiva bhavitumarhati ityapi asādhveva bhavet — evamāśaṅkanamapi niryuktikameva bhavati ; amuṣmin hi vākye - kaṭhavallīgate aṅguṣṭhamātrapadaghaṭite vākye eva, 'īśāna' śabdaśravaṇāt – 'aṅguṣṭhamātraḥ puruṣo madhya ātmani tiṣṭhati | īśāno bhūtabhavyasya' ityevaṃ jagadīśvaratvabodhakasya 'īśāna' śabdasya śrutatvāt || 34||

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ prāṇeśo'ṅguṣṭhamātraḥ kvacidanukathitaḥ sañcaran karmabhiḥ svai- ranyatrā'ṅguṣṭhamātraṃ puruṣamapi yamo niśkarteti dṛṣṭam | tasmādetat pramāṇapramitamupaniṣajjīvamāhetyayuktaṃ
vākyasyeśānatāderna ra hṛdayaparicchittitastaddhi mānam || 18 ||

kvacit—śrutiviśeṣe, straiḥ karmabhiḥ svakīyaiḥ sukṛtaduṣkṛtaiḥ sādhanabhūtaiḥ, sañcaran – vividhadehadhāraṇena sarvatra gamanaśīlaḥ, prāṇeśaḥ – prāṇādhipapadavācyo jīvaḥ, aṅguṣṭhamātraḥ- aṅguṣṭapramāṇapramitatayā, anukathitaḥ - anūditaḥ, anyatra kvacit - itihāsādirūpe granthaviśeṣe, aṅguṣṭhamātram — aṅguṣṭhapramāṇaparimitam, puruṣam - puruṣapadavācyamartham, yamaḥ - vaivasvataḥ puruṣaḥ, niśkarṣa – śarīrāt pṛthak ākṛṣṭavān ityapi dṛṣṭam evaṃ smṛtivākyātmanā dṛṣṭamasti, tasmāt — uktādetasmāt kāraṇāt upaniṣat – 'aṅguṣṭhamātraḥ puruṣo madhya ātmani tiṣṭhati' ityevaṃrūpā kaṭhopaniṣat etatpramāṇapramitam - aṅguṣṭhapramāṇapramitamartham, jīvamāha—jīvātmānameva vadati, iti ayuktam — etat yuktihīnam hi yataḥ, tat mānam - aṅguṣṭhapramāṇam, vākyastheśānatādeḥ - tadvākyata eva bodhyāt jagadīśvaratvā diliṅgāt, nara- hṛdayaparicchittitaḥ manuṣya de hī yahṛdayātma deśakṛta paricchedata eva na svabhāvakṛtam iti niścīyate || 18 ||

na hyaṅguṣṭhapramāṇaṃ hṛdayamakhilajantvāśrayaṃ tat parasmin

vyāpte tanmātratoktiḥ kvaciditi manujādhikriyoktiprasaṅge | sūtradvandvadvayāntastribhiradhikaraṇaiścintyate tadviśeṣaḥ
tārtīyaiḥ sthāpanīyā tvavajigamipitā netikartavyatā'tra || 19 ||
aṅguṣṭhapramāṇam – aṅguṣṭhākāraparicchinnam, hṛdayam - hṛdayākhyaṃ sthānam, na hi akhilajantvāśrayam—sakalaprāṇisambandhi naiva bhavati, tat— tasmāddhetoḥ, vyāpte — vibhau, parasmin pare brahmaṇi, tanmātra toktiḥ śrutau aṅguṣṭhapramitatā kathanam, kvacit – upāsanādhi - kṛtanarahṛdaye eva; na sarvatra, iti - evamuktyā, manujādhikriyoktiprasaṅge– 'manuṣyāṇamevopāsa- nādhikāra:' iti kathanasya prasaṅge sampanne sati, mūtradvandvadvayāntaḥ - ' śabdādeva' 'hṛdyapekṣayā tu manuṣyādhikāratvāt ' iti sūtradvikasya (tathā) 'kampanāt '

1 tanmānatoktiḥ - ā. 2 tātave -- ā.

' jyotirdarśanāt ' iti sūtradvikasya ca garbhabhūtaiḥ tribhiḥ adhikaraṇaiḥ–devātādhikaraṇa-madhvadhikaraṇa apaśūdrādhikaraṇarūpaiḥ, trisaṅkhyākaiḥ nyāyaḥ, tadviśeṣaḥ —adhikāraviśeṣaḥ, cintyate vicārya nirṇīyate | tārtīyaiḥ – tṛtīyādhyāya- sambandhibhiḥ adhikaraṇaiḥ, sthāpanīyā - vicārato nirṇeyā, iti kartavyatā - upāsanaṃ prati aṅgakala| pabhūtā, adhikriyā tu — adhikārapadābhidheyā tu, atra – upāsanādhikāra viśeṣa- nirṇayārtha pravṛtte'sminnadhikaraṇatraye, na avajigamipitā na bubodhayiṣitā (antarbhāvitaṇyarthaḥ) || adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ
pramitādhikaraṇe—‘aṅguṣṭhamātraḥ puruṣaḥ' ityādivākye aṅguṣṭhamātratvaśravaṇāt, 'aṅguṣṭhamātro ravitulyarūpaḥ' ityādiṣu jīvasya aṅguṣṭhamātratvaśravaṇāt aṅguṣṭhapramitaḥ jīvaḥ -

ityāśaṅkaya,

'īśāno bhūtabhavyasya' iti bhūtabhavyeśānatvaśravaṇāt aṅguṣṭhapramitatvasya hṛdayopādhikasya paramātmanyupapatteḥ, aṅguṣṭhamātraḥ paramātmā – ityuktam ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā
brahmaṇo'stvalpamānatvaṃ pramitādhikṛtau punaḥ | tadviśeṣaśruterjīvaḥ syāditi pratyudīryate || 54 || || iti pramitādhikaraṇam || 6 ||
devatādhikaraṇam || 2-3-7 ||
śārīrakanyā yakalāpasaṅgrahaḥ
avigrahā vigrahavatprasādhye saṃrādhane nā'dhikṛtāstu devāḥ |
ityapyasat kalpitanāmarūpāḥ tasmānmanuṣyā iva te'pi śaktāḥ ||35|| vigrahavatprasādhye_saśarīraireva kartumarhe, saṃrādhane-yāgopāsanādau devaprasādanasādhanī-

bhūte karmaṇi, avigrahāḥ – aśarīrāḥ, devāḥ – labdhadevā dibhāvāḥ cetanāḥ, na tu adhikṛtāḥ- adhikāriṇo naiva bhaveyuḥ, ityapi — evamāśaṅkanamapi, asat - na sādhu | te'pi - prāptadevabhāvā api cetanāḥ, manuṣyā iva - labdhamartyabhāvāścatanā iva kalpitanāma-rūpāḥ – īśasaṅkalpa- prabhavadevādināmarūpabhāja eva, tasmāt tata eva hetoḥ, te'pi – devā api, śaktāḥ- arthitvasāmarthyātmakādhikāravanta eva ||35||

tattvasāraḥ paṭutara vigrahādimatāṃ bhavatāpavatāṃ bhagavadupāsanaṃ diviṣadāmapi sambhavati | nanu śatamarthavādavacasāmapi mantragirāṃ vidhivadiha pramāṇamapadoṣamapuṃ prabhavam ||31||
sauṣṭhavātiśayasampanna dehendriyādiyuktānām, bhavatāpavatām — paramātmānanubhavarūpaṃ bhavaṃ

paṭutaravigrahaḥ dimatām – manuṣyāpekṣayā diviṣadāmapi devalokavāsināmapi cetanānām tāpatvena cintayatām bhagavadupāsanam - bhagavaccintanarūpaṃ dhyānam, sambhavati – yujyata eva | arthavādavacasām — karmādyanuṣṭhānavidhyapekṣitārthasamarpaṇaparatayā vidhyā diśeṣabhūtānāṃ vedavākyānām, (tathā) mantragirām - dravya devatādyātmaka prayoga samavetārthasmārakāṇāṃ vedavākyānāmapi śatam- asaṅkhyātaṃ svarūpam, iha–asmin viṣaye vidhivat – 'yajeta svargakāma:' ityādividhi- - vākyānīva, apuṃprabhavam – apauruṣeyam, ata eva, apadoṣam – bhrama - vipralambhādidoṣasambhāvanā- vidūram pramāṇam - yathārthajñānasādhanamasti || 31 ||

stutiparamādyamatra nijasādhya vidheyaruci-
prajanana siddhaye svavacanīyaguṇādaravat | taditarathā na sidhyati vidhiṣviva tatvavidāṃ
kathamasatā guṇena kathitena nutirbhavati || 32 ||

atra - vidhiśeṣatvena abhimateṣvarthavādādiṣu, ādyam - prāthamikam, stutiparam - vidhyuktārthapraśaṃsāparaṃ arthavādātmakaṃ vedavākyam, nija... siddhaye – svotpādyo yaḥ vidhiviṣayī - bhūtayāgādyarthaviṣayakānuṣṭheyatva prakāra kecchā viśeṣaḥ tanniṣpatyartham svavacanīyaguṇādaravat- sva (arthavāda) pratipādyeṣu guṇeṣu mukhyatātparyavadeva vartate | itarathā - arthavādavacasāṃ svabodhyeṣu guṇeṣu mukhyatātparyābhāve, vidhiṣviva – guṇakathanāyāṃ pravṛtteṣu vidhivākyeṣviva arthavādeṣvapi tat- guṇakathanaparatvam, na sidhyati naiva nivṛttaṃ bhavati | asatā vastutaḥ avidyamānena, kathitena- vāṅmātrato varṇitena, guṇena stutyena dharmeṇa taccavidām — vastusvarūpaparijñānavatāṃ prekṣāvatāṃ vā, nutiḥ–yajanīyopāsanīyādau vāstavikaṃ praśastatvājñānam, kathaṃ bhavati – kena vā prakāreṇa jāyate ? || 32||

atha manavo vidheyaparibodhanakāryamukhā- dvidhimupakurvate hi diviṣadvapurādidhiyam | upajanayanta enamupakartumalaṃ khalu te
kathamitra devatā dhiyamupaiti vapurvidhurā ||33||
atha—–arthavādānantaram, manavaḥ – mantrāḥ, vidheyaparibodhanakāryamukhāt – anuṣṭheya- yāgādisambandhidravya-devatātmakārthasmāraṇadvārā vidhim - vidhāyakaṃ śāstram, upakurvate -- apekṣitātiśayayuktāt kurvanti hi yataḥ, te mantrāḥ diviṣadvapurādidhiyaṃ janayantaḥ- (asmāsu) devānāmapi śarīrādimatvajñānaṃ utpādayantaḥ, enam - vidhim, upakartum - vivakṣitena atiśayena yojayitum, alaṃ khalu – samarthā bhavanti hi ! devatā - yāgoddeśyabhūtā ammīndrādi- vyaktiḥ, vapurvidhurā - vigrahaśūnyā cet, kathamiva – kena vā prakāreṇa, dhiyamupaiti – buddhyā- rūḍhā bhavati || 33 ||

atha sura-nara- tiryak- sthavarātmaprapaśca- prajananagaṇanāsu cchandasāmantabhāge | prakaṭataramadhītā vigrahā devatānāmava-
'kalaya' gatiṃ copāsanaprakriyāsu ||34||

atha_vedasaṃhitānāṃ paścāttane, chandasāmantabhāge – upaniṣadbhāge, sura-nara.... gaṇanāsu- devādicaturvidha jagatsṛṣṭipratipādikāsu śrutiṣu devatānāṃ vigrahāḥ - ammīndrādīnāṃ śarīrādayaḥ, prakaṭataram - asandigdhaṃ yathā bhavati ; tathā, adhītāḥ – paripaṭhitāḥ upāsanaprakriyāsu ca- bhagavadupāsanapratipādakaprakaraṇeṣvapi gatim – devatānāmadhikārapratipādanaprakāram, avakalaya- vicārapūrvakaṃ niścinu ||34||

nigamaśata nidānairdevatāvigrahādi
sphuṭataramitihāsairdharmaśāstraiḥ purāṇaiḥ |
api gaditamanantopāsane' 'syādhikāra'-

stadayamamaravargastatphalārthī samarthaḥ ||35||

nigamaśatanidānaiḥ – nānā vedavāṅmūlaiḥ, itihāsaiḥ – purāvṛttapratipādakaiḥ bhāratādibhiḥ pranthasandarbhaiḥ, dharmaśāstraḥ – manvādipravaktṛkaiḥ smṛtigranthaiḥ, purāṇai: - sṛṣṭi-pralayādipratipādakaiḥ

'purāṇanāmakaiḥ granthaiḥ, devatā vigrahādi - yā goddeśya bhūtānāmindrāgnyādīnāṃ tanvādikam, sphuṭataram - vācyavṛttyaiva, gaditamapi – uktameva | tat — vigrahādimattvāt, ayam – śrutismṛtītihāsa- prasiddhadehendriyādiyuktaḥ, amaravarga : agnīndradevatāstomaḥ, tatphalārthī - prastutabrahmopāsanaphala- bhūtamokṣākāṅkṣī, samarthaḥ – upāsanopayogiśamādiyuktaśca bhavatīti, anantopāsane– deśakālā- dyupādhividhurasya paramātmanaḥ upāsanānuṣṭhāne, asya – arthitva - sāmarthyābhyāṃ yuktasya devagaṇasya, adhikāra : - śāstrataḥ prakṛtitaśca siddhā yogyatā durapahnavā ||35||

atraivādhikaraṇe_

saṃsthānaṃ kalayākṛtiṃ tvanugataṃ tāvat pratīmo hi tat brūmaścāparamapyadarśina tathā pakṣāntare jātiṣu | gotvātvapuraḥsarīṣu dhiṣaṇā bahvī sādhāraṇī
jātirjātiritīdṛśī phaṇitirapyevaṃ madīye pathi || 36 ||

atraivādhikaraṇe–devatādhikaraṇe eva, ākṛtim – 'ākṛti'padavācyāṃ jātim, saṃsthānaṃ kalaya - tattadavayavasanniveśākhyasaṃsthānā bhinnaṃ niścinu | hi yataḥ, tattu — tat saṃsthāna. padavācyaṃ vastveva, anugataṃ tāvat - anugatatvenaiva pratīmaḥ – jānīmaḥ, viśvasimo vā, brūmaḥ — tathaiva vyāharāmaśca, aparamapi— saṃsthānātiriktaṃ yatkiñcidapi tathā anugatatvena, na adarśi kairapi na dṛṣṭam, pakṣāntare— saṃsthānātiriktajātivāde'pi, gotvāśvatva purassarīṣu- gotvāśratvapramukhāsu, bahvīṣu jātiṣu – bahusaṅkhyākāsu jātiṣu jātirjātiriti sādhāraṇī dhiṣaṇā – 'jāti: jāti:' ityevamākārā anugatā pratītiḥ, phaṇitirapi vyavahṛtirapi, evam evameva, madīye pathi – saṃsthānasyaiva jātitvapakṣe'pi yujyata eva ; ( tattadasādhāraṇā- kārasyaiva saṃsthānatvāt, tasyaiva jātitvācceti) ||36||

ekā vyaktiṣu bhūyasīṣvanugatā pūrṇā prativyakti yā
saṃsthānādaparā nirādinidhanā sā jātirāsthīyatām | gaurgaurityanuvṛttabuddhi-vacasī no ceṭete katham ?
vyutpattiśca kathaṃ gavādivacasāṃ vyaktiṣvasaṃkhyāsu naḥ || 37 ||
bhūyasīṣu vyaktiṣu anugatā - bahusaṅkhyā keṣu dharmiṣu anuvṛttā, prativyakti pūrṇā- ekaikasyāmapi vyaktau anyūna (akhaṇḍita) svarūpā, ekā vyakti bhedāghīnasvabhedābhāvavatī, nirādinidhanā - utpattivināśarahitā, saṃsthānādaparā - avayavasanniveśaviśeṣāpekṣayā vilakṣaṇā

ca, yā-yā nāma bhavet sāmānyapadavācyā, sā jātirāsthīyatām – saiva jātipadābhidheye- tyabhyupagamyatām, no cet — tathā yadi nābhyupagamyate tarhi, gaurgaurityanuvṛtta buddhivacasī- 'ayaṃ gauḥ' iti sarvagovyaktyanugatagau pratītivyavahārau, kathaṃ ghaṭete—kathaṃ vā yujyete ! naḥ- asmākam asaṅkhyāsu vyaktiṣu — gaṇayitumaśakyāsu vyaktiṣu gavādivacasām— 'gauḥ ' ityādīnāṃ śabdānām, vyutpattiśva – pada padārthasaṅgatigraho'pi vā katham -- kena prakāreṇa sidhyati ? || 37 |

atra brūmaḥ pratīmaḥ kimapi kimaparaṃ vastu sāsnādimadhvāt gotvaṃ nāmānuvṛtti vyavahṛtiviṣayaṃ taddhi tādṛkṣamaikṣi | gotvājatvāśvatādiṣvanugatamaparaṃ vastu jātiṣvanekā-

svekaṃ jātyādiśabdavyavahṛtighaṭakaṃ jātivāde kimūce || 38 ||

atra – pūrvapakṣe'smin prāpte, brūmaḥ – vayamevaṃ pratyuttaraṃ vadāmaḥ, sāsnādimatvādaparam- gavādigatagalakambalaśṛṅgāderbhinnam, anuvṛttivyavahṛtiviṣayam – anekeṣvekākāra buddhi- tādṛśa- śabdaprayogayorgocaram, gotvaṃ nāma – gotvapadābhidheyam, kimapi vastu – pramāṇasambandhārha yatkiñcit kiṃ pratīmaḥ - prayatnato gaveṣaṇe'pi kiṃ jānīmaḥ ? naiva,

taddhi – sāsnādimatvameva

khalu, tādṛkṣam —anuvṛttapratyayavyavahārādhāyakam aikṣi – dṛṣṭamasti | jātivāde —atirikta- sāmānyāṅgīkārakarūpe'pi, gotvājatvāśvatādipu – gotva ajatva - aśvatvaprabhṛtiṣu, anekāsu jātiṣu - asaṅkhyāsu jātipadābhidheyāsu, jātyādiśabdavyavahṛtighaṭakam – jātyādipada- vyavahāropapādakaṃ, aparam ekaṃ vastu – saṃsthānāpekṣayā bhinnaṃ yatkiñcidekaṃ vastu, kimūce- pratipāditamasti vā? naivāstītyarthaḥ || 38 ||

anyasyādarśanena vyavahṛtighaṭanasyā'nyathaivopapanyā kalpyatvāsambhavenā'pyubhayasamayasaṅgītasaṃsthābalambī |
yukto jātyādiśabdaḥ susahahi mithaḥ sāhyanusyūtadhīvā-
gvyutpattyarheva yādṛk parasamayamatāmākṛtiṃ sā vyanakti || 39 ||

anyasya – saṃsthānātiriktasya jātipadābhidheyatā yogyasyārthasya, adarśanena gavādi -- vyaktiṣu anupalambhena, (tathā) vyavahṛtighaṭanasya – sarva vyavahārasāmarasyasya, anyathaiva upapacyā prakārāntarataḥ siddhyā, kalpyatvāsambhavenā'pi - anupalabdhasyā'pi anyathānupa- panyadhīnakalpanāviṣayatvasyāpyabhāvena, ubhayasamayasaṅgītasaṃsthāvalambī- pakṣadvayanirvivādābhyupa- gatasaṃsthānaviṣayakatvenaiva, jātyādiśabdaḥ – jāti-guṇa- kriyā yadṛcchābhedena caturdhā vibhaktaḥ

sarvo'pi śabdaḥ, iha – taistaiḥ śabdairbodhyeṣu sajātīyeṣu teṣu teṣvartheṣu, yuktaḥ svabodhyena saṅgataḥ, hi yataḥ, sā – saṃsthā, saṃsthānam, mithaḥ susadṛk-anyonyamatyantasau sādṛśyavatyeva vartate | (ata eva ), anusyūtadhī - vāgvyutpattyarheva — anugatapratīti tādṛśavyavahāra- tādṛśaśaktigrahāṇāṃ niyāmakatvārthaiva, yādṛk sā-yādṛśyaiva sā – yādṛśyaiva saṃsthā avayava saṃniveśaviśeṣātmikā saiva, parasamayamatām – atirikta jātivādibhiraṅgīkṛtām, ākṛtim - mīnāṃsakaiḥ ākṛtipadābhidheyāṃ sāmānyātmikāṃ jātim vyanakti - 'ākṛtigrahaṇā jātiḥ - anugatasaṃsthānavyaṅgayā jātiḥ' ityuktarītyā vyañjayati || 39 ||

abhāvasyābhāvo bhavati khalu bhāvaḥ paramate ghaṭāderapyevaṃ kathaya kimabhāvo na bhavati |
ghaṭasya pradhvaṃso bhavati hi kapālatvamapara-
stvabhāvaḥ piṇḍatvaṃ kimiha dadṛśe kiñcidaparam ||40 ||

paramate naiyāyikādīnāṃ mate, bhāvaḥ astīti vyavahārāspadaḥ ghaṭādirūpo'rthaḥ abhāvasyābhāvo bhavati khalu - ghaṭapratiyogi kābhāvasya abhāvarūpaḥ san 'ghaṭābhāvo nāsti ' iti pratītyā viṣayīkriyate khalu ; ( dvitīyābhāvasya prathamābhāvapratiyogisvarūpatvāt ), evam - uktayā rītyā ghaṭādeḥ abhāvo'pi 'ghaṭo na' ityullikhyamānaḥ ghaṭabhedātmakaḥ- abhāvo'pi, kim abhāvo na bhavati — abhāvapadavācyo bhavituṃ nārhati vā ? bhavatyeva, kathaya – ālocya vada | ghaṭasya pradhvaṃsaḥ - 'iha pūrva ghaṭa āsīt, adya nāsti' ityullikhyamānaḥ ghaṭapratiyogikaḥ dhvaṃsapadavācyaḥ abhāvo'pi, kapālatvaṃ hi bhavati – ghaṭāvayavaka pālavṛttidharmātmaka eva siddhyati | ghaṭasya aparaḥ abhāvastu – 'iha ghaṭo bhaviṣyati, adya nāsti' ityullikhyamānaḥ ghaṭapratiyogikaḥ prāgabhāvapadavācyaḥ abhāvastu, piṇḍatvam - ghaṭopādānabhūtamṛgatā piṇḍatvākhyābhāvo bhāvarūpa eva | iha—abhāvabuddhivyabahāragocare, aparaṃ kiñcit kimasti – sarvathābhāvatvānārūḍhaṃ kiñcit nañādyullekhyaṃ vartate vā ? sarvathā naivetyarthaḥ ||40||

saṃsargābhāvamevaṃ viduriha sudhiyo bhāvameva tvabhāvaṃ
manvānāḥ kumbhavadbhūtalata itarabhūbhāga bhedastvabhāvaḥ | yastattatkālabhedavyatikaritagṛha prāṅgaṇādipradeśaḥ
kumbhābhāvāśrayo'nyairagaṇi sa iha naḥ kvā'dhikasyopalambhaḥ 1 ||42||
abhāvam – nanullikhyaṃ sarvamapi bhāvameva manvānāḥ – bhāvarūpa eveti vicārapūrvakaṃ nirṇayantaḥ, sudhiyaḥ—sūkṣmamatayo vidvāṃsaḥ, iha saṃsargābhāvam — abhāveṣvanyatamaṃ 'nāsti

ghaṭo gehe' ityādau ghaṭa - gehādiṣu geha - ghaṭādisaṃsargapratiṣedhātmakama bhāvam evaṃ viduḥ - vakṣyamāṇarītyā niścinuyuḥ ; abhāvaḥ tatra abhāvapadavācyo'rthaḥ kumbhavadbhūtalataḥ — ghaṭā ghi- karaṇībhūtabhūtalāpekṣayā, itarabhūbhāgabhedastu –— anyo bhūpradeśaviśeṣa eva, anyaiḥ – abhāvapadārthasya pṛthaktvavādibhiḥ, yaḥ– yādṛśaḥ, tattatkāla pradeśaḥ – pūrvāparakṣaṇādyātmaka kālaviśeṣasambaddha- gehīyacatvarādirūpaḥ pradeśaviśeṣaḥ, kumbhābhāvāśrayaḥ - tannirṇīta ghaṭābhāvapadārtha prati ādhāratvena, agaṇi— nirṇītaḥ | saḥ tādṛśaḥ pradeśaviśeṣaḥ

naḥ bhāvātmakābhāvavādināmasmākam,

iha — abhāvapadavyavahāryasya sthāne bhavati | adhikasya -vyavahārārhasya yasya kasyacanārthasya, upalambhaḥ – darśanam

etadapekṣayā vilakṣaṇasya abhāvapada-
kka - kutra vidyate ? || 41 ||

sacca tyacceti sṛṣṭvā viśasi cidacitau tena cokto'si viśvaṃ tvacā' valayana' tvamiti ca nikhilaṃ tvaccharīraṃ kileti | taistaiḥ śāstrairabhedavyavahṛti hṛdayaṃ vistṛṇānairnirastāḥ

bhedābhedāvabhedaṃ bhramamapi bhagavan kāraṇaṃ kalpayantaḥ || 42 ||

bhagavan– he jñānādiṣaḍguṇaiśvaryasampanna ! svāmin! tvam, sacca tyacceti – 'sat'- padavācyaṃ svarūpato'vikāri, (tathā) svarūpa- svabhāvābhyāṃ vikāri 'tyat' padavācyaṃ ca, cidacitau - cetanācetanajātam sṛṣṭvā utpādya viśasi teṣu anupraveśaṃ kṛtavānasi | tena ca evaṃ cetaneṣvacetaneṣu ca kṛtānupraveśatvādeva viśvam ukto'si - ' viśvamevedaṃ puruṣa:' ityādiṣu viśvatvena pratipādito'si | nikhilam - cetanācetanātmakaṃ sarvamapi jagat, tvajatvāt — brahmapadavācyatvadabhinnanimittopādānakatvāt tvallayanatvāt - brahmapadavācye tvayi līyamānatvāt brahmapadavācyatvadāyattaprāṇanatvācca hetoḥ, tvaccharīraṃ kileti tvāṃ prati śarīratvenaiva śrutismṛtyādiṣu prasiddhamiti tvam iti ca ukto'si sarva khalvidaṃ brahma iti ca pratipādito'si | (a) abhedavyavahṛtihṛdayam - jagadrahmaṇoḥ abhedavyavahārasya nidānabhūtaṃ śrutyāśayam vistṛṇānaiḥ – vistāreṇa varṇayadbhiḥ, taistaiḥ śāstraiḥ—jagati paramātmānupraveśasya paramātmanaḥ sakāśādutpattisthitilayānām, paramātmaśarīratāyāśca prati- pādana paraiḥ śrutismṛtītihāsapurāṇādyātmakaiḥ śāstra: ( jñāpakatvenāśritaiḥ), bhedābhedau kāraṇaṃ - kalpayantaḥ — 'brahmaṇi jagati ca mitho vidyamānau yau bhedābhedau tāveva jagadbrahmaṇorabhedavyavahāra- hetubhūtau' iti śrutyādikamananuruddhya svabuddhyā cintayantaḥ, abhedaṃ bhramaṃ ca kāraṇaṃ kalpa-

་

yantaḥ jīve brahmābhedaṃ, jagadviṣaye brahmaṇo bhramaṃ ca abhedavyavahārahetuṃ kalpayantaḥ bhāskara- yādavīya - śāṅkarāḥ, nirastāḥ - nirākṛtāḥ || 41 ||
atha prāsaṅgikam aruṇādhikaraṇam – devatā dhikaraṇa vicāraprasaktānuprasaktānām eteṣā- marthaviśeṣāṇāṃ nirūpaṇānantaram, tathaiva prasaṅgākhyasaṅgatimat aruṇādhikaraṇamapi pūrvamīmāṃsoktaṃ vicāraṇīyaṃ prāptamastītyarthaḥ ||
unnītaṃ guruṇā'ruṇādhikaraṇe na hyekahāyanyagāt
āruṇyānvayamābhidhānikamapi tvākṣepataḥ prāpitam |
yadbrūte'ruṇayeti kevalaguṇaṃ vāgdravyanirdeśava-
dvākyasthā na girastu kārakavibhaktyantā mithaḥ saṅgatāḥ || 43 ||

pūrvamīmāṃsānyāye, unnītam — vicārya evaṃ nirṇītamasti ; ekahāyanī - ' aruṇayā piṅgākṣyā ekahāyanyā" gavā somaṃ krīṇāti' iti somakrayaṇasādhanopadeśapare vākye śrutā ekahāyanī , ābhidhānikam – śabdataḥ prāptam, āruṇyānvayam - raktatā rūpaguṇasambandham, na hi agāt- naiva prāptavatī, api tu kiṃ tu, ākṣepataḥ prāpitam — anyathānupapattisiddhameva, yat - yasmāddhetoḥ, dravyanirdeśavadvākyasthā – dravyavācakagopadavadvākyagatā, aruṇayā iti vāk- śrutisthā tṛtīyāvibhaktyantā aruṇapadātmikā gīḥ, kevalaguṇam - dravyaṃ vinaiva viśeṣaṇamātrātma- kam āruṇyam, brūte – abhidhatte kārakavibhaktyantāḥ karmatvakaraṇatvādikāraka pratipādaka- dvitīyā divibhaktatyantatayā dṛśyamānāḥ, giraḥ – 'aruṇayā ekahāyanyā' ityādyāḥ vācaḥ, mithaḥ – parasparam, na tu saṅgatāḥ – naiva anvitārthakāḥ bhavitumarhanti || 43 ||

guruṇā — prābhākaramīmāṃsakena,

aruṇādhikaraṇe—- etannāma ke

aruṇādhikaraṇasaraṇiḥ samagaṇi rāmānujāryamunibhiryā | uciteyamanucitānyā pariṣadi viduṣāṃ sphuṭībhavati || 44 ||

rāmānujāryamunibhiḥ – rāmānujācāryākhyaiḥ yativaraiḥ, yā— yādṛśarītimatī, aruṇādhi karaṇasaraṇiḥ– aruṇādhikaraṇasambandhinī pūrvapakṣasiddhāntayoḥ prakriyā, samagaṇi - niṣkṛṣya pradarśitā, iyamucitā – iyameva aduṣṭā, saprayojanārthanirṇayopayoginī ca, anyā - etadbhinnā prābhākarādibhiḥ pradarśitā saraṇistu, anucitā – sadoṣā, saprayojanārthanirṇayāya nā'lam iti, viduṣāṃ pariṣadi—–tattvārthasaṃśīlanai kapaṭūnāṃ sabhāyām, sphuṭībhavati vyaktībhavati | (na gṛhītagrāhiṇāṃ mandānām ayamarthaḥ sunirṇayaḥ ityarthaḥ) || 44 ||

upātte'pi dravye visadṛśavibhakti vyapagamāt
prakṛtyā vaiśiṣṭyaṃ pariṇamati khalvā kṛtinayāt | vibhaktistvarthaikyaṃ 'kathayati' samānaiva padayoḥ
trirūpā sā saṅkhyānvayamitra diśet kārakadhiyam || 45 ||

dravye upāttespi 'aruṇayā ekahāyanyā gavā somaṃ krīṇāti' ityādau ekahāyanītva- viśiṣṭagorūpe dravye kaṇṭhataḥ ukte satyapi visadṛśa vibhaktivyapagamāt – 'paṭasya śuklaḥ ityādāviva asamānāyāḥ vibhakternirdeśābhāvāt, ākṛtinayāt – ākṛtyadhikaraṇanyāyataḥ prakṛtyā - tṛtīyā vibhakteḥ prakṛtibhūtayā aruṇetyanayā, vaiśiṣṭayam - āruṇyaguṇavattvam, pariṇamati khalu – paryavasyati khalu ! padayoḥ – 'aruṇayā' 'ekahāyanyā' iti padayoḥ śrūyamāṇā, samānā vibhaktistu – tṛtīyātmikā ekā vibhaktistu, arthaikyam-- āruṇyaikahāyanatvayordharmya bhedam kathayatyeva – pratipādayatyeva | trirūpā – kārakavibhaktitva- | samānavibhaktitvaikavacanatvātmakarupatrayavatī, sā - tṛtīyātmikā vibhaktiḥ, saṅkhayānvayamiva --- ekavacanatvena rūpeṇa ekatvasaṅkhyā saṃsargamiva, kārakadhiyam -' karaṇādhikaraṇayośca' iti karaṇārthavihitavibhaktitatvena rūpeṇa, krayasādhanatva viṣayakaṃ bodhamapi, diśet - janayituṃ śaknuyādeva || 45 ||

śrīvātsyavaradāryāṇāṃ tattvasāre'yamāditaḥ |
adhyāyo rāmabhadreṇa ṭippaṇyā''yojitaḥ śubhaḥ ||

-0-

-yasyāḥ sā ) lokaparidṛśyamānākāra viśiṣṭaśabdākhyasvarūpā bhavet ; na tu vilakṣaṇā avedyā | ataḥ śabdasvarūpatvāt, laukikāgnyādirūpatvādvā, tasyāḥ – devatāyāḥ, brahmopāsā - vedāntaiḥ mokṣārthaṃ vihitaṃ brahmadhyānātmakamupāsanam, na naiva sambhavati, iti – īdṛśaṃ varṇanam, anārtham - śrutitātparyavidūram | ( yataḥ ) śrutapariharaṇam - śrutisiddhāyāḥ ('enaṃ prīṇāti' iti) devatāprīteḥ, ('kṣepiṣṭhā devatā' ityādyuktāyāḥ) kṣepiṣṭhatvādikāyāśca devatāgatāyāḥ anabhyupagamaḥ, aśrutasya kalpanaṃ ca śrutau anuktaṃ yat 'devatāyāḥ śabdātmakatvam, laukikā- kṛtimantvam, karmajanyāpūrvasyaiva phalapradatvaṃ; na devatāyā:' ityādi, tasya asato'rthasya svabuddhayā cintanaṃ ceti, (dvau doṣau āpatetām ), viśvasraṣṭā ca sarvajagannirmātā paramapuruṣastu, anumitiviṣayo mā bhūt - śāstratarānumānagamyo na bhavatītyayaṃ nirṇayaśca acalita evāstu | (yataḥ) eṣaḥ – jagatkāraṇabhūtaḥ paramātmā, tatparaiḥ - īśvarapratipādanaidamparyavadbhiḥ nirvādha ḥ— bādhakapramāṇāntararahitaiḥ, śāstrastu – 'sadeva somyedamagra āsīt ' 'ātmā vā idameka evāgra āsīt ' 'brahma vā idamekamevāgra āsīt ' ityādiśāstraḥ, prāka śāstrayonitvādhikaraṇe eva, sthāpitaḥ — prayatapratiṣṭhaḥ | (adya) svayamapi vibhunā --sarvaśaktena sākṣātparamātmanaivā'pi, naiva śakyāpalāpaḥ – sarvathā anapala- panīsvarūpā vijṛmbhate ||20||

sāmārthya devatānāmucitatanubhṛtāmarthitā tāpabhājāṃ

sampadyeteti tāsāmapi bhavati paropāstivargādhikāraḥ | khyātaṃ mantrārthavādaprabhṛtiṣu nikhilaṃ bādhadoṣa dyabhāve
mithyetyudghoṣayantaḥ svata iha kathitāṃ mānatāṃ 'prasmaranti || 21 ||

ucitatanubhṛtām — upāsanānuṣṭhānayogya śarīrasampattimatām, devatānām — devabhāvajuṣāṃ cetanānām, sāmarthyam — janmanā, karmaṇā, buddhyā ca sādhanārambhe ārabdhasya sādhanasya paripūrtyāpādane tataḥ phalaprāptau ca yogyatā, tāpabhājām – tāpatrayabhāgitvādeva teṣām, arthitā - ( tāpavimoca ke sādhane) icchā ca, sampadyeteti sampannā bhavedeveti, tāsāmapi- devatānāmapi paropāstivargādhikāraḥ sarvāsu paravidyāsu mokṣasādhanībhūtāsu anuṣṭhātṛtvam, bhavati vartata eva | mantrārthavādaprabhṛtiṣu karmāṅgatayā viniyukteṣu mantreṣu, karmaprāśastye- dampareṣu arthavādeṣu karmavidhāyakeṣu brāhmaṇabhāgeṣu tathaivopaniṣatsu ca khyātam – spaṣṭapratipannameva,

nikhilam - tattaddevatā vigrahaguṇavibhūtyādikaṃ sarvam, bādha-dopādyabhāve – pramāṇāntarasiddhaca | dhaka pratyayasya, etatpramājana ke doṣasya cābhāve'pi, mithyeti — asaditi, aparamārthamiti vā, udghoṣayantaḥ—niḥśaṅkamāḍambareṇa pralapantaḥ nirīśvaramīmāṃsakāḥ, iha – samagra vedarāśiviṣaye, svataḥ kathitām — svaireva kathitām svato mānatām – pramāṇāntarānadhīnaṃ prāmāṇyam, prasmaranti asmṛtvaiva yatkiñcidvadanti na tu svoktapūrvāparāviruddhaṃ, viṣaya viruddhaṃ vā || 21 ||

dvedhā vṛttiḥ stutau syāt sva- paraguṇamukhī prāktanī tāvadarthyā nirdhāryaḥ paścimāyāmapi nipuṇadhiyā mukhyadharmaikadeśaḥ | rucyarthāyāṃ ca tasyāmanṛtakathanato rocanā na hyamugdhe
bhūtārthe kā stutiḥ syāditi munigaditaṃ gauṇatādernivṛtyai || 22 ||

nānādehāptiśaktā: - yugapadeva anekaśarīragrahaṇasāmarthyavanto devāḥ, śrutiṣu — vedeṣu, tattadvṛttāntasacce – tattathāvidhadevagatamāhātmya pratipādakavṛttāntānāṃ vidyamānatve, kathamiva yugapat karmasaṃnidhyanarhāḥ 1 – kathaṃ vā ekadānuṣṭhīyamāneṣu bahuṣu karmasu svayaṃ saṃnidhātumana bhaveyuḥ, pravāhāt — devānāṃ pravāhato'nāditvāt anityayogo na bhavati - anityavigrahādi - maddevatāvarṇane'pi vedānām anityārthasambandharūpo doṣa: nā'patati | vedanityatvasiddheḥ – vedeṣu kadāpya viparyastānupūrvīkatvarūpa nityatvasya pramāṇasiddhatvāt kāṇḍādau–kāṭhaka- vaiśvāmitra- suktā divedabhāgaviśeṣe, kartṛvādaḥ kaṭhādisaṃjñānāṃ munīnāṃ nirmātṛtva pravādaḥ, pravacananiyataḥ — taistaireva kalpādau tattadbhāgapravaktṛtvāyattaḥ ; na tu svādhīnaprayoktatvakṛtaḥ, varṇasarge'pi varṇānā- mekaikaśaḥ sṛjyamānatve'pi, īśa: – sarveśvaro bhagavān nārāyaṇa:, vedān - vedasaṃjñān viśiṣṭānu- pūrvīkān śabdarāśīn prācīna kalpakramataḥ – pūrvakalpasiddhānupūrvī mavihāyaiva, upadiśet-. paścāttane kalpe'pi brahmaṇe upadeśaṃ karotīti, (vedanityatvasya na virodhaḥ) || 23||

vedānāmīśabuddhayā kramaniyamahatiḥ kalpabhede yadīṣṭā
mantrāṃśānāṃ tathā syānna khalu taducitaṃ vrīhisomādisāmyāt |
itthaṃ vidhyarthavādakrama iti 'niyate' pākṣike vā tathātve
pakṣo'sāvākṣapādaḥ paramatapariṣatkoṭimāṭīkatāṃ naḥ || 24 ||

kalpabhede – kalpāntare pravṛtte sati vedānām - śrutīnām, krama niyamahatiḥ - ānupūrvī - niyamabhaṅgaḥ, īśabuddhyā – īśvarasya saṅkalpena bhavitumarhatīti, iṣṭā yadi – sammanyate yadi ; nadā, mantrāṃśānām — mantrapadābhidheyānāṃ vedaghaṭaka bhāgānāmapi tathā syāt – ānupūrvīniyama- bhaṅgaḥ sammataḥ syāt | tat – niyatānupūrvīkatāyā bhaṅgasya mantreṣvanabhyupagamanam, nocitaṃ khalu - naiva saṅgataṃ khalu | (kutaḥ ?) vrīhisomādisāmyāt – yā gīyahaviḥprakṛtibhūtānāṃ trīcākhyadhānya- somākhyalatādirūpāṇāṃ dravyāṇāṃ yāgasampādakatvamiva prayogasamaveta dravya devatā disamarpakāṇāṃ mantrāṇāmapi, yāgaṃ prati anvayavyatirekayoḥ niyamagrāhakatāyāḥ samānatvāt ; ittham - mantreṣu niyatānupūrvīkatvamiva vidhyarthavādakramaḥ -- yāgasvarūpajñāpakeṣu vidhivākyeṣu (tathā) yāga- praśaṃsāsamarpakeṣu arthavādavākyeṣvapi ca avarjanīyaḥ ānupūrvyaviparyāsa:, iti niyate— ukta- hetunā aviśeṣato vedatvāvacchedena ānupūrvyavaiparītye'bhyupagate, pākṣike vā tathātve vedaika- deśātmakamantrabhāgagatānupūrvīnyame vā svīkṛte sati, asau – vedāntisammatividūraḥ, ākṣapādaḥ pakṣaḥ - gautamīyatvena prāptapracāraḥ pakṣaḥ, naḥ – vedāntināmāśayā nurodhena, paramatapariṣatkoṭim - avaidikasiddhāntapraveśam, āṭīkatām – prāpnotu nāma; kā hāniḥ || 24 ||

saukṣmyāttulyābhicārāt sahakṛdapagamācchādakādānyaparyāt
atyāsacyātidūrādbalavadabhibhavānudbhavākṣopaghātaiḥ |
nekṣyante vartamānānyapi hi suragaṇāsta' dvadantardhiśakteḥ
prakhyātāḥ siddhibhedā api janana- tapo - yoga- mantrauṣadhibhyaḥ || 25 ||

vartamānānyapi – adūrasannikṛṣṭāni vastūnyapi saukṣmyāt – ( paramāṇvādaya iva ) sūkṣmaparimāṇatvāt, tulyā bhighārāt – (tailaniṣiktatailamiba) atyantasu sadṛśa vastusaṃsargāt, sahakṛdapagamāt – (andhakārasthapadārtha iva) ālokādisahakārikāraṇavirahāt chādakāt - (paṭādyantaritavastunība) tirodhāyakāt, ānyaparyāt draṣṭuḥ puruṣasya viṣayāntaravyāsakta- manaskatvāt, atyāsattyā— (nayana gola sthadhūlyāderiva ) atyantanaikaṭyāt, atidūrāt – (nabho- maṇḍalagatanakṣatrādisthasaṃniveśa iva) atyantadūratvāt, balavadabhibhavāt – (madhyāhnagatapradīpādi- vi) balavatā vastvantareṇa gṛhītena etadgrahaṇanirodhāt anudbhavāt – (cakṣuḥ kiraṇā iva) pratyakṣataupayikodbhūtatvābhāvāt, akṣopaghātāt – (netrarogādineva) indriyavaikalyāccatyetebhyo hetubhyaḥ, na īkṣyante hi naiva cakṣuṣā gṛhyante khalu, tadvat – tatra tatra teṣu teṣu indriyagrāhyā api yathā na gṛhyante ; tathā antardhiśakteḥ – svasvarūpatirodhānaupayikaśaktimatvāt, suragaṇāḥ - sarvatra sthātumahaḥ devasamūhā api na īkṣyante – cakṣuṣā na dṛśyante | janana.... padhibhyaḥ janmata eva vā, tapobalena vā.. yogabalena vā, jāpyamānamantrabalena vā, oṣadhi- śaktito vā, siddhibhedā api antardhānādyanukūlāḥ siddhiviśeṣā api, prakhyātāḥ - lokaśāstrādiṣu prasiddhā eva | (ataḥ vidyamānānāmapi devatānāmanupalambho yujyata eveti) || 25 ||

adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ

devatādhikaraṇe_devādīnāṃ nirdu:khānām aśarīratvena, arthitva- sāmarthyayorabhāvena, brahmavidyāyāmadhikāro nāsti ityāśaṅkaya,

sṛṣṭiprakaraṇeṣu upāsanaprakaraṇeṣu ca arthavāde śarīrapratipādanāt tāpatrayābhibhūtatvācca arthitva-sāmarthyayoḥ sambhavāt teṣāmapyadhikāro'sti - iti siddhāntitam ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā
upāsanaṃ manuṣyādhikāraṃ cet pramitoktitaḥ | devādhikārāpavādo devatānayavāritaḥ || 55 ||
|| iti devatādhikaraṇam || 7 ||

upāsakasyaiva cetanasya, ārādhyabhāvaḥ — upāsanena prasādanīyatā, (tathā) svapadamapi svenānubhūyamānaṃ sthānameva phalam - upāsanena labhyaṃ prayojanam | atra – īdṛśe upāsanaviśeṣe na adhikāraḥ kartṛtvātā naivā'stīti cintane prāpte, maivam anayā rītyā nirṇayo nocitaḥ | muktikāmā:- mumukṣavaḥ, sarvāntarātmā_sarveṣāṃ cetanācetanānāmantaḥ praviśya niyantā, svatanubhṛt – svātmaśarīrakaḥ, iti ca - iti prakāradvayena, upāsate – upāsanaṃ kurvanti | ( ataḥ ) kalpa- manvantarādau – kalpāntara - manvantaraprabhṛti ke kālaviśeṣe, svapadamapi – vasutvavataḥ vasutva- prādhyātmakaṃ phalameva ca phalam - vasvādikṛtopāsanasya phalabhūtaṃ sat kāmāt – 'anantara- kalpe'pyahameva vasurbhaveyam' ityādirūpādicchāviśeṣāt, āvartate tu — punarapi labhyata

eva || 26 ||

adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ

madhvadhikaraṇe – svādīn prati vastrādīnāmupāsyatvāyogāt madhvā dividhāsu basvā - dīnāmanadhikāraḥ - ityāśaṅkaya,
vasvādiśarīrakabrahmaṇa eva upāsyatvāt, kalpāntare vastrāditvasya prāpyatvasambhavācca, - madhvādividyāsu vasvādīnāmadhikāro'sti iti siddhāntitam ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā
yadyapyupāstisāmānye syāt sāmarthyādisambhavaḥ | pratyudāharaṇatvena madhvādinayasambhavaḥ || 56 ||
|| iti madhvadhikaraṇam || 8 ||
