paraṃ pumāṃsam — paramapuruṣam vijñāya - mananena buḍhīkṛtya, upāsatām – bhaktiyogataḥ ārādha- yantu nāma ; itīdam — evaṃ kalpanam na sat naiva sādhu, śravaṇādikasya śravaṇamārabhya nidhidhyāsanāntasya sarvasyāpi, niṣedhāt – atraivarṇikānāṃ pratiṣiddhatvāt || 37 ||

.

taiḥ– śrutairbhāratādibhiḥ, na upabṛṃhyeta – viśadīkṛtārthajñānavān na bhavitumarhati | murabhit- bhagavān śrīkṛṣṇa eva dvisapta vidyāsthānānyapi - caturdaśa vidyā sthānānyapi śūdraiḥ- traivarṇiketaraiḥ, na spraṣṭavyānīti - adhyayanādinā sambaddhuṃ naiva yogyānīti, pāṇḍavāya yudhiṣṭhiramuddiśya, akathayat– 'tasmānnaitāni śūdrādyaiḥ spraṣṭavyāni yudhiṣṭhira !' iti ślokena kathitavān | tasmāt—asampāditaveda - tadarthāpātajñāṃnatvāt hetoḥ, vikaladhiyām - śrutyādi- codite jñāne vaikalyavatām, upāsādhikāraḥ - mokṣārthopāsanapravṛttyarhatā, na hi syāt naika siddhā bhavati || 28 ||
gītaṃ śūdrādikānāmapi parabhajanaṃ kevalaṃ svārhadharmaiḥ
dharmavyādhastulā' dhṛgvidura' iti ca 'te' prāgbhavābhyāsasiddhāḥ | vaktā śudreti jānaśrutimabhimukhayan śokamasya vyanakti

kṣattapraipādiliṅgaiḥ sphuṭataraviditaṃ kṣatriyatvaṃ hi | tasya || 29 || śūdrādikānāmapi — caturthādivarṇīyānāmapi, parabhajanam – paramātmasevanam, svārhadharmaiḥ kevalam – bhagavannāmasmaraṇatadvandanapuṣpādyāharaṇasamarpaṇādirūpaiḥ svayogyaiḥ karmabhireva ; upāsanāparaparyāyeṇa jñānena kāryam; ityabhiprāyeṇa gītam – śrīkṛṣṇena bhagavadgītāyāmuktaṃ 'māṃ hi pārtha vyapāśritya ' ityādau | dharmavyādhaḥ tulādhṛk viduraḥ iti ca te- 'dharmavyādhaḥ, tulādhāraḥ vidura:' ityete mahābhāratādiprasiddhāḥ vedatattvārthajñāstu, prāgbhavābhyāsa- siddhā: - vedādhikṛta pūrvajanmānuṣṭhitadhyānābhyāsalabdhajñānavanta eva ; na tu tajjanmīyena | vaktā- paratantvopadeśapravṛtto raikvastu, jānaśrutim - etannāmānam kṣatriyam śūdreti abhimukhayan - 'śūdra' śabdena sambodhayan asya – jānaśruteḥ upadeśyasya, śokam – brahmavidyāyā alābhāyattaṃ du:kham, vyanakti – spaṣṭīkaroti | hi yataḥ, tasya – jānaśruteḥ, kṣatriyatvam – kṣatriyavarṇa- jātatvam, kṣacapraiṣādiliṅgaiḥ—samabhivyāhṛtaiḥ raikvānveṣaṇārthaṃ sūtapreṣaṇādirūpaiḥ jñāpakaiḥ, sphuṭa- taraviditam – suspaṣṭaṃ jñātameva bhavati khalu ! || 29 ||

na tu

nayasaṅgatimālikā
śudro vidyādhikārī syāt sāmarthyārthitvayogataḥ | ityāśaṅkāpavādārthamapaśūdranayodayaḥ || 57 ||
iti apaśūdrādhikaraṇam || 9 ||

deva- manuṣyādisaṃjñāṃ tattadanuguṇāmākṛtiṃ ca bibharti | tadanu brahmabhāve muktyanantarakālike brahmatve labdhe sati, jahāti -saṃjñāmākṛtiṃ ca parityajati | ( ataḥ jīva evā'tra 'ākāśa' padārthaḥ) iti anyāyyam - īdṛśaṃ śaṅkanamayuktam, puroktaḥ - 'brahmalokam' iti pūrvavākyoktaḥ, ayam abhisambhāvya eva hi ! – abhisambhavānītyuktā bhisambhavana kriyā (prāpti) karmabhūtaḥ brahmatapo'rtha eva khalu, punarupāttaḥ- bhūyaḥ 'ākāśo havai' iti vākyena gṛhītaḥ, brahmatvam- paramātmagataḥ svābhāviko brahmatvarūpo dharmaḥ ('brahmaloka' śabdoktaḥ), na hyavasthā - āgantuka- dharmātmako na khalu vivakṣitaḥ ; kintu svābhāvika eva | ( yataḥ) śrutiṣu ca - śrutivākyeṣvapi, yugapat - samānakālikaḥ, jñājñatādi : - 'sarvajñatvaṃ sarveśvaratvaṃ ca brahmaṇi, ajñatvam anīśvaratvaṃ ca jīve' ityevaṃ jīva-brahmaṇordharmaḥ, vibhaktaśca – asādhāraṇyena pṛthakpṛthagvarṇito'pi vartate | ( ataḥ paramātmaparamevedaṃ vākyam ) || 30 ||

".

|

adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ

arthāntaratvādivyapadeśādhikaraṇe - 'ākāśo ha vai nāma-rūpayornirvahitā' ityādivākye, 'ākāśa'śabdanirdiṣṭo muktātmā; 'aśva iva romāṇi' iti vākye muktasya prakṛtatvāt— ityāśaṅkaya,
‘nāma-rūpayornirvahitā' iti, nāma-rūpanirvodṛtvasya paramātmā sādhāraṇatvaśravaṇāt, ‘brahmalokamabhisambhavāni' iti paramātmano'pyanantara prakṛtatvācca, ākāśaḥ paramātmā – iti siddhāntitam ||
nayasaṅgatimālikā prasaktānuprasaktyetthamadhikāracatuṣṭayam | dahanītyutthāpavādamarthāntaranayo'cchinat || 58 ||
iti arthāntaratvādivyapadeśādhikaraṇam || 10 |

akliṣṭabhāvaḥ -- śarīra-tadgatanāḍī sambandhādikṛtakleśānāśrayatvam sukharūpatā ca- nirupadhimahānandarūpatvam praśāsitṛtvam - nikhila jaganniyantṛtvam śrayaṇīyatā ca- trimātrapraṇavopāsyatvam guṇottaratvam – (svīyaguṇānāmapi svavadeva upāsyatvokteḥ) upāsya- kalyāṇaguṇāśrayatvam, ajugupsatā ca- mumukṣvanugraha saṅkalpāyattā ṅgaṣṭhapramāṇavattayā anupāsyatve- nā''śaṅkanīyatā ca, nirvor̤hatā ca svena sṛṣṭānāṃ cetanānāmacetanānāṃ ca anuguṇanāmarūpa- nirvāhakatvam, ityevaṃrūpā paramātmagatā vibhūtiḥ, asmin pratipāditā - tṛtīye pāde samyagvodhitā ||
pradhānajīvayorvyaṣṭayoḥ samaṣṭayoścātmatā ' kramāt ' |
uktā tṛtīye tu punastasyāḥ śāsanatā'pi ca ||

pradhāna- jīvayoḥ vyaṣṭayoḥ samaṣṭayośca – acidvayaṣṭeḥ acitsamaṣṭe : (tathā) cidvayaṣṭeḥ citsamaṣṭeśca, ātmatā — paramātmanaḥ eteṣu vyāpanapūrvakaṃ niyantṛtā, kramāt - prathama dvitīya- yoradhikaraṇayoruktā | tṛtīye tu punaḥ - tṛtīyādhikaraṇe tāvat tasyāḥ – uktāyā ātmatāyāḥ śāsanatā'pi ca - śāsitṛtvarūpatā'pi uktā pratipāditā vartate ||40||

caturthapañcamābhyāṃ tu svarūpeṇa guṇairapi |

upāsyatārthamātmatvamityātmatvaprayojanam ||42||
caturtha pañcamābhyāṃ tu caturtha pañcamābhyāmadhikaraṇābhyāṃ tāvat, svarūpeṇa guṇairapi — śuddha brahmasvarūpeṇa, tathā tadīyakalyāṇaguṇadvārā'pi ca, upāsyatārtham – dhyeyatvasiddhyartham, ātmatvam — paramātmanaḥ sarvatrāntarvyāpya vartamānatvam iti ātmatvaprayojanam — evaṃ paramātma- gatasyāSStmatvasya phalam nirūpitaṃ bhavati ||41||

heye'pi tattatsambandhe naiveśatvājjugupsate |
iti nyāyena ṣaṣṭhena proktamātmatvasiddhaye ||42||

tattatsambandhe - paramātmanaḥ devādi vividharūpāpanna cetanā cetanātmaka jagatsambandhe, heye'pi - jugupsite satyapi, īśatvāt - paramātmanaḥ sarveśvaratvāt naiva jugupsate - jugupsāṃ naiva prāpnoti paramātmā, iti — evaṃrūpaṃ pratipādyam, ātmatvasiddhaye - paramātmani uktasyātmasya upapatti - -lābhāya, ṣaṣṭhena nyāyena – ṣaṣṭhādhikaraṇena, proktam – sphuṭīkṛtam ||42||

madhye prasaṅgāt paṣṭhasya deva-deva' viśeṣayo': | vidyādhikāraḥ śūdrāṇāṃ tadabhāvaśca sādhitaḥ |
saptamādīśitṛtvaṃ tu nirvoddhṛtvāt sthirīkṛtam || 43 ||

ṣaṣṭhasya prasaṅgāt - paṣṭhādhikaraṇakṛtāt prasaṅgarūpāt sambandhāt, deva-devaviśeṣayoḥ devatānāṃ sāmānyataḥ devāvāntarabhedasya ca vidyādhikāraḥ sādhitaḥ —arthitvasāmarthyādi- nibandhanaḥ vidyāpravṛttiyogyatārūpo'dhikāraḥ ucitatayā jñāpitaḥ | śūdrāṇām – traivarṇiketa- reṣām, tadabhāvaśva – adhikārābhāvo'pi sādhitaḥ | saptamāt – saptamādadhikaraṇāt, nirvo- ḍhatvāt–nāmarūpanirvāhakatvaṃ hetukṛtya, īśitṛtvam - paramātmanaḥ sarvaniyantṛtvam, sthirīkṛtam asandigdhatāmāpāditam || 43 ||
evametaiḥ tribhiḥ pādaiḥ kāraṇaṃ brahma sādhitam |
caturthāt tacca hetutvaṃ tasyaiveti prasādhyate || 44 ||

evam uktayā rītyā tribhiḥ pādaiḥ prathamadvitīyatṛtīyaiḥ pādaiḥ, brahmakāraṇaṃ sādhitam–brahma jagatkāraṇamiti samarthitam | caturthāt - turīyāt pādāt, tacca hetutvam- uktaṃ jagatkāraṇatvam, tasyaiveti – brahmaṇa eva ; nānyasyeti, prasādhyate – samyak nyāyataḥ sthirīkriyate || 44 ||

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ

viśvātmā'nantabhūmā niyamanadhṛtikṛnmuktabhogyasvabhāvaḥ dahrasvādhārasava hṛdayaparimitāvasthayā sarvayantā | devādīnāmupāsyo vasumukhavibudhaiḥ svātmabhāvena sevyaḥ

śūdrādyopāstyanahaḥ prabhurihabubudhe nāmarūpaikakartā || 32 || viśvātmā—pṛthivyantarikṣadyulokātmakasya jagataḥ ātmatvenādhārabhūtaḥ, anantabhūmā- niravadhikavipulaiśvaryasukhātmakaḥ, niyamana - dhṛtikṛt — niyamanadvārā sarvakāryādhārabhūtaḥ, mukta- bhogyasvabhāvaḥ – saṃsārabandhādvimuktānāṃ 'so'śnute sarvān kāmān saha brahmaṇā vipaścitā' ityuktarītyā bhogyataikasvabhāvaḥ, dahrasvādhārasarvaḥ - daharaśabdavācyopāsanārthalpaparimāṇa- svādhāra kasarvaprapañcaḥ, hṛdayaparimitāvasthayā sarvayantā – hṛdayā khyālpasthāna parimitāṅguṣṭhamātrā- ntaryāmitvāvasthāmāśritya nikhilajaganniyamanakartā, devādīnāmupāsyaḥ – devāsurarakṣogandharvakinna-

rādīnāṃ devayonisambhavānāmapi upāsanayogyaḥ, vasumukhavibudhaiḥ svātmabhāvena sevyaḥ - prāptavasvādipadairdevaviśeṣairapi svaśarīritayā upāsyatāvān śūdrādyopāstyanaha: traivarṇiketaraiḥ svādhyāyānadhikṛtairjanaiḥ śrutyuktayā rītyā upāsitumaśakyaḥ, nāmarūpaikakartā-

- sṛṣṭasya cida- cidātmakasya jagataḥ pradhāno nāmarūpapradaḥ prabhuḥ sarveśvaraḥ uttamaḥ puruṣaḥ, iha - tṛtīye'smin pāde, bubudhe yathāyogyaṃ bodhaviṣayīkṛtaḥ || 32 ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā

e macatuṣpeṭiko'yaṃ daśādhikaraṇātmakaḥ | adhyāye prathame pādastātayīkaḥ susaṅgataḥ || 59 ||
|| iti prathame'dhyāye tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ ||

śarīrameva tu śarīrākhyaṃ niyantavyaṃ vastveva, avyaktam — etacchrati paṭhitāvyaktapadavācyam | puruṣaḥ- sarvasmātparatvena śrutyā pratipāditaḥ puruṣapadārthaḥ, so'yaṃ paraḥ pūruṣaḥ sarveṣu vedānteṣu parātparatastvatvena gīyamānaḥ paramātmaiva na tu sāṅkhyoktaḥ pañcaviṃśako jīvaḥ || 45 ||

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ

nirṇītaṃ vākyajātaṃ paraviṣayatayā spaṣṭajīvādiliṅgaṃ tattacchāyānusāri prathayati tu vacastatparaṃ turthapāde | pabhirdvābhyāṃ ca tatra praśamayati nayaiḥ sāṅkhyayogotthaśaṅkāṃ ghāghaṭṭa itthaṃ kathitanigamanaṃ tvaṣṭamaṃ kecidūcuḥ || 1 ||

spaṣṭa jīva | diliṅgam – sphuṭatayā jñāyamānajīvādyasādhāraṇadharmapratipādakam jātam — śrutivākyavṛndam, paraviṣayatayā nirṇītam - paramātmatātparyakamiti vicārapūrvakaṃ

vākya-

caturthe tu pāde tattacchāyānusāri - sāṅkhyādi-

tṛtīye pāde niścitam | turya pāde tu
tantra gatavākya sadṛśārthakatvena bhāsamānam

vacaḥ – vedāntavākyam, tatparam — paramapuruṣaprati - pādanābhiprāyakamiti, prathayati – nyāyapūrvakaṃ prakaṭayati sūtrakāraḥ | tatra – caturthapādagatādhi- karaṇastomamadhye, par̤abhiḥ - prāthamikaiḥ ānumānika - camasa-saṅkhyopasaṅgraha - kāraṇatva- jagadvācitva- vākyānvayanāmakaiḥ ṣaḍadhikaraṇaiḥ, sāṅkhyotthaśaṅkām – sāṃkhyadarśanādhyayanāhitāṃ bhrāntim, dvābhyāṃ ca tata uparitanābhyāṃ prakṛti-sarvavyākhyānanā makābhyāmadhikaraṇābhyām, yogottha- śaṅkām- -yogadarśanābhyāsāhitāṃ bhramātmikāṃ matiṃ ca praśamayati — dūrīkṛtya, vedāntamadhīya- mānāyāṃ matau purupaṃ sthāpayati | ittham - anayā rītyā ghaṭṭau - dve peṭike, jāghaṭṭaḥ – samyak saṅgatau bhavataḥ | kecit — anye kecana ācāryāstu, aṣṭamam — antimaṃ sarvavyākhyānādhi-

karaṇam, kathita nigamanam — pratipāditānāṃ samanvayādhyāyīyānāṃ sarveṣāṃ nyāyānām upasaṃhāra- paryavasitamiti, ūcuḥ pratipādayāmāsuḥ || 1 ||
dvābhyāṃ kṣepyaṃ pradhānaṃ kapilamatamatha tvekato'nyoktasaṅkhyā turyeṇā'vyākṛtokterapi vibhuravadhiḥ sthāpyate dvāravṛttyā | śuddhāśuddhau ca jīvāvadhikaraṇayuge'nantaraṃ vāraṇīyau
śeṣaṃ tantrāntarokteśvara nirasanakṛtturthapādāṣṭake'smin || 2 ||
asmin — vivarītuṃ prakrānte, turyapādāṣṭake – caturthapādīye

aṣṭādhikaraṇasamūhe,

dvābhyām – ādimābhyāmānumānika - camasanāmabhyām adhikaraṇābhyām, kapilamatam — kapila-

nāmno maharṣerabhimatam, pradhānam — mūlaprakṛtyavaraparyāyam abrahmātmakaṃ pradhānam, kṣepyam- śrutigatāvyaktapadābhidheyatvābhāvavattayā (tathā) 'ajā' padāvācyatayā ca nyāyato nirṇeyam | atha ekatastu – tatpaścāttanāt saṅkhyopasaṅgrahanāmna ekasmādadhikaraṇāttu, anyoktasaṅkhyā kṣepyā— kāpilābhimatā pañcaviṃśatimātraviśrāntā tattvānāṃ gaṇanā, yuktyā dūrīkaraṇīyā, turyeṇa caturthena kāraṇatvādhikaraṇena, avyākṛtokterapi – 'avyākṛta' śabdasyāpi śrutigatasya, dvāravṛtyā — prakṛtyātmaka brahmaviśeṣaṇābhidhānamukhena, vibhuḥ - nikhilavyāptimān paraḥ puruṣaḥ, avadhiḥ- śabdaśaktiparyavasānabhūmitvena, sthāpyate - yuktyā dṛḍhīkriyate | tadanantaramadhikaraṇa- yuge - tatpaścāttanādhikaraṇadvaye jagadvācitva - vākyānvayanāmake śuddhāśuddhau ca jīvau- karmabandhavinirmuktaḥ karmabandhavāṃśceti dvividhāvapi cetanau, vāraṇīyau – (yathāyogyaṃ ) paratattvatā - śaṅkātaḥ dūrīkaraṇīyau | śeṣam – avaśiṣṭaṃ sarvavyākhyānādhikaraṇam, tantrāntarokteśvaranirasana- kṛt — patañjalayogadarśana siddhasya īśvarasvarūpasya śrutyanaminatatva nirṇāyakam || 2 ||

|

akṣādya vyakta neṣṭhaṃ jaḍamatha puruṣaṃ tacakāṣṭhāṃ vivicya
brūte vallī kaṭhānāṃ paramatapaṭhitāṃ prakriyāmityayuktam ! 'tatratyāneka' vākyoditavividhavaśī kārya mukhyakramokteḥ
śāntātmā viṣṇuruktaḥ puruṣa iha paraḥ pratyabhijñāpyate ca || 3 ||

kaṭhānāṃ vallī – kaṭhanāmnāṃ yājuṣāṇāmadhyeyā vallīpadavācyo bhāgaviśeṣaḥ, akṣādi- akṣāparaparyāya|ṇi indriyāṇyupakramya, avyaktaniṣṭham – avyaktāparaparyāya pradhānaparyantam, jaḍam - cetanetarat taccavargam, atha — tadanantaram, tattvakāṣṭhām tattvānāṃ paramasīmābhūtam, puruṣam - puruṣapadābhidheyaṃ tattvam, vivicya — vibhajya darśayitvā paramatapaṭhitām – sāṅkhyamatābhya- stam, prakriyām — tattvasaṅkhyānapaddhatim brūte iti varṇayatītyevamāśaṅkanam, ayuktam —

saṅgataṃ na bhavati | tatratyāneka mokteḥ kaṭhavallīgatapūrvottara vividhavā kyā bhihitanānāvidha-- svāyattīkaraṇīyapradhānabhūtārthagatapūrvāparībhāvasyaiva pratipāditatvāt iha — kaṭhavallīgate vākye, uktaḥ paraḥ puruṣaḥ – 'paraḥ puruṣaḥ' ityanenābhihito'rthaḥ, śāntātmā viṣṇuḥ svayaṃ śāntatvena, ātmanāṃ śāntisādhanatvena ca uktaḥ viṣṇunāmā bhagavāneva ; na kevalaścetanaḥ, pratyabhijñāpyate ca - śrutyā ca 'sa evā'yam' iti pratyabhijñāviṣayī kriyate || 3 ||

...

na hyarthā indriyāṇāṃ prakṛtiratha mano hetureṣāṃ na ceṣṭaṃ buddhicaitanna sūte na ca mahati mahāñjāyate buddhisaṃjñaḥ | bhokturyuktaṃ mahattvaṃ mahati 'ca' na bhavedātmatā pāriśeṣyāt
avyaktoktiḥ śarīre tadiha na kapilaprakriyā pratyabhijñā || 4 ||

arthāḥ –indriyagocarāḥ śabdādayaḥ, indriyāṇām – śabdādigrāhakāṇāṃ śrotrādīnām, na hi prakṛtiḥ - (sāṅkhyamate) kāraṇabhūtā iti na khalvabhyupagatam | manaḥ— antaḥkaraṇam eṣām – indriyāṇām, arthānāṃ ca hetuḥ na ca iṣṭam – kāraṇatvena naivābhimatam | buddhiśca buddhiśabdenā'bhihito'rthastu, etat – manorūpaṃ vastu, na sūte naiva janayati, mahati - mahacchabdabodhye'rthe, buddhisaṃjñaḥ mahān – buddhyaparaparyāyaḥ mahadrūpo'rthaḥ na ca jāyate— naivotpadyate khalu ! bhoktuḥ mahatvaṃ yuktam - acetanāni tattvāni svabhogārthaṃ svīkurvato jīvasya tadapekṣayā mahatvamityetat lokadṛṣṭaṃ saṅgataṃ bhavati | mahati ca na bhavedātmatā — jaḍe mahattatve'pi ātmapadabodhyatā naiva saṅgacchate | pāriśeṣyāt – 'ātmānaṃ rathina' miti mantropātteṣu madhye itarāṇi sarvāṇi 'indriyebhyaḥ parā hyarthā:' iti mantre nirddiśya avaśiṣṭatvāt, avyaktoktiḥ - 'mahataḥ paramavyaktam' iti 'avyakta' śabdaḥ, śarīre - 'śarīraṃ rathameva tu' iti rathatvena rūpite sādhakamanuṣyīye sādhanārthamatyantaṃ vaśīkārye dehe eva bodhakatayā vartate | tat - uktādasmāddhetoḥ iha 'indriyebhyaḥ parā hyarthā:' ityasmin mantre, na kapila: .... pratyabhijñā – 'kapilatantro - pāttārthā eveme' iti cintanena taduktasṛṣṭyādiprakriyā pratisandhānaṃ, naiva saṅgataṃ bhavati || 4 ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā
ādyādhyāye turthapāde nītyaṣṭakama sūtrayat |
tatra pabhirnayaiḥ sāṅkhyān dvābhyāṃ yaugānaśīśamat || 60 || muktavailakṣaṇyamuktaṃ prāk pādānte na yujyate | parasyaiva pratikṣepa dākṣepo hyānumānike ||| 61 || || iti ānumānikādhikaraṇam || 1 ||
camasādhikaraṇam || 1-4-2 || śārīrakanyāyakalāpa saṅgrahaḥ
ajāmityetasmin vacasi gaditaṃ tantragaditaṃ pradhānaṃ svātantryāt sakalajagatīsargakaraṇāt | idaṃ naivaitasmin khalu sṛjatimātraṃ tvabhihitaṃ
'viśeṣasyābhāvād' vadati ca viśeṣaṃ śrutirataḥ || 46 ||

'ajām' ityetasmin vacasi – 'ajāmekām' iti taittirīya- śvetāśvatarobhayagate kiñcidvayatyastasamānānupūrvīke vākye, gaditam – 'ajā' śabdena pratipāditaṃ vastu, tantragaditaṃ pradhānam – sāṅkhyatantroktaṃ pradhānāparaparyāyaṃ mūlaprakṛtirūpaṃ vastu, svātantryāt - svādhīnatā- māśritya sakalajagatīsargakaraṇāt – nikhila viśvotpādanasya 'bahrīṃ prajāṃ janayantīm' 'bahvīḥ prajāḥ sṛjamānām' ityevaṃvidhayā bodhanāta idaṃ naiva-uktaṃ śaṅkāvidhānaṃ sarvathā na saṅgatam | etasmin khalu - uktavākye hi, abhihitam - pratipādya- mānam, sṛjatimātraṃ tu – kevalaṃsargakaraṇameva ; na tu svāyattatvam, viśeṣasyābhāvāt – svātantryādibodhakāṃśasya śrutau śravaṇābhāvāt, ataḥ śrutiḥ - etanmantrānantaramantre 'hapsa : śuciṣat' 'dvā suparṇā' ityevamupaniṣadvāṇī, viśeṣam – sarvasya cetanācetanasya paramātmāyattatvaṃ paramātmavibhūtitvamityādi, vadati ca - spaṣṭīkarotyeva || 46 ||

kvā'pi aupaniṣada sthalaviśeṣagate, vākye - 'ajāmekāṃ lohitaśuklakṛṣṇām' iti vākye, ajāyāḥ – 'ajā' padābhidheyāyāḥ, nikhilajanakatā – sarvasraṣṭṛtā, hi – yataḥ, svātantryeṇa – anyānadhīna kartṛtvātmiketi sūcyate- 'bahrīṃ prajāṃ janayantīm' (tathā) 'bahvīḥ prajāḥ sṛjamānām' iti itarādhīnatvabodha śabdā sahakṛtābhyāṃ śatṛśānajbhyāṃ pratyayābhyāṃ bodhyate ! baddhaḥ ajaḥ - karmavaśyo nityaḥ jīvaḥ, tatra śete-ajāpadabodhye'rthe, (māturake vatsa iva prītyā nirbharaṃ) supto bhavati | anyaḥ ajaḥ tadvilakṣaṇo nityaḥ kaścana jīvastu, bhuktabhogām–svenāsvāditabhogyāṃśavatīm, imāṃ punaḥ – ajāpadābhidheyāṃ svena prītyā āśritāṃ prakṛtimeva, tyajati — jahāti, dūrīkaroti, ityukteḥ – evaṃ varṇanāt, iyam - ajāpadavācyā, tāntrikī sā tu – sāṅkhyatantrapratipāditā mūlaprakṛtireva ; nā'tra sandehaḥ, iti yadi - evamāśaṅkanaṃ cet; na–nocitam ajātvamātrābhidhānāt – parāyattatvamabādhitvā utpatti- rāhityasya kevalaṃ pratipādanāt, asvātantryaprasiddheḥ – acetanabhūteṣu vastuṣu itarānadhīna- svapravṛttyabhāvasya lokaparidṛṣṭatvena tatpṛthagukteranapekṣaṇāta, sṛjatirapi sṛjadhātoḥ prayogo'pi, parapreryatām — kantarādhīna kartṛtārūpaṃ prayojyakartṛtvaṃ karaṇatvaṃ vā pramāṇāntarapratipannam, na uparundhyāt – naiva bādhituṃ śaknuyāt | ( ataḥ brahmātmikā prakṛtireva brahmasaṅkalpādhīna- kriyāvatī sṛṣṭerhetuḥ) || 5 ||

|

adhikaraṇārtha saṅgrahaḥ

camasādhikaraṇe- 'ajāmekām' ityādivākye, 'prajāḥ sṛjamānām' iti, svātantryeṇa sraṣṭṛtvādipratīteḥ, 'ajā' śabdena abrahmātmikā prakṛtirabhidhīyate ityāśaṅkaya,

abrahmātmakā jāgrahaṇe viśeṣahetvabhāvāt, 'aṇoraṇīyān' ityādiśākhāntaravākyena ajāyāḥ brahmātmakatvāvagamāt brahmātmikā prakṛtireva 'ajā' śabdenocyate ityuktam ||

saṅkhyopasaṅgrādhikaraṇam ||1-4-3 ||
śārīrakanyāyakalāpasaṅgrahaḥ
yasmin pañcajanāśca pañca gaganaṃ cā'pīti vākye sphuṭaṃ
saṅkhyāsaṅgrahaṇāttu tantragaṇanetyetanna hi prāṇiti | brahmādhāratayā sthiteratiśayasphūrteśca saṃjñātvataḥ
prāṇādīndriyapañcakasya ca yathā sapteti saptarṣayaḥ ||47 ||

yasmin .... vākye – ' yasmin pañca pañcajanā ākāśaśca pratiṣṭhitaḥ' ityasmin bṛhadāraṇyakagate vākye, sphuṭaṃ saṅkhyā saṅgrahaṇāt – pañca pañca' ti śabdena apandigdhatayā, pañcaguṇitapañcasakhyātmikāyāḥ pañcaviṃśati saṃkhyāyāḥ bodhanāt, tantragaṇanā tu - bṛhadāraṇyakaśrutiḥ kāpilābhimatatattvasaṃkhyānasaraṇireva ; nānyā, ityetat – īdṛśaṃ śaṅkanam, na hi prāṇiti- naiva labdhātmakaṃ bhavati ; prāṇādīndriyapañcakasya - prāṇatvopalakṣitavāyvāpyāyitatvagindriya- prabhṛtīndriyapañcakasya brahmādhāratayā sthiteḥ – 'yasmin pratiṣṭhitaḥ' iti yacchabdabodhyaṃ brahma ādhāratayā āśritya supratiṣṭhitatva varṇanāt, atiśayasphūrteśca – 'jyotiṣāṃ jyotiḥ ' iti pūrvamantrasiddhasphūrti (prakāśa) majjyotirantararātiśayāvadhibhūtajyotirūpatvācca, saṃjñātvataḥ - ‘pañcajana'padasya pañcendriyeṣu rūḍhyā, yogarūḍhyā vā bodhakatayā vṛtterapi hetoḥ | 'pañcajanāḥ' iti vaktavye, pañca pañcaceti katham ? iti tu na śaṅkayam ;] yathā saptarṣayaḥ sapteti - 'saptarṣayaḥ' iti pade saptapadasya satre'pi punaḥ saptapadaṃ, saptarṣisaṃjñiteṣvartheṣu saptatvasaṅkhyā- bodhanārtham, tadvat pañcajanasaṃjñitevidriyeṣu pañcasaṅkhyatvabodhanārthaṃ pañcapadamiti ||47||

yasmin paśceti mantre – ' yasmin pañca pañcajanā ākāśaśca pratiṣṭhitaḥ' ityasmin mantre, paraparigaṇitā - sāṅkhyābhyupagatā, pañcayuktā viṃśatiḥ - ādhikyena pañcasaṃkhyāsahitā viṃśatyātmikā saṃkhyā prakṛtyā dipañcaviṃśatitatvagatā, proktā - asandigdhamuktā, iha–asmin vākye. saptamyadhītastu – 'yasmin' iti saptamīvibhaktyantena yacchabdena śrutibodhyo'rthastu, ananya niṣṭhaḥ –aupaniṣadairiva paramātmādhīnatayā anabhyupagataḥ, puruṣagaṇaḥ astu – sāṃkhyaiḥ puruṣasaṃjñāsaṃjñitajīvasamūha eva bhavatu, (iti śaṅkite ) maivam — evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ niṣedhyameva, ākāśasya—–ākāśākhyasya sāṃkhya saṃgatapañcaviṃśatyantarbhūtasyaiva tattvasya svanāmnā 'ākāśa'- śabdena pṛthaganukathanāt – pañcaviṃśatisaṅkhyā vyatirekeṇa kathitatvāt, (tathā) saptamī- śaktyabādhāt – 'yasmin' iti padagatāyāḥ sarvādhāratvabodhakatā rūpāyāḥ saptamī vibhaktiśakteḥ abādhamanurudhyā'pi, atra—prastute vākye, sarvāśraya iti viditaḥ sarvāśrayatvena śrutyantare prasiddhaḥ, brahmatādyaiḥ – nirupādhikabrahmatvādibhiḥ dharmairviśiṣṭaḥ, ṣaḍviṃśo hi pañcaviṃśakātiriktaḥ paramapuruṣa eva, anūdyate – pūrvaṃ jñāta eva anuvādalakṣaṇena yacchabdena dhārakatayā śrutyā jñāpyate || 6 ||

saṃjñopādhiḥ samāso'hyayamiha nigame sapta saptarṣinītyā

prāṇādyaṃ tanmano'ntaṃ prakaraṇa niyataṃ pañcakaṃ dhīndriyākhyam | jyotiḥśabdena śākhāntaraviditamidaṃ nyūnavādastu pūryo
ghrāṇaṃ vaktayannaśabdo rasanamapi saha prāṇaśabdastvagarthaḥ || 7 ||

iha nigame — upātte'smin ' yasmin pañca pañcajanā:' iti śrutivākye śrūyamāṇaḥ, ayaṃ samāsaḥ - 'pañcajana' śabdātmakaḥ samāsaḥ, sapta saptarṣinītyā- 'saptarṣayaḥ sapta' iti prayoga sādṛśyena, saṃjñopādhiḥ saṃjñāviśeṣapravṛttinimittakaḥ, samudāyaśaktyā arthaviśeṣabodhakaḥ, 'diksaṅkhye saṃjñāyām' iti ; na tu 'pañcānāṃ janānāṃ samāhāraḥ' iti samāhāradvandvaḥ | tat – 'pañcajanāḥ pañca' ityanena bodhyam, prakaraṇaniyatam - asmin prakaraṇe anyūnānatiriktayā sphuṭapratipannam prāṇādyaṃ mano'ntam - prāṇa cakṣuḥ śrotra - anna manorūpam, dhīndriyākhyam – jñānendriyarūpaṃ, pañcakam – pañca saṅkhyā viśiṣṭaṃ vastujātam | idam-uktaṃ jñānendriyapañcakam, 'jyotiḥ ' śabdena – 'taṃ devā jyotiṣāṃ jyotiḥ' iti parajyotirgatāti- śayāvadhibhūtajyotirvācakena 'jyotiṣām' iti śabdena, śākhāntaraviditam — kāṇvaśākhāyāṃ jñātam, nyūnavādastu – 'anna' śabdarahita: 'prāṇasya prāṇamuta cakṣuṣaścakṣuruta śrotrasya śrotraṃ manaso ye mano viduḥ' iti pāṭhastu, pūrya : 'anuktamanyato grāhyam' iti

nyāyāt, āvaśyakādhikāṃśayojanena anusandheyaḥ, annaśabdaḥ – 'annasyānnam' iti vākya- stho'nnaśabda:, ghrāṇaṃ rasanamapi prāṇa- rasanākhyendriyadvayamapi, saha vakti — ekayaiva vṛttyā bodhayati, prāṇaśabdaḥ tvagarthaḥ ' prāṇasya prāṇam' ityuktaḥ prāṇaśabdaḥ, prāṇatvopalakṣitavāyvā- pyāyitatvena hetunā tvagindriyabodhakaḥ || 7 ||

adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ

saṃkhyopasaṅgrahādhikaraṇe—'yasmin pañca pañcajanā :' iti vākye, 'pañca'śabdaviśeṣitāt 'pañcajana' śabdāt pañcaviṃśatitattvapratīteḥ, idaṃ vākyaṃ tantrasiddha tattvapratipādakam – ityāśaṅkya, 'yasmin... pratiṣṭhitaḥ' iti yacchabdanirdiṣṭabrahmāśrayatayā avagatānāṃ pañcajanānāṃ, tantrasiddhebhyaḥ tattvebhyaḥ pṛthagbhāvāt, na idaṃ tantrasiddha tattvapratipādakam — ityuktam ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā
vākyāntarasya cā'bhāvāt tattvasaṅkhyāvirodhataḥ | saṅkhyopasaṅgrahanaye śaṅkātaḥ pratyudāhṛtiḥ || 63 || || iti saṅkhyopasaṅgrahādhikaraṇam || 3 ||

ca keṣucit 'brahma' iti, evam uktayā rītyā hetuvacasām — kāraṇavākyānām, vividha- vacanāt — kāraṇabodhakavividhānupūrvīkaśabdaghaṭitatvāt, kvāpi kasminnapyarthe, na nirṇītiḥ- 'idameva kāraṇaṃ nānyat' ityevaṃ nirṇayo naiva śakyaḥ iti – evamāśaṅkanam, na khalu - naiva saṅgataṃ bhavitumarhati ; yataḥ yasmāddhetoḥ, sarvatra —- sadasadādivividhaśabdaghaṭiteṣu kāraṇa- vākyeṣvapi, liṅga - prakaraṇa- guṇādyaiḥ - paratvavyāptaiḥ dharmaiḥ paramātmasambandhinā prakaraṇena, (tathā) paramātmamātravṛttitayā śrutyantarāvagataiḥ guṇaiścaṃ jñāpakībhūtaiḥ paraḥ samākṛṣṭa iti paramātmaiva anuvartya viśeṣyatayā anvita iti niścīyate || 48||

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ viśvopādānavaktrī śrutiṣu sadasadavyākṛtoktiḥ parokte hyavyakte'nveti tasmāttaditaradakhilaṃ neyamatreti cenna | yatrāssaccādi dṛṣṭaṃ prakaraṇaviditaṃ tatra sarvajñatādyaṃ

liṅgaṃ 'syāmityavītaṃ sthiramapi tadihābādhya ātmādiśabdaḥ || 8 ||

śrutiṣu vividhāsu śrutiṣu vartamānā, viśvopādānavaktrī jagadupādānakāraṇabhūtārtha- bodhikā, sadasadavyākṛtoktiḥ – ' sadeva somye' tyatra saduktiḥ, 'asadvā idam' ityādau asaduktiḥ, 'tadbhedaṃ tavyākṛtamāsīt' ityādau avyākṛtoktiśca parokte – sāṅkhyaiḥ pratipādite, avyakte hi anveti –pradhāne khalu saṅgacchate tasmāt — evaṃ niścitatvāt taditaradakhilam—uktebhyo bhinnam ātmādivacaḥ sarvam, atra – pradhāne eva, neyam – gauṇa vṛttimāśritya yojanīyam iti cet — evaṃ śaṅkanam, na na sādhu bhavati, yatra – yādṛśe śrutivākye, asaccādi dṛṣṭam — jagatkāraṇe asatvam, satvam, avyākṛtatvaṃ vā avagatam, tatra — tādṛśe sthale, prakaraṇaviditam - viṣayaprakramādavagatam, sarvajñatādyam - acetanatvā samānādhi- karaṇaṃ 'vipaścit 'padāvagataṃ sarvajñatvādikam syāmityadhītam – 'bahu syām' iti śrutipaṭhitaṃ cetanāsādhāraṇaṃ saṅkalpātmakam liṅgamapi - asadādiśabdābhidheyasya paramātmajñāpakamapi, sthiram - — anyathā yojayitumaśakyatvāt ananyathāsiddham, tat — uktebhyo hetubhyaḥ, iha- jagatkāraṇabhūtārthabodhake vākye, ātmādiśabdaḥ - ātma- puruṣa - nārāyaṇādyātmakaḥ śabdaḥ, abādhya - mukhyavṛttyaiva yojanīyaḥ || 8 ||

āsīdagre tvasadvā idamiti vilayāvasthatāmātra muktaṃ naivāsīt 'kiñcidi' tyādyapi vilayaparaṃ śūnyatāderniṣedhāt | sarvasyā'tryākṛtatvaṃ vibhajanavirahāt tādṛśāvasthatattad-
dravyasto māntarātmā tadiha sadasadavyākṛtādyuktivācyaḥ || 9 ||

āsīdagre .... idamiti - 'asadvā idamagra āsīt' iti śrutivākyena vilayāvasthatā - mātram – pralayākhyāvasthāviśeṣāpannatvameva kevalam uktam – (asatpada bodhyatāprayojakaṃ ) varṇitam naivāsīt... dyapi – ' naiveha kiñcanāgra āsīt amūlamanādhāram' ityādirūpaṃ saubālaṃ vākyamapi vilayaparam – jaganaḥ pralayāvasthāvasthitatātparyakameva, śūnyatādeḥ- bauddhābhyupagatasya sarvaśūnyatvasya, vaiśeṣikādyabhyupagatasya niranvayavināśasya vā niṣedhāt — layaśrutibhiḥ ayuktatvakhyāpanāt, sarvasya avyākṛtatvam – avyākṛtapadābhidheyatvamapi, vibhajanavirahāt – nāmarūpavibhāgasya sṛṣṭeḥ prākkāle abhāvāyattāt, tat — uktāt kāraṇāt, iha — śrutigateṣu vividheṣu kāraṇavākyeṣu sadasadavyākṛtādyuktivācyaḥ – 'sat, asat, avyākṛtam' ityādivāgbhiḥ pratipādyaḥ arthaḥ, tādṛśāvastha....rātmā - sukṣma-sthūla avibhakta- nāmarūpatvādyatrasthāvasthitacetanācetanātmakadravya viśeṣaniyantṛtvavān paramātmaiva || 9||

.

jagadbācitvādhikaraṇam || 1-4-5 || śārīrakanyāya kalā pasaṅgrahaḥ
yaḥ kartā yasya karmetyabhihitapuruṣo jīva evātra vṛttaḥ kartṛtvāt karmavactvāt sahajanijaguṇairbhoktṛbhāvapratīteḥ | ityevaṃ naiva śaṅkathaṃ jagadiha vacane karmaśabdābhidheyaṃ
dṛṣṭyartha prāṇa 'jīva' grahaṇamiha suṣutyāśrayo brahma yasmāt || 49 ||

yaḥ kartā .... puruṣaḥ 'yo vai bālāka eteṣāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ kartā, yasya vā etat karma sa veditavyaḥ' iti kauṣītakyāṃ vedyatayoktaḥ puruṣaḥ, atra vṛtto jīva eva – sāṅkhyatantropāttaḥ pañcaviṃśakaḥ puruṣa eva ; na tu ṣaḍaviṃśaḥ aupaniṣadoktaḥ | kartṛtvāt – prakṛtisaṃsargakṛtasya kartṛtvasya jīve eva sambhavāt karmavattvāt puṇya-pāpātmakakarmaṇāmapi jīvamātragatatvāt, sahajanijaguṇaiḥ–svābhāvikaiḥ svaniṣṭhairguṇaiḥ, bhoktṛbhāvapratīteḥ sukhaduḥkhādibhoktṛtāyāḥ jīve eva sarvānubhavasiddhatayā svabhogopayogi jagatsraṣṭṛtāyāḥ tasminneva susaṅgatatvāt | ityevaṃ naiva śaṅkayam—uktaprakāreṇa śaṅkanaṃ yuktaṃ na bhavati, iha vacane - pūrvokte kauṣītakī vākye, karma- śabdābhidheyam – 'kriyate iti karma' ityevam avayavavyutpannena karmapadena pratipādyaṃ vastu, jagat — sṛjyamānaṃ viśvameva ; na tu jīvīyaṃ puṇyapāpātmakaṃ karma, iha – asmin prakaraṇe, prāṇajīvagrahaṇam – 'athāsmin prāṇa evaikadhā bhavati' iti prāṇasya, 'evamevaiṣa prajñātmā etaiḥ svātmabhirbhuṅkte' iti jīvasya ca upādānam dṛṣṭyartham – prakṛtopāsanasampantyartham, (upāsyo- pāsakayoḥ strarūpasya itaraviviktatayā paricayārtham ) yasmāt – yato hetoḥ, suṣutyāśrayaḥ- suṣuptyavasthāprāptau jīvasya āśrayabhūtaḥ arthaḥ, brahma-prājñākhyaḥ paraṃ brahmaiva || 49 ||

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ
yasyaitatkarma vedyaḥ sa iti vacanataḥ karmavaśyapratīteḥ
kartā puṃsāṃ sa eva svakṛtapariṇaterityupakrāntibhagnam | bālākyajñātatattvāntaramupadiśataḥ syādihājātaśatro-
stajjñātoktirnirarthā jagati kṛtatayā 'karma' śabdo'tra mukhyaḥ || 10 ||
yasyai .... vacanataḥ — kauṣītakyupaniṣadgatāt 'yasya vā etat karma, sa vai veditavyaḥ' ityetasmāt ajātaśatrorvākyāt, karmavaśyapratīteḥ – puṇyapāpātmaka karma paratantrasyaiva arthasya

avagamāt sa eva – tādṛśo jīvātmaiva, svakṛtapariṇateḥ svīya puṇyāpuṇyā khyakarmapariṇāmaṃ dvārīkṛtya, puṃsāṃ kartā-surādicāturvidhyamāpannānām, upāsyatayoktānāmādityādīnāṃ vā cetanānāṃ sraṣṭā syāt, iti — evamāśaṅkanam, upakrāntibhagnam - prakaraṇopakrameṇa pratiruddham | tathā hi, bālākyajñātatattvāntaram - bālāṃki nāmnā gārmyeṇa brāhmaṇena aviditaṃ eteṣā~ nirmātṛ ca anyat tattvam, upadiśataḥ nūtanatayā jñāpayataḥ, ajātaśatroḥ tannāmnaḥ kṣatriyasya, tajjñātoktiḥ—tena bālākinā avagatānāmeva arthānāṃ veditavyatvavarṇanapravṛtti:, iha — eta- prakaraṇasthā, nirarthā syāt – viphalaivāpadyeta | atra 'karma' śabda : - 'yasya vā etat karma' iti vākyagataḥ 'karma' iti śabdaḥ, kṛtatayā - (kṛtikarmatvaṃ) kriyamāṇatvena hetunā ; jagati-- prapañcarūpe'rthe, mukhyaḥ puṣkalapravṛttinimittavāneva ; na tu gauṇaḥ || 10 ||

|

evaṃ jīvātirikte prakaraṇa niyate tatra yajjīvamukhya-

prāṇākhyānaṃ na tena kṣatiriha 'ca' tathā tadvi' śiṣṭe pāsā | prāṇasya prāṇabhājo'pyadhikaraṇatayā vājivākyoktarītyā '
brahmajñaptyai tadanyaprakathanamiti hi ! sthāpanā sārvabhaumī || 11 ||

evam uktāṃ rītimanurudhya, jīvātirikte – jīvāpekṣayā vilakṣaṇe vastuni, prakaraṇa- niyate—arthanirṇāyakena prakaraṇākhyena pramāṇena asandigdhaṃ nirṇīte sati, tatra — jīvātirikta- nirṇāyake prakaraṇe eva, jīva - mukhya prāṇākhyānaṃ yat - 'bṛhan pāṇḍaravāsa:' iti mukhyaprāṇasya, ' sa tata eva samuttasthau ' iti jīvasya ca yat kīrtanamasti, tena prāṇa jīvayoḥ kīrtanena, iha ca na kṣatiḥ prakṛte ca prāṇajīvavilakṣaṇaparamātmaparatvasya naiva bhaṅgaprasaktiḥ | hiyataḥ, tathā - jīva. mukhya prāṇaprakāratayā, tadviśiṣṭe— jīva - mukhya prāṇākhyaviśeṣaṇasahite paramātmani viṣayabhūte, upāsā – upāsanaṃ pravartate | vājivākyoktarītyā - bṛhadāraṇyakagatabālākya- jātaśatru saṃvādagatārthasādṛśyena, antaryāmibrāhmaṇoktanyāyena vā prāṇasya prāṇabhājo'pi - mukhyaprāṇasya, tathā mukhyaprāṇāśrayabhūtasya jīvasyā'pi ca, adhikaraṇatayā - dhārakatva viśiṣṭaveṣeṇa, brahmajñaptyai–brahmopāsanasiddhyartham, tadanyaprakathanam - brahmetarayorjīva - mukhyaprāṇayoḥ kīrtanam, iti sthāpanā hi - evaṃ tātparyavarṇana rītireva khalu sārvabhaumī - abādhitatayā utkṛṣṭā bhavati ||11||

.

adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ

jagadvā citvādhikaraṇe – 'yo vai bālāka eteṣāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ kartā' ityādivākye, 'yasya 'vaitatkarma' iti sambandhaśravaṇāt, veditavyatayoktaṃ na brahma ; kintu jīvaḥ - ityāśaṅkaya,

'kriyate' iti vyutpattyā, 'karma' śabdasya jagadvā citvāt jagatkāraṇabhūtaḥ paramātmaika veditavyatayā upāttaḥ — ityuktam ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā
siddhāntaḥ prāk prakaraṇādīkṣitṛtvā diyogataḥ | jīvaliṅgājjagadvācinayaḥ syādapavādataḥ || 65 ||
|| iti jagadva citvādhikaraṇam || 8 ||

rūpasya jīvaliṅgasya śrutyā pratipādanametat, antaḥ utpādavināśāśraya deha sambandhi jīvāntaryāmi- tvena parasya - paramātmanaḥ, avasthānataḥ – 'ya ātmani tiṣṭhan' ityuktayā rītyā avasthite: satvāt || 50 ||
adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ patyādīnāṃ priyatvaṃ śrutiranuvadati hmātmanaḥ kāmasiddhayai tenā'sau puṇya-pāpoditaphalabhugiti prakramādipratīpam | tattadbhogapradātuḥ prathayati hi vibhoḥ kāmatastatpriyatvaṃ
draṣṭavyaścaiṣa muktyai śrutinikaramitaḥ pratyabhijñāpyate'tra || 12 ||

hi yataḥ, śrutiḥ – bṛhadāraṇyakaśrutiḥ ātmanaḥ – svasya jīvasya, kāmasiddhayai - iṣṭāpūrtaye, patyādīnāṃ priyatvam bhāryādīn sambandhinaḥ prati bhartrādīnāṃ sambandhināṃ prīti- bhāgitvaṃ, sevanīyatvaṃ vā, anuvadati - lokasiddhameva jñāpayati | tena tasmādeva hetoḥ, asau - priyaṃ sevamānaḥ draṣṭavyatayoktaḥ ātmā, puṇyapāpoditaphalabhug - puṇyapāpātmaka karmaprayuktaphalā svādanakartṛ- bhūto jīva ; na paramātmā, iti evaṃ śaṅkanam prakramādipratīpam - upakramāderviruddham | hi-yataḥ, tattadbhogapradātuḥ-taistaiḥ patijāyāputrādibhiḥ nimittabhūtaiḥ cetanānāṃ svasaṅkalpaviśeṣeṇa bhogaviśeṣa- sampādakasya vibhoḥ – paramapuruṣasya, kāmataḥ – viśiṣṭena saṅkalpenaiva, tatpriyatvam - patyādīnāṃ tāṃstānātmanaḥ prati icchayā sevyamānatvam | eṣaḥ – svīyena saṅkalpaviśeṣeṇa tāṃstān priyān kurvāṇaḥ paramātmaiva, muktyai— amṛtatvaprāptimuddiśya, draṣṭavyaḥ śrutinikaramitaḥ - sākṣātkaraṇīya- tayā samastavedāntapratipannaḥ, atra – 'ātmā vā are draṣṭavyaḥ' ityasmin vākye, pratyabhijñāpyate- ' sa evāyam' iti pratisandhīyate || 12 ||

vyutpattyā hyātmaśabdaḥ prathayati paramaṃ brahma yadvā 'samāsāt '
'svārtho'syaṃ jīvaśabdo vadati ca paramaṃ dvāravṛcyeti pakṣāḥ " | vyaktyaikyādāśmarathyo nirupadhikadaśā dvaitata' cauḍa 'lomiḥ tatsthatvāt kāśakṛtsnaḥ 'paraviṣayatayā jīvaśabdaṃ jagāda ||13|| ātmaśabdaḥ – 'ātmā vā are draṣṭavyaḥ' iti vākyastha :
vyutpacyā hi - 'āpnotīti ātmā' iti avayavaśaktyaiva, paramaṃ brahma

ātmetyayaṃ śabdaḥ, cetanā cetanābhyāmati-

śayitaṃ brahmarūpaṃ vastu, prathayati – prakaṭīkaroti | yadvā – athavā, samāsāt – rūḍhipadavācyāṃ samudāyaśaktimāśritya ayam – uktaḥ ātmaśabdaḥ, sārtha:- sarvaśarīriṇā paramātmanā abhi-- dheyavān | yadvā—athavā, jīvaśabdaḥ ayam – acetanetara sāmānyabodhana svarūpayogyatāvānapi prāṇadhāraṇanibandhanatayā bāhulyena jīvātmani prayujyamānaḥ ātmaśabda:, dvāravṛcyā – śarīrāntargata- prāṇākhyavāyudhāraṇasvabhāvaṃ cetanaṃ dvārīkṛtya, paramaṃ ca vadati – (viśeṣaṇatayā jīvaṃ, viśeṣyatayā ) paramātmānaṃ cetyubhayamapyabhidhatte, iti pakṣāḥ - evaṃrītyā trayo'bhiprāyāḥ dṛśyante ; āśmarathyaḥ- etannāmā maharṣiḥ, vyaktyaikyāt - mṛtpiṇḍakāryasyā'pi ghaṭasya mṛtsvarūpeṇa abhedavat, paramātma- kāryasyā'pi jīvasya āttasvarūpeṇa abhedamavalambya jīvaśabdam – jīvabodhanasvābhāvyavattayā - jñātam ātmaśabdam, paraviṣayatayā — paramātmā bhidheyakamiti, jagāda – svāśayaṃ vyaktīkṛtavān | auḍalomiśca – oḍulomināmā ācāryastu, nirupadhikadaśādvaitataḥ avidyopādhikaśarīra- sambandhādyapagamāvasthākṛtajīvabrahmābhedamāśritya, jīvaśabdaṃ paraviṣayatayā jagāda | kāśakṛtsraḥ- etannāmā brahmatatvacintaka ācāryastu tatsthatvāt – 'ya ātmani tiṣṭhan' ityuktarītyā jīvātma- niyantṛtayā taccheṣitayā taddhārakatayā ca paramātmanaḥ tasmin jīve avasthitatvamāśritya, jīvaśabdaṃ paraviṣayatayā jagāda || atratyā vividhāḥ pāṭhāḥ yathāyogyaṃ neyāḥ || 13 ||

bhedopādhivyapāye bhavabhṛdayamiyād brahmatāmityayuktaṃ

nityaṃ tadbhedadṛṣṭeratipatitabhave sāmyasādharmyaśabdāt | mṛttatkāryakramaśca śrutiśatavihatastena jīvoktimīśe

tatsthatvāt kāśakṛtsno yadiha niravahad vyāsasiddhānta eṣaḥ || 14 ||

bhedopādhivyapāye jīvātmaparamātmabhedaprayojakā vidyādidoṣāpagame sati, ayaṃ bhava- bhṛt — paridṛśyamānaśarīritvāśrayo jīvaḥ, brahmatām iyāt – brahmabhāvaṃ prāpnuyāt ityayuktam- īdṛśamauḍulomyācāryamataṃ yuktiviruddham, atipatitabhave saṃsārāduttīrṇaṃ jīvaṃ viṣayīkṛtya, sāmyasādharmyaśabdāt–'nirañjanaḥ paramaṃ sāmyamupaiti' iti, 'mama sādharmyamāgatāḥ' iti ca sāmya-sādharmyaśabdau prayujya nityaṃ tadbhedadṛṣṭeḥ – jīvabrahmaṇoḥ sārvakālikatayā bhedasyaiva śrutismṛtiṣu varṇanadarśanāt, mṛttatkāryakramaśca mṛtpiḍa - tajjanya ghaṭa śarāvādidṛṣṭāntasadṛśa- bhāvaśca, śrutiśatavihataḥ - 'kṣaraṃ pradhānamamṛtākṣaraṃ haraḥ kṣarātmānāvīśate deva ekaḥ' ityādi- jīvaparamātmavailakṣaṇyajñāpakabahuśrutibādhitaḥ | tena tena hetunā, kāśakṛtsnaḥ – kāśakṛtsna- nāmā ācārya:, īśe jīvoktim - īśvare jīvavācakaśabdaprayogam, tatsthatvāt iha

niravahat yat — paramātmano jīvātmanyavasthānaṃ nimittatayā avalambya asmin prakaraṇe samarthita- vāniti yadasti, epaḥ - ayamaṃśaḥ, vyāsa siddhāntaḥ - sūtrakṛto bādarāyaṇasya nirṇīta:- svābhipreto'rtha eva || 14||

adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ

vākyānvayādhikaraṇe 'na vā are patyuḥ kāmāya patiḥ priyo bhavati ; ātmanastu kāmāya patiḥ priyoṃ bhavati' ityādivākye, 'ātmā vā are draṣṭavyaḥ' iti draṣṭavyatayoktaḥ ātmā, tantrasiddhaḥ puruṣaḥ ; tasyaiva ādimadhyāvasāneṣu pratipāditatvāt — ityāśaṅkaya,
'amṛtatvasya tu nā''śā'sti' ityādinā, amṛtatvaprāptihetutayā upāsyatvakāraṇatvādi- pratipādakavākyānāṃ paramātmaparatve eva anvayasāmañjasyāt, draṣṭavyatayā uktaḥ, paramātmā – iti siddhāntitam || 6 ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā

anyathā copapattyākhyanyāyasyātra tvasambhavāt | śaṅkāto vākyanītau syāt pratyudāhṛtisaṅgatiḥ || 66 ||
|| iti vākyānvayādhikaraṇam || 6 ||

paraḥ - paramātmā, na upādānam - upādānakāraṇaṃ na bhavitumahati iti kathayitum — evaṃ vaktum, na śakyam – naḥ sādhyam ; pratijñānāt -- upādānatvāvinābhāvina: kāraṇa vijñānena kāryavijñānasya ‘yasminnu khalu vijñāte sarvamidaṃ vijñātaṃ bhavati' iti śrutau nirṇaya rūpeṇoktatvāt, kiṃ sviditi ca – 'kiṃ svidvanaṃ ka u sa vṛkṣa āsīt, yato dyāvāpṛthivī niṣṭatakṣuḥ ityādibhirvākyaiḥ sākṣāt — paramapramāṇabhūtayā śrutyaiva (ākṣepādikamadvārīkṛtya), ubhaya- kathanāt — paramātmana eva upādānatvasya nimittatvasya ceti dvividhakāraṇatvasyā'pi kaṇṭhataḥ pratipādanāt || 51 ||
adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ mṛtpiṇḍādeḥ kulālaprabhṛtiriha pṛthak tadvadevādikartā nopādānaṃ vikārairvirahata iti na dvāramātre vikārāt | mṛddaṣṭāntādimātrānna ca vikṛtirasau syāt parasya svarūpe dehadvārorṇanābhiprakṛti vikṛtivad vyāpṛterdaśitatvāt ||15||

ihalokaparidṛṣṭe ghaṭādikāryakāraṇakalāpe, mṛtpiṇḍāde:- mṛtpiṇḍādirūpādupādāna- kāraṇāt, kulālaprabhṛtiḥ - nimittakāraṇabhūtaḥ kulālādirarthaḥ, pṛthak — atirikta eva khalu dṛṣṭaḥ | tadvadeva – tatsādṛśyenaiva, ādikartā -- jagadādikāraṇa (nimitta ) bhūto'pyarthaḥ, vikāraiḥ virahataḥ – 'niṣkalaṃ.... nirañjanam' ityādiśrutisiddhāt vikārasāmānya hityāddhetoḥ, na upādānam - pariṇāmapadavācya vikārayuktam upādānakāraṇaṃ naiva bhavitumarhati iti -- evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ naiva saṅgatam | dvāramātre – kāraṇatāṃ prati dvāratvena abhyupagate prakṛtyaṃśe eva, vikārāt — upādānatvaprayojakasya pariṇāmaśabdavācyasya vikārasya abhyupagamāt (dvāragatasya dvāriṇi vyavahāre mukhyatvānapāyāt 'bālo yuvā jāta:' itivat ) mṛddaṣṭāntādi mātrāt- 'yathā somyaikena mṛtpiṇḍena' ityādinā vikāriṇo'rthasya śrutau dṛṣṭāntatvapradarśanamātrāt', asau śrutisiddhā upādānatā, parasya svarūpe - satyajñānādipadavācye avikāriṇi paramātmanaḥ svarūpe, na ca vikṛtiḥ syāt - naiva vikārāśrayatvāpādikā bhavet | ūrṇanābhiprabhṛti- vikṛtivat – ūrṇanābhipadābhidheyalūtākhya krimyādīnāṃ tattvākhyavikāra prāptivat, dehadvārā vyāpṛteḥ– cetanā cetanātmakaṃ svaśarīraṃ dvārīkṛtya paramātmanaḥ svasmin jagadupādānakāraṇatā -- nirvāhasya, darśitatvāt – 'yathorṇanābhiḥ sṛjate gṛhṇate ca' ityādiśrutiṣu spaṣṭī- kṛtatvāt ||15||

svajñānādyaṃ svajanyaṃ bhavati sṛjati ca svānyasaṃyogamīśaḥ saṃyoge mūrtaniṣṭhe prakṛtirapi hi tat syānnimittaṃ kriyātaḥ | ekasyāssaau bahu syāmiti bahubhānaṃ saubharinyāyasiddhuṃ
bhedābhedaśrutīnāmavihatiriha ca syādviśiṣṭaikyayogāt || 16||

svajñānādyam – (vaiśeṣikādīnāṃ mate'pi ) ātmasamavetatayā ātmasamavāyikāraṇakaṃ jñāna- cikīrṣā - prayatnādikam svajanyaṃ bhavati – ( ' jānāti, icchati, karoti' ityādinā kartari lakāraprayogāt ) ātmakartṛkatayā ātmanimittakamapi bhavatyeva | īśaḥ – sarveśvaraḥ, svānya- saṃyogam – ātmanaḥ svetareṇa saha saṃyogākhyaṃ sambandhaviśeṣam, sṛjati ca – utpādayati ca, (svasamavetasaṃyogaṃ prati kartṛtvāt saṃyoganimittakāraṇamapi ) | mūrtaniṣṭhe – paramāṇvādimūrtadravya- dvayasamavete, saṃyoge - saṃyogākhye guṇe jāyamāne, tat — mūrtadravyadvayam prakṛtirapi — samavāyi kāraṇāparaparyāyam upādānakāraṇaṃ sadapi kriyātaḥ saṃyogajanikāṃ svaniṣṭhāṃ kriyāṃ dvārīkṛtya, nimittaṃ syāddhi–nimittakāraṇaṃ bhavedeva khalu ! ādau sṛṣṭeḥ prāk ekasya nānānāmarūpa- virahiṇaḥ, advitīyasya vā sato brahmaṇa: 'bahu syām' iti bahubhavanam - 'bahu syām' iti saṅkalpapūrvakaṃ nānānāmarūpaprāptiyogyatāpādanam, saubharinyāya siddham – saubharināmnaḥ ekasyaiva ṛṣeḥ svasaṅkalpena nānāśarīraparigraharītitaḥ susthameva (pūrvāvasthā viśiṣṭa vaṣeṇopādānatāyāḥ, uttarāvasthā viśiṣṭaveṣeṇopādeyatāyāśca mithaḥ abādhāta ) | iha ca - asmin brahmopādānatā- siddhānte ca viśiṣṭaikyayogāt – avasthādvayaviśiṣṭe dharmiṇi viśeṣyabhūte abhedasyācādhāt, bhedābhedaśrutīnām - 'dvāsuvarṇā' 'kṣarātmānāvīśate deva ekaḥ' ityādīnāṃ cidacidīśvarabheda- pratipādakānāṃ (tathā) 'neha nānā'sti kiñcana' 'tatvamasi' ityādyabhedapratipādakanāṃ ca śrutīnām avihatiḥ syāt – anyonyaviruddhārthapratipādakatvam siddhayedeva | (tatratraye svarūpakṛtasya mitho bhedasya, anuthasiddhayā cetanācetana vaiśiṣṭyakṛtasyābhedasya ca lokaveda- saṃmatattvāt ) || 16 ||
kāryaikye hi pratijñā tadanuguṇa udāhāri dṛṣṭāntavargaḥ
sraṣṭuḥ syāmityabhidhyāṃ śrutiriha vanatāṃ vṛkṣatādiṃ ca vakti | ātmānaṃ caiṣa eva svayamakuruta tadbhūtayonitvamuktaṃ
tasmāt kartā'pi devaḥ prakṛtirapi bhavet sarvatattvāntarātmā || 17 ||
pratijñā' yasmin nu khalu.... bhavati' ityākārikā ekavijñānena sarvavijñānapratijñā,
kāryaikye hi—kāraṇasya kāryeṇa sahābhede siddhe eva khalu ghaṭate; tadanuguṇaḥ -

abhedapratipatteḥ anukūla eva dṛṣṭāntavarga:mṛttatkāryādirūpo nidarśanastomaḥ, udāhāri lokasiddha eva uddhṛtya pradarśitaḥ | iha – kārya-kāraṇayorbhedābhedādivicāre prastute, syām iti śrutiḥ 'bahu syām prajāyeya' itīyaṃ vedavāṇī, sraṣṭuḥ – jagatkarturīśvarasya, abhidhyām- kāryopayogisaṅkalpātmikāṃ nimittatvaprayojikāṃ jñānāvasthāma, vakti-sphuṭaṃ pratipādayati | (tathā) vanatāṃ vṛkṣatādiṃ ca vakti - 'brahma vanaṃ brahma sa vṛkṣa āsīt, 'yato dyāvā - pṛthivī niṣṭatakṣuḥ' iti śrutireva upādānatvopalakṣakaṃ vanatvaṃ, upakaraṇatvopalakṣakaṃ vṛkṣatvaṃ ca abhidhatte | (ataḥ - nimittatvopādānatvopakaraṇatvātmaka trividhakāraṇatvamapi brahmaṇi avagataṃ bhavati | sūkṣma- cetanācetanaviśiṣṭaveṣeṇa upādānatvam, kālādṛṣṭa| diviśiṣṭaveṣeṇa upakaraṇatvam, saṅkalpaviśiṣṭa- veṣeṇa nimittatvam—iti) | eṣa eva - paramātmaiva, ātmānameva – svasvarūpameva, svayameva- itarānapekṣa eva (svasaṅkalpamātrāt ), akuruta – nānāvidhavicitrānantāścaryā kāraviśiṣṭamāpādayā- māseti, (‘tadātmānaṃ svayamakuruta' iti śrutirvakti) | tadbhūtayonitvam — tasyaiva parasya brahmaṇa: sarvabhūtopādānatvam uktam — 'yadbhūtayoniṃ paripaśyanti dhīrāḥ' iti śrutyā pratipannam, tasmāt — pradarśitāt jñāpakastomāt devaḥ svayaṃjyotiḥ paramātmā sarvatattvāntarātmā - yataḥ sarveṣāṃ tattvānāmantaryāmitayā niyantā | ataḥ, kartā'pi kāryānuguṇajñānacikīrṣākṛtimattayā nimittakāraṇamapi, (tathā) prakṛtirapi - pariṇāmi cetanācetanaviśiṣṭatayā upādānamapi bhavet — bhavitumarhatyeva (nā'tra vipratipattiheturasti) || 17 ||

nopādānaṃ nimittaṃ kimapi taditarat kāraṇaṃ taddhi vidmo
yadvā siddhaṃ nimittaṃ na bhajati tadupādānatāmityayuktam | iṣṭādākārabhedādubhayaghaṭanato lokavedānurodhe
siddhe svacchanda lakṣmapraṇayanakusṛtiḥ pākacintāvikāpaḥ || 18 ||

upādānaṃ kimapi — upādānakāraṇatvayuktaṃ yatkimapi vastu, na nimittam - nimitta- kāraṇavākrāntaṃ naiva bhavitumarhati | yadvā yadapi vastu, nimittaṃ siddham - nimittakāraṇamiti niścitam tat upādānatāṃ na bhajati tadvastu upādānakāraṇatāṃ naiva labhate | hiyataḥ tat — nimittakāraṇabhūtaṃ vastu taditarat vidmaḥ samavāyyasamavāyikāraṇabhinnaṃ kāraṇamityeva niścinuma:, iti ayuktam - evamālocanaṃ yuktividūram iṣṭāt - prāmāṇikasaṃmatāt, ākāra- bhedāt — nimittatvopādānatvaprayojakāt mitho vilakṣaṇāt dharmaviśeṣāt ubhayaghaṭanataḥ- nimittatvopādānatvarūpakāraṇatādvayasyāpi sāmānādhikaraṇyacintanāt, lokavedānurodhe siddhe- lokavedobhayagata kāryakāraṇavyavahāre susaṅgate sati, svacchandakusṛtiḥ pramāṇānanurodhi-

svecchāmā trānurodhi nimittopādānalakṣaṇanirmāṇātmakadurmārgānusaraṇam, pākacintāvipākaḥ— apariṇataprajñānāṃ bālānāṃ vicārapariṇāma eva ; na vidvajjanacintāpariṇāmo'yam || 18 ||
uktvā taccāntarāṇāṃ vilayamatha tamasyekatā mātramuktaṃ
proktaṃ cānāditādi prakṛti-puruṣayorveda - tadvedivākyaiḥ | līyete tau parasminniti tu layavacaḥ syādayastoyanītyā
tenā'sau bhoktṛ-bhogyaprabhṛtikavacitādviśvasṛṣṭiḥ samīcī || 19 ||

tattvāntarāṇām –tamobhinnānāṃ pṛthivyādīnāṃ tattvānām, vilayam - kāryāvasthā- prahāṇapūrvaka kāraṇāvasthā prāptirūpaṃ layam uktvā - ( subālopaniṣadi ) 'pṛthivyapsu pralīyate' ityādinā ' akṣaraṃ tamasi līyate' ityantena pratipādya, atha - paścāt, tamasi - tamorūpe tave, ekatāmātram - ekībhāvāparaparyāyaṃ paramātmanā saha vibhāgānarha saṃsarga viśeṣamātram, na tu pūrvokto layaḥ) uktam — 'tamaḥ pare deva ekībhavati' iti śrutivākyenābhihitam, prakṛti- puruṣayoḥ pradhānatattva - jīvatantvayoḥ, anāditādi - utpattivināśarā hityam, veda- tadvedi- vākyaiḥ–'ajāmekām, jñājñau dvāvajau' ityādinā vedavākyena, (tathā) 'prakṛtiṃ puruṣaṃ caiva viddhyanādī ubhāvapi' ityādirūpeṇa vedavedināṃ vyāsādīnāṃ vākyena ca, proktaṃ ca asandigdhaṃ yathā bhavati tathā pratipāditamapi, tau prakṛti - puruṣāvubhāvapi parasmin paramātmani, līyete - layaṃ prāpnutaḥ ityarthakam, layavacastu – 'prakṛtiryā mayā''khyātā vyaktāvyakta- svarūpiṇī | puruṣaścāpyubhāvetau līyete paramātmani' iti parāśaravacanaṃ tu, ayastoyanītyā syāt – agniniṣṭapte āyudhādirūpe ayovikārabhūte dravye nīraṃ saṃsṛṣṭaṃ cet yathā bahirna jñāyate ayasaiva ca avibhaktaṃ tiṣṭhati, tathārūpaṃ prakṛtipuruṣayoḥ paramātmanā saha avibhaktasaṃsargameva pratipādayet ; na tu prakṛtitva- puruṣatvāvasthā prahāṇapūrvaka parabrahmatvāvasthā prāptiṃ pratipādayet | tena- uktena hetunā, bhoktṛ-bhogyaprabhṛti kavacitāt — cetanācetanaśarīra kā t kālaśarīrakā cca parasmāt brahmaṇaḥ, asau viśvasṛṣṭiḥ - śruti smṛtipratipannā jagadutpattiḥ, samīcī— abādhita- siddhikā || 19 ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā

uktaṃ hyubhayahetutvaṃ sarvavijñānataḥ purā | prakṛtyadhikṛtau tasya samyagākṣipya nirṇayaḥ || 67 ||
|| iti prakṛtyadhikaraṇam || 7 ||

0-

śrīpatiḥ– śriyaḥ patiriti vaiṣṇavaiḥ sevyamāno nārāyaṇaḥ, viśveśaścet — sarvajaganniyantā īśvara ityucyate yadi, asau – sarveśvarapadavācyo nārāyaṇaḥ, trāṇamātrādhikārī katham ? - sṛṣṭi-pālana-saṃhāreṣu madhye pālanamātre kathaṃ prabhurbhavet ? sarvasyāpi khalu prabhuṇā bhavitavyaṃ tena | evaṃ sthite, dūraṃ gatvā'pi bhavatā nārāyaṇe vidhi-śivāpekṣayā vailakṣaṇyasādhanena bahudūragamana- sadṛśaṃ prayāsamanubhūyā'pi duḥkhyadvidhiśivatulayā – trimūrtyantargatasya viṣṇorapi, svādhikāra- prāpte karmaṇi pravartya tadā tadā duḥkhaviśeṣamanubhavato: brahmarudrayoḥ samānatāyā avarjyatayā, ghaṭṭakuṭyāṃ prabhātam — śulkagrahaṇasthānayukta panthānamapahāya anyatra yātuṃ pravṛttasya, paṇyabhāravāhino vaṇijaḥ prabhātasamaya eva suviditaṃ śulkagrahaṇasthānameva mārgabhraṃśādinā prāpnuvataḥ samaṃ jātam ; prayāsasya viphalatvāt, maivam — evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ naiva kriyatām, niravadhika bṛhatpauruṣe – sarvāti- śāyini bṛhatvabṛṃhaṇatvarūpa mahāsāmarthyavati, brahmaśasraṣṭari - viriñciśivayorapi janake, naḥ pūruṣe -- puruṣasūkta pratipādya paratattvatayā asmadabhimate śrīmati nārāyaṇe, etat - madhye viriñciśivayoḥ vihitāvatāratvam, matsyādibhāveṣviva bhagavataḥ matsyakūrmādyavatāreṣviva nijavibhavānu- kriyānāṭyam – svavibhūtyantargatayoḥ brahma-rudrayoḥ anukaraṇarūpaṃ svalīlātmakaṃ jagannāṭakakṛtyameva; na tu vidhi- śivayoriva anyapāratantryāyattaṃ kṛtyam ||21||

adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ

sarvavyākhyānādhikaraṇe – pūrvādhikaraṇānudāhṛtavākyaviśeṣeṣu yeṣu yeṣu jagatkāraṇatvamātraṃ śrutam, na tu īkṣaṇaviśeṣādayaḥ paramapuruṣāsādhāraṇadharmāḥ śrutāḥ teṣu yathāpratipanna vastvabhyupa- game'pi virodhābhāvāt, na paramātmatvaparam - ityāśaṅkaya,

parabrahmāsādhāraṇadharmāntarāśravaṇe'pi jagatkāraṇatvasya īkṣaṇaviśeṣādivat paramapuruṣā- sādhāraṇyasya pūrvairadhikaraṇaiḥ nirṇītatvāt jagatkāraṇatvameva cidacidvilakṣaṇasarvavidhakāraṇabhūta- parabrahmaśabdavācya devatāviśeṣaparatvasādhakamiti teṣvapi vākyeṣu sa eva pratipādyate iti siddhāntitam ||

nayasaṅgatimālikā sarvatroktaprakāreṇa nirvāhaḥ svayamūhyatām |
iti cātrā'tidiśyante prāguktāḥ sarvanītayaḥ || 68 || itthamaṣṭādhikaraṇī peṭikādvayasaṅgatā |
ādyādhyāye turya pāde sandarbha samupāgamat ||69 ||
iti sarvavyākhyānādhikaraṇam || 8 ||

śārīrakanyāyakalāpa saṅgraha:
prakriyāpratyabhijñānāt svabhāvakathanāt tathā | saṅkhyopasaṅgrahāccā'pi vyākulatvāt parasparam ||52|| karmasambandhakathanāt patyādiśravaṇāttathā |
loke ca bhedasandṛṣṭeḥ upādāna - nimittayoḥ || 53|| brahmaiva kāraṇaṃ nānyadityeṣā naiva niścitiḥ |
iti yat tāntrikeṣūktaṃ tadanena nirākṛtam ||54 ||

prakriyā pratyabhijñānāt – 'indriyebhyaḥ parā hyarthā:' ityādibhiḥ kaṭhavallīgataiḥ vākyai -- co॒dhye arthe, 'sāṅkhyaprakriyaiveyam' iti pratyabhijñāyāḥ siddhatvāt tathā svabhāvakathanāt - evameva 'ajāmekām' iti taittirīya śvetāśvatarīyaśrutyoḥ sāṅkhyābhimataprakṛtigatasya svabhāvasyaiva pratipādanāt saṅkhyopasaṅgrahāccāpi – ' yasmin pañca pañcajanā:' iti bṛhadāraṇyakaśrutau sākhyasaṃmatatactvagatapañcaviṃśati saṃkhyāyāḥ 'pañca pañca' tyanena ekatra saṅgṛhya kathanāt, parasparaṃ vyākulatvāt – śrutigateṣu jagatkāraṇavākyeṣu parasparaṃ viruddhārthakanāyā darśanāt, karmasambandha- kathanāt – kauṣītakīṃ vākye 'yo vai bālāke.... yasya vaitat karma' ityasmin puṇya pāpātmakasya jīvagatasya karmaṇaḥ sambandhapratipādanāt patyādiśravaṇāttathā - evameva patipatnyādeḥ karmavaśya- jīvāsādhāraṇasya śrutau pratipādanāt upādāna- nimittayoḥ bhedasya – upādānakāraṇe nimitta- kāraṇe ca ekakāryanirūpite anekatvasyaiva, loke sandṛṣṭeśca ghaṭopādānabhūte mṛdravye, ghaṭanimitta- bhūte kulāle ca sphuṭamavagamāt brahmaiva kāraṇaṃ nānyat ityeṣā – paraṃ brahmakameva jagataḥ kāraṇaṃ- mubhayavidham iti vedāntavādibhirucyamānā, naiva niścitiḥ - abādhitasiddhikā na bhavitumarhati, iti tāntrikeṣu uktaṃ yat -- evaṃ prakāraṃ sāṅkhyatantrābhyupagantṛṣu paridṛśyamānaṃ yat vacanamasti, tadanena nirākṛtam — tat sarvaṃ turyeṇānena pādena yuktividūramiti vivecakānāṃ kṛte -pradarśitam ||52, 53, 54||

".

tribhiḥ pradhānasya nirākṛtiḥ syāt caturthato'nyonyavirodhabhaṅgaḥ |
muktiṃ gato bandhagataśca jīvo vyāvartitaḥ pañcamaṣaṣṭhakābhyām || 55 || tribhiḥ—ānumānika-camasa- saṅkhyopasaṅgrahākhyaiḥ tribhiradhikaraṇaiḥ, pradhānasya nirā- kṛtiḥ syāt – sāṅkhyābhimatapradhānasyaiva śrutibodhyatvamityasyāṃśasya dūrīkaraṇaṃ bhavet |

caturthataḥ — kāraṇatvādhikaraṇātturīyāt, anyonyavirodhabhaṅgaḥ — jagatkāraṇavākyānāṃ mitho vibhinnatātparyakatābhramavimocanam, pañcama-ṣaṣṭhakābhyām —jagadvā citva - vākyānvayādhikaraṇā-
bhyām, muktiṃ gataḥ bandhagataśca jīvaḥ – saṃsārabandhānmukti prāptaḥ, saṃsārabandhe nigaḍitaśca jīvātmā, vyāvartitaḥ— śrutigata kāraṇavādivākya tātparyaviṣayo na bhavatīti spaṣṭīkṛtam | etāvatā sūtrakalāpakena nirīśvaraṃ sāṃkhyamapācakāra |
śeṣāddhunoti sma ca seśvaraṃ tadaikyaṃ nimitteśvarayoḥ samicchan || 56 || etā .... lāpakena - ānumānikasūtramārabhya, avasthiteriti sūtrāntena sūtrajātena, nirīśvaraṃ sāṃkhyam–īśvarasyānabhyupagantṛkāpilaṃ sāṃkhyam, apācakāra — śrutyanabhimatārthakatayā viśadī- cakāra | śeṣāt – prakṛtisūtramārabhya ' etena sarve vyākhyātā:' ityantāt sūtrajātāt, nimitteśvarayoḥ aikyaṃ samicchan – jagannimitte jagadupādāne sarvaniyantari īśvare ca abhedaṃ sādhayitukāmaḥ sūtrakāraḥ, seśvaraṃ tat – īśvarābhyupagantṛpātañjalaṃ sāṃkhyam, dhunoti sma - śrutyanabhimatārthagrāhi iti bodhayati bādarāyaṇaḥ || 56 ||

evaṃ jagatkāraṇavādivākyairuktaṃ samastairapi caikakaṇṭhaiḥ |
brahmaiva sarvajñamanantaśakti nidānameṣāṃ jagatāmitīdam || 57 ||

evam pūrvoktayā rītyā samastairapi – akhilarapi, jagatkāraṇavādivākyaiḥ- jagatkāraṇabhūtatattvapratipādakaiḥ sarvavedāntāntargatairvākyaiḥ, ekakaṇṭhaiḥ – avirudvaikatātparyakaiḥ sadbhiḥ, sarvajñam - nikhilacetanā cetanātmakasthūla sūkṣmarūpaviśvaviṣayakajñānavat, anantaśakti-apa- nikhilacetanācetanātmakasthūla ricchinnasāmarthyavat, brahmaiva paraṃ brahmakameva, eṣāṃ jagatāṃ nidānamitīdam - sarveṣāmeṣāma- saṃkhyānānāmapi bhuvanānām ādikāraṇamityetat uktam – paryāptairhetubhiḥ samarthitam || 57 ||

sūkṣmacidaciccharīraṃ kāraṇatāṃ yāti tat paraṃ brahma |
sthūlacidaciccharīraṃ punaśca tat kāryatāṃ yāti || 58 ||
tat paraṃ brahma-śrutyā nikhila jagatkāraṇatāvattayā pratipannaṃ parabrahma, sūkṣmacidaciccharīram- sūkṣmapadavācyāvasthāviśeṣāpannacetanācetanakavacitaṃ sat, kāraṇatāṃ yāti -sṛjyamānārthasṛṣṭi- hetutāṃ prāpnoti, punaśca tat — tadeva brahma, sthūlacidaciccharīram - sthūlapadavācyā vasthā viśeṣā- pannacetanācetanakavacitaṃ sat, kāryatāṃ yāti — sṛjyamānārthasvarūpatāṃ prāpnoti || 58 || seneśvarāryaracita sannyāyakalāpasaṅgrahasyāsya |

adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ sāṃkhyoktaprakriyoktestadabhimatasṛje statprasaṃkhyāna klṛpteḥ tatproktā vyākṛtaikyāt svavṛjinavacanāt tatphalāvadyayogāt | bhedāt kartṛ-prakṛtyorduhiṇaśivamukhāneka hetu śruteśca
kṣiptaṃ pādatrayoktaṃ śrutihṛdayasamudghāṭanādanvarakṣat || 22|| sāṃkhyo....yokteḥ_sāṃkhyābhimatapradhānāditattvābhyupagamādividhāyā eva kaṭhaśrutyādau

uṣasteruttaramidam—‘prastāvabhaktau adhyasya upāsyā devatā 'prāṇa' rūpā ( prāṇayatīti prāṇa:) paramātmā | yataḥ paridṛśyamānāni cetanā cetanātma- kāni sarvāṇyapi bhūtāni prāṇākhye vastunyeva layaṃ gacchanti, prāṇātsa- kāśa! deva udgacchanti, ataḥ sarvabhūtalayotpattisthānatvena vedāntasiddhā prāṇarūpā devataiva sā | tāṃ ca prāṇarūpāṃ devatāmanabhijñāya yadi prastāva - makariṣyaḥ ; tadā tava śiraḥ vyapatiṣyadeva, nātra saṃśayaḥ' iti ||

are ! haṃsa ! ayaṃ jānaśrutirnāma rājā, bhavatā 'mahātejasvī' iti praśasyate khalu ! imaṃ kena vā mahimnā yuktaṃ praśaṃsasi tvam ? śakaṭasyādhastādupaviṣṭo brahmajño raika iva ayaṃ rājā kiṃ mahāmahimayukto bhavati ? naiva | ato viphalo'yaṃ praśaṃsā tava |

329. I 'pṛthivyapsu pralīyate, āpastejasi līyante, tejo vāyau līyate, vāyurākāśe līyate, ākāśamindriyeṣu, indriyāṇi tanmātreṣu, tanmātrāṇi bhūtādau līyante, bhūtādirmahati līyate, mahānavyakte līyate, avyaktamakṣare līyate, akṣaraṃ tamasi līyate, tamaḥ pare deva ekībhavati' II ' pṛthivyapsu līyate'
pṛthivī pṛthivyavasthāṃ tadupakramabhūta gandhatanmātrāvasthāṃ ca hitvā ambbavasthā satī prāgavasthiteṣu samudrādijaleṣu saṃsṛṣṭā bhavati | nirantara - saṃśleṣamāpannā satī ekatāmāpannetyucyate | tyaktajalāvasthāḥ tyaktarasa- tanmātrāvasthāśca āpaḥ, tejovasthāḥ satyaḥ prāgavasthitatejasā sampṛktāḥ tadekatāṃ prāptā bhavanti | tejaśca tyaktatejovasthaṃ tyaktarūpatanmātrāvasthaṃ ca vāyvavasthaṃ sat, prāgavasthite vāyau līyate - ekatāṃ prāpnoti | vāyuśca tyaktavāyvavasthaḥ tyaktasparśatanmātrāvasthaśca san ākāśāvasthāmāpannaḥ, prāgavasthite ākāśe ekatāṃ layaṃ gacchati | ākāśaṃ ca tyaktākāśā- vasthaṃ śabdatanmātrāvasthaṃ sat indriyaiḥ saha saṃsṛṣṭaṃ bhavati | pūrvam ākāśasaṃsṛṣṭāni indriyāṇi paścāt śabdatanmātreṣu saṃsṛṣṭāni bhavanti | śabdatanmātrāṃśāḥ ahaṅkāre līnatāṃ gacchanti | ahaṅkāraśca mahadākhye tattve, mahattattvaṃ ca guṇasāmyalakṣaṇe avyakte ; tacca avyaktākhyaṃ tattvam, asphuṭaguṇasāmyalakṣaṇe cetanasamaṣṭigarbhe akṣarapadavācye tattve ; tacca akṣaram_ atisūkṣme pradhānapadavācye vibhakte tamasi, akṣarādyavasthāprāsyonmukhya- viśiṣṭe tattve ca yathākramaṃ līyante | tacca vibhaktaṃ tamaḥ avibhaktatama... śśabdābhilapye akṣarādyavasthāprāptyau nmukhyarahite tamasi - tādṛśatama- iśarīra ke paramātmani ekatāmāpadyate śarīra- śarīribhāvo'pi yathā vaktuṃ na śakyeta tathā saṃśliṣṭaṃ bhavati |

650. I ' so'bhidhyāya śarīrāt svātsisṛkṣurvividhāḥ prajāḥ | apa eva sasarjādau tāsu vīryamapāsṛjat || tadaṇḍamabhavaddhaimaṃ sahasrāṃśusamaprabham |
tasmiñjajñe svayaṃ brahmā sarvalokapitāmahaḥ ||'

649.

II ' so'bhidhyāya .... sṛjat'

paramātmā sanātanaḥ, jagatsṛṣṭyupayogi saṅkalpaṃ kṛtvā, svakīyāt tanoḥ, vividhavicitrānantaprakārāḥ prajāḥ sraṣṭumicchuḥ san prathamataḥ aptattvameva sṛṣṭavān ; tatra ca svīyaṃ vīryaṃ vimuktavān | apsu visṛṣṭaṃ paramātmano vīryaṃ sūryasamaprakāśaṃ sauvarṇam aṇḍaṃ sañjātam | tasmiṃśca sauvarṇe aṇḍe sarvalokapitāmaho brahmā svayaṃ sañjātavān |

651. ' sthānādivyapadeśācca'

652. ' anādinidhanā hyeṣā vāgutsṛṣṭā svayambhuvā | ādau vedamayī divyā yataḥ sarvāḥ prabhūtayaḥ || '

ananyādhīnasattākena paramātmanā sṛṣṭerārambhasamaye yā kevalaṃ śravaṇa- mātrasamadhigamyā tādṛśī, utpatti vināśābhyāmasaṃspṛṣṭā atīndriyatattvārthā- vedanaikasvabhāvaśabdamātrapracurā apauruṣeyī vāgeva niḥśvāsarūpeṇa svasya antaraṅgādudbahiḥ prakaṭatāṃ prāpitā | tāmavalambyaiva laukikyaḥ sarvā sṛṣṭayaḥ pravṛttāḥ ||

pra. saṃ.

654. 'avyaktādiviśeṣāntaṃ pariṇāmarddhisaṃyutam |

657. 'krīḍato bālakasyeva ceṣṭāṃ tasya niśāmaya' ramamāṇasya śiśoḥ līlāsadṛśīṃ paramātmanaḥ līlāṃ śṛṇu |
658. 'jagatāmupakārāya na sā karmanimittajā'

659. ' tatprāptiheturjñānaṃ ca karma coktaṃ mahāmune |

pra. saṃ.

pra. saṃ.

660. 'tatra suptasya devasya nābhau padmamajāyata |
tasmin padme mahābhāga ! vedavedāṅgapāragaḥ ||
brahmotpannaḥ sa tenoktaḥ prajāssṛja mahāmune |'
he mahābhāgya sampanna ! kṣīrābdhau yoganidrayā nidritasya paramātmano viṣṇoḥ nābhisthāne kiñcit kamalam utpannam | nābhisthānasthe kamale veda- tadaṅgeṣu svayameva paripūrṇajñānavān brahmā – caturmukho jātaḥ, sa ca brahmā bhagavatā uktaḥ ; 'he mahāmananaśīla ! prajānāṃ sṛṣṭi kuru' iti | 661 tadeva prītaye bhūtvā punarduḥkhāya jāyate |

tadeva kopāyaṃ yataḥ prasādāya ca jāyate | tasmāduḥkhātmakaṃ nā'sti na ca kiścitsukhātmakam ||'

yataḥ ekameva vastu, kāla- deśa - vyaktyā divailakṣaṇyānurodhena prīti- duḥkhobhayasyāpi janakaṃ bhavati, kopa- prasādayorubhayorapi heturbhavati ; tasmāt kimapyekaṃ vastu, sukhātmakamiti vā duḥkhātmakamiti vā nirūpayituṃ na śakyate |
662. ' tasmādavyaktamutpannaṃ triguṇaṃ dvijasattama'

yasya paramātmanaḥ dyulokameva śirastvena, ākāśameva nābhisthānāpanna - tvena, śaśi divākarau netratvena prācī pratīcyā didiśa: śrotratvena, (tathā) bhūmiṃ ca pādasthānāpannāṃ jñāninaḥ āmananti | samasta carācarātmaka- jagatsraṣṭā saḥ virārūpa: paramātmā aparicchedya svarūpa iti jānīhi | 'na bhūtasaṅghasaṃsthāno deho'sya paramātmanaḥ' loke avatāraṃ prāptasyā'pyasya uktavaibhavasya paramātmanaḥ avatārakālīno dehaḥ, na pṛthivyādipañcabhūtasamudāyāvayavakaḥ |

pra. saṃ.

sura-nara-tiryak-sthāvarākhyānāṃ caturvidhānāmapi dehināṃ deva manuṣya - mṛga- vṛkṣādijātyabhidhānaṃ nāma, evam animeṣatva- dvipātva- catuṣpātva- śākhitvādikaṃ rūpam, havirbhāgayajñādyanuṣṭhānādirūpāṇāṃ kartavyānāṃ vistaraṇaṃ ca

667. 'prakṛtiryā mayā''khyātā vyaktāvyaktasvarūpiṇī | puruṣaścāpyabhāvetau līyete paramātmani ||

'bālaḥ krīḍanakairiva'

677. 'sarvagatvādanantasya sa evāhamavasthitaḥ |

678. 'sṛṣṭvā nāraṃ toyamantaḥ sthito'haṃ yena syānme nāma nārāyaṇeti | kalpe kalpe tatra śayāmi bhūyassuptasya me nābhijaṃ syādyathā'bjam || evambhūtasya me devi nābhi padme caturmukhaḥ |
utpannaḥ sa mayā coktaḥ prajāḥ sṛja mahāmate |'

684. 'īśvaraḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ hṛdeśe'rjuna tiṣṭhati |

692. 'bhūmirāpo'nalo vāyuḥ khaṃ mano buddhireva ca |

' mattaḥ parataraṃ nā'nyat kiñcidasti dhanañjaya |

.

696. 'sattvaṃ rajastama iti guṇāḥ prakṛtisambhavāḥ |
nivadhnanti mahābāho dehe dehinamavyayam ||'

697. 'sarvabhūtāni kaunteya prakṛtiṃ yānti māmikām |

he kaunteya ! sthāvarajaṅgamātmakāni sarvāṇi bhūtāni antimabrahma- divasāvasāne tamaśśabdavācyāṃ nāmarūpavibhāgānahī maccharīrabhūtāṃ prakṛti matsaṅkalpādeva pṛthaksthitipratipattyanarhatayā prāpnuvanti | punaḥ kalpārambhe tāni sarvāṇyapi bhūtāni svasaṅkalpenaiva ahameva utpādayāmi |
698. 'sarvasya cā'haṃ hṛdi sanniviṣṭo mattaḥ smṛtirjñānamapohanaṃ ca '
sarvasyāpi bhūtajātasya sakala pravṛttinivṛttimūlabhūta jñānodaya deśe hṛdayākhya- sthāne, aham antaryāmitayā sanniviṣṭaḥ | ataḥ matta eva sarveṣāṃ pūrvānubhūtaviṣayānubhava saṃskāramātrajajñānarūpā smṛtiḥ, indriyaliṅgāgama- yogajavastuniścayātmakaṃ jñānam, tathā jñānanivṛtyātmakam ūhātmakaṃ vāspohanamapi matta eva madāyattameva |
699. 'asti hi madhvādiṣu sambhavo brahmaṇa eva sarvatra nicāyyatvāt '
madhuvidyādiṣu vidyāviśeṣeṣu, vasvādipadaṃ prāptānāmapi upāsanādhi- kārasya sambhavaḥ astyeva khalu ! ; yataḥ sarvatrā'pi vidyāsu, sarvatra dehabhede vā brahmaiva upāsanena sākṣātkaraṇīyam; tasya copāsanasya teṣvapi abādhitatvāditi |

92 paramātmā hi ! karmabhirārādhitaḥ, tattatkarmānuguṇaṃ pratiniyata deśakālasvarūpa- parimāṇam, ārādhakānāṃ tattadvastugataṃ priyatvamāpādayati |
93 'svaśarīrabhūte jīvātmani ātmatayā avasthite:, 'jīva' śabdena brahmaprati- pādanam' iti kāśakṛtsna ācāryo manyate sma | 'jīva' śabdaśca jīvasya paramātmaparyantasyaiva vācakaḥ ; na jīvamātrasya_iti |
94 evam ātmaśarīrabhāvena tādātmyopapādane, parasya brahmaṇaḥ apahatapāpmatva- sarvajñatvā digocarāḥ, jīvasya aviduṣaḥ śocataḥ brahmopāsanāt mokṣa- vādinyaḥ, jagataḥ brahmatādātmyopadeśaparāśca sarvāḥ śrutayaḥ samyagupapāditā bhavanti iti kāśakṛtsnīyameva mataṃ sūtrakāraḥ svīkṛtavān |
95 saba letara vilakṣaṇasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ sarvaśakteḥ sarvajñasya ekasyaiva, sarvam upapadyate | mṛdādeḥ acetanasya jñānābhāvena adhiṣṭhātṛtvāyogāt, adhiṣṭhātuḥ kulā- lādeḥ vicitrapariṇāmaśaktivirahāt, asatyasaṅkalpatayā ca, tathā darśana niyamaḥ | ataḥ brahmaiva jagato nimittam upādānaṃ ca |
96 avibhaktanāmarūpaḥ ātmā kartā ; sa eva vibhaktanāmarūpaḥ kāryamiti kartṛtva- karmatvayoḥ na virodhaḥ | 'svayameva ātmānaṃ tathā akuruta' iti nimittam upādānaṃ ca |
97 na atra upadiśyamānasya pariṇāmasya parasmin brahmaṇi doṣāvahatvaṃ svabhāvaḥ,
pratyuta niraṅkuśaiśvaryāvahatvameva — ityabhiprāyaḥ |

'tamaḥ' śabdavācyā tisūkṣmācidvastvekaśeṣe sati, tamasi ca svaśarīratayā'pi pṛthanirdeśānarhātisūkṣmadaśāpatyā svasmin ekatāmāpanne sati, tathā- bhūtatamaśśarīraṃ brahma, 'pūrvavat vibhaktanāmarūpa cidacinmiśraprapañcaśarīraṃ syām' iti saṅkalpya, adhyayakrameṇa jagaccharīratayā ātmānaṃ pariṇamayatīti, sarveṣu vedānteṣu pariṇāmopadeśaḥ |
99 sarveṣāmātmanāṃ jñānāvaraṇānarthamūlatvena, tadeva hi | tamaḥ 'mṛtyu' śabdavyapadeśyam | 100 avibhāgāpattidaśāyāmapi, cidacidvastu atisūkṣmaṃ sakarmasaṃskāraṃ tiṣṭhati | 101 'kāraṇāvasthāyām ātmatayā avasthitaḥ paramātmaiva, kāryarūpeṇa vikriyamāṇa- dravyasyāpi ātmatayā avasthāya, tattadabhavat' ityucyate |
102 evaṃ paramātma-cidacitsaṅghā tarūpa jagadākārapariṇāme, paramātmaśarīrabhūtacidaṃśa- gatāḥ sarve eva apuruṣārthāḥ ; tathābhūtācidaṃśagatāśca sarve vikārāḥ | paramātmani kāryatvam— tattadavasthayoḥ tayoḥ niyantṛtvenā''tmatvam | paramātmā tu tayoḥ svaśarīrabhūtayoḥ niyantṛtayā ātmabhūtaḥ tadgatā'puruṣārthaiḥ vikāraiśca na spṛśyate ; aparicchinnajñānānandamayaḥ sarvadā ekarūpa eva, jagatparivartanaśīlayā avatiṣṭhate |
